#CH 151
[ZH] 文渊已恢复平静，陈浩宇也不再计较，申简辰不用继续担心。他揪心千里寻，为不引起大家疑心，吩咐白风将说话艺人唤来。
[ZH] 已有下人摆上桌子，不多时，说话艺人走进，手中依旧端着紫砂壶，抿了一口，将紫砂壶放置桌上，先向诸位打招呼：“诸位，本人可是第一次被人请到家中说书，还真有些不习惯，若有不到之处，还请见谅。”
[ZH] 曹班说：“不要废话，快些说故事，我们都等着听呢！”
[ZH] 说话艺人说道：“客官别急，这就道来。”
[ZH] 将手中惊堂木一拍：“诸位，上回说到武玄英英雄气概，替父带兵出征，平定叛乱。话说，武玄英带领五万人马，下夸红色赤兔马，手拿轩辕剑，带领将士浩浩荡荡向南进发……”
[ZH] 申简辰无心听下去，用手招来喜兰。喜兰不敢怠慢，走到王爷身边。
[ZH] 申简辰向她耳语几句，喜兰只顾点头。待王爷叮嘱完，回到郡主身旁，在她耳边说：“王爷要去趟茅房，让郡主招呼大家，尤其注意不要让乔公子与陈公子再打起来。”
[ZH] 乔梦容听完，向哥哥点点头。
[ZH] 申简辰投以微笑，起身离开。
[ZH] 白风想要跟上，被王爷阻止：“我去茅房，你不用跟着，在这里替我照顾客人。”
[ZH] 白风只得留下。
[ZH] 申简辰哪里是去茅房，而是去寻千里寻。他从正门出去，运气不错，刚走出院子，瞧见苦荞站在高台之下。
[ZH] 抬头向上望，千里寻正在高台之上欣赏风景。
[ZH] 苦荞瞧见仪王，上前行礼：“仪王。”
[ZH] “白风在后院等你，你却站在这里，是何道理？”申简辰忍不住欺骗苦荞，只有将她支开，他才方便登上高台。
[ZH] 听到白风等她，苦荞犹豫不决：“我要伺候小姐，没法儿去见白风。”
[ZH] 这丫头坚持不走，又如何能难住申简辰：“放心去吧，一会儿幽栖居士问起，有我在，她绝不会怪罪你。”
[ZH] 有王爷这话，苦荞不再犹豫：“谢王爷。”高高兴兴往后院而去。
[ZH] 申简辰一步一步登上高台，他特意加快速度，想快些与千里寻说话，这么好的机会，只有他与她，极目远眺的高台之上，能看到带他们一起穿越而来的神柳……
[ZH] 千里寻眺望着远处的神柳，想着个人的感情之路，她在心中说：“神柳啊神柳，我不愿再涉足感情，想一个人生活下去，可命运跟我开了一个大大的玩笑，给我安排一段毫无道理的姻缘，难道陈浩宇是我的归宿？不！我无法接受！神柳，你告诉我，我的归宿到底在哪里？”
[ZH] 也许是离得远，神柳并未给她任何答复，可她却清楚地听到一个声音：“千里寻！”
[ZH] 转过身，仪王出现在面前，千里寻更加恍惚，在心中自问：“难道他是我的归宿？”
[ZH] 很快她变得清醒：“不！怎么可能？他是梦容爱的人，我不能做对不起梦容之事。”
[ZH] 千里寻顾不上拜见，责怪道：“都给仪王提醒过，不要随便叫出那个名字，为何又叫？”
[ZH] “我喜欢这么叫你，现在没人，叫叫又何妨？”
[ZH] 仪王的眼神中充满柔情，千里寻在心中骂道：“又来勾引老娘！”她向高台下瞧去，不见苦荞身影。
[ZH] “既然仪王上来看风景，我先下去，把地方让给您。”千里寻说着准备走下高台。
[ZH] 申简辰故意问：“难道幽栖居士已喜欢上我，才要落荒而逃？”
[ZH] 千里寻只好收回脚步：“什么落荒而逃？我出来许久，也该回去欣赏舞蹈。还有，我根本不喜欢王爷，以后不要再说同样的话，否则梦容知道会伤心。”
[ZH] 申简辰望着远处的神柳：“我深爱着一个姑娘，也希望她能爱上我，你看到了吗？那棵神柳就是我们感情的见证。”
[ZH] 千里寻想起仪王与梦容一起在神柳下挂红绸的情景，难道仪王说的姑娘是梦容？好像又不是。
[ZH] 千里寻打算替梦容教训教训花心的王爷：“仪王最大的本事就是背着梦容向其他姑娘示好，您又偷偷带哪个姑娘去过神柳下？该不会又在神柳上挂过红绸？可怜梦容还认为她的哥哥是位感情专一之人。”
[ZH] 申简辰靠近千里寻，继续用深情的目光注视着她：“我喜欢的姑娘就是幽栖居士，就是千里寻，你可喜欢我？”
[ZH] 尽管仪王以前表白过，千里寻还是愣住，向后退出一步：“我跟您可从来没在神柳上拴过红绸……”
[ZH] 申简辰向前一步，用几乎是命令的口吻说：“不许再喜欢文渊，也不许嫁给陈浩宇，你只能属于我！”
[ZH] 千里寻攥紧的拳头没有伸出去，今日不知怎么了，仪王温暖的眼神如同冬日温暖的阳光，驱赶走不少她内心的不适，他近乎命令的口吻，她竟然一点儿都不反感。
[ZH] 为什么会是这样？千里寻感到紧张。想到梦容，她不能再待下去，仪王太懂得如何摄人心魄，只怕继续待下去，她会迷失自我。
[ZH] 千里寻瞅准下去的台阶，一把推开仪王，落荒而逃……
[ZH] 关键的话还未说，千里寻就逃走，申简辰只好再次寻求机会。他瞧向顺着台阶向下走的背影，大声说：“诗会后，神柳下见，我有重要的话对你讲。”
[ZH] “王爷还是不要有期待，我是不会去的。”千里寻边说边匆忙向下走。
[ZH] “你要是不去，我就把我喜欢你的事告诉梦容。”
[EN] With Wenyuan calm again and Chen Haoyu letting the clash drop, Shen Jianchen had no further fight to worry about. His mind stayed anchored on Qianli Xun, yet to avoid drawing suspicion, he told Bai Feng to summon the storyteller.
[ZH] 这招还真灵，千里寻停下脚步，抬头望向一脸得意的仪王：“算你狠，我去，行了吧，但别指望我会对你动心，还有，去了有可能仪王会被我揍一顿。”
[EN] Servants had already set up a small table. Moments later the storyteller entered holding his purple clay teapot, took a sip, set the pot down, and greeted the hall. "Honored guests, this is the first time in my life anyone invited me into a grand home to tell tales. Feels a bit odd, so forgive any slips!"
[ZH] “我正好想找人切磋切磋，与心爱的人切磋，求之不得。”
[EN] "Cut the chatter and start the story. We're all waiting!" Cao Ban called out.
[ZH] 既然打算告诉千里寻赵朴就是申简辰，最好的证明便是申拳。
[EN] "Patience, young masters, right away!" Clapping his gavel block crisp on the wood, the storyteller launched in. "Last time we left off with heroic Wu Xuanying taking command of the army in his father's place to crush the rebellion! Mounted astride his crimson Red Hare steed and gripping the Xuanyuan Sword, Wu Xuanying led fifty thousand troops south in a thunderous march..."
[ZH] 千里寻迅速走下高台，匆忙进入院子，她可不想被仪王赶上，与他一同进入屋内，容易引起大家误会。
[EN] Having no patience to sit through the set, Shen Jianchen beckoned Xilan over. She stepped up at once and nodded along as Prince Yi whispered instructions in her ear, then hurried back to murmur beside Princess Jiahe. "His Highness needs to step out to the latrine and asks Princess to look after the guests. Above all, don't let Young Master Qiao and Young Master Chen clash again."
[ZH] 回到屋内，饭食撤去，桌面已摆上水果和茶点。舞伎已不在，说话艺人正在讲红香与玄英的故事。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong glanced over and gave her brother a quiet nod. Answering with a reassuring smile, Shen Jianchen rose and walked out. When Bai Feng started to follow, Prince Yi halted him. "Just heading to the latrine. Stay here and tend the guests for me." Bai Feng had no choice but to stay behind.
[ZH] 说话艺人正是住在西三胡同之人，千里寻找不到苦荞的身影，还好这丫头不在，否则岂不暴露身份？
[EN] Shen Jianchen was nowhere near heading to a latrine; he was hunting Qianli Xun. Stepping out the main doors, luck smiled on him: right past the courtyard stood Kuqiao beneath the wooden tree platform, and looking up, he spotted Qianli Xun standing atop the platform gazing over the forest.
[ZH] 千里寻回座位坐下，乔梦容凑过来小声问：“怎么出去这么久？”
[EN] Spotting Prince Yi, Kuqiao stepped forward and curtsied. "Your Highness."
[ZH] “幽林别居风景太美，我不由自主多走几步。”
[EN] "Bai Feng is waiting for you in the back courtyard. Why are you idling out here?" Shen Jianchen fibbed without blinking; sending Kuqiao off cleared his way up the platform.
[ZH] 乔梦容笑而不语，继续听书，故事实在太吸引人。
[EN] Hearing Bai Feng waited for her, Kuqiao hesitated. "I'm supposed to attend Miss. I can't run off to see Bai Feng."
[ZH] 千里寻向身后的夏果招手，夏果只顾看乔公子，没有注意到小姐招呼她。
[EN] As if that could stop Shen Jianchen! "Go right ahead. If Youqi Jushi asks later, I'm right here; she won't blame you."
[ZH] 一旁的化雨用手戳戳夏果，夏果才清醒过来，注意到小姐招呼，靠近后问道：“小姐有何吩咐？”
[EN] With royal backing, Kuqiao lit up at once. "Thank you, Your Highness!" Off she skipped toward the back courtyard.
[ZH] “苦荞呢？”
[EN] Climbing the wooden steps fast, Shen Jianchen hurried upward, eager to catch Qianli Xun alone at last. Just the two of them high above the woods, facing the very Divine Willow that had carried them across a thousand years.
[ZH] “跟小姐出去，一直没回来。”
[EN] Up on the platform, Qianli Xun gazed toward the distant willow, turning her tangled fate over in silence. Divine Willow, oh Divine Willow, I swore off romance and just wanted a quiet solo life, yet fate plays a cruel joke tying me to a ridiculous betrothal. Is Chen Haoyu really my ending? No, never! Tell me, Divine Willow, where on earth do I belong?
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，见白风好好站在那里，便猜到苦荞应该怕被说话艺人认出，躲起来。
[EN] Too far away to answer, the willow gave no sign, yet right behind her sounded a clear voice: "Qianli Xun!"
[ZH] 她开始认真听书，听着自己写的故事被人讲出，这种感觉实在美好。
[EN] Spinning around to find Prince Yi standing right there, Qianli Xun froze in a daze, a wild thought flashing across her mind: Is he where I belong? In the next breath she shook herself awake. No, impossible! He's the man Mengrong loves. I could never betray Mengrong!
[ZH] 说话艺人站在桌前，说得动人心弦：
[EN] Skipping formal greetings, she scolded him sharp. "I already warned Your Highness not to blurt out that name. Why say it again?"
[ZH] 武玄英打完胜仗归来，四处寻找华红香，怎么找都找不到。
[EN] "Because I like calling you by it. Nobody's around; what harm?"
[ZH] 武玄英十分痛苦，他与红香好不容易走到一起，难道就这么分开？武玄英不愿认命，去找城主，也就是他的父亲，质问道：“红香去了哪里？”……
[EN] Warmth and open devotion filled Prince Yi's eyes. Qianli Xun cursed under her breath—Trying to seduce me again!—and glanced over the rail only to find Kuqiao gone. "Since Your Highness came up for the view, I'll head down and leave you the platform." She turned toward the stairs.
[ZH] 这时，申简辰已从外面回来，他坐回座位，瞧一眼千里寻，想着她刚才仓皇逃跑的样子，甚觉开心。
[EN] Shen Jianchen baited her on purpose. "Has Youqi Jushi fallen for me already, fleeing in panic?"
[ZH] 说话艺人继续讲着：
[EN] Halting her steps, she snapped back, "Who's fleeing?! I've been out long enough and ought to get back inside for the dances! Besides, I have zero feelings for Your Highness. Quit saying things like that before Mengrong hears and gets hurt!"
[ZH] 城主说道：“孩子，忘了红香吧。”
[EN] Turning his gaze toward the distant willow, Shen Jianchen murmured, "I love one girl with all my heart and long for her to love me back. See over there? That Divine Willow stands witness to our love."
[ZH] “不！我不可能忘记她。”武玄英跪在地上，“父亲，告诉我，您到底将红香藏在哪里？”
[EN] Remembering how Prince Yi and Mengrong had hung red ribbons beneath that willow together, Qianli Xun frowned. Did he mean Mengrong? Didn't sound like it! Ready to dress down a playboy prince on Mengrong's behalf, she shot back, "Your Highness's greatest gift is flirting with other women behind Mengrong's back! Which other girl did you sneak under the Divine Willow to tie ribbons with? Poor Mengrong still thinks her brother is loyal to the bone!"
[ZH] 城主说道：“孩子，我为何要将红香藏起来？她已从这个世界上消失，永远不会再出现。”
[EN] Stepping closer, eyes brimming with earnest feeling, Shen Jianchen spoke plain. "The girl I love is Youqi Jushi, Qianli Xun! Tell me, do you love me?"
[ZH] “消失？”武玄英难以接受，失声痛哭，哭晕在地，不省人事。
[EN] Even having heard his confession before, Qianli Xun froze and stepped back a pace. "I never tied ribbons under the Divine Willow with you..."
[ZH] 说到这里，说话艺人将惊堂木一拍：“预知后事如何，且听下回分解！”
[EN] Taking a step forward to close the gap, Shen Jianchen spoke with a tone bordering on command. "Stop loving Wenyuan, and do not marry Chen Haoyu! You belong to me alone!"
[ZH] 写这段时，千里寻心情不好，故而写得悲情。众人听后，大部分人感慨万千。
[EN] Her clenched fist stayed pinned at her side. Strange—today the warmth in Prince Yi's eyes felt like winter sunshine melting away her chill, and his bossy decree did not even grate on her nerves. Why? Alarm jolted her chest. Thinking of Mengrong, she dared not linger another second; Prince Yi knew how to shake her heart too well, and staying risked losing her head. Spotting the stairway opening, she shoved Prince Yi aside and fled down the steps.
[ZH] 尤其是乔文仙，感同身受，淑真对于他来讲，就像红香消失一般。
[EN] Before he could utter the crucial words, she was gone! Leaning over the railing watching her hurry down the stairs, Shen Jianchen called aloud, "After the salon, meet me under the Divine Willow! I have something vital to tell you!"
[ZH] 仪王刚进来，没听几句，加之沉浸在刚刚与千里寻美好的经历中，这么感人的情节对他毫无影响。
[EN] "Don't hold your breath, Your Highness. I'm not going!" she tossed back without breaking stride.
[ZH] 陈浩宇最不喜听些男欢女爱的故事，觉得实在矫情。武玄英为一个女人晕倒，实在不男人。
[EN] "Skip it, and I tell Mengrong straight to her face that I love you!"
[ZH] 说书艺人打算退下，被东方尘泽唤住：“先生，且慢！”
[EN] That hit dead-center. Qianli Xun skidded to a halt and glared straight up at his smug grin. "Low blow! Fine, I'll show up! Don't dream of me falling for you, and don't whine if you catch a beating!"
[ZH] 说话艺人站住：“公子有何事？”
[EN] "Just looking for a good sparring partner. Sparring with the woman I love couldn't be sweeter!" Since he meant to reveal that Zhao Pu was Shen Jianchen, what better proof than Shen Fist itself?
[ZH] 东方尘泽问：“接下来的故事是什么？”
[EN] Hurrying down the remaining stairs and straight back into the courtyard, Qianli Xun refused to let Prince Yi catch up and spark gossip by walking in together.
[ZH] “恕在下不能告知，公子若想听，明日去桥头瓦，我在那里说接下来的故事。”说书艺人讲完，拜别众人离开。
[EN] Inside the hall, lunch platters had been cleared for fruit and tea snacks. The dancers were gone, and the storyteller was mid-set spinning Hongxiang and Xuanying's tale—none other than the storyteller from West Third Alley! Scanning for Kuqiao and finding her absent brought huge relief; if Kuqiao walked in here, her secret pen name would blow wide open.
[ZH] 乔文仙又看向淑真，他们离得那么近，想与她说句话却难上加难，他再瞧一眼陈浩宇，纨绔的样子，如何能配得上淑真？
[EN] Slipping back into her seat, she met Qiao Mengrong leaning close to whisper, "What kept you out so long?"
[ZH] 乔文仙对无法操控自己与淑真的未来而忧伤，对身边的南双说：“去！给我要壶酒来。”
[EN] "Youlin Retreat's views looked too lovely; I wandered a few extra steps."
[ZH] 南双领命而去。
[EN] Smiling without pushing, Qiao Mengrong turned back to the gripping tale. Qianli Xun beckoned to Xiaguo standing behind her, yet Xiaguo stayed glued on Young Master Qiao without noticing until Huayu poked her arm. Jolted awake, Xiaguo leaned down. "Your orders, Miss?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜慨叹道：“千里寻公子的故事写得实在感人，明日我定会约闺中密友前去听听。”
[EN] "Where's Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 曹班说：“不知为何，我总觉得千里寻是位姑娘，可传言都说他是位公子，你们觉得呢？”
[EN] "She went out with you and never came back inside."
[ZH] 听到这个问题，申简辰偷偷瞟一眼千里寻，嘴角露出笑容。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. Seeing Bai Feng standing right at his post, she assumed Kuqiao had spotted the storyteller and hidden away to keep her cover safe. Settling back, she gave herself over to the story; hearing her own writing come alive before an enthralled crowd felt wonderful.
[ZH] 安明轩认可道：“我也这么觉得。听传言，千里寻公子曾在街市出现过，虽是男子装扮，却长相俊美，极像一位姑娘。”
[EN] Standing behind his table, the storyteller delivered every twist with gripping drama. Returning victorious from battle, Wu Xuanying searched high and low for Hua Hongxiang without a trace. Torn apart—having struggled so hard to be together, could they lose each other now?—Wu Xuanying marched straight to his father the City Lord demanding, "Where did Hongxiang go?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不屑道：“依我看，千里寻就是个娘娘腔，否则怎能写出这么不男人的故事？那个叫武玄英的，哭都能哭晕，你们谁见过？乔文仙，你应该哭过，有没有晕过去？”
[EN] Right then Shen Jianchen returned from outside, took his seat, and cast a warm glance across at Qianli Xun, still chuckling inside at her hasty flight off the platform.
[ZH] 说千里寻是娘娘腔，千里寻倒不生气，她本就是女子，只是伪装成男子而已。
[EN] The storyteller pressed on: "The City Lord said, 'Forget Hongxiang, my son.' 'Never!' Wu Xuanying dropped to his knees. 'Father, tell me where you hid her!' The City Lord answered, 'Why would I hide her? She has vanished from this world and will never return.' 'Vanished?' Crushed beyond bearing, Wu Xuanying broke into piercing sobs and fainted cold onto the floor!" Clack! Down came the gavel block. "To learn what follows next, stay tuned for our next session!"
[ZH] 乔文仙却很不爽，凭什么淑真能嫁给陈浩宇？
[EN] Having written that chapter during a dark mood, Qianli Xun had laid the tragedy on thick, leaving nearly the whole room sighing in sympathy. Qiao Wenxian felt every word cut to the bone, Shuzhen lost to him just as Hongxiang had vanished. Having walked in late and still glowing from his moment on the platform, Prince Yi felt none of the gloom, while Chen Haoyu scoffed at melodramatic romance: what real man faints crying over a girl?
[ZH] “陈浩宇，别得了便宜卖乖，我哭没哭，晕没晕倒，跟你有什么关系？”
[EN] As the storyteller bowed to leave, Dongfang Chenze halted him. "Wait a moment, sir! What happens next?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇调侃道：“一听乔文仙这话，就知道他不是一个一哭就晕倒的人，所以说，千里寻肯定是个娘娘腔。”
[EN] "My lips are sealed, young master! Want the next chapter, come to Qiaotou Wa tomorrow!" Bowing to all, the storyteller withdrew.
[ZH] 乔梦容担心陈公子与乔公子又打起来，让淑真难堪，赶紧说：“这么说来，千里寻应是位美男子，估计他露面，绝对会出现潘安掷果的盛况，所以，他才躲着不出。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian's eyes drifted back to Shuzhen—seated mere paces away, yet speaking one word to her felt impossible—then shifted onto Chen Haoyu's cocky slouch. How could a dandy like that deserve Shuzhen? Ache crushing his chest, he told Nan Shuang, "Go fetch me a pot of wine." Nan Shuang obeyed at once.
[ZH] 申简辰猜到梦容的心思，应和道：“梦容说得没错。美食已享用，舞蹈也欣赏过，故事也听了，接下来该到品鉴诗词的环节，幽栖居士已答应现场做首词，正好供大家品鉴。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu sighed in admiration. "Young Master Qianli Xun writes with such piercing feeling. Tomorrow I'll bring my close friends to hear the next part!"
[ZH] 接着吩咐道：“为幽栖居士摆上纸笔。”
[EN] "Funny thing," Cao Ban mused aloud. "I always get the hunch Qianli Xun is a young lady, even though street rumors insist he's a young gentleman. What do you all think?"
[ZH] 有丫鬟进来，将笔墨纸砚为幽栖居士摆好。
[EN] At that remark, Shen Jianchen shot a sly glance at Qianli Xun, hiding a grin.
[ZH] 千里寻出去转悠时，已想好要写哪首词，立刻提笔写下：
[EN] "Same hunch," An Mingxuan agreed. "Word is Young Master Qianli Xun was spotted once in the market dressed as a man, yet graceful and pretty as a girl."
[ZH] 冬晴无雪，是天心未肯，化工非拙。不放玉花飞堕地，留在广寒宫阙。云欲同时，霰将集处，红日三竿揭。六花翦就，不知何处施设。
[EN] Chen Haoyu snorted in scorn. "Bet Qianli Xun's just some soft sissy. How else write a spineless tale like that? Who on earth faints crying like Wu Xuanying?! Qiao Wenxian, you've cried before; ever pass out?"
[ZH] 应念陇首寒梅，花开无伴，对景真愁绝。待出和羹金鼎手，为把玉盐飘撒。沟壑皆平，乾坤如画，更吐冰轮洁。梁园燕客，夜明不怕灯灭。
[EN] Being called a sissy did not faze Qianli Xun one bit; she was a woman disguised as a guy! Yet Qiao Wenxian's blood boiled. On what merit did Chen Haoyu get to marry Shuzhen? "Quit gloating after stealing a prize, Chen Haoyu! Whether I cried or fainted is none of your business!"
[ZH] 《念奴娇·冬晴无雪》
[EN] Chen Haoyu smirked back. "Hear that? Even Qiao Wenxian doesn't pass out crying. Proves Qianli Xun is a sissy!"
[ZH] 千里寻认真书写时，苦荞已从外面进来。
[EN] Worried another clash would embarrass Shuzhen, Qiao Mengrong cut in smooth. "Sounds more like Qianli Xun is a breathtaking beauty. Step out in public and ladies would toss fruit at his carriage like Pan An, so he stays out of sight!"
[ZH] 仪王说白风在后院等她，她信以为真，赶到后院，根本没看到白风的人影，才知道仪王欺骗她。
[EN] Reading Mengrong's cue, Shen Jianchen backed her right up. "Well said, Mengrong! We've feasted, watched dances, and heard a tale. Now for poetry! Youqi Jushi promised to compose a fresh lyric on the spot for us to appreciate. Set out brush and paper for Youqi Jushi!"
[ZH] 可仪王为何要欺骗她？难道他想跟小姐单独相处？苦荞认为是这样。
[EN] Maids hurried in laying out the Four Treasures of the Study. Having already chosen her piece while strolling outside, Qianli Xun lifted the brush at once and penned:
[ZH] 她被自己的认为惊住，仪王该不会喜欢上小姐？若真是这样，岂不乱套？
[EN] "Clear winter sky without snow—Heaven's heart withholds it, not that Creation lacks craft. It will not let jade blossoms flutter down to earth, holding them back inside the Palace of Vast Cold. Just as clouds gather and sleet starts to form, the red sun climbs three poles high. Six-petaled flakes cut ready—where on earth will they be scattered?
[ZH] 不可能再回高台处，她打算从后门回到屋内，还未走进，听到说话艺人的声音。
[EN] Pity the winter plum upon the ridge, blooming alone without a mate, grieving deep before this view. Waiting for the hand that stirs the golden cauldron to scatter down the jade salt. Gullies all smoothed level, heaven and earth painted fair, crowned by the icy moon's pure light. Feasting guests in Liang Garden fear not the lamps going out in the bright night." ("Niannujiao: Clear Winter Sky, No Snow") [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 她从屏风后面向内瞧，果然是西三胡同那位，还是不要进去，继续待在后院，省得被认出，暴露小姐就是千里寻的事实。
[EN] While Qianli Xun wrote with full focus, Kuqiao slipped back indoors. She had hurried to the rear courtyard only to find no trace of Bai Feng and realize Prince Yi had tricked her. Why trick her away unless he wanted Miss alone? Wait—did Prince Yi have feelings for Miss?! Panic chilled her veins. Too late to return to the platform, she had headed to the rear door of the hall, caught the storyteller's voice, peeked around the screen to spot the man from West Third Alley, and stayed hidden out back until he left lest she expose Miss as Qianli Xun.

#CH 152
[ZH] 南双担心公子喝醉，没有找来真正的酒，端上两瓶桂花酿。乔文仙开始一杯一杯地饮着。
[ZH] 千里寻写完朱淑真的《念奴娇·冬晴无雪》，搁下笔，无意中接触到文渊投来的目光。他一边饮桂花酿，一边看向她，目光中充满埋怨、期待和不舍。
[ZH] 千里寻不知如何回应，躲闪开来：“诸位，淑真在此献丑，今日是入冬以来难得的晴朗之日，便写下‘冬晴无雪’一词，不敢奢谈品鉴，只要能帮大家解解闷儿，我便心满意足。”
[ZH] 申简辰在心里笑道：“多此一举！你又何必替幽栖居士谦虚？人家可是名留后世的女词人。”
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“幽栖居士太过自谦，我等今日来，除了仪王与郡主盛情邀请不胜荣幸外，就是奔着你的诗词而来。幽栖居士的诗词哪能只有解闷儿的功效？还是我等必需的精神食粮。”
[ZH] 两位公子无不认同。
[ZH] 苗知瑜虽失落，也点头认可。
[ZH] 乔文仙顾不上其他，一味盯着淑真，她有意避开他的目光，令他心酸。
[ZH] 乔梦容很是期待淑真到底写些什么。
[ZH] 陈浩宇则一副不屑的样子，一只母老虎舞文弄墨，被大家赞赏，实在可笑。
[ZH] 申简辰命令道：“来人，将幽栖居士的词展示给众人。”
[ZH] 两名丫鬟取了纸张，站在中间，向众人展示。
[ZH] 安明轩主动将词为大家念一遍，他念的过程中，申简辰越听越觉得熟悉，再次回忆起穿越前的经历。
[ZH] 那是一年一度的拳法交流活动，千里寻表演千里拳时，新编一套并非用于格斗的拳法，将拳法与古诗词相融合，她每表演一招，便吟诵一句，再配上古筝演奏，真是美轮美奂。
[ZH] 拳法的阳刚之美，加上诗词和古筝的阴柔与典雅，将众人深深吸引，最终千里寻以独特的表演取胜，获得评委和观众一致认可。
[ZH] 当时申简辰在台下看得呆住，除千里寻的美貌外，主要是她的一招一式仿佛为词句而创。申简辰在心中惊叹她是如何做到？
[ZH] 他仔细品味词句，觉得无非是为表达对冬雪的向往，与拳法毫无关系，可千里寻却能在二者之间找到联系，申简辰佩服不已。
[ZH] 表演结束后，他在台下堵住千里寻，拿出让徒弟刚买好的奶茶：“累了吧？我给你买的奶茶。”
[ZH] “老娘最讨厌喝奶茶。”千里寻没有理会，继续往出走。
[ZH] 申简辰跟上：“你不喝我喝，不过你得给我解释，刚才念的那首词下阕是什么意思，我没听明白。”
[ZH] 千里寻站住，盯着正在吸奶茶的申简辰，觉得十分可笑：“你喝奶茶的动作与你的气质实在不搭。”
[ZH] “别管我的气质，你刚才表演时气质实在绝，赶紧给我解释下阕的意思。”申简辰继续吸着奶茶。
[ZH] 千里寻不满道：“老娘最讨厌别人问古诗词是什么意思，这个问题一听就不懂，诗词重在一个‘品’字，只要一个字一个字，一句一句慢慢品，便能品出其中滋味。”
[ZH] 千里寻说完，又要走，又被申简辰堵住：“你我都表演完，不远处有家咖啡馆儿，去那里坐会儿，你教我如何逐字逐句品诗词。”
[ZH] “现在是下午，老娘从来不在这个时间段喝咖啡，晚上睡觉容易失眠。”千里寻说完扬长而去，留下申简辰一人怅然若失。
[ZH] 此时回忆起来，依然怀念当日的时光，千里寻表演过的拳法申简辰还依稀记得，不如趁此机会表演一番，也好向她表明真实身份。
[ZH] 安明轩念完后，申简辰说道：“幽栖居士的词，使我兴致大增，想为各位表演一番。”
[ZH] 在座诸位不知仪王想干什么，还是装出兴致满满的样子。
[ZH] 曹班说：“我等能欣赏到仪王的表演，真是三生有幸。”
[ZH] 其他人都无不认同。
[ZH] “将琴抬上来！”
[ZH] 听到仪王的命令，已有下人抬上一张古琴，挑合适的位置放好。
[ZH] “梦容，可否为哥哥演奏？”
[ZH] “当然，不知哥哥想听哪首？”
[ZH] “你常弹的那首。”
[ZH] “好，我就为哥哥演奏《佩兰》曲。”
[ZH] 申简辰本不想这么快暴露身份，朱家与陈家的亲事，逼得他不得不暴露。
[ZH] 以前不暴露是担心千里寻拒绝他，果然他的真情并未打动她。
[ZH] 现在想暴露，是因他觉得一旦千里寻知道他是申简辰，肯定高兴，毕竟两人有穿越前的友情，还有共同穿越的经历，也算患难与共，千里寻定会对他另眼相待，说不定因为特殊的关系愿意接受他的感情。那么，他就有正当理由要求陈家退婚
[ZH] 乔梦容已走到琴旁坐好，开始演奏。
[EN] Worried his master might get drunk, Nan Shuang skipped strong liquor and brought two flagons of sweet osmanthus wine instead. Cup after cup, Qiao Wenxian drank on in silence.
[ZH] 琴音一起，申简辰起身来到大家面前，瞧着纸张上的词句，跟着琴曲的节奏，边吟诵，边使出拳法……
[EN] Setting down her brush after finishing Zhu Shuzhen's "Niannujiao: Clear Winter Sky, No Snow," Qianli Xun happened to meet Wenyuan's gaze. Sipping osmanthus wine, his eyes burned with quiet reproach, yearning, and refusal to let go. At a loss how to respond, she looked away fast. "Shuzhen embarrasses herself before you all. Today brings a rare clear winter sky, so I wrote 'Clear Winter Sky, No Snow.' Hardly worthy of high critique; if it helps pass an idle hour, I'm content."
[ZH] 大家都被仪王的表演吸引，心中赞叹他竟能想出这种品鉴诗词的方法，不用说一句话，用拳法表达对词句的理解，实在妙哉！
[EN] Shen Jianchen chuckled to himself. Why act modest on Youqi Jushi's behalf? She's a poet remembered for centuries!
[ZH] 当仪王刚开始表演时，千里寻想起穿越前她曾将拳法与诗词结合的做法，没想到这个时代也有人与她的想法一致。
[EN] "Far too humble, Youqi Jushi!" Dongfang Chenze protested. "Honored as we are by Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe's invitation, your poetry is what drew us here! Far more than passing an hour, your verse feeds our spirits!"
[ZH] 仪王刚打出第一招时，千里寻只觉十分熟悉，不就是她的拳法招式？继续往下看，越来越确定。
[EN] Cao Ban and An Mingxuan echoed warm agreement; downcast as she felt, Miao Zhiyu nodded too. Qiao Wenxian ignored every voice, eyes fixed on Shuzhen, wincing inside every time she avoided his stare. Qiao Mengrong leaned forward eager to read every character, while Chen Haoyu sneered in silence at a tigress swinging a brush and winning applause.
[ZH] 不同的是，仪王打出的招数虽有千里拳的痕迹，用的内力却是她熟悉的申拳。
[EN] "Hold up Youqi Jushi's lyric for everyone to see," Shen Jianchen directed. Two maids lifted the sheet center stage while An Mingxuan volunteered to recite it aloud.
[ZH] “难道仪王就是申简辰？”
[EN] As An Mingxuan read line after line, a jolt of recognition struck Shen Jianchen, carrying him straight back before the earthquake. At an annual martial-arts showcase, Qianli Xun had unveiled a brand-new performance form blending Qianli Fist with classical poetry: reciting one line for every move to the strains of a live guzheng. Martial vigor fused with lyrical poise held the whole arena spellbound, sweeping both judges and audience off their feet. Seated below, Shen Jianchen had stared transfixed—not just by her beauty, by how every turn of her wrist seemed born straight from the words. Pondering the lyric back then, it looked like a simple wish for winter snow with zero link to boxing, yet she had bridged the two with pure magic.
[ZH] 千里寻在心中自问，不由激动万分，真想上前揪住他问个究竟。可身边有其他人在，尤其是梦容，冒失追问，既不合礼数，也会惹得梦容伤心。
[EN] Right after her set, he had cornered her backstage holding milk tea his apprentice had just bought. "Tired? Got you milk tea."
[ZH] 千里寻忍住，尽量让自己平静下来，避免一时冲动做下错事。
[EN] "I hate milk tea," Qianli Xun snapped, brushing past.
[ZH] 她继续分析，仪王懂得申拳的功力并不能以此判定他就是申简辰，毕竟她曾在街上打败的延迟男也懂申拳，看来申拳在这个时代依然有踪迹可循。
[EN] Falling into step beside her, he said, "Fine, I'll drink it. Just explain what the second half of that poem meant; it flew right over my head."
[ZH] 关键是仪王竟然能打出她的招数，那可是她费尽心思根据朱淑真的《念奴娇·冬晴无雪》创造的拳法，不为格斗，只为表演的同时展示千里拳的内功。以此断定，仪王八成就是申简辰。
[EN] Stopping to eye him slurping through the straw, she smirked. "Drinking milk tea clashes hard with your whole image."
[ZH] 想到此处，千里寻有些坐不住，想尽快去神柳下与仪王相约。
[EN] "Forget my image. Your aura onstage blew everyone away! Explain the second stanza!"
[ZH] 申简辰表演拳法的同时，会偶尔向千里寻瞟来，发现她露出惊喜而又疑惑的表情，便知目的已达成。
[EN] "Nothing annoys me more than people asking what a poem 'means'!" Qianli Xun scoffed. "Proves you don't get poetry at all! You don't dissect verse; you savor it word by word, line by line, till the taste sinks in!"
[ZH] “这下你应该比我更渴望到神柳下相会。”申简辰想着，口中继续吟出：“应念陇首寒梅，花开无伴，对景真愁绝。”每念一句，都以相应的拳法配合……
[EN] She started walking off again, and he stepped ahead blocking her. "We're both done onstage, and there's a coffee shop right down the block. Sit with me and teach me how to savor poetry word by word."
[ZH] 千里寻忽然之间想明白很多事，仪王起死回生，是因为申简辰穿越而来所致，他失忆也与此有关，还有他看自己时充满爱意的眼神，以及表达爱意的话语，都是因为他只有申简辰的记忆。
[EN] "It's afternoon. I never drink coffee in the afternoon unless I want insomnia all night!" Striding off without looking back, she had left him standing hollow-hearted.
[ZH] 仪王表演完最后一个动作，梦容也停下琴音，二人配合默契。乔梦容觉得她与哥哥天衣无缝的配合，足以说明他们真的是天生的一对儿。
[EN] Reliving that afternoon warmed his chest, and he still remembered most of her performance form. What better way to reveal who he really was right here and now?
[ZH] 乔梦容坐回位置，发现淑真一脸怔住的表情，悄悄问：“你被哥哥的表演吸引住了？”
[EN] The moment An Mingxuan finished reading, Shen Jianchen spoke up. "Youqi Jushi's lyric fires my spirit! Let me perform something for everyone."
[ZH] 千里寻哄骗道：“我是被你的琴音折服。”
[EN] Puzzled over what Prince Yi had in mind, everyone put on eager smiles. "Witnessing a performance by Prince Yi is a lifetime honor!" Cao Ban cheered, echoed around the hall.
[ZH] 梦容笑了笑，继续坐好。
[EN] "Bring in the guqin!" At his command, servants carried in a guqin and placed it at a prime spot. Turning to his sister, Shen Jianchen asked, "Play for Brother, Mengrong?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽见仪王已落座，赞道：“仪王的表演令我等钦佩，没想到幽栖居士的词也能与拳法相合，实在妙！尤其‘不放玉花飞堕地，留在广寒宫阙。’仪王的拳法似乎让人感受到‘玉花飞堕’的场景，弥补了‘留在广寒宫阙’带给人的失落和惆怅。”
[EN] "Of course! Which piece, Brother?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽赞美仪王时，千里寻朝仪王看了一眼，瞧不出任何申简辰的痕迹，就像自己，换副皮囊，是千里寻，又不是千里寻。
[EN] "Your favorite."
[ZH] 申简辰觉出千里寻的目光，他心中暗喜，早知如此，应早些告诉她，也许早已美人在怀。
[EN] "Then 'Orchid Pendant' it is!"
[ZH] 安明轩接着说：“仪王的拳法，既是对幽栖居士词作的解析，同时也弥补冬晴无雪带给人的遗憾，真是此时无声胜有声。鄙人接着仪王的拳法，用我这张嘴品鉴一番。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen had not meant to expose his identity so soon, yet the Zhu-Chen betrothal forced his hand. Before, he held back fearing rejection, and sure enough, his feelings as Zhao Pu had not won her over. Now he gambled that learning he was Shen Jianchen would thrill her: old ties from their own era, crossing time together through life and death—surely she would look at him with fresh eyes and give him a chance! Once she accepted him, he would have every right to make the Chens cancel the match.
[ZH] 安明轩又朝纸张瞧去，思索一会儿：“幽栖居士的词倒应景，冬日下过一场雪，说句心里话，我还没玩够，连西湖的断桥残雪还未来得及欣赏，真希望再来一场雪，可日日不是阴天，就是冬日高挂，尤其今日，是入冬以来少的晴朗之日。幽栖居士的词算是满足我对冬日雪景的美好想象。鄙人认为最妙处在于用‘玉花’和‘玉盐’描绘想象中的冬雪，既写出冬雪像玉一样晶莹剔透，还写出像花一样美的形态，也写出像盐一样色泽洁白，搞得我越来越希望再下一场雪。”
[EN] Taking her seat at the guqin, Qiao Mengrong plucked the opening notes. Rising into the center facing the hanging sheet, Shen Jianchen matched his breath to the melody, reciting verse while flowing into boxing forms. Every guest sat riveted, marveling at such an inventive way to interpret poetry: saying not a word of critique, letting crisp martial motion embody the soul of every phrase.
[ZH] 苗知瑜虽未怎么扭头看身旁的乔公子，可心无时无刻不在他的身上。乔公子不像往日参加别的诗会时滔滔不绝，他自顾沉默，苗知瑜明白，他心里还念着幽栖居士。
[EN] From the very first note, Qianli Xun recalled combining boxing with poetry back in her old life, amused that someone in the Song dynasty shared the same idea. Then Prince Yi threw his opening stance, and her heart skipped a beat. That was her choreography! Move after move confirmed it beyond a shadow of a doubt: every form traced Qianli Fist's exact showcase routine, powered underneath by the unmistakable force of Shen Fist.
[ZH] 失落之余，不时瞧向幽栖居士，她倒安然自若，自来到诗会，并未与乔公子眉来眼去。苗知瑜心安许多，见安明轩讲完，立刻说：
[EN] Was Prince Yi none other than Shen Jianchen?!
[ZH] “我最喜‘应念陇首寒梅，花开无伴，对景真愁绝。’寒冬之际，寒梅与冬雪乃绝配，可冬日，寒梅独开无雪，寂寞无伴，也令赏景之人无比惆怅……”
[EN] Shock and joy surged so hard she almost bolted forward to grab his sleeve and demand the truth, catching herself only at sight of the crowded room, above all Mengrong. Making a scene here would violate etiquette and break Mengrong's heart. Fighting her pulse steady, she forced herself to think cool. Knowing Shen Fist alone proved nothing, since that "Delayed" guy she had beaten on the street used Shen Fist too, meaning Shen Fist had roots in this era. What sealed it was that choreography: a non-combat showcase she had invented from scratch to pair with Zhu Shuzhen's "Clear Winter Sky, No Snow"! Eight or nine parts out of ten, Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen! Restless now, she could hardly wait to meet him beneath the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 苗知瑜体会了一会儿自己内心的感情：“孤独的寒梅，如同此时此刻的我，花开无伴。……”
[EN] Stealing glances her way between turns, Shen Jianchen caught the flash of stunned joy in her eyes and smiled inside. Bet you're racing to the Divine Willow faster than me now! Chanting aloud—"Pity the winter plum upon the ridge, blooming alone without a mate, grieving deep before this view"—he matched every phrase with crisp, flowing strikes.
[ZH] 她转向乔文仙：“乔公子，你说呢？”
[EN] Every mystery snapped into place for Qianli Xun: Prince Yi's revival, his amnesia, the tender heat in his eyes, even his bold confessions. All because Shen Jianchen sat inside Zhao Pu's body carrying only Shen Jianchen's memories!
[ZH] 乔文仙根本没有心思鉴赏诗词，他除研究淑真的表情，就是喝闷酒，还好是桂花酿，对他来讲，根本喝不醉。
[EN] On the final note, Prince Yi closed his stance in exact sync with Mengrong's last chord. Returning to her seat glowing with pride—such harmony proved Brother and she were a match made in Heaven—Qiao Mengrong noticed Shuzhen staring dazed and whispered, "Spellbound by Brother's performance?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜的问话，乔文仙没听进耳朵里，并未做出任何反应。
[EN] Recovering fast, Qianli Xun teased back, "Spellbound by your guqin playing!" Mengrong smiled content and settled back.
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧见，心里冷笑道：“哼！落花有意，流水无情，以后嫁给心上人，也没好日子过。”
[EN] Once Prince Yi sat down, Dongfang Chenze broke into praise. "Inspired beyond words, Your Highness! Who knew Youqi Jushi's lyric paired so well with boxing, above all 'It will not let jade blossoms flutter down to earth, holding them back inside the Palace of Vast Cold'! Your sweeping strokes made 'jade blossoms' seem to snow right before our eyes, soothing the ache of snow locked up in heaven!"
[ZH] 鉴赏诗词环节，陈浩宇甚觉无趣，想要离开，见仪王兴致勃勃，还亲自上场表演一番，他不敢提出离开的请求，耐心坐好，忍受大家说些无聊的话。
[EN] While Dongfang spoke, Qianli Xun stole a long look at Prince Yi. Not a physical trace of Shen Jianchen's old face remained, just as she wore another skin herself: Qianli Xun, yet not Qianli Xun. Catching her eyes on him, Shen Jianchen beamed inside: Should have told her ages ago!
[ZH] 东方尘泽见气氛尴尬，赶紧说：“这首词还有一处甚妙，上阕让人产生失落的情绪，下阕则给人希望，告诉我们终有一日和羹金鼎手，把玉盐飘撒。因此，苗姑娘不必伤感，你这枝寒梅，终会等到被‘玉盐’装饰之日。”
[EN] An Mingxuan picked up next. "Prince Yi's boxing both interprets the poem and fills the gap left by a snowless winter—silence speaking louder than words! Allow me to follow with plain speech." Studying the sheet a beat, he went on, "Spot-on for the season! One winter snowfall wasn't near enough; I haven't even seen Lingering Snow on Broken Bridge at West Lake yet, and day after day brings gray clouds or bright winter sun, above all today's clear sky. Youqi Jushi's lyric grants my dream of winter snow! Purest touch lies in calling snow 'jade blossoms' and 'jade salt'—clear as jade, delicate as flowers, pure white as salt—making me pray even harder for snow!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜转过头，回礼道：“感谢东方公子安慰，希望正如你所言。”
[EN] Without turning much toward Young Master Qiao beside her, Miao Zhiyu's whole heart tracked his every breath. Usually lively and eloquent at salons, today he sat locked in mute gloom, still hung up on Youqi Jushi. Yet glancing across at Youqi Jushi, seeing her poised and distant without trading one flirtatious look with Young Master Qiao eased Miao Zhiyu's nerves. As An Mingxuan finished, she spoke up. "My favorite line is 'Pity the winter plum upon the ridge, blooming alone without a mate, grieving deep before this view.' Winter plum and snow belong together; blooming alone without snow leaves both flower and viewer aching..." Lingering on her own quiet sting, she added, "A lonely winter plum, just like me right now, blooming without a mate... wouldn't you agree, Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 大家鉴赏幽栖居士的词，千里寻不便多说，只端坐着细细听大家的品鉴。
[EN] Having zero mind for poetry—either watching Shuzhen's face or pouring himself cups of harmless sweet osmanthus wine—Qiao Wenxian did not even register her question and made no reply.
[ZH] 苗知瑜的话使得她更加谨慎，低眉垂目，不敢有一丝松懈，怕稍加松懈，会不由自主看向文渊。
[EN] Catching the snub, Chen Haoyu scoffed inside: Hmph, falling petals pine for flowing water that couldn't care less; marrying the man you chase won't bring happy days! Bored stiff by poetry talk, itching to bolt yet scared to offend an enthusiastic Prince Yi, Chen Haoyu stayed glued to his chair enduring the chatter.
[ZH] 曹班忍了半晌，终于发话：“诸位，我说两句……”大家知道，只要曹班一开口必然谈及典故。
[EN] Stepping in to rescue the awkward hush, Dongfang Chenze smiled. "Yet the genius of this lyric turns in the second half! Where the opening brings letdown, the close brings hope: someday the hand stirring the golden cauldron will scatter down jade salt! Take heart, Lady Miao; your winter plum will be sure to greet its dusting of jade salt!"
[ZH] “幽栖居士这首词，我看出一处用典，‘梁园燕客，夜明不怕灯灭。’梁园始建于西汉，建成一时轰动天下，成为人们向往的胜地，冬日时梁园被白雪覆盖，万树着银，翠玉相映，景色更加迷人，故有‘梁园雪霁’之称。幽栖居士引用此典故，无非是想描绘出想象中雪景之美，给人以美好的希望。”
[EN] Turning with a gracious nod, Miao Zhiyu thanked him. "Kind words, Young Master Dongfang. May it come true as you say."
[ZH] 申简辰时不时观察千里寻，又不敢一直观察，担心被大家看出他的心思。他发觉千里寻从刚才猜出他是申简辰时的激动已变得平静，应该是因众人在此，不方便表露情绪。
[EN] Holding her tongue while everyone critiqued her work, Qianli Xun kept her eyes lowered with care after Miao Zhiyu's pointed words, refusing to let her gaze stray once toward Wenyuan.
[ZH] 他能想象到，若众人不在，她一定会焦急地追问：“快告诉我，你是不是申简辰？”
[EN] Having waited his turn, Cao Ban cleared his throat, signaling another classical citation. "I spotted one rich allusion here: 'Feasting guests in Liang Garden fear not the lamps going out in the bright night.' Built in the Western Han, Liang Garden dazzled the empire; blanketed in winter snow, silver boughs gleaming beside jade pines, it earned the famed name 'Snow Clearing at Liang Garden.' Invoking it paints imagined snow in bright splendor, leaving readers full of hope."
[ZH] 想到此处，申简辰心中愉悦，结果发现梦容正看向他，掩饰道：“梦容，你也鉴赏鉴赏。”
[EN] Stealing quick glances at Qianli Xun without giving himself away, Shen Jianchen saw her initial jolt settle back into poised calm—only holding back because of the crowd. Alone, he knew she would already be firing questions: Tell me straight, are you Shen Jianchen? Amused by the thought, a faint smile curved his lips right as he caught Mengrong watching him. Covering smooth, he prompted, "Share your thoughts too, Mengrong."
[ZH] 乔梦容正在研究哥哥的表情，他嘴角露出笑容，显然心情不错，哥哥开心她也开心，既然哥哥让她鉴赏，她不好继续沉默。
[EN] Watching Brother's happy grin warmed Mengrong's heart; invited to speak, she did not stay silent.

#CH 153
[ZH] 乔梦容面向大家：“听过诸位品鉴，使我对这首‘念奴娇’有了深入理解。以前梦容也读过淑真的词，大都忧伤哀怨，这首却不一样，有股男子豪迈之气，主要体现在下阕‘沟壑皆平，乾坤如画，更吐冰轮洁。’……”
[ZH] 乔梦容念完词句，继续品道：“如若不是亲眼目睹是淑真所写，我会以为出自东坡居士之手，只因这三句有东坡居士豪迈之风，还充满丰富的想象：大雪填平沟壑，一片银白世界，天黑时，雪停月出，一轮圆月，与白雪世界上下辉映，更显得无比皎洁。画面空旷苍茫，全无女儿脂粉之气，说明淑真的心里还装着另一个境界。”
[ZH] 全场只剩下陈浩宇与乔文仙还未鉴赏。大家了解陈浩宇，可以不必理会他，于是众人将目光投向乔文仙。
[ZH] 乔文仙像身处另一个世界，自顾沉默，一杯一杯饮着桂花酿。
[ZH] 大家品鉴的话语多少进入他耳中，他不断猜测，淑真为何要写“冬晴无雪”？难道她想要提醒他不要忘记雪地里说下拒绝的话？
[ZH] 淑真竟然不喜欢冬季阳光明媚的日子，反而想象出颇具境界的雪夜之景，看来他们分手并未对她产生任何影响，难道她从未深爱过他？
[ZH] 想到这些，乔文仙更伤感，一味沉浸在个人感受中，并未注意大家向他投来目光。
[ZH] 申简辰提醒道：“文渊，你可有话要说？”
[ZH] 听到仪王的声音，乔文仙才放下酒杯，双眼迷离，随口回道：“今日我只有酒兴，而无品鉴诗词的兴致，恕我无话可说。”
[ZH] 千里寻更不敢瞧向文渊，既然已分手，又何必再给对方希望。
[ZH] 申简辰早都想去神柳下与千里寻私会，此时不结束，更待何时？
[ZH] “今日诗会到此为止，接下来诸位自由安排。”
[ZH] 三位公子起身，一起向仪王告辞：“仪王，我们三人还有事，先行离开。”
[ZH] 申简辰点点头：“三位公子以后要常来幽林别居，若身边有姊妹，也把她们带来，梦容甚是寂寞，常有女孩子来，可以陪她说说话。”
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“只要有空，我们三人必然常来，既然仪王允许，以后来时定然带上妹妹们。”
[ZH] 三位公子离开后，陈浩宇也起身辞别：“仪王，我也该回去。”
[ZH] 申简辰想到朱陈两家的亲事，面露不满。
[ZH] 陈浩宇行完礼后，转身往出走，边走边纳闷，仪王的表情显然对他不满，难道还为乔文仙不能与相爱之人在一起埋怨他？既如此，又何必请他来诗会？不管了，以后还是少跟仪王打交道，说不定还会被揍。
[ZH] 走到大门外，青瓜悄悄问：“公子，我怎么觉得仪王对您很不满？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脚踢在青瓜身上：“再瞎说！以后不带你出来！”
[ZH] 青瓜不敢再言语。
[ZH] 屋内，乔梦容拉住淑真：“能不能别走？陪我多聊会儿？”
[ZH] 千里寻也想留下，可因想要早些知道仪王是否是申简辰，故而拒绝道：“我本也想留下，怎奈临行前，我娘和嫂嫂派人传话，说晚些要去望月轩，我已答应，只好改日专程来幽林别居陪你。”
[ZH] 申简辰心中偷乐：“也让你体会体会不得已欺骗梦容的难处。”
[ZH] “既如此，我放你回去，后日必须来，陪我住两日。”乔梦容失望之余提出要求。
[ZH] 千里寻满口应下，起身带着苦荞、夏果与仪王拜别，以前不觉得他是申简辰时，拜起来心安理得，可今日总想起申简辰嘚瑟的样子，要拜他，实在不习惯，还是草草拜了一下：“仪王，淑真也该回去。”
[ZH] 申简辰提醒道：“幽栖居士答应家人要按时回去，果然守约，希望也不要辜负他人的邀约。”
[ZH] 千里寻当然明白：“仪王放心，他人之约淑真定不辜负。”
[ZH] 乔梦容以为哥哥担心淑真会违背与她的约定，才强调那些话，更觉哥哥是天下最好的男子。
[ZH] 千里寻带着丫鬟要离开，乔文仙已顾不上别的，跟了出去，南双也只好跟上自家公子。
[ZH] 乔公子既离开，苗知瑜没必要再待下去，她向两位贵人行完礼，带上丫鬟匆匆离去。
[ZH] 千里寻刚走到木质高台边，听到文渊的声音：“淑真！”
[EN] Facing the gathering, Qiao Mengrong began, "Listening to you all deepened my grasp of this 'Niannujiao.' Most of Shuzhen's lyrics I read before carried tender sorrow, yet this one breaks free with bold, sweeping vigor, above all 'Gullies all smoothed level, heaven and earth painted fair, crowned by the icy moon's pure light.' Had I not watched Shuzhen write it myself, I would have sworn it came from Dongpo Jushi's brush! Grand scope and soaring vision: deep snow leveling every ravine into a vast silver expanse, then night falling as snow stops and a full moon rises to mirror the white earth in dazzling purity. Vast and boundless, untouched by dainty parlor airs—proof Shuzhen holds a far wider world inside her heart."
[ZH] 千里寻停下，犹豫片刻才转过身：“文渊，唤我何事？”
[EN] Only Chen Haoyu and Qiao Wenxian remained. Everyone knew better than to expect poetry from Chen Haoyu, so every eye turned toward Qiao Wenxian.
[ZH] “我……我还是放不下你。”
[EN] Locked inside his own private chill, drinking osmanthus wine cup after cup, Qiao Wenxian heard snatches of their praise and tormented himself with guesses. Why write "Clear Winter Sky, No Snow"? Was she reminding him never to forget her rejection out in the snow? Disliking bright winter sunshine to dream up sweeping snowy moonlight—had their breakup left not a scratch on her? Had she never truly loved him?
[ZH] 千里寻的心弦被这句话重重拨动，她立即用理智压制住感情，装出冷冷的样子：“放不下我又能如何？我可是很快将你放下，你有婚约，以后不要随便与我说话，省得再有人胡乱议论我。”
[EN] "Any words to add, Wenyuan?" Shen Jianchen nudged.
[ZH] 夏果见乔公子刚刚充满希望的眼神，变得更加失望，她心疼不已。
[EN] Setting down his cup with misty eyes, Qiao Wenxian answered flat, "I only have taste for wine today, none for poetry. Forgive me, nothing to add."
[ZH] 千里寻说完转身继续向幽林别居门外走去，将文渊甩在身后。
[EN] Qianli Xun refused to look his way; once broken off, offering false hope only hurt worse. Itching to meet Qianli Xun beneath the Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen wrapped things up right there. "That closes today's poetry salon! Feel free to spend the rest of the day as you please."
[ZH] 令她欣慰的是，她仅仅痛苦那么一下，便恢复正常。还要赴仪王之约，还要亲自验证仪王是否是申简辰，她哪有心情关注一段已不属于自己的爱情。
[EN] The three young gentlemen rose together to bow. "We have errands waiting, Your Highness. Allow us to take our leave."
[ZH] 乔文仙被淑真的冷漠再次伤到，他站在原地，脚步无法挪动。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded warm. "Drop by Youlin Retreat often, you three, and bring your sisters along next time! Mengrong gets lonely out here; having other girls around to chat with would brighten her days."
[ZH] 苗知瑜走来，见此情景，小心问道：“乔公子，幽栖居士可否已离开？”
[EN] "Whenever time allows, we'll visit and bring our sisters!" Dongfang Chenze promised.
[ZH] 乔文仙并未搭话，他无法将心思放在苗知瑜身上。
[EN] Once the three left, Chen Haoyu stood to bow too. "Your Highness, I ought to head back as well."
[ZH] 南双帮忙回道：“幽栖居士刚走出没几步。”
[EN] Thinking of the Zhu-Chen betrothal darkened Shen Jianchen's brow. Bowing out and walking toward the gate, Chen Haoyu muttered to himself: Prince Yi looked mad at me—still sore on Qiao Wenxian's behalf? Then why invite me? Better steer clear of Prince Yi before catching another beating!
[ZH] 苗知瑜顾不上安慰乔文仙，再说她的安慰没有任何用处，她快走几步，打算追上幽栖居士。
[EN] Past the outer gate, Qinggua whispered, "Young Master, didn't Prince Yi look displeased with you?"
[ZH] 正当千里寻要登上马车时，听到苗知瑜唤她：“幽栖居士，可否借一步说话？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu kicked his shin. "Run your mouth again and stay home next time!" Qinggua clamped his lips shut.
[ZH] 千里寻停下，转身问：“苗姑娘找我何事？”
[EN] Back inside, Qiao Mengrong caught Shuzhen's hand. "Don't rush off. Stay and chat a bit longer?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜上前拉住幽栖居士的手：“我们到那边去说。”
[EN] Torn—yet burning to confirm whether Prince Yi really was Shen Jianchen—Qianli Xun excused herself smooth. "Love to, except before I left, Mother and Sister-in-law sent word they're coming by Wangyue Pavilion later and I promised to be there. I'll come back another day just to keep you company!"
[ZH] 显然苗知瑜不想他人听到二人谈话，丫鬟们都明白，乖乖待在原地等待。
[EN] Shen Jianchen smirked inside: Now you get a taste of how tough lying to Mengrong is!
[ZH] 二人来到一边，苗知瑜才放开手：“幽栖居士可否原谅我？”
[EN] "Fine, I'll let you go today, on condition you come back the day after tomorrow and stay two full days!" Qiao Mengrong bargained.
[ZH] “原谅你什么？”千里寻明明已猜到，还是故意发出疑问。
[EN] Agreeing at once, Qianli Xun rose with Kuqiao and Xiaguo to bid Prince Yi farewell. Back when she didn't suspect he was Shen Jianchen, bowing felt natural; now picturing Shen Jianchen's cocky grin made curtsying to him feel ridiculous, so she gave a quick, minimal bob. "Shuzhen takes her leave, Your Highness."
[ZH] “原谅苗家与乔家定亲。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen dropped a crisp hint. "Keeping your word to family is admirable, Youqi Jushi. See you don't break other promises either."
[ZH] “跟你有什么关系？是两家之事，再说跟我也没任何关系。”
[EN] "Rest assured, Your Highness, I keep every promise," Qianli Xun shot back. Thinking Brother meant Shuzhen's vow to return in two days, Qiao Mengrong glowed—best brother under Heaven!
[ZH] “怎么没关系？两家订亲，幽栖居士与乔公子没法在一起。”
[EN] As Qianli Xun stepped out with her maids, Qiao Wenxian threw caution aside and trailed straight behind her, Nan Shuang hurrying after. With Young Master Qiao gone, Miao Zhiyu saw no reason to linger; she bowed farewell to Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe and hastened out with her maid.
[ZH] 苗知瑜明明喜欢文渊，好事已成，却装作关心的样子，千里寻十分不爽：“苗姑娘放心，我与文渊是有过一段情，可我们没有婚姻之缘，既然他要娶的人是苗姑娘，我自然放手放得干脆，绝不拖泥带水。”
[EN] Right beside the wooden tree platform, Wenyuan's voice caught Qianli Xun. "Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 千里寻说完打算离开，又被苗知瑜拉住：“幽栖居士放下，可乔公子还是老样子……”
[EN] Halting, she paused a beat before turning. "Yes, Wenyuan, what is it?"
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“文渊是什么样，已与我没有任何关系，苗姑娘与其在我身上浪费时间，不如想想如何让文渊眼中和心里只有苗姑娘。”
[EN] "I... I still can't let you go."
[ZH] 苗知瑜不好再拉下去，她放开手，目睹幽栖居士登上马车离开，叹道：“乔公子眼里心里从来没有我，我如何做才能让他忘记你？”
[EN] His raw plea tugged hard at her heartstrings. Clamping reason down fast, she put on an icy mask. "What good does that do? I moved on fast! You're betrothed now; stop pulling me aside to talk before gossip starts flying over me again!"
[ZH] 申简辰与乔梦容说了会儿话，从屋内走出，见乔文仙站在原地，一动不动，猜到他再次受伤。
[EN] Seeing the flicker of hope die straight out of Young Master Qiao's eyes broke Xiaguo's heart. Turning brisk, Qianli Xun strode on toward the gate leaving Wenyuan behind, relieved that the pang passed in one heartbeat. With a duel awaiting beneath the Divine Willow to test if Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen, she had zero room left for a closed chapter.
[ZH] “梦容，你先回屋歇着，我送送文渊，回来时会晚些，你早些睡，不用等我。”
[EN] Stung raw by her chill, Qiao Wenxian stood rooted to the earth. Walking up behind him, Miao Zhiyu asked hesitant, "Has Youqi Jushi already left, Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 申简辰正愁没有理由离开幽林别居到神柳下与千里寻幽会，文渊伤心的样子，真是神来之笔，为他提供了再恰当不过的理由。
[EN] Locked in pain, Qiao Wenxian gave no answer; Nan Shuang filled in polite, "Just walked a few steps ahead, Lady Miao." Seeing no comfort of hers would reach him anyway, Miao Zhiyu picked up her pace to catch Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 乔梦容对哥哥深信不疑：“哥哥不用担心我，路上要好好劝劝乔公子。”
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun lifted her foot toward her carriage step, Miao Zhiyu hailed her. "Youqi Jushi, may I speak with you a moment?"
[ZH] 申简辰微笑点头，待梦容离开后，搂住文渊：“别这么站着，我送你回去。”
[EN] Turning back, Qianli Xun asked, "What is it, Lady Miao?"
[ZH] 文渊倒听仪王的话，正痛苦时找到一个结实的臂膀，不由靠上去。被仪王搂着来到大门外，苗知瑜刚刚乘坐马车离开，他们还能听到车轮碾压马路的声音。
[EN] Taking her hand, Miao Zhiyu drew her aside out of earshot while the maids stood obedient back by the carriage. Letting go, Miao Zhiyu asked low, "Can you ever forgive me, Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 申简辰虽跟着文渊上了乔家的马车，却也吩咐白风赶着幽林别居的马车跟在后面。
[EN] "Forgive you for what?" Qianli Xun asked, knowing the answer full well.
[ZH] 一路上，乔文仙不说话，申简辰并不去打扰，他深知别人说什么都不管用，只有文渊自己想清楚，才能彻底走出情伤。
[EN] "For the Miao and Qiao families sealing a betrothal."
[ZH] 快到城门口，申简辰不愿进城，该找什么理由离开？他思忖一会儿说道：“文渊，我还是担心梦容，她一个人在幽林别居太孤单，不如我就送你到这里？”
[EN] "What's that got to do with you? Parents arranged it, and it's got nothing to do with me either."
[ZH] 乔文仙苦笑道：“真羡慕仪王有自己爱的人可以关心，我与淑真之间的感情已无法挽回，总不能因为我影响你们，快回去吧。”
[EN] "How could it not? Because of our betrothal, you and Young Master Qiao can't be together."
[ZH] “就知道你最好。”
[EN] Irritated seeing the winner act apologetic, Qianli Xun kept crisp. "Relax, Lady Miao. Wenyuan and I shared feelings once, yet fate didn't grant us marriage. Since you're his bride-to-be, I let go clean with zero loose threads."
[ZH] 申简辰立刻跳下马车，目睹文渊的马车进了城，确定安全后，才登上自己的马车：“去山脚下。”
[EN] She turned to leave, only for Miao Zhiyu to tug her sleeve again. "You let go, yet Young Master Qiao stays stuck..."
[ZH] 白风心中疑惑，临走时明明说要送乔公子，还未送到乔府，王爷就半途而废。半途而废也罢了，可以理解为他担心郡主，可王爷不着急回别居，却要求去山脚下，明显又要去看神柳。
[EN] Qianli Xun gave a cool smile. "How Wenyuan feels is no longer my concern. Instead of wasting breath on me, Lady Miao, figure out how to fill Wenyuan's eyes and heart with yourself alone."
[ZH] 往常看神柳，都是从幽林别居去，今日怎么从山脚下？白风越想越觉得奇怪，又不便多问一个字，只好默默听从王爷吩咐。
[EN] Unable to hold her further, Miao Zhiyu let go and watched her carriage pull away, sighing soft. "Young Master Qiao never once held me in his eyes or heart. How on earth do I make him forget you?"
[ZH] 到达山脚下，发现望月轩的马车停靠在一边，白风终于明白，原来王爷与幽栖居士私会，他更加确定王爷背着郡主喜欢上幽栖居士。
[EN] After chatting a bit with Mengrong, Shen Jianchen stepped outside and spotted Qiao Wenxian standing frozen like stone, struck down once more. Turning to Mengrong, he seized the golden excuse. "Head inside and rest, Mengrong. I'll escort Wenyuan home. I might get back late, so turn in early without waiting."
[ZH] 苦荞本也疑惑，不明白小姐为何要来神柳处，直到看见仪王的身影，才恍然大悟，往日心中的猜疑在今日得到确证。
[EN] Trusting him with all her heart, Qiao Mengrong nodded. "Don't worry about me, Brother. Talk some comfort into Young Master Qiao along the way."
[ZH] 夏果本就猜到仪王对小姐的心思，这两人在外人面前装得挺好，背开外人，立马约会，也不知他们是什么时候约好来此相会？
[EN] Once Mengrong withdrew, Shen Jianchen slung an arm around Wenyuan's shoulders. "Quit standing here freezing; I'll ride back with you." Leaning into that sturdy support, Wenyuan walked with him out to the gate just as Miao Zhiyu's carriage wheels faded down the road. Climbing into the Qiao carriage beside Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen signaled Bai Feng to trail behind in Youlin Retreat's own rig.
[ZH] 千里寻乖乖站在神柳下等他，这是从未有过的事，申简辰感到异常喜悦。
[EN] Wenyuan stayed mute the whole ride, and Shen Jianchen respected his hush. Words could not heal a broken heart; Wenyuan had to walk out on his own. Approaching Lin'an's city gate, needing an exit without entering town, Shen Jianchen spoke up. "Truth is, Wenyuan, I hate leaving Mengrong alone out at the retreat. Mind if I see you off right here?"
[ZH] 他走近后，吩咐道：“你们走远些，我与幽栖居士有重要的事情说。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian gave a bitter smile. "I envy you having someone you love to fuss over. My mess with Shuzhen is past saving; don't let me cut into your time with her. Head back."
[ZH] 白风拉起苦荞向远处走去，夏果也跟着往一边走，谁让人家是王爷，身份尊贵，他们不得不听。
[EN] "Knew you'd understand!" Hopping down and watching Wenyuan's carriage roll safe through the city gate, Shen Jianchen boarded his own rig at once. "To the foot of the hill!"
[ZH] 千里寻一句话未说，伸出拳头打向仪王，她需与他过招，才能最终确定仪王是不是申简辰。
[EN] Confusion wrinkled Bai Feng's brow. Leaving to escort Young Master Qiao, stopping short at the city gate, then detouring straight to the foot of the hill to see the Divine Willow from the far side instead of walking from the retreat? Strange beyond words, yet keeping his mouth shut, he drove where ordered.
[ZH] 面对袭击而来的拳头，申简辰毫不示弱，出拳迎接，与千里寻打起来。二人的目标不是袭击对方，而是用拳头交流……
[EN] Arriving at the base of the hill and spotting Wangyue Pavilion's carriage parked beside the clearing, everything clicked for Bai Feng: secret tryst with Youqi Jushi! Prince Yi really was sweet on her behind Princess Jiahe's back! Kuqiao had wondered why Miss detoured to the willow too, till Prince Yi stepped out and confirmed every hunch. And Xiaguo smirked inside: acting proper before crowds, sneaking off for secret dates the second backs turned!
[ZH] 最开心的竟然是白风，他从未见王爷与人对打过，今日算是开眼：“没想到幽栖居士这么厉害！难道他们二人约到此处是为比试拳法？”
[EN] Seeing Qianli Xun standing right beneath the Divine Willow waiting for him filled Shen Jianchen with pure elation. Walking close, he waved the servants back. "Step far off, all of you. I have important business with Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] “我家小姐当然厉害！”苦荞算是轻松下来，这么看来，小姐与王爷并不是约会。
[EN] Bai Feng tugged Kuqiao down the trail while Xiaguo trailed aside; nobody disobeyed an imperial prince.
[ZH] 夏果也开始怀疑她的猜测。
[EN] Without uttering one syllable, Qianli Xun fired a punch straight at Prince Yi. Fists would prove the truth once and for all! Answering crisp with a textbook block and counter, Shen Jianchen met her strike for strike, trading blows not to hurt, but speaking straight through boxing.
[ZH] 越打千里寻越兴奋，对方不是申简辰又会是哪个？他出拳的方式她再熟悉不过。
[EN] Watching from afar, Bai Feng beamed widest of all—first time seeing his master trade live moves! "Whoa, Youqi Jushi fights like a beast! Did they meet up just to spar?!"
[ZH] 大约打过十来回合，千里寻已忍不住，停下拳头，抱住仪王：“你就是申简辰！你真的和我一起穿越而来！”
[EN] "Course my Miss is tough!" Relief washed over Kuqiao; just martial sparring, not a secret romance! Even Xiaguo blinked doubting her own guess.
[ZH] 第一次被千里寻拥抱，申简辰兴奋不已，更是紧紧地抱住她：“我来临安是为找你，没想到被我找到，我早认出你，好几次想告诉你我就是申简辰，可都事与愿违。”
[EN] Every clash lit brighter fire in Qianli Xun's veins. Who else on earth moved with that exact Shen Fist rhythm?! Ten exchanges in, unable to hold back another second, she dropped her guard and threw both arms around Prince Yi. "You ARE Shen Jianchen! You really crossed over with me!"
[ZH] 见两人抱在一起，苦荞担心道：“白风，你说你家王爷与我家小姐到底是什么关系？”
[EN] Held in Qianli Xun's arms for the very first time in either life, Shen Jianchen locked his arms tight around her. "I came all the way to Lin'an hunting for you, and I found you! I recognized you ages ago and tried telling you over and over, only for something to block me every single time!"
[ZH] 白风为难道：“不好说，王爷与郡主已有婚约，与幽栖居士抱在一起会不会对不住郡主？”
[EN] Watching them embrace from afar, Kuqiao fretted. "Bai Feng, what on earth is going on between your prince and my Miss?"
[ZH] 夏果说：“有什么对不住？我家小姐未来可以给仪王当妾室。”
[EN] Bai Feng scratched his head uneasy. "Hard to say. His Highness is betrothed to Princess Jiahe; hugging Youqi Jushi like that, doesn't that betray Princess Jiahe?"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“可朱家已与陈家订亲，小姐未来要嫁给陈公子。”
[EN] Xiaguo chimed in breezy, "Betray how? My Miss can always become Prince Yi's concubine!"
[ZH] “仪王是什么身份？想让朱陈两家取消亲事还不容易？我终于明白，小姐为何不愿嫁到陈家，原来她想嫁的人是仪王。再说小姐与仪王在一起，你们两个岂不是更方便？”
[EN] "Except the Zhu and Chen families sealed a betrothal," Kuqiao protested. "Miss is slated to marry Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] 夏果的话说的有些道理，苦荞与白风听后甚至产生希望两人在一起的想法。
[EN] "Think of Prince Yi's rank! Canceling a Zhu-Chen betrothal is snapping his fingers! Now I get why Miss hated marrying into the Chens—she wants Prince Yi! Plus if Miss and Prince Yi end up together, won't it be twice as easy for you two to see each other?"
[ZH] 很快白风又觉得自己的想法太自私，要是郡主知道幽栖居士与王爷搂抱在一起，肯定会伤心。
[EN] Xiaguo's spin almost tempted Kuqiao and Bai Feng, until guilt pricked Bai Feng fast: if Princess Jiahe ever learned Prince Yi held Youqi Jushi in his arms, it would shatter her!

#CH 154
[ZH] 千里寻因高兴忘乎所以，抱了一会儿申简辰，猛然想起梦容，不得已推了推他：“行了行了，放开吧。”
[ZH] 申简辰根本不愿，这可是他第一次搂抱心爱的姑娘，就此放手，以后还不知道有没有机会。可千里寻要放开，他也只好听话，松开双手。
[ZH] 天色逐渐暗下，一轮明月升起，今晚的月亮出奇得圆，也出奇得亮。神柳光秃秃的枝丫无法挡住月光，周围被皎洁的月光映衬得如同白昼。
[ZH] 树下的人仿佛忘记还是冬日的夜晚，逐渐生起的寒意对他们没有任何影响。
[ZH] “你何时认出我？”
[ZH] “你与文渊第一次约会时。”
[ZH] 千里寻想起红绸之事：“因为我挂的那条红绸？”
[ZH] “是的，要不是红绸从高处落下，我怎么知道幽栖居士正是我日思夜想之人。”
[ZH] 千里寻又有些许失落，本以为仪王是她的铁杆粉丝，今日才知不过是她一厢情愿的认为罢了。
[ZH] “你既认出老娘，为何不早说？”千里寻没有必要在仪王面前继续装下去。
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“这样说话我才喜欢。至于为何没及时告诉你，刚才已解释过。任何事都要讲究机缘，今日机缘总算成熟，才能顺利告诉你我是谁。”
[ZH] “终于理解堂堂王爷为何对我不一样，以前误解你是个花心王爷，实在抱歉。”
[ZH] 申简辰瞧一眼粗壮的神柳，尽管满眼是光秃秃的柳枝，也掩饰不住婀娜的身姿。
[ZH] “不要嫁给陈浩宇。”申简辰再次说出心里话。
[ZH] “放心，老娘不会嫁。”千里寻的语气十分肯定。
[ZH] “可婚约已定，只怕由不得你，毕竟这个时代与你我穿越前的时代不同。”
[ZH] 千里寻沉默不语，为何穿越而来的人生之路总被人安排？乔家人安排了她与文渊，逼得她狠心断绝与文渊的感情。刚好些，朱家与陈家又莫名其妙联姻。
[EN] Swept up in pure joy, Qianli Xun hugged Shen Jianchen a long moment before Mengrong flashed across her mind. She nudged him back. "Alright, alright, let go!"
[ZH] “即使再由不得我，老娘也会努力改变。”
[EN] Loath to release his very first embrace of the woman he loved—no telling when another chance might come—Shen Jianchen still obeyed and opened his arms.
[ZH] 申简辰本想将陈浩宇散播流言一事告知，话到嘴边又咽下，他不愿千里寻太难过，希望她快乐。
[EN] Twilight deepened as a bright moon climbed overhead, uncommonly round and luminous. Bare winter willow twigs could not block its silver flood, lighting the clearing bright as day. Standing beneath the tree, neither seemed to notice the biting winter chill settling in.
[ZH] “要不……你答应跟我在一起，我便有理由帮你。”
[EN] "When did you figure out who I was?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 如若在渴望爱情之时申简辰出现，身边又没有其他牵绊，千里寻肯定会答应。可现在她深受情伤，已决定不再涉足感情。
[EN] "Your very first date with Wenyuan."
[ZH] 还有梦容，她很可怜，深爱着她的哥哥，可她真正的哥哥已死，整日面对着不是哥哥的哥哥。千里寻不愿梦容受伤，希望她能永远幸福。
[EN] Remembering the silk ribbon, she asked, "Because of the red ribbon I tied up there?"
[ZH] 她装作开玩笑的样子：“老娘说过，除非猪能上树，才答应嫁给你，这话依然有效。”
[EN] "Yes. If that ribbon hadn't drifted down from the upper boughs, how would I ever have known Youqi Jushi was the very girl I'd been searching for?"
[ZH] 一抹受伤的表情掠过申简辰的脸颊，很快他恢复正常，郑重地问：“难道你从来没爱过我？”
[EN] A pinch of sheepish letdown crossed Qianli Xun. All along she had assumed Prince Yi was her die-hard literary fan; turns out she had flattered herself! Dropping every noble-lady air, she snapped in her old sassy tone, "If you recognized me way back then, why keep your mouth shut?!"
[ZH] 千里寻想起刚刚穿越而来渴望爱情那阵儿，心里想的全是申简辰：“我爱过你，只可惜那时你不在我身边。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen laughed warm. "Now THAT'S the voice I missed! As for waiting, I already told you: timing never lined up until today."
[ZH] 得到意外的答案，申简辰如获至宝：“你竟然爱过我？我……我高兴得都不知该说什么。”
[EN] "Makes total sense now why an imperial prince singled me out. Sorry for pegging you as a shameless playboy."
[ZH] “本以为永远见不到你……，后来遇见文渊，他令我怦然心动，再往后的事你都知道，无需我多说。”千里寻淡淡地叙述着往事。
[EN] Glancing up at the massive Divine Willow, its bare winter boughs still curving with quiet poise, Shen Jianchen turned earnest. "Don't marry Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] 申简辰开始自责，如若他早些来临安，早些遇到幽栖居士，早些认出幽栖居士就是千里寻，那么她就不会爱上文渊，然后深受情伤，也不会与陈浩宇有婚约。
[EN] "Relax, zero chance I'm marrying him," Qianli Xun answered crisp.
[ZH] “都怪我没有及时出现在你身边。”
[EN] "Betrothal is sealed; might not be up to you! This era isn't our old world."
[ZH] “怎能怪你？净光法师说过，朱淑真情路坎坷，我穿越成为她，必然不会一帆风顺，需经历几段情路之后，人生才会顺畅。”
[EN] Silence fell over Qianli Xun. Why did everyone in this century keep scripting her life? First the Qiaos crushed her romance with Wenyuan; just as she got back on her feet, the Zhus and Chens slapped a betrothal right over her head! "Even if the deck's stacked against me, I'm fighting to flip it!"
[ZH] “几段情路？”申简辰像是自问，更像是自责，他不愿千里寻再受情伤，果断说道，“那就由我斩断你的情路。”
[EN] Words about Chen Haoyu spreading those filthy rumors rose to Shen Jianchen's tongue, then he swallowed them back. Why add fresh hurt when all he wanted was her smiling? Instead he ventured soft, "Why not say yes to being with me? That gives me ironclad reason to step in and break it off."
[ZH] “怎么斩断？”
[EN] Had Shen Jianchen appeared back when she hungered for romance, with zero strings attached, she would have jumped straight in. Yet now fresh heartbreak had sworn her off love for good. Worse stood Mengrong: loving her brother with her whole soul while never knowing her real brother had died, pouring devotion into a stranger wearing his face! Qianli Xun refused to crush Mengrong's world.
[ZH] “跟我在一起，我绝不会让你受伤。”申简辰再次提出要求。
[EN] Masking gravity behind a teasing smirk, she shot back, "I already told you: only when pigs climb trees will I marry you! Still stands!"
[ZH] “梦容呢？仪王与她可是有婚约。”
[EN] Pain flickered across Shen Jianchen's eyes before he steadied himself and asked grave, "Did you never once love me?"
[ZH] 提到乔梦容，提到婚约，申简辰变得沉默，在婚约面前，即使王爷也无可奈何。
[EN] Memories of her first lonely days after crossing over surfaced, every dream filled with Shen Jianchen. She answered plain, "I did love you... pity you weren't beside me back then."
[ZH] “命运与你我开了一个大大的玩笑。”千里寻见申简辰不语，继续说道，“遇到地震，被神柳之力带到这个时代，我们重新活过来，拥有新的人生，必然与穿越前不同，需重新来过，包括感情。……申简辰，忘了我吧，好好爱梦容。”
[EN] Those quiet words lit Shen Jianchen up like buried treasure. "You loved me?! I... I don't even know what to say!"
[ZH] 本以为表明身份后，千里寻会乐意接纳他，还是被拒绝，还拒绝得如此干脆利落。
[EN] "I figured I'd never see you again," Qianli Xun went on level. "Then Wenyuan came along and stole my heart, and you know how the rest played out. No need to rehash."
[ZH] 申简辰不愿死心，明明知道会被拒绝，还是抱着试试的态度：“梦容很善良，只要我好好与她说，她肯定会答应让我纳你为妾，我就可以出面解决朱陈两家的联姻。”
[EN] Self-blame hit Shen Jianchen hard. Had he reached Lin'an sooner, met Youqi Jushi sooner, recognized Qianli Xun sooner, she never would have fallen for Wenyuan, never shattered her heart, never landed engaged to Chen Haoyu! "All my fault for showing up too late."
[ZH] “老娘说过，绝不给人当妾室，难道你忘了？你既说梦容善良，更不应该纳妾室伤害她，应与她白头偕老。”
[EN] "Not your fault. Master Jingguang warned me Zhu Shuzhen's destiny carries a rocky road in love. Crossing into her body means passing through romantic trials before life smooths out."
[ZH] 虽然千里寻的话语毫不留余地，可申简辰仍旧不想放弃：“告诉我，如何帮你，才能让你摆脱与陈浩宇的婚约，还有要经历的情路？”
[EN] "Romantic trials?" Shen Jianchen repeated grim, refusing to watch her bleed again. "Then let me cut those trials short right now!"
[ZH] “每个人的人生之路要自己走，他人根本帮不了，即使你是王爷也帮不了。我已想好，陈浩宇不愿娶，我不愿嫁，我们会联手想方设法破坏婚约；至于情路，与文渊算是经历过一次，以后还会有几次，我不得而知，可我已做好万全准备，只要不涉足爱情，不轻易爱上别人，情路便轻而易举被我斩断，我的人生自然顺遂。”
[EN] "How?"
[ZH] 原来千里寻已将接下来的人生想得一清二楚，根本无须申简辰插入一脚。他无法再说出任何要求，半晌才说道：“我会听你的话照顾好梦容，也会等着你，等你哪天想要涉足爱情，我自然不会放过你！”
[EN] "Be with me. I swear I'll never let you get hurt!"
[ZH] 申简辰喊了声：“白风！”
[EN] "And Mengrong? Prince Yi has an imperial betrothal to her!"
[ZH] 白风来到王爷身边，跟上他，借着月色，顺着山脚下越走越远。
[EN] Mention of Qiao Mengrong and the imperial decree silenced him; even a prince held no magic wand over an emperor's decree.
[ZH] 千里寻目送仪王离开后，面向神柳，冬夜的月光下，神柳是否已睡着。千里寻希望它醒着，听她内心的声音，希望神柳保佑她不要嫁到陈家，不要再经历情路，一个人安安稳稳度过。
[EN] Seeing him muted, Qianli Xun spoke gentle. "Fate played a giant prank on both of us. Surviving that quake through the Divine Willow handed us brand-new lives. Everything changes, love included. Let me go, Shen Jianchen, and love Mengrong well."
[ZH] 也祈祷神柳保佑梦容与起死回生的哥哥顺利成亲，相亲相爱。
[EN] Thinking revealing his identity would unlock her heart, getting turned down this crisp stung deep. Yet stubborn hope made him try one last long shot. "Mengrong is kind-hearted! If I talk it through gentle with her, she'll agree to let me take you as concubine, and then I can dissolve the Zhu-Chen betrothal on the spot!"
[ZH] 一阵冷风吹来，千里寻将披风裹紧。
[EN] "I swore I'd never be anyone's concubine; forgot already? And if you know Mengrong is that kind, don't hurt her bringing another woman home! Grow old together with her alone."
[ZH] 苦荞提醒道：“小姐，该回去了。”
[EN] Shut down with zero wiggle room, Shen Jianchen still refused to walk away empty-handed. "Tell me how to help you break Chen Haoyu's betrothal, and dodge whatever love trials wait ahead!"
[ZH] 千里寻挪动脚步，与两个丫鬟向山路入口处走去。她故意放慢脚步，希望申简辰在她到达马车旁时已离开。
[EN] "Everyone walks their own road; not even a prince can walk mine for me! Plan's already set: Chen Haoyu doesn't want me, I don't want him, so we'll team up to wreck the match from both sides! As for love trials, Wenyuan was round one. No clue how many wait next, yet I'm armored up: swear off love, never hand my heart to another soul, and every love trial snaps dead on contact! Clear skies ahead!"
[ZH] 计划得不错，走到上山的入口处时，幽林别居的马车已不在。
[EN] She had mapped out every inch of her future leaving zero space for him to wedge in. Standing silent a long beat, Shen Jianchen spoke low. "I'll heed your words and care for Mengrong, and I'll wait for you! The day you ever open your heart to love again, don't think I'll let you slip away!"
[ZH] 主仆三人登上望月轩的马车，直到进入闹市，没说一句话。夜晚的街上依旧热闹，嘈杂的人声衬得车内愈加安静。
[EN] Calling "Bai Feng!" he strode off down the moonlit mountain path with his guard trailing close behind.
[ZH] 今日千里寻也算开心，找到申简辰，知道他安然无恙，还成为王爷。
[EN] Watching his silhouette fade, Qianli Xun faced the Divine Willow once more, wondering whether the tree slept beneath winter moonlight, praying in silence it stayed awake to hear her wish: Keep me out of the Chen house, spare me more heartbreak, let me live quiet on my own, and bless Mengrong and her reborn brother to wed in joy and love each other well.
[ZH] 人的命运真是难以捉摸，申简辰那个嘚瑟样儿，竟然穿越成为王爷，给她十个脑袋都想不到。可事实摆在眼前，她不得不信。
[EN] A sharp winter gust swept through. Pulling her cloak tighter, Qianli Xun heard Kuqiao remind her, "Time to head back, Miss."
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐面露笑容，忍不住打破沉默：“小姐是不是想到开心的事？”
[EN] Leading both maids down toward the trailhead, she slowed her stride on purpose so Shen Jianchen's rig would clear out first. Sure enough, Youlin Retreat's carriage was gone when they arrived. Climbing aboard Wangyue Pavilion's carriage, all three rode silent all the way into lively evening avenues, bright street clamor underscoring the hush inside.
[ZH] “当然。”
[EN] Truth be told, joy still warmed Qianli Xun: finding Shen Jianchen alive, safe, and an imperial prince! Wild how fate spun—that cocky CEO waking up royalty! Never in ten lifetimes would she have guessed it!
[ZH] “什么开心的事？”
[EN] Spotting her quiet smile, Kuqiao broke the silence. "Thinking of something happy, Miss?"
[ZH] 千里寻不能把仪王是申简辰的事告诉苦荞：“不该你关心别瞎问。”
[EN] "Course."
[ZH] 夏果大胆问：“小姐不会是喜欢上仪王，才这么开心？”
[EN] "What happy news?"
[ZH] 苦荞批评道：“夏果！你怎么能瞎说？”
[EN] Unable to spill Prince Yi's real identity, she waved it off. "None of your business. Stop nosing around!"
[ZH] 夏果误解倒也正常，与仪王单独约会，因遇故人，兴奋过头，搂抱在一起，谁看到，也会往这方面想。
[EN] Xiaguo ventured bold, "Did Miss fall for Prince Yi—is that why you're grinning?"
[ZH] 千里寻郑重要求道：“你们两个记着，今日所见，不准到处乱说！”
[EN] "Xiaguo! Watch your tongue!" Kuqiao snapped.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟点点头：“小姐放心，绝不乱说。”
[EN] She could not blame Xiaguo for guessing wrong; a secret moonlight meeting ending in a tight hug looked mighty romantic to outsiders! Setting her tone grave, Qianli Xun warned both girls, "Listen close, both of you: not one word about tonight leaves this carriage!"
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“我与仪王清清白白，不是你们想的那样。至于为什么单独相约，又……又搂抱在一起，不好告诉你们，反正不是因为我喜欢他。”
[EN] Both maids nodded fast. "Never a word, Miss!"
[ZH] 两个丫鬟听得云里雾里，无法理解。
[EN] "Prince Yi and I are spotless, nothing like what you're imagining," Qianli Xun added. "I can't explain why we met alone or hugged; just know romance has zero to do with it."
[ZH] 夏果又大胆说：“小姐不喜欢仪王，可我看仪王对小姐有意思，您若答应跟仪王在一起，就不用担心嫁给陈公子。”
[EN] Both maids blinked baffled, still in a fog. Xiaguo risked one more bold nudge. "Even if Miss doesn't like Prince Yi, plain as day Prince Yi fancies you! Say yes to him and you dodge marrying Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] “你说的办法是不错，可你家小姐我根本不可能这么做，我有我做人做事的准则，一旦违背，再好的事我都不会做。”千里寻说得斩钉截铁。
[EN] "Clever idea, and zero chance I touch it!" Qianli Xun declared iron-firm. "I live by my own rules; cross them, and no prize on earth tempts me!"
[ZH] 两个丫鬟终于不再多嘴，继续默默陪伴……
[EN] Silenced by her resolve, neither maid breathed another word the rest of the ride home.

#CH 155
[ZH] 第二天，陈文心见天气不错，打算看望爹娘，到向夫人跟前说了，得到允许，让丫鬟月桂和奶娘抱着莱宝坐上马车回陈府。
[ZH] 儿媳兴高采烈回娘家，向夫人不由想到自家姑娘，什么时候也能抱个外孙，满面春光回娘家来看望她。
[ZH] 眼见春节将近，淑贞搬出朱府已不少时日，向夫人虽时常去望月轩看望她，可总比不上女儿待在身边让她安心。
[ZH] 朱陈两家定亲，淑贞的婚事算是有着落，几次向夫人想要提出接女儿回家，却欲言又止，面对老爷严肃的表情，她着实不敢开口。
[ZH] 有一次，朱晞颜无意中提到等淑贞与陈浩宇成亲时，将她接回来，从朱府嫁出去。向夫人高兴的同时，揣摩出老爷的意思，应该暗含着成亲前不允许女儿搬回来。
[ZH] 自此，向夫人不敢再有接女儿回朱府的想法。
[ZH] 陈文心回到娘家，来到父母的房中，只有姚轻琰在。
[ZH] 姚轻琰见到莱宝喜上眉梢，从奶娘手中接过孩子，抱在怀里亲了一口：“莱宝又大了，也该学走路。”
[ZH] “在家学着呢，需要人扶。”陈文心坐下，“娘，我爹呢？怎么没陪您？”
[ZH] “你爹去忙生意，陈家的生意又要往外地拓展，你爹最近很少陪我。”姚轻琰说完继续逗莱宝，“叫外婆！”
[ZH] 莱宝操着稚嫩的声音叫道：“外婆……外婆……”虽不是很清晰，姚轻琰还是高兴不已，在莱宝脸上亲个够。
[ZH] 陈文心笑着说：“外孙都让娘这么高兴，等到您有亲孙子，不知会高兴成什么样。”
[ZH] “那还用说，只是我没你婆婆有福气，早早抱上孙子，只盼淑真快些嫁过来，跟浩宇生个儿子，女儿也行，娘都喜欢。”
[ZH] 姚轻琰想起件事，对奶娘和丫鬟月桂说：“你们先把莱宝抱到里屋去玩会儿，我有事要问文心。”
[ZH] 奶娘抱过莱宝，与月桂一起把孩子带进里屋。
[EN] Next morning dawned bright and mild, so Chen Wenxin asked Madam Xiang's leave to visit her parents, then boarded a carriage back to Chen Manor with Yuegui and the wet nurse carrying little Laibao.
[ZH] 陈文心关切地问：“娘，您要问我什么？”
[EN] Watching her beaming daughter-in-law ride home stirred a quiet ache in Madam Xiang. When would her own daughter return beaming with a grandchild in her arms? New Year loomed right around the corner; Shuzhen had lived outside Zhu Manor for ages now, and frequent visits to Wangyue Pavilion could not match having her daughter safe under her own roof. With the Zhu-Chen betrothal settled, Madam Xiang had almost begged Master several times to bring Shuzhen home, only to lose her nerve before his stony face. Once Zhu Xiyan mentioned in passing that Shuzhen would come back to Zhu Manor to leave in her bridal sedan on her wedding day. That thrilled Madam Xiang while tipping her off he meant not one day earlier, killing any hope of bringing her home ahead of time.
[ZH] “还不是你弟弟的事。”姚轻琰叹口气，“唉，给他订亲，咱们高兴，浩宇他可不满意，说淑真是母老虎，担心娶回来家暴他。上次他不知从哪里弄了一身的伤，骗我跟你爹，硬说是淑真打的。我跟你爹不相信，可浩宇说的话又不由得让我心里犯嘀咕，淑真该不会表面柔弱，其实是个母夜叉？”
[EN] Arriving at Chen Manor, Chen Wenxin walked straight to her parents' rooms, finding only Yao Qingyan inside. Lighting up at sight of Laibao, Yao Qingyan scooped her grandson from the wet nurse's arms and kissed his chubby cheek. "Growing like a weed! Almost walking age!"
[ZH] 陈文心凑近，满脸神秘：“娘，我跟您说件事，可不能告诉外人。”
[EN] "Practicing at home; just needs someone holding his hands," Chen Wenxin smiled, taking a seat. "Where's Father? Not keeping you company?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰点点头，疑惑道：“什么事？”
[EN] "Off chasing business. Expanding Chen ventures into other prefectures, so he's seldom home these days." Bouncing Laibao playful, she coaxed, "Say Grandma!"
[ZH] “淑真失忆了，以前的事一点儿都不记得。”
[EN] "Gwa-ma... Gwa-ma..." Babbling unclear yet adorable, Laibao earned showered kisses across both cheeks.
[ZH] “失忆？怎么会失忆？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin teased, "Going this wild over a daughter's son, wonder what you'll do holding your own son's baby!"
[ZH] “是被林觉打晕过去，醒来什么都不记得，人变得也跟以前不一样。”
[EN] "Need you ask? I only envy your mother-in-law getting a grandson first! Praying Shuzhen marries in fast and gives Haoyu a boy—or a girl, Mother adores both!" Remembering something urgent, Yao Qingyan told the wet nurse and Yuegui, "Take Laibao inside the inner room to play a bit; I need a private word with Wenxin."
[ZH] “变成母夜叉？”
[EN] Once they withdrew, Chen Wenxin leaned forward. "What's on your mind, Mother?"
[ZH] “那倒不至于，就是不那么高高在上，爱跟我聊天儿，嫂子长嫂子短地叫着，让人感到很舒服。”
[EN] "Your brother again!" Yao Qingyan let out a heavy sigh. "We're thrilled sealing his match, yet Haoyu hates it—swears Shuzhen is a tigress who'll beat him black and blue! Turned up covered in bruises last time trying to swear to your dad and me Shuzhen did it! We didn't buy it, yet he put a bug in my ear. Could Shuzhen look gentle outside while hiding a she-demon underneath?"
[ZH] “那是好事，还有没有其他变化。”
[EN] Leaning close with a secretive glint, Chen Wenxin whispered, "Let me tell you a secret, Mother—never breathe a word outside!"
[ZH] 陈文心想想说：“对了，她有一次跟我公爹吃饭，一拳把饭桌劈成两半。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan nodded curious. "What secret?"
[ZH] “我的妈呀！力气这么大！这么说来，浩宇怕她也有些道理，可浩宇是怎么知道她力气大的？”
[EN] "Shuzhen got amnesia! Wiped out every memory of the past!"
[ZH] “我不清楚，反正她跟浩宇合不来，一见面就掐，一个瞧不上一个，要不怎么会搞出流言一事？至于为什么，浩宇也没仔细跟我说过，我只觉他们之间的关系跟以前大不一样，以前见面不怎么说话，现在见面虽不友好，起码还说话。”
[EN] "Amnesia?! How on earth?!"
[ZH] “你一说，我算明白，原来浩宇是跟淑真合不来才编瞎话，并不是淑真性情不好。两人以前见面不怎么说话，现在一见面就掐，呵呵，说明失忆后的淑真跟浩宇之间有缘分，两个人肯定会越掐越亲，呵呵……”
[EN] "Lin Jue beat her unconscious. She woke up remembering nothing and turned into a whole different person!"
[ZH] 看到娘高兴的样子，陈文心故意说：“娘笑得这么开心，就不担心淑真力气大，嫁过来打浩宇？”
[EN] "Into a she-demon?!"
[ZH] “你爹说过，这个家没人管得了浩宇，娶个厉害的媳妇回来专门管他，看他一天到晚还敢到处浪荡。”姚轻琰又愁道，“老太太疼浩宇，一门心思认为浩宇身上的伤是淑真所打，想要取消婚约，碍于知府大人的身份，老太太不敢闹，一心想耗着朱家。”
[EN] "Nothing like that! Just dropped her frosty airs—chats warm with me calling 'Sister-in-law' left and right, super pleasant to be around!"
[ZH] “亏奶奶想得出！怎么耗？”
[EN] "Well, that's lovely. Any other changes?"
[ZH] “我怎知？老太太八成心里已有主意。我跟你爹表面上应承，心里希望成亲时千万不要出什么差错。”
[EN] Pausing to think, Chen Wenxin grinned. "Oh, once during dinner with Father-in-law, she split a solid dining table clean in half with one bare punch!"
[ZH] 陈文心站起来：“我这就去找奶奶，告诉她，她被浩宇骗了。”
[EN] "Good heavens! Strength like that?! Guess Haoyu has some reason to sweat! Wait—how did Haoyu even know she packs punches like that?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰拉住女儿：“你又不是不知道，老太太也是经历过风雨之人，她能不知道浩宇欺骗她？她只是爱孙子心切，不愿他受到任何伤害。”
[EN] "No idea! All I know is those two clash like fire and water, snapping at each other on sight, probably why those ugly rumors blew up! Funny thing is, before they seldom spoke two words; now at least they bicker nonstop!"
[ZH] 陈文心坐下，埋怨道：“依我看，奶奶是老糊涂！”
[EN] Relief broke into laughter on Yao Qingyan's face. "Now it all makes sense! Haoyu just spun tall tales because they lock horns—nothing rotten in Shuzhen's heart! Seldom spoke before, bickering nonstop now... hehe, proves amnesia sparked real fate between them! Couples who squabble grow closest!"
[ZH] 正说着，陈浩宇走进来：“姐姐竟然敢在背后编排奶奶，小心我到奶奶跟前告你的状。”
[EN] Watching her mother beam, Chen Wenxin teased, "Grinning ear to ear—aren't you worried strong-fisted Shuzhen will beat Haoyu once she moves in?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇在外面逛完，想着不能老这么逛下去，得找机会破坏婚约。他先去朱府跑一趟，门口的下人告诉他少夫人回娘家了，这会儿应该快到陈府。陈浩宇便着急忙慌往回赶。
[EN] "Your father already said nobody in this house can rein Haoyu in. Bringing home a fierce wife to keep him in line might cure his wild ways! Only snag is Old Madam Xia. She dotes on Haoyu blind, swears Shuzhen bruised him, and wants the match broken! Daren't cross the Prefect to his face, so she plans on stalling the Zhus out!"
[ZH] 陈文心故意不满道：“你又跑去哪里？都快成亲的人，也不知道收敛收敛。”
[EN] "Leave it to Grandma! How's she stalling?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐到母亲身边：“我能去哪里？还不是跑去朱府找你，结果你回娘家。”
[EN] "No clue; bet she's got a scheme cooking! Your dad and I nod along to keep peace while praying nothing derails the wedding."
[ZH] “你编瞎话骗得了奶奶，可骗不了我。”陈文心走到弟弟身边，拧住他的耳朵，“老实交代，是去了青楼还是赌坊？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin jumped up. "I'm marching straight to Grandma to tell her Haoyu played her!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇被拧疼：“哎吆！哎吆！你是不是我亲姐姐？一见面就欺负我！”
[EN] Pulling her back down, Yao Qingyan shook her head. "Think Grandma lived all these decades without seeing through Haoyu's lies? She just loves her grandson too much to bear seeing him scraped!"
[ZH] 陈文心松开手，又坐下：“快说，找我什么事？”
[EN] Sinking back into her seat, Chen Wenxin groused, "If you ask me, Grandma's gone senile!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇揉着被拧疼的耳朵，先不回答，向姚轻琰说：“娘，您好好说说我姐，我都这么大人，一见面就拧我耳朵，传出去，多没面子。”
[EN] Right on cue, Chen Haoyu sauntered through the door. "Talking trash about Grandma behind her back, Sister? Careful or I tattle straight to her!" Out roaming town hunting ways to wreck his betrothal, he had dropped by Zhu Manor first, heard Young Madam had gone home to Chen Manor, and hurried straight back.
[ZH] 姚轻琰并未护着儿子：“你但凡在外面干些正事，你姐姐也不会这么对待你。”
[EN] Putting on a stern elder-sister glare, Chen Wenxin barked, "Where've you been gallivanting now?! Almost a married man and still wild as ever!"
[ZH] “娘，您偏心。”陈浩宇埋怨着。
[EN] Dropping beside his mother, Chen Haoyu grinned. "Where else? Ran over to Zhu Manor looking for you, only to hear you came home!"
[ZH] 陈文心故意表现出不耐烦的样子：“就你那样，还有脸让娘偏袒你？赶紧的，快说找我什么事？”
[EN] "Pull wool over Grandma's eyes, not mine!" Stepping over, Chen Wenxin pinched his ear hard. "Confess—brothel or gambling den?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不想引起家人怀疑，故意说：“我这不快成朱家的好女婿，想打听打听我未来的老丈人最近都做些什么，好找机会到他面前表现表现。”
[EN] "Ow! Ow! Real sister or torture master?! Attacking me the second I walk in!"
[ZH] 母女两个甚感疑惑，浩宇的态度竟然大变。
[EN] Releasing his ear and sitting back, she snapped, "Spill it—what do you want with me?"
[ZH] 陈文心觉得事出反常必有妖，警惕地问：“真这么想？”
[EN] Rubbing his red ear, he whined to Yao Qingyan, "Mother, scold Sister! Grown man getting his ear twisted; I'll lose all my face if word gets out!"
[ZH] “那还用问？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan offered zero backup. "Do one decent thing outside and your sister wouldn't touch you!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“儿子，你真的愿意娶淑真？”
[EN] "Playing favorites, Mother!"
[ZH] “愿意愿意。”
[EN] "Zero shame asking Mother to cover for you!" Chen Wenxin snapped impatient. "Out with it—why were you looking for me?"
[ZH] “既愿意，先去告诉你奶奶，让她别再想耗着的办法。”姚轻琰的心里还是觉得不踏实。
[EN] Keeping his real scheme hid, Chen Haoyu put on a pious mask. "About to become a proper Zhu son-in-law, so I figured I'd ask what my future father-in-law's up to lately to find a chance to impress him!"
[ZH] “放心，我迟早会劝奶奶。”
[EN] Mother and daughter traded startled stares. What flipped overnight?
[ZH] 陈文心对弟弟充满怀疑：“你以为我会信你，打听我公爹到底想干什么？”
[EN] Smelling a rat at once, Chen Wenxin narrowed her eyes. "Serious?"
[ZH] “不都告诉你，找机会好好在他面前表现呗。”
[EN] "Need you ask?!"
[ZH] “你想在我公爹面前表现，随时去朱府即可，何必向我打听？”
[EN] "Really willing to marry Shuzhen, son?" Yao Qingyan pressed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇心想，知府大人不让朱淑真回府，自己一个人跑到朱府，表演个什么劲儿。
[EN] "Willing, willing!"
[ZH] “我去朱府表现有什么意思？如果在外面见到未来的岳父大人，当着他那些同僚们表现表现，他才更有面子。”
[EN] "Then march over to Grandma first and tell her to drop her stalling games!" Doubt still nagged Yao Qingyan.
[ZH] 陈文心搞不懂弟弟葫芦里卖的是什么药，量他再纨绔，也不敢在临安官员面前造次，放心说：“听你姐夫说，快过年，临安官府包下聚香楼，明日在那里宴饮聚会。我劝你还是别去，省得给朱家和陈家丢脸。”
[EN] "Relax, I'll talk Grandma round soon enough."
[ZH] 打听到这么重要的消息，陈浩宇自是欣喜：“姐姐放心，你弟弟我再怎么不着调，也不会丢两家的人。你们母女两个聊着，我去找奶奶，劝说她别再想耗着朱家。”
[EN] Still skeptical, Chen Wenxin probed, "Think I buy that? What are you really digging into Father-in-law's schedule for?"
[ZH] 儿子屁股还没坐热，又一溜烟儿离开。
[EN] "Told you—impressing him!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰批评道：“没说两句又走！这孩子！”
[EN] "Want to impress Father-in-law, visit Zhu Manor anytime. Why pump me?"
[ZH] “娘，您可相信浩宇？”
[EN] He could not put on a show solo at Zhu Manor when Zhu Shuzhen was barred from the gate! Spinning fast, he grinned. "Where's the glory at home? Impressing my future father-in-law in front of all his fellow officials gives him real face!"
[ZH] “难以相信，他说那些话不过是哄我们，你不该告诉他你公公的事。”
[EN] Clueless what scheme her brother had cooking yet sure even he would not dare make a scene before Lin'an's officials, Chen Wenxin relented. "Heard from your brother-in-law that with New Year coming, the prefecture office booked Juxiang Tower tomorrow for an officials' banquet. Do yourself a favor and stay clear so you don't shame both houses!"
[ZH] “娘放心，给他十个胆儿也不敢在临安官员面前造次。”
[EN] Jackpot! Beaming inside, Chen Haoyu promised smooth, "Relax, Sister. Wild as I get, I'd never shame both families! Keep chatting; I'm off to Grandma's to tell her to quit stalling!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇根本没有去找夏老太太，而是出陈府往去望月轩。
[EN] He had scarce warmed his chair before bolting right back out.
[ZH] 到达望月轩，他不愿进去，让守门的小厮把朱淑真叫出来。
[EN] "Two words and gone again—that boy!" Yao Qingyan scolded.
[ZH] 小厮一路小跑来到闺房门外：“姑娘，陈家公子来访，他不愿进来，让您出去见他。”
[EN] "Believe him, Mother?"
[ZH] 千里寻刚收拾好，正打算去幽林别居，听到小厮的声音，想着陈浩宇前来定是想与她商量取消婚约的办法。
[EN] "Not a word! Just feeding us sugar. You shouldn't have told him your father-in-law's plans!"
[ZH] 苦荞了解小姐的心思，过去掀开厚厚的帘子：“去回陈公子，小姐过会儿见他。”
[EN] "Relax, Mother. Give him ten extra guts and he still wouldn't dare cause trouble in front of every Lin'an official!"
[ZH] 小厮正要回，千里寻阻止道：“不用回了，我这就去见他。”
[EN] Skipping Old Madam Xia altogether, Chen Haoyu bolted straight out of Chen Manor toward Wangyue Pavilion. Arriving at the gate, he refused to step inside and had the gate boy summon Zhu Shuzhen out.
[ZH] 千里寻只身来到大门口，见陈浩宇也只身一人，没带家仆，八成是从哪里干完坏事过来，心中十分不爽：“你来找老娘该不会是想到什么招数？”
[EN] Jogging to her room, the page boy called, "Miss, Young Master Chen is at the gate. He won't come in and asks you to step out."
[ZH] “你以为呢？本公子来你这里可不是为谈情说爱。”
[EN] Just finished dressing to visit Youlin Retreat, Qianli Xun guessed at once he had cooked up a break-off scheme. Reading her mind, Kuqiao lifted the heavy winter curtain. "Tell Young Master Chen Miss will be out in a bit."
[ZH] “快说！说完赶紧滚！”
[EN] "Skip the message—I'm heading right out!" Qianli Xun stepped past him.
[ZH] 朱淑真凶巴巴的样子，陈浩宇更加觉得这个女人娶不得。
[EN] Walking solo to the gate and seeing Chen Haoyu standing alone without attendants, fresh off some mischief no doubt, she scowled. "Coming to my door—cooked up a plan?"
[ZH] “给你个建议，不要老凶我，对我倒没什么影响，只怕你会越来越丑。”
[EN] "Of course! Think I came over to whisper sweet nothings?!"
[ZH] “再不说正事，小心老娘揍你！”
[EN] "Spill it and get lost!"
[ZH] “正好有个机会让你好好揍我，不过不能来真格的。”
[EN] Her fierce bark convinced him twice over he could not marry this woman. "Free tip: quit snarling at me. Doesn't hurt me, just makes you uglier!"
[ZH] “什么机会？”
[EN] "Talk business or eat my fist!"
[ZH] “明日临安官员包下聚香楼，你爹是临安知府，肯定在，不如我们当着临安官员的面演出戏，你跟我打一架，让所有人看到你与我根本不合适，说不定你爹受到影响，主动要求退婚。”
[EN] "Speaking of hitting me, I've got the golden stage for you—just don't hit for real!"
[ZH] 千里寻有些犹豫，当着众人的面岂不是伤了朱晞颜的面子？想让他主动要求陈家退婚估计没那么容易。
[EN] "What stage?"
[ZH] “出的什么馊主意！我爹最看重面子，那么多官员盯着，你让他面子往哪里搁？再说我名声刚好些，你想让别人再议论我？”
[EN] "Tomorrow Lin'an officials booked Juxiang Tower. Your dad's the Prefect, guaranteed to be there! Let's put on a brawl right in front of the entire prefecture office so everyone sees we're oil and water! Shamed enough, maybe your dad pushes to cancel the match himself!"
[ZH] “我本想去朱府跟你演出戏，可你爹不让你回去，好不容易从我姐那里打听到这么好的机会，你却不愿意，那你说该怎么办？”
[EN] Qianli Xun hesitated. Trashing Zhu Xiyan's face before every official sounded harsh, and pushing him into backing down might backfire. "Terrible idea! My dad guards his face with his life; making a scene before every official leaves him nowhere to hide! Plus my name just recovered—trying to start fresh gossip over me?!"
[ZH] 千里寻想想说：“不需要进朱府，也无需当着那么多官员的面儿，择日不如撞日，就今日，你我提前躲在朱府侧门外，等我爹申时从府衙回来，当着他的面，我们打一架。”
[EN] "I meant to stage it inside Zhu Manor, except your dad banned you from the door! I sweated getting this hot tip from my sister and you shoot it down. Fine, what's your brilliant plan?!"
[ZH] “好！就这么说定！不见不散！”
[EN] Thinking fast, Qianli Xun countered, "Skip Zhu Manor and skip the officials! No day like today. Right this afternoon we ambush outside Zhu Manor's side gate before the Hour of the Monkey when Dad gets off office duty, and stage our brawl right in front of him!"
[ZH] ﻿陈浩宇说完往永宁居走去，打算在永宁居吃过午饭，睡一觉，差不多时再去朱府门外等朱晞颜。
[EN] "Deal! Done—see you there!" Pivoting off toward Yongning Residence, Chen Haoyu planned on grabbing lunch and a quick nap before heading over to wait for Zhu Xiyan.

#CH 156
[ZH] 千里寻回到闺房，吩咐苦荞去趟幽林别居，说她有重要的事情办，明日必然前去陪伴郡主。
[ZH] 苦荞去后，好不容易熬到可以出发的时间，千里寻只身一人来到朱府侧门外。
[ZH] 侧门紧闭，她站着欣赏了一会儿，想起第一次回朱府的情景……
[ZH] 那时带着好奇与期待来到这里，希望穿越而来能在朱府获得踏实与心安。的确获得过，亲情让她越来越愿意适应穿越而来的生活。
[ZH] 后来被朱晞颜赶出朱府，也是从这道门离开，走得心甘情愿，走得毫无眷恋，只因这道门里的亲人给了她足够的照顾和勇气。
[ZH] 父亲虽严厉，又喜欢毫无道理为她定亲，千里寻还是对这位父亲产生莫名其妙的亲情，甚至能理解父亲的举动。
[ZH] 毕竟穿越而来的时代不够先进，婚姻之事哪轮得上子女做主？再说有个离过婚，名声又不好，还被乔家放鸽子的女儿，父亲朱晞颜肯定心中不安，在他看来能定下陈家这门亲，已是上天眷顾。
[ZH] 能理解父亲，并不代表会接受他的想法，千里寻希望今日一闹，能产生些作用。
[ZH] 正想着，陈浩宇出现：“喂，朱淑真，站在自家门外发什么呆？”
[ZH] 千里寻转过头，瞧向与她有婚约之人，不仅不学无术，不求上进，还没事到处欺负人，一脸毫无因由得意的样子，她看着就来气。心想：“凭什么要嫁给这样的人？”
[ZH] 她懒得理会陈浩宇，自顾走到竹子后面躲起来。
[ZH] 陈浩宇叹道：“你也挺可怜，到家门口却进不去，还不如我，只要说找我姐，自然被放进去，知府大人也不管。”
[ZH] 见千里寻依然没理会，陈浩宇也走到竹子后面。
[ZH] “你怎么不说话？”
[ZH] “看到你就不爽，正在积攒力气一会儿打你。”
[ZH] “说好了，不能真打。”
[ZH] “哼，到时打起来，只怕我管不住自己的拳头。”
[ZH] “你要是真打，我立刻离开，不跟你演戏。”
[EN] Back in her room, Qianli Xun dispatched Kuqiao to Youlin Retreat to tell Princess Jiahe urgent business had come up and she would be there without fail tomorrow. Waiting out the afternoon hours, she headed solo to Zhu Manor's side gate.
[ZH] 千里寻没好气地说：“哄你的，谁让你一副欠揍的样子。”
[EN] Shut tight beneath swaying bamboo, the gate stirred memories of her very first visit back to Zhu Manor. Back then she had walked up tense and hopeful, craving solid footing in an alien era, and found genuine family warmth that anchored her here. Even later, ordered out by Zhu Xiyan, she had stepped past this threshold steady and unafraid thanks to the love and courage those inside had given her. Strict and arbitrary about arranging her marriages as Zhu Xiyan was, an unexpected bond had grown inside her, letting her see his side. In an era where daughters held zero say in marriage, having a divorced, gossip-scarred daughter jilted by the Qiaos must have kept him awake nights; sealing a wealthy Chen match looked like sheer mercy to him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇第一次离朱淑真这么近，一股香气从她身上散发而来，心里不由想道：“母老虎身上竟然也有香气，还比春香楼姑娘身上的香气好闻，难以理解！明明是只母老虎，却生得貌美，要是性情好些，本公子勉为其难把你娶回家，也算替朱家解决个大麻烦，可谁让你拳头那么厉害，本公子是万万不能娶。”
[EN] Understanding him did not mean surrendering to him. She hoped today's show shook him loose.
[ZH] 千里寻扭头瞧见陈浩宇露出色眯眯的表情，更是来气，没忍住，一拳打在他的脸上：“谁让你这么盯着老娘看！”
[EN] Right then Chen Haoyu walked up. "Hey, Zhu Shuzhen, zoning out outside your own door?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇的脸上立刻出现淤青，他捂着脸，不由自主从竹子后面走出：“打人不打脸！这个道理你难道不明白？”
[EN] Glancing at her unwanted fiancé—idle, spoiled, forever picking on folks, wearing a smug smirk for zero reason—her temper flared: On what planet do I marry this guy? Ignoring him altogether, she slipped behind a thick clump of bamboo to hide.
[ZH] “老娘不明白！对你这种人最好照脸打！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu sighed in mock pity. "Pretty rough, huh? Standing outside your own home barred from stepping in. Beats me! All I do is say I'm visiting my sister and gates fly wide open; even the Prefect says nothing!"
[ZH] 千里寻刚骂完，听到哥哥朱景元的声音：“淑真，你站在竹子后面干什么？怎么浩宇也在？”
[EN] Meeting cold silence, he ducked behind the bamboo beside her. "Cat got your tongue?"
[ZH] 两人看到朱晞颜身着官服，一脸严肃地站在朱景元身旁，互相使了使眼色。
[EN] "Looking at your face ticks me off. I'm saving up steam to punch you in a minute."
[ZH] 陈浩宇迅速来到朱晞颜面前，露出被打得紫青的脸：“朱伯伯，朱淑真打我，您瞧，我的脸被她打成什么样？”
[EN] "Agreed—no real punches!"
[ZH] 千里寻也冲过来：“陈浩宇！你给我爹告状也没用！再吃我一拳！”
[EN] "Hmph, once fists fly, can't promise I hold back!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇立刻躲到朱晞颜身后：“有朱伯伯在，看你还敢打我！”
[EN] "Throw one real punch and I walk straight off. No show!"
[ZH] 千里寻的拳头本就出得慢些，陈浩宇躲开后，拳头不偏不倚出现在朱晞颜面前。
[EN] "Relax, just messing with you. Blame your punchable face!"
