[ZH] 她赶紧收起拳头：“爹，您让开，我今日要好好收拾收拾陈浩宇，不把他收拾乖了，坚决不嫁到陈家。”
[EN] Standing inches from Zhu Shuzhen for the very first time, a crisp, clean fragrance drifted off her, surprising him. Tigress smells nicer than every girl in Chunxiang Brothel—how does that even work? Fierce tigress wrapped in a stunner's face... if her temper weren't lethal, he wouldn't even mind marrying her to help the Zhus out, yet with fists like hers, zero chance he risked it!
[ZH] 朱晞颜什么大场面没见过，已猜出两个孩子的小心思，一脸平静：“景元，交给你处理，为父先回屋歇息。”
[EN] Glancing sideways to catch Chen Haoyu ogling her with a sleazy stare, Qianli Xun snapped and clocked him square in the cheek. "Who gave you permission to stare at me like that?!"
[ZH] 已有下人为老爷打开侧门，朱晞颜甩了甩袖子像什么都没发生一般离开。
[EN] An instant bruise darkened his cheek. Clutching his face, he stumbled clean out from behind the bamboo. "Never hit the face! Don't you know basic rules?!"
[ZH] 仅表演两下，没起到效果，千里寻很失望：“哥，爹怎么不理我们？”
[EN] "Not for you! Guys like you belong hit right in the face!"
[ZH] 朱景元笑道：“你们两个明显是故意表演给爹看，我都能瞧出来，何况爹？”
[EN] Her words were no sooner out than Zhu Jingyuan's voice rang down the lane. "Shuzhen, why are you hiding behind the bamboo? And Haoyu's here too?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇委屈道：“哪里是表演？姐夫你瞧，我脸上是不是被你妹妹打得乌青。”
[EN] Turning to see stone-faced Zhu Xiyan in official robes standing beside Zhu Jingyuan, the two flashed a quick signal glance.
[ZH] 朱景元早已注意到陈浩宇脸上的伤：“跟我进去，让丫鬟给你上些药。”
[EN] Bolting straight up to Zhu Xiyan, Chen Haoyu thrust out his purple cheek. "Uncle Zhu! Zhu Shuzhen hit me! Look what she did to my face!"
[ZH] 千里寻埋怨道：“哥哥只顾关心别人，却把亲妹妹扔在一边。”
[EN] Charging right behind him, Qianli Xun yelled, "Tattling to my dad won't save you, Chen Haoyu—eat another fist!"
[ZH] 朱景元解释道：“我当然最关心你，浩宇那伤是你打的，我让他进府上药，无非是希望他别再埋怨你，你正好也跟着进去与娘坐坐。”
[EN] Ducking straight behind Zhu Xiyan's back, Chen Haoyu crowed, "Uncle Zhu's right here! Dare hit me now?!"
[ZH] 千里寻很愿意见向夫人，尽管朱晞颜不允许她再进朱府，可都到家门口，不进去与娘聊两句，好像也说不过去。
[EN] Pulling her punch on purpose, Qianli Xun halted her fist right in front of Zhu Xiyan's chest as Chen Haoyu dodged, pulling back crisp. "Step aside, Father! Today I'm beating Chen Haoyu straight. Until he's whipped into shape, zero chance I marry into the Chen house!"
[ZH] “既如此，我们快进去，我想陪娘多说会儿话。”
[EN] Having weathered decades of official storms, Zhu Xiyan saw straight through two kids' amateur theater without blinking. "Jingyuan, handle them. Father is going in to rest." As gate servants swung the side door open, Zhu Xiyan flicked his sleeve and strolled inside as if nothing had happened.
[ZH] 千里寻正要走，被陈浩宇拉住：“与你娘聊天儿，什么时候都可以，我们还有重要的事情商议，改日再聊。”
[EN] Two whole punches and zero reaction! Deflated, Qianli Xun groaned, "Brother, why'd Dad ignore us?!"
[ZH] 千里寻甩开陈浩宇的手：“别拉着老娘！”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan chuckled. "Blatant stage play put on for Father. Even I spotted it in one glance, let alone Father!"
[ZH] “淑真，又说不文雅的话。”朱景元批评道，转向陈浩宇，“快跟我进去。”
[EN] "Stage play?!" Chen Haoyu yelped aggrieved. "Look at my face, Brother-in-law—does this purple shiner look fake to you?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇心中有事，哪有心情上药：“这么点儿伤不碍事，我与朱淑真有事商议。”
[EN] Having already noted the swelling, Zhu Jingyuan gestured inside. "Come on in and let maids put salve on it."
[ZH] 今日没成功，证明此办法无法撼动朱晞颜。千里寻寻思，与向夫人聊天儿有的是机会，不在乎一时半刻，还是尽快想想接下来该怎么办。
[EN] "Fussing over an outsider and leaving your own little sister hanging!" Qianli Xun pouted.
[ZH] “哥，我改日再找娘聊。”千里寻向陈浩宇使眼色，两人没等朱景元说话，迅速登上各自的马车。
[EN] "Of course you come first! You gave him that bruise; getting him treated keeps him from holding a grudge! Come inside too and sit a spell with Mother."
[ZH] 朱景元自语道：“你们两个还是别瞎忙活，根本没用！”
[EN] Longing to visit Madam Xiang—banned from moving back or not, standing right at the door without saying hello to Mother felt wrong—she brightened. "Fine, let's go! I want to chat with Mother!"
[ZH] 两人来到街市，陈浩宇透过马车的窗户瞧到一家茶肆，探出脑袋，大声喊道：“喂！有家茶肆！进去坐下来商议！”
[EN] Just as she stepped forward, Chen Haoyu caught her arm. "Chat with your mother anytime; we've got urgent business to plot! Next time!"
[ZH] 千里寻本不愿与陈浩宇坐下喝茶，为退婚，勉强答应。
[EN] Yanking her arm free, she snapped, "Keep your hands off me!"
[ZH] 两人跳下马车，走进茶肆，要壶茶。陈浩宇倒主动，提起茶壶，为二人分别倒上一盏。
[EN] "Mind your language, Shuzhen," Zhu Jingyuan chided, turning back to Chen Haoyu. "Come on inside."
[ZH] 千里寻盯着陈浩宇的动作，丝毫不觉得他是懂礼之人：“你出的主意实在烂！一点儿用都没有！”
[EN] With schemes burning in his head, who cared about ointment? "Tiny scrape, nothing to fuss over. I've got urgent business with Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 茶已斟完，陈浩宇放下茶壶：“怎么能怪我头上？我说明日去春香楼，你偏要在朱府门口堵你爹……”
[EN] With today's stunt flopping flat against Zhu Xiyan's iron wall, Qianli Xun figured she could visit Mother anytime; plotting Plan B came first. "Catch Mother another day, Brother!" Trading a quick look with Chen Haoyu, before Zhu Jingyuan could protest, both hopped straight into their waiting carriages.
[ZH] “什么？春香楼？”
[EN] Left shaking his head at the gate, Zhu Jingyuan muttered, "Quit spinning your wheels, you two—total waste of time!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇才意识到口误，将聚香楼说成春香楼：“口误口误，谁让这两个地方名字只有一字之差？”
[EN] Rolling into the market quarter, Chen Haoyu spotted a teahouse out his window and leaned out yelling, "Hey! Teahouse right there! Let's sit and talk!"
[ZH] “差之毫厘，谬以千里。什么叫口误？你这种人满脑子只有春香楼。”
[EN] Loath to share a table with him yet desperate to kill the betrothal, Qianli Xun hopped down and followed him inside. Ordering a pot of tea, Chen Haoyu played gentleman for once, pouring a bowl for each of them.
[ZH] “别光顾着批评我，怎么样？明日再到聚香楼走一趟？”
[EN] Watching him pour without buying his manners for a second, Qianli Xun snapped, "Lousy idea you cooked up—total bust!"
[ZH] “跟今日有什么区别？还不都是当着我爹的面把你揍一顿？”千里寻瞧着陈浩宇脸上的淤青，“老娘倒不介意在你另外半边脸上再来一拳。”
[EN] Setting down the teapot, Chen Haoyu protested, "Blaming me?! I said stage it tomorrow at Chunxiang Brothel; you insisted on ambushing your dad at his gate..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇摸摸淤青之处，感到疼痛，嘴角咧了一下：“区别大了，今日周围没外人，明日都是临安官员，说不定仪王也在，事闹大，你爹面子上挂不住，八成会主动到我爹娘面前要求退婚。”
[EN] "Wait—CHUNXIANG BROTHEL?!"
[ZH] “怎么闹大？”
[EN] Catching his Freudian slip—Chunxiang Brothel instead of Juxiang Tower!—he scrambled. "Slip of the tongue! Both names sound almost identical!"
[ZH] “明日你打我，我向所有官员诉说我的苦楚，包括仪王，他们见我可怜，见你凶悍，肯定会劝说你爹。”
[EN] "Off by a hair, off by a thousand miles! Slip of the tongue my foot; your head is stuffed full of Chunxiang Brothel!"
[ZH] 千里寻露出怀疑的目光：“在场的都是临安官员，我的名声岂不被你搞坏？”
[EN] "Quit lecturing. What do you say—hit Juxiang Tower tomorrow?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇反问道：“你觉得名声被搞坏与嫁给我哪个更让你难以接受？”
[EN] "How's tomorrow any different from today, just decking you in front of my dad again?" Eyeing his bruised cheek, she smirked. "Not that I mind evening out the other side!"
[ZH] 千里寻仔细思忖，她的名声已被流言所坏，再坏些也没什么，只要不嫁给陈浩宇比什么都强。再说明日申简辰肯定在，凭他们的关系，他必然出手相助。
[EN] Touching his tender bruise with a wince, he explained, "Huge difference! Today nobody watched; tomorrow every Lin'an official is packed in there, maybe even Prince Yi! Blow it up big so your dad loses face in front of the whole prefecture, and odds are he marches straight to my parents calling off the wedding!"
[ZH] “成交！明日聚香楼不见不散！”
[EN] "Blow it up how?"
[ZH] 朱淑真爽快答应，陈浩宇当然开心。一开心，竟然生起从未有过的怜悯之情。
[EN] "You whack me, I bawl out my misery to every official and Prince Yi! Seeing pitiful me and fierce you, they'll all push your dad to drop it!"
[ZH] “其实我觉得你也挺可怜，嫁给林觉那小子，被他家暴不说，还闹到和离，又被乔文仙给骗了，好不容易跟我定亲，我又瞧不上你。实在不行，本公子勉为其难把你给娶了，你我之间只有一纸婚书，其他跟婚前一模一样，你过你的，我过我的，只要你别对我动拳头，什么都好商量……”
[EN] Qianli Xun squinted wary. "Every official in town watching—won't that trash my name?"
[ZH] 千里寻用拳头在陈浩宇面前晃晃：“老娘要钱有钱，要貌有貌，要才华有才华，要拳头有拳头，何须你可怜？你要有同情心，就同情同情那些被你欺负的良家妇女。别让老娘再见到你欺负人，见到必然一拳将你打趴下！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shot straight back, "Which sounds worse: a trashed name, or marrying me?!"
[ZH] 朱淑真的拳头在陈浩宇面前晃着，他瞅着粉白的拳头，小心避开，生怕真的打来，实在想不通，朱家的女词人什么时候变得这么厉害。
[EN] Weighing it one beat, she saw the truth: her name was already dragged through rumor mud anyway, and worse could not beat marrying Chen Haoyu! Plus Shen Jianchen would be there tomorrow for sure, and given who they were to each other, he would back her up! "Deal! Tomorrow at Juxiang Tower—don't flake!"
[ZH] 千里寻收起拳头，未喝一口茶，起身离开。
[EN] Thrilled she agreed fast, a bizarre flicker of pity crept into Chen Haoyu. "Truth is, you got a pretty raw deal yourself: wed that Lin Jue guy, beaten into divorce, played by Qiao Wenxian, now stuck engaged to me when I don't even want you! Tell you what—if all fails, I'll take one for the team and wed you in name only! Paper marriage, zero change from single life: you do you, I do me. Long as you keep those fists to yourself, smooth sailing..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇才放心坐正，瞧着朱淑真走出茶肆的背影，不屑道：“哼！张狂个什么劲儿！拳头再厉害还不是被你爹想许给谁就许给谁！”
[EN] Waving a tight fist inches from his nose, Qianli Xun snarled, "I've got cash, looks, talent, and fists—who asked for your pity?! Save your sympathy for every decent girl you harass! Catch you bullying anyone again and I drop you cold!"
[ZH] 回望月轩的路上，千里寻坐在马车里，回想刚才陈浩宇说过的话……
[EN] Flashing back from her pale fist inches away, Chen Haoyu shook his head amazed. When on earth did Zhu Manor's refined poetess turn into a bruiser? Pulling back her fist without touching one drop of tea, Qianli Xun rose and walked out.
[ZH] 当着陈浩宇的面并未在意，此时回想，也不由自顾怜悯起自己来。
[EN] Sitting straight once she cleared the door, Chen Haoyu sneered after her. "Act tough all you want! Iron fists and all, your dad still hands you over to whoever he pleases!"
[ZH] 她仔细思索，若不与林觉和离，也许就没后面的事，在外人看来，应该还算不错。可要是跟林觉继续过下去，她会觉得自己的人生连呼吸的力气都没有。她并不后悔和离，不管后面有多少不幸等待着她，她都会积极应对。
[EN] Riding back to Wangyue Pavilion alone, Chen Haoyu's words echoed back through Qianli Xun's mind. Shrugged off to his face, they stirred a quiet sting of self-pity in the hush of the carriage. Sure, had she never divorced Lin Jue, outsiders might have called her life smooth—yet staying trapped under Lin Jue would have suffocated every breath out of her. She held zero regrets over divorcing; whatever storms waited ahead, she would meet every single one head-on.

#CH 157
[ZH] 每年春节前府衙官员都要找地方聚聚，在饭桌上总结总结一年的功绩，重要的是饭桌上大家的关系会更近一层，便于以后更好地开展工作。
[ZH] 申简辰自然被邀请，接到请帖时，满心不愿到吃吃喝喝的环境中应承，怎奈人在江湖，身不由己。
[ZH] 与千里寻在神柳下相认后，对方还是拒绝，搞得他一不高兴转身离开，离开又十分后悔，为何不与她多聊会儿？
[ZH] 本来盼着她按时来陪梦容，趁机再与她多多相处，可苦荞前来，说自家小姐临时有事，明日才来。
[ZH] “明日”这两个字说起来多容易，可对于期待见到想见之人的申简辰来讲，却十分有力度。
[ZH] 别说他，就连梦容也甚觉失落：“回去告诉淑真，她要是明日再不来，我就去望月轩找她。”
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“我定将郡主的话一字不落说与我家小姐。”
[ZH] 申简辰决定还是去参加聚会，不为别的，只为能从朱晞颜或者他儿子口中听到关于千里寻的信息，哪怕他们只会提到“淑真”这个名讳，他也会觉得心满意足。
[ZH] 苦荞从幽林别居回来，将郡主的话回了。
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“看把梦容急的，明日我办完事，肯定去幽林别居陪她，大不了陪她住三五日。”
[ZH] “小姐只能住两日。”
[ZH] “为何？”
[ZH] “两日后就是春节。”
[ZH] “看我这记性，好吧，两日就两日。”
[ZH] 官员们聚会放在午时，申简辰踩着点儿赶来，既不显得为一顿饭过于积极，也不会让人觉得他身为王爷竟然不守时。
[ZH] 宴席安排在聚香楼一层，只因一层够开放，方便众人交流。里面摆着十几张桌子，每张桌子都坐得满满当当。
[ZH] 当申简辰带白风走进聚香楼时，众位大人迎上来，行礼高呼：“仪王殿下！”
[ZH] 王爷被这么多老家伙拜，白风感到十分荣耀。
[ZH] “诸位在知府大人的带领下，为临安政事操持，十分辛苦，我替官家感谢诸位，大家尽快落座，吃好喝好。”申简辰说着他不喜欢的客套话。
[ZH] 众人不再多礼，各自归座，朱晞颜则将仪王请到最尊贵的位置。
[ZH] 申简辰大略瞧了一下，这一桌坐的全是从五品以上。
[ZH] 接着朱晞颜代大家请仪王讲两句，申简辰拒绝，他不想再说些没有营养的话。
[ZH] 朱晞颜通过仪王的表情和语气判断他并非客气，便不再强求，也更放心大胆当着仪王的面向诸位官员总结府衙一年以来的卓越贡献。
[ZH] 申简辰并未仔细听，但也听到一些内容，无非是夸赞炫耀之辞，即使提到问题，也都无伤大雅，有些问题仔细思索，竟然也可成为功绩之一。
[ZH] 终于讲完，大家开吃，然后有舞伎前来献舞。舞乐声响，大家觥筹交错，个个脸上挂满笑容。
[ZH] 互相敬酒是少不了的，官员们三三两两一起前来敬酒，申简辰应接不暇，还好他酒量够大，否则会被众人喝趴下。
[ZH] 白风一直站在王爷身边，现在的他除拳脚功夫长进以外，最大的变化就是见到美食不再像以前一样流哈喇子，因而无需心猿意马，全部注意力都能放在王爷身上。
[ZH] 大家络绎不绝向王爷敬酒，白风很担心，本想替王爷挡酒，见王爷面不改色，应付自如，便知王爷酒量比他想象的要大得多。
[ZH] 大家敬得差不多时，林觉端着酒盅前来：“仪王，下官敬您，您随意，下官干了。”
[ZH] 申简辰见林觉换上新官服，想起他已被升为统制。之前的统制被朱晞颜随便找个理由同级调走，向申简辰汇报时，他猜到是因林觉查找散播流言之人有功，便点头默认。
[ZH] “恭喜林统制官升一级。”申简辰举起酒杯。
[ZH] “下官定不辜负仪王、知府大人和诸位大人信任，以后会加倍用心做好每件事。”林觉说完一饮而尽。
[ZH] 申简辰也一饮而尽。
[ZH] 正当二人饮完，林觉打算向知府大人敬酒时，陈浩宇闯进来：“救命啊！救命啊！要杀人啦！”
[ZH] 舞乐立刻停下，陈浩宇躲在舞伎身后。
[ZH] 众位官员瞧去，只见朱家千金冲进来：“陈浩宇！你躲得了初一，躲不了十五，陈家若不退婚！我见你一次打一次！”
[ZH] 二人绕着舞伎追赶起来……
[ZH] 见到喜爱之人，申简辰自然欢喜，心里乐道：“没想到你竟想出这种办法，只要是为退婚，我自然乐意帮你一把。”
[ZH] 林觉心里冷笑道：“哼！离开我又能如何？陈家虽比我林家有钱，可陈浩宇哪一点能比得上我？估计你的肠子都该悔青了。”
[ZH] 其他官员面面相觑，表示不理解，然后都看起热闹来。
[ZH] 朱晞颜心里清楚，他们昨日未成功，今日又闹到此处，不过是想逼迫他，他绝不能让两个年轻人得逞。
[ZH] “胡闹！你们两个还不赶紧退下！”
[ZH] 知府大人的声音还是将他们镇住，两人都站住，不再追逐。
[ZH] 陈浩宇哪能随意放过这么好的机会：“朱伯伯，您也看到，朱淑真还没过门儿就这么对待我，要是嫁到陈家，我肯定没有好日子过！”
[EN] Every year before New Year, prefecture officials gathered over a banquet to review the year's achievements and—just as vital—loosen up over wine to grease the wheels for the year ahead. Of course Shen Jianchen received an invitation. Though loath to sit through hollow banquet chatter, holding office meant playing the part.
[ZH] 陈浩宇指着昨日被打得乌青的脸：“诸位大人都瞧瞧，是朱淑真打的，再这样下去，我小命不保……”
[EN] After revealing himself beneath the Divine Willow only to get turned down again, he had stormed off in hurt, then kicked himself all the way back for not lingering to talk longer. Hoping she would show up on schedule to stay with Mengrong so he could spend more time beside her, instead Kuqiao arrived saying Miss had urgent business and would come tomorrow. "Tomorrow" rolled easy off the tongue, yet felt endless to a man aching to see her, leaving Mengrong pouting too. "Tell Shuzhen if she skips tomorrow, I'm marching straight to Wangyue Pavilion!"
[ZH] 令所有人没想到的是，陈浩宇竟然跪下：“朱伯伯，我当着众人的面给您跪下，求您答应我取消朱陈两家婚约，晚辈将感激不尽！”
[EN] "I'll repeat every word to Miss, Princess," Kuqiao promised.
[ZH] 千里寻心里不由赞叹：“陈浩宇干起这种事情来倒十分机灵。”
[EN] So Shen Jianchen decided to attend the officials' banquet after all, if only to catch stray mentions of Qianli Xun from Zhu Xiyan or his son; even hearing the name "Shuzhen" would warm his day.
[ZH] 她也跟着跪下：“爹，陈浩宇不学无术，是青楼和赌坊的常客，还欺负良家妇女，这样的人女儿就是当一辈子老姑娘也不愿嫁！”
[EN] Back at Wangyue Pavilion, Kuqiao relayed Princess Jiahe's warning. Qianli Xun laughed. "Look how impatient Mengrong gets! Soon as tomorrow's stunt wraps, I'm heading straight to Youlin Retreat—stay three or five days if she wants!"
[ZH] 瞅到周围官员看热闹的样子，朱晞颜气不打一处来，还是努力忍住：“此乃家事，你们两个先回去，等宴饮结束再说。景元！”
[EN] "Only two days, Miss."
[ZH] 朱景元知道父亲的意思，从人群中走出，来到二人跟前：“你们两个快起来，跟我回去。”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] 两人不情愿，朱景元先去扶自家妹妹：“淑真，你是姑娘家，更要注意形象，赶紧起来。”
[EN] "New Year's Day is in two days!"
[ZH] 千里寻还是不愿意，向申简辰投去求助的目光。
[EN] "What a memory I've got! Fine, two days it is!"
[ZH] 申简辰接收到目光，见到心中之人本就开心，帮她义不容辞。
[EN] With the banquet set for noon, Shen Jianchen arrived right on the dot, neither looking too eager for free food nor showing up rude and late. Hosted across Juxiang Tower's open ground floor to let guests mingle easy, over a dozen round tables sat packed brim to brim. The instant Shen Jianchen stepped inside with Bai Feng, every official rose bowing in unison: "Greetings, Prince Yi!" Watching gray-bearded bigwigs bow low to his master swelled Bai Feng's chest with pride.
[ZH] “知府大人，不如将二位带到楼上的雅间，问清楚缘由，再决定如何处理。”
[EN] "Under Prefect Zhu's leadership, you gentlemen have toiled all year for Lin'an's welfare," Shen Jianchen delivered the polite lines he hated. "On His Majesty's behalf, my thanks. Take your seats and feast well!"
[ZH] 仪王主动发话，朱晞颜当然得听：“下官遵命。”
[EN] Returning to their tables, everyone watched Zhu Xiyan escort Prince Yi to the head table, reserved for senior officials of vice-fifth rank and above. Invited by Zhu Xiyan to offer opening remarks, Shen Jianchen declined flat, refusing to spout hot air. Reading genuine reluctance in his tone, Zhu Xiyan did not push, stepping forward himself to summarize the prefecture's sterling record before Prince Yi. Half tuning out, Shen Jianchen caught the drift: glowing self-praise where even minor hiccups somehow got spun into triumphs.
[ZH] 千里寻主动起身；陈浩宇见朱淑真不再跪着，也站起来。
[EN] Speeches done at last, platters rolled out and dancing girls took the floor to bright music as cups clinked and smiles beamed around the hall. Wave after wave of officials stepped up in twos and threes to toast Prince Yi, keeping him busy. Good thing his liquor tolerance ran deep! Standing watchful beside his master—martial skills sharper by the month and no longer drooling over gourmet dishes—Bai Feng kept sharp focus on Prince Yi, itching to block toasts until he noticed His Highness did not even flush.
[ZH] 二人跟着朱晞颜上二楼雅间，朱景元自然也跟上去。
[EN] As toasts tapered off, Lin Jue stepped up holding his cup. "Your Highness, this humble officer toasts you. Sip at your ease; I drink mine dry!" Noticing Lin Jue's fresh uniform, Shen Jianchen recalled his promotion to Division Commander: Zhu Xiyan had shifted the former commander sideways on a tidy pretext after Lin Jue cracked the rumor case, and Shen Jianchen had nodded off on it.
[ZH] 音乐响起，舞伎再次舞动起来，一切恢复正常。官员们除议论议论刚才的事，继续饮酒攀谈，拉关系。
[EN] "Congratulations on your promotion, Division Commander Lin," Shen Jianchen said, raising his cup.
[ZH] 申简辰坐了一会儿，再也坐不住，起身也向二楼雅间而去，白风紧跟上王爷。
[EN] "This officer will never fail the trust of Your Highness, Prefect Zhu, and fellow lords. I'll double my efforts on every duty!" Down went Lin Jue's cup, Shen Jianchen draining his in return.
[ZH] 众官员瞅着仪王向楼上走去，心中疑惑，都悄悄议论着。
[EN] Right as Lin Jue turned to toast Prefect Zhu, Chen Haoyu burst through the front doors screaming at the top of his lungs: "Help! Help! Murder!!"
[ZH] 林觉只觉这位王爷不喜欢操心临安军政，倒喜欢管朱家的家事，实在令人费解。
[EN] Music cut off dead as Chen Haoyu ducked behind the startled dancers. Every official spun round to see Miss Zhu charge straight through the entrance yelling, "Hide today, can't hide forever, Chen Haoyu! Till the Chens cancel this betrothal, I beat you every time I lay eyes on you!" Round and round the dancers they went in full chase!
[ZH] 来到二楼的一个雅间，门上写着一句诗“葡萄美酒夜光杯”，申简辰示意白风推开门。
[EN] Delight sparked in Shen Jianchen at seeing her. What a wild scheme! Anything to kill that betrothal, count him in! Meanwhile Lin Jue smirked cold inside: Left me for this?! Richer house or not, Chen Haoyu can't hold a candle to me; bet you're kicking yourself raw! Around the room, baffled officials traded wide eyes before leaning back to enjoy free theater.
[ZH] 白风上前将门推开，朱晞颜正在怒批二人：“今日官员都在，你们这么闹，让我的脸往哪里搁？……”
[EN] Knowing exact what these two pulled—flopping yesterday, now hauling their circus right here to force his hand—Zhu Xiyan refused to budge. "Outrageous! Halt at once and get out!"
[ZH] 想要继续批评时，见仪王进来，赶紧起身拜见：“仪王怎么也上来？”
[EN] The Prefect's thunder froze both mid-step. Seizing his cue, Chen Haoyu cried, "Look for yourself, Uncle Zhu! Not even married yet and Zhu Shuzhen beats me like this; marry her into the Chen house and my life is over!" Pointing straight at yesterday's purple bruise, he shouted, "Look, esteemed lords! Zhu Shuzhen gave me this! Keep this up and she'll kill me..." Dropping straight onto both knees before the whole room, he wailed, "Uncle Zhu, right here before every lord I beg you on my knees: cancel the Zhu-Chen betrothal, and I'll owe you forever!"
[ZH] “我最喜多管闲事，故而上来瞧瞧。”
[EN] Sharp mover, Qianli Xun cheered inside, dropping right onto her own knees beside him. "Father! Chen Haoyu is an idle loafer who haunts brothels and gambling dens and preys on respectable girls! I'd sooner stay an old maid for life than marry a man like him!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜想起上次仪王在他面前说过的话，那时他怀疑仪王对自家姑娘有意思，现在看来多半如此。
[EN] Steaming under every amused official gaze, Zhu Xiyan clamped his rage down tight. "Domestic business! Leave at once; we settle this after the banquet! Jingyuan!"
[ZH] “朱家的家事竟然惊动仪王，是下官疏于管教，还请仪王莫要怪罪。”
[EN] Stepping out of the crowd at once, Zhu Jingyuan hurried over. "Get up, both of you, come home with me!" Reaching for his sister first, he urged, "Mind your image, Shuzhen. Stand up!"
[ZH] “我为何要怪罪知府大人？”申简辰挑椅子坐下，“刚才我大概听明白，他们二人前来不过是对两家的亲事不满，既然不满，又何必硬把他们拴在一起？”
[EN] Refusing to budge, Qianli Xun shot an urgent SOS glance straight at Shen Jianchen.
[ZH] 千里寻对申简辰投去赞赏的目光，有仪王帮忙，说不定退婚之事能成。
[EN] Catching her eye—thrilled just seeing her, eager to jump in—Shen Jianchen spoke smooth. "Prefect Zhu, why not take these two up to an upstairs private room, hear their side proper, and decide?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜立刻跪下，朱景元也跟着跪下。
[EN] With Prince Yi speaking, Zhu Xiyan bowed crisp. "As Your Highness commands."
[ZH] 只听朱晞颜说：“请王爷不要插手下官的家事。……”
[EN] Qianli Xun hopped up at once, Chen Haoyu scrambling up right behind her as both trailed Zhu Xiyan and Zhu Jingyuan upstairs. Music struck back up, dancers resumed twirling, and officials returned to drinks and hushed gossip.
[ZH] 千里寻没想到父亲会来这招；陈浩宇也觉准岳父实在老奸巨猾，棋高一着。
[EN] Unable to sit still a minute longer, Shen Jianchen rose and headed upstairs too, Bai Feng right on his heels. Watching Prince Yi leave stirred quiet whispers around the tables, Lin Jue frowning puzzled why a prince uninterested in military administration poked so hard into Zhu family drama.
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续说：“淑真年纪不小，许多像她这个年纪的姑娘已儿女双全，何况她还离过婚？名声之事仪王也尽知，又被乔家所欺，陈家愿意结亲已是不错……”
[EN] Reaching an upstairs room labeled "Fine Grape Wine in Luminous Jade Cups," Shen Jianchen nodded to Bai Feng to push open the door. Inside, Zhu Xiyan was tearing into both culprits. "Every official in town downstairs and you pull a stunt like this—where do I put my face?!..." Cutting short as Prince Yi stepped in, he rose bowing. "What brings Your Highness upstairs?"
[ZH] “知府大人不必担忧，这桩婚事没了，朱家姑娘会有更好的……”
[EN] "Born busybody; thought I'd check in," Shen Jianchen answered breezy.
[ZH] 仪王话中有话，朱晞颜又怎能听不出？还是坚持道：“下官不敢奢望更好的，毕竟淑真名声在外，再说陈浩宇也该承担起他应当承担的责任，还望仪王应允！”
[EN] Recalling Prince Yi's loaded hints once before, Zhu Xiyan felt his old hunch lock in: Prince Yi really did fancy his daughter! "Troubling Your Highness with private family mess—my failure in discipline. Forgive this humble official!"
[ZH] 千里寻纳闷，为何父亲要说“陈浩宇也该承担起他应当承担的责任”？
[EN] "Why blame you, Prefect Zhu?" Taking a chair smooth, Shen Jianchen went on, "Caught the gist downstairs: neither of them wants this match. Why force two unwilling kids together?"
[ZH] 申简辰被噎得半天说不出话，他站起来，走到千里寻身边：“幽栖居士，可否与我到隔壁雅间一叙？”
[EN] Qianli Xun flashed Shen Jianchen a grateful look; with Prince Yi leaning in, maybe this match would die today!
[ZH] 千里寻自然同意：“仪王邀请，淑真没有拒绝的道理。”
[EN] Without blinking, Zhu Xiyan dropped straight onto both knees, Zhu Jingyuan dropping right beside him. "Your Highness, I beg you not to intervene in this humble official's family affairs..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一脸疑惑，目送二人出去，他想不通，为何仪王会邀请朱淑真到隔壁雅间？
[EN] Whoa—Qianli Xun had not seen that coming, while Chen Haoyu marveled to himself at his slick future father-in-law outfoxing a prince!
[ZH] 白风很懂事，负责乖乖守在雅间外。
[EN] "Shuzhen is no longer young," Zhu Xiyan pressed steady. "Half the women her age already raise sons and daughters. Worse, she is divorced, her reputation scarred as Your Highness knows well, and jilted by the Qiaos! Having the Chen house still willing to wed her is fortune enough..."
[ZH] 一进门，申简辰立刻说：“你也看到，知府大人态度强硬，不如今日答应跟我在一起，我好以此为理由要求两家退婚。”
[EN] "No need to worry, Prefect Zhu. Drop this match, and Miss Zhu has far brighter prospects waiting..."
[ZH] 千里寻毫不犹豫：“我绝不会做令梦容难过之事，既然仪王的身份也帮不了我，我再想别的办法。”
[EN] Catching every hidden barb yet standing iron-firm, Zhu Xiyan answered, "I dare not hope higher given Shuzhen's notoriety! Besides, Chen Haoyu owes her the responsibility he brought on himself. Grant this humble father's plea, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 申简辰很想不管不顾当着众人的面承认他喜欢朱家姑娘，不！是千里寻，可他又不愿违背千里寻的意愿。
[EN] Wait—why did Dad say Chen Haoyu "owes her responsibility"? Qianli Xun blinked confused.
[ZH] ﻿沉默半晌，艰难说出：“好，你既如此想，我便不再强求。”
[EN] Stopped cold by Zhu Xiyan's kneel, Shen Jianchen rose and stepped beside Qianli Xun. "Might Youqi Jushi step into the next room with me for a private word?"
[ZH] 申简辰走出雅间，并未进“葡萄美酒夜光杯”，不进，便已表明态度。
[EN] "Honored to oblige, Your Highness," Qianli Xun agreed at once. Watching them walk out left Chen Haoyu baffled: why pull Zhu Shuzhen aside alone? Outside the adjoining room, discreet Bai Feng took watch by the door.
[ZH] 仪王去隔壁时，朱晞颜与儿子朱景元已起身。朱晞颜猜到仪王对淑真的心思，担心他们在隔壁会有亲密的举动，见女儿进来，担忧地问：“仪王呢？”
[EN] The instant the door clicked shut, Shen Jianchen plunged in. "You saw how stubborn your dad is! Say yes to being with me right now, and I march back in calling off their wedding!"
[ZH] “下楼了，他以后不会再管朱家的事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun did not hesitate a split second. "I'm never doing anything that breaks Mengrong's heart! If even a prince's rank can't budge him, I'll find another way!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜终于放心，按说女儿能跟仪王在一起再好不过，可谁让朱陈两家之事在先，最好不要再有意外发生，他实在折腾不起。
[EN] Every urge screamed at Shen Jianchen to throw caution away and proclaim his love for Qianli Xun before the whole world, yet he refused to trample her wish. Standing silent a heavy stretch, he forced out quiet, "Fine. If that's your stand, I won't push." Stepping out of the room, he bypassed "Fine Grape Wine in Luminous Jade Cups" straight for the stairs, his silent answer plain.
[ZH] 朱晞颜指着女儿及未来的女婿：“告诉你们，趁早断了退婚的心思，无论你们做什么，我都不会让步！”
[EN] Already back on their feet inside, anxious over what might happen next door, Zhu Xiyan exhaled sharp when his daughter walked back alone. "Where's Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 说完甩袖走下一楼，他发现仪王已离开。不在也好，没有仪王在，大家才能畅所欲言。
[EN] "Headed downstairs. Done meddling in Zhu business."
[ZH] “你们两个在这里好好想想，不要再异想天开。”朱景元批评完，下楼继续陪父亲。
[EN] Relief flooded Zhu Xiyan. Sure, marrying an imperial prince sounded grand on paper, yet with the Chen match sealed first, fresh upheaval was the last risk he could afford. Pointing sharp at daughter and future son-in-law alike, he declared, "Hear me clear: bury every dream of canceling this wedding! No stunt you pull will move me one inch!" Flicking his sleeve, he marched downstairs, pleased to find Prince Yi already departed so everyone could drink free.
[ZH] 有什么好想的，还得继续努力。千里寻说：“喂！靠你了，回家让你奶奶认真耗着。”
[EN] "Sit here and cool your heads. Quit chasing fantasies!" Zhu Jingyuan scolded before following his father down.
[ZH] “还用你说？”陈浩宇露出神秘的表情，“你刚跟仪王在隔壁说什么？他该不会是看上你了？”
[EN] Nothing to cool down over—keep fighting! Turning to Chen Haoyu, Qianli Xun prodded, "Your turn now. Go home and make sure your grandma keeps stalling hard!"
[ZH] “狗嘴里吐不出象牙！再胡说，小心老娘揍你！”千里寻亮了亮拳头，走出雅间。
[EN] "Need you tell me?" Leaning in with a sly grin, Chen Haoyu poked, "What'd Prince Yi whisper to you next door? Tell me he isn't sweet on you!"
[ZH] 到一楼时，父亲正满面春光应对各位官员，已忘却刚才的不快。也对，酒桌上大家巴结他的样子足以挽回刚才丢失的面子。
[EN] "Filthy mouth! Say one more word and catch these fists!" Flashing a clenched fist, Qianli Xun marched out the door. Down on the ground floor, her father beamed bright greeting flattering officials, earlier anger already forgotten; nothing patched wounded pride faster than wine-table applause.

#CH 158
[ZH] 接下来的冬日过得倒也平静。
[ZH] 千里寻去过几次幽林别居，只为陪伴梦容，互相解闷儿。遇到仪王，相处起来与往日大不相同，起码从内心来讲，已没有距离感，他们之间的聊天儿如同老朋友一般，事实上本就是老朋友。
[ZH] 过春节那日，朱晞颜竟然破天荒地派人将女儿叫回朱府。千里寻没有拒绝，乖乖回去。回去后避免提到退婚之事，她不想惹得父亲不高兴，破坏新年气氛，一切等年后再说。
[ZH] 再没见过文渊与苗知瑜，也许是他们有意避开，也许是命运安排大家最好不见，无论如何，他们出现或不出现，对千里寻来讲都已经无所谓。
[ZH] 开始写新话本，还要训练十名下人练习千里拳，千里寻忙得不亦乐乎。
[ZH] 叫红豆的丫鬟已练得一身拳法，千里寻有意将她带在身边。出门时，身边带着三个丫鬟，实在有面儿。
[ZH] 正月十五以后，冷过一段时日，天气开始回暖。
[ZH] 当枯草焕发生机，神柳的枝条上冒出新芽时，乔家竟然派人送来请帖。
[ZH] 千里寻接过打开一看，原来乔家要搞捶丸比赛，邀请她参加。
[ZH] 正当犹豫之际，嫂嫂陈文心来到望月轩，没有让下人禀报，自顾进入。在下人的眼中，陈文心是家人，家人无须禀报。
[ZH] 陈文心一进屋便问：“淑真，你可收到乔家的请帖？”
[ZH] 紧接着看到淑真手中正拿着请帖：“看来你已收到，乔家真是过分，给公爹和婆婆送也就罢了，还给你送，是几个意思？千万不要去！”
[ZH] 千里寻将请帖放在桌上，迎上去：“嫂嫂难道是为此事而来？”
[ZH] “那还用说？婆婆知道乔家邀请众人去捶丸，担心也邀请你，让我过来瞧瞧。”
[ZH] “嫂嫂坐下说。”
[ZH] 千里寻拉陈文心在椅子上坐下，吩咐苦荞做两盏茶。
[ZH] 陈文心坐下，继续拉开话匣子：“乔老爷的夫人爱捶丸，隔上一两年搞那么一次，烦都烦死，要不是因为他们家是皇亲国戚，又租用陈家的捶丸场地，我才懒得去！”
[ZH] 千里寻故意开玩笑：“我猜嫂嫂肯定是因乔家租用陈家的场地，担心乔家把场地给搞坏，才跑去监督。”
[ZH] “你嫂嫂我有那么闲吗？以前没嫁到朱家，是代表陈家去，现在嫁到朱家，公爹婆婆都不去，派我和你哥去，没办法，只能走一趟。我虽不喜欢乔家，面儿上的功夫还是不能少。”自从乔家伤害了淑真，陈文心便对乔家更加不满。
[ZH] 千里寻开始犹豫要不要去。
[ZH] 去吧，不想面对乔家人以及文渊和苗知瑜，尽管已见过他们，也检验过面对他们的勇气，但在千里寻看来，能不见还是不要再见。
[ZH] 不去吧，又担心别人觉得她故意逃避，反而更显得她还未放下与文渊的感情。
[ZH] 陈文心见淑真不说话又问道：“你是怎么想的？去还是不去？”
[ZH] 没等千里寻回答，韦嬷嬷正好进来：“为何不去？我想问姑娘，若是别家邀请，你会不会去？”
[ZH] 穿越前，千里寻打过几次高尔夫球，因千里拳功力影响，学得很快，而且打得不错。
[ZH] 后来写网络小说因情节需要，她专门了解过古代的捶丸，发现两者之间很相似，既然有机会实战，何必想那么多。
[ZH] “当然去，去了可以好好玩玩儿。可现在是乔家邀请，我……”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷坐下：“依我看，乔家与别家没有什么区别，再说姑娘已有新的婚约，没必要再纠结。”
[ZH] 嬷嬷的话简单明了，千里寻不再犹豫：“嬷嬷说的有道理，乔家既然敢送来请帖，我便敢去。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷赞道：“这就对了！”
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“呵呵，我看婆婆的担心都是多余，有韦嬷嬷在，这种小事无需我们操心，三言两语便解决。”
[EN] The rest of winter rolled by in calm peace. Qianli Xun visited Youlin Retreat several times to keep Mengrong company and chase off winter boredom. Crossing paths with Prince Yi felt nothing like before: zero awkward walls, bantering easy like the old friends they truly were.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷提到与陈浩宇的婚约，千里寻又高兴不起来，嫂嫂在此，她不好说什么。上次跟陈浩宇闹过后，一直未见过他，也不知陈家老太太现在是否还想耗着？
[EN] On New Year's Day, Zhu Xiyan broke his own silence and sent word summoning his daughter home to Zhu Manor. Qianli Xun went without fuss, steering clear of any mention of canceling the betrothal so she would not sour the holiday; plenty of time to fight after New Year! Neither Wenyuan nor Miao Zhiyu crossed her path once. Whether they avoided her on purpose or fate kept their orbits apart, Qianli Xun could not care less anymore. Between drafting a brand-new huaben and drilling ten household servants in Qianli Fist, her days stayed packed and lively. A maid named Hongdou picked up Qianli Fist so sharp that Qianli Xun promoted her to personal attendant; stepping out flanked by three maids felt pretty grand.
[ZH] “嫂嫂，陈家谁会去？”
[EN] After the Lantern Festival came one last cold snap before spring warmth crept back. Just as pale grass turned green and fresh buds broke along the Divine Willow's boughs, a messenger arrived from Qiao Manor bearing an invitation card. Opening it, Qianli Xun blinked: the Qiaos were hosting a tournament of chuiwan, a Song-dynasty lawn game played with clubs and balls, and inviting her!
[ZH] “怎么？难道想浩宇了？”陈文心故意开着玩笑。
[EN] Mid-hesitation, Sister-in-law Chen Wenxin walked straight into Wangyue Pavilion unannounced; servants treated her as family. Past the threshold, she blurted, "Shuzhen, did the Qiaos send you an invitation too?" Spotting the card right in Shuzhen's hand, she bristled. "They did! The nerve of those Qiaos—inviting Father-in-law and Mother-in-law is bad enough, dragging you in too?! Whatever you do, don't go!"
[ZH] “嫂嫂别瞎说，我只是找陈浩宇有事要问。”
[EN] Setting the card down, Qianli Xun stepped up smiling. "Came all this way just over this, Sister-in-law?"
[ZH] “放心，定是浩宇与我的两位堂弟去。陈家的捶丸场地，不过是陈家的生意而已，我爹娘从不喜欢捶丸，肯定不去。别的长辈见我爹娘不去，也都不会去。”
[EN] "Need you ask? Mother-in-law heard the Qiaos were throwing a chuiwan match, feared they'd invite you, and sent me straight over!"
[ZH] 姑娘与陈家公子定亲，韦嬷嬷一直未发表过任何意见，也从未与姑娘畅聊过。当她听说此事时，便知姑娘定然不满，可她也理解朱晞颜的不易。
[EN] "Sit down and talk." Leading Chen Wenxin to a chair, she told Kuqiao to whisk two bowls of tea.
[ZH] 朱家老爷顾着家族的面子，岂能让朱家的姑娘永远嫁不出去被人笑话？韦嬷嬷两边都理解，两边则都不说什么。
[EN] Settling in, Chen Wenxin rattled on. "Madam Qiao adores chuiwan and throws a match every year or two—drives me crazy! If they weren't imperial kin renting the Chen family's chuiwan grounds, I wouldn't set foot near it!"
[ZH] 苦荞又多做一盏茶，为三人奉上。韦嬷嬷端起一盏，品完一口，问道：“夏果去了哪里？该不会又在丫鬟房读书？”
[EN] Qianli Xun teased, "Bet you're only going to guard Chen family turf so the Qiaos don't wreck the lawn!"
[ZH] 苦荞不敢言语。
[EN] "Think I have that much spare time?! Before marrying into the Zhu house I went for the Chens; now Father-in-law and Mother-in-law skip out and dump it on your brother and me! Can't stand the Qiaos, yet appearances still matter." Ever since the Qiaos jilted Shuzhen, Chen Wenxin's disgust ran twice as deep.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“是我让她读的，这丫头挺聪明，多读书，没什么不好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun weighed both sides. Go, and she would face Qiao elders plus Wenyuan and Miao Zhiyu; even knowing she could keep her cool, skipping awkward reunions sounded nicer. Skip it, though, and folks might whisper she was hiding out still heartbroken over Wenyuan.
[ZH] 陈文心先批评道：“淑真，这就是你的不对，怎么能把自个丫鬟当主子供着？夏果既是你的贴身丫鬟，就应该每日像苦荞一样在屋里伺候你。”
[EN] "Well? What's your call—going or skipping?" Chen Wenxin pressed.
[ZH] “少夫人说得没错。”韦嬷嬷放下手中的茶盏，“夏果因自己长相好，早已不安分，姑娘再让她读些书，知道得多，会更加不安分，哪有心思伺候主子？”
[EN] Before Qianli Xun answered, Matron Wei walked in speaking crisp. "Why skip? Ask yourself, young lady: if any other house invited you, would you go?"
[ZH] 千里寻虽已观察出夏果的不安分，可她并不觉得有什么错：“嬷嬷关心我，我打心里感激，至于夏果，您就别再理会她，还是想想要不要陪我去捶丸。”
[EN] Back in her old world, Qianli Xun had played golf a few times; thanks to Qianli Fist coordination, she picked it up fast and hit crisp. Researching ancient chuiwan later for a web novel, she had marveled how close it mirrored golf, and getting to play live sounded like a blast. "Course I'd go—fun game! Only because it's the Qiaos..."
[ZH] 姑娘如此说，韦嬷嬷不再与夏果计较：“姑娘都不计较，我还理会个什么劲儿。捶丸那日，我当然要陪姑娘去，我虽不会捶丸，却可代表望月轩与各家的夫人们聊聊，拉拉关系。”
[EN] Taking a seat, Matron Wei settled it in one line. "The Qiaos are no different from any other house! Besides, you have a new betrothal now; nothing left to fret over!"
[ZH] 陈文心一脸羡慕：“真希望我身边也有个像韦嬷嬷一样的老人儿，走到哪里都让人踏实。”
[EN] Crisp and clear! Hesitation gone, Qianli Xun grinned. "Matron's right! If the Qiaos have the nerve to send a card, I've got the nerve to show up!"
[ZH] “少夫人可别羡慕，您是福窝里长大的，生得好嫁得好。哪像姑娘，她生得虽好，感情婚姻之路却……”韦嬷嬷不觉伤感起来。
[EN] "That's the spirit!" Matron Wei approved.
[ZH] 千里寻被韦嬷嬷感染，也伤感起来，不想言语。
[EN] Chen Wenxin chuckled. "Mother-in-law fretted for nothing! With Matron Wei here, two sentences clear any headache!"
[ZH] 陈文心装作生气的样子提醒：“嬷嬷这么说，我可生气了，淑真已与浩宇定亲，难道我们陈家不好？难道淑真嫁到陈家不会享福？”
[EN] Hearing mention of the Chen betrothal dimmed Qianli Xun's smile again, yet with Sister-in-law right here, she held her tongue. She had not seen Chen Haoyu since their Juxiang Tower flop; she wondered if Old Madam Xia was still stalling. "Who's going from the Chen house, Sister-in-law?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷了解姑娘的心思，知道她不想嫁到陈家，装作刚刚想起的样子：“看我这脑子，只想以前不顺心的事，忘了朱陈两家亲上加亲，希望姑娘嫁到陈家一切能好起来。”
[EN] "Ooh, missing Haoyu already?" Chen Wenxin teased.
[ZH] “那还用说？淑真，你说呢？”陈文心瞧向淑真，眼中充满询问。
[EN] "Quit teasing, Sister-in-law! I just need to ask him something."
[ZH] 千里寻不想让嫂嫂失望，可让她说出肯定的话，又难以启齿，只好端起茶盏品起茶来，想到转移话题这招，才放下茶盏：“嫂嫂的捶丸技术如何？”
[EN] "Relax, Haoyu and my two male cousins will be there. That chuiwan field is just Chen business; my parents hate chuiwan and never attend, and none of the other elders go without them."
[ZH] 淑真未接话，陈文心猜到她还是不愿嫁给浩宇，也不好追问下去：“还行，不过比你强。”
[EN] Matron Wei had stayed silent on Shuzhen's Chen betrothal until now, knowing full well her young lady hated it while also seeing Prefect Zhu's bind: guarding family honor meant refusing to leave his daughter unmarried as a laughingstock. Understanding both sides, Matron Wei kept quiet.
[ZH] “嫂嫂可与我比过捶丸？”
[EN] Kuqiao served three fresh bowls of whisked tea. Taking a sip, Matron Wei asked, "Where's Xiaguo? Hiding in the maids' quarters reading again?"
[ZH] “那倒没有，你以前很少捶丸，偶尔玩过一次，被我看到，技术可不怎么样。”
[EN] Kuqiao kept mum. Qianli Xun answered easy, "My idea. Bright girl—no harm reading."
[ZH] 嫂嫂说的是以前的朱淑真，现在的朱淑真并非如此：“嫂嫂有所不知，现在的我捶丸技术精进不少，到时你我可得好好比比。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin jumped in scolding. "That's on you, Shuzhen! Pampering a maid like a young lady—she's your personal maid and belongs right here waiting on you like Kuqiao!"
[ZH] “那是自然。”陈文心端起茶盏，一口气喝完，“行了，话已说完，茶也喝过，该走了，婆婆在家还等着我回话。”
[EN] "Young Madam speaks truth!" Matron Wei set down her tea bowl. "A pretty face already left Xiaguo restless; stuffing her head with books only makes her twice as ambitious, with zero heart left for serving!"
[ZH] 千里寻与韦嬷嬷将陈文心送至门口，目送她乘马车离开。
[EN] Noticing Xiaguo's ambition herself yet seeing zero sin in wanting more out of life, Qianli Xun waved it off. "Appreciate you looking out for me, Matron, yet let Xiaguo be. More important, coming with me to the chuiwan match?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷主动邀请：“姑娘，去我屋中坐会儿？”
[EN] Seeing her mistress drop it, Matron Wei shrugged. "If you don't mind, why should I fret? Course I'm coming! I won't swing a club myself, yet representing Wangyue Pavilion to mingle with the madams keeps doors open!"
[ZH] 千里寻猜到嬷嬷应该有话想与她说：“嬷嬷请我，岂能不去？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin sighed envious. "Wish I had a rock like Matron Wei by my side; puts your mind at ease everywhere you go!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷吩咐苦荞不必陪着。
[EN] "Don't envy us, Young Madam. You grew up wrapped in blessings, born lucky and wed lucky! Unlike our young lady: lovely as a flower, yet her road in marriage and love..." Emotion caught in Matron Wei's throat, casting quiet gloom over Qianli Xun too.
[ZH] 苦荞明白，嬷嬷不想别人在一旁打扰，应该是想与小姐说些不愿被别人听到的话。
[EN] Feigning mock anger to lift the room, Chen Wenxin cut in, "Hey, Matron, watch out or I get mad! Shuzhen's engaged to Haoyu—calling our Chen house bad luck?! Won't Shuzhen live like a queen with us?"
[ZH] 来到韦嬷嬷房中，虽是春季，天气回暖，韦嬷嬷还是怕冷，依然燃着火盆，只是放的炭火没有冬季那么多。
[EN] Knowing her young lady's real dread yet playing smooth, Matron Wei tapped her brow. "Look at my memory, stuck brooding over past storms and forgetting our two houses sealing double kinship! Praying everything blooms bright once our young lady marries into the Chens!"
[ZH] 两人坐在火盆旁，火盆上架着壶，里面正煮着茶。身边没有丫鬟伺候，韦嬷嬷亲自动手伺候姑娘。
[EN] "Need you ask? Right, Shuzhen?" Chen Wenxin looked over hopeful.
[ZH] 壶中冒出热气，韦嬷嬷提起壶，给姑娘和自己各倒一盏：“老吃苦荞做的茶，姑娘也尝尝我煮的毛尖茶。”
[EN] Hating to crush her sister-in-law yet unable to force a fake yes, Qianli Xun took a slow sip of tea before changing gears. "How's your chuiwan game, Sister-in-law?"
[ZH] 千里寻端起茶盏，品了一口：“不错，茶味芬芳，好像有股熟悉的味道，是什么……”
[EN] Catching the dodge—plain she was still unwilling—Chen Wenxin did not push. "Decent; beats yours anyway!"
[ZH] “熟栗子香。”韦嬷嬷笑着也品了一口。
[EN] "When'd we ever play?"
[ZH] “对！是这个味道。”
[EN] "Never played you. You used to hate chuiwan; I caught you swinging once ages ago, looked pretty rough!"
[ZH] 千里寻想起小时候，父亲给她第一次买熟栗子的情景……
[EN] "That was the old me! I improved tons lately—wait till we match up on the lawn!"
[ZH] 那时，有家人在旁，吃每一颗栗子，都觉得无比甜美。
[EN] "You're on!" Draining her bowl clean, Chen Wenxin stood. "Message delivered, tea drunk. I'm off home before Mother-in-law paces a hole in the floor!"
[ZH] 长大后，她赚了钱，每年都买，可每次吃到嘴里，再也吃不出童年时的记忆，倒是这毛尖茶的味道让她的记忆回到穿越前的童年时期。
[EN] After seeing Chen Wenxin off to her carriage, Matron Wei invited, "Step into my room for a spell, young lady?"
[ZH] “姑娘在想什么？”
[EN] Sensing a heart-to-heart ahead, Qianli Xun smiled. "Lead the way, Matron!" Matron Wei signaled Kuqiao to stay back; understanding private talk awaited, Kuqiao stayed put.
[ZH] “没想什么？嬷嬷把我叫进来，应该不只是为品茶。”
[EN] Even with spring warming outdoors, chill-prone Matron Wei still kept a low charcoal brazier glowing with a kettle steeping tea overhead, pouring two bowls herself without calling servants. "Sick of Kuqiao's whisked tea—try my steeped Maojian tea!"
[ZH] “自从朱陈两家亲上加亲，我还没与姑娘好好聊过，不如今日说说此事。”韦嬷嬷在姑娘面前没有必要藏着掖着，开门见山才能体现她们之间亲密的关系。
[EN] Taking a sip, Qianli Xun blinked. "Delicious—fragrant with something super familiar... what is that?"
[ZH] 千里寻放下茶盏，其实她早想与韦嬷嬷聊聊，担心嬷嬷会像家人一样对陈家满意，故而一直未提起。今日既然提到，她根本不想隐瞒真实想法。
[EN] "Roasted chestnuts," Matron Wei smiled, sipping hers.
[ZH] “嬷嬷，我不想嫁给陈浩宇，他是什么样的人，嬷嬷也领教过，也知道我与他不合，你帮我出出主意，好让陈家退了这门亲事。”
[EN] "Yes! Roasted chestnuts!" An instant memory flashed back to childhood: her father buying her very first bag of hot roasted chestnuts, every kernel tasting pure magic surrounded by family. Buying them as an adult after earning her own money never tasted the same, yet one sip of Song Maojian tea carried her straight back to childhood.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷无比心疼自家姑娘，嫁给林家前有喜欢的人，却因徐家搬走，感情无疾而终。后又和离，好不容易跟乔家公子有情，又遇流言蜚语，好好的亲事被破坏，又偏偏要嫁给一个连林觉都比不上的人。
[EN] "Lost in thought, young lady?"
[ZH] “姑娘有没有想过，如果退不了婚，必须嫁到陈家，你该如何？”
[EN] "Nothing. Guess you didn't invite me in just for tea."
[ZH] “没想过，我只想以后不涉足感情和婚姻，就住在望月轩，有嬷嬷和苦荞陪伴，也挺好。”
[EN] "Since the Zhu-Chen betrothal landed, we haven't had one real talk. Let's speak open today." Nothing stayed hidden between them; straight talk proved their bond.
[ZH] “怎么不提夏果？”
[EN] Setting down her bowl, Qianli Xun spoke plain; she had held back earlier fearing Matron Wei favored the Chens too. "I don't want to marry Chen Haoyu, Matron! You've seen firsthand what he is and how we clash. Help me find a way to make the Chens cancel this match!"
[ZH] 千里寻沉默不语，夏果那样子，心根本不在望月轩，迟早会离开。
[EN] Matron Wei's heart ached raw for her girl: lost her first sweetheart when the Xus fled, endured a brutal marriage and divorce, fell for Young Master Qiao only for rumors to smash it, and now saddled with a spoiled dandy worse than Lin Jue! "Ever think what you'll do if breaking it off fails and you have to marry into the Chen house?"
[ZH] “看来姑娘心中明得跟镜子一般，也知道夏果靠不住，可我看苦荞也不一定能陪你，她跟仪王身边的侍卫关系不一般，那孩子每隔几日在望月轩门外等她，我都碰见好几回。”
[EN] "Haven't! I'm done with love and marriage—happy living solo right here in Wangyue Pavilion with you and Kuqiao!"
[ZH] “大不了我把苦荞嫁出去，和嬷嬷在一起也不错。”
[EN] "Notice you skipped Xiaguo."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷幸福地笑着：“我老了，怎能陪姑娘一辈子？还得找个可心的人陪你才行。姑娘还是想想若退不了婚，该怎么办？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stayed mute; everyone knew Xiaguo's heart lay far past Wangyue Pavilion's gates.
[ZH] “若……若退不了婚……”千里寻还未想好，她不知该如何回答。
[EN] "Clear-eyed as a mirror, young lady; you see Xiaguo won't stay! Hate to tell you, Kuqiao won't stay forever either. She and Prince Yi's guard have something going; I've caught that boy waiting outside Wangyue Pavilion's gate for her plenty of times!"
[ZH] “姑娘不用回答我，只需你想清楚便可。结果无非两种，嫁或不嫁。为不嫁努力的同时，还需做好最坏的打算，省得最坏的结果出现，搞得心慌意乱。”
[EN] "Fine, I'll marry Kuqiao off happy and stay with you!"
[ZH] 从韦嬷嬷的话中，千里寻算是听出来，想要退婚不是那么容易，估计比与林觉和离还难：“嬷嬷说得极是。”
[EN] Matron Wei smiled warm. "Old bones like mine can't walk beside you forever; you still need a true soul to share your years! Think through the worst case: only two paths exist, marrying or not marrying. Fight tooth and nail not to wed, yet prepare for the worst too so panic never catches you flat-footed!"
[ZH] “听说你打算把红豆收在身边？”
[EN] Reading between Matron Wei's calm lines, canceling this betrothal looked even tougher than divorcing Lin Jue. "Wise words, Matron."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“红豆的拳法练得不错，让她再去做洗衣裳的事太浪费，所以我打算把她收在身边。”
[EN] "Hear you're promoting Hongdou to your side?"
[ZH] “姑娘喜欢，就收在身边，以后夏果走了，苦荞再嫁人，也好有个贴心的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. "She picks up Qianli Fist fast. Criminal waste leaving her scrubbing laundry!"
[ZH] “嬷嬷总是替我考虑得周全。”
[EN] "Good call. Once Xiaguo leaves and Kuqiao weds, you'll have someone loyal close at hand."
[ZH] “我不过是多吃了几年饭，多走些路，自然想得周全些，等你到我这个年纪也是一样。”
[EN] "Always thinking ten steps ahead for me, Matron."
[ZH] “我哪能与嬷嬷相比？”
[EN] "Just eaten a few extra bowls of rice and walked more miles. You'll see just as clear at my age."
[ZH] 千里寻觉得韦嬷嬷的每一句话就像火盆中散发的热气，温暖着她的身心，将春季还透着的丝丝寒意涤荡殆尽。
[EN] "Never catch up to you, Matron!"
[ZH] 尽管韦嬷嬷并未给她出如何退婚的主意，也令她心满意足。
[EN] Every word from Matron Wei radiated cozy heat like the charcoal brazier, melting away spring's last chill. Even without a magic trick to break the betrothal, Qianli Xun walked out feeling warmed to the bone.

#CH 159
[ZH] 临安捶丸场地要数陈家的最大，乔家今年花大价钱租了陈家的，无非是因为儿子乔文仙快要成亲，成亲前举行一场捶丸赛先热热场，给儿子的婚事添些人气，也讨个吉利。
[ZH] 乔守仁对钱氏的建议无不赞同，一方面满足了钱氏的喜好；另一方面也好与临安有头有脸的人家继续保持良好关系。
[ZH] 要不要往朱家送请帖，乔守仁没犹豫多久，决定还得送，不送便是将矛盾摆在明面上；送了若朱家拒绝，乔家没面子，礼数尽在，朱家不来，就不能怪乔家什么事。
[ZH] 捶丸这日，春光明媚，千里寻带着韦嬷嬷、苦荞、夏果，还有新收在身边的红豆，乘坐马车，向陈家捶丸场而去。
[ZH] 陈家的捶丸场地位于城东最偏僻处，千里寻到达时，从马车上刚走下，瞧见哥哥扶着嫂嫂也从马车上下来，她迎上去打招呼。
[ZH] “哥哥嫂嫂，没想到我们竟然一起到。”
[ZH] “要不怎么是一家人？”朱景元见妹妹前来，没有悲戚之情，心中甚觉欣慰。
[ZH] 陈文心拉起淑真：“走，我们一起进去。”
[ZH] 姑嫂两个走在前面，朱景元和韦嬷嬷跟在一旁，身后则跟着众位丫鬟，一起向捶丸场地内走去。
[ZH] 捶丸场外筑有高墙，一时瞧不到里面的场景。大门敞开，走进后，千里寻以为自己进入到古代园林中。
[ZH] “嫂嫂，你确定这是捶丸之地？怎么还有亭台楼阁，假山池水？该不会是陈家的别居？”
[ZH] 陈文心笑道：“你还真是失忆，什么都不记得。现在看到的不过是供大家休闲娱乐赏景之处，再往里走，才是捶丸的场地。”
[ZH] “哦，原来如此。”
[ZH] 众人顺着园林的小径向里走，穿过池水上的长亭，再穿越一道园门，眼前变得开阔起来，一片草地映入眼帘，零星冒出的新芽在枯草中显得鲜艳夺目。
[ZH] 千里寻总算看清捶丸场地是怎么回事，确实与高尔夫球的场地有异曲同工之妙，也有平坦、凹凸的地势，球窝处插有小小的旗子。
[ZH] 已有人正在捶丸，千里寻瞧那人手中的球杆，心中“嘿嘿”一笑：“的确与高尔夫球的球杆很像。”
[ZH] 乔家的丫鬟迎上来：“这边请。”
[ZH] 千里寻发现，捶丸场地的两边有凉亭，专供人歇息观看，凉亭里有桌椅，茶水和果品，已有好些人坐在凉亭下，边说笑，边观看众人捶丸。
[ZH] 在丫鬟的带领下，他们来到一处凉亭刚坐下，钱氏与米氏前来打招呼。
[ZH] “朱家公子、少夫人，还有朱家姑娘，你们能到来，乔家的捶丸赛举办得更有意义。”
[ZH] 说话的正是雪天跪在她面前的钱氏，当时痛哭流涕，此时却春光满面。
[ZH] 千里寻觉得人真的是一种善变的动物。出于礼节，她与哥哥嫂嫂，以及韦嬷嬷都站起来。有嫂嫂在，千里寻不用说任何话。
[ZH] “钱夫人严重，此处是陈家的场地，我好久未来，正好借此机会过来瞧瞧，景元疼爱我，自然陪着；至于淑真，她在我的指导下，捶丸技术大大提升，收到乔家的请帖，甚是开心，想来一展身手。”
[ZH] 陈文心的话显然带着情绪，钱氏与米氏心中不快，怎奈是东道主，又是为儿子的婚事热场，不好与对方计较。
[ZH] “不管因为什么，既然来了，就是乔家的座上宾。”钱氏说完，对身边管事的丫鬟吩咐，“告诉下人，好生伺候朱家人。”
[ZH] 丫鬟回道：“是。”
[ZH] 钱氏瞧见苗家人来，立即说：“你们先坐，我去招呼客人。”
[ZH] 千里寻又与嫂嫂等人坐下，向钱氏走的方向瞧去，原来她们迎接的是苗知瑜与秦夫人，两家已是亲家，见面分外热情。
[ZH] 钱氏携着秦夫人的手，米氏也拉起苗知瑜，向另外一处凉亭而去。
[ZH] 陈文心很不屑：“有什么了不起！苗家怎能和朱家相比？苗知瑜也比淑真差远了。”
[ZH] 千里寻内心仍有酸楚之感，却挤出笑容：“嫂嫂不用替我说话，我已经对此事没什么感觉。”
[ZH] 朱景元说：“淑真已有好人家，没必要再跟乔家置气。”
[ZH] 想到弟弟要娶淑真，陈文心开心道：“是啊，我还跟乔家置什么气？也不知浩宇这家伙有没有来？”
[ZH] 朱景元说：“你与淑真在这里坐着，我去跟乔老爷打声招呼，顺便找找浩宇。”
[ZH] “去吧。”
[ZH] 得到允许，朱景元起身离开。
[ZH] 哥嫂说话之际，千里寻开始认真观察捶丸之人，有男有女，手执球杆，在固定的位置向球窝内击球，那动作与打高尔夫球如出一辙。
[EN] Boasting the largest chuiwan grounds in Lin'an, the Chen estate rented its venue at top silver to the Qiaos this spring so Madam Qian could host a lively warm-up tournament ahead of Qiao Wenxian's wedding, drumming up festive cheer and good luck. Qiao Shouren backed his wife on both counts, indulging her hobby while keeping ties polished with every notable house in Lin'an. Debating whether to send an invitation to Zhu Manor took him only a minute: skipping them publicized bad blood, whereas sending one preserved courtesy; if the Zhus declined, nobody could fault the Qiaos.
[ZH] 千里寻在心中笑道：“捶丸定是高尔夫球的老祖宗。”
[EN] Tournament day dawned crisp and sun-drenched. Boarding her carriage with Matron Wei, Kuqiao, Xiaguo, and newly promoted Hongdou, Qianli Xun rode out to the Chen chuiwan estate tucked on Lin'an's quiet eastern outskirts. Stepping down right beside another carriage, she spotted her brother helping Sister-in-law out and hurried over. "Look at that timing, Brother, Sister-in-law!"
[ZH] 夏果站在小姐身后，一直寻找乔公子的身影，终于看到乔公子在球场一边，向这边瞧来，他应该是看小姐，看来他还未真正放下小姐。
[EN] "What else makes us family?" Zhu Jingyuan smiled, relieved to see zero gloom on his sister's face.
[ZH] 小姐只顾欣赏别人打球，根本没注意乔公子。夏果为乔公子感到心酸，悄悄溜出凉亭，从亭子后面迂回着来到乔公子身边。
[EN] Linking arms with Shuzhen, Chen Wenxin led the way—"Let's head in!"—with Zhu Jingyuan, Matron Wei, and the cluster of maids trailing behind.
[ZH] “乔公子，能否与我到那边说话？”
[EN] High outer walls hid everything from the lane; stepping past open gates made Qianli Xun blink in surprise at pavilions, tiered halls, rockeries, and clear ponds. "Sure this is a chuiwan field, Sister-in-law, and not a private Chen villa?"
[ZH] 乔文仙本不愿理夏果，可又没有其他办法接近淑真，也许夏果能帮他，犹豫片刻后跟上夏果。
[EN] Chen Wenxin chuckled. "Amnesia really wiped everything! This front garden is just for strolling and tea; chuiwan lawns lie straight past!"
[ZH] 南双拉住自家公子：“公子，我喜欢夏果。”
[EN] Winding along garden walks, crossing a long bridge pavilion over water, and passing an inner moon gate opened onto sweeping emerald turf where bright green spring shoots broke through winter grass, complete with rolling slopes, flats, and tiny flags marking each target hole. Seeing players already swinging curved wooden clubs made Qianli Xun grin inside: Dead ringer for golf!
[ZH] 乔文仙并未理会，甩开南双，继续跟上夏果。南双只好跟在公子身后，一脸委屈。
[EN] Qiao family maids stepped forward guiding them to shaded wooden pavilions ringing both flanks of the turf, stocked with tables, tea, and seasonal fruit where clusters of guests already chatted while watching matches. Right after they sat in their pavilion, Madam Qian and Concubine Mi approached beaming. "Young Master Zhu, Young Madam, and Miss Zhu, your presence makes our chuiwan match shine twice as bright!"
[ZH] 他们来到一棵树的后面，树枝上已冒出新芽，还未长成大片的树叶，无法更好地挡住身影。
[EN] The very Madam Qian who had knelt sobbing in the snow weeks ago now radiated effortless springtime polish. How fast human faces flipped! Rising polite beside brother, sister-in-law, and Matron Wei, Qianli Xun left talking to Chen Wenxin.
[ZH] “你叫我来这里做什么？”乔文仙虽跟来，却也不满。
[EN] "Too generous, Madam Qian! Being Chen family grounds I haven't visited in ages, I dropped by to look around—with Jingyuan doting along—and under my coaching Shuzhen's chuiwan form shot straight up, so she jumped at your invitation to show off!"
[ZH] 夏果跪在地上：“乔公子对我家小姐依然情根深种，可您也看到，她根本未瞧见您，说明小姐心中已无乔公子。您醒醒吧，瞧瞧我，我对乔公子的感情就像乔公子对小姐的感情一样深。”
[EN] Sharp barbs hid under every smile. Displeasure flickered across Madam Qian and Concubine Mi, yet playing host before their son's wedding kept them polite. "Whatever the reason, every guest under our roof is honored!" Turning to her head maid, she ordered, "Make sure the Zhu party wants for nothing!" Spotting the Miao clan arriving just then, Madam Qian excused herself fast. "Sit tight; more guests to greet!"
[ZH] “那又如何？只要我喜欢她即可，哪怕远远看她一眼，我也心满意足。”
[EN] Resuming their seats, they watched Madam Qian clasp Madam Qin's hands while Concubine Mi linked arms warm with Miao Zhiyu, escorting their new in-laws toward another pavilion.
[ZH] 南双更委屈，夏果又跪下求公子，显然没把他当回事，他此刻决定，这么无情的人，还是不要再惦记。
[EN] Chen Wenxin snorted in scorn. "Big deal! The Miao house can't hold a candle to the Zhus, and Miao Zhiyu isn't fit to tie Shuzhen's shoes!"
[ZH] 夏果见不奏效，使出最后一招，反正小姐已不可能与乔公子在一起，她如何说，小姐应该不会介意。
[EN] A faint pinch still tugged Qianli Xun's chest, masked behind an easy smile. "Save your breath, Sister-in-law; doesn't faze me one bit anymore."
[ZH] “小姐不允许我以后再跪下求乔公子纳我为妾，她说……她说……她说既然要与乔公子了断，就要断得干干净净，不留一丝痕迹，不给乔公子任何希望……”
[EN] "Shuzhen has a fine match ahead; no sense nursing grudges with the Qiaos," Zhu Jingyuan added smooth.
[ZH] 乔文仙内心被深深击痛：“她真这么说？”
[EN] Remembering her brother marrying Shuzhen cheered Chen Wenxin right up. "Right! Why waste breath on Qiaos? Wonder if Haoyu even showed up yet!"
[ZH] “小姐亲口……亲口对我说，还说……”夏果一边说，一边想着怎么说，才能将乔公子激怒，“还说即使把我许给临安城最丑的乞丐做妾，也不会许给乔公子……”
[EN] "Stay here with Shuzhen; I'll go greet Master Qiao and track down Haoyu," Zhu Jingyuan said, rising as his wife waved him off.
[ZH] 乔文仙彻底被激怒，因为怒气，不管不顾，转身就走。
[EN] Watching players grip wooden clubs on the tee and drive balls across rolling turf toward flagged holes, Qianli Xun chuckled to herself: Chuiwan has to be golf's great-grandfather!
[ZH] 刚走出没几步，碰见韦嬷嬷站在路边，显然他与夏果说的话已被听到。乔文仙才不管这些，也没有心情打招呼，直奔凉亭而去。
[EN] Standing behind her mistress, Xiaguo kept scanning the sidelines until she spotted Young Master Qiao standing off at the edge of the field staring straight toward their pavilion, eyes glued on Miss, still pining! Yet Miss never looked his way once, absorbed in the game. Aching for him, Xiaguo slipped unseen out the back of the pavilion and circled behind the turf to reach his side. "Young Master Qiao, could we speak over there a moment?"
[ZH] 原来韦嬷嬷瞧见夏果悄悄离开，尾随而来。夏果与乔文仙在树后说的话她尽数听在耳中。
[EN] Reluctant to talk to Xiaguo yet desperate for any bridge to Shuzhen, Qiao Wenxian hesitated a beat before following. Tugged back by Nan Shuang—"Master, I like Xiaguo!"—Qiao Wenxian shook him off without a word, leaving a crestfallen Nan Shuang trailing behind.
[ZH] 当夏果为自己的计谋感到高兴，起身要走时，被韦嬷嬷一巴掌打在脸上。
[EN] Halting behind a budding tree whose sparse spring shoots offered thin cover, Qiao Wenxian frowned. "Why pull me over here?"
[ZH] 夏果捂着被打疼的脸：“嬷嬷凭什么打我？”
[EN] Dropping straight onto both knees, Xiaguo pleaded, "You're still devoted to my Miss, Young Master Qiao, yet look—she didn't even spare you a glance! Her heart holds zero room for you! Wake up and look at me; my devotion to you runs every bit as deep as yours for her!"
[ZH] “哼！凭什么？凭你跪在地上求着当人家的妾室，给姑娘丢脸；凭你信口雌黄，给姑娘编排的那些瞎话；凭你不顾丫鬟本分，喜欢不该喜欢的人。”
[EN] "So what? Loving her is enough for me. Even gazing from afar fills my heart."
[ZH] 夏果放下捂着脸的手，理直气壮：“嬷嬷说那么多有什么用？很快我就不是小姐的丫鬟，嬷嬷也管不着我。”
[EN] Behind him, Nan Shuang's chest sank cold. Kneeling before Master again like he didn't even exist! Time to cut this heartless girl out for good.
[ZH] “我倒盼着你早些离开望月轩。”
[EN] Seeing gentle pleas fail, Xiaguo played her trump card, figuring since Miss and Young Master Qiao were done forever, bending truth would not hurt Miss anyway. "Miss forbade me from ever begging you to take me as concubine again! She said... she said since she broke clean with Young Master Qiao, she wants every last trace wiped out, zero hope left..."
[ZH] “既然嬷嬷和我的想法一致，就别再管我。”
[EN] Pain jabbed straight through Qiao Wenxian's ribs. "She said that?!"
[ZH] “管你？你也配！我是担心你伤害姑娘。”
[EN] "Word for word! Even swore..."—improvising sharp to push him past boiling—"...swore she'd hand me to Lin'an's ugliest beggar as concubine before ever giving me to Young Master Qiao!"
[ZH] “嬷嬷放心，小姐是我的恩人，我绝不会做伤害她的事，我编的那些瞎话，对小姐来讲无伤大雅，却可达到我的目的。”
[EN] Blind fury snapped Qiao Wenxian's last thread of restraint. Pivoting on his heel without another glance, he stormed straight away, brushing right past Matron Wei standing beside the path, too furious even to greet her as he marched straight toward the Zhu pavilion.
[ZH] “只盼你别再给姑娘惹什么大麻烦！”
[EN] Having spotted Xiaguo sneak off and shadowed her step for step, Matron Wei had caught every poisonous word! Just as smug Xiaguo stood to leave, crack! A stinging slap across the face stopped her cold.
[ZH] 乔家公子被夏果激怒，从走去的方向判断，应该是朝朱家人歇息的凉亭而去。韦嬷嬷担心姑娘，不再理会夏果，转身沿原路返回。
[EN] Clutching her burning cheek, Xiaguo flared, "What right do you have hitting me, Matron?!"
[ZH] 乔文仙满载怒气，来到淑真待的凉亭下，她依旧在观看别人捶丸，不时与陈文心说笑。淑真越高兴，乔文仙越受伤，他更不愿让她如愿以偿。
[EN] "What right?! Kneeling in the dirt begging to be a concubine, shaming our young lady! Spinning filthy lies slandering your own mistress! Forgetting a maid's place chasing a man above you!"
[ZH] “淑真……”
[EN] Dropping her hand with brazen defiance, Xiaguo shot back, "Lecture all you want, Matron! I won't be Miss's maid much longer; soon you won't have any say over me!"
[ZH] 听到熟悉的声音，千里寻刚刚露出的笑脸变得平静，文渊什么时候来到身边，她竟然没有注意到。
[EN] "Can't wait for the day you pack out of Wangyue Pavilion!"
[ZH] “文渊……不……乔公子找我何事？”
[EN] "Then since we both want the same thing, stay out of my lane!"
[ZH] 淑真竟然开始称呼他为“乔公子”，如此生分，看来她连相爱前朋友的情谊都不愿再继续，又深深刺痛乔文仙的自尊，他失去理智，只想挽回自尊，只想留下他与她千丝万缕的联系。
[EN] "Care about you? Don't flatter yourself—I only guard my young lady from your venom!"
[ZH] “幽……幽栖居士……”乔文仙尽管不想这样称呼，却不由自主脱口而出，“我想问你要个人，望幽栖居士答应并成全。”
[EN] "Relax, Matron. Miss saved my life; I'd never harm her! A couple harmless fibs won't scratch Miss and win me what I want!"
[ZH] “什么人？”千里寻感到害怕，担心乔文仙说出朱淑真或是幽栖居士。
[EN] "Pray you don't bring real disaster down on her head!" Seeing furious Young Master Qiao bearing down on the Zhu pavilion, Matron Wei left Xiaguo behind and hurried straight back.
[ZH] 乔文仙脱口而出：“你身边的丫鬟夏果。”
[EN] Boiling with hurt pride, Qiao Wenxian strode up to Shuzhen's pavilion, where she still sat laughing and chatting with Chen Wenxin over the game. Her bright smiles twisted the knife deeper; no way was he letting her erase every tie clean! "Shuzhen..."
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，同时也感到一丝失落，好好的感情真真实实就这么没了，两人以后只能客气地说话。
[EN] Hearing that familiar voice wiped the grin off Qianli Xun's face. When had he walked right up? Catching herself fast, she said, "Wenyuan... no, Young Master Qiao, what brings you here?"
[ZH] 淑真不说话，乔文仙以为她不想答应，毕竟她不想再与他有任何联系：“幽栖居士难道想让我跪在你面前，才肯答应吗？”
[EN] "Young Master Qiao"—so frosty she would not even keep old friendship! Stung raw in his pride and stripped of cool sense, clinging to one last twisted thread linking them, he blurted, "You... Youqi Jushi! I come to ask one person from you—grant my wish!"
[ZH] 千里寻刚想说答应的话，乔守仁走来：“文渊，有很多公子找你，你怎么在这里？”
[EN] Heart lurching lest he blurt out "Zhu Shuzhen" or "Youqi Jushi," Qianli Xun asked tense, "Who?"
[ZH] 乔文仙还在与父亲赌气，他来得正好，不如将气发泄在他的身上：“父亲来得正好，儿子想纳幽栖居士身边的丫鬟夏果为妾，还望父亲成全。”
[EN] "Your personal maid, Xiaguo."
[ZH] 听到是纳妾，不是与朱家姑娘旧情复燃，乔守仁“呵呵”笑道：“原来是想纳妾，为父当然同意，不知幽栖居士意下如何？”
[EN] Relief washed over Qianli Xun, trailing a whisper of hollow ache: their whole love story truly dead and buried, reduced to stiff formality.
[ZH] 千里寻自觉没有福气嫁到乔家，文渊既然愿意纳夏果为妾，也算是给她谋个好去处。
[EN] Mistaking her silence for refusal—determined to sever every bond—Qiao Wenxian pressed bitter, "Do I have to drop to my knees before Youqi Jushi before you say yes?!"
[ZH] “乔老爷，乔公子，夏果是我的贴身丫鬟，希望她到乔家后，乔家人能善待她。”
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun opened her mouth to agree, Qiao Shouren strolled up. "Crowds of young gentlemen looking for you, Wenyuan. What are you doing over here?"
[ZH] 淑真爽快答应，乔文仙又感到难过，他一句话未说，转身离开。南双行礼后，去追自家公子。
[EN] Still raw and spiteful toward his father too, Qiao Wenxian aimed his anger straight at him. "Right on time, Father! Your son wishes to take Youqi Jushi's maid Xiaguo as concubine. Grant your consent!"
[ZH] 乔文仙突然离开，搞得千里寻不知所措。
[EN] Relieved it was just taking a maid as concubine rather than reigniting things with Miss Zhu, Qiao Shouren chuckled breezy. "Just taking a concubine? Father agrees, of course! What says Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 陈文心赶紧说：“乔老爷，乔公子今日看着奇怪，不知他怎么了？”
[EN] Since fate barred her from the Qiao door, letting Xiaguo enter as Wenyuan's concubine gave the girl her wish anyway. "Master Qiao, Young Master Qiao, Xiaguo is my personal maid; I only ask your house to treat her well."
[ZH] “呵呵……，文渊即将成亲，又要纳妾，一时适应不过来。”乔守仁解释完，又转向朱家姑娘，“文渊成婚那日，乔家会派人将轿子抬到望月轩，还望朱家姑娘做好准备。”
[EN] Her crisp consent sliced Qiao Wenxian fresh. Without another syllable, he spun around and stalked off, Nan Shuang bowing fast before chasing after him.
[ZH] 文渊的婚事将近，一丝难过之后，千里寻很快恢复正常：“乔老爷放心，我在望月轩静候佳音。”
[EN] Stunned by his abrupt exit, Qianli Xun blinked speechless while Chen Wenxin smoothed it over. "Young Master Qiao looks off today, Master Qiao—everything alright?"
[ZH] 乔守仁离开后，韦嬷嬷才回到座位坐下，刚才大家说的话，她在一旁已听到。
[EN] "Haha, wedding right around the corner plus taking a concubine—just adjusting!" Turning courteous to Shuzhen, Qiao Shouren added, "On Wenyuan's wedding day, Qiao Manor will send a sedan chair straight to Wangyue Pavilion. Please have her ready, Miss Zhu."
[ZH] “夏果终于得偿所愿，姑娘就当做了件善事。”
[EN] Hearing his wedding loomed so close pinched one brief second before calm returned. "Rest assured, Master Qiao. We'll await your glad news at Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 陈文心开玩笑道：“嬷嬷刚刚去了哪里？又从哪里突然冒出来？”
[EN] Once Qiao Shouren walked away, Matron Wei stepped back into the pavilion—having overheard the closing lines from outside—and took her seat. "Xiaguo got her wish at last; chalk it up as doing a good deed, young lady."
[ZH] “我到别处转转，回来时见乔老爷在此说话，就在一旁待了会儿。”
[EN] "Where'd you vanish to, Matron, popping out of thin air?" Chen Wenxin teased.
[ZH] “嬷嬷的话说得没错，就当做件善事，不知夏果那丫头是否乐意？”
[EN] "Took a short stroll and paused outside seeing Master Qiao talking."
[ZH] 面对嫂嫂的问题，千里寻没有心情回答，她正在琢磨文渊刚才的表现。
[EN] "Good deed indeed—only hope Xiaguo wants it!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷应道：“少夫人不必为一个丫鬟操心，主子想把她许给谁就许给谁，即使不愿意也由不了她，何况夏果这丫头觍着脸想给乔家公子当妾室，也算得偿所愿。”
[EN] Lost puzzling over Wenyuan's bizarre outburst, Qianli Xun did not answer; Matron Wei stepped in smooth. "No need fretting over a maid, Young Madam. A mistress decides her maid's match anyway, and besides, Xiaguo was shameless enough chasing Young Master Qiao to begin with! Pure wish come true!"
[ZH] “哦，原来这丫头早有此心思，她人呢？”陈文心四下瞧瞧，不见夏果的身影。
[EN] "Aha, had her eye on him all along! Where'd she disappear to?" Scanning the pavilion, Chen Wenxin saw no sign of her.
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“她刚去了那边，一直没过来。”
[EN] "Walked off that way earlier and hasn't come back," Kuqiao replied.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“少夫人不要管她，捶丸场上人挺多，您要不要去试试？”
[EN] "Forget her, Young Madam. The lawn's packed with players—aren't you going out to swing?" Matron Wei prompted.
[ZH] “待会儿我定要上场露一手。”陈文心朝场上瞧去，竟然瞧见弟弟浩宇的身影，“这小子，什么时候到的？”
[EN] "About to head out and shine!" Scanning the turf, Chen Wenxin spotted a familiar silhouette. "Wait—when'd my rascal brother Haoyu get here?!"

#CH 160
[ZH] 千里寻思忖着文渊刚才的表现，想不出所以然。
[ZH] 传来梦容的声音：“淑真，原来你坐在这里，我一进来到处找你，终于在这儿找到。”
[ZH] 众人起身拜见郡主：“佳和郡主。”
[ZH] “你们别对我这么客气，朱家少夫人，韦嬷嬷，我能不能把淑真带走？”乔梦容上前已拉住淑真。
[ZH] 陈文心与韦嬷嬷相视一笑，主动说：“郡主相邀，我们二人岂有阻拦之理？”
[ZH] “谢谢二位。”乔梦容拉起淑真就要离开。
[ZH] 苦荞与红豆想跟上，被小姐阻止：“在此处候着，不用跟来。”
[ZH] 二人齐声回道：“是。”
[ZH] 梦容拉着淑真出了捶丸场，边走边说：“我不喜欢捶丸，你陪我走走。”
[ZH] “不喜欢，为何要来？”
[ZH] “乔家邀请哥哥，哥哥不好不来，他到哪里，我当然得跟着。”
[ZH] 自从知道仪王是申简辰后，千里寻不再批判他是花心大萝卜，也不再认为他不懂得知恩图报，反而更加怜惜梦容。
[ZH] “梦容，仪王起死回生后，以前的事都不记得，你有没有想过他不再是以前的仪王？而是另外一个人。”
[ZH] “另外一个人？”梦容想起哥哥刚刚起死回生后，说他不是赵朴的话，可她根本不愿承认，“怎么可能，哥哥还是以前的哥哥，只是失忆而已。”
[ZH] 如此认为倒也不错，千里寻觉得自己又何必替梦容操心，只要她不接受申简辰，仪王才会永远是梦容心中的好哥哥。
[ZH] “我胡乱说的，你别往心里去，仪王人不错，你们以后一定能幸福。”
[ZH] 梦容站住，扭过头，好奇地看着淑真：“你为何突然对哥哥印象变好？”
[ZH] “唉，我这人识人不准，后来渐渐发现还是你最了解仪王。”
[ZH] 以前淑真说过的话，乔梦容虽未完全听进去，多少还是受到影响，更担心哥哥身边会出现其他女子。现在淑真改变看法，乔梦容彻底轻松下来。
[ZH] 她们在园林内边走边聊，周围的植物渐渐焕发生气，许多桃树叶子还未长出，已满树桃花，满眼尽是春光烂漫。
[ZH] 陈浩东陪同颜池从另一条小径走来。
[ZH] “颜兄，后面正举行捶丸赛，要不要去玩玩？”
[ZH] “我有要事，等办完事再陪你玩儿。”
[ZH] 颜池一眼瞅到走来的两位姑娘，其中一位正是大街上偶然遇到之人，心想：“踏破铁鞋无觅处，得来全不费功夫。”
[ZH] “陈兄，她们你可认识？”
[ZH] 陈浩东向两位姑娘瞧去：“认得，左边那位是佳和郡主，右边是临安知府的千金朱淑真。”
[ZH] “朱淑真。”颜池重复一遍，“没想到她叫这个名字。”
[ZH] 陈浩东从颜池的表情中看出他对朱家姑娘颇有兴趣：“颜兄瞧上她？”
[ZH] “什么都逃不过你的眼睛。”颜池毫不掩饰。
[ZH] 陈浩东叹口气：“可惜啊，她已与我家堂弟定亲，再过一个月，就要举办婚礼。”
[ZH] “那有什么？我喜欢的人，即使跟别人生了娃娃，迟早我也会把她搞到手。”
[ZH] “颜兄好气魄！”
[ZH] “等她嫁给你的堂弟，你可得帮我盯紧她。”
[ZH] 陈浩东不解：“为何？”
[ZH] “别让她生病离开人世，等我忙完正事，自然会从你堂弟手中将她抢走。”
[ZH] 颜池说完，拐向另一条小径，向园林外而去。
[ZH] 陈浩东将颜池送出后，拐回来，见两位姑娘依旧在散步，便上前打招呼。
[ZH] “佳和郡主，朱家姑娘，二位要是逛累，可以去那边品茶歇息，陈家园林内的下人若有不周到之处，还请二位及时向我反馈，我好吩咐管事改进。”
[ZH] 二位姑娘愣住，她们从未见过眼前之人，从他所说的话中判断，应该是陈家人。
[ZH] 梦容试探地问：“你是？……”
[ZH] “在下名叫陈浩东，是浩宇的堂兄。”
[ZH] 对方的身份已确定，与他不熟，没必要多言语。
[ZH] 乔梦容以郡主的身份说：“不必客气，你尽管去忙，不用理会我们。”
[ZH] 明显是赶人的话，陈浩东本也只是打声招呼，让贵人知道有他这号人物。贵人既然赶人，他自然乖乖退下：“在下谨遵郡主吩咐。”
[ZH] 二人继续散步，欣赏春色，聊着闺中密友之间的话题。
[ZH] 乔家举办捶丸赛，为给乔家撑脸面，申简辰早早到来，他与梦容被安排在最利于观看比赛的位置。
[EN] Still turning over Wenyuan's strange stunt without making heads or tails of it, Qianli Xun heard Mengrong's cheerful voice call out. "There you are, Shuzhen! Hunted everywhere since arriving—found you at last!"
[ZH] 自进入，四处寻找千里寻的身影。捶丸场地倒挺大，依地势而建，四周有木质凉亭，供来客观看比赛。申简辰将场地瞧了个遍，都没看到千里寻的身影。
[EN] Everyone rose with bows. "Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 正当无聊之际，见她与哥嫂一起到来，甚是开心。
[EN] "Quit being so stiff with me! Young Madam Zhu, Matron Wei, mind if I kidnap Shuzhen?" Already tugging Shuzhen's hand, Qiao Mengrong beamed.
[ZH] “梦容，你的闺中密友来了。”
[EN] Trading warm smiles, Chen Wenxin and Matron Wei answered together, "How could we ever block Princess Jiahe's invitation?"
[ZH] 梦容也瞧去，的确是淑真：“哥哥，我去找淑真。”
[EN] "Thank you both!" Leading Shuzhen out, Qianli Xun waved Kuqiao and Hongdou back when both moved to follow. "Wait right here; no need to come." Both maids stayed put.
[ZH] 申简辰阻止道：“等会儿再去，他们刚来，乔家还得寒暄几句。”
[EN] Stepping out of the chuiwan lawn into the quiet gardens, Qiao Mengrong linked arms. "Can't stand chuiwan. Walk with me!"
[ZH] 果不其然，淑真坐下没多久，乔家人前去打招呼。直到见文渊去而离开后，申简辰已坐不住，告诉梦容可以去找他的闺中蜜友。
[EN] "Hate chuiwan, why even come?"
[ZH] 自从与千里寻相认，申简辰既开心又不开心。
[EN] "The Qiaos invited Brother, so he couldn't say no, and wherever Brother goes, I tag along!"
[ZH] 开心的是，千里寻的确对他与以往不同，与他说话放得更开，行礼也没以前那么谨慎，也不再误解他，让他感受到浓浓的朋友情谊。
[EN] Knowing Prince Yi was really Shen Jianchen had wiped out every bit of Qianli Xun's old resentment toward him as a fickle prince, leaving only deeper pity for Mengrong. "Mengrong... after Prince Yi revived forgetting everything before, did you ever wonder if he isn't the old Prince Yi anymore, if he's someone else altogether?"
[ZH] 不开心的是，他与千里寻的关系并未得到实质性进展，她在神柳下说过的话，还回荡在耳边。因为梦容，他无法放开手脚去爱千里寻，心里却难以放下她，无时无刻不念着。
[EN] "Someone else?" Flashing back to Brother's very first words upon waking—"I'm not Zhao Pu"—she pushed the thought miles away. "Impossible! Brother is still my Brother, just lost his memories!"
[ZH] 梦容去找千里寻，她们两个出了捶丸场，应该是去园林处赏景，迟迟不见回来。申简辰更坐不住，该找什么正当理由与千里寻搭话？
[EN] Better she believed that, Qianli Xun decided; long as she never knew about Shen Jianchen, Prince Yi stayed her devoted brother forever. "Just talking nonsense; don't mind me! Prince Yi's a good man, and you two are bound to be happy."
[ZH] 为难之际，钱氏身边的丫鬟站出来大声向众人说：“诸位！场上的捶丸爱好者均已热身，现在进入正式比赛环节，我家夫人说两人一队，男女不限，大家可以自由组队，彩头均由我家夫人出。第一场！翡翠玉镯一只！”
[EN] Stopping short to peer curious into Shuzhen's face, Mengrong asked, "How'd your opinion of Brother flip overnight?"
[ZH] 有下人用盘子端着一只翡翠玉镯，成色不错，对于在场的富贵人家来讲，并不会将彩头放在眼里，大家只图个乐子。
[EN] "Bad judge of character before. Came to see you know Prince Yi best after all."
[ZH] 申简辰倒开心，不如借此邀请千里寻出战。他对身边的白风说：“你不必跟着，我去找郡主。”
[EN] Though Mengrong had brushed off Shuzhen's past doubts, they had left quiet thorns worrying another woman might steal Brother away; hearing Shuzhen's praise now lifted every cloud clean. Strolling deeper through the blooming estate, spring pulsed everywhere, peach trees bursting into pink clouds even before green leaves unfolded.
[ZH] 白风明白，王爷的凉亭需有人守着，否则别人会以为王爷与郡主已离开。他便老实待着，向苦荞那边瞧去。
[EN] Down an intersecting walk approached Chen Haodong alongside Yan Chi. "Chuiwan tournament's underway out back, Brother Yan. Care to play a round?"
[ZH] 幽栖居士被郡主邀请已离开，苦荞没有跟着，倒便宜了白风，主人不在，他可以肆意与苦荞打招呼。
[EN] "Urgent errands waiting. I'll play with you once business is done." Suddenly Yan Chi froze, eyes locking straight onto the two ladies strolling ahead—one the exact woman from his street clash! Talk about wearing out iron sandals hunting in vain, only to stumble across treasure with zero effort! "Know those two ladies, Brother Chen?"
[ZH] 苦荞见白风向她挥手，也偷偷抬手回应，被韦嬷嬷瞧见。
[EN] Glancing over, Chen Haodong replied, "Left is Princess Jiahe; right is Prefect of Lin'an's daughter, Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] “这小伙子不错，算你有眼光。”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen," Yan Chi rolled the name on his tongue. "So that's her name."
[ZH] 得到韦嬷嬷夸赞，苦荞红在脸上，喜在心里。
[EN] Reading clear intrigue on his friend's face, Chen Haodong grinned. "Caught Brother Yan's eye?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷瞧见身后的夏果，立刻拉下脸，刚要批评，陈浩宇走来，她不好再说什么。
[EN] "Nothing slips past you!" Yan Chi owned it brazen.
[ZH] 陈浩宇手拿球杆，满脸笑容，站在凉亭下，朝上面说：“姐！你跟我组队吧！”
[EN] Chen Haodong sighed. "Tough break—betrothed to my cousin Haoyu! Wedding's in one month."
[ZH] 陈文心笑着埋怨道：“你什么时候来的？也不知道过来跟我打个招呼，都是快成家的人，还这么不懂礼数？”
[EN] "So what? Anyone I want, even after she bears another man's kids, ends up mine sooner or later!"
[ZH] “都是自家人，计较那么多干吗？快下来，跟我捶丸。”
[EN] "Bold spirit, Brother Yan!"
[ZH] 陈文心站起来，脱下披风，身边的丫鬟主动将襻膊替她绑好，再将球杆取了，走下凉亭，跟着亲弟弟来到比赛场地。
[EN] "Once she marries your cousin, keep sharp eyes on her for me."
[ZH] 他们的对手是乔文仙的二妹妹文秀与本家的一名公子，场上开始比赛，负责比赛的人将球放在球基处，两队开始轮流将球打向指定的球窝。
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] 捶丸赛相比较马球赛而言，没那么激烈。看客们目的不在捶丸，而是借助捶丸赛，互相走动走动，寒暄寒暄，关系有了，或许大事也会办成。因此，看比赛的人少，互相走动的人多。
[EN] "Make sure she stays healthy and alive. Soon as my business wraps, I'm snatching her straight out of your cousin's hands!" Turning down a side trail, Yan Chi headed toward the exit.
[ZH] 场上捶丸的人并不理会他人是否关注，完全沉浸在比赛中，也只有他们能够感受到比赛的激烈。
[EN] After seeing Yan Chi off, Chen Haodong doubled back, spotted the two ladies still strolling, and stepped up bowing. "Princess Jiahe, Miss Zhu, if you tire of walking, tea pavilions await right over there! Should any servant in our Chen gardens fall short, tell me at once so I instruct the steward!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷无心看比赛，少夫人一离开，便对夏果说：“刚才少夫人在，我不便说什么，趁这空挡，我替姑娘向你传句话，乔家公子向姑娘提出纳你为妾，乔家老爷也同意，姑娘当然不会阻止，你如愿以偿，乔家举行婚礼那日，也会来望月轩用轿子把你抬走。”
[EN] Startled—neither recognized him, though his words pegged him as a Chen—Mengrong probed polite, "And you are...?"
[ZH] 夏果已知是这样的结果，亲耳听到，还是欣喜不已：“谢小姐，谢嬷嬷。”
[EN] "Chen Haodong, Haoyu's older cousin."
[ZH] “不用谢我和姑娘，还是谢你自己会筹谋，三言两语激得乔公子说出纳你的话，只怕乔公子这会儿正后悔。”
[EN] Strangers—no need for small talk! Pulling gentle royal rank, Qiao Mengrong waved. "Don't stand on ceremony; tend your duties without minding us." Taking the crisp dismissal smooth, Chen Haodong bowed out. "At Princess Jiahe's command." Both girls resumed strolling amid spring blossoms, trading warm girl talk.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的话，夏果并未当回事，只要能跟喜欢的人在一起，什么都无所谓，再说乔公子无论家世，还是人品才学比林觉不知强多少倍。
[EN] Meanwhile, back at the chuiwan grounds, having arrived early to lend the Qiaos royal prestige, Shen Jianchen sat with Mengrong in the best viewing pavilion scanning the rolling field for Qianli Xun. Nothing turned up until she arrived at last alongside her brother and sister-in-law!
[ZH] 正想着，瞧见不远处，林觉身边多了一位女子，二人正走向对面的凉亭。
[EN] "Look, Mengrong, your best friend's here!"
[ZH] 夏果问道：“嬷嬷，林练兵官身边的女子是谁？”
[EN] Spotting Shuzhen, Mengrong jumped up. "Going to grab Shuzhen, Brother!"
[ZH] “还练兵官呢！他已连升两级，现在是林统制，身边那位是新娶的夫人。”
[EN] "Give them a minute; the Qiaos need to greet them first." Sure enough, Qiao elders stepped up, and only after Wenyuan strode over and stalked off did restless Shen Jianchen let Mengrong go fetch her.
[ZH] 苦荞忍不住问：“新娶的夫人？是哪家姑娘？”
[EN] Since revealing his identity to Qianli Xun, joy and ache tugged him in two. He felt thrilled she talked breezy and real with him like old times, zero stiffness or misunderstandings, yet ached because her iron line beneath the Divine Willow had not budged an inch. With Mengrong standing between them, he could not chase her in the open, yet could not tear her out of his heart for a single breath.
[ZH] “听说是隔了好几门子的亲戚，反正不是临安人。”
[EN] Watching Mengrong lead Qianli Xun off into the gardens and linger long made him antsy. What decent excuse got him over to talk? Right on cue, Madam Qian's maid stepped forward calling loud: "Attention all players! Warm-ups done—official matches start now! Madam sets teams of two, men or women mixed free, prizes courtesy of Madam! Round One prize: one fine emerald jade bangle!" A servant held up a glowing jade tray, pocket change to wealthy guests, pure sport.
[ZH] “他们看上去挺不错，林统制应该不会再将春金巧带回府中了吧？”
[EN] Perfect opening to invite Qianli Xun onto the lawn! Telling Bai Feng, "Stay put, I'm going to fetch Princess," Shen Jianchen strolled off. Knowing an empty royal pavilion looked like they had departed, Bai Feng held the post—and seized the golden moment with both masters away to grin and wave right across the grass at Kuqiao! Catching Kuqiao sneaking a shy wave right back, Matron Wei smiled warm. "Solid lad—good eyes you've got!" Turning bright red to her ears, Kuqiao beamed inside.
[ZH] 苦荞不由为小姐感伤，林觉都已另娶，看上去还很幸福，自家小姐虽有婚约，可小姐并不中意，时刻想着退婚之事。
[EN] Then Matron Wei's gaze hit Xiaguo standing behind her and iced straight over, cut off before scolding as Chen Haoyu strolled up gripping a wooden club and grinning ear to ear beneath the pavilion steps. "Sis! Team up with me!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“没听过有一句话吗？……”
[EN] "When'd you slink in without even saying hello?!" Chen Wenxin laughed scolding. "Almost a married man and still zero manners!"
[ZH] 夏果接道：“狗改不了吃屎！”
[EN] "Family—skip the rules! Come down and swing clubs with me!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷第一次对夏果露出笑容：“不错，倒说了句我爱听的话。”
[EN] Standing up, Chen Wenxin slipped off her cloak while her maid bound her wide sleeves tight with a sleeve-tying cord. Grabbing her club, she trotted down onto the lawn beside her brother. Facing Qiao Wenxian's younger sister Wenxiu paired with a Qiao cousin, both sides teed off from the starting mark taking turns driving balls toward the flagged hole. Far calmer than polo, chuiwan served most guests as a social playground; few kept score, crowds strolling pavilion to pavilion sealing friendships and deals, while players on the grass drowned out everything in fierce focus.
[ZH] 钱氏和米氏正与秦夫人坐在一处，苗知瑜挨母亲坐着，面对未来的婆婆，更表现出矜持。
[EN] Indifferent to the match, once Young Madam left, Matron Wei turned crisp to Xiaguo. "Didn't speak in front of Young Madam earlier. Passing Miss's word straight to you now: Young Master Qiao asked for you as concubine, Master Qiao consented, and Miss won't block you. Got your wish—on the Qiao wedding day, their sedan chair comes to Wangyue Pavilion for you."
[ZH] 钱氏看未来的儿媳妇，是越看越顺眼，米氏也笑得合不拢嘴。
[EN] Even knowing the outcome, hearing it confirmed made Xiaguo beam. "Thank you, Miss! Thank you, Matron!"
[ZH] 大家拉了一会儿家常话，秦夫人向朱家的凉亭瞅去，不见朱家姑娘。
[EN] "Save your thanks for your own scheming, winding Young Master Qiao up with two slick lies till he blurted it out! Bet he's kicking himself blind right now!"
[ZH] “亲家可听说朱家将那位女词人许给陈家？”
[EN] Brushing off the jab—long as she won the man she wanted, nothing else mattered, and Young Master Qiao's lineage, grace, and learning crushed Lin Jue by a hundred miles—Xiaguo's eye caught a familiar man escorting a young woman toward an opposite pavilion. "Matron, who's that lady beside Training Officer Lin?"
[ZH] 钱氏说：“怎能没听说？也不懂知府大人是怎么想的，陈家虽好，可陈家二公子却不成器，把女儿嫁给他，恐怕还得闹到和离的份儿。”
[EN] "Training Officer? Jumped two ranks—Division Commander Lin now! And that's his new bride."
[ZH] 米氏搭腔道：“我猜肯定是因为朱家姑娘名声不好，知府大人才会同意。”
[EN] Kuqiao blinked curious. "New bride? From which house?"
[ZH] “不管因为什么，只要别影响文渊便好。”
[EN] "Distant relative from outside Lin'an, word says."
[ZH] 钱氏的话令秦夫人心中欢喜。
[EN] "They look happy enough. Guess Division Commander Lin quit hauling Chun Jinqiao home?" A quiet sting pricked Kuqiao for Miss: even Lin Jue had remarried smiling while Miss fought tooth and nail to escape an unwanted match.
[ZH] 钱氏又瞅瞅苗知瑜，向丫鬟吩咐：“去把公子寻来。”
[EN] Matron Wei scoffed. "Ever heard the old saying?..."
[ZH] 丫鬟领命而去。
[EN] Xiaguo finished crisp, "A dog can't quit eating filth!"
[ZH] 一时失去理智，问淑真要了夏果，等清醒过来，乔文仙后悔不已。不愿与任何人交流，一个人坐在捶丸场角落的石头上，垂头丧气。
[EN] For the very first time, a genuine smile cracked Matron Wei's face toward Xiaguo. "Fair point—said one line I like at last!"
[ZH] 南双因公子主动要纳夏果为妾，不愿与公子说话，站在一旁，黯然伤神。
[EN] Across the lawn, Madam Qian and Concubine Mi sat chatting warm with Madam Qin while poised Miao Zhiyu stayed demure beside her mother, drawing fond grins from her future mother-in-law and beaming smiles from Concubine Mi. Glancing over toward the Zhu pavilion and noticing Miss Zhu absent, Madam Qin asked offhand, "Hear the Zhus betrothed that ci poet to the Chen family, in-law?"
[ZH] 钱氏身边的丫鬟走过来：“公子，夫人叫你过去。”
[EN] "Who hasn't heard?" Madam Qian sniffed. "No clue what Prefect Zhu's thinking. Wealthy house, sure, yet their second son's a hopeless scamp! Marry her over there and they'll end up in mutual divorce all over again!"
[ZH] 乔文仙不言语。
[EN] Concubine Mi chimed in, "Bet Prefect Zhu settled just because her reputation's shot!"
[ZH] 丫鬟又强调一遍：“公子，夫人叫你过去。”
[EN] "Whatever their reason, long as she stays clear of Wenyuan!" Madam Qian's words warmed Madam Qin straight through. Glancing fond at Miao Zhiyu, Madam Qian told a maid, "Go fetch Young Master over."
[ZH] 乔文仙才站起来，没有回答，径直朝钱氏的凉亭走去。南双再难过也得跟上，他已决定，要彻底把夏果从心里清除掉。
[EN] Meanwhile, cooling down after his rash fit demanding Xiaguo, bitter regret choked Qiao Wenxian. Shunning every guest, he sat hunched alone on a boulder at the lawn's edge while heart-sore Nan Shuang stood silent beside him, refusing to speak after Master claimed the very girl Nan Shuang liked.
[ZH] 丫鬟对自家公子的表现并不感到奇怪，自从与苗家定亲后，公子一直如此。
[EN] Madam Qian's maid hurried up. "Young Master, Madam summons you."
[ZH] 乔文仙走到钱氏的凉亭内，行礼道：“母亲找我何事？”
[EN] Silence. Repeating once more—"Young Master, Madam calls for you"—she watched him rise mute and stalk straight toward Madam Qian's pavilion, glum Nan Shuang trailing behind swearing to wipe Xiaguo clean from his heart. Used to Young Master's bleak gloom ever since the Miao betrothal, the maid did not bat an eye.
[ZH] 钱氏笑道：“知瑜也来了一会儿，园林内风景不错，你带她去逛逛。”
[EN] Stepping into Madam Qian's pavilion, Qiao Wenxian bowed stiff. "You called for me, Mother?"
[ZH] 乔文仙没立刻回应，而是说道：“有一事需告知母亲，儿子决定纳幽栖居士身边的丫鬟夏果为妾，父亲已点头答应。”
[EN] Madam Qian smiled warm. "Zhiyu's been sitting long enough. Lovely spring gardens out there—walk her around!"
[ZH] 钱氏愣了一下，瞧一眼米氏。
[EN] Skipping any reply to that, Qiao Wenxian announced flat, "One matter to report, Mother: your son has decided to take Youqi Jushi's maid Xiaguo as concubine, and Father already consented."
[ZH] 米氏尴尬地笑道：“我当什么呢，纳个把妾室没什么，秦夫人，您说呢？”
[EN] Frozen a split second, Madam Qian glanced over at Concubine Mi, who forced an awkward chuckle. "Thought it was big news! Taking a concubine or two is nothing, right, Madam Qin?"
[ZH] 秦夫人当然不爽，可乔家是皇亲国戚，能看上自家女儿，苗家也算高攀，她只能露出笑容：“男子纳几个妾室，无伤大雅，只盼妾室能像米姨娘这般好相处，我家知瑜嫁过去不至于被欺负。”
[EN] Burning inside yet swallowing pride before imperial kin—marrying into the Qiao house lifted the Miaos high—Madam Qin pasted on a polite smile. "No harm in a gentleman taking a concubine or two. Only hope she's sweet-tempered as Concubine Mi so my Zhiyu won't suffer grief!"
[ZH] 钱氏说：“知瑜嫁过来，是乔家未来的主母，谁敢欺负，我第一个饶不了她。”
[EN] "Zhiyu enters as future head matriarch of Qiao Manor," Madam Qian declared firm. "Whoever dares cross her answers to me first!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜还未过门，就有人前来抢夫君，她心中着实不快。听了钱氏的话，心情稍微好些。
[EN] Stung sour having another woman claim a slice of her groom before she even crossed his threshold, Miao Zhiyu felt Madam Qian's iron promise soothe at least a fraction of her chill.

#CH 161
[ZH] 钱氏接着说：“ 你父亲是一家之主，他既然同意，我与你娘没有理由反对，不管你纳多少妾室，都需记住，知瑜嫁过来就是你的正室，要把她放在心上。去吧，带知瑜逛逛。”
[ZH] 乔文仙本来想用纳妾一事发泄发泄对婚姻的不满，结果并未成功，他只能听从安排，礼貌地说声：“苗姑娘，请。”
[ZH] 苗知瑜站起来，她头一次与乔公子单独相约，十分珍惜，暂时忘记对纳妾的不满，走到乔文仙身边：“乔公子，请。”
[ZH] 乔文仙不再言语，下了凉亭，朝园林方向走去。
[ZH] 苗知瑜快步跟上，努力与乔文仙并肩而行。
[ZH] 自家儿子带着未来的儿媳妇去逛，钱氏与米氏看着他们离去的背影直乐，秦夫人也开心。
[ZH] 钱氏朝球场上瞧去，陈文心正好用球杆将球打进一个球窝。
[ZH] “朱家少夫人捶丸的技术不错，虽比不上我年轻时，可在她们这一辈的女子中也算少有。”
[ZH] 秦夫人说：“陈家公子也打得不错，看来第一场这兄妹二人要取胜。”
[ZH] 三人说着笑着继续欣赏他人捶丸。
[EN] Madam Qian went on, "Your father is the head of this household. Since he agreed, your mother and I have no reason to object. No matter how many concubines you take, remember this: once Zhiyu marries into our family, she will be your primary wife, and you must hold her in your heart. Go on now, take Zhiyu for a stroll."
[ZH] 南双跟在公子与苗姑娘身后，心里念着公子纳夏果为妾一事，他想尽快忘记夏果，否则以后跟公子可怎么相处？南双觉得要快速忘记一个人，必须找一个新的人放在心里。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had meant to use taking a concubine to vent his bitterness over the marriage, yet the gesture fell flat. Left with no choice but to obey, he gave a courteous nod. "After you, Lady Miao."
[ZH] 该在心里重新放进谁呢？他瞅到身边的丫鬟，以前没太注意过，今日仔细瞧，长得也不赖。南双凑近，小声问：“喂，你叫什么名字？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu rose to her feet. This was her first time spending time alone with Young Master Qiao, and she treasured the chance enough to set aside her displeasure over the concubine for the moment. She stepped beside him and said, "After you, Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 丫鬟倒不忌讳，毕竟自家小姐都要嫁给乔公子，她必然要与乔公子身边的人搞好关系，小声回道：“我叫幼凡。”
[EN] Saying no more, Qiao Wenxian descended from the pavilion and headed toward the gardens. Miao Zhiyu hurried after him, striving to keep pace shoulder to shoulder. Watching their son lead his bride-to-be off for a walk, Madam Qian and Concubine Mi beamed with delight, and Madam Qin looked just as pleased.
[ZH] “我叫南双，以后我们做朋友，如何？”南双小心征求意见。
[EN] Madam Qian turned her gaze back to the playing field just as Chen Wenxin swung her club and knocked a ball straight into a hole. "Young Madam Zhu has a fine hand at chuiwan," she remarked. "She may not match what I could do in my youth, yet among women of her generation, skill like hers is rare."
[ZH] 幼凡爽快答应：“好。”
[EN] "Young Master Chen plays well too," Madam Qin added. "It looks like the brother and sister will take the first match."
[ZH] 南双觉得这声“好”一下子令他释然许多，脸上不由露出满足的笑容。
[EN] Chatting and laughing, the three women went on watching the game.
[ZH] 申简辰离开球场，来到园林处，寻了一会儿，终于瞧见要找的人迎面走来。他快走两步迎上去，瞟一眼千里寻，对梦容说：“让哥哥好找。”
[EN] Trailing behind his master and Lady Miao, Nan Shuang could not stop thinking about Qiao Wenxian taking Xiaguo as a concubine. He needed to forget Xiaguo fast, or how could he face serving his master in the days ahead? The quickest way to forget one person, he decided, was to put someone new in his heart.
[ZH] 哥哥主动寻来，乔梦容感到很幸福：“哥哥找我何事？”
[EN] Who should take that place? His eyes fell on the maid walking beside him. He had paid her little mind before, yet looking closer today, she was not bad-looking at all. Leaning in, he murmured, "Hey, what is your name?"
[ZH] “捶丸赛已开始，我想参加，必须两人组队，我又不愿与其他人一起，你能不能跟我一起？”
[EN] The maid took no offense. Her mistress was about to marry Young Master Qiao, so getting on good terms with his attendants was only sensible. "My name is Youfan," she whispered back.
[ZH] 哥哥的要求令乔梦容为难：“我很想与哥哥组队，可我不会捶丸，也不喜欢捶丸。”
[EN] "I am Nan Shuang. Let us be friends from now on, alright?" he asked with care.
[ZH] “没关系，我会教你，其实捶丸很简单，两下就学会。”申简辰装作鼓励的样子。
[EN] "Sure," Youfan agreed without hesitation.
[ZH] 乔梦容继续为难，担心自己球技不佳给哥哥丢脸。
[EN] That single crisp word lifted a weight off Nan Shuang's chest, and a satisfied smile spread across his face.
[ZH] 千里寻猜出申简辰心中的小九九：“既然梦容为难，仪王不如别去捶丸，陪梦容在凉亭中看别人捶丸即可。”
[EN] Meanwhile, Shen Jianchen left the field and entered the gardens. After searching a short while, he spotted the two he was looking for walking toward him. Quickening his stride to meet them, he flicked a glance at Qianli Xun before addressing Mengrong. "You made Brother search quite a while."
[ZH] 申简辰有说不出的失望：“幽栖居士的建议未尝不可，梦容，你不用为难，哥哥放弃参加比赛。”
[EN] Happy that he had come looking for her on his own, Qiao Mengrong smiled. "What did Brother need me for?"
[ZH] 乔梦容不愿哥哥失望，想到一个好办法：“哥哥，要不你与淑真组队？”
[EN] "The chuiwan matches have started, and I want to join. It takes a team of two, and I do not care to partner with anyone else. Will you pair up with me?"
[ZH] 终于听到梦容给出想听的建议，申简辰装作勉强接受的样子：“好吧，看在幽栖居士是你的闺中密友，我勉强答应。”
[EN] The request put Qiao Mengrong in a bind. "I would love to team up with Brother, truly, yet I do not know how to play chuiwan, and I do not like it either."
[ZH] 千里寻虽理解申简辰，可他利用梦容的单纯和善良如此引导她，实在令人气愤。她瞪了申简辰一眼，对梦容和颜悦色道：“梦容，我今日不想捶丸，只想陪你聊天儿。”
[EN] "No matter. I can teach you," Shen Jianchen said in an encouraging tone. "Chuiwan is simple enough. You will pick it up in two swings."
[ZH] 申简辰不禁自责，他不该为个人私情，利用梦容：“算了，我还是不去捶丸，你与幽栖居士继续聊。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stayed hesitant, afraid clumsy playing would embarrass him.
[ZH] 他转身打算回球场坐着，被乔梦容拦住：“哥哥别走，看我的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun saw straight through Shen Jianchen's little scheme. "Since Mengrong feels uncomfortable, Your Highness would do better to skip the match and sit in the pavilion watching the others play with her."
[ZH] 乔梦容向淑真央求道：“淑真，你就答应吧？哥哥愿意跟你组队，给了你很大的面子，他是王爷，你也需给他些面子才对。”
[EN] Disappointment washed over Shen Jianchen. "Youqi Jushi makes a fair point. Do not force yourself, Mengrong. Brother will sit the match out."
[ZH] 千里寻犹豫着，忽见乔文仙与苗知瑜肩并肩走在一起欣赏园中的风景。本以为见到他们会毫无感觉，可她竟然清晰地感受到一阵伤痛袭来，说是忘记以前的感情，其实哪有那么容易？
[EN] Unwilling to let him down, Qiao Mengrong hit on an idea. "Brother, why not team up with Shuzhen instead?"
[ZH] 眼见他们即将走近，她故意将声音放大说：“好！我答应与仪王组队！”
[EN] Hearing the exact suggestion he had angled for, Shen Jianchen put on a reluctant front. "Very well. Seeing that Youqi Jushi is your closest confidante, I suppose I can make do."
[ZH] 申简辰奇怪，尽管他渴望千里寻答应，又为她如此爽快感到疑惑。他转过身，文渊与苗姑娘出现在眼前，便明白千里寻为何会答应。他愈加失落。
[EN] Qianli Xun understood what drove him, yet watching him play on Mengrong's innocence and kindness to steer her like that set her temper flaring. She shot Shen Jianchen a glare, then turned a warm smile on Mengrong. "I am not in the mood for chuiwan today, Mengrong. I just want to stay and chat with you."
