[ZH] 乔文仙瞧见淑真，也知对方瞧见自己，想到刚才她冷漠的样子，以及答应将夏果许给他做妾，气就不打一处来。
[EN] Guilt pricked at Shen Jianchen. He had no business using Mengrong for his own private feelings. "Forget it. I will not play after all. Go on chatting with Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 正好旁边有一枝盛开的桃花伸将过来，乔文仙顺手摘下一朵：“知瑜，这朵花很美，我为你戴在头上。”
[EN] He turned to head back to the pavilion, only for Qiao Mengrong to catch his sleeve. "Do not go, Brother. Watch me handle this." She turned to plead with Shuzhen. "Shuzhen, please say yes? Brother offering to partner with you does you great honor. He is a prince, so you ought to show him some face."
[ZH] 乔公子突然亲切地称呼她，还要为她头上插花，苗知瑜喜出望外，也觉诧异，猛然瞧见不远处的幽栖居士，才恍然大悟。
[EN] Qianli Xun hesitated, then froze as Qiao Wenxian and Miao Zhiyu came into view, strolling side by side as they admired the garden scenery. She had thought seeing them together would leave her untouched, yet a sharp ache pierced her chest. Letting go of old feelings was never as simple as saying the words.
[ZH] 不如配合乔公子，再说他们已有婚约，不必忌讳他人的目光：“乔公子……不，文渊，你摘的花确实很美，我很喜欢。”
[EN] As the pair drew near, she raised her voice on purpose. "Alright! I agree to team up with Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 乔文仙将桃花插入苗知瑜的发髻旁，在桃花的衬托下，苗知瑜本就娇美的面庞显得更加娇艳。
[EN] Shen Jianchen blinked in surprise. Much as he had wanted her agreement, her sudden turnaround struck him as odd. One look over his shoulder showed him Wenyuan and Lady Miao walking up, and he understood at once why she had said yes. His spirits sank lower still.
[ZH] 曾喜欢的人为其他女子头上戴花，千里寻多少觉出尴尬来。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian saw Shuzhen and knew she had seen him too. Remembering her icy manner earlier and how she had handed Xiaguo over to be his concubine, anger flared inside him. A branch of blooming peach blossoms hung right beside the path, so he reached up and plucked a flower. "Zhiyu, this blossom is lovely. Let me place it in your hair."
[ZH] 乔梦容担心淑真，扯了扯哥哥的衣角，轻声唤道：“哥哥。”
[EN] Startled and thrilled to hear him use her given name and offer to pin a flower in her hair, Miao Zhiyu glanced past him, spotted Youqi Jushi a short distance away, and grasped the truth at once. She might as well play along. They were betrothed, after all, with no reason to shy from onlookers. "Young Master Qiao... no, Wenyuan, the flower you picked is lovely indeed. I adore it."
[ZH] 申简辰也瞧到，失落之余，甚觉开心，文渊的激将法使得一点作用都没有，只会让千里寻更加死心。梦容扯他的衣角，唤“哥哥”，申简辰当然猜到梦容的意思。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian tucked the peach blossom beside Miao Zhiyu's chignon. Against the soft petals, her delicate face looked more radiant than ever.
[ZH] 他走上前打招呼：“文渊，刚才我找你找不到，原来陪佳人在此。”
[EN] Seeing the man she had once loved place a flower in another woman's hair left Qianli Xun fighting a wave of awkwardness.
[ZH] 苗知瑜赶快行礼：“仪王殿下，知瑜这厢有礼。”
[EN] Worried for her friend, Qiao Mengrong tugged on her brother's sleeve and called out in a low voice, "Brother."
[ZH] “苗姑娘不必多礼，再过十日，你就要嫁到乔家，以后是乔家的一分子，见我不必客气。”申简辰暂时忘记婚约一事，认为千里寻定是属于他，要不与文渊那么相爱，还是被分开，不觉脸上露出笑容。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had seen the whole display. Beneath his own pang of gloom, a spark of satisfaction flickered. Wenyuan's clumsy attempt to provoke jealousy would backfire and only push Qianli Xun to give up on him for good. Reading Mengrong's silent plea from her tug on his robe, he stepped forward to greet them. "Wenyuan, I looked everywhere for you earlier, and here you are keeping a fair lady company."
[ZH] 苗知瑜被仪王的话说得更是开心，当初喜欢乔公子仅仅因为他的才华，现在想来，家世也十分重要，等她嫁到乔家后，在整个临安城，除了郡主，还有谁能与她相比。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu dropped into a swift curtsy. "Your Highness Prince Yi, Zhiyu pays her respects."
[ZH] 乔文仙没有心情理会仪王，向淑真瞥了一眼，她竟然携着佳和郡主走来，还面不改色。
[EN] "No need for ceremony, Lady Miao," Shen Jianchen said. "In ten days you will marry into the Qiao family. Once you are one of them, there is no call to stand on formality with me." For a brief moment, his own betrothal slipped his mind. If Qianli Xun and Wenyuan had loved each other so much and still ended up torn apart, it could only mean she was meant for him. A smile crept onto his face.
[ZH] 走近后，苗知瑜又拜见佳和郡主：“我要是知道幽栖居士与郡主来园中逛，也会随你们一道来，捶丸我可是一点儿都不喜欢。”
[EN] Prince Yi's warm words lifted Miao Zhiyu's spirits higher still. She had first fallen for Young Master Qiao over his literary talent, yet now she saw how much family standing mattered too. Once she became a Qiao bride, aside from Princess Jiahe, who in all of Lin'an could rival her?
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“我也不喜欢捶丸，不过，有乔公子在，我可不敢约你。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had no heart to banter with Prince Yi. He flicked a glance at Shuzhen, who strolled up arm in arm with Princess Jiahe without a flicker of distress on her face.
[ZH] 千里寻不愿沉默不语，得说些话才能让文渊觉得她并不在乎：“我倒喜欢捶丸，刚跟仪王约好，一起组队，好久没运动，正好趁此机会舒展舒展筋骨。”
[EN] Once they drew close, Miao Zhiyu curtsied to the princess as well. "Had I known Youqi Jushi and Your Highness were strolling in the gardens, I would have joined you. I do not care for chuiwan in the slightest."
[ZH] 申简辰很想笑，千里寻掩饰尴尬的话语实在不怎么样，在不爱捶丸的人面前说自己爱捶丸，哪会有人回应？
[EN] "Nor do I," Qiao Mengrong replied. "Still, with Young Master Qiao by your side, I would hardly dare steal you away."
[ZH] 果然没人接她的话。
[EN] Unwilling to stand in silence, Qianli Xun forced out a remark so Wenyuan would think she cared nothing at all. "I happen to enjoy chuiwan. I just agreed to team up with Prince Yi. I have not had a proper workout in ages, so this is a good chance to stretch my limbs."
[ZH] 申简辰可不想自己喜欢的人太尴尬：“幽栖居士既答应与我组队，不如现在随我去捶丸场？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen bit back a laugh. Her cover for her awkwardness was clumsy at best. Who on earth would chime in when you bragged about loving chuiwan to two women who hated it?
[ZH] 千里寻早不想待下去，文渊一言不发，老盯着她，再待下去恐怕苗知瑜会伤心：“仪王发话，淑真不敢不从。”
[EN] Sure enough, nobody picked up the thread.
[ZH] 申简辰问乔文仙：“文渊，可否愿意前去观看？”
[EN] Unwilling to leave the woman he loved hanging in silence, Shen Jianchen stepped in. "Since Youqi Jushi has agreed to partner with me, shall we head to the field now?"
[ZH] 乔文仙将目光从淑真的脸上挪开：“既然仪王要参加比赛，我当然前去捧场。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had been itching to leave anyway. Wenyuan stood mute, staring holes into her, and lingering longer might upset Miao Zhiyu. "When Prince Yi commands, Shuzhen dares not refuse."
[ZH] 于是五人沿原路返回。
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced at Qiao Wenxian. "Wenyuan, care to come watch?"
[ZH] 刚走出园林来到捶丸场，林觉携夫人前来，他不为别的，只为拜见仪王。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian tore his gaze from Shuzhen's face. "If Your Highness is taking the field, I shall of course go cheer you on."
[ZH] 林觉来得晚，本想到仪王的凉亭中拜见，只看到贴身侍卫白风站在亭中，不见仪王踪影，向别人打听后才得知仪王出了捶丸场。
[EN] The five of them retraced their steps together.
[ZH] 于是林觉携夫人下了凉亭往外走，没承想碰上想见之人，他上前行礼：“仪王，下官来晚一步，没有及时拜见，还请见谅。”
[EN] Hardly had they stepped out of the gardens onto the chuiwan grounds when Lin Jue approached with his bride, seeking out Prince Yi. Having arrived late, Lin Jue had gone to Prince Yi's pavilion first, only to find the personal guard Bai Feng standing alone inside. Word from bystanders sent him out toward the gardens, and now he stumbled right into the prince himself. Stepping forward, he bowed low. "Your Highness, this humble official arrived late and failed to pay respects in time. Please forgive the oversight."
[ZH] “这是捶丸场，不是临安府衙，没有身份之别，林统制不必太紧张，放轻松。”
[EN] "This is a chuiwan field, not Lin'an Prefecture's yamen," Shen Jianchen said. "Rank does not count here. Relax, Division Commander Lin; no need to be so stiff."
[ZH] “仪王说得极是，下官谨遵仪王吩咐。”
[EN] "Your Highness speaks true. I shall obey your instructions."
[ZH] 申简辰瞧一眼林觉身旁的女子，虽谈不上花容月貌，配林觉绰绰有余：“这位是你新娶的夫人？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced at the woman beside Lin Jue. She was no peerless beauty, yet she was more than a match for Lin Jue. "Is this your new wife?"
[ZH] 没等林觉说，新妇继续行礼：“成婚那日，听夫君说，王爷也前来捧场，本想拜见王爷，怎奈王爷走得早，今日才得礼全。”
[EN] Before Lin Jue could answer, the bride dipped into another deep curtsy. "On our wedding day, my husband told me Your Highness graced us with your presence. I had hoped to pay my respects then, yet Your Highness departed early, so only today can I fulfill the rite."
[ZH] 申简辰想起那日，他看完新人拜堂，甚觉无聊，趁众人不注意，找了去茅房的理由，趁机离开：“那日梦容突然有事，我不得不先走，还望见谅，再说见面的机会甚多。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen recalled that day: bored to tears after watching the couple bow to heaven and earth, he had slipped out on the excuse of visiting the latrine. "Mengrong had an urgent matter that day, so I had to leave ahead of time. I hope you will excuse it. Besides, we will have plenty of chances to meet."
[ZH] 新妇说：“王爷说得极是，今日夫君携奴家前来，是想一起拜见王爷，只有拜见过王爷，奴家与夫君的婚礼才算真正礼成。”
[EN] "Your Highness is most gracious," the bride replied. "My husband brought me here today for the sole purpose of paying respects together. Only after greeting Your Highness is our wedding truly complete."
[ZH] 林觉娶新妇之事，千里寻已知，慨叹无论什么时代，男人离婚后都难耐寂寞，会很快再找一个。能看得出，眼前这位新妇对林觉甚是满意，她主动搭话，凸显女主人身份便是证明。
[EN] Qianli Xun already knew Lin Jue had remarried, and she sighed inwardly. No matter the era, divorced men hated being lonely and rushed straight into another match. Plain as day, this bride felt quite content with Lin Jue, chiming in to assert her place as lady of the house.
[ZH] 乔梦容看出哥哥不想再与林觉的新妇说下去，主动说：“听说你不是临安人，不知娘家在哪里？”
[EN] Seeing her brother had no wish to drag out small talk with Lin Jue's bride, Qiao Mengrong stepped in. "I hear you are not from Lin'an. Where is your maiden home?"
[ZH] 新妇猜到主动与她说话之人定是夫君专门提到的佳和郡主，又拜道：“奴家的确不是临安人，家住渭州。”
[EN] Guessing this must be Princess Jiahe, whom her husband had made a point of mentioning, the bride curtsied again. "Indeed, I am not from Lin'an. My family hails from Weizhou."
[ZH] “确实挺远，嫁到这里很不容易，不知怎么称呼？”
[EN] "That is quite a distance. Marrying so far from home cannot be easy. How should we address you?"
[ZH] “奴家娘家姓白，名唤梅若。”
[EN] "My maiden surname is Bai, and my given name is Meiruo."
[ZH] “大家年纪相仿，以后我们都唤你梅若。”
[EN] "We are all around the same age, so from now on we will call you Meiruo."
[ZH] “郡主能如此唤奴家，是梅若之幸。”
[EN] "To be called so by Your Highness is Meiruo's great fortune."
[ZH] 新娶的夫人与郡主攀上关系，林觉自然欢喜，瞟向朱淑真，心想：“你瞧不上我，总有人欣赏我，怎么样？我都娶了娘子，你却一堆破事，呵呵……！”
[EN] Pleased to see his new bride winning the princess's favor, Lin Jue threw a smug glance at Zhu Shuzhen, thinking, You turned up your nose at me, yet plenty of others know my worth. Look at us now: I have a fresh bride, while you are tangled in a pile of messes. Heh!
[ZH] 郡主既提到“我们”，千里寻不好再沉默，上前说：“既然郡主都唤你梅若，我也唤你梅若，以后大家都是朋友，要是想去临安街市逛，尽管约我们。”
[EN] Since Mengrong had said "we," Qianli Xun could not stay silent. She stepped forward. "If Her Highness calls you Meiruo, I shall call you Meiruo too. We are all friends now. Whenever you feel like strolling through Lin'an's markets, just invite us along."
[ZH] 又多一位朋友，白梅若自是欢喜：“不知姑娘怎么称呼？”
[EN] Delighted to gain another acquaintance, Bai Meiruo asked, "May I ask how to address you, miss?"
[ZH] 乔梦容介绍道：“她可是江南大名鼎鼎的女词人，人称幽栖居士。”
[EN] "She is the famed poetess of Jiangnan," Qiao Mengrong introduced her, "known far and wide as Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 白梅若听林觉说过与他和离之人，没想到竟然在此处遇到，见对方并不尴尬，她也轻松下来：“在渭州时，有位好友甚是喜欢幽栖居士的诗词，老听她说起，自然记在心里。今日竟碰上，真是三生有幸。”
[EN] Lin Jue had already told Bai Meiruo about his divorced former wife, though she had never expected to run into her here. Seeing no hint of awkwardness on the other woman's face, Bai Meiruo relaxed at once. "Back in Weizhou, a dear friend of mine loved Youqi Jushi's ci lyrics and spoke of them nonstop, so your name stayed fresh in my mind. Meeting you today is the blessing of three lifetimes."
[ZH] 苗知瑜也上前说：“我姓苗，名唤知瑜，以后你想怎么称呼都行。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu stepped up as well. "My surname is Miao, given name Zhiyu. Call me whatever you like."
[ZH] “苗姑娘好，我来临安印象最深的就是临安的姑娘一个个水灵俊秀，今日见到两位与郡主，才知先前见的都是俗物。”白梅若说着奉承话。
[EN] "Pleased to meet you, Lady Miao," Bai Meiruo said, pouring on the flattery. "What struck me most upon arriving in Lin'an was how fresh and lovely the young women here are. Yet meeting you two and Her Highness today shows me everyone I saw before was plain as mud."
[ZH] 乔梦容解释道：“我可不是临安人。”
[EN] "I am not from Lin'an, though," Qiao Mengrong clarified.
[ZH] 白梅若继续奉承：“郡主虽不是临安人，怎奈天生丽质，再受临安水土滋养，自然更加水灵。”
[EN] "Even so, Your Highness was born with peerless beauty, and nourished by Lin'an's water and air, you look lovelier still."
[ZH] 申简辰不愿再听下去，正好传来捶丸场上裁判的声音：“陈家公子先得中筹！赢！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen had heard enough syrupy chatter. Just then, the referee's call rang out across the field: "Young Master Chen takes the Middle Tally first! Victory!"
[ZH] 众人朝捶丸场上瞧去，陈浩宇举着球杆，兴奋地跳起来。
[EN] Everyone looked toward the green, where Chen Haoyu hoisted his club and jumped up and down in glee.
[ZH] 千里寻心中不屑：“幼稚！”
[EN] Qianli Xun scoffed inside: How childish!
[ZH] 陈文心上前从桌上放的盘子里取了翡翠玉镯，向众人举起来炫耀，一脸得意。嫂嫂高兴，千里寻自然也高兴。
[EN] Chen Wenxin stepped up to the prize table, lifted a jadeite bangle from the tray, and held it high for all to see with a triumphant grin. Seeing her sister-in-law happy warmed Qianli Xun's heart too.
[ZH] 陈浩宇兴奋的样子，令申简辰十分不爽，刚刚觉得千里寻会是自己的人，瞧见陈浩宇他立刻清醒，婚约横在面前，让他情何以堪。
[EN] Chen Haoyu's strutting rubbed Shen Jianchen the wrong way. One moment ago he had let himself believe Qianli Xun would end up his; one look at Chen Haoyu snapped him back to reality, the betrothal blocking his path like a wall.
[ZH] “幽栖居士，还不快陪我去捶丸，不如就与陈公子对战一回。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi, come play chuiwan with me at once. Let us take on Young Master Chen for a round."
[ZH] “待我回凉亭准备一番。”千里寻爽快应答，实在不想与这些让自己不得不尴尬的人周旋下去。
[EN] "Give me a moment to get ready at my pavilion," Qianli Xun agreed on the spot, more than eager to escape this circle of awkward company.

#CH 162
[ZH] 仪王发话，众人不好再聊下去，各自散去。
[ZH] 林觉带白梅若回到凉亭下属于自己的位置，乔文仙与苗知瑜也来到长辈身边。
[ZH] 哥哥准备上场，乔梦容负责安排，跟上哥哥去帮他换装。
[ZH] 千里寻回到朱家所在的凉亭，不见韦嬷嬷的身影，猜她定是跑去与夫人们聊天儿拉关系。
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐回来，迎上去：“小姐可回来。”
[ZH] 千里寻笑着批评：“我只去逛了一会儿，说得好像我离开许久。”
[ZH] “少夫人跑去捶丸，韦嬷嬷又去找人聊天儿，夏果刚还在，这会儿又不知去哪里，就剩我跟红豆，她又不爱说话，很没意思。”
[ZH] 千里寻瞧一眼红豆，她确实话少，估计是刚被提拔为贴身丫鬟还不适应。
[ZH] “苦荞，你要是觉得无趣，去找白风玩，这里有红豆伺候。”
[ZH] “真的可以吗？”
[ZH] “有什么不可以？去吧。”
[ZH] “谢谢小姐！”苦荞高高兴兴离开凉亭，去找白风。
[ZH] 红豆靠过来：“小姐，您需要我做什么？”
[ZH] “我要去捶丸，给我绑好襻膊，再将球杆拿来。”
[ZH] 红豆很听话，拿出提前准备好的襻膊，为小姐绑好，取来球杆，双手递给小姐后，又不再说话，默默站在一边。
[ZH] 千里寻叮嘱道：“以后跟着我不要拘谨，要多说话，我喜欢话多的丫鬟。”
[ZH] “是，小姐。”
[EN] Once Prince Yi spoke, the group broke off their chatter and scattered. Lin Jue led Bai Meiruo back to their seats in the pavilion, while Qiao Wenxian and Miao Zhiyu returned to their elders. Since her brother was taking the field, Qiao Mengrong followed him to see to his change of clothes.
[ZH] “你要是无聊可以到处转转。”
[EN] Qianli Xun returned to the Zhu family's pavilion and found no sign of Matron Wei, guessing she had gone off to mingle and build ties with the other matrons.
[ZH] “我不喜欢到处转，就在这里看小姐捶丸。”
[EN] Spotting her mistress, Kuqiao hurried up. "Miss, you are back at last."
[ZH] 千里寻并不强求，手拿球杆走下凉亭，来到捶丸场。申简辰已换好衣装，站着等她。
[EN] "I only went for a short stroll," Qianli Xun teased. "You make it sound like I vanished for hours."
[ZH] 千里寻走在薄薄的一层冒出新芽的枯草上，有些不忍心踩踏绿芽，她尽量绕开。又转念一想，今日已有许多人踩踏过，她再注意又有何用？再说一会儿比赛，肯定没有心思在意这些新生命，于是大胆踩上去。
[EN] "Young Madam went off to play chuiwan, Matron Wei went off chatting, and Xiaguo wandered off somewhere just a moment ago. That left only me and Hongdou, and she never says a word. I was bored to death."
[ZH] 被人踩踏过的青草，要么拼尽全力顽强生长，要么偃旗息鼓，化为尘土，不见踪迹。就像申简辰站的那一处，只剩下泥土，肯定是那里的草在命运的折磨下已丧生。
[EN] Qianli Xun glanced at Hongdou. The girl really was quiet, probably still adjusting after being promoted to personal maid. "Kuqiao, if you are bored, go hang out with Bai Feng. Hongdou can attend me here."
[ZH] 千里寻想到自己，会不会也要屈服命运，让它把自己玩弄于股掌之间……
[EN] "Can I really?"
[ZH] “在想什么？”
[EN] "Why not? Go on."
[ZH] 申简辰主动打招呼，她不再想下去：“没什么，什么时候开始捶丸？”
[EN] "Thank you, Miss!" Kuqiao skipped out of the pavilion to find Bai Feng.
[ZH] “过一会儿。”
[EN] Hongdou stepped closer. "Miss, what do you need me to do?"
[ZH] 千里寻瞧见嫂嫂和陈浩宇向他们走来：“对手过来了。”
[EN] "I am going to play chuiwan. Tie my sleeve-tying cord and fetch me a club."
[ZH] 申简辰故意调侃：“你与我一起跟自家人对打，会不会不太好？”
[EN] Obedient as ever, Hongdou took out the cord she had prepared in advance, tied back her mistress's sleeves, handed over the club with both hands, and fell back into silence at her side.
[ZH] 千里寻生气道：“嫂嫂是自家人，陈浩宇可不是。少在我面前瞎胡说，小心老娘到梦容面前说你坏话。”
[EN] "Do not be so stiff around me from now on," Qianli Xun told her. "Talk more. I like chatty maids."
[ZH] 千里寻不认为陈浩宇是自家人，申简辰很开心：“我可没做任何坏事。”
[EN] "Yes, Miss."
[ZH] “故意引诱梦容求我跟你组队，就是坏事。”
[EN] "If you get bored, feel free to wander around."
[ZH] 刚说完，嫂嫂与陈浩宇已走到过来，一起向仪王行礼。
[EN] "I do not care to wander. I will stay right here and watch Miss play chuiwan."
[ZH] 陈文心说：“我听说仪王要跟我和浩宇对打，带他赶来拜见，一会儿捶丸时若有对不住之处，还请仪王见谅。”
[EN] Qianli Xun did not push her. Club in hand, she stepped down from the pavilion and walked onto the field, where Shen Jianchen already stood waiting in fresh sporting attire.
[ZH] “捶丸场上无尊卑，一会儿文心嫂嫂可不要手下留情。”
[EN] Stepping across the thin mat of dry grass poked through with tender green shoots, Qianli Xun felt reluctant to crush the sprouts and tried stepping around them. Then she caught herself: dozens of people had trampled this turf all morning, so what difference would her caution make? Once the match started, she would have no mind to spare for new grass anyway. She planted her boots down and strode straight ahead.
[ZH] 陈浩宇行着礼，心中纳闷：“仪王又是跟朱淑真组队，又称姐姐为嫂嫂，难道他喜欢朱淑真？应该不会吧？母老虎我都瞧不上，王爷又如何能瞧得上？”
[EN] Trampled grass either fought with all its might to keep growing or surrendered, withered to dust, and vanished without a trace. Just like the bare patch of dirt under Shen Jianchen's feet, where the turf had given up under fate's relentless pounding. Would she too have to bow to fate and let it toss her around in the palm of its hand?
[ZH] 仪王这声“嫂嫂”称得陈文心甚是疑惑：“捶丸场上我自然不会留情，不过，仪王称呼草民为嫂嫂，好像不太……”
[EN] "What are you thinking about?"
[ZH] 千里寻瞪了一眼申简辰，心中不满：“都穿越成王爷，说话还如此不着调！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen's greeting snapped her out of it. "Nothing. When do we start?"
[ZH] 见到陈浩宇，申简辰不由自主想宣示主权，一不小心用一个亲密的称呼表露，被千里寻瞪一眼，赶紧解释：“梦容与幽栖居士是闺中密友，你是幽栖居士的嫂嫂也就是梦容的嫂嫂，我称呼一声嫂嫂也无妨。”
[EN] "In a moment."
[ZH] 陈浩宇松口气，继续想：“原来是这个原因，并不是喜欢朱淑真。”
[EN] Spotting her sister-in-law and Chen Haoyu walking up, Qianli Xun nodded toward them. "Here come our opponents."
[ZH] 又一想，自己紧张个什么劲儿？要是仪王喜欢上朱淑真，岂不是可以帮他解决掉大麻烦？
[EN] "Teaming up with me to play against your own family—are you sure that is alright?" Shen Jianchen teased.
[ZH] 传来乔家丫鬟的声音：“接下来的一场由陈家公子与仪王对战！彩头是我家夫人最喜欢的金如意！”
[EN] "My sister-in-law is family; Chen Haoyu is not," Qianli Xun snapped. "Cut the nonsense in front of me, or I will go tattle on you to Mengrong."
[ZH] 众人不管远近都朝丫鬟端的木盒中瞧去，果然有一把金灿灿的如意。
[EN] Hearing she did not count Chen Haoyu as family brightened Shen Jianchen's mood. "I have done nothing wrong."
[ZH] 有人议论：“要不是仪王捶丸，乔家夫人能拿出这么贵重的物件当彩头？”
[EN] "Baited Mengrong into begging me to be your partner—that counts as wrong."
[ZH] 另一人应和着：“说的可不是？”
[EN] Before he could reply, Chen Wenxin and Chen Haoyu arrived and bowed to Prince Yi.
[ZH] 裁判宣布：“本场比赛开始！仪王队，红旗！陈家公子队，黄旗！”
[EN] "I heard Your Highness wants to play a match against Haoyu and me, so I brought him right over to pay respects," Chen Wenxin said. "If we give offense during the game, please forgive us."
[ZH] 不能再聊下去，四人手拿球杆向第一处球基走去，裁判早已将球放置在球基处。
[EN] "There is no high or low on the chuiwan field," Shen Jianchen said. "Do not hold back on my account, Sister-in-Law Wenxin."
[ZH] 千里寻向远处瞧去，草丛中有的球窝旁插着黄色旗子，有的插着红色旗子，如果她没猜错，他们需要将球打入黄色旗子中才算赢。她目测球窝的距离，这一局应该很好打。
[EN] Mid-bow, Chen Haoyu frowned to himself. Prince Yi teams up with Zhu Shuzhen and calls my sister Sister-in-Law? Does he fancy Zhu Shuzhen? No way. Even I would not touch that tigress, so why would a prince?
[ZH] 捶丸开始，黄队先来。陈浩宇第一个走到球基跟前，摆好姿势，一杆下去，球乖乖地被打入插有红色旗子的球窝中。
[EN] The address bewildered Chen Wenxin as well. "I will certainly play my best, yet for Your Highness to call a commoner like me Sister-in-Law seems rather..."
[ZH] 第二个是陈文心，也顺利打入。
[EN] Qianli Xun shot Shen Jianchen a sharp glare, fuming inside: Transmigrated into a prince and you still talk without a filter!
[ZH] 第三个是申简辰，打这种球对他来讲毫无压力，轻轻一击，球顺利跳进插黄色旗子的球窝。
[EN] Seeing Chen Haoyu had triggered Shen Jianchen's urge to stake his claim, making an intimate title slip out. Under Qianli Xun's glare, he hurried to patch it over. "Mengrong and Youqi Jushi are closest friends. Being Youqi Jushi's sister-in-law makes you Mengrong's sister-in-law too, so calling you Sister-in-Law does no harm."
[ZH] 最后是千里寻，经过观察，确实与她打过的高尔夫球区别不大，对她来讲根本没有难度，一杆子下去，成功打入。
[EN] Chen Haoyu let out a breath. So that was all it was—he did not like Zhu Shuzhen after all. Then again, why was he even nervous? If Prince Yi really took a shine to Zhu Shuzhen, wouldn't that take a massive headache right off his hands?
[ZH] 陈文心赞道：“淑真，你的球技的确精进不少。”
[EN] A Qiao family maid called out, "Next match: Young Master Chen versus Prince Yi! The prize is our madam's favorite gold ruyi scepter!"
[ZH] 接着每人又在原处各捶两次，两队不分胜负，各获一筹。
[EN] Spectators near and far craned their necks toward the wooden box in the maid's hands, where a gleaming gold ruyi scepter rested.
[ZH] 四人又跟裁判向另一处球基走去。
[EN] "Would Madam Qiao ever put up a prize that lavish if Prince Yi were not playing?" someone muttered.
[ZH] 陈浩宇见仪王走在最前面，紧跟裁判，朱淑真则跟姐姐陈文心走在一起。他趁机靠近：“朱淑真，你会功夫，仪王也会功夫，你们两个组队，要是赢我们，胜之不武。”
[EN] "You can say that again," another voice agreed.
[ZH] 千里寻站住，没好气地盯着陈浩宇：“劝你没事多读些书，都不知道胜之不武是什么意思，净瞎胡用！”
[EN] The referee shouted, "Let the match begin! Prince Yi's side, red flags! Young Master Chen's side, yellow flags!"
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[EN] Ending their chat, the four players walked club-in-hand to the first starting pad, where the referee had already placed the ball.
[ZH] “懒得告诉你。”千里寻想起退婚一事，将陈浩宇拉到一边。
[EN] Qianli Xun squinted down the turf. Some holes in the grass bore yellow flags and some bore red. If she guessed right, their team had to sink balls into the yellow-flagged holes to score. Eyeballing the distance, she judged this opening round an easy shot.
[ZH] 陈文心并不理会，笑了笑，继续朝前走。
[EN] The yellow team went first. Chen Haoyu stepped up to the starting pad, set his stance, swung, and sent the ball rolling straight into a red-flagged hole. Chen Wenxin went second and sank hers with equal ease. Third came Shen Jianchen; a shot like this held zero pressure for him, and a light tap sent his ball hopping straight into a yellow-flagged hole. Last was Qianli Xun. Having watched closely, she found it little different from modern golf. One clean stroke, and her ball dropped straight in.
[ZH] 陈浩宇被拉到一边，甩开朱淑真的手：“你拉我干什么？”
[EN] "Your skill has come on in leaps and bounds, Shuzhen," Chen Wenxin praised.
[ZH] 千里寻悄悄问：“赶紧告诉老娘，你们家老太太是否还想耗着？”
[EN] Each player took two more shots from the same pad, leaving both teams dead even with one tally apiece.
[ZH] “那还用说？”
[EN] The four followed the referee toward the next starting pad. Prince Yi strode out front alongside the referee while Zhu Shuzhen walked with Chen Wenxin. Seeing an opening, Chen Haoyu sidled up. "Zhu Shuzhen, you know martial arts and Prince Yi knows martial arts. If the two of you team up and beat us, that is an unfair victory."
[ZH] “那便好，眼看快到成婚的日子，后面的事全靠你，可得上点心。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped dead and glared at him. "Do yourself a favor and crack open a book sometime. You do not even know what 'unfair victory' means, throwing phrases around at random!"
[ZH] “放心，奶奶最疼我，什么都听我的。”
[EN] "Then what does it mean?"
[ZH] 申简辰已到第二个球基处，这一筹，由红队开始，他想让千里寻第一个捶丸，发现她并未跟上，朝后面一瞧，原来跟陈浩宇在一处说话，不由嫉妒起来。
[EN] "Cannot be bothered to teach you." Remembering their engagement, she grabbed Chen Haoyu and pulled him aside. Chen Wenxin smiled and kept walking without minding them.
[ZH] “幽栖居士！赶紧过来！该你捶丸！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shook off her hand. "What are you dragging me over here for?"
[ZH] 千里寻听到呼唤她的声音，甩下陈浩宇快步向第二个球基处走去。
[EN] "Tell me straight," Qianli Xun whispered. "Is your grandmother still planning to stall?"
[ZH] 陈文心在一旁偷偷观察仪王的表情，总觉哪里不对劲儿，又不敢往下想，她晃晃脑袋：“瞎想什么？毫无道理！”
[EN] "Of course she is."
[ZH] 第二处球基面对的球窝地势显然凹凸不平，想要把球打入球窝，有一定难度。可难不倒千里寻，她拿起球杆，一棒下去，球飞过凹凸不平之地，不偏不倚掉进对方的球窝……
[EN] "Good. The wedding date is right around the corner. Everything from here on rests on you, so stay sharp."
[ZH] 捶丸比赛不如打马球激烈，即使是仪王登场，也没有将所有人吸引住，该聊天儿的人照样聊天儿，该走动的照样走动。
[EN] "Relax. Grandmother dotes on me most and listens to everything I say."
[ZH] 只有像钱夫人那样喜欢捶丸的，或是像林觉那样需要巴结仪王的，抑或是像梦容那样不会捶丸但关心哥哥的，才会不断用目光照顾捶丸场上的人。
[EN] Having reached the second starting pad, where the red team had the first stroke, Shen Jianchen turned to invite Qianli Xun up—only to spot her huddled in private conversation with Chen Haoyu. Jealousy flared in his chest. "Youqi Jushi! Hurry over! It is your turn to strike!"
[ZH] 乔文仙关心淑真，自然也会瞧去。苗知瑜关心文渊，只会向他瞧的方向瞧去。
[EN] Hearing his call, Qianli Xun ditched Chen Haoyu and strode over to the second starting pad.
[ZH] 当千里寻顺利将球打进球窝里，钱氏赞道：“没想到朱家姑娘捶丸的技术这么好！”
[EN] Watching from the side, Chen Wenxin studied Prince Yi's expression. Something felt off, though she dared not follow the thought further. She shook her head. What wild nonsense am I imagining? It makes no sense at all!
[ZH] 米氏不屑道：“捶丸好，有什么用？女子关键要名声好。”
[EN] The turf stretching between the second pad and its target hole rose and dipped over uneven mounds, making a clean sink tricky. None of that fazed Qianli Xun. She raised her club and swung; the ball sailed over the bumps and dropped dead center into the opposing hole.
[ZH] 秦夫人也说：“米姨娘说得极是。”
[EN] Chuiwan lacked the roaring thrill of polo, so even with Prince Yi on the field, plenty of guests kept chatting and strolling as they pleased. Only die-hard chuiwan fans like Madam Qian, climbers eager to curry Prince Yi's favor like Lin Jue, or devoted onlookers like Mengrong kept their eyes fixed on the match. Qiao Wenxian watched because he cared about Shuzhen, and Miao Zhiyu watched only wherever Wenyuan's eyes went.
[ZH] 两人说完，瞧到钱氏脸色不好看，立刻意识到说错话，米氏赶紧解释：“姐姐别往心里去，我说的是朱家姑娘。”
[EN] When Qianli Xun sank her ball, Madam Qian clapped in admiration. "I never guessed Miss Zhu had such talent at chuiwan!"
[ZH] 秦夫人赶快改口：“女子捶丸好，名声好，会更好，就像夫人您。”
[EN] Concubine Mi sniffed. "What good is skill at chuiwan? For a woman, a good reputation is what counts."
[ZH] 钱氏这才露出笑容：“看他们捶丸，我也手痒，不如一会儿也去场上玩玩儿。”
[EN] "Concubine Mi speaks the truth," Madam Qin chimed in.
[ZH] 乔家人瞧不上幽栖居士，苗知瑜心里多少还是舒服些。
[EN] Both women saw Madam Qian's face darken and caught their slip at once. "Do not take it to heart, Sister," Concubine Mi scrambled to explain. "I meant Miss Zhu."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷转了好几个凉亭，拉过一阵关系后，终于转到乔家，其实是她有意晚些过来。
[EN] Madam Qin hurried to mend her words too. "A woman with both chuiwan skill and a sterling reputation is best of all—just like you, Madam."
[ZH] 乔家那样对待自家姑娘，她故意晚些，既能表明态度，也可表达不满。
[EN] Only then did Madam Qian smile again. "Watching them play has my hands itching. I might step out for a round myself later."
[ZH] “钱夫人是捶丸的好手，应该给年轻一辈露一手。”
[EN] Seeing the Qiao women look down on Youqi Jushi eased the tightness in Miao Zhiyu's chest a little.
[ZH] 大家向声音传来的方向看去，原来是韦嬷嬷。
[EN] After making the rounds of several other pavilions, Matron Wei at last strolled over to the Qiaos' pavilion. She had saved them for last on purpose; after how the Qiaos had treated her young mistress, showing up late made both her stance and her displeasure clear.
[ZH] 钱氏笑道：“我还在想，韦嬷嬷到处走动，为何不来与我们说话？可巧，这就来了，看座！”
[EN] "Madam Qian is a master at chuiwan," Matron Wei called out. "You really ought to give the younger generation a demonstration."
[ZH] 亭中人多，准备的凳子早已用完，丫鬟说：“夫人，没有凳子了。”
[EN] Everyone turned to see Matron Wei approaching. Madam Qian laughed. "I was just wondering why Matron Wei was visiting every pavilion except ours! Look how timely you are. Bring a seat!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜主动站起来：“我的凳子让与嬷嬷。”
[EN] The pavilion was crowded, and every stool was already taken. "Madam, there are no stools left," a maid reported.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷客气道：“那怎么行？苗姑娘也是金尊玉贵的，赶紧坐下，没有凳子，我便站着与夫人说话。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu stood up at once. "Let Matron Wei have mine."
[ZH] “嬷嬷不必推让，知瑜坐了许久，想去走走。”
[EN] "Oh, I could never do that!" Matron Wei demurred. "Lady Miao is a precious noble daughter. Please sit back down. I can stand while I chat with Madam."
[ZH] 苗知瑜的确不想再坐下去，也不想看乔文仙一味盯着捶丸场的人，她想一个人到园子里透透气。向众人行过礼后，没经过同意，自作主张离开。
[EN] "Do not stand on ceremony, Matron. I have been sitting a long while and would like to go for a walk anyway."
[ZH] 丫鬟将凳子搬到钱夫人旁边，韦嬷嬷不再推让，上前坐下，瞧一眼乔公子，他根本不管苗家姑娘，盯着球场看，顺那目光瞧去，果然是自家姑娘。看来乔公子还未完全放下姑娘。
[EN] In truth, Miao Zhiyu could not bear sitting there another minute watching Qiao Wenxian stare nonstop at the playing field. She wanted fresh air in the gardens by herself. Dropping a curtsy to the elders, she left without waiting for leave.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷重点不在乔公子，很快聊起来：“不知两家的婚礼定在何时？”
[EN] A maid moved the stool beside Madam Qian, and Matron Wei accepted it. One glance at Young Master Qiao showed he paid zero heed to Lady Miao, his eyes locked onto the field—and right onto her own young mistress. So Young Master Qiao had not let go after all.
[ZH] 秦夫人急忙说：“亲家着急，又提前一周，定在三月初三。”
[EN] Matron Wei kept her focus on the elders and struck up conversation. "May I ask when the wedding between your two families has been set?"
[ZH] “算算日子也快到，到时我们望月轩定会送上一份厚礼。”韦嬷嬷如此说，不过是想向众人表明自家姑娘已放下乔家公子。
[EN] "Our in-laws are eager," Madam Qin jumped in, "so they moved it up another week, to the third day of the third month."
[ZH] 除了乔文仙，其他人听到此话，都露出会心的笑容。
[EN] "That is right around the corner," Matron Wei said. "Wangyue Pavilion will be sure to send a generous gift." She said it solely to signal that her mistress had moved on from Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 钱夫人瞧着场上正在捶丸的陈浩宇：“朱陈两家亲上加亲，羡煞旁人，今日我怎么瞧着，都觉朱家姑娘与陈家公子实在相配，也不知他们两个的婚事安排在什么时候？”
[EN] Aside from Qiao Wenxian, every person in the pavilion smiled knowingly.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的口碑并不好，说自家姑娘与其相配，不就是暗含着自家姑娘名声不好？
[EN] Madam Qian looked out at Chen Haoyu swinging on the field. "A second marriage tie between the Zhu and Chen families—everyone is green with envy. The more I look today, the better matched Miss Zhu and Young Master Chen seem. When have the two families set their wedding?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷甚是不满，依然礼貌地回应：“确实令人羡慕，我听说陈公子未来要接替陈家家业，所以陈家长辈希望他能娶一位品貌绝佳，才学一流的女子。朱家姑娘文武双全，是陈家打着灯笼都难找的，因此着急娶回去，日子也定在三月，不过在乔公子之后。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's name was mud across Lin'an; calling her young mistress a fine match for him was a barbed dig at her reputation. Irritation flared in Matron Wei's chest, yet her tone stayed polished. "Envied indeed. I hear Young Master Chen is slated to inherit the Chen family business, so his elders insisted on a bride of peerless virtue, beauty, and learning. My young mistress excels with both brush and fist—the sort of treasure the Chens could search for with a lantern and still not find. That is why they are in such a rush to welcome her home. Their date is in the third month too, just after Young Master Qiao's."
[ZH] 三人听出些不满来。
[EN] The sting in her words did not go unnoticed.
[ZH] 钱氏自知失言：“这个春天真是喜事连连，到时乔家也会送份大礼。”
[EN] Realizing she had spoken out of turn, Madam Qian smoothed things over. "What a spring of double blessings! The Qiao family will send a grand gift when the time comes."
[ZH] 米姨娘紧接着说：“孩子们越来越好，我们听着也高兴，嬷嬷以后可要常与我和姐姐说说话，回到朱府告诉向夫人，我与姐姐甚是欢迎她，以后可要常来参加姐姐举办的活动。”
[EN] Concubine Mi jumped right in. "Hearing the children are thriving delights us all. Matron, you must drop by to chat with Sister and me more often, and when you return to Zhu Manor, tell Madam Xiang she is always welcome at Sister's gatherings."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不好再与乔家人计较：“那是自然，我会专程回趟朱府，将二位的话转达给我家夫人。”
[EN] With nothing more to gain by pressing them, Matron Wei nodded. "Of course. I will make a special trip back to Zhu Manor to pass your message along to my madam."
[ZH] 乔文仙坐在一旁，听着拉家常的话，听出些许意味来，心中不满，一句话不说，起身离开。
[EN] Sitting beside them, Qiao Wenxian caught every undercurrent of the polite fencing. Fed up, he stood and walked off without a word.
[ZH] 米氏大声问：“文渊，你去哪里？”
[EN] "Wenyuan, where are you going?" Concubine Mi called out.
[ZH] 乔文仙并不理会。
[EN] He ignored her.
[ZH] 米氏埋怨道：“这孩子！”
[EN] "That boy!" Concubine Mi grumbled.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷和秦夫人在，不好揭儿子的短，钱氏急忙掩饰：“他还能去哪里？定是去找知瑜，不要管他，聊我们的。”
[EN] Unwilling to air her son's faults in front of Matron Wei and Madam Qin, Madam Qian covered for him at once. "Where else could he be going? Off to find Zhiyu, of course. Leave him be and let us keep talking."
[ZH] 钱氏的解释令秦夫人高兴：“文渊这孩子，刚跟我家知瑜分开就不习惯，婚前感情好，婚后感情会更好。”
[EN] The excuse thrilled Madam Qin. "Wenyuan cannot bear being parted from my Zhiyu even for a moment. So fond before the wedding, they will be closer still once married."
[ZH] 钱氏与米氏更是高兴不已，都“呵呵”笑起来。
[EN] Madam Qian and Concubine Mi chuckled along in delight.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷觉得可笑，这三个人可真会自欺欺人。
[EN] Matron Wei shook her head inside. What a trio of master self-deceivers.

#CH 163
[ZH] 钱氏笑完，面向捶丸场，再次赞叹：“朱家姑娘捶丸技术真不错，与仪王不相上下，瞧这样子陈家兄妹必然败下阵来。”
[ZH] 米氏并不认同：“姐姐此刻下定论还尚早，我一直瞧着呢，他们四人均得三筹，还看不出胜负。”
[ZH] 钱氏是捶丸高手，早已看出结果：“妹妹不常捶丸，自然看不出名堂，前三筹两队虽打了平手，可从技术和力度上来讲，陈家姐弟均不如仪王与朱家姑娘。刚才陈家姐弟仅以中筹取胜，那是对方技术太差，这次碰到厉害的对手，估计到大筹得败下阵来。”
[ZH] 自家姑娘被人夸赞，韦嬷嬷心中高兴，瞧着姑娘又将一球击打到对方的球窝内，甚是欢喜：“我从未见我家姑娘捶丸，也不知她什么时候练得这身本事。”
[ZH] 秦夫人回想起以前的情景：“嬷嬷这么一说，我仔细想想，好像以前朱家姑娘玩过一次，那技术可不怎么样，现在倒是精进不少。”
[ZH] 钱氏想起陈文心刚到捶丸场时所说：“韦嬷嬷您忘了，朱家嫂嫂刚来时说过，是她教的，真是青出于蓝而胜于蓝。”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷呵呵笑着：“瞧我这记性。”
[ZH] 捶丸场上，两队打得并不算激烈，四人边捶丸边聊天儿。千里寻跟申简辰一队，两人必然聊得多些。
[ZH] 申简辰觉得与千里寻组队捶丸的主意真不错，可以光明正大与她走近，也可以毫无顾忌聊天说话，输赢不重要，重要的是捶丸的时间不要过得太快。
[ZH] 乔梦容坐在凉亭内，一直注视哥哥。见他捶丸时，动作有力而矫健，许久以来没有的陌生感再次袭上心头。
[ZH] 小时候，哥哥偶尔还会出去。那是哥哥将她捡回仪王府的第二年，哥哥身体好些时，他们来到府外，见到有一群小孩儿在土地上捶丸，哥哥也参与其中。
[ZH] 哥哥是第一次玩儿，技术并不好，球根本不听使唤，怎么都进不了球窝，哥哥最终放弃，还说以后再也不捶丸。他说到做到，真的再没玩过。
[ZH] 当哥哥找到园林中主动邀请她捶丸时，乔梦容因已渐渐习惯起死回生后的哥哥，并未多想。现在安静下来，用心观察，不由感伤起来，他确实不是以前的哥哥。
[ZH] 喜兰瞧出郡主的心思，在一旁悉心安慰：“王爷现在什么都厉害，您应该高兴，更应该将现在的王爷放在心上。”
[ZH] “喜兰，你说我喜欢的是以前的哥哥还是现在的哥哥？”
[EN] After her laugh faded, Madam Qian turned back to the field and marveled again. "Miss Zhu's chuiwan skill is remarkable, neck and neck with Prince Yi. Looking at how this is going, the Chen siblings are bound to lose."
[ZH] “不管是以前的还是现在的，都是王爷本人。”
[EN] "Too soon to call it, Sister," Concubine Mi disagreed. "I have kept my eyes on them the whole time. Both sides have three tallies each; there is no telling who will win yet."
[ZH] “你说得对，我怎么突然之间又瞎想起来？好久没这么想过。”
[EN] A seasoned player herself, Madam Qian had already read the match. "You seldom play, Sister, so you miss the fine points. They tied the first three tallies, yet in both technique and power, the Chen siblings fall short of Prince Yi and Miss Zhu. The Chens took the Middle Tally earlier only because their rivals played poorly. Against real opponents today, they will fall apart by the Grand Tally."
[ZH] 乔梦容担心白风将刚才的话听去，将凉亭内瞧了一遍，不见白风身影，才放心地问：“白风呢？”
[EN] Hearing her young mistress praised warmed Matron Wei's heart. Watching Shuzhen knock yet another ball cleanly into the opposing hole, she beamed. "I never saw my mistress play chuiwan before. I wonder when she picked up such skill."
[ZH] “苦荞把他叫走了。”
[EN] Madam Qin searched her memory. "Now that you mention it, Matron, I believe Miss Zhu played once before, and her form was nothing special then. She has improved by leaps and bounds."
[ZH] 乔梦容半认真半开玩笑地说：“白风人不错，我本想撮合你们两个，结果被苦荞捷足先登。”
[EN] Madam Qian recalled what Chen Wenxin had remarked upon arriving. "Have you forgotten, Matron Wei? Young Madam Zhu said earlier that she taught her. Truly, the student has surpassed the teacher."
[ZH] “我才不想嫁人，一辈子跟着郡主。”
[EN] Matron Wei chuckled. "Look at my failing memory."
[ZH] 喜兰的话让乔梦容感动，任何语言此时都显得苍白无力，她用沉默回应喜兰，继续向捶丸场瞧去。哥哥与淑真站在一起，边说话边看陈浩宇将球打向球窝。
[EN] Out on the field, the match moved at an easy pace, the four players chatting as they went. Paired together, Qianli Xun and Shen Jianchen naturally talked the most. Shen Jianchen thought partnering up for chuiwan had been a stroke of genius: he could walk openly beside her and talk without restraint. Winning or losing mattered little; he only hoped the match would stretch on as long as possible.
[ZH] 他们看上去很开心，哥哥第一次在她面前与其他女子聊得如此开心。对方要不是淑真，乔梦容绝对会认为哥哥喜欢她。
[EN] From her pavilion, Qiao Mengrong kept her eyes fixed on her brother. Watching his strong, nimble swings, that old sense of strangeness she had not felt in months crept back into her chest.
[ZH] 乔梦容在心中自问：“为何哥哥与我在一起说话时，从未这么开心过？”
[EN] When they were children, a year after her brother had brought her into Prince Yi's Manor and his health had briefly improved, they had wandered outside the gates and come upon a group of boys playing chuiwan on packed dirt. Her brother had joined in for the first time, yet the ball refused to obey him and never went near a hole. He had quit on the spot and sworn never to touch chuiwan again—and he had kept his word.
[ZH] 很快她找到答案，肯定是因为今日春光明媚，捶丸场上又热闹，周围又有那么多人盯着，哥哥要是太严肃，会与氛围不搭，也会让大家产生距离感。
[EN] When he had sought her out in the gardens earlier to play, she had grown so used to the brother who had come back from the brink of death that she had given it little thought. Now, sitting still and watching close, sorrow tugged at her. He truly was not the brother she used to know.
[ZH] 可为何哥哥每次都主动走向淑真，搞得她都没办法与朱家嫂嫂在一起？乔梦容很快又找到答案，肯定是因为哥哥与淑真是队友，必然要在一起商量对策。
[EN] Reading her mistress's mood, Xilan offered gentle comfort. "His Highness excels at everything now. You ought to be glad, and hold the prince he is today in your heart."
[ZH] 想通之后，乔梦容继续欣赏哥哥捶丸。说句心里话，她觉得捶丸实在没意思，要不是哥哥在场，她连看的欲望都没有。
[EN] "Xilan, tell me—do I love the brother from before, or the brother now?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜离开凉亭后，独自一人到园林中散步，回想乔文仙为她头上插花的情景，不由用手摸摸头上的花，乖乖地待在发髻上，她会心一笑，幻想着自己离开，乔文仙会尾随而来。
[EN] "Before or now, they are both His Highness himself."
[ZH] 想着想着，不由朝后瞧瞧，只看到幼凡，并未见乔文仙的踪影。苗知瑜感到失落，继续缓缓散步。
[EN] "You are right. Why am I letting my mind wander again? I have not had thoughts like that in ages." Worried Bai Feng might have overheard, Qiao Mengrong scanned the pavilion, saw no sign of him, and asked in relief, "Where is Bai Feng?"
[ZH] 婚事算是有着落，还要嫁给她心里所想之人，老天待她不薄。可想到纳妾一事，又高兴不起来。
[EN] "Kuqiao called him away."
[ZH] 正想着，身后传来一个声音：“苗姑娘！”
[EN] Half joking and half earnest, Qiao Mengrong sighed. "Bai Feng is a good lad. I had half a mind to match the two of you up, yet Kuqiao beat me to him."
[ZH] 苗知瑜转过身，原来是幽栖居士身边的丫鬟夏果。幽栖居士也真是，自己不能嫁给乔公子，便想把身边的丫鬟放在乔公子身边，也不知打的是什么主意。
[EN] "I never want to marry," Xilan replied. "I mean to stay by Your Highness's side for life."
[ZH] 苗知瑜没好气地问：“唤我何事？”
[EN] Touched beyond words, Qiao Mengrong answered with warm silence and looked back at the field. Her brother stood beside Shuzhen, chatting as they watched Chen Haoyu line up a shot. They looked so cheerful. It was the first time she had ever seen her brother talk with another woman in such bright spirits. Were it anyone other than Shuzhen, she would have sworn he fancied the girl.
[ZH] 夏果走近，像见到主子一般行着丫鬟的礼节：“苗姑娘应该已知，您与乔公子成亲那日，我也会被抬进乔府，以后要在一个屋檐下相处，还望苗姑娘能容得下我。”
[EN] Why had her brother never looked that carefree when talking to her?
[ZH] 幼凡替自家小姐不满：“喂！你这话是什么意思？”
[EN] An answer soon came to her: the spring sun was bright, the field was lively, and dozens of eyes rested on him. Staying stiff would ruin the mood and make him seem aloof. And why did he keep drifting straight to Shuzhen's side, leaving her no chance to chat with her sister-in-law? Because they were teammates, of course—they had to discuss strategy.
[ZH] “我没什么意思，就是想知道苗姑娘的真实想法，也希望到了乔家能与苗姑娘和谐相处。”
[EN] Satisfied with her own reasoning, Qiao Mengrong went back to watching him play. Truth be told, chuiwan bored her stiff; if her brother were not on the field, she would not even bother looking.
[ZH] 苗知瑜将夏果上下打量一番，倒有几分容貌，身段不比幽栖居士差。
[EN] After leaving the pavilion, Miao Zhiyu strolled alone through the gardens. Recalling how Qiao Wenxian had placed the peach blossom in her hair, she reached up to touch the petals nestled neatly by her chignon. A soft smile curved her lips as she imagined him trailing after her.
[ZH] “说句心里话，此事来得突然，我还未完全接受。只是想不通，为何幽栖居士要把你许给文渊？”
[EN] She glanced over her shoulder, only to see Youfan behind her and no trace of Qiao Wenxian. Disappointment dulled her mood as she walked on at a slow pace. Her marriage was settled, and to the man she had longed for—Heaven had been kind to her. Yet the thought of that concubine soured her joy all over again.
[ZH] “苗姑娘别误会，跟我家小姐没任何关系，是……是我喜欢乔公子，求他纳我为妾，开始他并不同意，我求得多了，他见我可怜，便答应下来，今日已向我家小姐当面提出，我家小姐点了头，当时乔家老爷也在场。”
[EN] Just then, a voice called from behind: "Lady Miao!"
[ZH] 夏果说得恳切，不像是编瞎话，苗知瑜态度缓和许多：“你喜欢文渊是什么时候的事？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu turned and saw Xiaguo, Youqi Jushi's maid. Really, what nerve Youqi Jushi had—unable to marry Young Master Qiao herself, she was planting her own maid beside him. What scheme could she be cooking up?
[ZH] “我家小姐第一次见乔公子时。”
[EN] "What do you want?" Miao Zhiyu asked in a cool tone.
[ZH] “第一次？”苗知瑜记得第一次与幽栖居士见文渊时，她的身边只有苦荞一位丫鬟，况且那时幽栖居士心中还有徐公子，“我怎么不记得那时幽栖居士身边有你这等丫鬟。”
[EN] Stepping close, Xiaguo dropped into a proper maid's curtsy, as though greeting her mistress. "Lady Miao must already know that on the day you wed Young Master Qiao, I am to be carried into Qiao Manor too. Since we will live under the same roof, I hope Lady Miao will find room to tolerate me."
[ZH] 夏果意识到她一着急说错话：“不是那个第一次，是乔公子从汴梁回来，我家小姐第一次见他的时候，我便喜欢上乔公子，本想着等小姐嫁给乔公子后，再求小姐答应让乔公子纳了我，可后来却发生那样的事……”
[EN] Youfan flared up on her mistress's behalf. "Hey! What kind of talk is that?"
[ZH] “既然不是幽栖居士故意将你放在文渊身边，我也不必计较。再说，我计较有什么用？乔老爷都答应，以后到乔家，你只要守好本分，我自然容得下你。”
[EN] "I mean no disrespect," Xiaguo replied. "I only wished to learn Lady Miao's true thoughts, and to hope we can live in harmony once we are in the Qiao household."
[ZH] “多谢苗姑娘！”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu looked Xiaguo up and down. The girl had decent looks, and a figure no worse than Youqi Jushi's. "To speak plain, this came out of nowhere and I have not made full peace with it yet. What baffles me is why Youqi Jushi would give you to Wenyuan."
[ZH] “去吧，不要再来打扰我。”
[EN] "Do not misunderstand, Lady Miao. My mistress had nothing to do with it. It was... it was me. I fell in love with Young Master Qiao and begged him to take me as a concubine. At first he refused, yet I pleaded over and over until he took pity on me and agreed. Today he asked my mistress in person, and she nodded yes with Master Qiao standing right there."
[ZH] 夏果行礼离开，高高兴兴原路返回，乔家的捶丸赛对她来讲实在有意义，这么快人生大事得到解决，只等花轿上门。
[EN] Xiaguo's earnest manner sounded genuine enough, and Miao Zhiyu's tone softened. "When did you fall for Wenyuan?"
[ZH] 幼凡瞅着夏果离开的背影，一脸不悦：“小姐，夏果一看就是个不安分的丫头，您应该拿出主母的款儿来，将她压住，省得日后到了乔家骑到您头上。”
[EN] "The very first time my mistress met Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 苗知瑜很不屑：“我是大家闺秀，岂能跟一个乡下丫头计较？乔家是什么人家，怎会把一个丫鬟出身的人当回事？”
[EN] "The first time?" Miao Zhiyu remembered well that when she and Youqi Jushi had first met Wenyuan, only Kuqiao had been at Shuzhen's side—and Shuzhen's heart had still belonged to Young Master Xu back then. "I do not recall Youqi Jushi having a maid like you at that time."
[ZH] 幼凡不再说什么，继续陪自家小姐散步。
[EN] Catching her own slip, Xiaguo hurried to correct herself. "Not that first time! I meant the first time my mistress saw him after he came back from Bianliang. That was when I fell for him. I meant to wait until my mistress married him and then beg her to let him take me in, yet then everything went sideways..."
[ZH] 乔文仙离开凉亭，躲在一个偏僻处瞧着场上的淑真，回想他们曾经约会的甜蜜场面……
[EN] "Since Youqi Jushi did not plant you beside Wenyuan on purpose, I need not fuss over it," Miao Zhiyu said. "Besides, what good would fussing do when Master Qiao has already agreed? Once we are at Qiao Manor, know your place, and I will allow you yours."
[ZH] 渐渐想起淑真与他分手那日的表情，还有她说出的话，比冬日的积雪还要寒冷。再瞧向捶丸场，她与仪王有说有笑，哪像自己还沉浸在失恋的痛苦中。
[EN] "Thank you, Lady Miao!"
[ZH] 乔文仙不由悲叹：“狠心的女人！”
[EN] "Run along now. Do not bother me again."
[ZH] 南双一直陪伴公子，他心里所想已能猜出八九分：“公子别再惦记幽栖居士，您马上要成亲，还要纳……纳妾，多美的事。”
[EN] Xiaguo curtsied and skipped back the way she had come in high spirits. The Qiao chuiwan tournament had paid off beyond her wildest dreams: her future was settled in a single afternoon, leaving only the wedding sedan to arrive.
[ZH] 乔文仙听出些情绪来，他稀里糊涂答应纳夏果为妾，南双不高兴。
[EN] Watching Xiaguo's retreating back, Youfan scowled. "Miss, anyone can tell Xiaguo is a scheming little minx. You should show a primary wife's authority right now and put her in her place, or she will walk all over you once you are in Qiao Manor."
[ZH] “南双，你是不是埋怨我？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu scoffed. "I am a lady from an official household. Why stoop to sparring with a country maid? What sort of family are the Qiaos? Do you think they will ever take a servant girl seriously?"
[ZH] “那是自然，公子又不是不知道我喜欢夏果。”
[EN] Saying no more, Youfan resumed walking beside her mistress.
[ZH] “唉，你跟我都是可怜人，喜欢上得不到的人。”
[EN] Meanwhile, after leaving the pavilion, Qiao Wenxian had hidden in a quiet corner watching Shuzhen on the field, replaying sweet memories of their old outings together. Then his mind drifted to the day she broke things off—her face and her words colder than winter snow. Looking back at the green, he saw her chatting and laughing with Prince Yi, nothing like himself, still drowning in heartbreak.
[ZH] “我跟公子不一样，公子起码还跟幽栖居士相爱过，夏果可从来没把我放在眼里。”
[EN] "Heartless woman!" Qiao Wenxian sighed.
[ZH] “事情成这样，你跟夏果已不可能，以后，我为你找个更好的。”
[EN] Staying close beside his master, Nan Shuang could guess nine-tenths of his thoughts. "Stop dwelling on Youqi Jushi, Young Master. You are about to take a bride—and a... a concubine too. What a grand occasion."
[ZH] 南双很快变得高兴：“公子，我可不可以喜欢幼凡？”
[EN] Catching the sour edge in his tone, Qiao Wenxian knew his impulsive promise to take Xiaguo had stung Nan Shuang. "Do you hold it against me, Nan Shuang?"
[ZH] “幼凡？”乔文仙从未在意过苗知瑜身边的丫鬟，对这个名字一点儿印象都没有。
[EN] "Of course I do! Young Master knew very well that I liked Xiaguo."
[ZH] “是苗姑娘身边的丫鬟，今日她跟我说话态度很好，也答应跟我做朋友。”
[EN] "Ah, you and I are two pitiful souls, both in love with women we cannot have."
[ZH] 南双兴奋的样子，乔文仙羡慕不已，他如果能像南双一样轻易放下一段感情，那该多好。
[EN] "I am not like you, Young Master. At least Youqi Jushi loved you back once. Xiaguo never spared me a single glance."
[ZH] 曹班与安明轩正好从旁边经过，无意中看到乔文仙带着南双躲在不容易被人发现的地方。
[EN] "With things as they are, you and Xiaguo are out of the question. Later, I will find you someone far better."
[ZH] 两人甚觉纳闷，靠近后，曹班问：“乔兄，怎么躲在这里？”
[EN] Nan Shuang perked up at once. "Young Master, may I fancy Youfan?"
[ZH] 乔文仙转过身：“你们什么时候来的？”
[EN] "Youfan?" Having paid zero notice to Miao Zhiyu's maid, Qiao Wenxian drew a total blank.
[ZH] 安明轩说：“早来了，见你要么陪苗姑娘，要么一直跟家人在一起，没敢打扰，现在要是无事，不如与我们一起，东方兄也在。”
[EN] "Lady Miao's maid. She was very pleasant to me today and even agreed to be my friend."
[ZH] “他躲在哪里玩？我怎么在捶丸场没见到他人影儿？”
[EN] Seeing Nan Shuang bounce back with such cheer filled Qiao Wenxian with envy. If only he could let go of a love half as fast.
[ZH] 乔文仙虽心系淑真，倒也没忘记朋友，除寻找过仪王的身影外，也找过三位公子。仪王找起来倒容易，三位公子却不见踪影。
[EN] Just then, Cao Ban and An Mingxuan strolled past and spotted Qiao Wenxian tucked away in the shadows with Nan Shuang. Puzzled, they walked over. "Brother Qiao, why are you hiding back here?" Cao Ban asked.
[ZH] 曹班故意开玩笑：“乔兄的心思都在苗姑娘身上，怎会注意我们在哪里？捶丸太无趣，我们都在场外无人之处野餐，放风筝，要不要一起去？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian turned around. "When did you two get here?"
[ZH] 没等公子说话，南双先兴奋起来：“好啊好啊！我最喜欢放风筝，公子，答应去吧？”
[EN] "Ages ago," An Mingxuan said. "You were either with Lady Miao or sitting with your elders, so we dared not interrupt. If you are free now, come join us. Brother Dongfang is here too."
[ZH] 乔文仙向场上瞧了瞧，淑真又打入一球，她完全沉浸在球飞入球窝的兴奋中，根本不知道他正在一处默默关注她，既如此，还不如去散散心。
[EN] "Where is he hiding out? I did not see hide nor hair of him at the field." Preoccupied with Shuzhen as he was, Qiao Wenxian had not forgotten his friends; Prince Yi had been easy to spot, yet the three scholars had vanished without a trace.
[ZH] “捶丸的确无趣，不如跟你们一起聊聊。”
[EN] "Your mind was all on Lady Miao, so how could you notice us?" Cao Ban teased. "Chuiwan is a bore. We found a quiet stretch outside the field for a picnic and kite-flying. Care to come along?"
[ZH] 捶丸场之外，是一大片草地，春日的阳光洒在草地上，温暖而舒适。
[EN] Before his master could answer, Nan Shuang jumped in excitement. "Yes, yes! I love flying kites! Say yes, Young Master!"
[ZH] 不喜捶丸，又不愿拉关系的年轻人都来这里踏青晒太阳，还有人正在放风筝，风筝飞得老高，似乎要冲入云霄。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian glanced back toward the field. Shuzhen had just sunk another shot, beaming in triumph without a clue that he stood watching from the sidelines. Better to clear his head elsewhere. "Chuiwan is dull enough. I will come chat with you."
[ZH] 乔文仙慨叹陈家的捶丸场竟然也有这么一处好地方，他远远瞧见东方尘泽与几位公子坐在铺有粗布的草地上，粗布上放着一张木桌，桌上吃的喝的均有。
[EN] Beyond the chuiwan grounds stretched a broad meadow bathed in warm spring sunlight. Young people with no taste for chuiwan or social climbing had gathered here to enjoy the spring breeze and send kites soaring high into the clouds.
[ZH] 乔文仙的大妹妹乔文慧也在其中，她与东方尘泽已定亲，凑到一起，倒也正常。
[EN] Marvelling that the Chen estate boasted such a fine meadow, Qiao Wenxian spotted Dongfang Chenze sitting with several young gentlemen on a rough cloth spread over the grass, a low wooden table loaded with food and drink set before them. His eldest sister, Qiao Wenhui, sat among them too—natural enough, since she and Dongfang Chenze were already betrothed.

#CH 164
[ZH] 乔文仙踩踏着刚刚抽出芽的青草，向众人走去。
[ZH] 文慧眼尖，瞧见哥哥，起身迎上去：“哥，你也来了，怎么没带苗姐姐？”
[ZH] 乔文仙不愿提起苗知瑜，并未回答妹妹的问题，转而埋怨道：“你偷偷跑到这么一处好地方玩儿，也不知道叫上哥哥。”
[ZH] “我倒是想叫你，你一进捶丸场，跟丢了魂儿似的，只顾带南双在到处瞧，我叫你几声都不理我。”文慧提到南双，看向哥哥身后，“怎么没见南双？”
[ZH] 安明轩指着不远处：“瞧，在那里，南双一来就跑去抢别人的风筝。”
[ZH] 果然南双正从化雨手中接过风筝，开始玩起来，根本不像刚刚失去心爱之人的样子。
[ZH] 文慧笑着批评：“哥哥以后不能再宠着南双，否则他都不知道自己是伺候哥哥的人。”
[ZH] 乔文仙反过来批评她：“你一个姑娘家跟一群公子在此处玩耍，似有不妥，还是回去凉亭下陪陪娘，或是找文秀玩儿。”
[ZH] “知道了，这就回去。”文慧说完，向身后坐在草地上的东方尘泽笑了笑，辞别哥哥往凉亭处走去。
[ZH] 三人来到东方尘泽身边，其他坐在桌边的公子们让开，都跑去放风筝。于是三人在粗布上坐下，与东方尘泽一起享受春日的阳光。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stepped across the newly sprouted grass toward the group. Sharp-eyed Wenhui spotted her brother first and rose to meet him. "You came too, Brother. Why did you not bring Sister Miao?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽为乔文仙倒上一杯酒酿：“乔兄可来了，我还以为你即将当新郎，有意把我们几个抛到九霄云外。”
[EN] Unwilling to speak Miao Zhiyu's name, Qiao Wenxian brushed past the question and chided her instead. "You sneak off to a pleasant spot like this and do not even call your brother?"
[ZH] 乔文仙端起酒酿一口饮下：“别跟我提当新郎的事，你们又不是不知道我的心思。”
[EN] "I tried calling you! The moment you stepped onto the chuiwan grounds, you wandered around with Nan Shuang as if your soul had left your body. I shouted several times and you never even heard me." Mentioning Nan Shuang, she peered behind him. "Where is Nan Shuang anyway?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽劝道：“乔兄也该走出来，过去的事情就让它过去吧。”
[EN] An Mingxuan pointed a short distance off. "Look over there. The second he arrived, he ran off to hijack someone else's kite."
[ZH] “话虽如此，我就是放不下，真羡慕你跟文慧，有情人终成眷属，为何我就不能娶心爱之人？”乔文仙自顾倒上一杯，又喝起来。
[EN] Sure enough, Nan Shuang was taking a kite spool from Huayu and launching into play, looking nothing like a lad who had just lost his sweetheart.
[ZH] 曹班也劝道：“我刚见幽栖居士与仪王组队捶丸，她看上去很开心，应该已放下，乔兄是男子，更应洒脱些。”
[EN] Wenhui laughed and scolded, "Brother, stop spoiling Nan Shuang, or he will forget he is supposed to be waiting on you."
[ZH] 乔文仙沉默不语，淑真笑得越开心，他会越伤心，又倒一杯，重重喝下。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian turned the scolding right back on her. "A young lady sitting out here among a pack of young gentlemen is hardly proper. Head back to the pavilion to keep Mother company, or go play with Wenxiu."
[ZH] 安明轩向曹班使眼色，他才意识到说错话：“瞧我这张嘴，净说些不该说的，自罚一杯。”
[EN] "Fine, I am going." Wenhui cast a parting smile back at Dongfang Chenze on the grass, bade her brother farewell, and strolled toward the pavilions.
[ZH] 曹班自斟一杯，端起来，向乔文仙谢罪。
[EN] The three young men walked over to Dongfang Chenze, and the other gentlemen around the table gave up their spots to go fly kites. Settling onto the cloth, the quartet soaked up the spring sun.
[ZH] 乔文仙并不理会，依然沉浸在伤感中。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze poured Qiao Wenxian a cup of sweet rice wine. "You made it at last, Brother Qiao. I thought you were so busy getting ready to be a groom that you tossed the rest of us out of your mind."
[ZH] 东方尘泽想到一个新话题：“近期临安城的诗会突然多起来，我听说除了往年常见的诗会外，又出现大大小小不下十个诗会，参加的人数都在五十人以上。什么时候我东方尘泽举行的诗会也能在临安城挂上名头，我也就心满意足。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian knocked the cup back in one gulp. "Do not mention being a groom to me. You all know where my heart lies."
[ZH] 安明轩说：“东方兄一直认为参加诗会之人关键在精，而不是多，怎么突然羡慕起别人来？”
[EN] "It is time to step forward, Brother Qiao," Dongfang Chenze urged. "Let the past stay in the past."
[ZH] “事实证明我以前的想法是错误的，本想着我的诗会有乔兄和幽栖居士撑场面，怎么说也应在临安城挂上名头，可到现在，临安城有几个人知道我举办诗会？也没人愿意挤破头想要得到我的请帖。所以，我认为人多才是关键。”
[EN] "Easier said than done. I just cannot let go. I envy you and Wenhui—lovers united at last. Why can I not marry the woman I love?" He poured himself another cup and drank.
[ZH] 东方尘泽为岔开话题感到满意，尽管乔兄依然不搭话，希望说着说着，他的注意力能够被转移。
[EN] Cao Ban chimed in to comfort him. "I just saw Youqi Jushi teamed up with Prince Yi for chuiwan. She looked cheerful enough—she has probably moved on. As a man, Brother Qiao, you ought to take it in stride."
[ZH] 曹班看出东方尘泽的心思，继续讨论岔开的话题：“我给东方兄出个主意，自去年中秋节后，你收集到不少幽栖居士的诗词，不如拿出来，再广发请帖，相信很多人会慕名而来。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian fell mute. The brighter Shuzhen smiled, the deeper his heart ached. He poured another cup and downed it hard.
[ZH] “好主意，就这么定了。”东方尘泽转向乔文仙，“乔兄，到时你必须来，给我撑场面。”
[EN] An Mingxuan shot Cao Ban a sharp look, and Cao Ban realized he had put his foot in his mouth. "Listen to my big mouth, always saying the wrong thing. I will drink a penalty cup." He poured one out and raised it in apology.
[ZH] 乔文仙又喝下一杯酒酿：“好，既是她的诗词，我当然会去，才不辜负曾经我们互相视对方为知己的情分。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian paid no attention, lost in gloom.
[ZH] 三位公子互相看看，无可奈何，只能继续瞎聊着。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze cast about for a fresh subject. "Poetry gatherings have been popping up all over Lin'an lately. On top of the usual annual salons, I hear at least ten new ones have sprouted up, each drawing over fifty guests. When will a poetry gathering hosted by Dongfang Chenze make a name for itself across Lin'an? That would make me content."
[ZH] 捶丸场上，两队已打到中筹，当陈文心将球打入对方的球窝内，双方依然是平局，也终于进入大筹阶段。
[EN] "You always swore quality mattered more than headcount," An Mingxuan pointed out. "Why envy big crowds all of a sudden?"
[ZH] 裁判将球在球基处放好，指着更远处的球窝：“一人一棒，决定胜负。”
[EN] "Turns out my old view was dead wrong. I figured having Brother Qiao and Youqi Jushi headline my salon would put it on the map, yet how many people in Lin'an even know I host one? Nobody is clawing over one another for my invitations. So yes, numbers are the key."
[ZH] 四人看去，那球窝所在的地势较高，没有插任何旗子。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze felt pleased with his change of topic; even if Brother Qiao remained quiet, maybe listening would pull his thoughts away from heartbreak.
[ZH] 陈文心想放弃：“我看我还是主动认输，能打到这个份上，已经算不错，那么高，我可打不进去。”
[EN] Catching his friend's intent, Cao Ban kept the ball rolling. "Here is an idea, Brother Dongfang. Since last Mid-Autumn Festival, you have collected a fair stack of Youqi Jushi's poems and lyrics. Bring those out and send invitations far and wide. Half the city will flock to your door on reputation alone."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不想放弃：“姐，别说丧气话，打完才知输赢。”
[EN] "Brilliant! Consider it done." Dongfang Chenze turned to Qiao Wenxian. "Brother Qiao, you have to come and back me up when the time comes."
[ZH] 听了弟弟的话，陈文心决定试一试。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian drank yet another cup of sweet rice wine. "Count me in. If it features her verses, of course I will go—if only to honor the days when we called each other kindred spirits."
[ZH] 千里寻接触到申简辰询问的目光，点点头，表示自己没有任何问题。
[EN] The three friends exchanged helpless glances and carried on with idle chatter.
[ZH] 凉亭里看的人多起来，毕竟是最后一局，大家都想目睹花落谁家。
[EN] Back on the chuiwan field, both teams had fought through the Middle Tally. When Chen Wenxin sank her ball into the opposing hole, the match stayed deadlocked, pushing them into the Grand Tally.
[ZH] 仪王第一个打，摆好姿势后，目测距离，使出适当的力气，将球从地上击打出去，球在空中划过漂亮的弧线，乖乖落入高高的球窝内。
[EN] The referee set the ball on the pad and gestured toward a distant hole perched on high ground with no flag beside it. "One stroke each. This decides the match."
[ZH] 乔梦容的心提到嗓子眼儿，见进了球，脱口而出：“我就知道哥哥行！”
[EN] Seeing the steep rise, Chen Wenxin nearly gave up. "Maybe I should concede right now. Getting this far is already plenty. I will never clear a slope that high."
[ZH] 钱氏赞道：“仪王真不愧是官家的儿子，球打得实在漂亮！”
[EN] "Do not talk defeat before we swing, Sis," Chen Haoyu protested. "We will not know who wins until we play."
[ZH] 林觉留下夫人，独自跑去乔守仁等有地位之人的凉亭下，互相觥筹交错。朱景元也在其中。众人都向捶丸场看去，见仪王将球打入，个个赞不绝口。
[EN] Buoyed by her brother, Chen Wenxin decided to give it a try. Qianli Xun caught Shen Jianchen's questioning gaze and gave a crisp nod to show she had no trouble at all.
[ZH] 接下来该陈文心，她自知很难进球，还是努力打着，也不知是力道小，还是地势过高，球落在球窝附近，滚落下来。
[EN] More onlookers gathered at the pavilion rails now that the final round had arrived, eager to see who would claim the prize.
[ZH] 乔守仁议论道：“朱家少夫人球技在女子中也算厉害，能打到这个份上已然不错。”
[EN] Prince Yi stepped up first. Setting his stance and gauging the distance, he struck with measured force. The ball traced a clean arc through the air and dropped neatly into the elevated hole.
[ZH] 朱景元搭话道：“乔老爷说得是，不过文心的球技与仪王比起来还是相差甚远。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's heart had climbed into her throat; the instant it landed, she blurted out, "I knew Brother could do it!"
[ZH] 第三个是千里寻，申简辰能将球打入，她更没问题。可就这么轻松打入，太没意思，不如来点儿特别的。千里寻将拳法的功力通过球杆传到球上，再使力气将球打出。
[EN] "Truly His Majesty's son!" Madam Qian applauded. "What a gorgeous shot!"
[ZH] 只见那球被打到空中后，竟然转起圈来，看的人都被吸引住。
[EN] Having left his bride behind, Lin Jue had wandered over to the pavilion of Lin'an's eminent men—Qiao Shouren and Zhu Jingyuan among them—where wine cups clinked back and forth. Every man turned to watch the field and erupted in praise at Prince Yi's stroke.
[ZH] 钱氏一脸惊讶：“第一次见这种打法，韦嬷嬷，朱家姑娘真厉害，不知那球能不能落在球窝内。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin stepped up next. Knowing her odds were slim, she gave it her best swing anyway, yet either her force fell short or the slope proved too steep; the ball kissed the rim of the hole and rolled back down.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷用骄傲的语气说：“我家姑娘能这么打，肯定会掉进去。”
[EN] "Young Madam Zhu is formidable among women players," Qiao Shouren remarked. "Reaching this stage is already impressive."
[ZH] 捶丸场上，申简辰在心里笑道：“你从来都喜欢跟别人不一样，捶丸都能玩出新花样。”
[EN] "Master Qiao is kind," Zhu Jingyuan replied. "Still, Wenxin's skill falls far short of Prince Yi's."
[ZH] 陈浩宇十分不屑：“朱淑真，这么个玩儿法，小心一会儿掉下来，还不如我姐姐打得离球窝近。”
[EN] Qianli Xun came third. If Shen Jianchen could make the shot, she certainly could—yet sinking it plain and simple felt too dull. Why not put on a little show? Channeling her martial internal strength down the club shaft into the ball, she swung.
[ZH] 千里寻瞪了陈浩宇一眼：“要你多嘴！”
[EN] The ball shot skyward and began spinning in tight circles midair, holding every spectator spellbound.
[ZH] 陈文心见二人掐起来，赶快阻止：“浩宇，你少说两句！”
[EN] Madam Qian gaped. "I have never seen a stroke like that! Matron Wei, Miss Zhu is extraordinary—do you think it will drop in?"
[ZH] 陈文心说话之际，千里寻将手中提前捡的石子扔出，那石子正好打在空中旋转的球上，因受石子力道作用，球停止旋转，向球窝冲去，如同提前设计好一般，瞄准球窝轻松落下。
[EN] "If my mistress hit it that way," Matron Wei said with pride, "in it goes."
[ZH] 观看的人都不由发出赞叹声：“好！”
[EN] Out on the field, Shen Jianchen smiled to himself. You always have to outdo everyone else—even turning chuiwan into a stunt show.
[ZH] 裁判说：“输赢已定，不必继续。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu snorted. "Show off like that, Zhu Shuzhen, and it will drop straight short—worse than my sister's shot."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不情愿：“虽已定，我还想把最后一球打了，也让大家赞赞我的球技。”
[EN] "Nobody asked for your commentary!" Qianli Xun shot back.
[ZH] 裁判是捶丸场的人，捶丸场又是陈家的，当然会给陈家公子面子：“公子，请。”
[EN] "Hold your tongue, Haoyu!" Chen Wenxin intervened before they could start snapping at each other.
[ZH] 陈浩宇走到球基跟前，挥杆而上，只见那球飞得老高，只可惜半道落下。
[EN] Even as Chen Wenxin spoke, Qianli Xun flicked a pebble she had palmed beforehand. The stone struck the spinning ball dead on in midair; knocked out of its spin by the impact, the ball shot forward as if guided by design and dropped straight into the hole.
[ZH] 申简辰心中不屑：“切！如此球技还想娶我喜欢的人！要不是她拒绝我，你小子这会儿早已与她退婚！”
[EN] A chorus of cheers rolled across the pavilions: "Bravo!"
[ZH] 千里寻不会放过任何奚落陈浩宇的机会：“陈浩宇，你这球打得比我嫂嫂差远了。”
[EN] "The match is decided," the referee announced. "No need to continue."
[ZH] 陈浩宇心态倒好：“仪王与你都那么厉害，本公子输了，也不丢人。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu balked. "Decided or not, I still want my final swing so everyone can admire my form."
[ZH] 千里寻无意中抬头，见远处的空中有风筝随风飘动，兴奋地说：“你们看！有人在放风筝！”
[EN] Since the grounds belonged to the Chen family, the referee gave Young Master Chen his face. "Go right ahead, Young Master."
[ZH] 申简辰靠近她：“一会儿我们过去放风筝？”
[EN] Stepping to the pad, Chen Haoyu swung hard. The ball soared high—only to plummet halfway short of the target.
[ZH] 千里寻还未回答，陈文心过来行礼：“仪王，两队比赛已结束，该去领彩头，我要带淑真离开，彩头交给仪王。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen scoffed inwardly. Hah! Skill like that, and he thinks he can marry the woman I love? If she had not turned me down, you would be out of an engagement by now!
[ZH] 申简辰意识到他太过主动，引起朱家少夫人怀疑，只得说：“请便。”
[EN] Never one to miss a chance to needle Chen Haoyu, Qianli Xun jeered, "Your shot was miles worse than my sister-in-law's, Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] 陈文心拉起淑真，喊了声：“浩宇！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu took it in stride. "Both Prince Yi and you are monsters out here. Losing to the two of you is no shame at all."
[ZH] 陈浩宇将手中的球杆递给一旁的裁判，向仪王行了礼，跟姐姐离开。
[EN] Glancing up by chance, Qianli Xun spotted kites bobbing in the distant sky and brightened. "Look! People are flying kites!"
[ZH] 目送三人离去的背影，他们才是一家人，申简辰甚觉伤感。
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped beside her. "Shall we go fly kites in a bit?"
[ZH] 他不愿千里寻就这么走了，还是赶紧拿了彩头，再想办法。
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could answer, Chen Wenxin stepped up and curtsied. "Your Highness, the match is over and the prize awaits. I must take Shuzhen back now, so I will leave the prize to you."
[ZH] 彩头无需仪王亲自动手去拿。白风跟苦荞聊得差不多，回到凉亭时，正好见王爷赢了，冲下凉亭替王爷拿彩头，金灿灿的如意被他高高举起。
[EN] Realizing he had pushed too close and caught Young Madam Zhu's suspicion, Shen Jianchen gave a polite nod. "As you wish."
[ZH] 裁判在一旁高呼：“仪王队先得大筹！赢！”
[EN] Chen Wenxin took Shuzhen's hand and called, "Haoyu!" Handing his club to the referee, Chen Haoyu bowed to Prince Yi and trailed after his sister.
[ZH] 钱氏已换好衣装，手拿球杆，走到仪王跟前：“仪王，可否再与朱家姑娘组队与我对打？”
[EN] Watching the three walk off together—looking every inch a real family—sent a cold pang through Shen Jianchen. Unwilling to let Qianli Xun slip away so soon, he moved to wrap up the prize and think of a plan.
[ZH] 申简辰已对捶丸失去兴趣，心里想着怎么约千里寻去放风筝，借此机会与她亲近，故而直接说出拒绝的话来：“外祖母盛情邀请，本不应拒绝，怎奈我有些累，朱家姑娘也说想休息一会儿，因此我还是回凉亭欣赏外祖母的球技。”
[EN] Not that he had to fetch it himself. Having wrapped up his chat with Kuqiao and returned just in time to see his master win, Bai Feng dashed onto the field, claimed the gold ruyi scepter, and hoisted it gleaming into the air.
[ZH] “既如此，仪王请便。”钱氏只能去寻找新的对手。
[EN] "Prince Yi's team takes the Grand Tally! Victory!" the referee proclaimed.
[ZH] 通过这次捶丸，陈文心总觉得仪王对淑真有些不对劲儿。他要么瞅准机会靠近淑真，要么对淑真说些与王爷身份不符的话。
[EN] Already changed into playing clothes with a club in hand, Madam Qian approached Prince Yi. "Your Highness, would you and Miss Zhu care to pair up again for a match against me?"
[ZH] 她担心仪王对淑真动了其他心思，边拉着淑真向凉亭走，边批评亲弟弟：“浩宇，眼看要跟淑真成亲，你也不知道带淑真到处走走？”
[EN] Having lost all interest in chuiwan and thinking only of how to get Qianli Xun out to the kite meadow, Shen Jianchen turned her down straight away. "I would not dare refuse Grandmother's gracious invitation, yet I am a bit winded, and Miss Zhu mentioned wanting a rest too. Better I return to the pavilion and admire Grandmother's form from there."
[ZH] 千里寻立刻说：“别！我可不愿跟陈浩宇走在一起。”
[EN] "As Your Highness pleases," Madam Qian said, turning to look for another opponent.
[ZH] 陈浩宇得意道：“瞧，不是我不乐意，是她不同意，我去别处去逛，你们忙。”
[EN] Walking back toward the pavilion, Chen Wenxin could not shake her unease. Throughout the match, Prince Yi had acted odd around Shuzhen, grabbing every chance to edge close and dropping remarks ill-fitting for a prince. Worried he might harbor untoward designs on Shuzhen, she scolded her younger brother along the way. "Haoyu, your wedding to Shuzhen is right around the corner, and you do not even think to take her for a stroll?"
[ZH] 陈文心很无奈，她再使劲儿一点用处都没有，这俩人根本不当回事。
[EN] "No thanks!" Qianli Xun jumped in at once. "I have zero desire to walk anywhere with Chen Haoyu."
[ZH] 她跟淑真上了凉亭，韦嬷嬷已回来，见到自家姑娘，当面夸赞：“姑娘的球打得前无古人，后无来者。”
[EN] "See?" Chen Haoyu grinned. "Not my fault—she is the one turning me down. I am off to look around elsewhere. Enjoy yourselves."
[ZH] “嬷嬷是自家人，您夸我，我就不客气，照单收下。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin sighed in defeat. Push as she might, neither of the two cared a whit.
[ZH] 大家都被逗乐。
[EN] Back in the pavilion, Matron Wei had already returned and heaped praise on her young mistress. "Miss played like nobody before or since!"
[ZH] 千里寻刚想坐下，瞧见梦容带喜兰向这边走来：“梦容又来找我，我去迎她。”
[EN] "Since Matron is family, I will accept the compliment without false modesty."
[ZH] 千里寻快步走出凉亭，来到梦容跟前：“梦容，你是不是又要约我去散步？”
[EN] Laughter rippled through the pavilion.
[ZH] “我是来约你，并不是散步，我想去放风筝，能不能陪我？”
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun moved to sit, she spotted Mengrong approaching with Xilan. "Mengrong is coming for me again. I will go meet her." Stepping down from the pavilion, she greeted her friend. "Coming to drag me out for another walk, Mengrong?"
[ZH] 陈文心听得一清二楚，也走出凉亭：“佳和郡主约我家淑真去放风筝，不知仪王可去？”
[EN] "Coming to invite you, yes, though not for a walk. I want to fly kites—will you come with me?"
[ZH] “我走到哪里哥哥都会陪着，他自然会去。”
[EN] Overhearing every word, Chen Wenxin stepped out of the pavilion. "Princess Jiahe is inviting our Shuzhen to fly kites—is Prince Yi going too?"
[ZH] 佳和郡主的话令陈文心不安，又一想，仪王与佳和郡主有婚约，她都没多想，自己瞎操心个什么劲儿。也许因淑真与佳和郡主是闺中密友，仪王才对淑真不一样。
[EN] "Brother accompanies me wherever I go, so of course he is coming."
[ZH] “淑真，既然郡主亲自来约，你就跟着去玩会儿吧。”
[EN] The answer poked at Chen Wenxin's nerves again, yet she talked herself down. Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe were betrothed; if the princess saw nothing amiss, why was she fretting over shadows? Perhaps Prince Yi treated Shuzhen with special favor simply because she was the princess's closest friend.
[ZH] “谢谢嫂嫂！”
[EN] "Since Her Highness invited you in person, Shuzhen, go have fun for a while."
[ZH] 千里寻拉起梦容：“我们走吧，不知放风筝的地方在哪里？”
[EN] "Thanks, Sister-in-Law!" Qianli Xun linked arms with Mengrong. "Let us go. Where is the kite meadow?"
[ZH] “哥哥说就在捶丸场后面的草地上。”两人高高兴兴往草地方向走去。
[EN] "Brother says it is on the big meadow right behind the chuiwan grounds."
[ZH] 三个丫鬟都要跟上，韦嬷嬷阻拦道：“红豆去即可，你们两个留下。”
[EN] The two headed off in bright spirits. All three of Shuzhen's maids moved to follow, only for Matron Wei to step in front of them. "Hongdou alone will do. You two stay here."
[ZH] 红豆听后赶紧跟上小姐。
[EN] Hongdou hurried after her mistress at once.
[ZH] 苦荞甚是不解：“嬷嬷为何要拦我们？”
[EN] "Why stop us, Matron?" Kuqiao asked in puzzlement.
[ZH] “你们也不瞧瞧，郡主身边只带一位，仪王身边的侍卫还站在凉亭里，姑娘却要带三个，岂不是压贵人一头？”
[EN] "Use your eyes," Matron Wei replied. "Her Highness brings only one maid, and Prince Yi's guard is still standing in his pavilion. If our young mistress brings three attendants, would that not look like she is trying to outshine royalty?"
[ZH] 苦荞见白风没去，便不再说什么。
[EN] Noticing Bai Feng had stayed put, Kuqiao let the matter drop.
[ZH] 夏果甚是不解：“以前小姐身边总是带着我和苦荞两个，也没见郡主说什么，依然跟小姐关系那么好，我看嬷嬷是心思太细，想得太多。”
[EN] Xiaguo, however, frowned. "Miss always brought both Kuqiao and me along before, and Her Highness never minded—they stayed close as ever. Matron is being way too fussy."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷瞪了夏果一眼：“苦荞都没说什么，你快成别人的妾室，还说个什么劲儿？以前姑娘带两个尚可，若一下子带三个，太过招摇，必然引起他人非议。”
[EN] Matron Wei fixed Xiaguo with a sharp glare. "Kuqiao did not complain, so what are you mouthing off about when you are about to become another family's concubine? Bringing two maids was passable before, yet marching around with three is flashy and begs for gossip."
[ZH] 夏果对这个回答并不满意：“嬷嬷既然如此说，小姐收红豆时，您为何不阻止？哪怕小姐出门时，您让她别带上红豆也行，省得这会儿又拦我们两个。”
[EN] Unconvinced, Xiaguo pushed back. "If you feel that way, Matron, why did you not stop Miss from taking Hongdou on? Or tell her to leave Hongdou home today instead of blocking the two of us now?"
[ZH] “你的嘴皮子倒利索，姑娘以后不用担心你到乔家吃亏。至于为什么，不是你该关心的事，总之，姑娘去放风筝，你们两个就是不能跟着。”
[EN] "Sharp little tongue you have there," Matron Wei retorted. "Miss need not worry about you getting bullied at Qiao Manor. As for my reasons, that is none of your concern. Bottom line: when Miss goes to fly kites, the two of you stay put."
[ZH] 夏果无话可问，便老实待着，反正她的人生已有着落，其他都无所谓。
[EN] Left with nothing to say, Xiaguo sat back down. Her own future was locked in anyway; none of the rest mattered to her.

#CH 165
[ZH] 陈文心对韦嬷嬷的判断深信不疑，也帮忙劝说两个丫鬟：“你们两个听韦嬷嬷的话准没错，整日伺候你家小姐，还不趁此机会好好歇着。”
[ZH] 陈文心边说边朝捶丸场上瞧去，钱氏已找人组好队，她的对手是林觉与其夫人。
[ZH] 两个丫鬟不再多言，韦嬷嬷也不再说什么，她虽对夏果不满，也觉夏果刚刚问的有些道理。姑娘心好，总喜欢给身边收丫鬟，却不知身边丫鬟多了，也不见得是好事。
[ZH] 收夏果时，韦嬷嬷不在，无法阻拦；收红豆时，韦嬷嬷在，姑娘却深受情伤，又被定下不满意的婚约，心里苦，韦嬷嬷便纵着她。
[ZH] 重要的是夏果心不在姑娘身边，苦荞迟早也会出嫁，总得给姑娘身边再培养一个贴心的丫鬟，红豆既勤快又老实，无疑是最佳人选。
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 在捶丸场时，眼界受限，千里寻根本看不出捶丸场之外还有一处放风筝之地。
[ZH] “梦容，你怎知道后面能放风筝？”
[ZH] “哥哥告诉我的，本来没想着去，他见我不喜捶丸，太无聊，主动要求带我去放风筝。”
[ZH] “仪王前来捶丸，还随身带了风筝？”
[ZH] “那倒没有，哥哥说陈家在后面的草地上准备了风筝。”
[ZH] 千里寻半信半疑，跟着梦容继续走着，越走视野越开阔。渐渐，眼前竟然出现一片比捶丸场还大的草地。极目远眺，看不到边。
[EN] Trusting Matron Wei's judgment without question, Chen Wenxin backed her up to soothe the two maids. "Listen to Matron Wei—she is never wrong. You wait on your mistress all day long, so take the chance to catch your breath." As she spoke, she looked out over the field, where Madam Qian had formed a fresh team and stood squared off against Lin Jue and his bride.
[ZH] 千里寻不由慨叹：“真是踏青的好去处！”
[EN] Neither maid said another word, and Matron Wei held her peace as well. Irritated as she was with Xiaguo, she admitted the girl had a point. Her mistress had a soft heart and loved taking in maids, not realizing a swollen retinue brought trouble of its own. Matron Wei had been away when Xiaguo was taken in; when Hongdou joined, her mistress had been reeling from heartbreak and saddled with an unwanted betrothal, so Matron Wei had indulged her. More to the point, Xiaguo's heart was no longer with her mistress and Kuqiao would marry sooner or later. Someone loyal and dependable had to be trained up to take their place, and hardworking, honest Hongdou was the clear choice.
[ZH] “难道比西湖还美？”
[EN] Back on the chuiwan grounds, the surrounding walls and pavilions had blocked the view, so Qianli Xun had never guessed a kite meadow lay beyond. "How did you know we could fly kites back here, Mengrong?"
[ZH] “西湖当然美，只不过经常去，已没新鲜感，这片草地我可是第一次来，该不会也是陈家的？”
[EN] "Brother told me. I had no plans to come, yet he saw how bored I was with chuiwan and offered to bring me out."
[ZH] 申简辰有意走过来，听到千里寻发问，主动回答：“当然是陈家的。”
[EN] "Did Prince Yi pack kites along for a chuiwan match?"
[ZH] 当着梦容的面，千里寻还是得行礼：“仪王殿下。”
[EN] "No, Brother said the Chen family set up kites on the back meadow."
[ZH] “感谢幽栖居士与我组队，彩头我可不能一个人独占，我已吩咐白风将彩头给苦荞。”
[EN] Half skeptical, Qianli Xun walked on beside Mengrong as the path opened wide. Soon a meadow even larger than the chuiwan field unfolded before them, stretching green as far as the eye could see.
[ZH] “那我岂不是独占了仪王的彩头？”
[EN] "What a splendid spot for a spring outing!" Qianli Xun exclaimed.
[ZH] “无妨，我是王爷，身边什么东西没有，一个金如意就别跟我客气。”
[EN] "Even lovelier than West Lake?"
[ZH] 千里寻还想说“我也不在乎”时，乔梦容劝道：“淑真，你就拿着吧，都是哥哥的一片心意，也是我的一片心意。”
[EN] "West Lake is gorgeous, of course, yet I go there so often the novelty has worn off. This meadow is brand new to me. Do not tell me the Chens own this too?"
[ZH] 千里寻爽快答应：“既是梦容的一片心意，我就不客气。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked up just in time to catch her question. "Of course it belongs to the Chen family."
[ZH] 申简辰指着不远处坐在草地上扎风筝的人：“梦容，我带你过去买风筝。”
[EN] With Mengrong right beside them, Qianli Xun dipped into a curtsy. "Your Highness Prince Yi."
[ZH] 乔梦容拉起淑真：“好，哥哥也要给淑真买一个。”
[EN] "Thank you for teaming up with me, Youqi Jushi," Shen Jianchen said. "I cannot hog the prize all to myself, so I had Bai Feng hand it over to Kuqiao."
[ZH] “好，你们两个一人一个。”申简辰答应的同时寻思，待会儿怎么找机会与千里寻一起放风筝。
[EN] "Does that not mean I am hogging Your Highness's prize instead?"
[ZH] 来到扎风筝处，待两位姑娘挑好后，申简辰问：“风筝怎么卖？”
[EN] "No matter. As a prince, I have all the treasures I could ask for. Do not stand on ceremony over one gold ruyi scepter."
[ZH] 扎风筝的老人回道：“不要银子，你们喜欢哪个，随便挑。”
[EN] Qianli Xun opened her mouth to say she did not care for it either, yet Qiao Mengrong cut in. "Take it, Shuzhen. It is a token of goodwill from Brother—and from me too."
[ZH] 千里寻关切地问：“这么好看的风筝，都是你辛苦扎的，不要钱，你怎么生活？”
[EN] "If it is a token from Mengrong, then I will accept without fuss," Qianli Xun agreed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇正好走来，青瓜跟在身后，听到朱淑真的问题，觉得十分可笑。
[EN] Shen Jianchen pointed toward an old man sitting on the grass not far away, framing bamboo kites. "Come, Mengrong, let me buy you a kite."
[ZH] “这片草地是陈家的，扎风筝的人也是陈家的，陈家给他工钱，比他在外面风餐露宿卖风筝强多了。”
[EN] "Yes, and buy one for Shuzhen too, Brother," Qiao Mengrong said, pulling her friend along.
[ZH] 老人放下风筝，站起来问好：“陈公子好！”
[EN] "Alright, one for each of you," Shen Jianchen agreed, already scheming how to steal a turn flying kites with Qianli Xun.
[ZH] “忙你的吧。”陈浩宇见贵人，还得行礼，“仪王，佳和郡主，二位贵人放风筝时若需有人指导，尽管告诉我，捶丸场的下人都会，我为二位找两个。”
[EN] Once the two girls chose their kites, Shen Jianchen asked, "How much for these?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇出现，申简辰本就不爽，又凑到跟前，实在令人不悦，他用更冷淡的语气说：“不必，本王放风筝的技术难道还比不上一个下人？退下吧，不必前来打扰。”
[EN] "No silver needed," the old kite-maker answered. "Pick whichever ones you fancy."
[ZH] 陈浩宇听出仪王对他依然有成见，还是赶紧撤，于是恭敬行礼：“二位贵人忙。”
[EN] Concern furrowed Qianli Xun's brow. "You put all this labor into making such fine kites—if you take no money, how do you make a living?"
[ZH] 千里寻对陈浩宇只顾巴结仪王十分不满，陈浩宇正好瞧向她，便趁机狠狠瞪他一眼。
[EN] Just then Chen Haoyu sauntered up with Qinggua trailing behind and burst out laughing at the question. "This meadow belongs to the Chens, and the kite-maker works for the Chens. We pay him regular wages—miles better than sleeping rough on the streets hawking kites."
[ZH] 陈浩宇则露出不屑的表情。
[EN] Setting his work down, the old man stood and greeted him. "Good day, Young Master Chen!"
[ZH] 这一幕被乔梦容看到，待陈浩宇离开后，关切地说：“淑真，你们两个看上去很不合，你嫁给他，以后该怎么办？”
[EN] "Carry on with your work," Chen Haoyu told him, then turned to bow to the royal pair. "Your Highness Prince Yi, Princess Jiahe—if either of Your Highnesses wants pointers on flying kites, just say the word. The servants here know how, and I can fetch a couple for you."
[ZH] “什么看上去不合，事实上就是不合，放心，我不会嫁给他。”
[EN] Already annoyed by Chen Haoyu's appearance, Shen Jianchen turned colder still at the man's intrusion. "Unnecessary. Do you think this prince flies a kite worse than a servant? Dismissed. Do not disturb us."
[ZH] “可以吗？婚姻向来是长辈做主，只怕……”
[EN] Sensing Prince Yi's lingering hostility, Chen Haoyu bowed low to beat a hasty retreat. "I leave Your Highnesses to your pleasure."
[ZH] 申简辰趁机再次表露心迹：“恐怕婚约挡着，你不得不嫁，除非找一个能压过朱陈两家之人，答应嫁给他，退婚之事尚可成功。”
[EN] Disgusted by his bootlicking, Qianli Xun caught Chen Haoyu's eye and shot him a fierce glare. Chen Haoyu answered with a disdainful sneer.
[ZH] 千里寻明白申简辰的意思，上次没答应他，这次更不会答应。令她不安的是，梦容还在身边，他竟然不管不顾，说出如此隐晦又引人怀疑的话，
[EN] Qiao Mengrong caught the exchange. Once Chen Haoyu walked off, she asked with concern, "Shuzhen, the two of you do not seem to get along at all. What will you do after you marry him?"
[ZH] 手里拿着风筝，行礼十分不便，千里寻还是郑重屈身谢道：“淑真感谢仪王关怀，自己的事还得自己解决，依靠他人也无非是用一段婚姻代替另一段婚姻而已，淑真已决定这辈子谁也不嫁。”
[EN] "Not just 'seem'—we flat-out cannot stand each other. Relax, I am not marrying him."
[ZH] 乔梦容正在思忖哥哥的建议：“能压得过朱陈两家之人，在整个临安城恐怕只有哥哥。哥哥说这话是什么意思？难道他看在淑真与我关系好，想要帮她？”
[EN] "Is that even possible? Parents decide marriages. I fear..."
[ZH] 乔梦容越想越担忧，开口说：“哥哥的建议别说淑真不同意，我听了也觉不妥，目前临安城能压过朱陈两家之人非哥哥莫属，可哥哥与我有婚约……”
[EN] Seizing the opening, Shen Jianchen let his true intent slip through. "With the betrothal in place, you will have no choice but to wed—unless you find someone who outranks both the Zhu and Chen families and agree to marry him instead. Only then could the engagement be broken."
[ZH] 千里寻担心梦容看出申简辰的心思，赶紧解释：“梦容，千万别误会，仪王肯定不是那个意思，他是看在我是你闺蜜的份上，才会这么说。”
[EN] Qianli Xun knew full well what he meant. She had refused him before, and she would refuse him again. What rattled her was his recklessness—dropping hints that transparent right in front of Mengrong! Holding her kite made curtsying awkward, yet she still sank into a solemn bow. "Shuzhen thanks Your Highness for your concern, yet I must settle my own affairs. Leaning on someone else only trades one marriage for another. Shuzhen has already decided never to marry anyone in this life."
[ZH] 申简辰很想告诉乔梦容，他喜欢的人是谁，又见不得千里寻为难，很不情愿地解释道：“你们两个都别多想，我随口说说而已，只是想警告幽栖居士，想要退婚，难上加难。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong mulled over her brother's words. Who in all of Lin'an could outrank both the Zhu and Chen families? Only Brother himself. What had he meant by that? Was he offering to step in because Shuzhen was her friend? Unease gripped her. "Never mind Shuzhen refusing—even I think Brother's idea is improper. Right now, nobody in Lin'an outranks both families except Brother, yet Brother is betrothed to me..."
[ZH] 听了哥哥的解释，乔梦容才放松心情：“确实难，与林觉和离之事，哥哥还能帮你，可退婚之事哥哥也无能为力。”
[EN] Terrified Mengrong might see straight through him, Qianli Xun jumped in. "Do not get the wrong idea, Mengrong! Prince Yi never meant that. He only spoke out of concern because I am your closest friend."
[ZH] “你与仪王已帮我很多，我打心里感激不尽。仪王以后不必再因乔家而帮我，我……我早已不怪乔家……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen burned to tell Qiao Mengrong outright whom he loved, yet seeing Qianli Xun cornered held him back. He forced out a stiff explanation. "Do not overthink it, either of you. I spoke off the cuff—just warning Youqi Jushi how impossible breaking off a betrothal is."
[ZH] 申简辰猜到千里寻提乔家，不过是寻一个合理的解释，以免梦容多想。
[EN] His words eased the knot in Qiao Mengrong's chest. "Impossible indeed. Brother could help with your divorce from Lin Jue, yet even he is powerless against a betrothal."
[ZH] 乔梦容比刚才更显轻松，原来哥哥为淑真出主意，不过是因乔家对朱家食言，硬生生拆散两个相爱之人。
[EN] "You and Prince Yi have already helped me more than I can repay, and I am grateful from the bottom of my heart," Qianli Xun said. "Your Highness need not go out of your way for me over the Qiao family anymore. I... I stopped holding a grudge against the Qiaos long ago."
[ZH] 申简辰很不情愿地帮千里寻圆谎：“我确实因乔家想要帮你，既然幽栖居士已想好，以后你的事，我不再理会。梦容，哥哥带你去放风筝。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen guessed she brought up the Qiao family just to hand Mengrong a clean explanation. Sure enough, Mengrong relaxed further—so Brother had been trying to make amends because the Qiaos broke their word to the Zhus and tore two lovers apart.
[ZH] 好意再次被拒绝，又听到心爱的人对他说出“谁也不嫁”这种决绝的话，申简辰再次被伤到，可又不愿放弃一直以来努力追求的人，不！是那个有趣的灵魂。
[EN] Reluctant to the bone, Shen Jianchen backed up her cover story. "I did wish to help because of the Qiao family. Since Youqi Jushi has made up her mind, I will meddle no further. Come, Mengrong, Brother will take you kite-flying."
[ZH] 他觉得自己很贱，无论身为大公司的CEO，还是身为官家之子，面对心爱的人，不由自主会贱起来，也许在爱情面前，身份并不那么重要。
[EN] Rejected once again—and told "never to marry anyone" with such finality—stung Shen Jianchen raw. Yet he still could not give up the woman he had chased so long, or rather, that vivid soul inside her. How pathetic he felt. Whether CEO of a major corporation or son of the Song Emperor, standing before the woman he loved stripped away every shred of pride. Maybe rank meant nothing in the face of love.
[ZH] 乔梦容主动上前拉起哥哥的手：“淑真，我不太会放风筝，得哥哥教我，你可会放风筝？”
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“曾经放过，有些经验。”
[ZH] 三人向草地中央走去，看到草地上有坐着晒太阳的，有情侣一起在周围踏青的，最多的当然是放风筝的人。
[ZH] 千里寻看到南双正在与化雨一起放风筝，便向草地四周瞧了一遍，果然看到乔文仙与三位公子坐在草地上饮酒聊天儿，看那样子，还未发现自己。
[ZH] 千里寻想要放下风筝离开，传来梦容的声音：“淑真！你快些！我的风筝都快飞起来！”
[ZH] 在申简辰的帮助下，梦容的风筝渐渐飘到空中。她满面笑容，一会儿瞧风筝，一会儿瞧他的哥哥，幸福的样子令千里寻羡慕，也令千里寻不忍伤害，她在内心祝福梦容永远这么幸福下去。
[ZH] 申辰帮梦容放风筝的样子很认真，他们看上去也很般配，希望他不要再执着穿越前的感情，能安心对待梦容，代替已故的赵朴成为梦容真正的哥哥。
[ZH] 刚才差一点引起梦容怀疑，千里寻决定以后还是尽量不要与申简辰见面。
[ZH] 既然不好离开，又得一个人放风筝，不如不要逃避，凭她一个人也能将风筝放到高空。
[ZH] 她把风筝放在地上，打算先将风筝线放得长一些，然后向后跑去，等风筝飞起来，再一点点将线放长。
[ZH] 红豆凑过来：“小姐，我帮您。”
[ZH] 这个声音，对不得不孤单的千里寻来讲，无疑是一种安慰：“你把风筝拿起来，向那边跑。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong took her brother's hand. "Shuzhen, I am hopeless with kites, so Brother has to teach me. Do you know how?"
[ZH] 红豆按照小姐吩咐，将风筝从地上拿起，向小姐指的方向跑去。千里寻一点一点放着线，很快风筝从红豆的手中飞到空中。
[EN] "I have flown them before," Qianli Xun nodded. "I know the basics."
[ZH] “小姐！风筝飞起来了！太好了！”红豆拍手喊起来。
[EN] The three strolled toward the center of the meadow, passing people sunbathing on the grass, couples strolling in the spring breeze, and crowds launching kites. Spotting Nan Shuang and Huayu running with a kite, Qianli Xun scanned the grounds and saw Qiao Wenxian drinking with the three scholars on the grass; none of them seemed to have noticed her yet.
[ZH] “红豆！你过来！跟我一起放！”
[EN] She thought about putting her kite down and slipping away when Mengrong called out, "Hurry up, Shuzhen! My kite is taking off!"
[ZH] “好！”
[EN] With Shen Jianchen guiding her hands, Mengrong's kite lifted into the air. Glowing with joy, she glanced back and forth between the soaring kite and her brother. Her radiant happiness roused a pang of envy in Qianli Xun—and deepened her resolve never to hurt her. Silently, she wished Mengrong happiness like this forever. Shen Jianchen looked attentive helping her, and the two made a handsome pair. If only he could let go of his pre-transmigration feelings, care for Mengrong wholeheartedly, and step into the late Zhao Pu's shoes as her true brother. Having come so close to raising Mengrong's suspicions just now, Qianli Xun resolved to avoid meeting Shen Jianchen from this day on.
[ZH] 红豆跑到小姐身边，两人一起放风筝，主仆二人的笑声回荡在周围……
[EN] Unable to leave and left flying a kite on her own, she decided to face it head-on—she could get a kite aloft by herself. Setting it on the grass, she unspooled a stretch of line, preparing to run backward and let the wind catch it.
[ZH] 听到笑声，申简辰向千里寻看去，她手中的风筝已飞得老高。
[EN] "Miss, let me help," Hongdou spoke up beside her.
[ZH] 申简辰有莫名失落感，本想故意冷落她，让她体会到被他冷落后的孤寂，从心里后悔拒绝他。可她即使没有他依然那么开心，反而令他开心不起来。
[EN] To someone bracing to stand alone, that quiet offer brought real warmth. "Hold the kite up and run that way."
[ZH] “梦容，我不想再放风筝，文渊在那边，我过去与他聊聊。”
