[EN] Following her lead, Hongdou lifted the kite and sprinted across the grass while Qianli Xun fed out the line bit by bit. In seconds, the kite leaped from Hongdou's hands and climbed high into the sky.
[ZH] 没等梦容答应，申简辰已放手，向乔文仙等人的方向走去。
[EN] "Miss! It is flying! Wonderful!" Hongdou clapped and cheered.
[ZH] 哥哥的表现与往日不同，到底怎么不同，乔梦容又说不出所以然。因忧心哥哥，手上的劲儿没跟上，已放到空中的风筝渐渐落下。
[EN] "Come over here, Hongdou! Fly it with me!"
[ZH] “哥哥！风筝落下了！”
[EN] "Yes!" Hongdou dashed back to her side, and mistress and maid laughed together as the kite danced above them.
[ZH] 申简辰听到梦容的呼唤，假装没有听到，继续向前走。他有种不想掩饰下去的冲动，甚至想要找适合的时机向梦容坦白真情。
[EN] Hearing her laughter, Shen Jianchen glanced over and saw her kite soaring high. An odd hollowness settled in his chest. He had meant to give her the cold shoulder, make her feel the sting of being left out and regret turning him away. Instead, she looked happier than ever without him—which only soured his own mood.
[ZH] 哥哥第一次不理她，乔梦容不知如何是好。
[EN] "Mengrong, I am tired of flying kites. Wenyuan is over there; I will go chat with him."
[ZH] 看到郡主站在原地，不知所措，喜兰猜到原因，上前帮王爷解释：“郡主不用担忧，王爷肯定是没听到，不如等王爷与各位公子聊完，您再找王爷问问。”
[EN] Without waiting for an answer, Shen Jianchen let go of the spool and strode toward Qiao Wenxian and his friends.
[ZH] “也许是这样。”乔梦容的声音显然失去一半的力气。
[EN] Something about her brother felt different today, though Qiao Mengrong could not pin down what. Distracted by worry, she slackened her grip on the string, and the kite sank back toward the grass.
[ZH] 一切被千里寻看在眼中：“红豆，你继续玩儿，我去看看梦容。”
[EN] "Brother! The kite is falling!"
[ZH] 千里寻放开风筝线，来到梦容身边：“怎么？仪王不在，你连风筝都不会放？我来帮你。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen heard her call and pretended not to, walking right on. An urge to drop the mask swelled inside him—even to find the right moment to tell Mengrong the whole truth.
[ZH] “我不想再放风筝，淑真，你陪我在草地上走走。”
[EN] Ignored by her brother for the very first time, Qiao Mengrong froze, at a loss.
[ZH] “好吧，不如我们去那边。”千里寻指指离几位公子较远的方向。
[EN] Seeing her mistress stricken, Xilan hurried to smooth it over. "Do not fret, Your Highness. His Highness must not have heard you. Wait until he finishes talking with the young gentlemen and ask him then."
[ZH] 得到乔梦容同意后，二人往远处走去。
[EN] "Perhaps so," Qiao Mengrong murmured, half the strength drained from her voice.
[ZH] 喜兰从地上捡起风筝，送到扎风筝处，才小跑着跟上郡主。
[EN] Having watched it all unfold, Qianli Xun handed her line off. "Keep playing, Hongdou. I will go check on Mengrong." Walking over, she teased gently, "What is this? Prince Yi steps away and you cannot keep a kite up? Let me help."
[ZH] 乔梦容回头向哥哥去的方向眺望一眼，哥哥已与几位公子坐在一处，应该聊得不错。她回过头，叹口气：“唉。”
[EN] "I do not feel like flying kites anymore, Shuzhen. Walk with me across the meadow."
[ZH] “为何叹气？”
[EN] "Alright. Let us head that way." Qianli Xun pointed away from where the gentlemen sat, and once Mengrong agreed, they strolled off together. Xilan gathered the fallen kite, returned it to the kite-maker, and trotted to catch up.
[ZH] “我刚才喊哥哥，他没理我，不知因何缘故？”
[EN] Glancing back once, Qiao Mengrong saw her brother seated comfortably with the young scholars, deep in conversation. Turning forward, she let out a heavy sigh.
[ZH] 千里寻充满自责，只怪今日捶丸与申简辰走得近了些。
[EN] "Why the sigh?"
[ZH] “我当什么事，肯定是仪王没听到你唤他。即使听到，没理你，也没什么，仪王毕竟是男子，男子有男子的想法，总不能时时刻刻围着女子转，再喜欢也不能这样。”
[EN] "I called out to Brother just now and he ignored me. I do not know why."
[ZH] “也许……你说得对，我以后还是注意些，不要老缠着哥哥，时间长了，他肯定会烦我，毕竟现在的哥哥跟以前不一样。”
[EN] Guilt stabbed Qianli Xun—she blamed herself for getting too close to Shen Jianchen during the chuiwan match. "Here I thought it was something serious! He must not have heard you. Even if he did, it is nothing to fuss over. Prince Yi is a man, with a man's own thoughts. He cannot orbit a woman every single second, no matter how fond of her he is."
[ZH] 千里寻不知如何搭话，真正的仪王已死，梦容实在可怜。可站在申简辰的角度，硬让他去爱并不爱的人，对他来讲无疑也是一种折磨。
[EN] "Maybe... you are right. I should be more careful from now on and stop clinging to Brother. Keep it up too long and he is bound to grow sick of me. After all, the brother now is not the same as before."
[ZH] 为何人无论怎么活着都要面对无奈的生活？哪怕申简辰穿越成为仪王也是如此。
[EN] Qianli Xun had no words to answer. The real Prince Yi was dead, leaving Mengrong in a heartbreaking spot—yet forcing Shen Jianchen to love someone he did not love was its own cruelty. Why did life force everyone into impossible corners, even after transmigrating into a prince?
[ZH] “淑真，你为何不说话？”
[EN] "Why so quiet, Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “哦，我是在想你以前怎样便怎样，没有必要故意改变，仪王也会不适应。”
[EN] "Oh, I was just thinking you should stay just as you have always been. No need to force yourself to change, or Prince Yi will find it strange."
[ZH] “好吧，待会儿哥哥来找我，我再当面问他为何不理我。”
[EN] "Alright. When Brother comes looking for me later, I will ask him face-to-face why he ignored me."
[ZH] “这就对嘛，问清楚，也就没有误会。”
[EN] "That is the spirit. Ask straight out and clear up any misunderstanding."
[ZH] 传来一阵烧烤的味道，乔梦容四下瞅瞅：“哪里传来的味道？真好闻。”
[EN] A whiff of sizzling roast meat drifted over on the breeze. Qiao Mengrong looked around. "Where is that smell coming from? It smells delicious."
[ZH] 千里寻仔细闻闻，想起在西湖时碰到陈浩宇吃烤肉的情景：“如果我没猜错，肯定是陈浩宇带人在周围吃烤肉，我们还是离他远些。”
[EN] Sniffing the air, Qianli Xun recalled catching Chen Haoyu roasting meat by West Lake. "If I am not mistaken, Chen Haoyu has dragged his buddies out here for barbecue. Let us steer clear of him."

#CH 166
[ZH] 千里寻与乔梦容正要拐向别处，青瓜用盘子端着几串炙肉兴冲冲跑过来：“二位等等！”
[ZH] 两人只好站住。
[ZH] 青瓜上前先对郡主行礼：“佳和郡主，朱家姑娘，我家公子与朋友在那边烤肉串，瞧见你们过来，让我端几串让你们品尝。”
[ZH] 两人向青瓜说的方向瞧去，并未看到任何人影。
[ZH] 千里寻问：“在哪里？怎么看不到人影？”
[ZH] 青瓜笑道：“那边地势低，走到跟前才能看到。”
[ZH] “哦，原来如此。”玩了许久，千里寻也觉饥饿，将肉串拿过，递给梦容一些，“陈浩宇请客，我们不吃白不吃。”
[ZH] 青瓜嘱咐道：“两位吃完，把竹签放到盘子里，我还要端过去。”
[ZH] 青瓜说完一直盯着两位姑娘。
[ZH] 被他人盯着，两人不好下嘴。
[ZH] 千里寻故意问：“你那双眼睛直勾勾地盯着肉串，难不成不想给我和郡主吃？”
[ZH] “怎么会？我这就转过身，二位放心吃。”
[ZH] 青瓜转过身后，两位姑娘开吃起来，边吃边夸赞肉串烤得不错。
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun and Qiao Mengrong turned to head the other way, Qinggua came bounding up with a platter of sizzling meat skewers. "Wait up, ladies!"
[ZH] 青瓜趁机夸道：“我家公子可会烤肉串，以后朱家姑娘嫁到陈家等着享福吧。”
[EN] Forced to halt, they watched Qinggua bow to the princess first. "Princess Jiahe, Miss Zhu—my young master and his friends are roasting skewers over there. Seeing you walk by, he sent me over with a few for you to taste."
[ZH] 千里寻并不会将青瓜的话当回事，咬一口肉串，边嚼边说：“陈浩宇是什么样的人，我见识过，你没必要在我面前替他说好话，说再多都没用。”
[EN] Both girls peered in the direction he gestured, yet saw nobody at all. "Where?" Qianli Xun asked. "There is not a soul in sight."
[ZH] 青瓜替自家公子解释：“我家公子是不着调，那是因为没人能管住他，我听公子说姑娘很厉害，他好像很怕您，等您嫁过来，有人管他，我家公子肯定会改掉以前的臭毛病。”
[EN] "The ground dips down over there," Qinggua grinned. "You only spot them once you walk right up to the rim."
[ZH] “你都说陈浩宇不着调，我可不愿嫁给一个不着调的人，再说，我有什么义务替陈家管教他？”
[EN] "Aha." Having been active for hours, Qianli Xun felt her stomach rumble. She took the skewers and passed half to Mengrong. "Chen Haoyu is treating—waste not, want not."
[ZH] 青瓜之所以说那些话，只是没话找话而已，朱家姑娘既然如此说，他不再多言，再说公子还嫌弃朱家姑娘是母老虎。青瓜觉得有趣，这俩人都不怎么样，还互相瞧不上。
[EN] "When you finish, please set the bamboo skewers back on the platter so I can carry them back," Qinggua reminded them—then stayed planted right there, staring straight at the two girls.
[ZH] 吃完肉串，千里寻接过梦容手中的竹签，递给青瓜：“转过来吧，竹签拿去，今日陈浩宇算是做了一件好事，替我和郡主感谢他。”
[EN] Under his unblinking gaze, neither felt comfortable taking a bite.
[ZH] 青瓜接过竹签：“那是自然，二位忙，我要去向公子回话。”
[EN] "Why are your eyes glued to these skewers?" Qianli Xun asked on purpose. "Do you grudge Her Highness and me a taste?"
[ZH] 千里寻继续与梦容踏青，她们随意走在宽敞的草地上，春风时不时拂面而来，温馨而舒适，两个姑娘各自体会着各自的心思。
[EN] "Never! I will turn right around—eat in peace, ladies."
[ZH] 乔梦容依然纠结哥哥刚才的表现，她又朝几位公子处望去，哥哥坐在那里，没有离开的意思。
[EN] Once Qinggua spun around, the two dug in, praising how well-seasoned the roast meat was.
[ZH] “淑真，不知为何，我总觉得哥哥心里装着另一个人。”
[EN] Seizing the opening, Qinggua boasted over his shoulder, "My young master is a wizard at roasting skewers! Once Miss Zhu marries into the Chen family, you are in for the good life."
[ZH] 女子向来心思细腻，能被梦容猜到实属必然，况且申简辰总是时不时流露出来，梦容不怀疑那才不正常。
[EN] Taking a bite and chewing, Qianli Xun brushed it right off. "I have seen firsthand what sort of man Chen Haoyu is. Save your breath—no amount of sweet talk on his behalf will move me."
[ZH] 千里寻努力帮仪王掩饰：“应该不会，你别瞎想。”
[EN] "My master acts wild only because nobody has ever kept him in line," Qinggua argued. "I hear him say Miss is fierce and he seems scared stiff of you. Once you marry in and rein him in, he will drop all his bad habits for sure."
[ZH] “我没有瞎想，哥哥刚刚起死回生时我就猜到。我陪他去街市，遇到卦摊，他便上前占卜，还说是寻人，当时我猜他想寻的一定是个女子。”
[EN] "Even you admit he is hopeless. Why on earth would I marry a hopeless rake? And since when is it my duty to raise the Chen family's son for them?"
[ZH] 原来申简辰刚刚穿越而来就想着找她，千里寻内心生起异样的情愫，可乔梦容伤感的表情令她无法再继续体会下去。
[EN] Qinggua had only been making conversation; hearing her sharp retort, he shut his mouth—especially since his master called Miss Zhu a tigress behind her back anyway. Funny pair, Qinggua mused: neither one was much of a prize, yet both looked down their noses at the other.
[ZH] “你肯定是想多了。”
[EN] Finishing her meat, Qianli Xun collected Mengrong's empty sticks and held them out. "Turn around and take your skewers. Chen Haoyu did one decent thing today—pass on our thanks from Her Highness and me."
[ZH] “后来我才知道哥哥要找的人是千里寻，那位写话本的才子，我才放心。可我着实想不通，哥哥为找那人先是占卜，来到临安又派白风守在西三胡同，既然知道话本才子就在临安，为何哥哥后来又不找了？”
[EN] "Will do! Enjoy your walk; I must report back to my master."
[ZH] 虽然千里寻已知申简辰为寻她颇费一番心思，可被梦容以怀疑的口吻叙述一番，她竟然感动起来，忘记接梦容的话。
[EN] Qianli Xun and Mengrong continued their stroll across the wide meadow, a soft spring breeze brushing their cheeks, each lost in her own thoughts.
[ZH] “淑真，你怎么了？”
[EN] Still gnawed by her brother's strange behavior, Qiao Mengrong glanced back toward the young gentlemen; her brother sat right where he was, showing no sign of coming over. "Shuzhen, I do not know why, yet I keep feeling there is another person in Brother's heart."
[ZH] 梦容的声音让千里寻变得更加清醒：“哦，没什么。我觉得想不通就不要想，反正你与仪王有官家赐婚，他必然会娶你。”
[EN] A woman's intuition was sharp, and with Shen Jianchen letting his feelings leak out every other turn, it would have been a miracle if Mengrong had not noticed.
[ZH] “话虽如此，我还是很担心，怕哥哥根本不想娶我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun scrambled to cover for him. "No way—do not go inventing worries."
[ZH] 这个话题太过麻烦，千里寻不愿再聊，她怕再聊下去，会愈加愧疚：“梦容，你看，大好春光，你我应该忘记所有不快，好好享受，相信明天一切会好！”
[EN] "I am not inventing anything. I suspected it the moment Brother came back from death's door. I went to the market with him, and the instant he saw a fortune-teller's stall, he stopped to ask for a divination about finding someone. I guessed right then he was looking for a woman."
[ZH] 乔梦容露出笑容：“淑真，你真好，总能给我带来积极健康的情绪。”
[EN] So Shen Jianchen had started searching for her the very instant he transmigrated. A warm, tangled flutter stirred in Qianli Xun's chest, yet one look at Mengrong's downcast face snuffed it out. "You are overthinking this."
[ZH] 被梦容夸奖，千里寻内心无法真正高兴起来，如果梦容知道仪王心里的人是她，肯定不会再觉得她好。
[EN] "Later I learned he was searching for Qianli Xun, that famed huaben writer, and I breathed easier. Yet I still cannot fathom why—after casting lots in Bianliang and posting Bai Feng outside West Third Alley in Lin'an, knowing the writer was right here in the city—Brother gave up searching out of the blue."
[ZH] “梦容，我永远不会做对不起你的事。”
[EN] Even though Qianli Xun already knew how hard Shen Jianchen had hunted for her, hearing the story told through Mengrong's uneasy questions moved her so much she forgot to reply.
[ZH] “怎么突然说这个？”乔梦容露出疑惑的表情。
[EN] "Shuzhen? What is wrong?"
[ZH] 千里寻掩饰道：“谁让你说让我感动的话。”
[EN] Mengrong's voice jolted her awake. "Oh, nothing. I just think you should stop racking your brain over puzzles with no answers. You and Prince Yi have an Imperial Marriage Decree; he is bound to marry you."
[ZH] 乔梦容露出朋友之间最真诚的笑容，拉起淑真，两人用心享受着春日的阳光……
[EN] "Even so, I keep worrying Brother does not want to marry me at all."
[ZH] 这片草地大部分比较平坦，只有一处洼地，倒成为陈浩宇的好去处。他提前让人将炉子放入，炭火烧好，再将肉串穿好。捶丸结束后，与约好的两位公子前来炙肉。
[EN] This topic was walking on thin ice, and pressing further would only pile more guilt on her head. "Look at this glorious spring day, Mengrong! Let us toss every gloomy thought aside and enjoy ourselves. Tomorrow will turn out bright!"
[ZH] 青瓜一手握着竹签，一手端着空盘子，下到低洼处。
[EN] A smile broke across Qiao Mengrong's face. "You are wonderful, Shuzhen. You always bring such bright, uplifting cheer."
[ZH] “公子，朱家姑娘与郡主把肉串全都吃完，直夸味道好，还让我代她们感谢您。”
[EN] The compliment sat heavy on Qianli Xun's conscience. If Mengrong ever learned who occupied Prince Yi's heart, she would never call her wonderful again. "Mengrong, I will never do anything to betray you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐在毛毡上，一边吃肉串，一边说：“我是看在郡主的面子上才让朱淑真品尝，否则她哪有机会吃到我亲自烤的肉。”
[EN] "Why say that out of the blue?" Mengrong asked, puzzled.
[ZH] 青瓜放下盘子和竹签，站在一旁赔着笑：“公子，我在朱家姑娘面前夸您，说您最会炙肉……”
[EN] "Blame yourself for saying something so touching," Qianli Xun deflected.
[ZH] “滚！本公子这么完美，需要你夸！”陈浩宇一脸不屑。
[EN] Smiling with pure, open trust, Qiao Mengrong took her hand, and the two soaked in the spring warmth side by side.
[ZH] 青瓜并未离开，他知道公子只是习惯用“滚”骂他而已，他唯一能做的就是闭嘴。
[EN] Most of the meadow lay flat, save for one sunken hollow that served Chen Haoyu's purposes well. He had servants haul in a brazier, kindle the charcoal, and thread the skewers ahead of time; once the chuiwan match wrapped up, he met two cronies there to roast meat.
[ZH] 坐在陈浩宇对面的一位公子说：“陈兄一直奉行放荡不羁，还说不碰到喜欢的人绝不成亲，结果怎样？还不是被订婚？”
[EN] Empty platter in one hand and bamboo sticks in the other, Qinggua hopped down into the hollow. "Young Master, Miss Zhu and Her Highness ate every single skewer! They raved about the flavor and sent their thanks."
[ZH] “订婚又能把我怎样？哪怕真的娶了朱淑真，我该怎样还怎样，谁都改变不了本公子。”陈浩宇为自己满上一杯酒，端起来，“喝！人生得意须尽欢，莫使金樽空对月。”
[EN] Sprawled on a felt rug munching a skewer, Chen Haoyu scoffed. "I only shared on Princess Jiahe's account. Otherwise Zhu Shuzhen would never get a taste of my handiwork."
[ZH] 对面的公子端起酒杯，还未饮，先用戏谑的口吻夸赞：“呵！陈兄竟然开始吟诗，难道是因为要娶女词人，为与其相匹配，趁机恶补？”
[EN] Setting the platter down, Qinggua grinned and fawned. "I talked you up to Miss Zhu too, told her nobody roasts meat better than—"
[ZH] 陈浩宇饮完酒，怼道：“能不能好好说话？本公子吟这两句，三岁孩童都知道，你敢说你不知？”
[EN] "Scram! Does perfection like me need your bragging?" Chen Haoyu sneered.
[ZH] “我错了，自罚一杯。”对面的公子也将酒饮下。
[EN] Qinggua stayed put, knowing "scram" was just his master's verbal tic; all he needed to do was zip his lip.
[ZH] 正在炙肉的公子翻了一遍肉串，撒了一遍香料，才开始说话：“朱家姑娘也是个美人儿，每次见到，我都神魂荡漾，只可惜敢看不敢上手，陈兄有福气，令人羡慕不已，洞房花烛夜之后，可得跟我们说说她的好……”
[EN] A young rake seated opposite Chen Haoyu chuckled. "Brother Chen always swore he would stay footloose and fancy-free, never marrying unless he met true love—and look at you now, betrothed all the same!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇骂道：“滚！狗嘴里吐不出象牙！”
[EN] "What can a betrothal do to me? Even if I end up marrying Zhu Shuzhen, I will live just as I please. Nobody changes this young master!" Chen Haoyu filled his cup and raised it high. "Drink up! 'When one has one's way in life, one must enjoy it to the full. The golden wine cup must not face the moon unfilled.'" [TN: Translated by Chan Sin-wai]
[ZH] “陈兄都开始护媳妇儿，少见少见。”
[EN] Cup raised, the friend mocked him before taking a sip. "Whoa! Brother Chen reciting poetry? Cramming in advance so you can keep up with your poetess bride?"
[ZH] “护个屁！你说那些话，被她听去，小心你的狗命！”
[EN] Draining his cup, Chen Haoyu shot back, "Can you speak like a normal person? Every three-year-old knows those two lines—dare tell me you do not?"
[ZH] 炙肉公子抬头向上望望：“都已走远，听不见。没想到陈公子这么怕朱淑真，只怕婚后得了怕老婆的美名，呵呵……！”
[EN] "My bad! Penalty cup." The friend downed his wine too.
[ZH] “笑你个头！本公子根本不会娶她。”
[EN] The other crony tending the grill flipped the skewers, shook spice over them, and leered. "Miss Zhu is a real looker, though. Sets my heart fluttering every time I see her—shame I only dare look and not touch. Brother Chen is a lucky dog! After the wedding night, you have to tell us all about how she—"
[ZH] “担心得了怕老婆的美名？”
[EN] "Get lost!" Chen Haoyu barked. "Nothing decent ever comes out of a dog's mouth!"
[ZH] “去你的！本公子要娶，也得娶个黄花大姑娘，她都结过一次婚，根本配不上我，要不是因为他爹是知府大人，我爹娘也不会同意。”
[EN] "Look at Brother Chen sticking up for his bride! Now that is a rare sight."
[ZH] 炙肉公子继续翻着肉串，用慨叹的语气玩笑道：“我可不嫌弃，为何知府大人不来找我？”
[EN] "Stick up for her, my foot! If she caught wind of what you just said, you could kiss your worthless hide goodbye!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇懒得再理会，拿起一串肉，大口嚼起来。
[EN] The griller glanced up toward the rim of the hollow. "They are long gone—cannot hear a thing. Who knew Young Master Chen was scared stiff of Zhu Shuzhen? Come your wedding, you will earn the grand title of henpecked husband! Hahaha!"
[ZH] 对面的公子嚼完一串肉，凑近陈浩宇：“其实你不必担心，我听说林觉虽娶了朱淑真，可并未得手，要不他怎么老往春香楼跑，还把春金巧往林府带，嘿嘿……！”
[EN] "Laugh all you want—I am not marrying her in the first place!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续解释：“所以说朱淑真更娶不得，她就是母老虎，林觉都无法得手，我娶了恐怕也难。”
[EN] "Scared of earning that henpecked title?"
[ZH] “她爹可是临安知府，估计你这婚不好退。”
[EN] "Drop dead! When I marry, it will be a fresh young maiden. She has been married once already—she is nowhere near my league. If her father were not the Prefect, my parents would never have agreed."
[ZH] “我都想好了，实在退不了，就把她娶回去，大家井水不犯河水，各过各的。”
[EN] Flipping the meat, the griller sighed in mock envy. "I would not turn her down. Why did the Prefect not come knocking at my door?"
[ZH] “原来陈兄什么都想好了。”
[EN] Ignoring him, Chen Haoyu grabbed another skewer and bit in.
[ZH] “人无远虑必有近忧，所以得考虑长远些。”
[EN] Chewing through his own skewer, the first crony leaned close. "You need not worry anyway. Word has it Lin Jue married Zhu Shuzhen yet never even laid a finger on her—why else did he haunt Chunxiang Brothel and drag Chun Jinqiao straight into Lin Manor? Heh!"
[ZH] “少见，陈兄不是提倡及时行乐，最讨厌远虑那一套？怎么跟以前不一样？”
[EN] "Which proves all the more she is poison," Chen Haoyu shot back. "She is a tigress! Even Lin Jue could not tame her, so what chance do I have?"
[ZH] “没办法，谁让这婚约耽误本公子及时行乐的宗旨，所以不得不远虑一下。”
[EN] "Her father is the Prefect of Lin'an. Breaking off that match will not be easy."
[ZH] “嘿嘿，原来如此。”
[EN] "I have it all mapped out. If I truly cannot break it off, I will marry her home and we will keep out of each other's hair—each living our own life."
[ZH] 炙肉公子感到尿急：“青瓜，你来炙肉，我去方便一下。”
[EN] "Look at Brother Chen, planning every move ahead."
[ZH] “遵命！”青瓜换了那位公子，认真炙起肉来。
[EN] "If a man takes no thought for the distant future, sorrow waits close at hand. Got to look ahead."
[ZH] 炙肉公子走出洼地，四下瞧瞧，都是放风筝的人，在此处方便不太合适。
[EN] "Miracles never cease! Since when did the champion of living for the moment start preaching foresight?"
[ZH] 他又向别处瞧瞧，看到一个小土坡，步行过去，躲到后面，方便起来……
[EN] "No choice—this betrothal threatens my whole creed of living for the moment, so I had to spare it a thought."
[ZH] 一泡尿过后，全身无比舒适，整理好衣裳，往回走。
[EN] "Heh, fair enough."
[ZH] 不再尿急，心情变得轻松，又朝放风筝的人瞧去，一个姑娘的身影映入他的眼帘，红扑扑的脸蛋甚是可爱，再配上一张笑脸，实在令人心动。再看那穿着，应该是哪家的丫鬟。
[EN] Feeling nature call, the grilling crony stood up. "Take over the skewers, Qinggua. I need to relieve myself."
[ZH] 炙肉公子心绪摇荡，心想，既是丫鬟，他要是得手，应该没人管，说不定主人家还会把她许给自己。
[EN] "Right away!" Qinggua stepped in and tended the meat.
[ZH] 于是大胆靠近那丫鬟……
[EN] Climbing out of the hollow, the young rake scanned the meadow. Kite-flyers dotted the grass, hardly a private spot. Spotting a low earthen mound nearby, he strolled behind it and took care of business.
[ZH] 丫鬟正是红豆，小姐与郡主散步，她一个人放风筝，越放越熟练，越熟练越开心，只顾体会风筝带来的快乐，根本没发现有人在偷看她。
[EN] Relieved and cheerful, he adjusted his robe and headed back. Glancing toward the kite-flyers, his gaze snagged on a young girl—rosy cheeks, bright grin, utterly charming. Judging from her dress, she was some household's maid. His heart leaped. Just a maid! If he got his hands on her, who would fuss? Her master might even gift her to him.
[ZH] 炙肉公子走近后，问道：“喂！你是谁家的丫鬟？”
[EN] Bold as brass, he sauntered over.
[ZH] 红豆瞧了一眼，对方色眯眯的样子，实在讨厌，她赶紧躲开。
[EN] It was Hongdou. While her mistress walked with the princess, she had flown the kite alone, growing handier and happier by the minute, wrapped up in the fun without noticing the lecher creeping up.
[ZH] 炙肉公子靠过去：“放风筝多没意思，跟哥哥去炙肉，吃完炙肉再去哥哥府上，只要让哥哥开心，哥哥让你有享不完福。”
[EN] "Hey there!" he called out as he closed in. "Which household do you serve?"
[ZH] 红豆又朝一边躲开。
[EN] One look at his greasy leer disgusted Hongdou, and she sidestepped away.
[ZH] 炙肉公子继续跟上：“哥哥一眼瞧上你，来，让哥哥香一个。”
[EN] He pressed closer. "What fun is flying a kite? Come join Big Brother for roast meat, then come back to my estate. Keep Big Brother happy and you will live in clover."
[ZH] 炙肉公子噘起嘴向红豆脸上亲去……
[EN] Hongdou sidestepped again.
[ZH] 红豆忍无可忍，无心再放风筝，小姐教的拳法不能白白浪费，一拳过去，打在炙肉公子的脸上，再一脚将炙肉公子踢得向后跌倒在地。
[EN] He trailed right after her. "Big Brother fell for you at first sight! Come here and give me a kiss!" Puckering his lips, he lunged for her cheek.
[ZH] 此时，飘在空中的风筝才乖乖落在草地上。
[EN] Pushed past her limit, Hongdou forgot the kite. Why waste the fist techniques Miss had drilled into her? She swung a punch straight into his face, then planted a kick that sent him sprawling backward onto the grass. Only then did the forgotten kite flutter down onto the turf.
[ZH] “哎吆！哎吆……！”炙肉公子捂着已乌青的半张脸直喊疼，努力爬起来，大骂道，“你个臭丫头！本公子瞧得上你，是你的福气，竟然敢打本公子！快说！你是谁家丫鬟？让你的主人好好修理你！”
[EN] "Ow! Owww!" Clutching half a face already swelling purple, the rake scrambled up and cursed. "You little wretch! Having this young master take a shine to you is your blessing, and you dare hit me?! Speak up! Whose maid are you? I will have your master flay you alive!"
[ZH] 草地太大，很多人并未注意到这里发生的事，就连千里寻也只顾与郡主边走边聊，自己的丫鬟发生了什么，她并不知道。
[EN] The meadow was vast, so few noticed the scuffle; engrossed in talking with Mengrong, Qianli Xun had no idea what had happened to her maid.
[ZH] 因低洼处离得近，倒惊动陈浩宇。他爬上来，仔细瞧那丫鬟，觉得脸熟，猛然想起正是朱淑真身边新带的丫鬟，赶紧上前劝：“去去去！快去放你的风筝！一个丫鬟拳头也这么厉害，真是有其主必有其仆。”
[EN] Down in the nearby hollow, however, Chen Haoyu heard the commotion and scrambled up the slope. One look at the maid rang a bell—Zhu Shuzhen's new girl! He rushed over and waved her off. "Go on, shoo! Back to your kite! Even her maid packs a wallop—like mistress, like servant!"
[ZH] 红豆捡起风筝，瞪了一眼炙肉公子：“以后再调 戏我，打得你脑袋开花！”
[EN] Retrieving her kite, Hongdou glared daggers at the rake. "Try getting fresh with me again and I will crack your skull open!"
[ZH] “陈兄，你认识那丫头？”
[EN] "You know that brat, Brother Chen?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧着红豆的背影：“你调 戏谁不成，偏偏调 戏朱淑真的丫鬟，吃亏了吧？”
[EN] Watching Hongdou's departing back, Chen Haoyu shook his head. "Out of everyone to grope, you pick Zhu Shuzhen's maid. Got what was coming to you, huh?"
[ZH] “不行，我要找朱淑真算账！”
[EN] "No way—I am going to settle accounts with Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] “算你个头！只怕你去找她，再被她揍一顿！”
[EN] "Settle accounts, my foot! Go find her and you will only catch a second beating!"
[ZH] “啊！陈兄，你真可怜，还要娶她，我今日算是明白你为何想方设法退婚。”
[EN] "Ah! Poor Brother Chen, stuck marrying her. Now I see why you are desperate to break it off."
[ZH] “理解我就好，这女子还需温柔，朱淑真一点儿都不温柔。刚才那丫头若温柔些，估计你已得手，哪会受这苦？”
[EN] "Glad you get it. A woman ought to be gentle, and Zhu Shuzhen does not have a gentle bone in her body. If that maid had been the gentle sort, you would have had your way instead of eating dirt."
[ZH] “难道我就这么吃哑巴亏？”
[EN] "So I just swallow this loss in silence?"
[ZH] “不吃亏，就得再吃拳头，你好好想想。”
[EN] "Swallow the loss, or swallow another fist. Take your pick."
[ZH] 炙肉公子故作认真地想想：“算了，我还是吃亏吧。”
[EN] The rake pretended to weigh his options. "Forget it. I will swallow the loss."
[ZH] “这就对了，走，我们继续炙肉。”
[EN] "Smart move. Come on, back to our skewers."
[ZH] 陈浩宇与炙肉公子回到低洼处，无非再解释一番脸上乌青的来历，再被嘲笑一番，再感慨一番朱家姑娘娶不得之类的话。
[EN] Descending back into the hollow, they spent the next stretch explaining the bruised cheek, enduring fresh rounds of mockery, and sighing all over again that no man in his right mind should marry Miss Zhu.

#CH 167
[ZH] 千里寻与乔梦容有说有笑，边走边聊，正聊得开心，乔文仙出现在眼前。
[ZH] 他身上散发着酒气，眼睛直勾勾地盯着淑真。
[ZH] 乔梦容拽拽淑真的衣角：“是乔公子，你们聊，我去那边待会儿。”
[ZH] 千里寻从文渊的脸上看出许多不舍，她感到害怕，怕自己会不小心改变心意。
[ZH] 她一把拉住梦容：“你别走，陪着我，也好做个见证，省得别人看到还以为我跟乔公子藕断丝连。”
[ZH] 乔梦容只好留下，通过眼神，她已感受到乔公子对淑真的感情有多深，更为两人无法在一起而惋惜。
[ZH] 乔文仙开口说：“淑真，我只问你一句话，如若没有流言，两家顺利定亲，你会不会心甘情愿嫁给我？”
[ZH] 千里寻犹豫片刻点点头：“会，因为那时我就是这么想的。”
[ZH] “我再问一句……”
[ZH] “你刚说只问一句。”
[ZH] “我还想问……，之前对我的感情可是真的？”
[ZH] 千里寻照实说：“是真的。”
[ZH] 乔文仙的嘴角出现一抹带着痛苦的笑容：“就知道那时的你真的爱我。……，现在呢？”
[ZH] 已是第三个问题，千里寻不愿再听对方问下去：“现在……，我已放下文渊，还请乔公子也放下淑真。”
[ZH] 乔文仙刚刚露出的笑容不得不消失：“难道从此以后你要这么客气地称呼我？连朋友的情谊都不顾？以前你我一直视对方为知己。”
[EN] Qianli Xun and Qiao Mengrong were strolling along in cheerful conversation when Qiao Wenxian stepped into their path. The scent of wine clung to him, and his eyes locked straight onto Shuzhen.
[ZH] “淑真以前不懂事，以后不会再有知己，还请乔公子不要再将淑真视作知己。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong tugged Shuzhen's sleeve. "It is Young Master Qiao. You two talk—I will wait over there."
[ZH] “你……竟如此……狠心，连最后……最后一丝希望……都不给我？”
[EN] Reading the raw longing on Wenyuan's face frightened Qianli Xun; she feared softening if she stood alone with him. She caught Mengrong's wrist at once. "Stay right here with me as a witness, or folks might think Young Master Qiao and I are still tangled up."
[ZH] 乔文仙的声音开始颤抖，颤抖的节奏敲击着千里寻的内心。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stayed put. From his gaze alone, she felt the depth of his love for Shuzhen and ached for the pair torn apart.
[ZH] “乔公子说笑，你我哪有希望，更何谈最后一丝希望。”
[EN] "Shuzhen," Qiao Wenxian began, "I will ask just one question. Had those rumors never spread and had our families sealed the betrothal, would you have married me willingly?"
[ZH] 乔文仙努力控制好情绪，尽量让声音变得平静：“都怪我……。你可否答应，即使我们不能在一起，还是朋友，即使你不再视我为知己，依然可以唤我文渊，我依然唤你淑真。”
[EN] After a beat of hesitation, Qianli Xun nodded. "Yes. Because back then, that was what I wanted."
[ZH] 千里寻不忍心再伤害文渊，点点头：“我答应你，你也必须答应我，以后不要再单独与我说话，安心过自己的日子，你过得好，我便开心。”
[EN] "Let me ask one more..."
[ZH] “好，我听你的，以后不再单独与你说话，安心过好日子，只要你高兴，我做什么都乐意。”乔文仙说完转身向几位公子走去。
[EN] "You said just one question."
[ZH] 梦容瞧见哥哥向这边张望，估计是担心乔公子。
[EN] "I need to know... were your feelings for me back then real?"
[ZH] “唉，我真替你和乔公子感到惋惜，希望他能快些走出来。”
[EN] "They were real," she answered with candor.
[ZH] “没什么好惋惜的，我已想通，每个人有每个人的路要走，可惜我的路与文渊的路没有交织在一起，最终还是要走在各自的路上，等他在自己的路上走习惯，自然会放下。”
[EN] A pained smile touched Qiao Wenxian's lips. "I knew you loved me then. And... now?"
[ZH] 乔梦容听后，想到自己：“也不知我与哥哥走的路会不会交织在一起。”
[EN] Three questions already. Unwilling to let him press further, Qianli Xun steeled her voice. "Now... I have let go of Wenyuan. I ask Young Master Qiao to let go of Shuzhen as well."
[ZH] “那是自然，婚约已将仪王和你的路紧紧绑在一起。”
[EN] His brief smile vanished. "Must you address me so stiffly from now on? Casting aside even friendship? We once looked upon each other as kindred spirits."
[ZH] “婚约是一方面，只怕哥哥的心不在我身上。就像乔公子，娶的是苗知瑜，心却在你身上，我倒可怜起苗姑娘。”
[EN] "Shuzhen was naive before. I will have no more kindred spirits. Please stop thinking of Shuzhen as one."
[ZH] 梦容说者无意，千里寻听者有心。若梦容知道仪王的心里装着她的好友，恐怕闺中密友的情谊也该走到尽头。
[EN] "You... can be this ruthless? Leaving me not even... a last shred of hope?"
[ZH] 千里寻后悔今日与申简辰组队捶丸，又跟着梦容放风筝。要知道女子最是敏感，估计梦容从仪王的表现中感觉出不对，才会变得忧心。
[EN] The tremor in his voice struck straight at Qianli Xun's chest. "Young Master Qiao jests. There was never any hope between us, let alone a last shred."
[ZH] 还是不要再待下去，只怕待下去申简辰又不知会做出什么来。
[EN] Struggling to steady his breathing, Qiao Wenxian forced his tone even. "It is all my fault... Can you promise me one thing? Even if we cannot be together, we remain friends. Even if you no longer call me a kindred spirit, call me Wenyuan, and let me still call you Shuzhen."
[ZH] “梦容，我有些不舒服，想回去。”
[EN] Unable to bear wounding him further, Qianli Xun nodded. "I promise. In return, promise me you will never seek me out alone again. Live your life in peace. If you live well, I will be glad."
[ZH] “刚还好好的，怎么突然不舒服？”
[EN] "Alright. I will listen to you. No more talking alone, and I will live a steady life. Whatever makes you happy, I will do." Turning on his heel, Qiao Wenxian walked back toward the young gentlemen.
[ZH] “我担心文渊一会儿再来找我，苗姑娘看到会伤心，我想尽快回望月轩。”
[EN] Mengrong noticed her brother glancing their way, likely worried about Young Master Qiao. "Ah, I truly feel for you and Young Master Qiao. I hope he recovers soon."
[ZH] “我刚才随口说说，你别往心里去。”
[EN] "Nothing to mourn," Qianli Xun replied. "I have made peace with it. Everyone has their own road to walk. Mine and Wenyuan's failed to intertwine, so we walk our separate paths. Once he grows used to his own road, he will let go."
[ZH] “跟你没关系。”
[EN] Her words turned Qiao Mengrong's thoughts inward. "I wonder if Brother's road and mine will intertwine."
[ZH] “那好吧，一旦没紧要事，记得来幽林别居陪我。”
[EN] "Of course they will. Your betrothal binds your road tight to Prince Yi's."
[ZH] “恐怕……这段时日我没有时间去幽林别居。”
[EN] "A betrothal is one thing, yet I fear Brother's heart is not with me. Just like Young Master Qiao—marrying Miao Zhiyu while his heart stays with you. I actually feel sorry for Lady Miao."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] Mengrong spoke without intent, yet every word hit Qianli Xun like a stone. If Mengrong ever discovered Prince Yi held her own best friend in his heart, their friendship would shatter on the spot. Qianli Xun regretted teaming up with Shen Jianchen for chuiwan and tagging along to fly kites. Women were perceptive; Mengrong had surely sensed something off in Prince Yi's manner today. Better to leave now before Shen Jianchen pulled another stunt.
[ZH] “婚期将近，我要想方设法让两家退婚，等成功后，再……再去幽林别居看你。”
[EN] "Mengrong, I am not feeling well. I want to head home."
[ZH] 乔梦容露出怜惜的表情：“淑真，这次只能靠你自己，哥哥也无能为力。”
[EN] "You were fine a moment ago—what came over you?"
[ZH] 千里寻敏锐地感觉出梦容并不希望她的哥哥关注此事，还好，她也是如此想。
[EN] "I worry Wenyuan might come over again and hurt Lady Miao if she sees. I want to get back to Wangyue Pavilion soon."
[ZH] 她并未接梦容的话，而是说：“我该走了，你快些去找仪王，恐怕他正在埋怨我一直霸占着你。”
[EN] "I was only rambling just now—do not take it to heart."
[ZH] 带着复杂的心情与梦容分别，千里寻径直向捶丸场走去。
[EN] "It has nothing to do with you."
[ZH] 忙退婚之事只是借口，她决定不再去幽林别居，不再接受仪王任何邀请才是真。
[EN] "Alright then. Once you have a free moment, come visit me at Youlin Retreat."
[ZH] 红豆瞧见自家小姐与郡主分别，看样子要离开，便收了风筝，小跑着送到扎风筝处，又小跑着追上小姐。
[EN] "I fear... I will not have time to visit Youlin Retreat for a while."
[ZH] 来到凉亭，与嫂嫂辞别后，千里寻带着韦嬷嬷和丫鬟坐上马车回望月轩。
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] 马车上，苦荞从怀里掏出金如意：“小姐，白风说仪王让把今日的彩头送给您，感谢您与他组队一起捶丸。”
[EN] "The wedding date is closing in. I have to find a way to get our two families to cancel the betrothal. Once it is settled, I will... I will visit you at Youlin Retreat."
[ZH] “知道了，你收起来，等乔家来接夏果时，放到她的嫁妆里。”
[EN] Pity crossed Qiao Mengrong's face. "You will have to rely on yourself this time, Shuzhen. Even Brother cannot help."
[ZH] 夏果见小姐提到自己，小心问：“小姐，您不会怪我吧？”
[EN] Qianli Xun caught the faint edge beneath her words: Mengrong did not want her brother involved in this. Just as well—neither did she. Leaving the remark untouched, she said, "I should be off. Go find Prince Yi before he blames me for hogging you all afternoon."
[ZH] “为何要怪你？”
[EN] Parting from Mengrong with a tangled heart, Qianli Xun headed straight back toward the chuiwan field. Being busy with the betrothal was only an excuse; the real truth was that she meant to stop visiting Youlin Retreat and decline every invitation from Prince Yi from here on.
[ZH] “乔公子同意纳我为妾室……”
[EN] Spotting her mistress parting ways with the princess, Hongdou wound up the kite, jogged back to return it to the kite-maker, and sprinted to catch up.
[ZH] 千里寻已不怪夏果，反而多少有些羡慕：“你想多了。你到乔家虽是妾室，嫁过去也不能太寒酸，我让韦嬷嬷给你备好嫁妆。自然比不上苗知瑜，毕竟你是嫁过去当姨娘，再说我的财力与苗家不能比，可足以让你在乔家过得不错。”
[EN] After bidding her sister-in-law farewell at the pavilion, Qianli Xun boarded her carriage with Matron Wei and her maids for the ride back to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 夏果流下几滴眼泪：“小姐，您的大恩大德我这辈子都忘不了，今世不能偿还，下辈子给您做牛做马。”
[EN] Inside the carriage, Kuqiao drew the gold ruyi scepter from her sleeve. "Miss, Bai Feng said Prince Yi sent today's prize for you, as thanks for partnering with him in chuiwan."
[ZH] “行了，什么下辈子，还做牛做马？不需要你偿还。你们三个不管因为什么原因跟着我，我都会把你们当家人一样看待，出嫁时，每人都有嫁妆，苦荞多些，毕竟她一直跟着我。”
[EN] "Put it away. When the Qiaos come to fetch Xiaguo, pack it in her dowry."
[ZH] 三个丫鬟感动不已。
[EN] Hearing her name, Xiaguo asked with caution, "Miss, you do not blame me, do you?"
[ZH] 千里寻离开时，申简辰清清楚楚注意到。喜欢的人既已离开，他也不想再待下去，起身与几位公子辞别，向梦容迎面走去。
[EN] "Why would I blame you?"
[ZH] 乔梦容面带笑容，她以为哥哥又变得贴心，见她过来，主动起身迎接，先前的疑虑顿时烟消云散。
[EN] "Young Master Qiao agreed to take me as his concubine..."
[ZH] 待哥哥靠近后，乔梦容说：“哥哥，淑真走了，我也想回去。”
[EN] Far from blaming Xiaguo, Qianli Xun felt a twinge of envy. "You are overthinking it. Even as a concubine, you cannot arrive at Qiao Manor empty-handed. I will have Matron Wei prepare a proper dowry for you. It cannot match Miao Zhiyu's, of course—you are entering as a concubine, and my purse cannot compare with the Miaos'—yet it will be plenty to keep you comfortable there."
[ZH] “幽栖居士为何着急离开，不继续陪你？”
[EN] Tears spilled down Xiaguo's cheeks. "Miss, I will never forget your immense kindness as long as I live! What I cannot repay in this life, I will serve you as ox and horse in the next!"
[ZH] “还不是被乔公子给吓的，他刚才不顾我在一旁，什么话都敢说，听得我脸红心跳。”
[EN] "Cut out the 'next life' and 'ox and horse' nonsense. You owe me nothing. No matter how each of you three ended up with me, I treat you all like family. Every one of you will get a dowry when you wed—Kuqiao gets the biggest share, since she has stood by me longest."
[ZH] 申简辰松口气，只要不是因为他便好。
[EN] All three maids blinked back tears, moved to the core.
[ZH] 乔梦容想起方才哥哥不理自己的情景：“哥哥，刚才唤你，为何不理我？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched Qianli Xun leave with clear eyes. With the woman he loved gone, he had no reason to linger. Rising to bid the scholars farewell, he walked over to meet Mengrong.
[ZH] 申简辰很想将实情告知，这种场合不适合表明心迹：“哦，刚才我在想事情，根本没听到你唤我。是哥哥不对，以后不会再发生同样的事。”
[EN] Seeing her brother walk straight toward her, Qiao Mengrong brightened, thinking his old warmth had returned. All her doubts vanished as she rose to meet him. "Brother, Shuzhen left, and I want to head home too."
[ZH] 乔梦容紧绷的面庞瞬间轻松下来：“我还以为哥哥心里有了别人，不愿再理我。”
[EN] "Why did Youqi Jushi hurry off instead of keeping you company?"
[ZH] 这话无疑说到申简辰心里，他并未正面否认，而是言说其他：“不知白风那小子有没有把金如意给苦荞，让她给幽栖居士带回去。我可不希望别人以为我是一个贪图小便宜的王爷。”
[EN] "Young Master Qiao scared her off! He did not care a bit that I was standing right there—said such bold things it made my ears burn."
[ZH] 哥哥没有急切否认，乔梦容又不再轻松：“哥哥为何如此关心金如意的去向？”
[EN] Relief washed over Shen Jianchen. As long as he was not the reason she fled.
[ZH] “你呀，想得太多，估计是累的缘故，我们赶快回幽林别居。”申简辰拉起乔梦容，也向捶丸场走去。
[EN] Then Qiao Mengrong recalled the kite incident. "Brother, why did you ignore me when I called you earlier?"
[ZH] 哥哥说她想得太多，又拉着她的手，也算是一种回应，乔梦容又轻松一些。
[EN] Part of him burned to spill the truth, yet this was neither the time nor the place. "Oh, I was lost in thought and never heard you. Brother's fault—it will not happen again."
[ZH] 也许是自己想得太多，眼看别人的婚期将近，她还需等到中秋节，实在太晚，如若也能在这个春季成亲，那该多好。
[EN] The tension drained from Qiao Mengrong's face. "I thought Brother had someone else in his heart and no longer cared to talk to me."
[ZH] 乔梦容想起在汴梁时，梁内侍常常代表官家到仪王府看望哥哥，每次由她接待。乔梦容总是偷偷给梁内侍塞些银子，故而与他有些交情。不如给梁内侍写封信，拜托他说服官家下旨将婚期提前，省得夜长梦多。
[EN] Her words hit straight on the mark. Skipping a direct denial, Shen Jianchen steered elsewhere. "I wonder if that rascal Bai Feng handed the gold ruyi scepter to Kuqiao for Youqi Jushi. I would hate for folks to think me a stingy prince."
[ZH] 打定主意后，乔梦容跟着哥哥绕过捶丸场，穿过园林，坐上马车回幽林别居。
[EN] Noticing he had not hurried to deny it, Qiao Mengrong stiffened again. "Why does Brother care so much about where that scepter ended up?"
[ZH] 千里寻离开时，也被乔文仙看到，他猜测淑真辞别郡主迅速离开与他有关。心中想道：“你既不愿见我，我便满足你。”
[EN] "You overthink everything—must be tired. Let us hurry back to Youlin Retreat." Taking her hand, Shen Jianchen led her toward the chuiwan grounds.
[ZH] 接着仪王也离开，乔文仙自斟自饮，连着饮完六杯，已有五分醉意：“我决定今日起放下这段感情……”
[EN] His gentle scolding and the warmth of his hand felt like reassurance enough, easing her heart a touch. Still, watching everyone else's wedding dates approach while hers dragged all the way to Mid-Autumn Festival felt far too long. What if they could marry this spring too?
[ZH] 东方尘泽赞同道：“这就对了！”
[EN] She recalled back in Bianliang how Imperial Attendant Liang often visited Prince Yi's Manor on His Majesty's behalf, and she had always received him—slipping him silver on the side until they had grown quite chummy. Why not write to Imperial Attendant Liang and ask him to persuade His Majesty to move the wedding date forward, before delays bred fresh trouble?
[ZH] 乔文仙继续说：“我会把淑真永远放在心里，她在我心里的位置谁都替代不了。”
[EN] Her mind made up, Qiao Mengrong followed her brother past the chuiwan grounds, through the gardens, and into their carriage back to Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 三位公子面面相觑。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had watched Qianli Xun leave too, guessing her hasty farewell to the princess was on his account. If you want nothing to do with me, he told himself, I will grant you your wish.
[ZH] 安明轩说：“都要放下感情，为何还要把人放在心里？”
[EN] Once Prince Yi left as well, Qiao Wenxian poured cup after cup for himself, downing six straight until a heavy buzz clouded his eyes. "Starting today, I am letting go of these feelings..."
[ZH] 曹班也不明白：“这人和感情有区别吗？”
[EN] "That is the spirit!" Dongfang Chenze applauded.
[ZH] 乔文仙借着五分醉意奚落道：“你们两个从来没爱过，什么都不懂。”
[EN] "...and keeping Shuzhen locked in my heart forever. Nobody will ever take her place."
[ZH] 两人转向东方尘泽：“东方兄，你可懂？”
[EN] The three friends stared at one another.
[ZH] 东方尘泽摇摇头，吟了两句诗：“世间无限丹青手，一片伤心画不成。”
[EN] "If you are letting go of the feelings," An Mingxuan asked, "why keep the woman locked in your heart?"
[ZH] 曹班很是不解：“东方兄为何吟诵这两句？好像这两句无法解惑？”
[EN] "Is there a difference between the person and the feelings?" Cao Ban scratched his head.
[ZH] 安明轩解释道：“东方兄的意思是说他虽有喜欢的女子，可如同‘丹青手’一般，无法描绘出乔兄的心情。”
[EN] Half-drunk, Qiao Wenxian sneered at them. "Neither of you has ever been in love—you know nothing."
[ZH] “哦，这么解释，倒有些意思。”曹班转向乔文仙，“乔兄，人和感情有什么区别我们可搞不明白，只要你能想通，知道什么该执着，什么该放下，我们三人也就放心。”
[EN] Both turned to Dongfang Chenze. "Do you get it, Brother Dongfang?"
[ZH] 乔文仙继续生无所恋地饮酒：“她喜欢我放下我便放下，她喜欢我不再去烦她我便不去烦她，她喜欢我娶苗知瑜我便娶，她喜欢我纳妾我便纳妾，她喜欢我好好过日子，我便好好过……”
[EN] Shaking his head, Dongfang Chenze recited a couplet: "'Though master painters fill this boundless world, / Not one can paint a single broken heart.'" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 三位公子又一次面面相觑，这哪是放下？分明是换一种方式折磨自己。
[EN] "Why quote those two lines?" Cao Ban asked, baffled. "How does that answer our question?"
[ZH] ……
[EN] An Mingxuan explained, "Brother Dongfang means that even though he has a woman he loves, like those 'master painters,' he cannot capture Brother Qiao's sorrow."
[ZH] 仪王既已离开捶丸场，其他人也都跟着离开，乔家举办的捶丸赛不用任何人宣布结束，自然而然也就结束。
[EN] "Oh, put like that, it makes sense." Cao Ban turned back to Qiao Wenxian. "We may not fathom the difference between the person and the feelings, Brother Qiao, yet so long as you figure out what to hold onto and what to release, the three of us can rest easy."
[ZH] ……
[EN] Drinking on with hollow eyes, Qiao Wenxian murmured, "She wants me to let go, so I let go. She wants me to stop bothering her, so I stop. She wants me to marry Miao Zhiyu, so I marry her. She wants me to take a concubine, so I take one. She wants me to live a good life, so I will live one..."
[ZH] 天黑时，乔梦容吃过晚饭，吩咐喜兰摆上笔墨，开始写书信。无非是求梁师成帮忙说服官家将她与仪王的婚期提前。
[EN] The three friends traded another helpless look. Letting go? He was just finding a brand-new way to torture himself.
[ZH] 到底提前到哪个月份？乔梦容提笔思索一会儿，乔公子的婚礼在三月，淑真与陈家公子也定在三月，看来三月是个好月份，不如也定在三月。于是提笔写下请求的月份。
[EN] Once Prince Yi departed the chuiwan grounds, the other guests drifted off in turn. Nobody needed to call an end to the Qiaos' tournament; it wound down on its own.
[ZH] 信写好后，盖上佳和郡主的印章，折好放进信封内，将信封上的绳子绑好，递给喜兰：“你连夜去趟驿馆，让明日一早将信送往汴梁。”
[EN] After nightfall and supper, Qiao Mengrong had Xilan lay out brush and ink and composed her letter—pleading with Liang Shicheng to talk His Majesty into advancing her wedding to Prince Yi. Which month should she ask for? Brush poised, she thought it over. Young Master Qiao's wedding fell in the third month, and Shuzhen's with Young Master Chen was in the third month too. Clearly the third month was auspicious; she would request the third month as well.
[ZH] 郡主写信时，喜兰大略扫了几眼，知道郡主是想求梁内侍想办法将婚期提前。
[EN] Stamping the page with Princess Jiahe's seal, she folded it into an envelope, tied the cord, and handed it to Xilan. "Take this to the relay station tonight so it rides out for Bianliang at first light."
[ZH] 别说郡主等不及，喜兰也等不及。王爷这么优秀，临安女子又招人喜欢，只有两人尽快完婚，她与郡主方可放心。
[EN] Having glimpsed a few lines as her mistress wrote, Xilan knew the letter begged Imperial Attendant Liang to move the wedding up. Never mind the princess—Xilan herself could not wait another day. His Highness was far too fine a catch, and Lin'an's maidens far too charming. Only once the wedding was sealed could either of them breathe easy.

#CH 168
[ZH] 没过几日，乔家又开始广发请帖，邀请大家去乔府参加儿子的婚礼。
[ZH] 这一次也派人将喜帖送到朱家，倒是没给望月轩送，除因要纳望月轩的丫鬟为妾室外，考虑到朱家姑娘与儿子的一段情，还是不送得好。
[ZH] 千里寻也是这么想的，乔家的做法令她满意。
[ZH] 夏果的嫁妆已提前五日准备好。出嫁这日，她穿上乔家送来的妾室婚服，装扮完毕后，头顶盖头，坐在丫鬟房等候。
[ZH] 苦荞坐在一旁陪她聊天儿：“你这下如愿以偿，可我对你就是不满，总觉得你是抢小姐喜欢的人。”
[ZH] 夏果并不怨苦荞：“错！我反而觉得是替小姐嫁给乔公子……”
[ZH] “小姐要是嫁过去肯定是正室，你可是去给人家做妾，替得了吗？”
[ZH] “说的也是，我只求替小姐守着乔公子，省得苗姑娘抢走乔公子的心。”
[ZH] 千里寻带红豆打算到丫鬟房看夏果，刚走到门口，听到两人对话。犹豫片刻后才走进去，叮嘱道：“夏果，你记着，无须你替我守着谁，你嫁到乔家是你的选择，跟我没有任何关系。”
[ZH] 夏果撩起盖头，跪在小姐面前：“小姐，我以后不能再伺候您，又深知乔公子对您的感情，才会如此打算。”
[ZH] 千里寻上前扶起夏果：“以后不要再跪我，也别想着我，到乔家好好过自己的日子。”
[ZH] 夏果点点头：“我记住小姐的话，好好过日子。”
[ZH] 千里寻唤了声“红豆”，红豆将手中的木盒打开，递到小姐面前。
[ZH] 千里寻从木盒中取出有字迹和红色印迹的纸张：“这是你的卖身契，今日我当着你的面把它撕了，以后你再也不是任何人的丫鬟。”
[ZH] 亲眼目睹小姐将卖身契撕毁，撕碎的纸片从小姐的手中落在脚下，夏果流下激动的泪水：“我会永远记着小姐对我的恩情。”
[ZH] 千里寻把夏果头上的红盖头放下：“不要总把恩情挂在嘴边。”
[EN] A few days later, the Qiao family sent out wedding invitations across Lin'an for their son's marriage. An invitation went to Zhu Manor as well, yet none came to Wangyue Pavilion—both because the Qiaos were taking a maid from there as concubine and out of tact regarding Miss Zhu's past bond with their son. Qianli Xun felt the same and appreciated their restraint.
[ZH] 门外传来韦嬷嬷的声音：“姑娘，乔家的花轿已到，正在外面候着。”
[EN] Xiaguo's dowry had been packed five days ahead. On the wedding morning, dressed in the concubine's bridal robes sent by the Qiaos and draped under a red veil, she sat waiting in the maids' quarters.
[ZH] 千里寻并未回话，向苦荞吩咐：“你与红豆一起将夏果送出去。”
[EN] Sitting beside her, Kuqiao kept her company. "You got your wish at last, yet I still cannot warm up to you. Feels like you stole the man Miss loved."
[ZH] 两个丫鬟扶起夏果，小心翼翼走出丫鬟房，生怕新娘一不小心被绊到。
[EN] Xiaguo held no grudge. "Wrong! The way I see it, I am marrying Young Master Qiao in Miss's stead..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷站院子里等待，见夏果顶着盖头出来，说道：“嫁妆已搬到屋外，跟花轿一起进入乔府，姑娘担心你到乔家没有自己的人，让我从望月轩给你挑名丫鬟带上。”
[EN] "If Miss married him, she would be primary wife. You are going in as a concubine—how does that count as taking her place?"
[ZH] “感谢小姐和嬷嬷。”夏果顶着盖头说着感激的话。
[EN] "Fair point. I just want to guard Young Master Qiao for Miss's sake, so Lady Miao does not steal his heart."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷唤道：“艾墨！”
[EN] Approaching the door with Hongdou to see Xiaguo off, Qianli Xun overheard the exchange. After a beat of hesitation, she stepped inside and spoke with quiet gravity. "Remember this, Xiaguo: I have no need for you to guard anyone for me. Marrying into the Qiao family is your own choice and has nothing to do with me."
[ZH] “奴婢在。”
[EN] Lifting her veil, Xiaguo dropped to her knees before her mistress. "Miss, I can no longer serve you, and knowing how deep Young Master Qiao's feelings for you run, I could not help thinking that way."
[ZH] “你以后跟着夏果，到了乔家好生伺候她。”
[EN] Qianli Xun reached down and pulled her up. "Do not kneel to me anymore, and stop fretting over me. Go to Qiao Manor and build a good life for yourself."
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] Xiaguo nodded. "I will remember Miss's words and live well."
[ZH] 艾墨走到夏果身后，跟着向门外走去。
[EN] Qianli Xun called Hongdou forward, and the maid opened a wooden box in her hands. From inside, Qianli Xun drew out a sheet of paper covered in ink and red seals. "Here is your deed of sale. Today I tear it up right before your eyes. From this moment on, you belong to no one as a maid."
[ZH] 望月轩外，乔家的花轿已在等候，苦荞与红豆把夏果送上花轿，艾墨跟在轿子旁。
[EN] Watching the torn scraps flutter from her mistress's hands to the floor, Xiaguo wept with emotion. "I will remember Miss's kindness forever."
[ZH] 当乔家的轿夫抬起轿子后，夏果掀开一边的帘子，撩起盖头，向外叮嘱：“我不在，你们两个要好生伺候小姐。”
[EN] Lowering the red veil back over Xiaguo's head, Qianli Xun said, "Stop keeping the word 'kindness' on the tip of your tongue."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“别再瞎操心，你在时，还不都是我伺候？”
[EN] Matron Wei's voice called from outside. "Miss, the Qiao bridal sedan has arrived and waits at the gate."
[ZH] 苦荞说得没错，她当丫鬟时，确实心思没用在小姐身上。今日要离开，想到小姐对她的好，竟然生出不舍来。
[EN] Without answering aloud, Qianli Xun instructed Kuqiao, "Walk Xiaguo out with Hongdou."
[ZH] 当放下帘子，再次顶好红盖头后，不舍已没有，只有幸福感，她不仅碰到像小姐这么好的主人，今日还要嫁给喜欢的人，哪怕是当妾室，也比其他女子幸福许多。
[EN] Supporting the veiled bride on either side, the two maids guided her with care so she would not trip.
[ZH] 乔家的轿子开始出发，两个丫鬟目送着轿子与抬嫁妆的人走远，才将望月轩的大门关上，往小姐的闺房而去。
[EN] Matron Wei waited in the courtyard. "Your dowry is already outside, ready to follow the sedan into Qiao Manor," she told Xiaguo. "Worried you would have no one of your own there, Miss had me pick a maid from Wangyue Pavilion to accompany you."
[ZH] 千里寻离开丫鬟房，回到自己的屋子，坐在书桌前发呆。
[EN] "Thank you, Miss! Thank you, Matron!" Xiaguo said from beneath her veil.
[ZH] 文渊今日成亲，她的内心有种到莫名的空寂，空到既没有心情编故事，更没有心情提笔写字，只顾坐着回想与文渊甜蜜的过往……
[EN] "Aimo!" Matron Wei called.
[ZH] 仔细想来，幸福的时光逝去的时间并不长，可对她来讲，仿佛已是很久很久以前的事。
[EN] "Here, Matron."
[ZH] 两个丫鬟走进，觉出小姐不对。苦荞拉起红豆，悄悄走出闺房。
[EN] "From now on, you follow Xiaguo. Serve her well at Qiao Manor."
[ZH] 千里寻丝毫没有觉出两个丫鬟进来过，只顾沉浸在往事的回忆中……
[EN] "Yes, Matron." Aimo stepped behind Xiaguo and trailed them to the gate.
[ZH] 越回忆越觉得悲伤，想到文渊今晚会与别人洞房花烛，她的内心像被刀剑割了一下，很想跑到乔家，当着众人的面，拉着文渊逃走，逃到天涯海角。
[EN] Outside Wangyue Pavilion, Kuqiao and Hongdou helped Xiaguo into the waiting sedan while Aimo took her place beside the poles. As the bearers lifted the chair, Xiaguo parted the curtain, peeked under her veil, and called out, "Now that I am gone, you two take proper care of Miss!"
[ZH] 她还是控制住这种不切实际的想法，告诫自己：“你在文渊和别人面前装得理智，为何此时会有这种想法？醒醒吧，眼看婚期将近，还是想想如何解决与陈浩宇的婚约。”
[EN] "Save your breath," Kuqiao retorted. "Even when you were here, I did all the work anyway!"
[ZH] 想到婚约，千里寻已坐不住，也没有心思再去想文渊的婚礼。
[EN] Kuqiao spoke truth; Xiaguo's heart had never been in serving her mistress. Yet leaving today, remembering how generously Miss had treated her, a genuine pang of parting tugged at her. Once the curtain dropped and she settled her veil back into place, though, regret gave way to pure bliss. Not only had she met a mistress this kind, she was marrying the man she loved—even as a concubine, she counted herself luckier than most women alive.
[ZH] 陈浩宇只说陈家老太太会耗着，到底怎么耗着？并未向她具体说明，总不能什么都不做就这么等下去。
[EN] The sedan moved off, and the two maids watched the bearers and dowry porters vanish down the street before latching Wangyue Pavilion's gate and heading toward their mistress's chambers.
[ZH] 两次在父亲面前闹，没闹出结果，不如去陈家闹，让陈家的长辈亲眼瞧瞧朱家姑娘彪悍的一面，说不定陈家十分不满，主动找朱家退婚。
[EN] Back in her own room, Qianli Xun sat at her desk staring into space. Today was Wenyuan's wedding, and a strange hollowness settled over her—too empty to invent plots, too empty to pick up a brush, drifting back through sweet memories of their time together. Those bright days had not ended so long ago, yet they already felt half a lifetime away.
[ZH] 千里寻打定主意，再次走出闺房，向丫鬟房大喊：“红豆！跟我出趟门！”
[EN] Entering the room, the two maids sensed her mood at once; Kuqiao tugged Hongdou's sleeve and slipped back out without a sound.
[ZH] 苦荞跟红豆一起出来，小姐只喊红豆，苦荞埋怨道：“小姐自从有了红豆，开始偏心，出门都不带我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun never even noticed them come and go, sinking deeper into memory. The more she remembered, the sharper the ache grew. Picturing Wenyuan sharing a wedding chamber with someone else tonight sliced at her chest like a blade; an absurd urge surged up to storm Qiao Manor, grab Wenyuan's hand before the whole crowd, and run to the ends of the earth.
[ZH] 千里寻笑着解释：“我出去闹事，只怕会伤到你。你好好在家里待着，等我的好消息。”
[EN] She clamped down on the fantasy and gave herself a mental slap. You played the cool, rational woman in front of Wenyuan and everyone else—what are you getting dramatic over now? Wake up! Your own wedding date is looming; figure out how to kill this engagement to Chen Haoyu!
[ZH] 苦荞怕小姐因乔公子成亲出门干傻事：“小姐，您可千万不要到乔家闹……”
[EN] One thought of the betrothal got her out of her chair, wiping Wenyuan's wedding from her mind. Chen Haoyu had only said his grandmother would stall, without explaining how. She could not sit around waiting for a miracle. Staging scenes before her father had failed twice; why not take the show straight to Chen Manor? Let the Chen elders see Miss Zhu's ferocious side firsthand—maybe they would recoil in horror and demand the engagement be broken themselves.
[ZH] “想什么呢？乔家与我何干？我是去陈家。”
[EN] Her mind set, she strode out of her room and shouted toward the maids' quarters. "Hongdou! Come out with me!"
[ZH] “陈家也不行，小姐名声刚好，再去闹，只怕名声会更不好。”
[EN] Both Kuqiao and Hongdou emerged, and Kuqiao pouted. "Ever since Hongdou arrived, Miss plays favorites and never takes me out anymore."
[ZH] “别瞎操心，名声算什么？幸福才重要。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. "I am going out to raise hell and do not want you getting hurt. Stay home and wait for good news."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷正好过来，听到苦荞与姑娘的对话，姑娘要去做什么，她已心知肚明。
[EN] Fearing her mistress might do something rash over Young Master Qiao's wedding, Kuqiao gasped. "Miss, please do not go make a scene at Qiao Manor—"
[ZH] “苦荞，别拦着姑娘，再说你也拦不住，让姑娘去吧，为人生搏一回，说不定还能如愿以偿。”
[EN] "What are you imagining? What is Qiao Manor to me? I am heading to the Chens'."
[ZH] 千里寻上前抱住韦嬷嬷：“还是嬷嬷最好。”
[EN] "The Chens' is no better! Your reputation just recovered—make another scene and it will sink worse than before!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷捋着姑娘鬓角的头发：“你的确跟以前不一样，敢拼敢做，嬷嬷为你高兴，只要你觉得对，想做什么就去做吧，不过得记住，不能伤到别人，如若今日不成功，就好好想想我之前的叮嘱。”
[EN] "Stop fussing. What is reputation worth next to happiness?"
[ZH] 千里寻放开韦嬷嬷，努力回想到底嬷嬷叮嘱过什么重要的话，可怎么都想不起来：“嬷嬷能否告诉我是什么叮嘱？”
[EN] Walking up just then, Matron Wei caught the exchange and knew at once what her mistress planned. "Do not stop her, Kuqiao—you could not stop her if you tried. Let her go fight one round for her own life. Who knows, she might get her wish."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷郑重地说：“如果必须嫁到陈家，姑娘该怎么办？”
[EN] Qianli Xun wrapped her arms around Matron Wei. "Matron is the best!"
[ZH] 千里寻这才想起，她还没认真思考过这个问题：“我答应嬷嬷，今日不成功，便好好想想。”
[EN] Smoothing a stray lock behind her mistress's ear, Matron Wei smiled. "You truly are not the girl you used to be—bold enough to fight for what you want. Matron is proud of you. Do whatever you judge right, only remember not to injure anyone. And if today fails, think hard on what I urged you before."
[ZH] “去吧，早去早回。”韦嬷嬷说完，目睹自家姑娘带着红豆离开。
[EN] Stepping back, Qianli Xun racked her brain, drawing a blank. "Could Matron remind me what you urged?"
[ZH] 苦荞担忧道：“嬷嬷纵着小姐就不怕老爷知道怪罪您？”
[EN] "What you will do if you truly must marry into the Chen family," Matron Wei said in earnest.
[ZH] “当然怕，可我更不愿看到姑娘不开心。行了，没什么可担心的，该干什么干什么，在家里安心等着姑娘的好消息。”
[EN] Only then did it come back to her; she had never sat down to face that question head-on. "I promise, Matron. If today fails, I will think it through."
[ZH] “如果是坏消息呢？”
[EN] "Go on, and come back early."
[ZH] “哪有什么坏消息。”
[EN] Matron Wei watched her mistress stride off with Hongdou.
[ZH] 苦荞甚是疑惑：“嬷嬷这话是什么意思？难道您相信小姐会闹得陈家同意退婚？”
[EN] "Matron," Kuqiao fretted, "aren't you scared Master will find out you indulged Miss and punish you?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷神秘地笑道：“你还年轻，不知道这人生之事无坏事的道理，行了，我还有事，懒得跟你耽搁时间。”
[EN] "Of course I am scared, yet I hate seeing Miss unhappy even more. Enough worrying—go about your chores and wait for good news."
[ZH] 见韦嬷嬷要走，苦荞上前拦住：“嬷嬷总是喜欢故弄玄虚，今日不把话说清楚，我就不放您走。”
[EN] "What if it is bad news?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷“呵呵”笑了两声：“看在你从小跟着姑娘，又对姑娘忠心的份上，不妨告诉你。你觉得姑娘是嫁到陈家好，还是不嫁好？”
[EN] "There is no such thing as bad news."
[ZH] 苦荞不假思索：“当然不嫁好，小姐一直是这么想的。”
[EN] Kuqiao blinked. "What do you mean, Matron? Do you really think Miss will scare the Chens into breaking the betrothal?"
[ZH] “在我看来，嫁也好，不嫁也好。”
[EN] Matron Wei gave a cryptic chuckle. "You are young yet and do not grasp that nothing in life is wholly bad. Enough chatter—I have errands to run."
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[EN] Blocking her path, Kuqiao pouted. "Matron always loves talking in riddles! I am not letting you leave until you explain!"
[ZH] “嫁有嫁的好处，不嫁有不嫁的好处，任何事情都要往好处想。”
[EN] Matron Wei chuckled. "Seeing how loyal you have been to Miss since childhood, I will humor you. Tell me: do you think it is better for Miss to marry into the Chen family, or not to marry?"
[ZH] “嬷嬷的话我不赞同，照这么说，您又何必支持小姐与林觉和离？”
[EN] "Not to marry, of course!" Kuqiao answered in a heartbeat. "That is what Miss has wanted all along."
[ZH] “那不一样。”韦嬷嬷不想再继续解释下去，“你好好琢磨琢磨，我要去忙了。”
[EN] "To my eyes, marrying is fine, and not marrying is fine too."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走后，苦荞琢磨半天，依然琢磨不透，干脆找出合适的理由：“韦嬷嬷肯定是因为年纪大，说话才不清不楚，要是像嬷嬷说的那样，小姐还努力个什么劲儿？”
[EN] "What does that mean?"
[ZH] 千里寻来到陈府大门外，大门紧闭，匾额上“陈府”两个大字显得无比威严，让人不由得小心胆怯起来。
[EN] "Marrying brings its own rewards, and staying unwed brings its own. Look for the bright side in everything."
[ZH] 红豆靠近小姐：“小姐，要不我们还是回去吧？”
[EN] "I do not buy that!" Kuqiao protested. "By that logic, why did you back Miss's divorce from Lin Jue?"
[ZH] 既然来了，哪有放弃的道理？
[EN] "That was different." Declining to explain further, Matron Wei patted her arm. "Mule over it yourself. I have work to do."
[ZH] “你会拳法，怕什么？来都来了，不闹上一回，实在可惜。”
[EN] After Matron Wei walked away, Kuqiao puzzled over it half the morning without cracking it, and gave up with a convenient excuse: Matron Wei must be getting old and rambling—if it were really true, why was Miss knocking herself out trying to escape?
[ZH] “好吧，小姐不怕，我也不怕，现在就陪您闯进去。”
[EN] Outside Chen Manor, the towering front gates stood shut beneath an imposing plaque reading "Chen Manor," radiating an air of wealth and authority that made visitors shrink back.
[ZH] “记着，不要把人伤得太重。”
[EN] Hongdou leaned close. "Miss, maybe we should turn back?"
[ZH] “记住了，我这就去敲门。”
[EN] "Why back out after coming all this way? You know kung fu—what is there to fear? Since we are here, leaving without a proper ruckus would be a crime."
[ZH] 红豆上前，在大门上敲了三下：“咚咚咚！”
[EN] "Alright! If Miss is not scared, neither am I. I will charge right in with you."
[ZH] 陈家的下人打开门，探出个脑袋：“你是谁？为何敲陈家的门？”
[EN] "Remember—do not hurt anyone too bad."
[ZH] 红豆用目光向小姐请示，千里寻使了一个肯定的眼色。
[EN] "Got it. I will knock."
[ZH] 只见红豆一脚将门踢开，下人被力道影响向后退去，红豆一把抓住那人的衣领：“陈家未来的少夫人过来探个路，顺便瞧瞧未来的公婆是否好相处，识相的赶紧把能叫的主子都叫出来，夹道欢迎朱家姑娘！”
[EN] Hongdou marched up and pounded three times: *Bang! Bang! Bang!*
[ZH] 明显对方来者不善，还好陈家看门的下人也不是吃素的，只见那下人伸出一拳打向红豆。
[EN] A gatekeeper cracked the door and poked his head out. "Who are you? What business do you have banging on Chen Manor's gate?"
[ZH] 红豆轻松闪过，一拳将下人打得老远。
[EN] Hongdou looked to her mistress; Qianli Xun gave a crisp nod.
[ZH] 瞬间许多下人围过来，有的手中还拿着大棒子。
[EN] With one kick, Hongdou blew the door wide open, sending the gatekeeper staggering backward, and snatched him by the collar. "The Chen family's future young madam has come to scout the territory and see if her future in-laws are easy to get along with! If you know what is good for you, call out every master in the house to form a welcoming line for Miss Zhu!"
[ZH] 倒地的下人嘴角被打得又肿又痛，他摸摸嘴角流出的血，大声命令：“一起上！”
[EN] Seeing trouble at the door, the gatekeeper was no pushover himself and swung a punch at Hongdou. Hongdou sidestepped with ease and punched him flying several paces back. Within seconds, a swarm of servants rushed up, several clutching heavy staves.
[ZH] 下人们一起向红豆打去，红豆努力应对着……
[EN] Wiping blood from his swollen lip, the downed gatekeeper bellowed, "Jump her together!"
[ZH] 千里寻迈开步子，走进陈府大门，向正在打红豆的众人说：“你们这点儿三脚猫的功夫，根本阻止不了老娘身边的丫鬟。劝你们识相些，去把我未来的公爹和婆婆叫来，老娘要与他们好好聊聊。”
[EN] The servants surged at Hongdou, who fought them off with brisk strikes.
[ZH] 打又打不过，还有人被打伤，众人只得罢手，立即有人跑去报告主人。
[EN] Stepping unhurried across the threshold, Qianli Xun called out to the brawling servants, "With your half-baked kung fu, you cannot even lay a hand on my maid! Do yourselves a favor and fetch my future father-in-law and mother-in-law—I want a proper chat with them!"
[ZH] 红豆对刚才开门的下人命令道：“你！还不快去给我家小姐找把椅子来！”
[EN] Outmatched and already nursing bruises, the servants backed off, and one sprinted inside to alert the masters.
[ZH] 那下人吃了亏，不敢再言语，再说朱家姑娘快要嫁到陈家，还是礼貌些，从守门下人待的屋子里找来一把椅子，放在朱家姑娘身边：“姑娘坐，老爷和夫人一会儿到。”
[EN] Hongdou barked at the gatekeeper who had opened the door, "You! Fetch a chair for my mistress at once!"
[ZH] 千里寻坐下，对今日的开场十分满意，她相信，陈浩宇的父母看到未来儿媳妇是这个样子，肯定会后悔订下亲事。
[EN] Having tasted her fist, the servant dared not argue—and besides, Miss Zhu was about to become his young madam anyway. He scrambled into the gatehouse, hauled out a chair, and set it beside her. "Please sit, Miss. Master and Madam will be right out."
[ZH] 乔家举办婚事，自然也给陈家送喜帖，考虑到乔家放了朱家鸽子，儿子又与朱家姑娘订亲，陈江林不好亲自出面，派二弟陈江树携夫人代表陈家前去贺喜。
[EN] Qianli Xun lowered herself into the chair, well pleased with the opening act. Once Chen Haoyu's parents laid eyes on a daughter-in-law like this, they were bound to regret ever sealing the betrothal.
[ZH] 陈江林正在陪姚轻琰一边下棋，一边聊为儿子举办婚礼之事。
[EN] The Qiaos had sent the Chen family a wedding invitation too, of course. Considering the Qiaos had jilted the Zhus while his own son was engaged to Miss Zhu, Chen Jianglin had found it awkward to attend in person and sent his second brother, Chen Jiangshu, and his wife to represent the Chens instead.
[ZH] 下人不顾丫鬟阻拦，冲进屋：“老爷，夫人，不好了，未来的少夫人，朱家姑娘带人打进陈府，说要见见未来的公爹和婆婆……”
[EN] Back in the inner hall, Chen Jianglin was playing chess with Yao Qingyan and talking over plans for their son's wedding when a servant burst past the maids into the room. "Master! Madam! Disaster! The future young madam—Miss Zhu—just fought her way through the gates demanding to see her future father-in-law and mother-in-law!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰放下刚拿在手中的棋子，惊讶地问：“什么？打进来？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan set down her chess piece in shock. "What? Fought her way in?"
[ZH] “是，是打进来，夫人您看，我脸上还有伤，是被朱家姑娘身边的丫鬟给打的。”
[EN] "Yes, fought right in! Look at my face, Madam—her maid beat me black and blue!"
[ZH] 那下人脸上果然青一块紫一块。
[EN] Sure enough, his face was blotched with fresh bruises.
[ZH] 姚轻琰问：“老爷，该怎么办？”
[EN] "What do we do, Master?" Yao Qingyan asked.
[ZH] 陈江林对下人说：“你先下去，告诉朱家姑娘，让她稍等片刻，我与夫人待会儿就到。”
[EN] "Go back and tell Miss Zhu to wait a moment," Chen Jianglin instructed the servant. "Madam and I will be right there."
[ZH] “是，老爷。”下人领命而去。
[EN] "Yes, Master." The servant hurried out.
[ZH] 姚轻琰担忧道：“看来朱家姑娘确实是个厉害角色，难怪儿子不愿娶她。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan frowned in concern. "Miss Zhu really is a handful. No wonder Haoyu refused to marry her."
[ZH] 陈江林点点头：“是厉害，不过知府大人给我叮嘱过，这俩孩子在他面前闹过两回，都没成功，估计朱家姑娘见她爹那里行不通，才来陈家闹，目的不过是想让两家退婚。”
[EN] "Formidable indeed," Chen Jianglin nodded. "Still, Prefect Zhu warned me ahead of time: those two staged scenes in front of him twice without getting anywhere. Seeing her father will not budge, Miss Zhu must have brought the act here to force a broken engagement."
[ZH] “退还是不退？”
[EN] "So do we break it off or not?"
[ZH] “当然不退，孩子们瞎闹，我们大人岂能跳进他们的圈套？”
[EN] "Of course not! Why would grown adults walk straight into a pair of brats' trap?"
[ZH] “老爷说的是，我听老爷的。”
[EN] "Master is right. I will follow your lead."
[ZH] “走，夫人随我去瞧瞧未来的儿媳妇，亲眼见识一下她有多厉害。”
[EN] "Come along, Madam. Let us go meet our future daughter-in-law and see firsthand just how fierce she is."
[ZH] 陈江林放下手中的棋子，与夫人下了榻；丫鬟们也都跟上，向大门口走去。
[EN] Setting his piece aside, Chen Jianglin stepped down from the couch with his wife and strode toward the main gate with their maids trailing behind.

#CH 169
[ZH] 千里寻坐在椅子上，耐心等待陈浩宇的父母前来。
[ZH] 她听嫂嫂说起过，他们不仅人好，二人之间的感情也甚好，唯一失败的便是培养了一个不成器的儿子。
[ZH] 千里寻思忖，等二位长辈到来，怎么能将其威慑一番？让他们觉得这个未来的儿媳妇绝对不能娶。
[ZH] ﻿她环顾四周，发现花坛旁边有块被打磨成方形的石头，比砚台大些。
[ZH] “红豆，去把那块石头给我取来。”
[ZH] 红豆跑到花坛旁，将石头双手搬起来，倒不沉，一只手托好，另一手掏出帕子，把石头上的尘土擦干净，回到小姐面前，双手呈上。
[ZH] 千里寻接过石头，一只手掂了掂，重量一般，待会儿靠它吓唬陈浩宇的爹娘应该不成问题。
[ZH] 正掂着石头，青瓜闻风而来：“朱家姑娘，您这是做什么？我家公子今日不在，您要有气，等他回来再撒。”
[ZH] 千里寻故作严肃的样子：“谁说是来找你家公子？老娘要想揍他，在哪里都能解决，何必来陈府？老娘今日是来见见未来的公爹和婆婆，让他们见识一下未来的儿媳妇是什么样的人，省得日后嫁到陈家他们不习惯。”
[ZH] 青瓜很无语，退到一边。心想，未来的少夫人一口一个“老娘”，与那日在捶丸场遇到时实在不同，真怀疑是不是同一人。
[ZH] 千里寻继续掂着石头，有意跷起二郎腿，余光瞟到有人走来。
[ZH] 朝那方向瞧去，一男一女被丫鬟簇拥着向这边走来，看上去年纪与朱淑真的父母不相上下，应该是陈浩宇的爹娘。
[ZH] 本应站起来行礼迎接，可今日是来闹事，礼节还是免了，说不定见到未来的儿媳妇是无礼之人，他们更会生出退婚的想法。
[ZH] 对方越走越近，千里寻故意露出不屑的表情，斜眼瞥向两位长辈。
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰走近后，将未来的儿媳妇上下瞧一遍。以前他们与朱家姑娘有过几面之缘，留下的印象均是知书达理，温婉动人。
[ZH] 眼前这位，如果手中不拿着石头，不斜眼瞧人，别跷二郎腿，再站起来唤一声“伯父伯母”，便妥妥是位大家闺秀。
[EN] Seated in her chair, Qianli Xun waited for Chen Haoyu's parents to appear. From her sister-in-law, she had heard they were decent, deeply devoted people whose sole failure in life was raising a useless son. How could she shake them up enough to swear off marrying her into their family?
[ZH] 要不是陈江林提前叮嘱过，姚轻琰真想拿出婆婆的姿态，好好教训一下晚辈。
[EN] Scanning the courtyard, she spotted a square dressed stone beside a flowerbed, a bit bigger than an inkstone. "Hongdou, fetch me that stone."
[ZH] 千里寻见二位不说话，只顾打量她，将二郎腿翘得更高：“你们就是陈浩宇的爹娘？也就是我未来的公爹和婆婆？”
[EN] Hongdou dashed over, scooped the block up with both hands—not too heavy—balanced it on one palm, wiped the dust off with her handkerchief, and presented it to her mistress.
[ZH] 陈江林没有回答而是问道：“你是朱家姑娘？”
[EN] Qianli Xun tossed the stone once in her palm. Decent heft—plenty to put a scare into Chen Haoyu's parents.
[ZH] “老娘行不改名，坐不改姓，朱淑真是也！”千里寻故意装出女侠客的口吻。
[EN] Just then Qinggua came hurrying out at word of the ruckus. "Miss Zhu, what is going on? My young master is not home today. If you are mad at him, wait until he gets back to vent!"
[ZH] 陈江林夫妇对视一番，这“老娘”的称呼实在与大家闺秀的形象不符。
[EN] Qianli Xun put on a stern scowl. "Who said I came for your young master? If I want to thrash him, I can do it anywhere—why bother coming to Chen Manor? I came today to meet my future in-laws so they can get a good look at the bride they are getting, lest they get a shock later!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰问：“你今日来所谓何事？”
[EN] Speechless, Qinggua shrank to the side. This future young madam swaggering around like a street boss felt worlds apart from the girl he had met at the chuiwan grounds—was she even the same person?
[ZH] 千里寻放下二郎腿，她实在不习惯这个动作，继续掂着石头：“老娘快跟陈浩宇成亲，成亲前，让二位见识见识未来儿媳妇的真实面目，省得日后后悔，如果看不上老娘，现在退婚也不迟。”
[EN] Tossing the stone up and down, Qianli Xun crossed one leg over the other on purpose as movement caught the corner of her eye. A man and woman approached surrounded by maids, roughly the same age as Zhu Shuzhen's parents—Chen Haoyu's father and mother. Proper etiquette called for rising to bow, yet she had come to pick a fight; letting them see an insolent brat might speed the breakup along. As they closed in, she put on a sneer and eyed the elders sideways.
[ZH] 提到“退婚”二字，陈江林与姚轻琰再次看一眼对方，互相会心一笑。
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan sized up their future daughter-in-law from head to toe. Having met Miss Zhu a handful of times before, they remembered an educated, gentle, graceful young lady. Strip away the stone in her hand, the sidelong glare, and the cocky crossed leg—have her stand and greet them as Aunt and Uncle—and she would still look every inch a noble daughter. Had Chen Jianglin not briefed her beforehand, Yao Qingyan would have stepped right into her mother-in-law role and dressed the girl down.
[ZH] 陈江林说：“今日我与你未来的婆婆算是见识过，你可以回去了。”
[EN] Seeing the pair study her in silence, Qianli Xun hiked her crossed leg even higher. "You two are Chen Haoyu's parents? My future father-in-law and mother-in-law?"
[ZH] 如此轻松回应，倒将千里寻整懵，目的还未达到，岂能离开？
[EN] Without answering, Chen Jianglin asked, "You are Miss Zhu?"
[ZH] “二位还没说能不能接受老娘这个样子。”
[EN] "Same name walking, same surname sitting—Zhu Shuzhen at your service!" Qianli Xun barked in a mock wandering-heroine swagger.
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑道：“能不能接受由两家父母说了算，你只管回去等消息。”
[EN] Husband and wife traded a glance. That brash tone fit zero part of a sheltered daughter.
[ZH] 难道今日表演又失败？不能就这么结束。
[EN] "What brings you here today?" Yao Qingyan asked.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧向手中的石头，也不能白白掂这么久：“二位先别赶人，既然老娘来都来了，也得让二位见识见识我的厉害。”
[EN] Dropping her crossed leg—the posture cramped her anyway—Qianli Xun kept hefting the stone. "Since I am about to marry Chen Haoyu, I came to show you my true colors ahead of time so neither of you cries regret later. If you do not like what you see, it is not too late to call off the wedding right now."
[ZH] 她站起来，左手托着石头，右手攥紧拳头，一拳朝那石头打去，只见石头被打成两半，掉落在地上……
[EN] At the words "call off the wedding," Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan exchanged a knowing smile.
[ZH] 陈浩东本打算去陈家铺子走走，刚走到离大门不远处，瞧见眼前的一幕，躲起来观看，不由慨叹：“如此厉害，难怪浩宇不愿娶。可颜兄却看上她，实在令人费解。”
[EN] "Well, your future mother-in-law and I have seen plenty today," Chen Jianglin said breezily. "You may head home now."
[ZH] 陈浩东继续偷瞧着，希望能看出些名堂来。
[EN] That unbothered brush-off threw Qianli Xun off stride. Leave before getting what she came for? Not a chance! "Neither of you said whether you can stomach me like this!"
[ZH] 当朱家姑娘用拳头向石头砸去时，姚轻琰吓得闭上眼睛，再次睁开时，看到地上掉落的两半石头，甚是惊讶，一个姑娘家怎么这么大力气？
[EN] "Whether we accept you is between the two sets of parents," Yao Qingyan smiled. "Go home and wait for word."
[ZH] 千里寻拍拍手掌的灰尘：“二位也看到，老娘的拳头力气不小，专打瞧不上的人。不瞒二位，在这临安城，老娘最瞧不上的人便是陈浩宇，只怕嫁到陈家，您家儿子没好日子过。二位也别犹豫，现在就给我个答复，是愿意接受老娘嫁到陈家日日暴揍陈浩宇，还是找我爹好好合计合计退婚之事？”
[EN] Was today's whole performance about to flop too? She was not letting the curtain drop yet. Eyeing the block in her palm—she had not been hefting it for nothing—she rose to her feet. "Hold off on chasing me out! Since I came all this way, let me show you what I can really do."
[ZH] 陈江林犹豫一会儿说道：“亲事是两家大人商议定下，岂能说退就退？浩宇向来纨绔，我这当父亲的管不了他，只盼你嫁过来替我好好管教。”
[EN] Cradling the stone on her left palm, she cocked her right fist and drove it straight down. The square stone cracked clean in two and clattered to the flagstones.
[ZH] 千里寻着实佩服陈浩宇的父母四两拨千斤的功力，几句话搞得她白白努力，此计不成再施一计：“既然你不爱护自己的儿子，老娘又何必多劝，不如取来纸笔，白纸黑字写下朱淑真若打死陈浩宇不必偿命，二位还要签字画押，我便即刻离开！”
[EN] Setting out to inspect the Chen family shops, Chen Haodong had reached the gate just in time to catch the spectacle and ducked out of sight to watch. "Fierce as a beast," he marveled under his breath. "No wonder Haoyu wants no part of her. Yet Brother Yan fell for her? Baffling." He stayed tucked in the shadows, eager to see how the showdown played out.
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰还未来得及为难要不要写，传来夏老太太的声音：“不许写！”
[EN] Yao Qingyan had squeezed her eyes shut the instant the fist came down; opening them to see two jagged halves of stone on the ground left her stunned. How did a slip of a girl pack strength like that?
[ZH] 众人都向夏老太太瞧去，她满面怒色，拄着拐杖，被丫鬟搀扶，急急忙忙颤颤巍巍走来。
[EN] Qianli Xun dusted off her hands. "You saw it yourselves. My fists hit hard, and they specialize in people I despise. I will level with you: in all of Lin'an, the man I despise most is Chen Haoyu. Marry me into this house and your son will not see a single peaceful day. Save the hesitation and give me an answer right now: do you want me marrying in to beat Chen Haoyu black and blue every day, or will you go talk to my father about calling off this betrothal?"
[ZH] 千里寻为自己将老人家激怒感到不安，可事情已发展到这种地步，她不得不坚持下去，也许老太太到来能进一步促成退婚一事。
[EN] Chen Jianglin paused a beat before answering. "This match was sealed by the elders of both houses; it is not something to cancel on a whim. Haoyu has always been an unruly rake, beyond a father's control. I am counting on you to marry in and whip him into shape for me."
[ZH] 姚轻琰迎上去：“母亲，您怎么来了？”
[EN] Qianli Xun almost had to applaud the pair's slick deflection—turning a thousand pounds of force aside with four ounces of effort and leaving her swinging at air. Plan A dead? Time for Plan B! "If you care so little for your own son, I will not waste my breath warning you! Bring brush and paper right now! Put it down in black and white that if Zhu Shuzhen beats Chen Haoyu to death, she owes no life in return, sign it and press your thumbprints, and I will walk straight out!"
[ZH] “我怎么来了？你还有脸问？发生这么大的事，你们二人也不知道派人知会我一声，多亏我听下人议论才知道有人闹到陈府。”老太太边走边批评。
[EN] Before Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan could weigh how to respond, a sharp voice rang out: "Write no such thing!"
[ZH] 已有下人搬来椅子，身边的丫鬟将拐杖接过，扶老太太坐下。
[EN] Every head turned toward Old Madam Xia, marching up in a fury on her walking stick, supported by maids as she hurried forward with trembling steps.
[ZH] 夏老太太坐好后，严厉地问：“你就是朱家姑娘？江南有名的女词人？人称幽栖居士？”
[EN] A flicker of unease pricked Qianli Xun at riling up an elderly woman, yet she had gone too far to retreat—and maybe the grandmother's arrival would seal the breakup at last.
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“老人家在上，正是在下。”
[EN] "Mother, why did you come out?" Yao Qingyan rushed up to meet her.
[ZH] “哼！今日闹到陈家所为何事？”
[EN] "Why did I come out? You have the nerve to ask?" Old Madam Xia snapped as she walked. "Something this huge happens and neither of you sends word! Good thing I overheard the servants gossiping, or I would never have known someone stormed Chen Manor!"
[ZH] 陈江林打算向母亲说明，被老太太阻止：“不用你回答，我要听她说。”
[EN] Servants scrambled to set down a chair; a maid took her cane and eased her onto the seat.
[ZH] “老娘刚才已解释过，不妨向老人家再解释一遍。”千里寻见老太太听到她自称“老娘”，面露不满，她则甚是满意，要的就是这个效果，于是继续说，“一来，让陈家见识见识我的真实面目，省得嫁过来，陈家人后悔；二来，想告诉陈家，若愿退婚，现在还来得及。”
[EN] Settling in, Old Madam Xia glared daggers. "So you are Miss Zhu? Jiangnan's famed poetess? The one they call Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] “你做人倒坦率，不欺不瞒，比你爹强。你爹仗着自己是临安知府，把嫁不出去的女儿往陈家塞，却从来没告诉我们众人称赞的幽栖居士竟然是个悍妇，难怪浩宇死活不愿娶你。今日我当着众人的面，给你个答复，这婚陈家必然要退，麻烦你回去知会一声知府大人。至于你爹当时逼迫浩宇娶你的原因，我们陈家愿意用金钱偿还，让你爹出个价。”
[EN] "Respects to you, Elder Madam. That would be me," Qianli Xun answered.
[ZH] 夏老太太一口气说那么多话，清清楚楚告知已同意退婚，千里寻兴奋不已。
[EN] "Hmph! What business brought you raising a storm at Chen Manor?"
[ZH] 可也疑惑，听老太太所言，父亲逼迫陈浩宇娶她是有原因，陈家竟然愿意用钱偿还，到底是什么原因值得陈家如此做？
[EN] Chen Jianglin stepped forward to explain, only for his mother to cut him off. "Not a word from you. I want to hear it from her."
[ZH] 一时想不明白，千里寻也不愿多想，同意退婚才是关键：“老太太就是明事理，比你儿子和儿媳强多了，淑真在此谢过，我这就回去告诉父亲。”
[EN] "I already explained once, yet I do not mind telling Elder Madam again," Qianli Xun said, well pleased to see the old woman bristle at her brash swagger. "First, so the Chens can see my true face before the wedding and not cry foul afterward. Second, to tell you straight: if you want to cancel this match, there is still time."
[ZH] 千里寻说完，准备转身离开。
[EN] "At least you are blunt and hide nothing—better than your father," Old Madam Xia scoffed. "Using his rank as Prefect of Lin'an to dump a daughter nobody else wants onto our doorstep, never once breathing a word that the celebrated Youqi Jushi is a raging shrew! No wonder Haoyu fought tooth and nail against marrying you. Right here in front of everyone, I give you my answer: the Chen family is calling off this match! Go back and notify Prefect Zhu yourself. Whatever score your father held over Haoyu to force this betrothal, the Chen family will settle it in coin—tell him to name his price!"
[ZH] “慢着！”陈江林阻止道，“朱家姑娘先别着急走，退婚之事有待商议。”
[EN] Hearing Old Madam Xia spell out the cancellation in plain, ringing words sent Qianli Xun's spirits soaring—even as one line snagged her ear. Her father had held something over Chen Haoyu to force the betrothal, something the Chens would pay silver to settle? What on earth had happened?
[ZH] 千里寻站住：“怎么？又想反悔？”
[EN] No time to unravel riddles right now; ending the betrothal came first. "Elder Madam talks real sense—leagues ahead of your son and daughter-in-law! Shuzhen thanks you, and I will go tell my father right away." She spun to leave.
[ZH] 陈江林面向老太太 ：“婚姻之事是人生大事，岂可说退就退？还望母亲三思。”
[EN] "Hold on!" Chen Jianglin stepped in. "Do not rush off, Miss Zhu. Calling off a betrothal takes deliberation."
[ZH] 老太太生气道：“有什么好三思的？你们两个就知道自己恩恩爱爱，不把浩宇的幸福当回事，再说浩宇未来要承担大任，怎能娶个名声不好的进门？”
[EN] Qianli Xun halted. "What now? Going back on it already?"
[ZH] 陈浩东听到老太太的话，心中不由紧张起来，难道奶奶有心将陈家家业交到浩宇手中，才同意退了这门亲事？
[EN] Chen Jianglin turned to his mother. "Marriage is a lifelong matter, not to be tossed aside lightly. I beg Mother to reconsider."
[ZH] 陈江林继续劝：“母亲，浩宇根本无法承担大任，即使母亲有这心，我也不会同意，我只求他娶妻生子，安稳度过一生即可。”
[EN] "Reconsider what?!" Old Madam Xia snapped. "All you two care about is billing and cooing with each other, never sparing a thought for Haoyu's happiness! Besides, Haoyu has heavy responsibilities ahead of him—how could he take a wife with a tarnished name?"
[ZH] 听到大伯的话，陈浩东紧张的心情稍微轻松一些。
[EN] Overhearing from the shadows, Chen Haodong's heart skipped a beat. Did Grandmother intend to hand the Chen family business to Haoyu, and was that why she wanted this match broken?
[ZH] 老太太依然坚持：“即使不能承担大任，也不能娶朱家姑娘……”
[EN] "Mother, Haoyu is nowhere near fit for heavy responsibilities," Chen Jianglin argued. "Even if you wish it, I will never agree. All I ask is that he marry, have children, and live out a peaceful life."
[ZH] 千里寻趁机搭话：“是，绝不能娶，我名声不好，再说我跟陈浩宇合不来，只怕嫁到您家三天两头揍他，老太太如此心疼孙子，肯定不愿见他被我揍，对吧？”
[EN] His uncle's words loosened the knot in Chen Haodong's chest a notch.
[ZH] 老太太更是担心不已：“你们两个听听，她都说些什么话！这样的人怎能娶到陈家？”
[EN] "Even without heavy responsibilities, he is not marrying Miss Zhu!" Old Madam Xia stood firm.
[ZH] 千里寻更是开心，有老太太在，嫁到陈家之事八成成不了：“陈伯伯，还是听老人家的，与我父亲好好商议退婚之事，我在望月轩静候佳音。”
[EN] Qianli Xun piled right on. "Exactly—do not marry me! My name is mud, I cannot stand Chen Haoyu, and once I move in I will probably thrash him every other day. Elder Madam dotes on her grandson so much—you surely cannot bear watching me pound him, right?"
[ZH] 千里寻使出拳头，将椅子一拳砸烂，带红豆扬长而去。
[EN] Alarm deepened on Old Madam Xia's face. "Listen to the words coming out of her mouth! How could we ever welcome someone like that into Chen Manor?"
[ZH] 大家再次被她拳头的力度镇住，一个个眼睛瞪得老大，嘴巴微微张开。
[EN] Thrilled—with the matriarch firmly on her side, the wedding was nine-tenths dead—Qianli Xun grinned. "Listen to Elder Madam, Uncle Chen, and go settle the breakup with my father. I will wait for the good news at Wangyue Pavilion."
[ZH] 老太太大声说：“瞧瞧！哪有个大家闺秀的样子！乔家不愿娶，想来祸害陈家，没门儿！”
[EN] Cocking her fist, she brought it smashing down on the chair she had sat in, splintering it to pieces, then swaggered out the gate with Hongdou.
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰很无奈，没想到朱家姑娘为退婚，一点儿不顾及大家闺秀的形象。
[EN] Every onlooker froze, eyes bugging wide and mouths hanging open at the sheer power in her punch.
[ZH] 姚轻琰向陈江林使使眼色，陈江林赶紧向老太太说：“母亲，您进屋，我们好好商议此事。”
[EN] "Look at that!" Old Madam Xia cried. "Not a shred of breeding! The Qiaos refused to take her, so they try offloading the menace onto us? Over my dead body!"
[ZH] “有什么好商议的？退婚就是！”
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan exchanged helpless sighs—who knew Miss Zhu would torch every scrap of a lady's dignity just to wriggle out of a match?
[ZH] 丫鬟把拐杖送到老太太手边，她扶着拐杖努力站起来。
[EN] Catching his wife's glance, Chen Jianglin stepped to his mother's side. "Come inside, Mother. Let us talk this over properly."
[ZH] 姚轻琰上前去扶婆婆：“母亲，就是商量退婚之事，总不能因退婚伤了两家和气，所以得想一个万全的法子。”
[EN] "What is there to talk over? Cancel the match and be done with it!"
[ZH] “既如此，就去你们屋里坐会儿。”
[EN] A maid handed over her cane, and the old woman struggled to her feet. Yao Qingyan hurried to take her arm. "Mother, we mean discussing how to cancel it cleanly. We cannot ruin goodwill between the two households, so we need a thorough plan."
[ZH] 得到老太太允许，陈江林也过去扶住母亲，往他居住的院子而去。
[EN] "Very well. I will sit in your rooms a while."
[ZH] 陈浩东心想，大伯并没有让浩宇继承家业的想法，可老太太却如此想，而长辈中老太太的意见又至关重要。
[EN] Supported by both son and daughter-in-law, Old Madam Xia headed toward their courtyard.
[ZH] 父亲携母亲代表陈家去参加乔家的婚礼还未归，无人商议，还是先去铺子再说。
[EN] Back in the shadows, Chen Haodong frowned. Eldest Uncle had no mind to give Haoyu the business, yet Grandmother clearly did—and among the elders, her vote carried enormous weight. With his parents away representing the Chens at the Qiao wedding, there was nobody to consult yet; he would head to the shops first.
[ZH] 目送众人离开后，陈浩东才走到大门口，下人正在收拾被劈成两半的石头和砸烂的椅子。陈浩东心中很是不屑：“真是个活脱脱的母夜叉，浩宇要是娶了她，有得受，哼！”
[EN] Once the courtyard cleared, he strolled past the gate, where servants were sweeping up split stone and splintered wood. "A real she-demon," Chen Haodong sneered under his breath. "Haoyu is in for misery if he marries her. Hmph!"
[ZH] 下人看到大公子，都有礼貌地打招呼，陈浩东并未理会，吹着口哨走出大门。
[EN] Ignoring the servants' polite greetings, he whistled his way out the gate.

#CH 170
[ZH] 扶老太太回到屋中，安顿她坐下，姚轻琰屏退下人，把门关上，向陈江林使了使眼色。
[ZH] 陈江林明白夫人的意思，坐下主动向老太太解释：“母亲，您今日中计了。”
[ZH] 夏老太太一脸严肃，对儿子和媳妇甚是不满：“中什么计？”
[ZH] 姚轻琰这才坐到老太太身边，赔着笑脸：“母亲，只怪江林没来得及叮嘱您，知府大人上周专程去绸缎庄找江林，当面告诉他，这俩孩子故意瞎闹，就是想逼两家退婚，在朱府外闹过一回，年跟前儿全临安城的官员在聚香楼宴饮，他两个也跑去瞎闹，都把仪王给惊动了，还好知府大人没中计。”
[ZH] 陈江林接着解释：“知府大人叮嘱我，俩孩子在他跟前没闹成功，肯定还会跑到陈家来闹，让我们别当回事。”
[ZH] 夏老太太虽听明白，可依然不满：“哼！就算她是瞎胡闹，我可是亲眼瞧见她无缘无故一拳把椅子砸烂，以后浩宇娶了她，稍微做些她不满意的事，岂不是连命都没了？”
[ZH] 陈江林被老太太的话给问住，不知如何继续往下说。面对生意，他处理得头头是道，可面对家事，只觉得常言说得好：清官难断家务事，要不怎么将儿子管教成那样。
[ZH] 姚轻琰见夫君无言，想出应对之语：“母亲，文心常常在我面前夸朱家姑娘，她肯定不会骗自家人，我想朱家姑娘既然故意来闹，粗鲁的样子肯定是有意装出来……”
[ZH] 老太太哪里肯听，用拐杖敲着地：“你到底是不是浩宇的娘？只管替别人说话！她再装，拳头的劲儿能装得出来？只怕我孙子与她成亲那日，连洞房都入不了，你们两个还想指望当爷爷奶奶？”
[EN] Once Old Madam Xia was settled in a chair inside their room, Yao Qingyan dismissed the maids, shut the door, and glanced at Chen Jianglin.
[ZH] 轮到姚轻琰不敢言语，再说下去，她就成了临安城最不合格的母亲。
[EN] Reading her cue, Chen Jianglin sat down and spoke straight to his mother. "Mother, you walked straight into a trap today."
[ZH] 陈江林心想不如用知府大人的身份压压老太太：“母亲既然想退婚，儿子当然同意，只是陈家的生意能做这么大，离不开临安府衙关照，若因退婚得罪知府大人，只怕……”
[EN] Old Madam Xia's brow knotted in displeasure. "What trap?"
[ZH] “哼，你别用知府大人吓唬我，且不说有文心这层关系维系，就是没有，凭陈家现今在临安商界的地位，府衙也不敢将我们怎样。我也相信知府大人是明事理的人，绝不会公私不分。”
[EN] Taking the seat beside her with a placating smile, Yao Qingyan explained, "Blame Jianglin for not briefing you in time, Mother. Prefect Zhu paid a special visit to our silk shop last week to warn him that those two brats are staging tantrums on purpose to force both families into breaking the betrothal. They staged a brawl outside Zhu Manor once, and before New Year's they wrecked the prefecture officials' banquet at Juxiang Tower—even startling Prince Yi. Luckily Prefect Zhu did not take the bait."
[ZH] 夏老太太并未受任何影响，自从有了“耗着”的念头，已将事情前后想明白，这一番话把儿子抛出的球顺利挡回去。
[EN] "Prefect Zhu warned me that after failing with him, they were sure to bring the circus to our gate, and told us to ignore it," Chen Jianglin added.
[ZH] 姚轻琰见夫君顺着老太太的方式不错，老人家面色缓和许多，不如也顺着她：“母亲这么一说我终于放心，就听母亲的，退了朱家的亲事，可我又担心……”
[EN] Even understanding the ploy, Old Madam Xia stayed grim. "Hmph! Put-on or not, I watched with my own two eyes as she smashed a solid chair to splinters with one bare fist! Once Haoyu marries her, one tiny misstep and she might snap his neck!"
[ZH] 儿媳顺着她，夏老太太也尽量将语气放平缓：“担心什么？”
[EN] Chen Jianglin had no answer for that. He ran an empire of trade with effortless precision, yet domestic tangles lived up to the saying: not even an upright judge could untangle family affairs—which explained how his son had turned out the way he had.
[ZH] 姚轻琰见老太太中计，装作为难的样子：“可我担心知府大人揪住谣言之事不放，他之所以愿意私了，就是打着想跟陈家结亲的想法，现在陈家主动退婚，只怕知府大人会把事闹大，说不定两家还得对簿公堂……”
[EN] Seeing her husband tongue-tied, Yao Qingyan stepped in. "Mother, Wenxin sings Miss Zhu's praises to me all the time, and she would never lie to her own family. Since Miss Zhu came specifically to make a scene, all that rough swagger must have been an act..."
[ZH] 陈江林悄悄向夫人竖起大拇指，以示表扬。姚轻琰偷乐一会儿，再瞧老太太，双眉紧锁，沉思着。
[EN] Old Madam Xia thumped her cane against the floor. "Are you Haoyu's mother or hers, sticking up for an outsider? Act all she wants—can you fake the force to split stone and wood? Come the wedding night, my grandson will not even make it into the bridal chamber! You two can kiss any hopes of grandchildren goodbye!"
[ZH] 过了一会儿，老太太开口说：“我已让朱家姑娘向知府大人传话，此事陈家愿意用钱解决。”
[EN] Now Yao Qingyan fell silent; one more word and she would be branded the worst mother in Lin'an.
[ZH] 陈江林趁机夸赞：“母亲的主意甚好，能用钱解决的事儿那都不叫事儿。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin tried leaning on the Prefect's authority. "If Mother insists on breaking off the match, I will obey. Only—Chen family trade grew this vast thanks in no small part to Lin'an Prefecture's goodwill. Offend Prefect Zhu over a jilted daughter, and I fear..."
[ZH] 夏老太太倒忧虑起来：“我听文心说过，她那公公极好声誉，只怕他不要钱，只要声誉，浩宇散播谣言毁人家姑娘清誉，我们再去退婚，知府大人肯定面子上挂不住，看来这事儿用钱不一定能解决，说不定到时真的闹到公堂上。”
[EN] "Hmph! Stop waving the Prefect to scare me! Even without Wenxin linking the two houses, with the Chen family's standing in Lin'an's merchant circles, the yamen would not dare touch us. Besides, Prefect Zhu is a fair man who will not mix private grudges with official duty." Having already mulled over her stalling plan, Old Madam Xia had worked through every angle and batted her son's argument right back.
[ZH] 姚轻琰故意装作不怕的样子：“上公堂就上公堂，官府要是把浩宇在狱中关起来，大不了我们拿出些钱财，上下打点，他在牢狱中过得也不会太差。再说只是散播谣言，应该关不了几日，也就放出来。”
[EN] Noticing her husband's yielding tone had softened the old woman's mood, Yao Qingyan followed suit. "Hearing Mother put it that way sets my mind at rest. We will obey Mother and cancel the Zhu match—though I do worry about one thing..."
[ZH] “呸呸呸！说这么不吉利的话！”夏老太太批评完问道，“散播谣言官府也会治罪？”
[EN] Pleased by her daughter-in-law's deference, Old Madam Xia eased her tone. "Worry about what?"
[ZH] 陈江林半真半假添油加醋：“儿子听说三年前有两家闹到公堂，就是因为一家散播另一家的谣言，官府经过核实，还真有此事，便将散播谣言之人打了五十大板。后来听人说，本来要把人在狱中关五日，只因散播者家中有儿子即将去汴梁参加科举，怕牢狱之灾影响儿子，便上下疏通用钱买了五十大板，五十大板打下去，整个人都快不行……”
[EN] Putting on a troubled frown, Yao Qingyan baited the hook. "I worry Prefect Zhu will dig up the rumor incident and refuse to let it go. He agreed to settle it privately only because he wanted a marriage alliance with our family. If we break off the betrothal now, what if he blows the matter open and drags both houses into court?"
[ZH] “别说了！”老太太被唬住，“江林，赶紧去朱家，告诉知府大人，这婚陈家不会退，不过因朱家姑娘这一闹，婚事得往后拖一拖，至于什么时候成亲，那得看朱家姑娘什么时候改了性子。”
