[EN] Out of his mother's sight, Chen Jianglin shot his wife a silent thumbs-up. Yao Qingyan hid a triumphant smirk as Old Madam Xia knitted her brows in deep thought.
[ZH] 夫妻二人一番机智应对，保住两家的亲事，可还是回到老太太“耗着”的法子，不同的是，那时只有老太太一个人这么想，现在被朱家姑娘一闹，“耗着”的法子不得不公开。
[EN] After a pause, Old Madam Xia said, "I already told Miss Zhu to tell Prefect Zhu we will settle it with money."
[ZH] 只是不知会耗到什么时候，要是朱家姑娘永远不改性子，岂不是浩宇永远无法娶妻？夫妻二人暂时没有更好的办法，只能走一步看一步。
[EN] "Brilliant idea, Mother," Chen Jianglin praised smoothly. "Any trouble silver can fix is no trouble at all."
[ZH] 陈浩宇从外面浪荡完，本想随便找所外面的宅子住几日，听到有人提起乔文仙今日成亲，他不由想到自己，眼见婚事将近，便没心思住在外面。
[EN] Yet doubt crept over Old Madam Xia's face. "Wenxin told me her father-in-law prizes his reputation above everything. What if he refuses silver and demands face instead? Haoyu spread rumors slandering his daughter's honor, and now we jilt her—Prefect Zhu will lose face all over the city. Silver may not smooth this over, and it really could end up in court."
[ZH] 他几次跟奶奶聊天儿，打探她如何耗着。夏老太太悄悄说，到时只要她装病，会搞得陈府上上下下没有心思再准备婚礼，也就有正当理由拖延婚期。
[EN] Yao Qingyan put on a brave, unbothered front. "Let it go to court, then! Even if the yamen throws Haoyu in jail, we can spread silver around upstairs and down so he lives decently inside. Besides, it is only slander—they will hold him a few days at most and turn him loose."
[ZH] 主意虽好，却不是一个了断的法子。陈浩宇别无他法，只能应允，现在还是尽快赶回去与奶奶商议装病的日期。
[EN] "Bah! Bah! Bah! Watch your unlucky tongue!" Old Madam Xia scolded, then paused in alarm. "Does the yamen actually convict people for spreading rumors?"
[ZH] 青瓜一直站在陈府大门口向外张望，见公子的马车回来，急匆匆上前，伸手去扶公子：“公子，您可回来，家中发生了大事。”
[EN] Mixing half-truth with tall tale, Chen Jianglin laid it on thick. "Three years back, I heard two households went to court over one spreading rumors about the other. Once the yamen confirmed it, they sentenced the culprit to fifty strokes of the heavy bamboo! Word was he was first slated for five days in prison, yet because his son was heading to Bianliang for the imperial exams and a jail record would ruin him, the family bribed their way into trading prison for fifty blows. Fifty heavy strokes nearly beat the poor man to death..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇没理会青瓜伸过来的手，跳下马车，以为奶奶已经开始实施计划，面露喜色：“难道奶奶生病了？”
[EN] "Stop! Say no more!" Thoroughly spooked, Old Madam Xia waved him off. "Jianglin, go to Zhu Manor at once! Tell Prefect Zhu the Chen family will not cancel the betrothal—yet after Miss Zhu's tantrum today, the wedding must be postponed. As for when it takes place, that depends on when Miss Zhu mends her temper!"
[ZH] “不是，老太太身体好着呢……”
[EN] Through nimble teamwork, husband and wife had saved the betrothal—only to land right back on Old Madam Xia's stalling tactic. The sole difference was that what had been the grandmother's private scheme was now forced out into the open. How long would the stall drag on? If Miss Zhu never mended her temper, would Haoyu stay single forever? Lacking a better move for now, they could only take it one step at a time.
[ZH] 陈浩宇向青瓜一脚踢去：“能不能说话干脆点儿，到底发生什么事？”
[EN] Out on a bender, Chen Haoyu had planned to crash at one of his outside residences for a few days—until gossip that Qiao Wenxian was marrying today reminded him his own wedding loomed right around the corner, killing his mood to stay out. He had quizzed his grandmother several times about how she meant to stall; Old Madam Xia had whispered she would fake an illness so the whole Chen household dropped wedding preparations, giving them an airtight excuse to push back the date. Not a permanent fix, yet with nothing better on hand, he hurried home to coordinate the day she would fall "sick."
[ZH] 青瓜向一旁闪开：“公子别急，我这就给您说，是朱家姑娘来府上闹事。”
[EN] Watching the street from Chen Manor's gate, Qinggua hurried up the instant his master's carriage pulled in and offered a hand. "Young Master, you are back! Something huge happened at home!"
[ZH] “呵！这么好的事，没叫上本公子，真不够意思！”陈浩宇边说边向府内走。
[EN] Ignoring the hand, Chen Haoyu leaped down, his face lighting up at the thought his grandmother had launched the plan already. "Did Grandmother fall ill?"
[ZH] 青瓜跟上，描述朱家姑娘闹事的场景：“公子，您都不知道，朱家姑娘身边的丫鬟真是厉害，三拳两脚打伤几名下人，朱家姑娘更是厉害，那么厚的石头她一拳砸断，还有椅子也被她一拳砸烂，吓得老太太直喊退婚。”
[EN] "No, Old Madam is healthy as an ox—"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住，喜出望外：“真的同意退婚？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu aimed a kick at Qinggua. "Spit it out straight! What happened?"
[ZH] “那还能有假？不同意朱家姑娘也不会离开。”
[EN] Dodging sideways, Qinggua hurried on. "Do not get mad, Young Master, I am telling you! Miss Zhu came and raised hell at the gate!"
[ZH] “我爹娘可同意？”
[EN] "Hah! Fun like that and she did not invite me? How rude!" Chen Haoyu chuckled, striding inside.
[ZH] “老爷和夫人似乎不愿意，可他们听老太太的话，最终也会同意。”
[EN] Trotting alongside, Qinggua painted the scene. "You should have seen it, Young Master! Miss Zhu's maid laid out several gatekeepers in three blows! And Miss Zhu was scarier still—split a thick stone block with one punch and smashed a chair to splinters! Scared Old Madam into yelling for a broken engagement on the spot!"
[ZH] “没想到朱淑真背着我做了件好事，不如今日请她吃饭，好好感谢。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu halted in his tracks, overjoyed. "She really agreed to cancel the wedding?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇直奔夏老太太的院子。
[EN] "Would I lie? Miss Zhu would never have left otherwise!"
[ZH] 青瓜跟在公子身边，跟个话痨似的：“以前老听人说朱家姑娘是女词人，写的词软了吧唧，想着本人应该柔弱，那长相看着也柔弱，没承想劲儿比我还大。”
[EN] "Did my parents agree too?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇在青瓜面前抖起文化来：“瞧你那没文化的样子，还‘软了吧唧’，那叫婉约，懂不懂？”
[EN] "Master and Madam looked reluctant, yet they always listen to Old Madam, so they will come around."
[ZH] 青瓜笑道：“嘿嘿，公子就是比我懂得多。我现在终于明白公子为何不愿娶朱家姑娘，还说她是母夜叉。只怕公子一旦娶了她，以后既没有机会去青楼、赌坊，更没有机会欺负黄花大姑娘……”
[EN] "Well I will be damned—Zhu Shuzhen went behind my back and pulled off a miracle! Maybe I should treat her to dinner tonight as thanks!"
[ZH] “滚！能不能说点儿好听的？”
[EN] Beaming, Chen Haoyu made a beeline for Old Madam Xia's courtyard.
[ZH] “当然能，青瓜祝公子退婚成功，娶个又温柔又听话的少夫人回来。”
[EN] Qinggua chattered nonstop at his heels. "Folks always rave about Miss Zhu being a poetess writing all those mushy lyrics, and she looks dainty enough, so I figured she was soft as silk—never guessed she packs more punch than I do!"
[ZH] 青瓜说话顺耳许多，陈浩宇不由美滋滋的。
[EN] Chen Haoyu flaunted his learning. "Listen to your uncultured mouth—'mushy'? That is called the delicate and restrained style, got it?"
[ZH] 夏老太太居住的院子挺大，种着花花草草，春天到来，真是春色满园。最吸引人的则是一树的杏花，时不时有花瓣落下。
[EN] "Heh, Young Master always knows more than me," Qinggua grinned. "Now I finally see why you refused to marry Miss Zhu and called her a she-demon. Marry her, and you could kiss brothels, gambling dens, and chasing pretty young maidens goodbye..."
[ZH] 天气回暖的时间不长，屋子里还有些凉，不如外面暖和。夏老太太从儿子房中回来，不想在屋里待着，来到院中，半躺在藤椅上。丫鬟为她盖上毯子，她则闭上眼睛尽情享受春日的阳光。
[EN] "Scram! Can you say something pleasant for once?"
[ZH] 边享受边思忖孙子的婚事该拖到什么时候？今日也看明白，朱家姑娘根本不想嫁到陈家，她肯定不会改性子，难道就永远这么拖下去？
[EN] "Sure! Qinggua wishes Young Master a smooth broken engagement and a gentle, obedient young madam in her place!"
[ZH] 老太太还没想明白，听到孙子的声音：“奶奶！”
[EN] Much nicer to the ears. Chen Haoyu strutted on, grinning ear to ear.
[ZH] 夏老太太睁开眼，瞧见孙子出现在眼前，不由自主满脸堆笑：“怎么才回来？快到奶奶跟前坐着。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia's spacious courtyard bloomed with spring flowers, centered around a tall apricot tree shedding pale petals on the breeze. With the indoor rooms still holding winter's chill, she had returned from her son's quarters and settled onto a rattan chair outside under a blanket, eyes closed to soak up the sun while wondering how long she could drag out her grandson's match. Plainly Miss Zhu wanted no part of the Chens and would never soften her ways—could they really stall forever?
[ZH] 一旁的丫鬟搬张凳子放在老人身边，陈浩宇坐上去，在奶奶脸上亲一口。
[EN] "Grandmother!"
[ZH] 老人家被亲得心花怒放：“还是我孙子最好，知道哄奶奶开心。”
[EN] Her eyes fluttered open, and a radiant smile broke over her face at the sight of her grandson. "Why are you just getting back? Come sit right here by Grandmother."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续哄道：“奶奶是我在这个世上最爱的人。”
[EN] A maid set a stool beside her chair; Chen Haoyu perched on it and planted a kiss on her cheek.
[ZH] “你爹娘呢？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia beamed with delight. "My grandson is the sweetest, always knowing how to cheer Grandmother up."
[ZH] “我爹娘要是不批评我，我就爱他们。”
[EN] "Grandmother is the person I love most in this whole world," Chen Haoyu cooed.
[ZH] “我要是批评你呢？”
[EN] "What about your parents?"
[ZH] “奶奶怎么会批评我？”
[EN] "I love them whenever they are not lecturing me."
[ZH] “臭小子，奶奶舍不得批评你。”
[EN] "And if I lecture you?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟奶奶一番真情流露后转入正题：“奶奶，我听青瓜说我那未过门儿的媳妇闹到家里来，您同意退婚，是也不是？”
[EN] "When would Grandmother ever lecture me?"
[ZH] “当然，我怎么会让你娶个厉害的媳妇回来找罪受？”
[EN] "Little rascal—Grandmother could never bear to."
[ZH] “还是奶奶最好。”
[EN] After sufficient buttering up, Chen Haoyu pivoted to business. "Grandmother, Qinggua told me my unbridled bride-to-be stormed the house and you agreed to cancel the match. Is that true?"
[ZH] “不过跟你爹娘商量后，这婚还是不能退……”
[EN] "Of course! How could I let you marry a terror like that and suffer?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了：“为何？”
[EN] "Grandmother really is the best!"
[ZH] 夏老太太微笑的面庞上略微露出批评的神色：“要问你自己，都是你惹下的事。”
[EN] "Still, after talking it over with your parents, we cannot cancel the match after all..."
[ZH] 应该是因散播谣言一事，陈浩宇后悔已来不及：“奶奶，您今日也见识朱淑真的厉害，也说不愿意我娶她，赶紧想个办法退了这门亲事。”
[EN] Alarm shot through Chen Haoyu. "Why not?!"
[ZH] “你以为奶奶不想？现在唯一的办法还是奶奶之前想的，耗着！”
[EN] A shadow of reproach touched Old Madam Xia's smile. "Ask yourself—this mess is all your own doing."
[ZH] “怎么还是耗着？有没有更好的办法？”
[EN] The rumors! Too late for regrets now. "Grandmother, you saw firsthand how terrifying Zhu Shuzhen is, and you said yourself you do not want me marrying her! Think of a way to break it off!"
[ZH] 夏老太太向孙子耐心解释：“现在耗着跟之前不一样，之前只有你与奶奶这么想，还没法向朱家说明。今日朱家姑娘这么一闹，我们就有理由向知府大人讲明，理直气壮提要求。”
[EN] "You think I do not want to? Our only move is still my original plan: stall!"
[ZH] “什么要求？”
[EN] "Stall again? Isn't there anything better?"
[ZH] “要求朱家姑娘什么时候改了她的性子什么时候娶她过门。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia explained with patience. "Stalling now is different from before. Earlier it was only between the two of us, with no excuse to give the Zhus. After Miss Zhu's spectacle today, we have every right to go straight to Prefect Zhu and lay down terms."
[ZH] “朱淑真不愿嫁给我，她肯定不会改性子，我与她的婚事就这么遥遥无期地耗着，之前没觉得这个主意好，现在仔细一想，真不错！”
[EN] "What terms?"
[ZH] 夏老太太又变得忧虑：“只是苦了你，以后想娶个可心的人过门儿都难。”
[EN] "Tell him we will welcome Miss Zhu through our doors the day she mends her temper."
[ZH] “我是男子，奶奶不用为我发愁，大不了我先纳妾，照样给您生小重孙，等哪天朱家实在耗不下去，主动要求退婚，我再将妾室扶正，不就万事大吉。”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen hates my guts—she will never mend her temper! That pushes the wedding off till kingdom come! I was lukewarm on this plan before, yet thinking about it now, it is genius!"
[ZH] 夏老太太立刻面露笑容：“还是我孙子聪明，三言两语让奶奶豁然开朗。”
[EN] A crease returned to Old Madam Xia's brow. "Only it leaves you in a bind—how will you bring home a wife you actually like?"
[ZH] 祖孙两个越说越开心，笑声充满整个院落……
[EN] "I am a man, Grandmother—no need to fret over me! Worst case, I take concubines first and still give you plenty of great-grandsons. The day the Zhus run out of patience and demand a breakup themselves, I promote a concubine to primary wife, and everybody wins!"
[ZH] 青瓜在一旁听明白，婚还是没法退，得耗着。可他不明白的是朱家姑娘都闹成那样，竟然还不能退？听老太太的意思是怪公子，公子到底做过什么事情搞得无法退婚？
[EN] Old Madam Xia lit up at once. "Trust my clever grandson to clear the clouds in two sentences!"
[ZH] 以前他总是怀疑公子轻薄过朱家姑娘，两家才不得不订婚。今日算是看出，公子连靠近朱家姑娘的机会都没有。
[EN] Grandmother and grandson laughed louder and brighter, filling the whole courtyard with glee.
[ZH] 究竟因为什么，青瓜想不通，只能陪一老一少笑着。
[EN] Standing nearby, Qinggua followed the drift: no cancellation, just endless stalling. What baffled him was why even a rampage like Miss Zhu's could not break the match. Old Madam blamed Young Master—what on earth had he done to trap himself so tight? Qinggua had once wondered if his master had taken liberties with Miss Zhu, yet today proved Young Master could not even get within arm's reach of her. Unable to crack the puzzle, he simply smiled along with the pair.

#CH 171
[ZH] 离开陈府，千里寻本想直奔朱府，当面告诉父亲陈家已同意退婚，又怕与父亲吵起来。
[ZH] 最终还是决定回望月轩亲笔写封信，将陈老太太说过的话一字不落表达清楚，再让苦荞将信送到朱府。既可避免与父亲正面冲突，又可仔细斟酌措辞，让父亲看后不得不面对陈家退婚的事实。
[ZH] 回到望月轩后，千里寻提起笔。给父亲写封信比写话本难度更大，主要是她太想两家退婚，因为太在意，总担心笔下的每个字每句话写得不到位，影响好不容易在陈家争取而来的口头承诺。
[ZH] 不知揉了多少团纸后，终于将信写好，折后装进信封，递给苦荞。
[ZH] “去趟朱府，亲自将信交到我爹手中。”
[ZH] 苦荞从小姐郑重的表情中意识到信的重要性，谨慎接过，塞进怀里：“小姐放心，我这就去。”
[ZH] 苦荞怀揣小姐的亲笔信，乘坐马车来到朱府侧门外。
[ZH] 刚走下马车，发现陈江林与姚轻琰也从马车上下来，看那样子，是来拜访老爷和夫人。
[ZH] 苦荞知道小姐去陈家闹过，也知陈家同意退婚，更知信中写些什么，她猜想二位前来肯定是为退婚之事。
[ZH] 苦荞为小姐感到高兴，忍不住上前拜见：“陈老爷，夫人，我家小姐本来想亲自过来，怕跟老爷吵起来，所以让我送来书信，没承想碰上二位，有二位前来，小姐的心愿定能达成。”
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰先愣一下，很快回过味儿来，原来这丫头以为他们是为退婚而来。
[ZH] 姚轻琰问：“你是朱家姑娘身边的丫鬟？叫什么名字？”
[EN] Leaving Chen Manor, Qianli Xun first thought of marching straight to Zhu Manor to tell her father the Chens had agreed to call off the engagement, then stopped herself lest they blow up into another shouting match. Better to return to Wangyue Pavilion, put every word Old Madam Xia had spoken down on paper, and send Kuqiao to deliver the letter. That spared a head-on clash while letting her weigh every phrase so her father could not brush off the Chens' refusal.
[ZH] “我是小姐的贴身丫鬟，名叫苦荞。”
[EN] Back at her desk, she found writing to her father harder than drafting a huaben. Caring too much made her agonize over every word, afraid a clumsy line might ruin the hard-won concession she had wrung from Chen Manor. After crumpling a small mountain of drafts, she finished at last, folded the sheet into an envelope, and handed it to Kuqiao.
[ZH] 姚轻琰继续问：“你要送的书信可是为两家退婚之事？”
[EN] "Go to Zhu Manor and place this straight into my father's hands."
[ZH] “是，小姐让我亲自送来。”
[EN] Reading the gravity on her mistress's face, Kuqiao tucked the letter with care inside her collar. "Leave it to me, Miss. I am on my way."
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰四目相对，总算来得及时，朱家姑娘还未将老太太同意退婚的话传给知府大人。
[EN] Boarding the carriage, Kuqiao rode to Zhu Manor's side gate. Just as she stepped down, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan alighted from their own carriage right beside her, plainly come to call on Master and Madam.
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“书信不必送了，我们来就是劝说知府大人退婚。”
[EN] Knowing her mistress had stormed Chen Manor and won their consent to cancel the match—and knowing what the letter contained—Kuqiao figured the Chen elders had come for the exact same purpose. Thrilled for her mistress, she hurried forward and curtsied. "Master Chen, Madam Chen! My mistress wanted to come in person yet feared quarreling with Master, so she sent me with a letter. What luck running into the two of you! With you here in person, my mistress's wish is sure to come true!"
[ZH] “不行！小姐交代的任务，我必须完成。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan blinked, then caught on at once: this maid thought they were here to call off the wedding.
[ZH] 姚轻琰见苦荞十分坚持，和颜悦色道：“不如把书信给我，一会儿我会面见知府大人，帮朱家姑娘送上。”
[EN] "Are you Miss Zhu's maid?" Yao Qingyan asked. "What is your name?"
[ZH] 苦荞犹豫后，果断拒绝：“小姐让我亲自交给老爷，我得听她的。”
[EN] "I am Miss's personal maid, Kuqiao."
[ZH] 陈江林想到一个好办法：“苦荞，朱家姑娘在信中写些什么，我们都知道，无非是说陈家同意退婚之事。可是想让知府大人同意，仅凭一封信根本无济于事。再说你一个丫头前去，把信一给，知府大人一看，不得气坏？朱家姑娘不在跟前，肯定拿你撒气，朱家家法甚言，打你几板子都是轻的。我们若帮你送进去，不仅事能办成，还帮你免去一顿板子，仔细想想，是不是这个理？”
[EN] "Is that letter about breaking off the betrothal between our families?"
[ZH] 陈家老爷一番说辞甚有道理，苦荞一路上也担心老爷看完信后一生气，动用家法惩罚她，既然有这么好的机会既能帮到小姐，又能避免自己被惩罚，何乐而不为？
[EN] "Yes, Miss told me to deliver it in person."
[ZH] “既然二位是来商谈退婚一事，与我家小姐的目的一致，将信给你们也无妨。”苦荞从怀里掏出信，递给姚轻琰，“有劳陈家老爷和夫人。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan exchanged a glance. Thank goodness they had arrived in time, before Old Madam Xia's outburst reached Prefect Zhu's ears!
[ZH] 姚轻琰接过，笑眯眯地应道：“放心，回去告诉朱家姑娘，我们二人定帮她办得妥妥当当。”
[EN] "No need to deliver it," Yao Qingyan said smoothly. "We came here to talk Prefect Zhu into canceling the match ourselves."
[ZH] 苦荞再次谢过二位后，上了望月轩的马车，心想：“陈公子的爹娘真好，要是陈公子人好些，小姐嫁到陈家也不错，可惜这么好的公婆与小姐无缘。”
[EN] "No! My mistress gave me an order, and I have to carry it out."
[ZH] 早已有下人打开朱府侧门，迎接陈家老爷和夫人。
[EN] Seeing the maid's stubbornness, Yao Qingyan put on a gentle smile. "Give the letter to me instead. I am about to see Prefect Zhu and can hand it to him on Miss Zhu's behalf."
[ZH] 二人走入后，陈江林从夫人的手上接过信，藏入怀中，悄悄说：“你我真是及时，正好拦下这封信，还是藏起来得好，决不能让知府大人知道。”
[EN] Kuqiao hesitated, then shook her head. "Miss told me to hand it to Master myself. I have to obey her."
[ZH] 姚轻琰点点头：“回到陈府后，赶紧把它烧了。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin thought fast. "Kuqiao, we both know what Miss Zhu wrote—only that the Chens agreed to break off the match. Yet a single sheet of paper will never sway Prefect Zhu. Worse, if a maid walks in and hands him that letter, imagine how livid he will be! With Miss Zhu not around, he is bound to vent his wrath on you. The Zhu household's discipline is strict; a round of the rod would be getting off light. Let us carry it inside, and not only does the job get done, you spare yourself a beating. Think about it—am I right?"
[ZH] 在朱府下人引领下，二人朝知府大人的院子走去。
[EN] His words hit straight on Kuqiao's secret dread; all the way over, she had fretted over Master flying into a rage and punishing her under house rules. Here was a chance to get Miss's task done and save her own skin—why not take it?
[ZH] 有下人已报告朱晞颜，他猜亲家前来定是商议两个孩子的婚事，让丫鬟将向夫人唤来，两人站在主屋门口笑脸相迎。
[EN] "Since you both came to discuss canceling the betrothal, matching my mistress's aim, letting you deliver it works fine." Pulling the envelope from her collar, she handed it to Yao Qingyan. "Much obliged, Master and Madam Chen."
[ZH] 当陈江林与姚轻琰来到朱府正院，见朱晞颜与向夫人满面春风，两人不由相视一番，面露愁容，估计一会儿知府大人不会笑得这么开心。
[EN] Yao Qingyan accepted it with a beaming smile. "Rest easy. Go tell Miss Zhu we will handle everything without a hitch."
[ZH] 两人很快掩饰住情绪，陈江林快走几步，露出笑容，行拱手礼：“有劳亲家相迎，我们二人没有事先告知，突然造访，还望亲家不要见怪。”
[EN] Thanking them once more, Kuqiao climbed back into Wangyue Pavilion's carriage, sighing to herself: Young Master Chen's parents are such kind folks! If only their son were half as decent, marrying into Chen Manor would not be bad at all. Shame Miss has no fate with in-laws this fine.
[ZH] 朱晞颜自然不会见怪：“亲家说的哪里话，朱府的大门随时为二位敞开，想什么时候来都行，不必事先告知。”
[EN] Servants had already swung Zhu Manor's side gate wide to receive the guests. Stepping inside, Chen Jianglin took the letter from his wife, tucked it into his robe, and whispered, "What timing! Intercepting this letter saved us. Hide it well—Prefect Zhu must never lay eyes on it."
[ZH] 向夫人则拉起姚轻琰的手：“妹妹，里边请。”
[EN] "We will burn it the second we get home," Yao Qingyan nodded.
[ZH] 姚轻琰客气地回道：“姐姐请。”
[EN] Guided by a servant, the pair walked toward Prefect Zhu's courtyard. Alerted ahead of time, Zhu Xiyan assumed his in-laws had come to finalize wedding details; summoning Madam Xiang, he waited at the main hall doorway with a broad smile.
[ZH] 大家进屋落座，丫鬟上了茶，一番寒暄过后，朱晞颜主动问：“亲家前来，可是为两个孩子的婚事？”
[EN] Seeing Zhu Xiyan and Madam Xiang beaming with spring cheer, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan traded a rueful glance—Prefect Zhu would not be smiling like that much longer. Masking their unease at once, Chen Jianglin hurried forward with cupped hands and a warm grin. "Forgive us for dropping in unannounced and troubling our in-laws to come welcome us."
[ZH] 陈江林装作坦然的样子：“是为两个孩子的婚事。”
[EN] "Nonsense! Zhu Manor's gates stand open to you both at any hour—no need for advance word," Zhu Xiyan laughed.
[ZH] 朱晞颜更是高兴：“婚期将近，淑真的嫁妆都已备齐，只等陈家来迎娶。至于婚礼怎么操办，全由陈家定，不用征求我们意见。”
[EN] Madam Xiang took Yao Qingyan's hand. "Come right inside, Sister."
[ZH] 陈江林依然装作坦然的样子：“婚礼前前后后已安排妥当，绝不比乔家的婚礼差，只等花轿上门将淑真接到陈家，以后也有人帮我们管那臭小子。”
[EN] "After you, Sister," Yao Qingyan replied with grace.
[ZH] 听到不比乔家的婚礼差，朱晞颜与向夫人都“呵呵”笑起来。
[EN] Once everyone was seated and maids served tea, Zhu Xiyan got straight to the point after pleasantries. "Did our in-laws come regarding the children's wedding?"
[ZH] 今日乔家举办婚礼，据说是近几年来临安城最盛大的婚礼，邀请了所有临安城有头有脸的人。当然也邀请了朱家，朱晞颜不愿亲自去，即使仪王去，他也不愿，让儿子领着儿媳代表朱家送上贺礼。
[EN] Chen Jianglin put on an easy air. "Indeed, regarding their wedding."
[ZH] 陈家不愧是与朱家亲上加亲，陈江林与夫人也没去，朱晞颜更加满意，也更觉得陈家是少有的好人家。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan beamed wider. "With the date close at hand, Shuzhen's dowry is packed and ready, awaiting only your sedan. Arrange the ceremony however the Chen family sees fit—no need to consult us."
[ZH] 见亲家笑得如此开心，陈江林不愿再隐瞒下去，今日来此，是为拖延婚期。他端起茶盏，啜一口茶，慢慢放下，待亲家停止笑声后，大胆说：“今日陈府发生一件大事……”
[EN] "Every detail of the wedding is already arranged," Chen Jianglin said smoothly. "It will not fall a hair behind the Qiao family's feast. We are just waiting to carry Shuzhen home so someone can finally rein in that rascal of ours."
[ZH] 朱晞颜笑得口干，也端起茶盏品了一口，饶有兴致地问：“哦？什么大事？说来听听。”
[EN] Hearing the wedding would rival the Qiaos' set Zhu Xiyan and Madam Xiang chuckling with satisfaction. Today's Qiao wedding was rumored to be Lin'an's grandest in years, drawing every notable family in the city. Zhu Manor had received an invitation too, yet Zhu Xiyan had refused to go—even with Prince Yi attending—sending his son and daughter-in-law with gifts instead. Showing solidarity as double in-laws, Chen Jianglin and his wife had skipped the Qiao feast too, raising the Chens higher than ever in Zhu Xiyan's esteem.
[ZH] 陈江林开始卖起关子：“亲家先答应我不生气我便说。”
[EN] Watching his host laugh in high spirits, Chen Jianglin hated bursting the bubble, yet postponing the wedding was why he had come. Lifting his bowl, he took a measured sip of tea, set it down, and waited for the laughter to ebb before diving in. "A major incident took place at Chen Manor today..."
[ZH] 朱晞颜敏锐地感觉到不对劲，面色变得平静：“我为何要生气？尽管说来听听。”
[EN] Throat dry from laughing, Zhu Xiyan took a sip of his own tea and perked up. "Oh? What sort of incident? Let us hear it."
[ZH] 陈江林看向夫人，见姚轻琰点点头，便不再卖关子：“今日朱家姑娘带一名丫鬟来陈府闹，打伤几名下人，还把石头和椅子砸烂，看她那样子，不退婚誓不罢休。”
[EN] "First promise not to lose your temper," Chen Jianglin hedged.
[ZH] 朱晞颜听后，心中生起怒火，当着亲家的面不好发作，强忍着：“亲家不必在意，我叮嘱过你，这俩孩子肯定会去陈府闹，果不其然，今日又闹开。”
[EN] Sensing trouble at once, Zhu Xiyan sobered. "Why would I lose my temper? Speak plainly."
[ZH] 陈江林和盘托出：“我与轻琰是不在意，还好言相劝，怎奈拗不过我家老太太，亲家也知道，老太太疼浩宇，不愿见他受半点儿苦，担心朱家姑娘那么厉害，浩宇娶了她肯定没好日子过，还说朱家姑娘什么时候改了性子什么时候娶她过门。亲家放心，婚事还在，只是得往后拖一拖。”
[EN] Glancing at his wife and catching her nod, Chen Jianglin laid it bare. "Miss Zhu marched up to Chen Manor today with a maid, beat several of our servants, and smashed a stone block and a chair to pieces. By the look of her, she will not rest until the betrothal is broken."
[ZH] 向夫人一直不敢多言，她羡慕陈江林亲切地称呼自己的夫人名讳，老爷从来没有如此称呼过她。
[EN] Fury surged in Zhu Xiyan's chest, though he choked it down before his guests. "Pay it no heed, my dear in-law. I warned you those two brats would bring their act to your door—and sure enough, she did."
[ZH] 听了亲家所说，她又没有心情再羡慕亲家母。女儿竟然跑到陈家闹，搞得陈家想要拖延婚期，令她担忧不已，怕老爷会撵到望月轩再鞭打女儿。
[EN] Chen Jianglin gave the full picture. "Qingyan and I paid it no mind and tried talking her down, yet we could not override our Old Madam. As you know, Mother dotes on Haoyu and hates seeing him suffer a scratch. Seeing how fierce Miss Zhu is, she fears Haoyu will know no peace married to her, and declared we will only welcome Miss Zhu home once she mends her temper. Rest assured, the betrothal stands—only the wedding must be pushed back a little."
[ZH] 朱晞颜此刻才明白，亲家前来确实为婚事，只是与他想的不一样。对方拖延婚期对朱家来讲无疑也是一种伤害，可自家女儿做出此等有失身份之事，对方不取消婚约已然不错。
[EN] Keeping quiet on the side, Madam Xiang felt a pang of envy hearing Chen Jianglin address his wife by her given name; her own husband had never once called her so warmly. Yet the next moment, envy vanished into sheer dread: her daughter had stormed Chen Manor and made them delay the wedding! What if Master stormed over to Wangyue Pavilion and whipped Shuzhen again?
[ZH] “只怪我教女无方，养下这等粗鲁的女儿，我定收拾得她改掉那性子。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan grasped the truth now: his in-laws had come about the wedding, only not in the way he had hoped. A postponed wedding stung Zhu pride, yet after his daughter's outrageous spectacle, it was already generous of the Chens not to cancel outright. "Blame my poor parenting for raising such an unruly daughter. I will discipline her until she mends that temper!"
[ZH] 老爷要收拾淑真，向夫人开始抹眼泪，姚轻琰在一旁看得甚是愧疚。
[EN] Hearing him vow to discipline Shuzhen, Madam Xiang began dabbing at her eyes, filling Yao Qingyan with guilt.
[ZH] 陈江林也担心自己一番说辞搞得朱家姑娘没好日子过，赶紧解释：“其实我很喜欢朱家姑娘的性子，不改也罢，她若嫁到陈家，定能将浩宇收拾得服服帖帖。只是……亲家您也知道，我不愿拗着老太太，她毕竟年纪大，身体也不好，要是因为这事气出个好歹来，我也不好过……”
[EN] Afraid his report might get Miss Zhu beaten, Chen Jianglin jumped in at once. "Truth be told, I like Miss Zhu's fiery spirit—no need to change a bit! Once she marries in, she will whip Haoyu right into line. Only... you understand my spot, in-law. I cannot go against Old Madam; she is frail and getting on in years, and if stress harms her health, I could never live with myself..."
[ZH] 听完这话，朱晞颜气焰消去一半儿：“亲家说，该如何是好？”
[EN] That took half the fire out of Zhu Xiyan's rage. "What do you propose, then?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰忍不住说道：“我与江林商量，淑真的性子挺好，无需改。但要给我们些时日，好慢慢说服老太太，最多将婚期推迟一个月，一个月后婚礼照常。”
[EN] Yao Qingyan spoke up without waiting. "Jianglin and I talked it over. Shuzhen's spirit is fine as it is—no need to change her. Just give us a little time to talk Old Madam around. We will push the wedding back a month at most; in one month, the ceremony goes on as planned."
[ZH] 向夫人不再抹眼泪，也大胆说：“这倒是个好办法。”
[EN] Drying her eyes, Madam Xiang dared chime in. "That sounds like a sensible plan."
[ZH] 朱晞颜瞪了向夫人一眼：“哪有你多嘴的份儿。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan shot her a sharp glare. "Who asked you to butt in?"
[ZH] 向夫人不敢再言语，更是羡慕姚轻琰，说得比她多，也没见陈江林批评半句，足以显示在家中的地位有多高。
[EN] Madam Xiang shrank back into silence, envying Yao Qingyan more than ever: she spoke twice as much without a single sharp word from Chen Jianglin, proving how respected she stood in her own home.
[ZH] 朱晞颜也赞同姚轻琰所说：“两位不嫌弃淑真已是万幸，一个月就一个月，只是我们两家在临安是有头有脸的人家，两个孩子的婚期估计很多人都打听着，突然推迟，也需有个合理的由头，不至于被人笑话。”
[EN] Turning back, Zhu Xiyan nodded to Yao Qingyan's proposal. "That you two have not soured on Shuzhen is blessing enough. One month it is. Still, both our houses have faces to keep in Lin'an, and plenty of folks have been asking about the wedding date. Delaying out of the blue requires a decent pretext so neither family becomes a laughingstock."
[ZH] 朱晞颜重视清誉，陈江林早有所耳闻，临行前已将由头想好：“若有人问起，就说陈家过分看重这门婚事，计划大操大办，目前准备不充分，等待准备充分后，再举办婚礼。”
[EN] Knowing well how tightly Zhu Xiyan guarded his reputation, Chen Jianglin had come prepared. "If anyone asks, say the Chen family prizes this marriage so highly that we mean to throw a grander feast than ever, our preparations fell short of the mark, and we pushed the date back to do it full justice."
[ZH] 这个理由不错，两家都不失颜面，朱晞颜自然满意：“有劳亲家考虑周全，若需要我出面说服老太太，尽管开口。”
[EN] Face preserved for both houses, Zhu Xiyan smiled in approval. "Thank you for thinking so thoroughly. If you ever need me to help persuade Old Madam, just say the word."
[ZH] “亲家忙得都是大事，这等家常小事我与轻琰应付得过来，如若实在应付不过去，再请亲家出面。”陈江林一番看似拒绝的话饱含着恭维，说得朱晞颜心中怒气全无。
[EN] "You have prefecture affairs to manage; Qingyan and I can handle a little family matter like this. If we truly hit a wall, we will ask for your help." Wrapping polite refusal in smooth flattery, Chen Jianglin swept away the last trace of Zhu Xiyan's anger.
[ZH] 两家商议完毕，统一意见后，陈江林与夫人没必要再待下去，辞别亲家，乘坐马车摇摇晃晃打道回府。
[EN] With both households aligned, Chen Jianglin and his wife took their leave and climbed back into their swaying carriage for the ride home.
[ZH] 回陈府的路上，姚轻琰还是充满担忧：“婚期只推迟一个月，真担心我们两个没能力说服老太太。还有，若家里其他人问起来，是该说只推迟一个月，还是按照老太太的意思说？”
[EN] Along the way, Yao Qingyan furrowed her brow. "Only one month's delay—I truly worry whether we can talk Mother around in time. And if the rest of the family asks, do we say it is postponed one month, or give Mother's version?"
[ZH] 陈江林感到为难：“若家里其他人问起，只说推迟，不要说那么细致，至于老太太那里，只能尽人事听天命，如果说服不了，把老太太送到乡下的别居住一段时间，等婚事成了，再把他接回来。”
[EN] "Tell the rest of the family only that it is delayed, without spelling out details," Chen Jianglin mused. "As for Mother, we do our best and leave the rest to Heaven. If she still will not budge, we send her out to the country villa for a spell and bring her back after the wedding is sealed."
[ZH] “唉，也只能这么办。”姚轻琰叹口气，继续说，“要是别的姑娘嫁到我们陈家估计没这么多事，朱家姑娘与浩宇一个不愿嫁一个不愿娶，搞得我们跟着为难。”
[EN] "Ah, I suppose that is our only play," Yao Qingyan sighed. "Any other bride marrying into our house would never cause this much fuss. Miss Zhu refuses to marry, Haoyu refuses to wed, and we are the ones caught in the middle."
[ZH] 陈江林笑道：“你难道没听说过好事多磨？依我看她要是嫁到陈家，也是陈家的福气。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin chuckled. "Never heard the saying that good things meet many twists? To my mind, marrying her into the house will prove a blessing for the Chens."
[ZH] 姚轻琰来了兴致：“为何如此说？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan perked up. "How do you figure?"
[ZH] “难道今日你没瞧出来？”
[EN] "Did you not notice today?"
[ZH] “瞧出什么？”
[EN] "Notice what?"
[ZH] “她根本不怕老太太。”
[EN] "She is not one bit afraid of Mother."
[ZH] “的确是，当着老太太面，该怎样就怎样。”姚轻琰忽然之间明白过来，“你呀，孝顺，不敢违拗老太太，我自然也顺着老太太，搞得浩宇没人敢管，若把朱家姑娘娶回来，就有人敢管，这么想来的确是件好事。”
[EN] "True enough—stood right before Old Madam without flinching." Realization dawned on Yao Qingyan. "You are too filial to defy Mother, so I defer to her too, leaving nobody in the house brave enough to rein in Haoyu. Bring Miss Zhu in, and at last someone will! Put like that, it really is a blessing."
[ZH] “还有，她也不怕你。”
[EN] "And she is not afraid of you either."
[ZH] “什么意思？”
[EN] "What is that supposed to mean?"
[ZH] “自己琢磨。”
[EN] "Think it over."
[ZH] 姚轻琰琢磨一会儿，琢磨出意味来，笑着说：“你的意思是我不让你管儿子？要我看，朱家姑娘也不怕你。她谁都不怕，嫁到我们家岂不是会闹翻天？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan mulled a second, caught the dig, and burst out laughing. "Saying I stop you from disciplining our son? If you ask me, Miss Zhu is not afraid of you either! Scared of nobody—will she not turn our whole house upside down?"
[ZH] 陈江林抓住夫人的手：“只要能把浩宇的天给翻过来，就是你我的福气。”
[EN] Chen Jianglin clasped her hand. "Long as she turns Haoyu's world upside down, that is our good fortune."
[ZH] 夫妻二人在马车上畅想着有人能帮他们管教儿子的美好场景，那时，他们便可以享受有个好儿子的幸福。
[EN] Riding home hand in hand, husband and wife pictured the bright day someone finally whipped their son into shape and let them taste the joy of having a decent heir.

#CH 172
[ZH] 苦荞回到望月轩，将遇到陈家老爷和夫人之事详细说明。
[ZH] 千里寻听后，甚是喜悦：“看来去陈家闹这步棋走得实在好，说不定今日退婚之事便成，等成功后，再去找我爹，想办法让他答应以后不要再随便给我定亲。”
[ZH] 要搁以前，苦荞肯定会劝小姐打消念头。而现在的小姐要胆量有胆量，要气魄有气魄，只要她想做的事，再难都会坚持。
[ZH] “我相信小姐肯定能说服老爷。”
[ZH] 又转念一想，小姐打算以后一个人过，实在可怜：“不过，小姐还是要找一个互相喜欢，又能托付终身的人，我才放心。”
[ZH] 千里寻陷入沉思，文渊算是一个，可他今日已成亲，一段好好的缘分就这样没了，以后还有谁会是与她互相喜欢之人？
[ZH] 不知为何，她脑海中竟然出现仪王的身影。千里寻晃晃脑袋，想要将仪王的身影晃走。
[ZH] ﻿刚晃走，又出现。
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐不言语，只顾沉默，便去茶桌旁做茶。
[ZH] 千里寻认为一定是文渊成亲，她内心变得空虚，想找个人填补，才不由自主想到仪王。
[ZH] 自从知道仪王是申简辰后，千里寻对仪王的态度正悄悄发生变化，也许是因为她刚刚穿越而来时，心中只有申简辰的缘故。
[ZH] 想到梦容，觉得自己挂念与好友有婚约之人，实在不该。
[ZH] 她还是没忍住，在心中悄悄自问，若没有梦容，是否愿意接受仪王的感情？她强迫自己说出：“也许吧。”只怕不强迫答案会是肯定的。
[ZH] 穿越前，面对申简辰的表白，千里寻总是一副拒绝的姿态，甚至说出“除非猪上树，老娘才会嫁给你”的话。
[ZH] 穿越而来，她与申简辰想要走到一起，还真得等到猪能上树的那一日。仔细想来，人说话果然需三思而行，否则一不小心曾经说过的话便会成为谶语。
[ZH] 千里寻不由自主琢磨起仪王想要纳她为妾的想法。按说在这个时代，像朱淑真这等出身嫁给一位王爷做妾也不为过。可千里寻难以跨越心中的坎儿。
[ZH] 一方面，她不愿给人做妾。与其他女子共享一夫，每日为一个男人勾心斗角，想着都累，还有失尊严。
[ZH] 另一方面，她不想伤害梦容。梦容对仪王的爱深入骨髓，真是我见犹怜。千里寻甚至觉得她对仪王稍动心思都龌龊至极。
[ZH] 为了不再想下去，她坐回书桌前，提笔准备写话本。却发现心思越乱越难以编故事，话本是无法再写，朱淑真的诗却无意中蹦入脑海。
[ZH] 她微微一笑，心想，诗不愧是情感的集中体现，她心中有情想要宣泄，朱淑真的诗自然而然被召唤出来。
[ZH] 便提笔写下五首《恨春》:
[EN] Back at Wangyue Pavilion, Kuqiao laid out everything she had overheard from Master Chen and his wife down to the last detail.
[ZH] 其一
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned from ear to ear. “Looks like making a scene at the Chen estate was a brilliant move. Who knows, the engagement might fall apart today. Once that’s settled, I’ll go talk to my dad and get him to promise he won’t just marry me off to whoever he feels like next time.”
[ZH] 樱桃初荐杏梅酸，槐嫩风高麦秀寒。
[EN] In the old days, Kuqiao would have begged her mistress to drop such reckless talk. Now, though, her Miss had nerves of steel and spine to match; once she set her mind on something, no obstacle could budge her.
[ZH] 惆怅东君太情薄，挽留时暂也应难。
[EN] “I’m sure you’ll talk Master Zhu round, Miss,” Kuqiao said. Then another thought struck her—spending a whole lifetime alone sounded far too lonely. “Still, I’d feel a lot better once you find someone who likes you back and whom you can rely on for life.”
[ZH] 其二
[EN] Qianli Xun fell quiet, thinking it over. Wenyuan would have fit the bill, yet he was getting married today. A good match, gone just like that. Who else out there could ever feel the same way about her?
[ZH] 一瞬芳菲尔许时，苦无佳句记相思。
[EN] Out of nowhere, Prince Yi’s face drifted into her mind. Qianli Xun shook her head hard to chase the image away.
[ZH] 春光虽好多风雨，恩爱方深奈别离。
[EN] The moment she shook him out, he popped right back in.
[ZH] 泪眼谢他花缴抱，愁怀惟赖洒扶持。
[EN] Seeing her mistress lapse into silence, Kuqiao stepped over to the tea table and began preparing tea.
[ZH] 莺莺燕燕休相笑，试与单栖各自知。
[EN] Qianli Xun told herself it was only because Wenyuan’s wedding had left an empty spot inside her, making her reach for someone else to fill it. Ever since learning that Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen, her feelings toward him had shifted bit by bit—perhaps because, when she first woke up in this body, Shen Jianchen was the only one on her mind.
[ZH] 其三
[EN] Then she thought of Mengrong, and guilt hit her at once. Letting her thoughts wander toward her best friend’s fiancé was inexcusable.
[ZH] 病酒厌厌日正方，一声啼鸟在花梢。
[EN] Even so, she couldn’t stop herself from asking silently: if Mengrong weren’t in the picture, would she accept Prince Yi’s feelings? She forced herself to murmur, “Maybe,” though deep down she feared the honest answer was a resounding yes.
[ZH] 惊回好梦方萌蕊，唤起新愁却破包。
[EN] Before she crossed over, whenever Shen Jianchen confessed his love, she had brushed him off every single time—even throwing out, “Only when pigs climb trees will I ever marry you!”
[ZH] 暗把后期随处记，闲将清恨倩诗嘲。
[EN] Now, in this lifetime, if she and Shen Jianchen ever wanted to be together, they really would have to wait for the day pigs learned to climb trees. Thinking back, people ought to watch what they said—one careless remark could turn into a prophecy.
[ZH] 从今始信恩成怨，且与莺花作交谈。
[EN] Her thoughts drifted again to Prince Yi’s proposal to take her as a concubine. In this era, for a daughter of Zhu Shuzhen’s background, becoming a prince’s concubine wouldn’t be considered beneath her. Yet Qianli Xun couldn’t get past the hurdle in her own heart.
[ZH] 其四
[EN] For one thing, she refused to be anyone’s concubine. Sharing one husband with other women and scheming every day for a man’s favor sounded exhausting—and utterly beneath her dignity.
[ZH] 迟迟花日上帘钩，尽日无人独倚楼。
[EN] For another, she couldn’t bear to hurt Mengrong. Mengrong loved Prince Yi to the marrow of her bones; she looked so frail and devoted it broke one’s heart. Qianli Xun felt dirty just for letting her mind stray toward him at all.
[ZH] 蝶使蜂媒传客恨，莺丝柳线织春愁。
[EN] To stop brooding, she sat back down at her desk and picked up a brush to work on her huaben tale. The more tangled her thoughts grew, however, the harder it became to spin a story. The huaben wouldn’t flow, yet Zhu Shuzhen’s own poems rose unbidden in her head.
[ZH] 碧云信断惟芳梦，红叶成诗想到秋。
[EN] She gave a faint smile. Poetry really was the distillate of emotion; with feelings pressed tight in her chest needing release, Zhu Shuzhen’s verses surfaced all on their own.
[ZH] 几许别离多少泪，不堪重省不堪流。
[EN] Dipping her brush, she wrote out all five poems of Hating Spring:
[ZH] 其五
[EN] Poem I: Cherries are just offered up, apricots and plums still sour; / Tender scholar-trees, brisk wind, wheat blades green in the chill. / Melancholy that the Lord of Spring is so fickle of heart— / Even to detain him a little while must prove hard indeed.
[ZH] 一篆烟消系臂香，闲看书册就牙床。
[EN] Poem II: A fleeting burst of spring bloom passes in so brief a space, / Hard to find fine lines enough to record this longing. / Though spring’s light is fair, it brings too much wind and rain; / Love has only just grown deep—alas for parting! / Tear-filled eyes thank the flowers for wrapping me in their embrace; / My sorrowing heart has only wine to lean on for support. / Orioles and swallows, cease your mocking laughter— / Try roosting alone yourselves, and each will know the ache.
[ZH] 莺声冉冉来深院，柳色阴阴暗画墙。
[EN] Poem III: Heavy-headed from last night’s wine, the sun climbs high and bright; / A single bird cries out from the tip of a flowering branch. / Startled back from sweet dreams just budding into bloom, / Fresh sorrow wakes as the calyx splits wide open. / Secretly I mark our future tryst wherever I may turn; / At leisure I borrow verses to mock this pure regret. / From this day on, I believe that kindness turns to grievance— / For now I’ll trade idle talk only with orioles and flowers.
[ZH] 眼底落红千万点，脸边新泪两三行。
[EN] Poem IV: Slowly the flower-dappled sun climbs to the curtain hooks; / All day long, with no one near, I lean alone on the upper rail. / Butterflies as envoys and bees as go-betweens bring only a traveler’s grief; / Oriole threads and willow strands weave the sorrow of spring. / Word from azure clouds is severed—nothing left but fragrant dreams; / Red leaves turned to verse carry thoughts all the way to autumn. / How many partings, how many tears— / Unbearable to recall again, unbearable to let fall.
[ZH] 梨花细雨黄昏后，不是愁人也断肠。
[EN] Poem V: One coil of incense smoke fades from the arm-scent sachet; / Idly reading a scroll, I lean beside my ivory-inlaid bed. / Oriole notes drift lingeringly into the deep courtyard; / Willow shadows fall dark across the painted wall. / Before my eyes, crimson petals scatter in ten thousand specks; / Down my cheeks, fresh tears run in two or three thin tracks. / Pear blossoms and fine drizzle after dusk— / Even one who knows no sorrow would break his heart at this. [TN: Translator’s rendering]
[ZH] 写诗期间，苦荞将做好的茶端来，见小姐写得认真，并未打扰，把茶盏轻轻放在桌上，站在一旁看小姐写诗。
[EN] While she wrote, Kuqiao brought over the freshly whisked tea. Seeing her mistress deep in concentration, she didn’t disturb her, setting the tea bowl down with a feather-light touch and standing beside the desk to watch.
[ZH] 小姐一句一句，一首一首写，苦荞心中暗自赞叹，重生后的小姐写诗作词并不比以前差，竟然一下子写了五首，实在厉害。
[EN] Line by line, poem by poem, Kuqiao marveled in silence. Since coming back from death’s door, her mistress hadn’t lost a shred of her poetic gift—turning out five full poems in a single sitting was nothing short of astonishing.
[ZH] 千里寻写完，内心郁结之情得到疏散，放下笔，端起茶盏，温度刚刚好，一口气喝完。
[EN] Once Qianli Xun finished, the tightness in her chest eased. She set down the brush, lifted the tea bowl—now at the perfect temperature—and drained it in one go.
[ZH] 她放下茶盏，向四周瞧瞧：“红豆呢？”
[EN] Setting the bowl down, she glanced around. “Where’s Hongdou?”
[ZH] “小姐您忘了？是您让红豆每日训练剩下的九个下人练习拳法，她此时正在后院。”
[EN] “Did you forget, Miss? You ordered Hongdou to drill the other nine servants in fist techniques every day. She’s out in the back courtyard right now.”
[ZH] “瞧我这记性。”
[EN] “Look at my memory.”
[ZH] 门外传来小厮的声音：“姑娘，东方公子身边的化雨又来了。”
[EN] From outside the door came a page boy’s voice. “Young Lady, Young Master Dongfang’s servant Huayu is here again.”
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“化雨倒是来得及时，把这五首全部给他。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. “Huayu has good timing. Give him all five.”
[ZH] 苦荞很不情愿：“小姐辛苦写的，为何要全给他？依我看两首即可。”
[EN] Kuqiao balked. “You worked so hard writing those, Miss. Why hand over all of them? Two would be plenty.”
[ZH] “诗写出来要让人又读又品，才有意义，放在我身边只怕会发霉。”
[EN] “Poems are meant to be read and savored. Keep them locked up next to me and they’ll just grow moldy.”
[ZH] 小姐说得有道理，苦荞将五首全拿了，走出闺房，来到大门口。
[EN] Seeing the sense in that, Kuqiao gathered all five sheets, stepped out of the boudoir, and walked to the main gate.
[ZH] 化雨正乖乖站在门口向里张望，见苦荞出现，满脸堆笑：“幽栖居士和苦荞姐姐都是好人，从来不会让我久等。”
[EN] Huayu was standing there obediently, craning his neck for a peek inside. The moment Kuqiao appeared, his face broke into a grin. “Youqi Jushi and Sister Kuqiao are both good souls—you never keep me waiting long.”
[ZH] 苦荞递上纸张：“你倒及时，每隔几日就来。今日乔家举行婚礼，怎么没跟你家公子一起去？”
[EN] Kuqiao handed over the stack of paper. “You sure keep a strict schedule, coming every few days. The Qiao family is holding a wedding today—why didn’t you go with your young master?”
[ZH] 化雨笑嘻嘻地从苦荞手中接过写有诗的纸张：“公子本要带我去，想起今日是该来望月轩讨要诗词的日子，便没让我跟着去。”
[EN] Beaming, Huayu accepted the pages. “My master meant to take me along, then remembered today was the day to come to Wangyue Pavilion for new poems, so he left me behind.”
[ZH] “你岂不是错过一次享用美食的机会？”
[EN] “Didn’t you miss out on a grand feast?”
[ZH] “没什么，我家公子说，只要我拿回幽栖居士的佳作，带我去聚香楼。”化雨只觉手中的纸张厚了些，仔细数数，竟然有五张纸：“没想到今日收获满满，幽栖居士送给我五首诗，公子肯定高兴，聚香楼是去定了。”
[EN] “Doesn’t matter! My master promised that as long as I bring back a fine piece by Youqi Jushi, he’ll take me to Juxiang Tower.” Noticing the stack felt thicker than usual, Huayu counted the pages—five whole sheets! “What a haul! Youqi Jushi gave me five poems today. My master’s going to be thrilled—Juxiang Tower is a sure thing now!”
[ZH] 化雨离开后，苦荞向白风每次见她的地方瞟了一眼，心想，白风今日应该跟随仪王去乔府贺喜，故而没来找她。
[EN] After Huayu left, Kuqiao cast a glance toward the spot where Bai Feng usually met her, figuring he must have accompanied Prince Yi to Qiao Manor to offer congratulations and wouldn’t be coming today.
[ZH] 正要准备回屋，听到车轮的声音，仔细瞧去，原来是陈家的马车。马车停下后，陈浩宇一个人从马车上下来，身边并未跟任何随从。
[EN] Just as she turned to head back inside, the rumble of carriage wheels reached her ears. Looking closer, she recognized a Chen family carriage. It rolled to a halt, and Chen Haoyu stepped down alone without a single attendant.
[ZH] 苦荞猜到他应该是来向小姐传达退婚的喜讯，便笑脸相迎。
[EN] Guessing he had come to bring the happy news that the engagement was off, Kuqiao stepped forward with a welcoming smile.
[ZH] 陈浩宇走下马车，瞧见苦荞，正要喊，话还未出口，不知从哪里出现一群人，将陈浩宇团团围住……
[EN] Chen Haoyu stepped down, spotted Kuqiao, and opened his mouth to call out—but before a single word left his lips, a swarm of men surged out from nowhere and boxed him in on all sides.
[ZH] 眼前的阵仗将苦荞吓坏，她愣了一会儿，匆忙逃进望月轩，掩住大门，又因好奇，露出门缝向外瞧。
[EN] The sight scared Kuqiao half to death. She froze for a heartbeat, then bolted back inside Wangyue Pavilion, shut the gate, and—unable to resist her curiosity—cracked it open a sliver to peek outside.
[ZH] 只见走来一个黑胖子，大腹便便，眯着眼睛，穿过众人，靠近陈浩宇。
[EN] A stout, dark-skinned man with a bulging belly waddled through the crowd with narrowed eyes, closing in on Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 苦荞自语道：“陈家公子平日不干好事，这下招灾了吧！有你罪受。”
[EN] Kuqiao muttered under her breath, “Young Master Chen never does anything decent—no wonder trouble found him! Serves him right.”
[ZH] 她继续从门缝向外瞧……
[EN] She kept watching through the crack in the gate.
[ZH] 当黑胖子靠近后，陈浩宇嬉皮笑脸相迎：“蔣爷，您怎么跑到这儿来？还带这么多人？”
[EN] As the stout man approached, Chen Haoyu put on a slick, cheerful grin. “Master Jiang! What brings you all the way over here with such a crowd?”
[ZH] 蔣爷“哼”了一声：“旁边就是陈家的宅子，你小子常常在这一带出没，别以为我不知道？”
[EN] Master Jiang let out a snort. “The Chen estate sits right next door, and you little punk hang around this neighborhood all the time. Think I didn’t know?”
[ZH] “蔣爷不愧是临安百事通，什么都知道。”
[EN] “Master Jiang truly lives up to your name as Lin’an’s know-it-all. Nothing gets past you.”
[ZH] “没想到陈大公子也会说奉承话，既然态度这么好，今儿就答应我，不要再去找春金沫，大爷我瞧上她了。”
[EN] “Didn’t know Eldest Young Master Chen had such a smooth tongue. Since your manners are so nice today, give me your word right here and now: stay away from Chun Jinmo. I’ve set my sights on her.”
[ZH] 春金沫正是陈浩宇散播谣言之事被查出后，开荤的第一人，自此春金沫只认陈浩宇，而陈浩宇对春金沫也出手阔绰，处处照顾。
[EN] Chun Jinmo had been the very first woman Chen Haoyu turned to after his rumor-mongering scheme was exposed and he broke his fast. From then on, Chun Jinmo had eyes only for Chen Haoyu, who lavished silver on her and looked after her every whim.
[ZH] “春香楼女子多的是，蔣爷又何必跟我过不去？您要多少银子尽管说，我给就是。”
[EN] “Chunxiang Brothel has plenty of women, Master Jiang. Why make things hard on me? Just name your price in silver, and it’s yours.”
[ZH] 对方并没有相让之意，蔣爷冷笑道：“你们陈家有几个臭钱就了不起，今儿爷爷我就让你知道这天底下有钱解决不了的事儿，爷爷我偏要春金沫不要你的银子，看你能把我怎样？”
[EN] Master Jiang had no intention of backing down. He gave a cold laugh. “Think the Chen family’s stinking pile of cash makes you special? Today I’ll teach you there are things in this world silver can’t buy. I want Chun Jinmo, and I don’t want your money. Let’s see what you can do about it!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇后悔为何今日出门不多带些人，对方来势汹汹，看来不打一架是不行。既然他还不想让出春金沫，不如打一架。
[EN] Chen Haoyu cursed himself for bringing no bodyguards today. These men meant business; there was no talking his way out of a brawl. Since he wasn’t about to hand over Chun Jinmo, he might as well fight.
[ZH] “蔣爷这边人多势众，我肯定把您怎样不了，不过我还不想把金沫随便让给别人。”
[EN] “You’ve got the numbers on your side, Master Jiang, so I’m in no position to stop you. Still, I’m not handing Jinmo over to anyone just like that.”
[ZH] “给我打！打到他同意为止！”蔣爷一声令下，向后退去。
[EN] “Beat him!” Master Jiang barked, stepping back. “Beat him until he agrees!”
[ZH] 蒋爷的手下一起向陈浩宇攻击而来……
[EN] His thugs lunged at Chen Haoyu all at once.
[ZH] 苦荞赶紧关好门：“活该！谁让你喜欢青楼女子！”
[EN] Kuqiao slammed the gate shut. “Serves you right! That’s what you get for chasing brothel women!”
[ZH] 苦荞快跑着回到闺房：“小姐！不好了！外面打起来！”
[EN] She sprinted back to the boudoir. “Miss! Trouble! There’s a fight outside!”
[ZH] 几首诗疏散了情怀，内心安静许多，千里寻正提笔写话本，并未理会苦荞。
[EN] Having poured her feelings into the poems, Qianli Xun felt much calmer and had returned to writing her huaben, paying no attention to Kuqiao.
[ZH] 苦荞强调道：“小姐，外面打起来了！”
[EN] Kuqiao stressed it again. “Miss, they’re fighting right outside!”
[ZH] “打起来就打起来，与我们何干？”千里寻继续写话本。
[EN] “Let them fight. What’s that got to do with us?” Qianli Xun kept writing.
[ZH] “打的可是陈公子。”
[EN] “They’re beating up Young Master Chen!”
[ZH] “打他再好不过，他本就该打。”
[EN] “Good. He had a beating coming anyway.”
[ZH] 千里寻写完一个字，突然停下笔：“陈浩宇来望月轩？”
[EN] Qianli Xun finished a character, then stopped her brush mid-air. “Wait—Chen Haoyu came to Wangyue Pavilion?”
[ZH] 苦荞点点头。
[EN] Kuqiao nodded.
[ZH] 千里寻继续问：“他来会不会是找我说退婚的好消息？”
[EN] “Could he have come to tell me the good news about breaking off the engagement?”
[ZH] 苦荞又点点头：“看样子是，不过还没说，就被一伙人围住，然后没说几句就打起来。”
[EN] Kuqiao nodded again. “Looked like it! Before he could say a word, a gang surrounded him. They exchanged a few words, and then a brawl broke out.”
[ZH] 千里寻撂下笔站起来：“看在他有可能给我带来好消息，不如帮帮他，你去后院，让红豆把人都给我带来，今日正好拿门外的人练练手。”
[EN] Qianli Xun tossed her brush down and stood. “Considering he might have brought me good news, I suppose I’ll lend him a hand. Run to the back courtyard and tell Hongdou to bring everyone here. We’ll use that lot outside for target practice.”
[ZH] 苦荞尽管讨厌陈浩宇，可小姐的嫂嫂人还是不错，看在她的面子上也应帮帮陈公子，她快步跑出闺房，跑到后院，唤了红豆，带上九名下人，来到院子与小姐会合。
[EN] Much as Kuqiao disliked Chen Haoyu, her mistress’s sister-in-law was a decent woman; for her sake alone, they couldn’t leave Young Master Chen to get pummeled. She hurried out to the back courtyard, called Hongdou, and rounded up the nine other servants to meet her mistress in the front yard.
[ZH] 千里寻已在院中听到外面的打斗声，命令小厮将门打开，然后与众人冲出门外……
[EN] Hearing the racket outside, Qianli Xun ordered the page boy to unbar the gate and charged out with her crew right behind her.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一个人与众人对打，的确力不从心，为了面子，努力撑着。他会的三脚猫功夫，根本没用，打了没多会儿，撑不下去，被众人将手脚箍住，动弹不得。
[EN] Facing a mob alone, Chen Haoyu was outmatched. Pride kept him swinging, though his half-baked martial arts did no good whatsoever. Before long he ran out of steam, and the thugs pinned his arms and legs so he couldn’t move an inch.
[ZH] 蔣爷走过去，左右开弓在陈浩宇脸上扇了两巴掌：“爷爷我本来买了春金沫，结果你小子出的银子多，春香楼的妈妈便给了你，今日就让你知道，跟爷爷抢人，没好下场！”
[EN] Master Jiang strolled over and slapped Chen Haoyu hard across both cheeks, left then right. “I paid for Chun Jinmo first, you little brat, and the madam only gave her to you because you flashed more silver! Today you learn what happens when you steal a woman from me!”
[ZH] 陈浩宇并不服软：“此事只怪春香楼妈妈爱财，你有本事去打她，何必找我麻烦？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu refused to grovel. “Blame the brothel madam for chasing money! If you’ve got the guts, go hit her—why pick on me?”
[ZH] “先收拾完你小子，我自然会收拾她。”
[EN] “Once I’m done teaching you a lesson, she’s next.”
[ZH] 蔣爷话音刚落，听到身后的大门被打开，转身看去，从里面冲出一群人来，三女七男。接着走出一位漂亮姑娘。
[EN] No sooner had Master Jiang finished speaking than the gate behind him swung wide. Three women and seven men poured out, followed by a striking young lady.
[ZH] 蔣爷见到漂亮姑娘，顿时眉开眼笑：“哪里来这么俊俏的小娘子？”
[EN] Master Jiang’s eyes lit up at the sight of her. “Well now, where did such a pretty little miss pop out from?”
[ZH] 抬头瞅到“望月轩”三个字：“小娘子难道是望月轩的主人？”
[EN] Glancing up, he read the three characters on the plaque: Wangyue Pavilion. “Could this beauty be the mistress of Wangyue Pavilion?”
[ZH] 千里寻还未说话，陈浩宇像是见到救星：“朱淑真，快救救我，他们都是坏人！”
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could open her mouth, Chen Haoyu cried out as if he’d spotted a savior. “Zhu Shuzhen! Help me! These men are all scoundrels!”
[ZH] 蔣爷听到“朱淑真”三个字，立刻恍然大悟：“哦，你就是那位江南女词人，长得真不赖……”
[EN] At the name Zhu Shuzhen, realization dawned on Master Jiang. “Aha! So you’re that Poetess of Jiangnan! Not bad to look at, not bad at all…”
[ZH] 蔣爷边说边靠近：“听说你跟陈浩宇有婚约，他可不是什么好东西，整日往春香楼跑，不如舍了他，跟爷爷我，如何？嘿嘿……！”
[EN] Sauntering closer, he leered at her. “Word is you’re betrothed to Chen Haoyu. He’s no good—spends all his days sneaking off to Chunxiang Brothel. Why not dump him and come home with me instead? Hehehe!”
[ZH] 千里寻哪会受这等奚落，再说对方色眯眯的样子实在恶心，她不由分说，一拳上去打在蔣爷的脸上：“让你见识见识老娘的厉害，以后跟我说话客气点！”
[EN] Qianli Xun wasn’t about to stomach an insult like that, and his sleazy grin made her skin crawl. Without wasting a syllable, she drove a fist straight into Master Jiang’s face. “Time you learned who you’re messing with! Speak politely to me from now on!”
[ZH] 蔣爷的脸被打得火辣辣地疼，他咧着嘴，一脸怒气：“你们还愣着干什么？给我上！”
[EN] His cheek stinging like fire, Master Jiang bared his teeth in fury. “What are you idiots gawking at? Get her!”

#CH 173
[ZH] 两边立刻打起来，没有人再理会陈浩宇。
[ZH] 他松松胳膊和腿，第一次觉得朱淑真厉害挺不错，故意嘲笑道：“蔣爷，你的人根本不是对手，还是见好就收吧。”
[ZH] 蔣爷忍着脸上的疼痛，躲开朱淑真，生怕对方再一拳打来。
[ZH] 他瞧着自己带来的人没打几下被望月轩的三女七男迅速制服，只怪今日出门没看黄历，遇到硬茬，好汉不吃眼前亏，还是尽快撤退。
[ZH] “女侠，我与你无冤无仇，今日若有得罪还请海涵。”
[ZH] 不清楚对方来历，千里寻不想将事闹大：“你在望月轩门口闹事，又对我无礼，老娘才打你，看在你是初犯，不与你计较，下次再到老娘的地盘欺负人，定不饶恕！”
[ZH] “不敢不敢，没有下次。”蔣爷赔着笑脸，瞧到陈浩宇一脸得意，气不过，不由多说两句，“真想不通，女侠乃女中豪杰，为何要嫁给陈公子？他可是天天往春香楼跑，那里有他相好的。”
[ZH] 千里寻气不打一处来：“你哪只耳朵听说老娘要嫁给他？”
[ZH] 蔣爷趁机问：“女侠既然不嫁给陈公子，为何要帮他？”
[ZH] 千里寻十分不屑：“帮他？笑话！难道你长耳朵没听明白？你在望月轩门口闹事，老娘就得管，你们两个有什么恩怨最好到别处去解决，老娘才懒得插手管你们的破事。”
[ZH] 蒋爷请求道：“还请女侠放了我的人，我这就带他们离开。”
[ZH] 千里寻将手一挥，红豆与其他九人松开手，歹徒们迅速跑到蒋爷身边。
[ZH] 蒋爷拱手道：“我能不能带走陈公子？”
[ZH] 千里寻说：“现在还不行，我有事要问他。”
[ZH] “女侠尽管问，我去陈公子必经之路上等他。”
[ZH] 蒋爷瞟了一眼陈浩宇，带着众人离开。
[EN] The two sides clashed at once, leaving Chen Haoyu forgotten on the ground. Flexing his sore arms and legs, he felt for the very first time that having a fierce fiancée had its perks, and jeered aloud, "Your boys are outmatched, Master Jiang! Better quit while you are ahead!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一直听朱淑真与蒋爷说话，听那意思并不是为帮他，心中十分不爽。蒋爷一离开，不满道：“朱淑真，我姐是你的嫂嫂，你怎么能看着她的亲弟弟不管？况且你我还有婚约，太不够意思。”
[EN] Nursing his throbbing cheek, Master Jiang edged clear of Zhu Shuzhen lest she swing again. Watching his thugs get flattened in moments by Wangyue Pavilion's ten servants, he cursed his luck for stepping out without checking the almanac and bumping into a brick wall. A wise man knew when to cut losses.
[ZH] “你被人打，我高兴都来不及，凭什么帮你？刚才出手救你，不过是想着你应该给我带来退婚的好消息。赶紧告诉老娘，是不是你家人已经去找我爹商议退婚之事？”
[EN] "Heroine, you and I have no grudge," Master Jiang called out. "If I gave offense today, please be magnanimous and forgive me."
[ZH] 千里寻自信应该如此，她都把朱淑真大家闺秀的形象糟践成那样，陈家退婚之心肯定坚不可摧。
[EN] Unsure who the man was and reluctant to blow things up, Qianli Xun held her stance. "You started a riot outside Wangyue Pavilion and spoke filth to my face—that is why I hit you. Since this is your first offense, I will let it slide. Pull another stunt on my turf and I will not be so lenient!"
[ZH] “哼！偏不让你如意，想要退婚遥遥无期，我奶奶说了，除非你改了性子，才娶你过门。”
[EN] "Never again, never again!" Master Jiang put on a placating grin, then caught Chen Haoyu's smug smirk and could not resist a parting jab. "Beats me why a heroine like you would marry Young Master Chen. He runs to Chunxiang Brothel every single day—has a sweetheart waiting there!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇故意装出高兴的样子，谁让朱淑真不愿帮他。本来还想请她去聚香楼吃顿饭，感谢她到陈家闹，现在被气得一点儿兴致都没有，只想看朱淑真失落生气的样子。
[EN] Temper flaring, Qianli Xun snapped, "Which ear told you I am marrying him?!"
[ZH] 还真凑效，朱淑真果然不高兴。
[EN] "If you are not marrying him," Master Jiang countered at once, "then why step in to help him?"
[ZH] “陈浩宇，你是不是有病？拿以前说过的话来搪塞老娘？老娘到陈府闹成那样，难道你没听说？”
[EN] "Help him? What a joke! Are your ears just for show? You picked a fight outside Wangyue Pavilion's gate, so I stepped in. Take whatever beef you two have somewhere else—I could not care less about your sordid messes!"
[ZH] 朱淑真生气，陈浩宇更得意：“我一进家门，青瓜追着赶着把一切都给我说了。我爹娘和奶奶是什么人？你那点儿计量，他们一眼看穿，所以退婚之事遥遥无期。”
[EN] "Then please tell your people to release my men, Heroine, and I will clear out right now."
[ZH] 千里寻充满担忧：“你那么得意干吗？好像你不愿退婚一样！”
[EN] Qianli Xun waved a hand. Hongdou and the other nine released their captives, who scrambled back behind Master Jiang.
[ZH] “我当然不愿娶你！”想到自己的意愿，陈浩宇恢复正常，“你到我家闹，也不是没有用，我奶奶说，以前耗着，只是她想想而已，你今儿一闹，就有理由光明正大告诉你爹，你什么时候改了性子，我再什么时候娶你过门。”
[EN] Cupping his hands, Master Jiang asked, "May I take Young Master Chen with me?"
[ZH] “难道你爹娘去找我爹就是为说这个？”千里寻猜测她写给父亲的信应该是被陈浩宇的爹娘截胡。
[EN] "Not yet," Qianli Xun said. "I have questions for him."
[ZH] 陈浩宇没有必要隐瞒：“听我奶奶说是这样，你现在要做的就是别改你的性子，这样你我的婚期便遥遥无期。”
[EN] "Ask away, Heroine. I will wait up the road on his way home." Shooting Chen Haoyu a dark glance, Master Jiang marched his gang away.
[ZH] 千里寻倍感失落，该做的都已做，毫无进展：“你们家人脑子是不是有问题？老娘都演成那样儿，他们怎么还不愿退婚？”
[EN] Listening to her deny she had helped him had soured Chen Haoyu's mood. The instant the thugs cleared out, he complained, "My sister is your sister-in-law, Zhu Shuzhen! How could you leave her own brother hanging—especially when we are betrothed? Cold-blooded!"
[ZH] 千里寻想起夏老太太提到朱晞颜逼迫结亲的原因，不等陈浩宇接话，继续问：“你奶奶说我爹逼你娶我有原因，原因到底是什么？赶紧告诉老娘。”
[EN] "Watching you get thrashed makes my day—why on earth would I help you? I only stepped out thinking you brought good news about canceling the engagement. Spit it out: did your parents go see my father to break it off?" Certain of it after shredding Zhu Shuzhen's lady-like image to ribbons, she expected nothing less than total surrender from the Chens.
[ZH] 陈浩宇担心，若让朱淑真知道散播谣言之人是他，恐怕以后不得安生，赶紧掩饰：“还有原因？我怎么不知道？恐怕是我奶奶随口胡说，或者是长辈们之间的陈年往事……”
[EN] "Hmph! I will not give you the satisfaction! Breaking off the match is off the table till kingdom come—Grandmother says she will only welcome you through our doors once you mend your temper!" Having meant to treat her to Juxiang Tower in thanks for storming his house, her cold shoulder killed all his goodwill; now he just wanted to watch her stew.
[ZH] 千里寻总觉得陈浩宇表情怪怪的：“你果真不知道？可你为何说话眼神闪烁？难道是编瞎话骗我？”
[EN] It worked like a charm—her face darkened at once. "Are you out of your mind, Chen Haoyu? Feeding me that same stale line after the ruckus I raised at Chen Manor?! Did nobody tell you what I did?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇稳住眼神，继续掩饰：“别把自己太当回事，你有什么值得本公子骗？该说的我已说，你也知道以后该怎么做，本公子还有事，懒得跟你瞎耽误工夫。”
[EN] Her anger only puffed him up further. "Qinggua gave me the play-by-play the second I stepped through the door! Who do you think my parents and grandmother are? They saw right through your cheap tricks! Breaking off this engagement is nowhere in sight."
[ZH] 陈浩宇刚要走，想到蔣爷在路上堵他，搞不好还得弄出满身伤来。
[EN] Dread pricked Qianli Xun. "Why are you grinning so smugly? Almost sounds like you do not want to break it off!"
[ZH] 他瞧瞧望月轩的十位下人：“你的手下能不能借我用用？”
[EN] "Of course I do not want to marry you!" Snapping back to his real goal, Chen Haoyu dropped the smirk. "Your little rampage was not totally useless, though. Before, stalling was just Grandmother's private idea; after your show today, she got an airtight excuse to tell your father outright that I will only wed you when you fix your temper."
[ZH] “你想干什么？”
[EN] "Wait—your parents went to Zhu Manor to tell my father THAT?" Qianli Xun realized at once that Chen Haoyu's parents must have intercepted her letter.
[ZH] “刚才那些人在路上堵我，难道你想见死不救？小心我下次见到我姐，在她面前说你坏话。”
[EN] "That is what Grandmother told me," Chen Haoyu confirmed. "All you need to do now is refuse to mend your temper, and our wedding gets stalled forever."
[ZH] 千里寻本不愿借，想到嫂嫂对她的弟弟上心，若是陈浩宇被打出残疾，嫂嫂肯定会伤心；若再知道她故意不帮陈浩宇，肯定会更伤心。
[EN] Deflation washed over Qianli Xun; she had pulled out every stop and ended up right back at square one. "Is your whole family touched in the head? I put on a show like that and they still refuse to cancel?!" Remembering Old Madam Xia's slip earlier, she pressed before he could reply, "Your grandmother said my father forced you to marry me for a specific reason. What reason? Spill it right now!"
[ZH] 千里寻即使再讨厌陈浩宇，也不好不帮：“红豆，带大家护送陈公子，只要别让刚才那帮人伤他就行。”
[EN] Terrified Zhu Shuzhen might tear him limb from limb if she ever learned he had spread those rumors, Chen Haoyu scrambled to cover. "A reason? How would I know? Grandmother was probably rambling, or talking about some old grudge between the elders..."
[ZH] 红豆干脆地应道：“是，小姐。”
[EN] Eyeing his twitchy face, Qianli Xun narrowed her eyes. "Really do not know? Then why are your eyes darting all over the place? Lying through your teeth?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说了声“多谢”，得意洋洋，大踏步上了马车。
[EN] Steadying his gaze, Chen Haoyu bluffed hard. "Do not flatter yourself—what makes you worth lying to? I told you all I know, and you know what to do. I have places to be; no time to waste jawing with you."
[ZH] 红豆带领九个下人跟在马车后面负责保护陈家公子。
[EN] Turning to leave, he remembered Master Jiang waiting in ambush down the road—step out alone and he would end up beaten to a pulp. Glancing at Wangyue Pavilion's ten servants, he asked, "Can I borrow your crew for a bit?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情纠结帮助陈浩宇之事，第三次闹事又没成功，令她倍感惆怅。
[EN] "What for?"
[ZH] 耗着？要耗到何时去？说不定耗到最后还得嫁。
[EN] "Those thugs are waiting to jump me down the street! You going to stand by and watch me get crippled? Watch out, next time I see my sister I will tell on you!"
[ZH] 千里寻不得不开始思考韦嬷嬷叮嘱的话。
[EN] Qianli Xun had zero desire to lend them, yet her sister-in-law doted on this brother. If Chen Haoyu got maimed—and Chen Wenxin learned she had refused to help—her sister-in-law would be heartbroken. Loathing him as she did, she gave in. "Hongdou, take everyone and escort Young Master Chen. Just keep that gang from laying a hand on him."
[ZH] 她边往回走，边思考，若必须嫁到陈家，该如何自处？
[EN] "Yes, Miss!" Hongdou answered crisply.
[ZH] 陈浩宇在茶肆时可怜她的话回响在耳边，忽然觉得这家伙做人不怎么样，说的话倒是给她出了个主意。若必须嫁到陈家，不如各过各的，互不侵犯。
[EN] Calling out a breezy "Much obliged!" Chen Haoyu swaggered back into his carriage while Hongdou and the nine servants fell in behind to guard him.
[ZH] 千里寻不由嗟叹，穿越而来的感情婚姻之路实在不顺。喜欢的人嫁不了，跟不喜欢的人退个婚竟然比当初与林觉和离还不容易，不容易到仪王也帮不了。
[EN] Qianli Xun had no energy to dwell on doing him a favor; her third attempt to blow up the betrothal had fizzled, leaving a hollow ache behind. Stall? Stall until when? Drag it out long enough and she might still end up shoved into a bridal sedan.
[ZH] 仪王真的帮不了吗？千里寻又立刻否认，关键在于她不愿接受仪王的感情，说得更准确些，是她不能接受。
[EN] At last, she turned her mind to Matron Wei's warning. Walking back inside, she asked herself: if she truly had to marry into the Chen family, how would she live? Chen Haoyu's words from the teahouse echoed back—useless as the guy was, he had tossed out one practical blueprint: live separate lives under one roof and stay out of each other's way.
[ZH] 想到仪王，自然想到申简辰，自捶丸场分别，再没见过，白风来找苦荞时，捎过一次话，说王爷和郡主邀请她随时去幽林别居。
[EN] She let out a long sigh. Her romantic road since transmigrating had been pure disaster. She could not marry the man she loved, and breaking an unwanted betrothal was proving harder than her divorce from Lin Jue—so hard even Prince Yi could not untangle it.
[ZH] 千里寻让白风回的话模棱两可，申简辰应该能听出些意味来。
[EN] Could Prince Yi really not help? She shot that thought down on the spot. The catch was that she would not accept his feelings—or rather, she could not. Thinking of Prince Yi brought back Shen Jianchen. Since parting at the chuiwan grounds they had not met; Bai Feng had passed along one message through Kuqiao saying the prince and princess welcomed her at Youlin Retreat anytime, and Qianli Xun had sent back an evasive reply Shen Jianchen could surely read between the lines.
[ZH] 回到闺房，她高兴不起来，还是继续写话本，沉浸在自己编织的故事中，也许会很快忘记所有烦恼。
[EN] Back in her room, unable to shake her gloom, she turned back to her huaben, hoping losing herself in a made-up world would sweep away real troubles.
[ZH] 苦荞已知小姐帮陈公子处理好麻烦，并不知退婚未成。见小姐不高兴，猜出七八分。小心问：“小姐，送给老爷的书信是不是不应该给陈家老爷和夫人？”
[EN] Kuqiao knew her mistress had bailed Young Master Chen out yet had not heard the engagement was still intact; one look at her mistress's downcast face told her most of it. "Miss," she asked with care, "should I not have handed Master's letter to Master and Madam Chen?"
[ZH] 千里寻在书桌前坐好：“给了或不给，都已不重要，退婚之事遥遥无期，谁知道结果是个什么样。算了，以后我不再想退婚之事，该干什么干什么。”
[EN] Sitting at her desk, Qianli Xun shook her head. "Handing it over or not makes no difference now. Breaking the engagement is pushed off with no end in sight—who knows how it will turn out. Forget it. I am done chasing an annulment. From now on, I live my life as usual."
[ZH] 小姐变得消极，苦荞很自责，总觉得与自己没把信亲自送到老爷手中有关：“小姐，都怪我，陈公子的爹娘实在狡猾……”
[EN] Guilt tugged at Kuqiao; she blamed herself for letting the Chen parents intercept the letter. "It is all my fault, Miss! Young Master Chen's parents were so sly..."
[ZH] “行了，都说跟你没关系，伺候笔墨，我要继续写话本。”
[EN] "Enough, I told you it was not your fault. Grind the ink—I am writing my huaben."
[ZH] 苦荞开始研磨，千里寻提起笔的一刻，努力让自己忘记无法退婚的不快，沉浸在新的故事中……
[EN] As Kuqiao ground the ink, Qianli Xun took up her brush, shoving the stalled engagement out of mind to dive into her story.
[ZH] 大概写过七八页纸后，红豆回来，见小姐忙着写字，怕打扰，正准备出去，被千里寻叫住：“怎么才回来？”
[EN] Seven or eight pages later, Hongdou returned. Seeing her mistress absorbed in writing, she started to slip back out when Qianli Xun called her back. "Why so long?"
[ZH] 红豆站住回道：“陈公子要求把他护送到正街，看在他与小姐订婚，我就没拒绝。”
[EN] "Young Master Chen asked us to escort him all the way to the main avenue," Hongdou reported. "Seeing he is betrothed to Miss, I did not refuse."
[ZH] 千里寻并没批评，关切地问：“那伙人可对你们动手？”
[EN] Qianli Xun did not scold her. "Did that gang try anything?"
[ZH] 红豆骄傲道：“他们哪敢？见我们跟着陈公子，黑胖子只好带人离开，说再找机会与陈公子算账。”
[EN] "They would not dare!" Hongdou lifted her chin with pride. "One look at us shadowing Young Master Chen, and the swarthy fat man pulled his crew back, muttering he would settle the score another day."
[ZH] “你去忙吧，这里有苦荞伺候。”千里继续提笔写话本。
[EN] "Go tend to your duties. Kuqiao can wait on me here."
[ZH] 红豆答应着离开。
[EN] Dipping her brush once more, Qianli Xun went back to her manuscript as Hongdou withdrew.

#CH 174
[ZH] 乔文仙成亲，申简辰自然会去，仪王是乔家的外孙，怎么说也应前去撑场面。
[ZH] 迎亲的过程申简辰并未参与，他直接参加婚宴，亲眼见证一对儿新人拜天地的过程。
[ZH] 整个过程，文渊一脸平静，平静到大家根本发现不了他内心的不快。苗知瑜顶着盖头，虽看不到表情，申简辰却能想象到她是快乐的。
[ZH] 想到苗知瑜的快乐，不由想起千里寻，不知她是否伤心？
[ZH] 几次邀请她去幽林别居，她都婉言拒绝，要么说身体不适，要么说近期不宜出门，打算窝在望月轩。
[ZH] 申简辰知道千里寻是有意避开他，她越是有意，他越是伤心。
[ZH] 梦容也跟着前来参加文渊的婚礼，她近期将哥哥看得更紧，到哪里都要跟着。
[ZH] 拜天地的程序结束后，大家进入宴席，梦容才与哥哥分开坐到女宾区。与乔家的女眷坐在一起，面对大家的热情与恭敬，她暂时没有心思关注哥哥。
[ZH] 不被梦容关注，申简辰觉出一身轻松，大家敬酒来者不拒。
[ZH] 文渊过来敬酒时，申简辰已有几分醉意，还是端起酒盅一口饮下。
[ZH] 文渊也一干为敬，仪王是他第一个敬酒之人，他还没有醉意，可今日他已决定，要喝得酩酊大醉，只有这样才能忘记成亲时娶的不是心爱之人。
[ZH] 接着文渊到处敬酒，申简辰也一盅一盅继续喝，醉意越浓，心中越想千里寻。
[ZH] 他招来白风，悄悄说：“出去套好马车。”
[ZH] 白风不敢多问，乖乖离开宴席，出了乔府。
[ZH] 申简辰瞧了瞧文渊，他正在与众人敬酒，便向身边的乔守仁耳语道：“外公，我有要事，先行一步。结束后，烦请外公派人将梦容送回。”
[ZH] 乔守仁不便多问，自然同意：“仪王请便，结束后我定派人将佳和郡主安全送到。”
[ZH] 趁众人不注意，申简辰起身离开。
[ZH] 文渊成亲后，朱陈两家的婚事也快了，申简辰根本坐不住，在酒精的刺激下，他想去朱府走一趟。
[ZH] 马车到达朱府时，天色已暗下，申简辰从马车上下来，径直向朱府侧门而去。
[ZH] 白风不明所以，紧跟上，朱府侧门紧闭，他上前将门敲开。
[ZH] 开门的下人认识仪王，赶紧行礼，刚要说话，仪王却不管不顾走进府内，走了两步，不知该往哪个方向。
[ZH] 白风对朱府的下人说：“仪王来见知府大人，前面带路。”
[ZH] 下人说道：“仪王，您这边请。”
[EN] When Qiao Wenxian's wedding day arrived, Shen Jianchen naturally attended; as the Qiao family's maternal grandson, Prince Yi owed them his presence. Skipping the bridal procession, he arrived straight for the banquet and watched the new couple bow to heaven and earth.
[ZH] 朱府的下人把仪王带到会客厅，安顿好后，急急匆匆去通知老爷。
[EN] Throughout the rites, Wenyuan kept a calm, unreadable face—so steady that guests never guessed the misery beneath. Though the red veil hid Miao Zhiyu's features, Shen Jianchen could picture her quiet bliss. Thinking of Miao Zhiyu's joy drew his thoughts straight to Qianli Xun. Was she hurting today? Every time he invited her to Youlin Retreat lately, she sent polite excuses—feeling under the weather, or preferring not to go out and staying shut inside Wangyue Pavilion. Knowing she was dodging him on purpose only sharpened the ache in his chest.
[ZH] 朱晞颜吃过晚饭，正在院中溜达，听到下人报告，来不及换衣裳，匆忙往会客厅而去。
[EN] Mengrong had come to Wenyuan's wedding too, keeping a tighter watch on her brother than ever and sticking close wherever he went. Once the ceremony ended and the feast began, however, she moved to the women's section. Surrounded by warm, deferential Qiao ladies, she had no time to monitor her brother.
[ZH] 一路上思忖，乔文仙大婚，按说仪王应该在乔家，为何天刚暗下却突然造访？也不知是好事还是坏事。
[EN] Freed from Mengrong's watchful eyes, Shen Jianchen breathed easier and accepted every toast offered. By the time Wenyuan came around to toast him, Shen Jianchen already felt a warm buzz yet drained his cup in one swallow. Wenyuan matched him drop for drop. Prince Yi was the very first guest he toasted; still sober now, he had resolved to drink himself blind tonight so he could forget the bride waiting in his chambers was not the woman he loved.
[ZH] 当朱晞颜走进会客厅时，闻到一股酒气，便知仪王是从乔文仙的婚宴而来，看那脸色，略微泛红，应该喝了不少酒。
[EN] As Wenyuan moved from table to table, Shen Jianchen kept knocking back cup after cup, the wine only making him miss Qianli Xun harder. Beckoning Bai Feng over, he murmured, "Go hitch the carriage outside."
[ZH] 朱晞颜恭敬拜道：“让仪王殿下久候，是下官失职。”
[EN] Without asking questions, Bai Feng slipped out of the feast and exited Qiao Manor.
[ZH] 申简辰坐在椅子上，端起桌上的茶盏，喝起茶来，他想试试，会不会因为喝过几口茶，人变得清醒，便不想说出接下来的话。
[EN] Glancing over to see Wenyuan busy toasting guests, Shen Jianchen leaned toward Qiao Shouren and murmured, "Grandfather, urgent business calls me away. Once the banquet ends, please have someone escort Mengrong home."
[ZH] 可喝下茶后，他的想法依然未改变。
[EN] Qiao Shouren knew better than to pry. "Go right ahead, Your Highness. I will see Princess Jiahe safely home when it wraps up."
[ZH] 朱晞颜见仪王一言不发，只顾喝茶，不由紧张起来，难道近期政务出现什么问题被仪王知晓？
[EN] Slipping out unnoticed, Shen Jianchen left the hall. With Wenyuan wed, the Zhu-Chen wedding loomed right around the corner. Restless and emboldened by wine, he headed straight for Zhu Manor.
[ZH] “下官若有什么错处，还请仪王明示。”
[EN] Dusk had fallen by the time the carriage rolled up. Alighting, Shen Jianchen marched straight to the side gate, with a baffled Bai Feng hurrying at his heels to knock.
[ZH] 只见仪王放下茶盏，抬起几分醉眼：“我过来是想求知府大人一件事。”
[EN] Recognizing Prince Yi, the gatekeeper dropped to his knees—only for the prince to stride straight past into the courtyard, then halt after two paces unsure which path to take.
[ZH] 朱晞颜心里的石头终于落地，原来不是为政务：“仪王请说，只要下官能办到，赴汤蹈火，在所不辞。”
[EN] "Prince Yi is here to see Prefect Zhu," Bai Feng told the gatekeeper. "Lead the way."
[ZH] “知府大人言重，不是什么大事，也不是难事，就看知府大人是否乐意。”
[EN] "Right this way, Your Highness."
[ZH] “乐意乐意，当然乐意，只要是仪王的事，下官哪有不乐意的道理。”
[EN] The servant ushered Prince Yi into the reception hall, saw him seated, and dashed off to summon Master Zhu.
[ZH] “好，既如此，也没必要瞒着，我喜欢幽栖居士，想把她放在我身边，还请知府大人明日去陈家商议退婚之事。”
[EN] Having finished supper, Zhu Xiyan was strolling in his courtyard when the report reached him. Without stopping to change robes, he hurried toward the reception hall, wondering what could bring Prince Yi knocking right after dusk when he ought to be at Qiao Wenxian's wedding feast—good news or bad?
[ZH] 尽管早已猜到仪王对自家女儿有想法，此刻仪王如此明确提出，还是将朱晞颜给惊住。
[EN] Stepping inside, he caught the sharp whiff of wine and saw the flush across the prince's cheeks. Dropping into a deep bow, he said, "Keeping Your Highness waiting is this humble official's dereliction."
[ZH] 白风也被惊住。
[EN] Seated in his chair, Shen Jianchen lifted a tea bowl and drank. Part of him tested whether a few sips of tea would sober him up enough to hold back what he was about to say. It did not.
[ZH] 神柳之下，王爷与幽栖居士抱在一起时他虽已知道王爷的心思，可想着两人都有婚约，王爷应该会慢慢放下，没想到他如此疯狂，直接逼知府大人找陈家退婚，这跟抢亲有什么区别？
[EN] Watching the prince drink in tight-lipped silence rattled Zhu Xiyan—had some blunder in prefecture administration reached his ears? "If this humble official has erred in any way, please speak plainly, Your Highness."
[ZH] 吃惊之余，朱晞颜跪在地上，低下头，一言不发。
[EN] Setting the bowl down, Shen Jianchen raised wine-heavy eyes. "I came to ask Prefect Zhu for one favor."
[ZH] 申简辰垂下醉眼：“怎么？你不愿意？难道觉得我配不上幽栖居士？”
[EN] A boulder rolled off Zhu Xiyan's chest—not official business, then! "Speak, Your Highness! If it lies within my power, I will go through fire and water without hesitation!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜感慨，如果仪王早些出现那该多好。可现在他着实不想再折腾：“仪王乃人中龙凤，是淑真无福，配不上殿下。”
[EN] "No need for dramatics, Prefect. It is neither huge nor hard—only a matter of whether you are willing."
[ZH] 在喜酒的作用下，申简辰忍不住表露心迹：“可我看幽栖居士甚是顺眼。”
[EN] "Willing, willing, of course I am willing! How could I ever refuse anything for Your Highness?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜赶紧磕头：“仪王是官家之子，娶妻纳妾自然要征得官家同意，淑真不仅和离过一次，在临安名声也不好，只怕官家不会同意，还望仪王三思而行。”
[EN] "Good. Then I will not beat around the bush. I love Youqi Jushi and want her by my side. I ask Prefect Zhu to go to the Chen family tomorrow and call off their engagement."
[ZH] 申简辰再次抬起醉眼，见朱晞颜的头几乎挨到地面，不满道：“什么三思而行？我看是知府大人根本不想将女儿许给我。”
[EN] Even though Zhu Xiyan had half-suspected Prince Yi's feelings for his daughter, hearing it laid out so bluntly stunned him speechless.
[ZH] 朱晞颜的头这次真的挨到地面：“下官不敢，只是淑真的婚姻之路并不顺遂，好不容易与陈家订亲，婚事在即，作为她的父亲，不愿旁生枝节，更不敢奢望能攀上高枝，只愿她平平安安，一切顺利。”
[EN] Bai Feng froze in shock too. Seeing them embrace beneath the Divine Willow had tipped him off to his master's heart, yet with both of them betrothed, he had assumed the prince would let go in time. Never had he imagined His Highness going this far—pressuring the Prefect to cancel a wedding on the eve of the ceremony! How was this any different from snatching a bride?
[ZH] 申简辰变得沉默，千里寻拒绝的话回荡在耳边：“老娘说过，除非猪能上树，才答应嫁给你，这话依然有效。”
[EN] Reeling, Zhu Xiyan dropped straight to his knees and bowed his head in silence.
[ZH] 他叹口气，站起来，绕过朱晞颜向门外走去。白风也绕过朱晞颜，紧跟上王爷。
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked down through hazy eyes. "What? You refuse? Think I am not good enough for Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 仪王离开后，朱晞颜才抬起头，从地上爬起来，走到椅子跟前坐下，深深吸了一口气，又吐出：“唉，没想到淑真这么招人喜欢，连仪王都看上，可惜啊，我朱家没有成为皇亲国戚的命。”
[EN] If only Prince Yi had spoken up months ago, Zhu Xiyan lamented inwardly—yet now he could not bear another storm. "Your Highness is a dragon among men. It is Shuzhen who lacks the blessing to match Your Highness."
[ZH] 离开朱府，申简辰没有立刻回幽林别居，而是朝望月轩方向而去。
[EN] Emboldened by wedding wine, Shen Jianchen pressed his heart out into the open. "Yet Youqi Jushi suits my eyes."
[ZH] 到达后，并未下马车，呆呆地坐在车里。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan knocked his forehead to the floor. "Your Highness is His Majesty's own son; taking wife or concubine requires imperial assent. Not only has Shuzhen been divorced once, her name in Lin'an has been bruised. I fear His Majesty would never approve. I beg Your Highness to think thrice!"
[ZH] 白风不敢言语，更不敢去找苦荞，默默陪伴王爷。
[EN] Raising his eyes to see Zhu Xiyan's brow near the floorboards, Shen Jianchen frowned in irritation. "Think thrice? Sounds to me like Prefect Zhu simply refuses to give me your daughter."
[ZH] 大约过了两刻钟，韦嬷嬷带着一名丫鬟和小厮从外面回来。姑娘去陈家后，韦嬷嬷便去铺子里收租，收完租又与约好的朋友在外面喝茶，所以回来晚些。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan pressed his forehead flush against the floor. "This humble official would never dare! Only Shuzhen's road in marriage has been rocky from the start. After all this trouble, she is finally betrothed to the Chen family with the wedding close at hand. As her father, I want no fresh complications, nor dare I dream of climbing into imperial ranks. I wish only for her to live safe and smooth."
[ZH] 她走下马车后，一眼瞧见仪王的马车停在外面，以为佳和郡主来找自家姑娘，仪王又在外面等候，便走过去行礼道：“仪王殿下，可否去屋里坐，边品茶边等郡主？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen fell silent as Qianli Xun's sharp rejection rang in his ears: "I told you—when pigs can climb trees, maybe I will marry you. That still stands."
[ZH] 马车内并未传出仪王的声音，韦嬷嬷感到纳闷，向坐在马车边缘的白风问：“白侍卫，仪王殿下可在车上？”
[EN] Letting out a long breath, he rose, stepped around the kneeling Prefect, and walked out the door, Bai Feng hurrying right behind him.
[ZH] 白风见王爷不搭话，连忙掩饰：“王爷怎么可能在车上？是我想见苦荞，才偷偷把马车赶过来，见天色已晚，正犹豫要不要见她，嬷嬷就出现，我想我还是不见了，这就回去。”
[EN] Only after Prince Yi had gone did Zhu Xiyan lift his head, climb stiffly to his feet, and drop into a chair with a deep exhale. "Ah, who knew Shuzhen was so irresistible that even Prince Yi fell for her? Shame our Zhu family lacks the destiny to become imperial kin."
[ZH] 白风急忙赶起马车离开望月轩。
[EN] Leaving Zhu Manor, Shen Jianchen did not head straight back to Youlin Retreat, steering instead toward Wangyue Pavilion. Arriving outside its gate, he stayed inside the carriage staring blankly into the dark. Bai Feng dared neither speak nor slip off to see Kuqiao, sitting silent on the box beside his master.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷立刻明白仪王肯定在马车上，而佳和郡主根本没来，看来这位仪王对自家姑娘确实不一般。韦嬷嬷决定将今晚所见烂在心里，不会在姑娘面前提起，省得令她心烦。
[EN] About half an hour passed before Matron Wei returned in a carriage with a maid and a page boy. After her young mistress had gone off to Chen Manor earlier, Matron Wei had collected shop rents and lingered over tea with old friends, arriving home late. Stepping down, she spotted Prince Yi's carriage parked outside and assumed Princess Jiahe was visiting inside while the prince waited. Walking over, she curtsied. "Your Highness, will you not step inside and wait for Her Highness over a bowl of tea?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有与韦嬷嬷搭话，并不是怕被人知道他对幽栖居士的心思，而是懒得说话。白风将马车赶走，他也未埋怨。
[EN] When no voice answered from inside, Matron Wei blinked and asked Bai Feng on the driver's bench, "Guard Bai, is Prince Yi inside?"
[ZH] 这个时间，梦容应该已从乔家回到幽林别居。申简辰想到梦容，心理重负再次出现，比往常任何时候都让他喘不过气来。
[EN] Seeing his master stay mute, Bai Feng scrambled to cover. "How could His Highness be inside? I only borrowed the carriage on the sly to see Kuqiao! Seeing how late it got, I was debating whether to knock when you showed up, Matron. Better I head back now!" Snapping the reins, he drove off in a hurry.
[ZH] 自捶丸场后，他几次想张口向梦容说明心中所爱，都因梦容充满爱意又让人不由产生怜惜的眼神，不得不将已到喉咙的话咽回去。
[EN] Matron Wei knew at once Prince Yi had been sitting inside—and Princess Jiahe was nowhere near Wangyue Pavilion. Prince Yi's feelings for her young mistress were far from ordinary. Resolving to bury tonight's sight deep in her chest, she decided not to breathe a word to Shuzhen and spare her fresh grief.
[ZH] 此刻，他坐在马车上，一阵醉意袭来，想要吐露真情的想法愈加强烈。
[EN] Inside the rolling carriage, Shen Jianchen had stayed silent not from fear of exposure, only from sheer weariness. He did not blame Bai Feng for driving away either. By this hour, Mengrong would be back at Youlin Retreat. One thought of her brought that suffocating weight crashing down on his chest harder than ever. Since the chuiwan grounds, he had tried telling her the truth more than once, only to choke every word back before her loving, vulnerable eyes. Now, with wine buzzing in his veins, the urge to lay the truth bare burned hotter than ever.

#CH 175
[ZH] 哥哥提前离开婚宴不知去向，乔梦容比以往任何时候都紧张。乔守仁安排人赶马车将她送回幽林别居，第一件事便问李管家哥哥回来没。
[ZH] 得到否认后，她待在闺房坐立不安。
[ZH] 哥哥到底去了哪里？会不会背着她跑去与美人幽会？
[ZH] 喜兰见郡主焦虑，安慰道：“乔老爷说过，王爷是去处理公务。”
[ZH] “公务？你何时见哥哥关心过公务？”乔梦容并不相信乔守仁的说辞，“喜兰，你帮我回想一下，哥哥自来临安后，有没有对其他女子不一样？”
[ZH] 喜兰仔细回想：“幽栖居士可算？”
[ZH] “淑真？我不觉得哥哥对她有什么特别。”
[ZH] “可除郡主之外，与王爷说话最多的只有幽栖居士。”
[ZH] “我与淑真交好，哥哥自然与她说得多些。再仔细想想，除淑真之外，还有没有其他人。”
[EN] When her brother vanished early from the wedding banquet without a word, anxiety gripped Qiao Mengrong tighter than ever before. Once Qiao Shouren's carriage delivered her back to Youlin Retreat, her first question to Butler Li was whether Brother had returned. Told he had not, she paced her chambers unable to sit still. Where had he gone? Had he slipped off behind her back to meet some beauty?
[ZH] 喜兰又认真想一回：“我实在想不出王爷还对哪个女子不一般。”
[EN] Seeing her mistress on edge, Xilan offered comfort. "Master Qiao said His Highness went to handle official business."
[ZH] “肯定是我太多疑。你到大门口盯着，哥哥回来第一时间告诉我。”
[EN] "Official business? When have you ever seen Brother care about official business?" Qiao Mengrong dismissed Qiao Shouren's excuse on the spot. "Think back for me, Xilan—since coming to Lin'an, has Brother treated any other woman differently?"
[ZH] 乔梦容想起捶丸那日，哥哥与淑真之间好像有些不一样，难道哥哥喜欢淑真？很快她又否认这个想法，哥哥怎么会喜欢有婚约之人？再说淑真与她是好朋友，绝不会做对不起她的事。
[EN] Xilan thought hard. "Does Youqi Jushi count?"
[ZH] 喜兰应了声“是”，拜别郡主去大门口候着。
[EN] "Shuzhen? I do not see Brother treating her specially."
[ZH] 大约等了半个时辰，终于看到王爷归来的马车。
[EN] "Yet aside from Your Highness, Youqi Jushi is the only woman His Highness talks to at length."
[ZH] 喜兰迎上去：“王爷，郡主担心您，派奴婢在门口一直守着。”
[EN] "Shuzhen and I are close friends, so of course Brother talks more with her. Think harder—anyone besides Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 白风心想：“若郡主知道王爷喜欢幽栖居士，不知道会伤心成什么样？”
[EN] Xilan racked her brain again. "I truly cannot think of another woman His Highness treats out of the ordinary."
[ZH] 申简辰走下马车，没说一句话，径直朝别居内走去。
[EN] "I must be imagining shadows. Go watch the front gate and tell me the instant Brother gets back." A flash of the chuiwan match crossed Qiao Mengrong's mind—something between Brother and Shuzhen had felt slightly odd that day. Could Brother like Shuzhen? She brushed the notion off at once: why would Brother fancy a betrothed woman? And Shuzhen was her dearest friend—she would never betray her.
[ZH] 白风要跟上时，被喜兰一把拉住：“王爷肯定去找郡主，我们还是慢些，别影响他们。”
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness." Xilan withdrew to wait by the gate.
[ZH] 白风觉得有道理，也满心希望王爷珍惜郡主。
[EN] Nearly an hour later, Prince Yi's carriage rolled up at last.
[ZH] 申简辰直奔梦容的闺房，在别人看来，他是担心郡主，只有他清楚，去见梦容，只是为向她表露内心真实的想法。
[EN] Xilan hurried out to meet him. "Your Highness, Her Highness has been worried sick and had me wait at the gate."
[ZH] 当申简辰踏进闺房小院儿时，乔梦容正站在院中，借着月色看到哥哥归来的身影，忍不住冲上去，抱住他：“哥哥，你跑去哪里？”
[EN] Bai Feng sighed to himself: if the princess ever learned His Highness loved Youqi Jushi, how shattered would she be?
[ZH] 哥哥身上有股酒气，看来他在乔家的婚宴上喝了不少酒。
[EN] Stepping down from the carriage without a word, Shen Jianchen strode straight into the retreat. Bai Feng moved to follow, only for Xilan to catch his sleeve. "His Highness is heading straight for Her Highness. Let us hang back and not interrupt."
[ZH] 申简辰像往日一样没有搂抱梦容：“梦容，我有话跟你讲。”
[EN] Agreeing—and praying His Highness would cherish the princess—Bai Feng held back.
[ZH] 乔梦容脸贴在哥哥的胸膛，闭上眼睛，用心感受将哥哥抱在怀里的踏实感：“哥哥有什么话尽管说。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked directly to Mengrong's courtyard. To onlookers, it looked like a devoted betrothed rushing to ease her worry; only he knew he was going there to lay bare his heart.
[ZH] 沉默片刻，申简辰鼓起勇气：“梦容，相处这么久，你应该能感受到我不是以前的赵朴……”
[EN] Stepping through her courtyard gate, he saw Qiao Mengrong waiting under the moonlight. Catching sight of him, she ran straight up and threw her arms around his waist. "Where did you run off to, Brother?" The smell of wine rose from his robes—he had drunk plenty at the Qiao feast.
[ZH] 此话一出，乔梦容无法再闭着眼享受哥哥身上的温度，她睁开双眼，手并未松开，怕一旦松开，哥哥会逃走。
[EN] Just as always, Shen Jianchen did not wrap his arms around her. "Mengrong, I have something to tell you."
[ZH] “哥哥为何要提起此事？”
[EN] Pressing her cheek against his chest, Qiao Mengrong closed her eyes to savor the solid comfort of holding him. "Tell me anything, Brother."
[ZH] “我不想再瞒着你……”
[EN] After a quiet pause, Shen Jianchen braced himself. "Mengrong, after all this time together, you must have sensed I am not the Zhao Pu from before..."
[ZH] 乔梦容觉出不对来，将哥哥抱得更紧：“哥哥不要再说，你就是我以前的哥哥，就是官家的十三子赵朴。”
[EN] Her eyes snapped open, the warmth gone in an instant, yet she refused to let go, terrified he would vanish if she loosened her grip. "Why bring that up, Brother?"
[ZH] “你何必欺骗自己，我这副皮囊是赵朴的没错，可心却不是，我……我有喜欢的人……”终于说出心里话，申简辰感到从未有过的轻松。
[EN] "I do not want to keep lying to you..."
[ZH] 听到一直担心的话，乔梦容无法再抱住哥哥，她艰难地松开手，话还未说，眼水已涌出：“哥哥喜欢的人是谁？”
[EN] Sensing dread ahead, Qiao Mengrong hugged him tighter. "Stop talking, Brother! You are my brother from before—you are Zhao Pu, His Majesty's thirteenth son!"
[ZH] 申简辰并未正面回答：“梦容，我会永远把你当亲妹妹看待，也希望你只当我是你的哥哥。”
[EN] "Why keep fooling yourself? This body is Zhao Pu's, yes, yet the heart inside is not. I... I already have someone I love." Getting the truth out at last lifted a crushing stone from his chest.
[ZH] 乔梦容泪流满面，继续追问：“她是谁？”
[EN] Hearing the exact words she had dreaded so long drained the strength from Qiao Mengrong's arms. She pried her hands loose, tears brimming before she could speak. "Who is the woman Brother loves?"
[ZH] “暂时还不能告诉你。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen did not answer directly. "Mengrong, I will always treat you like my own little sister, and I hope you will see me only as your brother."
[ZH] 听到此话，乔梦容更加难过，再次抱住哥哥：“哥哥，快说，你刚才的话都是骗我的，对不对？”
[EN] Tears spilled down her cheeks as she pressed on. "Who is she?"
[ZH] 尽管申简辰听到梦容带着哭腔的声音，心中着实愧疚，可又不愿违背心意：“这些话，我憋在心里许久，今日终于说出。”
[EN] "I cannot tell you yet."
[ZH] 乔梦容不得不承认哥哥刚才真真实实地说过他有喜欢的人，她再次放开哥哥，满脸泪痕，抽泣着，用泪眼盯着哥哥，月光下，他的脸显得轻松而平静。
[EN] Grief overwhelmed her, and she threw her arms around him again. "Tell me you were joking, Brother! Tell me every word was a lie!"
[ZH] “哥哥说这么多有什么用？官家赐婚，你只能娶我。”
[EN] Her sob-choked plea tore at Shen Jianchen's conscience, yet he refused to lie against his own heart. "I have held these words back for so long. Tonight I had to say them."
[ZH] 穿越而来，遇到一段不称心的婚约，还没有任何理由退掉，申简辰觉得自己比千里寻还不幸，她起码可以努力，可他连努力的机会都没有。
[EN] Forced to accept he meant every word, Qiao Mengrong released him again, sobbing through tear-streaked cheeks as she searched his face. Under the silver moonlight, he looked calm and relieved.
[ZH] 见哥哥沉默不语，梦容带着哭腔继续说：“哥哥，不管你是谁，不管你心里有没有我，你都是我乔梦容要嫁的人。大不了成亲后，我……我允许哥哥纳妾，我会与哥哥喜欢的人和睦相处……”
