[ZH] 千里寻自问自答：“说明我们根本不可能在一起，你最好死了这份心思，不要再对老娘有任何幻想。我都能移情别恋爱上文渊，你为何不能爱上梦容？”
[EN] Lowering her hands, Qianli Xun returned to the table. Like it or not, he wore Prince Yi's rank, so she left the seat of honor open. Watching him swagger straight into the host's seat drew a half-scoff, half-chuckle from her. "Sure know how to put on princely airs."
[ZH] 申简辰认真挑选他想听的：“今日总算明白你的心思，原来你已经爱上我，只是以为不会再见到我，才移情别恋；现在又因你我有婚约，才不得不劝我放下，对不对？”
[EN] Sitting down and meeting her eyes, Shen Jianchen smiled back. "You strut around playing the great literary talent—cannot I put on a few princely airs?"
[ZH] 千里寻着实佩服，面对不可能之事，申简辰还能面带笑容，搞得她不好再强硬下去，声音不由变得柔软许多。
[EN] Something in his gaze pulled Qianli Xun back to the earthquake: him charging through falling rubble straight toward her without a thought for his own life, the exact second she grasped how deep this man's love ran—deep enough to lay down his life. Her nose stung, and moisture pooled in her eyes.
[ZH] “如果没有梦容，如果我内心没有残留着对文渊的感情，我会答应跟你在一起，毕竟你是第一个愿意为我舍弃性命的人，毕竟我对你动过情。可我们之间有着无法跨越的障碍，不如放下，我愿与你做永远的朋友。”
[EN] "What is wrong? Why do you look ready to cry?" Shen Jianchen asked in surprise.
[ZH] 千里寻第一次对他说话温柔，申简辰不大习惯，内心也不由自主酥软起来：“怎么不可能？只要你答应跟我在一起，其他事情交给我处理。”
[EN] Forcing a bright smile to beat back the tears, she murmured, "During the earthquake, running straight toward me through all that danger—how could you be that dumb, getting yourself..."
[ZH] 说了一整，他油盐不进，千里寻又变得语气强硬：“老娘说过，除非猪上树，才答应嫁给你，难道你忘了？”
[EN] "Turned out great, did it not? We landed in the same dynasty and found each other again. Proof you can never slip out of my palm."
[ZH] 申简辰忍不住说出计划：“当然没忘，我已让白风买回一头小猪，准备训练它爬树，等训练好后，你可别反悔。”
[EN] Unwilling to bark him down like before, Qianli Xun spoke in earnest. "Have you ever considered that fate only wrote us down as friends?"
[ZH] “你是不是脑子有病？难道听不出老娘是拒绝你？”
[EN] "In my heart, you will only ever be mine."
[ZH] “我管不了那么多，只想让你找不到拒绝我的理由。”
[EN] "Hmph. Full of yourself."
[ZH] 女茶师推门而入：“幽栖居士，现在由我为您与这位公子做茶。”
[EN] "Not full of myself—dead serious."
[ZH] 两人不好再继续斗嘴。
[EN] His steady gaze stirred her chest; forcing thoughts of Mengrong back to the front, she steadied her voice. "I called you here today to make my heart plain. Before we transmigrated, I kept turning you down, and only when the earthquake hit did I regret never accepting you..."
[ZH] “有劳茶师。”
[EN] "About time you saw how good I am."
[ZH] 女茶师走进，身后跟着一位男琴师。女茶师来到茶桌前，男琴师则走到琴旁，两人均席地而坐。当茶师开始做茶时，琴师也开始抚琴，乐曲与做茶的动作配合得天衣无缝。
[EN] "When I first arrived in this world, I missed you every single day, thinking I would never see you again. Starved for love, I met Wenyuan—so gentle, so wonderful—and fell for him before I knew it. Then you showed up with a betrothed little sister by your side. Then the Zhu and Chen families sealed my engagement, and ONLY THEN did you tell me you were Shen Jianchen..."
[ZH] 两人已无心纠结如何说服对方，沉浸在乐曲和茶师的茶艺表演中。
[EN] "What is the point of rehearsing all that?" Shen Jianchen cut in. "I already know every word."
[ZH] 苦荞也趁机走进来，站在小姐身边，有这么好的机会向茶艺师学习做茶，她岂能轻易放过机会？
[EN] "The point is for you to see how many walls block us from ever being together! Do you know what that proves?"
[ZH] 女茶师做茶的步骤跟苦荞一模一样，不过是碾茶粉、放茶粉、放入热水、击拂、再击拂。可茶师的一举一动优雅多情，令人赏心悦目，百看不厌。最值得称赞的是在茶汤中作画，苦荞永远也学不会。
[EN] Afraid of the answer, Shen Jianchen stayed mute.
[ZH] 茶做好后，琴声也刚好停下，仿佛商量好一般。
[EN] "It proves we were never meant to end up together!" she answered herself. "Kill the hope right now and stop chasing illusions. If I could move on and love Wenyuan, why can you not love Mengrong?"
[ZH] 女茶师身边的丫鬟小心将两盏茶给二位先后摆上。茶师与琴师起身行礼后离开。
[EN] Cherry-picking only the pieces he wanted to hear, Shen Jianchen smiled. "Now I understand your heart at last! You DID fall for me—you only turned to Wenyuan thinking you would never find me again! And now you tell me to let go only because of our two betrothals—right?"
[ZH] 苦荞很知趣，也跟着出去，把门带好。
[EN] Qianli Xun almost had to hand it to him: grinning through impossible odds like that stripped away all her armor, softening her voice despite herself. "Without Mengrong—and without the lingering ache for Wenyuan—I would have said yes to you. You are the very first man willing to trade his life for mine, and my heart truly moved for you. Yet the walls between us cannot be crossed. Let it go, and let us be friends for life."
[ZH] 申简辰夸道：“你身边的丫鬟挺有眼色。”
[EN] Unused to hearing her speak so gently, Shen Jianchen felt his heart melt soft. "Who says they cannot be crossed? Just say you will be with me, and leave the rest to me."
[ZH] 千里寻端起茶盏，啜一口茶汤，不再像第一次一样不忍心破坏茶汤中的精致，放下茶盏，自夸道：“也不瞧瞧她的主人是谁？”
[EN] After all that breath, he had not budged an inch! Hardening her tone again, Qianli Xun snapped, "I told you before—when pigs climb trees, I will marry you! Did you forget?!"
[ZH] 申简辰瞧一眼茶汤，汤花中绘有一带远山然，充满意境，他无心过多鉴赏，抬起眉眼：“我知你因梦容今日特来劝说，我已做好万全准备，无论你说什么，都不会改变心意。”
[EN] Unable to keep his secret in, Shen Jianchen let it slip. "Not a word! I already had Bai Feng buy a piglet and start training it to climb trees. Once it makes it up, no backing out!"
[ZH] 千里寻被触动，试问有几个女孩子能招架得住一个王爷穷追不舍，何况他还是申简辰。她端起茶盏又品一口，掩饰表情，怕对方看出她的心思。
[EN] "Are you sick in the head?! Could you not tell I was brushing you off?!"
[ZH] 从知道仪王是申简辰后，她时常会想起眼前之人。开始以为是因得不到文渊而找的感情替补。现在才意识到，她在渐渐放下文渊的过程中，再次爱上眼前之人。
[EN] "I do not care—I am stripping away every excuse you have to say no."
[ZH] 她埋怨自己的情感变化太快，会不会被他人当作水性杨花？很快她便否认，文渊都已成亲，她有无数个理由喜欢上别人。
[EN] Just then, the door swung open and a tea mistress stepped in. "Youqi Jushi, I am here to prepare tea for you and this young gentleman."
[ZH] 要不要放下坚守的理念，做他的妾室？千里寻发现在爱情面前，她或许可以退让一步。可想到梦容，无法再沉迷下去。
[EN] Their bickering paused at once. "Much obliged, Tea Mistress."
[ZH] 梦容若知道闺中密友偷偷喜欢上她的哥哥，肯定不会原谅。若做出伤害好友之事，千里寻也无法原谅自己。
[EN] Behind her entered a male qin player. Taking seats on the floor—she by the tea stand, he behind his zither—they began in unison: the crisp notes of the qin wove around the graceful sweep of her wrists as she prepared the tea. Dropping their standoff, both Qianli Xun and Shen Jianchen surrendered to the music and art before them.
[ZH] 她更无法想象待在仪王身边，与好友共事一夫，两人由闺蜜变为仇敌的景象。
[EN] Kuqiao slipped inside to stand behind her mistress, eager to steal pointers from a master. Every step matched Kuqiao's own routine—grinding tea powder, spooning it in, pouring hot water, whisking and whisking again—yet the tea mistress turned every motion into pure poetry, topping it off by painting a delicate scene across the creamy froth, a feat Kuqiao knew she could never match.
[ZH] 千里寻一口气喝完茶，将茶盏重重放下：“老娘该说的已说完，听不听是你的事，你想干什么老娘管不着，以后也不想管，反正老娘不会配合你。”
[EN] Right on the final chord of the qin, the tea was complete. An attendant set the two bowls before the guests; tea mistress and musician bowed and withdrew, and tactful Kuqiao stepped right back outside and closed the door.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来走到门口，又转过身：“呵呵，想让猪上树，下辈子吧，不，下辈子也不可能！”
[EN] "Sharp maid you have got there," Shen Jianchen remarked.
[ZH] 她毫不犹豫推门而出。
[EN] Lifting her bowl, Qianli Xun took a sip without hesitating over spoiling the tea painting like she had on her first visit. "Look who her mistress is."
[ZH] 申简辰并不生气，他清楚千里寻是因为梦容才如此对待他。
[EN] Glancing down at his own bowl—where the foam depicted a faint range of distant hills—Shen Jianchen paid the artwork little mind and lifted his eyes. "I know you came today to lecture me for Mengrong's sake. My mind is locked in: nothing you say will sway me."
[ZH] 梦容是他们之间唯一的障碍，只要梦容在，他就无法与千里寻走到一起。申简辰端起茶盏像饮酒一般，将一带远山大口吞入。
[EN] A tremor ran through Qianli Xun. What girl on earth could stay untouched when a prince pursued her with everything he had—especially when that prince was Shen Jianchen? Taking another sip to hide her face, she realized a truth she had been dodging: since learning Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen, he had crept back into her thoughts day after day. At first she had blamed rebound heartbreak over Wenyuan; now she saw that as Wenyuan faded, she had fallen for Shen Jianchen all over again. Was she fickle? No—Wenyuan was already married; she had every right to love again. Could she swallow her pride and be his concubine? In the face of love, part of her actually wavered—until Mengrong's tear-streaked face snapped her cold sober. If Mengrong ever discovered her dearest friend had stolen her brother, she would never forgive her, and Qianli Xun could never forgive herself either. Sharing a husband with her best friend and watching sisterhood rot into venom was a nightmare she refused to live.
[ZH] 喜兰找的下人一路跟随仪王的马车，直到西湖外才下来。又悄悄尾随仪王走向永泰茶楼。
[EN] Draining the bowl in one gulp, she set it down hard. "I have said my piece. Listen or not, your call—do whatever you please, I am done meddling, and I will never play along." Rising to the doorway, she glanced back with a dry scoff. "Heh—getting a pig up a tree? Wait for your next life! No, not even then!"
[ZH] 仪王进入茶楼后，下人在外面找地方躲好，时不时朝茶楼门口瞧。大约半个时辰后，发现幽栖居士带丫鬟从茶楼出来，下人并未多想，继续观察。
[EN] Pushing the door open, she walked straight out without looking back.
[ZH] 不多会儿仪王也出来，下人嘀咕道：“难道仪王是与幽栖居士见面？若是，这下可有好戏看。”
[EN] Far from angry, Shen Jianchen knew Mengrong was the sole wall holding Qianli Xun back. While Mengrong stood between them, they had no path forward. Lifting his tea bowl like a cup of hard liquor, he swallowed the distant hills in one big gulp.
[ZH] 天色渐渐暗下，估摸着仪王已走远，下人才乘坐马车急急匆匆回幽林别居。
[EN] Down on the street, the servant Xilan had bribed had trailed Prince Yi's carriage to West Lake and shadowed him on foot to Yongtai Teahouse. Hiding across the lane to watch the entrance, he saw Youqi Jushi exit with her maid about an hour later—followed moments after by Prince Yi himself.
[ZH] 回别居的途中，申简辰一直思索如何处理与梦容的婚约。
[EN] "Wait a second," the servant muttered under his breath. "Was Prince Yi meeting Youqi Jushi in there? Oh boy, what a fireworks show!" Waiting until twilight fell and Prince Yi had ridden off, he hopped back into his carriage and hurried to Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 他后悔昨晚告诉梦容会与她成婚，决定回去后劝说梦容承认他不是赵朴，不要再有执着嫁给他的想法。等劝说好梦容再回趟汴梁，当面请官家取消婚约。
[EN] Inside his own rolling carriage, Shen Jianchen wrestled with how to untangle his betrothal to Mengrong. Regretting telling her last night that he would still marry her, he decided to go back, convince her to accept he was not Zhao Pu and let go of marrying him, and then ride to Bianliang to ask His Majesty face-to-face to rescind the marriage decree.

#CH 183
[ZH] 当申简辰到达幽林别居门口，看到林觉前来拜访，正与李管家说话。心想：“天都黑了，也不知林觉前来所为何事？”
[ZH] 李管家听到有马车声，仔细瞧去：“可巧，王爷刚好回来。”
[ZH] 林觉来到马车前拜见：“仪王殿下，下官有要事禀报。”
[ZH] 既是要事，门外说并不合适，申简辰下了马车，将林觉带入幽林别居，在正院的屋内听他禀报要事。
[ZH] 在仪王面前，林觉不敢随意坐下，况且是要事，站着才显得态度端正。
[ZH] “什么要事？林统制尽管讲。”申简辰暂时将个人情感之事搁置一边。
[ZH] “下官发现近期在临安有金人出没。”
[ZH] “金人？如何得知？”
[ZH] 申简辰也不由警惕起来，林觉能亲自前来禀报，应该不是与临安有生意往来的金人那么简单。
[ZH] “自去年金人从辽手中夺下燕云十六州，只愿将燕、蓟、檀、景、顺、涿、易七州归还大宋，还让大宋补交一百万贯作为燕京的代税钱，金国还要带走燕云地区的全部人口，条件实在卑鄙。下官便吩咐手下人警惕来往客商中的外族人，尤其是金人，果然发现一伙金人来到临安，大概有六七人，他们并非客商，有时会骑马行于闹市，搅得百姓不得安宁。乔家成亲那晚，一伙金人打入邵家镖局，搞得邵家鸡犬不宁，我的手下巡查街市时才发现。奇怪的是邵家并不愿官府介入。”
[ZH] 申简辰听完，先对林觉夸赞一番：“林统制爱国之心可嘉，只可惜你我爱国，官家却一再退让，退让到金人欲壑难填，我听说金人又乘机加码，提出归还西京可以，但需支付二十万两犒军费，官家眼睛都不眨一下，直接给了。我看官家要的只是‘收复燕云’的虚名而已。你继续警惕那伙金人，我会暗地里查探明白。”
[ZH] 得到仪王夸赞，林觉高兴不已。可仪王当着他的面议论官家，还表现出不满，林觉不敢搭话，只能听着。实话讲，他对官家也有不满，觉得他甚是纵容金人，这么纵容下去，只怕没有好结果。
[ZH] 林觉走后，尾随仪王的下人才回到幽林别居。
[ZH] 乔梦容依旧坐立难安，哥哥什么都不说，冷漠离开，搞得她焦心不已，在闺房中构想着各种各样的猜测，越构想越担心，也越难过。
[ZH] 天刚黑时，听喜兰说哥哥回来，她心里的石头才落地。本想去见哥哥，当面追问他到底去见谁？喜兰说林统制前来正与哥哥说话，乔梦容只好作罢。
[EN] Pulling up outside Youlin Retreat, Shen Jianchen spotted Lin Jue speaking with Butler Li at the gate. Already dark out—what brought Lin Jue knocking at this hour?
[ZH] 估摸着时间差不多时，刚要去见哥哥，下人回来，乔梦容便又坐下，急切地问：“你都看到什么？哥哥到底去了哪里？”
[EN] Hearing carriage wheels, Butler Li looked over. "What timing—His Highness is just back."
[ZH] 下人行完礼回道：“我一直尾随王爷，发现他去了西湖边的永泰茶楼……”
[EN] Stepping up to the carriage, Lin Jue bowed low. "Your Highness Prince Yi, this humble official has an urgent matter to report."
[ZH] 听到是永泰茶楼，乔梦容心情稍微轻松些，毕竟去永泰茶楼的都是些文人墨客，还好哥哥没去青楼。又一想，难道哥哥喜欢的人是位才女？不由又紧张起来，没等下人说完，急切地问：“你还看到什么？”
[EN] Sensitive business did not belong outside the gate. Alighting, Shen Jianchen ushered Lin Jue into his main courtyard chamber. Too respectful to sit—and treating the report with gravity—Lin Jue remained standing.
[ZH] “小的在茶楼外躲起来，耐心等待，天刚蒙蒙黑，我竟然看到幽栖居士从茶楼里出来，没过一会儿王爷也出来。郡主，该不会王爷与幽栖居士到茶楼喝茶？”下人不敢抬头看郡主，却能够想象到她的面色并不好。
[EN] Setting his personal turmoil aside for the moment, Shen Jianchen asked, "What urgent matter? Speak freely, Division Commander Lin."
[ZH] 乔梦容听到淑真也去了茶楼，还跟哥哥一前一后出来，心中五味杂陈，难道哥哥喜欢的人是淑真？
[EN] "This humble official has discovered Jurchens operating inside Lin'an lately."
[ZH] 喜兰见郡主不言语，问道：“还看到什么？”
[EN] "Jurchens? How did you learn this?" Shen Jianchen sharpened to full alert at once; if Lin Jue came in person, these were no ordinary Jurchen traders.
[ZH] 下人说：“小的就看到这些。”
[EN] "Since last year, after the Jurchens seized the Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun from the Liao, they offered to return only seven—Yan, Ji, Tan, Jing, Shun, Zhuo, and Yi—while demanding a million strings of cash from the Song as replacement tax for Yanjing AND stripping away the entire local population! Such shameless terms! I ordered my men to keep close tabs on foreign merchants, especially Jurchens. Sure enough, a band of six or seven Jurchens entered Lin'an—no merchants at all. They gallop horses through crowded markets, terrorizing citizens. On the night of the Qiao wedding, a group of Jurchens even raided the Shao Escort Agency, throwing the whole compound into chaos. My patrol discovered it while making rounds—yet oddly, the Shao family refused yamen involvement."
[ZH] “你下去吧。”待下人出去后，喜兰小心说，“郡主，该不会就是幽栖居士？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen commended him first. "Your patriotism does you credit, Division Commander Lin. Shame that while you and I care for the empire, His Majesty keeps giving ground—feeding Jurchen greed without end! I hear the Jurchens just jacked up the price again, demanding two hundred thousand taels of silver in army provisions to return the Western Capital, and His Majesty paid without blinking. All His Majesty cares about is the hollow glory of 'recovering Yanyun.' Keep watching that Jurchen crew, and I will investigate them quietly from my end."
[ZH] 乔梦容仔细回想淑真在她面前与哥哥相处的过程，怎么想都觉得可能性极小，她寻找出说服自己的理由：“也许淑真正好也去永泰茶楼，即使她与哥哥相约，或许是为劝说哥哥，毕竟我昨日找过她，向她诉说哥哥心中有喜欢之人，淑真对待朋友真诚，肯定是想着帮我说服哥哥。”
[EN] Flushed with pride at Prince Yi's praise, Lin Jue held his tongue over the prince's sharp criticism of His Majesty—though in his own chest, he too resented how softly the court coddled the Jurchens, fearing disaster down the road.
[ZH] 喜兰认可道：“郡主如此说，也有些道理。”
[EN] Not until after Lin Jue took his leave did the servant who had shadowed Prince Yi return to Youlin Retreat.
[ZH] 乔梦容已坐不住，她起身，放快脚步，她要面见哥哥，亲自问个明白。
[EN] Inside her room, Qiao Mengrong had been pacing on pins and needles since her brother's cold departure, spinning darker and darker scenarios until dusk fell and Xilan brought word he was home. She had started out to confront him, only to pause when Xilan reported Division Commander Lin was inside with him. Just as she rose to go over, the tailing servant returned. Sitting back down, she demanded at once, "What did you see? Where did Brother go?"
[ZH] 当她进入正院，哥哥在院子里练习拳法，身形矫健，动作有力。
[EN] Dropping to one knee, the servant reported, "I tailed His Highness straight to Yongtai Teahouse by West Lake..."
[ZH] 乔梦容早已从心底承认他不是以前的哥哥，除这副皮囊哪里都不像。可她不愿承认以前的哥哥离他而去，承认了她的一腔痴情又去找谁依附？
[EN] Yongtai Teahouse—at least it was a scholars' haunt, not a brothel! Wait: did that mean Brother's beloved was a literary beauty? Her chest tightened right back up. "What else did you see?"
[ZH] “哥……哥……”
[EN] "I hid outside watching the door. Right around dusk, I saw Youqi Jushi walk out—and moments later, His Highness walked out too. Your Highness, could Prince Yi have been taking tea with Youqi Jushi?" Head bowed low, the servant dared not look up at her darkening face.
[ZH] 听到乔梦容略微颤抖的声音，申简辰收起拳法，转过身，借着院中的灯光，已看到梦容可怜而又忧愁的面庞，她双眼中噙着泪，饱含感情看着他。
[EN] Hearing Shuzhen had been at the teahouse—leaving right before her brother—sent a storm surging through Qiao Mengrong's chest. Could the woman Brother loved be Shuzhen?!
[ZH] 申简辰不敢再继续看下去，他将头扭向一边：“梦容，天色已晚，你要早些休息。”
[EN] Seeing her mistress frozen mute, Xilan prompted, "See anything else?"
[ZH] 乔梦容走近哥哥身边，很想抱住他。想到哥哥已说过不喜欢被她抱，便收回即将伸出的手：“哥哥是不是从永泰茶楼回来？”
[EN] "That is all I saw."
[ZH] 申简辰心中一惊，立刻明白梦容差人盯着她，既如此，也不必隐瞒：“是的。”
[EN] "Dismissed." Once the servant exited, Xilan murmured with caution, "Your Highness... could it really be Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] “可是去见淑真？”乔梦容充满担忧地追问着。
[EN] Replaying every moment Shuzhen and her brother had shared in front of her, Qiao Mengrong clung to doubt and grabbed at a lifeline. "Maybe Shuzhen just happened to be at Yongtai Teahouse! Or even if they arranged to meet, maybe she went to talk sense into Brother on my behalf! I went crying to her yesterday, and Shuzhen is so loyal to her friends—she must have been trying to persuade him for me!"
[ZH] “没错，我是去见幽栖居士。”
[EN] "Put like that, it does make sense," Xilan agreed.
[ZH] 申简辰大胆说出，也大胆看向梦容，泪水从她的眼中涌出，她的嘴角正在微微颤抖。
[EN] Unable to sit still another second, Qiao Mengrong rose and hurried toward the main courtyard to ask her brother straight to his face.
[ZH] “难道……难道……难道哥哥喜欢的人是……是淑真？”
[EN] Stepping through the gate, she saw him practicing fist forms in the lamplit yard—lithe, crisp, brimming with power. Deep down, she already knew he was not her old brother—aside from the body, nothing matched—yet admitting her old brother was gone forever left her whole heart's devotion with nowhere to land.
[ZH] 申简辰很想承认，还未解决与梦容之间的婚事，仍需瞒着：“你既找过她，应该知道她不过是约我出去劝说我而已。可惜她说服人的水平有待提升，我的想法依旧未改变。”
[EN] "Bro... Brother..."
[ZH] 乔梦容紧张的心情略微轻松许多，她用丝帕擦去脸上的泪痕，声音也不再颤抖。
[EN] Hearing the tremor in her voice, Shen Jianchen halted his stance and turned. In the lantern light, her pale, tear-filled eyes gazed straight at him with raw pleading. Unable to bear meeting them, he turned his face away. "It is late, Mengrong. Get some rest."
[ZH] “我已想好，愿意接受哥哥心中有喜欢之人，等你我成亲后，哥哥把她接到身边，我会与她和谐相处，帮哥哥打理好后宅。哥哥不如告诉我她是谁，我好早些了解她的品性和喜好，提前与她处好关系。”
[EN] Stepping close, Qiao Mengrong nearly reached out to hug him, remembered his cold warning earlier, and pulled her hands back. "Did Brother just come back from Yongtai Teahouse?"
[ZH] 按说作为男人听到此话只会高兴，可谁让眼前的男人是申简辰，他心中只有千里寻，身边也只想有千里寻。
[EN] Startled—so she had put a tail on him—Shen Jianchen saw no point denying it. "Yes."
[ZH] “梦容，我会把你当亲妹妹看待，希望你也只将我当哥哥看待，这世上哪有哥哥娶妹妹的道理？你能不能放下我们的婚约……”
[EN] "To meet Shuzhen?" she pressed, voice taut with dread.
[ZH] 乔梦容已让到如此地步，哥哥仍不愿说出心中之人。不愿说倒也罢了，竟然劝说她放下婚约。乔梦容更加痛苦，心中生起恨意。因恨意，她反而显得平静许多。
[EN] "Yes. I went to meet Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] “我恐怕满足不了哥哥，官家赐婚，岂能说放就放？”
[EN] He said it straight out and met her eyes; tears spilled down her cheeks as her lips trembled. "Could it be... could the woman Brother loves be... Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “只要你答应放下，我会去汴梁，当面求官家取消婚约。”
[EN] Part of him longed to say yes, yet with their marriage decree unresolved, he kept Qianli Xun shielded. "Since you went to see her, you can guess why she called me out—only to lecture me on your behalf. Shame her powers of persuasion need work; my mind has not changed."
[ZH] 去年淑真在幽林别居住了一晚的第二日，哥哥明明说过会娶她，前日也刚说愿意遵循婚约，今日竟有取消婚约的想法，变化如此之快，肯定与他心中之人有关。
[EN] A rush of relief loosened the vice around Qiao Mengrong's heart. Dabbing her cheeks with her handkerchief, she steadied her voice. "I have thought it through, Brother. I accept that you have someone else in your heart. Once we marry, bring her into the manor—I will live in harmony with her and run your inner quarters well. Tell me who she is now so I can learn her tastes and get on good terms ahead of time."
[ZH] 这个不知名姓，与她竞争哥哥，又将哥哥拿捏得死死的人到底是谁？掘地三尺也要将她挖出来。
[EN] Any ordinary man would have cheered at such generosity—yet this was Shen Jianchen, whose heart and side had room for Qianli Xun alone. "Mengrong, I see you as my own little sister, and I want you to see me only as your brother. Where in this world does a brother marry his sister? Can you let go of our betrothal?"
[ZH] “普通人家的婚约想要取消尚且艰难，何况是官家赐婚？哥哥还是清醒些，不要做无谓的努力。”
[EN] Even after she bent this low, he still hid the woman's name—and dared ask her to give up their betrothal! Pain curdled into bitter hatred, and strangely, hatred brought ice-cold calm. "I fear I cannot grant Brother's wish. His Majesty decreed our marriage—how could anyone cast it aside?"
[ZH] 申简辰发现梦容虽然还噙着泪，面色却比刚来时平静许多。他以为梦容已接受他心中有喜欢之人的事实，他相信很快她会想通，愿意只把他当哥哥看待。
[EN] "If you agree to let go, I will ride to Bianliang and beg His Majesty face-to-face to cancel the decree."
[ZH] “只要你想通，愿意与我仅仅维持兄妹关系，其他的事情交给我。”申简辰上前像亲哥哥一样拉起梦容的手，“梦容，我会像亲哥哥一样疼你，爱你，照顾你。”
[EN] Last year, the morning after Shuzhen stayed overnight at Youlin Retreat, Brother had promised to marry her; just two days ago he had agreed to honor the decree—and now, straight after Yongtai Teahouse, he wanted to cancel it outright?! Whoever this faceless rival was, she had Brother wrapped tight around her finger! Mengrong swore she would dig three feet under the earth to drag her into the light.
[ZH] 乔梦容能感受到哥哥手掌的温度，可她并不满意，她要的爱人，而不是哥哥。她将手从哥哥宽大的手掌中抽出，行礼道：“我累了，想回房休息。”
[EN] "Canceling an ordinary betrothal is hard enough, let alone an imperial decree," she answered flatly. "Wake up, Brother, and stop wasting your breath."
[ZH] 未等哥哥说话，乔梦容以更快的速度离开。回到闺房，趴在床上哭起来。
[EN] Seeing her calm face despite the moisture in her eyes, Shen Jianchen mistook it for resignation and hoped she was coming around to staying brother and sister. Reaching out, he clasped her hands warmly like an elder brother. "Just make peace with staying brother and sister, Mengrong, and leave the rest to me. I will cherish, protect, and care for you like a true brother."
[ZH] 喜兰想安慰，已找不到合适的话语，王爷连取消婚约的话都已说出来，她还怎么安慰？
[EN] Warmth radiated from his broad palms, yet she wanted a lover, not a brother. Drawing her hands free, she dropped a crisp curtsy. "I am tired and going back to rest."
[ZH] 王爷实在无情，病着时需要郡主照顾，对她百依百顺；病好后，不仅忘记以前说过的话，还找出可笑的理由，说他不是以前的仪王。说出去谁会相信！
[EN] Without waiting for a reply, she strode swiftly out of the courtyard, reached her chambers, and threw herself sobbing onto her bed.
[ZH] 乔梦容哭够后，擦去脸上的泪水，将身子坐正：“喜兰，今日你亲眼看到也亲耳听到是哥哥对不起我，以后无论我做什么都不为过，是也不是？”
[EN] Xilan stood helpless—with His Highness talking about canceling the imperial betrothal itself, what comfort remained? How heartless a man could be! When he lay sick, he leaned on Her Highness and promised the moon; the second he got well, he tossed every vow aside with that ridiculous tale about not being the old Prince Yi—who on earth would buy that?!
[ZH] 喜兰点点头：“当然。”
[EN] Once her tears ran dry, Qiao Mengrong wiped her face and sat ramrod straight. "You saw and heard with your own eyes today, Xilan—Brother betrayed me first. From here on, whatever I do is justified. Am I right?"
[ZH] “好，目前我只需做两件事，一件是耐心等待婚礼提前的消息；一件是想方设法弄清楚哥哥心中喜欢的人到底是谁？掘地三尺也要把她挖出来，我要当面狠狠地教训她，让她离开哥哥。”
[EN] "Of course," Xilan nodded.
[ZH] “记下了，奴婢会替郡主安排妥当。”
[EN] "Good. Two tasks lie ahead of me now: first, wait for word advancing our wedding date; second, find out who holds Brother's heart—even if I have to dig three feet underground! I will confront her face-to-face and drive her clear of Brother!"
[ZH] 千里寻从永泰茶楼回到望月轩，仔细将她与申简辰说过的话回想一遍，自认为没有任何疏漏。她决定以后梦容的事不再插手，只怕越插手越乱。
[EN] "Understood. I will see it arranged."
[ZH] 还是专心忙碌写话本之事，不如今晚去趟赌坊。
[EN] Meanwhile, back at Wangyue Pavilion after Yongtai Teahouse, Qianli Xun replayed her talk with Shen Jianchen and judged she had left no loopholes. Done meddling in Mengrong's love life—the more she tangled in it, the worse it knotted. Time to dive back into her huaben—starting with that field trip to a gambling house tonight!
[ZH] “苦荞，去把红豆叫来，再把男装拿出来。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, fetch Hongdou and pull out my men's clothes."
[ZH] “小姐想现在去赌坊？会不会太晚？”
[EN] "Going to a gambling den right now, Miss? Isn't it too late?"
[ZH] “晚上去才有意思。”
[EN] "Nighttime is when the real action starts!"
[ZH] 苦荞拿出男装，再把红豆叫来。红豆与小姐换上男装后，像两个充满脂粉气的公子。
[EN] Fetching the disguises and calling Hongdou in, Kuqiao soon watched the pair emerge looking like two fresh-faced young dandies.
[ZH] “红豆，拿上一袋银子，你家小姐我根本不会赌博，到赌坊输完一袋银子，我们就撤。”
[EN] "Bring a pouch of silver, Hongdou," Qianli Xun instructed. "Your mistress knows zero about gambling—we will head home the moment we lose the whole bag."
[ZH] 红豆答应着，把银子装好。
[EN] "Got it!" Hongdou stowed the silver.
[ZH] 苦荞将小姐与红豆送上马车后，自语道：“又是忙退婚，又是写话本，又是劝说仪王，还要去赌坊，小姐这一天忙的，也不知道好好休息。早知写话本这么麻烦，写写诗词得了，偏偏在话本上极其用功夫，只有化雨快来时，才写几首诗词。”
[EN] After seeing the pair into their carriage, Kuqiao sighed aloud at the gate. "First fighting an engagement, then writing huaben, then lecturing Prince Yi, now off to a gambling den! Miss never sits still a minute! If writing huaben is this much fuss, why not stick to poetry—instead of only tossing off verses right before Huayu shows up?"
[ZH] 背后传来韦嬷嬷的声音：“你在嘀咕什么？”
[EN] "Muttering to yourself again?" Matron Wei's voice floated from behind.
[ZH] 苦荞转过身，陪着笑脸：“没嘀咕什么，嬷嬷怎么出来？”
[EN] Spinning around with a sheepish grin, Kuqiao answered, "Nothing, Matron! What brings you out?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷没有回答苦荞的问题，而是问：“这么晚，姑娘又跑去哪里？”
[EN] "Where is Miss sneaking off to at this hour?"
[ZH] 苦荞老实回答：“去赌坊。”
[EN] "A gambling house," Kuqiao confessed.
[ZH] “一个姑娘家怎么跑去那种地方？”韦嬷嬷语气中有些许责怪。
[EN] "A young lady going to a place like that?" Matron Wei frowned.
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“小姐不是为赌博去那种地方，她要写故事，故事里需写有关赌博的事儿，小姐不懂，只能去赌坊走一趟，这叫体验生活。”
[EN] "Not to gamble for real! She is writing stories that need a gambling scene, does not know how they run, so she went for 'field research.'"
[ZH] “就你理由多。别以为我不知道，姑娘偷偷写话本，全城人说的千里寻才子恐怕是姑娘。”
[EN] "Always an excuse with you. Think I did not already figure out Miss writes huaben on the sly—and that the famed writer Qianli Xun the whole city raves about is probably our young mistress?"
[ZH] “嬷嬷小心些，小姐不愿让人知道。”
[EN] "Hush, Matron! Miss does not want a soul knowing!"
[ZH] “就我们两个人，怕什么！”
[EN] "Nobody here but you and me—what is there to fear?"
[ZH] 苦荞警惕地向周围瞧瞧，确实只有她们二人，才搀起韦嬷嬷，边往里走边小声说：“什么都瞒不过您，夏果经常在小姐房中，她都不知道，嬷嬷又不贴身伺候，却什么都知道，真是神了。”
[EN] Scanning the empty courtyard before looping an arm through Matron Wei's, Kuqiao marveled in a whisper as they walked inside, "Nothing ever slips past your eyes! Xiaguo worked right in Miss's room and never guessed, yet you barely step inside and know everything—uncanny!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷只顾一路笑着。
[EN] Matron Wei only chuckled all the way down the walkway.

#CH 184
[ZH] 千里寻来到赌坊，吩咐车夫不用在外等待，将马车赶回去，然后带红豆一起走进赌坊。
[ZH] 里面挺大，有许多赌桌，每张桌子跟前围满人，大家玩得十分投入。
[ZH] 赌坊的伙计迎上来：“二位小爷看着面生，应该是第一次来吧？”
[ZH] 千里寻故意压粗嗓子：“问那么多干什么？赶紧给小爷介绍一下都有哪些玩法，小爷好选一个喜欢的玩玩儿。”
[ZH] 伙计说：“玩法多了去，有赌球、赌棋、掷骰子、牌九、斗鸡、斗蛐蛐，还有关扑，小爷想玩什么？”
[ZH] 千里寻甚是疑惑：“赌球？赌什么球？”
[ZH] 伙计解释道：“蹴鞠、马球、捶丸均可赌。”
[ZH] 千里寻向四周瞧瞧：“这里也没法蹴鞠，打马球之类，怎么赌？”
[ZH] 伙计继续解释：“这里当然没法赌球，您要是想玩儿，会有人专门带您前去。”
[ZH] “什么是关扑？”千里寻前来学习，对于不懂的必然要虚心请教。
[ZH] 伙计见对方连关扑都不知道，推测眼前的小爷涉世未深，必然是待宰的羔羊，劝道：“这位小爷，您连关扑都不知，还是回去吧，小心到时输得光屁股出去。”
[ZH] 千里寻带目的而来，还没了解清楚，怎能轻易回去？
[ZH] “哪有做买卖的把客人往出赶？赶紧的，给小爷我说说关扑怎么个玩法。”
[ZH] 不等伙计开口，红豆说：“公子，我知道关扑怎么玩儿。”
[ZH] 千里寻露出笑容，对伙计说：“你还是别说了，听我身边的人怎么说。”
[ZH] 伙计很不屑：“哼，不用听，我就知道他会说什么。”
[ZH] 红豆仔细介绍：“逛街的时候常常会看到，街边卖小玩意儿的，既可用银子买，还可与卖主商量好价钱，再商量好条件，然后拿几枚铜钱放进碗里摇，若卖家赢，银子都归他；若买家赢，免费将小玩意儿拿走。”
[ZH] “哦，原来这就叫关扑。”千里寻看向伙计，“难道你们赌坊也卖小玩意儿？”
[ZH] 伙计见此时无新客到来，耐心解释：“那位小爷只在街边玩过关扑，却不知我们赌坊玩关扑都是玩大的，赌资既可是人，也可是房屋、田产、铺子，甚至所有家财，有些人一晚上从这里出去就成富翁，有些人则一晚上输尽所有。”
[ZH] 千里寻心想关扑还真不能玩儿，她瞅向旁边，见有人掷骰子，简单好学，不如玩玩儿。便不再理会伙计，拉起红豆挤到桌子跟前。
[ZH] 只见一人拿着木盒，里面放着骰子，在大家面前晃来晃去，玩的人仅需买大买小而已，若与坐庄之人猜中便赢，猜不中便输。
[ZH] 千里寻让红豆拿出银子买几回大小，她买大，人家是小，她买小，人家又是大，看来她确实没有赌博的命。
[ZH] 接着又到推牌九处看一回，推牌九稍微复杂些，她看半天没学明白，觉得无趣，招手叫来伙计。
[ZH] “两位小爷有什么吩咐？”伙计倒客气。
[ZH] 千里寻问：“斗蛐蛐的在哪里？”
[ZH] “两位小爷跟我来。”
[ZH] 伙计在前面带路，千里寻和红豆跟上。
[EN] Arriving at the gambling house, Qianli Xun sent the carriage home rather than making the driver wait, then stepped inside with Hongdou. The cavernous hall buzzed with packed gaming tables and shouting bettors.
[ZH] 伙计推开赌坊的后门，来到一处院落，全是人，有斗蛐蛐的，也有斗鸡的。虽是晚上，却灯火通明，并不影响赌博人的兴致。
[EN] An attendant hurried over. "Two new faces—first time here, young masters?"
[ZH] 谢过伙计后，两人先凑到斗蛐蛐的人群中，看到两只蛐蛐在蟋蟀碗中互相撕咬，斗得不可开交，千里寻押一回，结果还是输了。
[EN] Pitching her voice low, Qianli Xun barked, "Less nosiness, more rundown! Tell this young master what games you run so I can pick one."
[ZH] 她与红豆挤出人群，打算去玩斗鸡。无意中瞧见陈浩宇带着小弟在斗鸡处，玩得不亦乐乎。顿时失去兴致，更不愿被陈浩宇认出。再说该了解的已了解清楚，写话本尽够，也是该回去的时候。
[EN] "Plenty of choices: ball wagers, chess wagers, dice, pai gow, cockfighting, cricket fighting, and guanpu! What strikes your fancy?"
[ZH] “我们回去吧。”
[EN] "Ball wagers? What balls?" Qianli Xun asked.
[ZH] 千里寻带红豆走出赌坊，天色比来时更晚些。没有留马车，二人步行回家。她本就这么打算，想逛逛夜晚的街市，大宋没有宵禁制度，夜晚的街市也是极其繁华。
[EN] "Cuju, polo, chuiwan—bet on any of them."
[ZH] 她无心留意街头的小吃和好看的玩意儿，看到陈浩宇，不由自主想到婚约。想到婚约，又不由自主想到申简辰。
[EN] Glancing around the crowded hall, she asked, "You can hardly play cuju or polo in here—how do you bet?"
[ZH] 他简直是疯了，竟然想到训练猪爬树。若真的训练成功，难道要答应嫁给他？
[EN] "Not right here, of course—want to wager, and someone escorts you straight to the pitch."
[ZH] 千里寻发现她竟然渐渐地不再去想文渊，而文渊给她留下的情伤也正在一点一点消退。
[EN] "And what is guanpu?" Here to learn, she asked freely.
[ZH] 她从心底感谢申简辰，尽管他们不可能在一起，他却用穿越前的奋不顾身和穿越后的执着慢慢住进她的内心，如同一剂良药，逐渐平复她内心的伤口。
[EN] Spotting a greenhorn who had never even heard of guanpu—a lamb begging to be sheared—the attendant actually warned her: "Young master, if you do not even know guanpu, better head home before you lose your trousers."
[ZH] 理智告诉她想仪王是不对的，可偷偷摸摸想一下应该不会伤害梦容。她敞开心扉，回忆她与申简辰的过往，一边走，一边露出让人捉摸不透的笑容。
[EN] "Since when does a shop chase paying customers out? Quit stalling and explain guanpu!"
[ZH] 红豆跟在小姐身边，见小姐名义上是逛街，却从不看街上的人和物件，仿佛沉浸在另外一个世界，脸上还时不时浮现笑容。
[EN] Before the attendant could speak, Hongdou chimed in. "Young Master, I know how guanpu works."
[ZH] 正当千里寻沉浸在个人的世界时，被一阵喊声拉回现实。
[EN] Qianli Xun grinned at the attendant. "Zip it—let my boy here explain."
[ZH] “人往那里跑！快追！”
[EN] The attendant sniffed. "Hmph, I already know what he is going to say."
[ZH] 千里寻不再沉迷于个人世界，借着夜晚街市的灯光，视线穿过人群，发现有个人影朝一旁的巷子跑去，人影看上去已受伤，行动起来有些艰难。
[EN] Hongdou explained clearly: "You see it all the time on market streets—peddlers selling trinkets let you pay cash OR agree on a price and terms, then shake copper coins in a bowl. If the vendor wins, he keeps your silver; if the buyer wins, you walk off with the goods for free."
[ZH] 快到巷口时，那人捂住伤口，紧张地向后瞧。
[EN] "Aha, so that is guanpu!" Qianli Xun glanced back at the attendant. "Don't tell me your gambling house hawks trinkets too?"
[ZH] 这一瞧不要紧，千里寻觉得他很是面熟，仔细一想，原来是曾在街上遇到的“延迟男”。心想：“当初那个张狂劲儿，现在怂了吧！”正想着，颜池已进入巷子，不见人影。
[EN] With no new patrons walking in, the attendant explained with patience. "Your lad only knows street-side guanpu. Inside our hall, we play guanpu for real stakes—people, houses, fields, shops, even whole family fortunes! Some walk out overnight tycoons, and others lose the shirt off their backs in one throw."
[ZH] 接着看到七八个人手执利刃，向巷口冲将而来。为首的公子长得浓眉大眼，相貌周正。刚才的声音应该是这伙人喊出，显然他们正在追“延迟男”。
[EN] Note to self: steer clear of guanpu! Spotting a lively dice table nearby—easy rules—she ditched the attendant and squeezed up to the rail with Hongdou. A dealer rattled dice inside a wooden box while players bet high or low. Having Hongdou drop silver a few rounds, Qianli Xun bet high when the dice came up low and low when they came up high—zero gambler's luck in her bones.
[ZH] 因好奇心使然，千里寻对红豆说：“跟上去瞧瞧。”
[EN] Next they watched pai gow for a spell, yet the tiles looked too fussy to crack on the fly, so she waved the attendant over again. "Where are the cricket fights?"
[ZH] 待七八个人追进巷子，千里寻带红豆放轻脚步尾随而去。
[EN] "Follow me, young masters."
[ZH] 别看街市人多，巷子里却乌漆墨黑，而且拐来拐去，很容易将人绕晕，那伙人绕来绕去，找不到人影，只能放弃。
[EN] Pushing open a rear door, the attendant led them into a brightly lit courtyard packed with bettors clustered around cricket bowls and cockfighting pits. Thanking him, they squeezed into the cricket ring, watched two crickets lock jaws in an earthenware bowl, dropped another bet—and lost again.
[ZH] 为首的说：“今日放他一马，再来邵家镖局，让他有去无回。撤！”
[EN] Working free of the crowd to check out the cockfights, Qianli Xun froze: Chen Haoyu stood right across the pit cheering with his lackeys! Her interest died on the spot, along with any desire to be recognized. She already had more than enough material for her huaben anyway. "Time to head home."
[ZH] 邵家镖局的人要原路返回，千里寻与红豆不想被对方看到，小心躲起来。待人走远后，没有什么热闹可瞧，她准备带红豆原路返回。
[EN] Stepping out into the deeper night, they set off on foot; without curfew in the Song capital and prefectures, Lin'an's night markets glittered with life. Yet Qianli Xun paid zero mind to snacks or trinkets. Seeing Chen Haoyu had dragged back thoughts of the betrothal—which bounced straight to Shen Jianchen. The guy really was nuts, training a pig to climb a tree! What if it actually climbed one—would she have to marry him?
[ZH] 说是原路返回，可在黑漆漆的巷子里瞎撞，怎么都走不出去。
[EN] With a jolt, she noticed Wenyuan barely crossed her mind anymore, the old heartbreak fading bit by bit. In her heart she thanked Shen Jianchen: impossible as their future was, his fearless dash through the earthquake and stubborn devotion here had slipped quietly into her chest like medicine soothing an old wound. Reason told her thinking of Prince Yi was wrong, yet sneaking a quiet memory hurt Mengrong none. Letting her guard drop, she drifted through memories of him, a faint, unreadable smile playing across her lips.
[ZH] “红豆，你家小姐我方向感不好，难道你也是？”
[EN] Walking beside her, Hongdou watched her mistress ignore every lantern and stall as if wandering another world, smiling at thin air.
[ZH] “我对这里不熟悉，跟着小姐进来时，一心都在那伙人身上，忘了记路。”
[EN] A harsh shout ripped Qianli Xun back to the street: "He ran that way! After him!"
[ZH] 千里寻很无奈，指着一个方向：“试试那边。”
[EN] Through the lamplit crowd, she saw a limping figure clutch a wound and duck into a side alley, shooting a panicked glance over his shoulder right at the mouth of the lane. Wait a second—that face! The "Delayed" guy from the market! So much for all his cocky swagger—running scared now! Moments after Yan Chi vanished into the dark, seven or eight men brandishing sharp blades charged past, led by a square-jawed young man with thick brows.
[ZH] 两人向另一个巷子拐去。
[EN] Curiosity winning out, Qianli Xun nudged Hongdou. "Let us tail them for a look."
[ZH] 走着走着，千里寻感觉有一只手拽住她的脚。要搁别的女子早已吓得魂飞魄散，她从小胆大，加之拳法高超，什么都吓不到她。
[EN] Silently shadowing the blades into the pitch-black alley, they watched the pursuers wind through twist after twist until the quarry vanished clean away.
[ZH] 只见她迅速转身，通过感觉判断那人的方向，一拳打去……
[EN] "Let him off easy tonight!" the leader barked. "Come near Shao Escort Agency again and he will never walk out alive! Pull back!"
[ZH] 拽住她的手很快松开，只听：“哎哟！原来……是你呀，叫……叫什么来着，对，朱……淑真。”
[EN] Ducking into shadow while the Shao men marched back out, Qianli Xun waited for quiet before turning to retrace their steps—only to hit dead end after twisting fork in the dark.
[ZH] 黑暗中竟然有人知道她的名字，看来对方了解她的拳法，千里寻更是好奇：“红豆，把火折子拿出来，让我看清他是谁。”
[EN] "Hongdou, your mistress has zero sense of direction—don't tell me you do too?"
[ZH] 红豆将火折子打开，吹出火苗，递给小姐。
[EN] "I do not know this maze either, Miss. Kept my eyes glued on those men and forgot the turns."
[ZH] 千里寻拿着火折子，蹲下，借着火光看清对方正是“延迟男”：“原来是你，你倒机灵躲过那帮人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sighed and pointed at random. "Try down there."
[ZH] 颜池说：“我受……重伤，能不能……看在我们认识，帮……帮我？”
[EN] Halfway down the next pitch-black turn, a hand shot out and grabbed her ankle! Any other lady would have screamed bloody murder; bold by nature and sharp with her fists, Qianli Xun whipped around, judged the target by feel, and fired a punch straight down.
[ZH] 千里寻“哼”一声：“你是好是坏，我还不清楚，为何要帮你？”
[EN] The grip dropped instantly, followed by a groan: "Ow! So... it is you... what was it again? Right—Zhu... Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 颜池从怀里掏出珠簪，递到千里寻面前：“你……你看，这个……这个……我还留着。”
[EN] Someone in the pitch dark knew her name—and recognized her fist?! "Hongdou, strike your fire-starter so I can see who this is!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞧一眼珠簪，很是疑惑：“一枚男子用的普通珠簪而已，跟我有什么关系？”
[EN] Blowing a flame alive on the tinder tube, Hongdou handed it over. Crouching down, Qianli Xun held the glow to a pale face—the "Delayed" guy himself! "Well, well—pretty slick dodging that mob."
[ZH] 颜池忍着伤痛说：“第一次……遇见……你时，你掉下的，我……我一直保留着。”
[EN] "I took... a bad cut..." Yan Chi panted. "For old acquaintance's sake... give me... a hand?"
[ZH] 千里寻想起在街上相遇对打的场景，那时她女扮男装，头发散落，掉下这枚珠簪。她并未当回事，对方却偷偷珍藏起来。千里寻甚是气愤，一把夺过：“既是我的东西，没必要让你留着。”
[EN] "Hmph! For all I know you are a crook—why should I help you?"
[ZH] 转而递给红豆：“送给你。”
[EN] Reaching inside his robe, Yan Chi held up a beaded hair-pin. "Look... I still... kept this..."
[ZH] 红豆看出小姐根本不想陌生的男子对她有念想，爽快接过：“谢小姐。”
[EN] Qianli Xun blinked. "Just a plain man's hair-pin—what is that to me?"
[ZH] “你啊，自求多福。”千里寻吹灭火折子，起身准备离开。
[EN] "The first time... we met... you dropped it... I kept it safe..."
[ZH] “没想到……你竟……见死不救，看来……那日在街头……姑娘帮人……不过是……是假装善良而已……”
[EN] Flashback to their street brawl when her disguised hair had come undone and lost that pin—she had forgotten it on the spot, yet this stranger had hoarded it?! Annoyed, she snatched it right back. "Since it is mine, no reason for you to hold onto it!" Tossing it to Hongdou, she added, "Yours now."
[ZH] 颜池这话，还真的击中千里寻的要害：“你既拿话噎我，我就帮你一回，把你送到医馆。”
[EN] Seeing her mistress wanted zero strange men harboring notions, Hongdou pocketed it crisply. "Thank you, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻正要吩咐红杏去扶颜池，旁边一户人家的门被打开。
[EN] "Fend for yourself!" Blowing out the flame, Qianli Xun rose to leave.
[ZH] 借着院内的灯光，千里寻一眼认出开门的人正是与她在竹林比试的高手。不如将大麻烦送给他，自己落得轻松。
[EN] "Never thought... you would leave a man to bleed out..." Yan Chi wheezed. "Guess helping folks on the street that day... was all just fake kindness..."
[ZH] “徒弟，师傅在此，为何不来跪拜！”
[EN] The barb hit square on Qianli Xun's pride. "Since you are playing the guilt card, fine—I will drop you at a clinic!"
[ZH] 小姐突然说这话，红豆甚是诧异。
[EN] Just as she turned to have Hongdou hoist Yan Chi up, a wooden courtyard door creaked open right beside them. Spilling lamplight lit up the man stepping out—the martial master who had dueled her in the bamboo grove! Jackpot! Dump the headache straight onto him!
[ZH] 颜池也不明所以：“你……到底帮不帮……我？”
[EN] "Disciple! Your master stands right here—why aren't you kneeling?!"
[ZH] 陆远峰边关门，边朝传来声音的方向看去，黑暗中有两个人影。他关好门后，大声问：“你是在和我说话吗？”
[EN] Hongdou stared in bewilderment at her mistress's sudden line, while Yan Chi groaned, "Are you... helping me... or not?"
[ZH] 千里寻打趣道：“在竹林时，你跪在我面前，强行认我当师傅，难道忘了？”
[EN] Shutting his gate and squinting into the dark, Lu Yuanfeng called out, "Talking to me?"
[ZH] 陆远峰明白过来，走上前：“师傅，真的是你？”
[EN] "Back in the bamboo grove, you dropped to your knees begging me to be your master—forgot already?" Qianli Xun teased.
[ZH] “如假包换，只不过我今日为出行方面，穿上男装而已。”千里寻又问，“那是你家？”
[EN] Recognition lit up Lu Yuanfeng's face as he hurried forward. "Master?! Really you?!"
[ZH] 陆远峰答道：“是，师傅进去坐坐？”
[EN] "In the flesh—just wearing men's clothes for getting around. That your house?"
[ZH] “不了。”千里寻哪有心情到别人家里坐，眼前的大麻烦得赶紧找人解决，“你当初认我做师傅，我还没答应，今日有缘遇见，不妨收下你这位徒弟……”
[EN] "Yes! Care to come inside for a seat, Master?"
[ZH] 陆远峰立刻跪在地上，激动地说：“师傅在上，请受徒弟一拜。”
[EN] "Pass!" No time for tea with a bleeding man at her feet. "Back then I had not accepted you yet. Since fate threw us together tonight, I will officially take you as my disciple..."
[ZH] 千里寻上前扶起陆远峰：“免礼免礼，既然我已将你收下，得送给你个见面礼，不过这见面礼有些麻烦，不知道你愿不愿意收？”
[EN] Lu Yuanfeng dropped straight to his knees in excitement. "Master above, accept your disciple's bow!"
[ZH] “师傅都发话，再麻烦，我也得收。”陆远峰倒豪爽。
[EN] Pulling him up, Qianli Xun grinned. "At ease, at ease. Since you are officially my disciple, you get a welcome gift—a bit of a hassle, though. Willing to accept?"
[ZH] 新收的徒弟态度不错，千里寻甚是满意：“有个受伤的人，你把他带到你家里，找个大夫帮他治伤。”
[EN] "When Master gives the word, hassle or not, I take it!" Lu Yuanfeng declared heartily.
[ZH] 陆远峰毫不犹豫，立即答应，又向师傅请示：“师傅，我能不能先把人扶进屋里，等办完事再给他请大夫。”
[EN] "There is an injured man right here. Carry him inside your house and fetch a doctor to patch him up."
[ZH] 千里寻随口问道：“你有事？”
[EN] Agreeing without hesitation, Lu Yuanfeng paused to ask, "Master, may I lay him inside first, run a quick errand, and fetch the doctor right after?"
[ZH] 陆远峰照实说：“陈公子雇我保护他，今晚他去赌坊，也要求我跟着，正要去，没想到一出来遇见师傅。”
[EN] "Got an errand?"
[ZH] “陈浩宇？”
[EN] "Young Master Chen hired me as his bodyguard. He went to the gambling house tonight and expects me there. I was just heading out when I bumped into Master."
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] “不用去了，我刚从赌坊出来，见过他，好得不行。”
[EN] "Yes."
[ZH] “我收过他的银子，不去不太好。”
[EN] "Skip it. I just walked out of that gambling house and saw him—healthy as a horse."
[ZH] “你是听师傅的，还是听陈浩宇的？”
[EN] "I took his silver, though—bad form not to show."
[ZH] “当然听师傅的，可也得赚银子。”
[EN] "Do you listen to your master, or to Chen Haoyu?"
[ZH] “既然听师傅的，就别去保护陈浩宇，他要怪你白嫖银子，让他来找我。”
[EN] "Master, of course—still got to earn my pay."
[ZH] “有师傅这话，我白拿银子也心安理得。”
[EN] "Listen to Master and ditch Chen Haoyu. If he gripes you pocketed silver for nothing, tell him to take it up with me!"
[ZH] 安排妥当后，千里寻为自己能顺利甩掉大麻烦感到满意：“喂，听到没？我徒弟愿意帮你。”
[EN] "With Master's backing, I will pocket his silver with a clear conscience!"
[ZH] 颜池说：“我不叫‘喂’，我叫……颜池。”
[EN] Thrilled to offload her problem so cleanly, Qianli Xun nudged the wounded man. "Hey, hear that? My disciple will patch you up."
[ZH] “延迟嘛，谁不知道？就是不守时呗！”
[EN] "My name is not 'Hey'..." Yan Chi muttered. "It is... Yan Chi."
[ZH] “是……颜色的颜，池水的……池，姑娘……可记清楚了？”
[EN] "'Delayed,' yeah, everybody knows—never on time!"
[ZH] 千里寻并未理会颜池，对陆远峰说：“徒弟，把他扶进你屋里，再把师傅带出这曲里拐弯的L巷子。”
[EN] "It is 'Yan' (颜, as in color) and 'Chi' (池, as in pond)... got it straight, Miss?"
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] Ignoring him, Qianli Xun told Lu Yuanfeng, "Haul him inside, Disciple, then lead your master out of this twisting maze of an alley."
[ZH] 陆远峰将颜池扶起来，再扶进屋里。出来后，将千里寻与红豆带出黑漆漆的巷子，来到大街上，与师傅分别后去找大夫。
[EN] "Right away!"
[ZH] 陆远峰走后，天色更晚，千里寻不想在街上逗留，租辆马车回望月轩。
[EN] Carrying Yan Chi indoors, Lu Yuanfeng came right back out, guided Qianli Xun and Hongdou back onto the main thoroughfare, and bade farewell to fetch a physician. With the hour late, Qianli Xun hailed a hired carriage straight back to Wangyue Pavilion.

#CH 185
[ZH] 陈浩宇正在赌坊玩斗鸡，下的赌注有赢有输，见陆远峰迟迟未到，心有埋怨，却无可奈何。担心遇到蒋爷寻事，便不想再玩下去，对身边的小弟说：“你们玩儿，本公子困了，回去休息。”
[ZH] 疤子拦道：“才玩儿一会儿，陈公子就要走，多没意思。”
[ZH] “去去去！少拦本公子。”陈浩宇推开疤子，进入屋内。
[ZH] 赌坊的伙计迎上来：“陈公子，您是想玩牌九还是掷骰子？”
[ZH] “本公子累了，准备回去。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇边说边往外走，还未走到门口，只见赌坊的门被推开，真是冤家路窄，蒋爷带人出现在面前。
[ZH] “怎么？陈公子这么快就要走？”
[ZH] 陆远峰还未到，陈浩宇不敢太强势，露出笑脸：“是蒋爷来得晚，我有事，先行一步。”
[ZH] 蒋爷身后的两名壮汉立刻上前堵住陈浩宇，一脸凶神恶煞的样子。
[EN] Back in the gambling house's rear yard, Chen Haoyu had won a few cockfights and lost a few, grumbling over Lu Yuanfeng's no-show. Nervous about bumping into Master Jiang unprotected, he called it a night. "Keep playing, boys—I am beat and heading home."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续笑道：“蒋爷，快让你身边的人闪开，别挡我的道。”
[EN] Scarface stepped in front of him. "Barely been here a spell, Young Master Chen—leaving so soon spoils the fun!"
[ZH] 蒋爷一挥手，两名壮汉闪到一边。陈浩宇想走，却被蒋爷挡住：“陈浩宇，今儿你我之间做个了断，如何？”
[EN] "Move it! Quit blocking my way!" Pushing Scarface aside, Chen Haoyu stepped back into the main hall.
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作若无其事的样子：“了断？什么了断？”
[EN] An attendant stepped up. "Pai gow or dice next, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] “陈公子真是贵人多忘事，那我就提醒提醒你，爷爷我还是放不下春金沫，今晚就以她为赌注，你若赢了，我以后离春金沫远远的，你若输了，她就是我的人，你以后连她一根手指都不许碰。”
[EN] "Done for the night. Heading home."
[ZH] 蒋爷的提议，陈浩宇觉得尚可，他也不想与蒋爷再耗下去：“好！我同意！不过不能以春金沫为赌注，她毕竟是人。”
[EN] Before he reached the front doors, they swung wide—talk about bad luck! Master Jiang strode in flanked by his gang.
[ZH] 陈浩宇的话正中蒋爷的心思：“陈公子倒是位有情有义之人，不用春金沫做赌注也可以。考虑到春金沫在爷爷心中的地位，能够替换她的赌注不能太差，否则爷爷可不认账。”
[EN] "Leaving so early, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇十分爽快：“那就由蒋爷开个价。”
[EN] With no Lu Yuanfeng in sight, Chen Haoyu forced a smooth grin. "Master Jiang arrived late—I have business and must run."
[ZH] 蒋爷一挥手，他身后的人立刻把赌坊的门关上，其他人都堵在门口，不允许任何人出去。赌徒们也无心再赌博，一个个都伸长脖子看热闹。
[EN] Two burly thugs stepped forward to block the exit, glaring murder.
[ZH] 只听蒋爷说：“让我开价，你可别后悔。”
[EN] Keeping his grin pinned on, Chen Haoyu said, "Tell your boys to step aside, Master Jiang—do not block my road."
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得不就是给钱吗？陈家有的是钱，还怕满足不了蒋爷：“蒋爷尽管开口，别犹豫。”
[EN] With a wave, Master Jiang parted them—only to block Chen Haoyu himself. "Let us settle things between us once and for all tonight, Chen Haoyu. Deal?"
[ZH] “好！陈公子就是爽快！我可说了。”见陈浩宇中计，蒋爷更是开心，“若用陈家家产做赌注，爷爷我以后绝不再惦记春金沫。”
[EN] Playing dumb, Chen Haoyu blinked. "Settle what?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇带的小弟此时也凑过来，听到蒋爷所说，一人小声议论：“蒋爷的胃口大得离谱，陈家富甲一方，也得吃得下才行，陈公子肯定不会答应。”
[EN] "Short memory for a rich young master! Let me jog it: I still cannot get Chun Jinmo out of my head. Put her on the table tonight—you win, I steer clear of Chun Jinmo forever; you lose, she is mine and you never lay a finger on her again."
[ZH] 疤子小声回道：“看着吧，好戏还在后头。”
[EN] Fair enough, Chen Haoyu thought—he was sick of dodging Master Jiang too. "Deal! Only no betting Chun Jinmo herself—she is a living person, not chips."
[ZH] 陈浩宇果然不同意：“不行！怎能随便拿我们陈家的家产做赌注？还是换一个。”
[EN] Walking straight into Master Jiang's script! "Spoken like a man of honor! Fine—we leave Chun Jinmo off the slip. Still, given how high she ranks in my heart, whatever replaces her cannot be cheap, or no deal."
[ZH] 蒋爷给身边两名手下使了使眼色，两名手下立刻冲上去将陈浩宇拿住，其中一名拔出刀架在陈浩宇的脖子上。
[EN] "Name your stake, Master Jiang!" Chen Haoyu agreed breezily.
[ZH] 陈浩宇被控制，脖子向一边闪去，躲开明晃晃的刀刃：“有话好好说，别动刀动枪的。待会儿我身边的高手来了，小心你们吃不了兜着走。”
[EN] At Master Jiang's flick of a wrist, his gang slammed the gambling house doors shut and blocked every exit. Gamblers dropped their dice and craned their necks for the show.
[ZH] 蒋爷指着赌坊的伙计：“去，到外面挂上休息的牌子，高手看到，肯定会原路返回。赌坊接下来的损失都由陈公子赔偿。”
[EN] "Let me name the stake, and do not cry foul later," Master Jiang warned.
[ZH] 伙计立刻答道：“是，东家。”
[EN] Just silver—the Chens swam in silver! "Spit it out already!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才知道原来这家赌坊是黑帮的产业，后悔只顾玩，没把赌坊背后的人了解清楚。
[EN] "Bold! I like it! Then hear my terms:" Grinning as the trap snapped shut, Master Jiang declared, "Wager the Chen family fortune—and I swear off Chun Jinmo forever!"
[ZH] 伙计去门外将休息的牌子挂好，进入赌坊内回道：“东家，已挂好。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's lackeys huddled close, one whispering, "Master Jiang's appetite is insane—even if he won the richest house in Lin'an, could he swallow it? Young Master Chen will never agree!"
[ZH] 蒋爷向周围的人说：“诸位，继续玩，我跟陈公子有些个人恩怨需解决，不会影响大家。”
[EN] "Just watch," Scarface murmured back. "The good part is coming."
[ZH] 大家比刚才轻松一些，还是没有人继续，谁让看热闹更有意思。
[EN] Sure enough, Chen Haoyu barked, "No way! How could I bet the whole Chen family fortune?! Pick something else!"
[ZH] 蒋爷并不去管众人，靠近陈浩宇：“陈家的家产与春金沫请陈公子任选其一。”
[EN] Master Jiang flicked his eyes; two thugs grabbed Chen Haoyu on the spot, and one pressed a gleaming broadsword flat against his neck.
[ZH] 陈浩宇谈不上多喜欢春金沫，也觉得与陈家的家业比起来春金沫显然逊色许多，可她是目前他接触过的女子中最满意的一位，重要的是自己的女人被别人的男人强行抢走，有失颜面，加之春金沫曾说过对他死心塌地，若陈浩宇把她让给蒋爷，她会一头撞死。
[EN] Pulling his chin clear of the cold steel, Chen Haoyu yelled, "Talk like civilized men—put the blade away! When my bodyguard gets here, you will regret this!"
[ZH] 于是陈浩宇一口回绝：“不选！”
[EN] Master Jiang pointed at the house attendant. "Go hang the 'Closed' sign outside. When his bodyguard sees it, he will turn straight around. Charge tonight's lost business to Young Master Chen's tab!"
[ZH] 大刀已靠近他的脖子，拉出一道血印。
[EN] "Yes, Proprietor!" the attendant obeyed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇感到脖子处一阵刺痛，他的嘴角轻轻咧开，脑中进行激烈的斗争，到底该选哪个？
[EN] Only now did it hit Chen Haoyu that this whole gambling house belonged to the Qiantang Gang—cursed himself for playing blind without checking who owned the roof!
[ZH] 蒋爷说：“陈公子若不愿用陈家的家产做赌注，那就还用春金沫，若我赢，陈公子以后不许再找她。也是，一个商妓怎么比得上你们陈家的家产重要。”
[EN] Once the attendant returned reporting the sign hung, Master Jiang called to the room, "Keep playing, folks! Just a private matter between Young Master Chen and me—do not let us spoil your evening." Nobody went back to their tables; watching a rich heir get squeezed beat dice hands down.
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得春金沫很可怜，无人能帮，若以她为赌注，输了，必然被蒋爷糟蹋，估计她连死的心都有。而陈家的家产多到他陈浩宇都不清楚有多少，蒋爷若赢，未必吃得下。再说有陈家长辈在，蒋爷想得手恐怕不容易。况且输赢未定，保不准赢的人是他陈浩宇。
[EN] Stepping right into Chen Haoyu's face, Master Jiang murmured, "The Chen fortune, or Chun Jinmo—pick one."
[ZH] 想好后，开口应道：“我同意！”
[EN] Chun Jinmo was nowhere near worth the Chen family empire, yet she was the one woman who suited him, handing his own girl over to another man would humiliate him across Lin'an, and Chun Jinmo had sworn she would smash her head against a wall rather than fall into Master Jiang's clutches.
[ZH] 蒋爷问：“同意哪个？”
[EN] "Neither!" Chen Haoyu snapped.
[ZH] “用……用陈家的家产做赌注。”
[EN] The broadsword nudged closer, slicing a thin red line across his skin.
[ZH] 看热闹的人都唏嘘不已，有人小声议论：“陈公子真不愧是败家子。”
[EN] Stinging pain flashed across his neck as his mind raced in panic.
[ZH] 蒋爷听到陈浩宇的选择，心中吁口气，总算选到他心里：“好！陈公子为女人愿意以陈家家产做赌注，令蒋某开了眼，诸位也都做个见证。”
[EN] "Unwilling to bet the Chen fortune?" Master Jiang cooed. "Then stick with Chun Jinmo—I win, and you never visit her again. Right enough—what is one brothel girl next to a family empire?"
[ZH] 很快陈浩宇后悔刚才说过的话，想收回为时已晚。
[EN] Poor Chun Jinmo had no one to protect her—bet her and lose, and Master Jiang would break her. Meanwhile the Chen estate was so vast even Chen Haoyu could not tally it all; could a street boss actually collect with all the Chen elders standing guard? And besides—who said he would lose?!
[ZH] 蒋爷走到赌桌前，陈浩宇也被带到蒋爷对面。拿刀的人收起大刀，依然守在他身旁。
[EN] Gritting his teeth, Chen Haoyu blurted, "I agree!"
[ZH] 赌坊的伙计拿来提前准备好的两张字据，放在陈浩宇面前：“陈公子，请您签字，再按下指印。”
[EN] "Agree to which?"
[ZH] 旁边已有人递上笔和印朱，陈浩宇总算是看出，对方早有准备，看来自己八成是跳进蔣爷挖的坑里。
[EN] "Wager... wager the Chen family fortune!"
[ZH] 他想反悔，准备逃走，给围观的小弟使眼色，让他们前来帮忙。疤子跟那些小弟一个个都摇头，不敢向前，像个缩头乌龟。
[EN] Gasps and whistles rippled across the hall. "True prodigal son!" someone muttered.
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才明白，关键时候，他们根本靠不住。他身边站着两名带刀的壮汉，门口也守着蒋爷的人，想逃没那么容易，陈浩宇只能自认倒霉。
[EN] Exhaling in quiet triumph—bull's-eye!—Master Jiang clapped. "Willing to stake a whole empire for a woman! You opened my eyes today, Young Master Chen. Everyone here, stand witness!"
[ZH] “蔣爷，还是先商量好条件。”
[EN] Regret slammed into Chen Haoyu two seconds later, far too late to swallow the words back.
[ZH] 蔣爷见陈浩宇不愿签字，不满道：“有什么好商量的？纸上都写着，你若输，陈家财产归我。”
[EN] Escorted across the gaming table from Master Jiang while the blade-wielding guard stood right behind his chair, Chen Haoyu stared as an attendant laid two pre-written contracts before him alongside brush and red ink paste. "Sign your name and press your thumbprint, Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇提出要求：“我想再加一条。”
[EN] Pre-written contracts?! He had walked blind into an ambush! Casting a frantic glance at his lackeys for help, he saw Scarface and the rest shaking their heads and shrinking back like turtles. Useless fair-weather parasites! Boxed in by armed thugs inside a locked den, he had zero escape.
[ZH] 蒋爷想听听他到底会提什么要求：“说！”
[EN] "Hold on—let us settle terms first!"
[ZH] “若我赢，以后蒋爷不准再欺负我，我被人欺负时，钱塘帮还得派人帮我。”
[EN] "What terms?" Master Jiang barked. "Written plain as day: you lose, Chen property is mine."
[ZH] 面对陈浩宇的要求，蔣爷没有任何犹豫：“好，我答应你。”
[EN] "I am adding one clause!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才提笔在两张字据上写下名字，并用食指沾了印朱，按下指印。
[EN] "Speak!"
[ZH] 伙计将字据收好：“东家，可以开始。”
[EN] "If I win, you never bully me again—and whenever anyone messes with me, the Qiantang Gang steps in to back me up!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又问：“蔣爷是要跟我推牌九还是掷骰子？”
[EN] Without blinking, Master Jiang nodded. "Done!"
[ZH] 蔣爷不想太浪费时间：“爷爷我喜欢快些赢你，我们还是玩关扑，一把定输赢。”
[EN] Dipping the brush, Chen Haoyu signed both slips and stamped his red thumbprint.
[ZH] “关扑就关扑，要九纯十纯。”陈浩宇觉得铜钱越多，蔣爷赢的可能性越小。
[EN] "Ready to begin, Proprietor," the attendant announced as he collected the contracts.
[ZH] 没想到蔣爷一口答应：“就按你说的来，我要是掷出九纯十纯，你小子可就输了。”
[EN] "Pai gow or dice?" Chen Haoyu asked.
[ZH] 蔣爷的爽快引起陈浩宇怀疑，当伙计将放有十枚铜钱的小木盒放在蔣爷面前时，他大喊：“慢着！总得让我与在场的诸位验一下里面的铜钱是否动过手脚。”
[EN] "Why waste all night? I prefer winning quick—guanpu, one throw takes all!"
[ZH] 蔣爷冷笑道：“陈公子竟然不信任我？既如此，让你查验一番。”
[EN] "Guanpu it is—and you need nine or ten of a kind!" More coins meant slimmer odds for Master Jiang.
[ZH] 蔣爷向伙计使使眼色，伙计心领神会，拿起盒子那一刻，神不知鬼不觉已换成另外一个，走到陈浩宇面前打开：“陈公子，请过目。”
[EN] "Agreed!" Master Jiang answered without batting an eye. "If I throw nine or ten matching, you lose."
[ZH] 陈浩宇用手指在盒子里拨拉一会儿，的确是十枚，每个铜钱正反两面都有，才放心说：“可以开始。”
[EN] His readiness set off alarm bells. As the attendant set a small wooden box holding ten copper coins before Master Jiang, Chen Haoyu shouted, "Wait! Let me and everyone here inspect those coins for tampering!"
[ZH] 蔣爷朝围观的赌徒瞧一遍，目光停留在疤子身上：“为让陈公子输得心服口服，就选陈公子身边的人来摇铜钱。你，过来，陈公子的输赢就由你来决定。”
[EN] Master Jiang sneered. "Do not trust me? Fine—inspect away." Catching his boss's glance, the attendant deftly swapped boxes in one sleek sleight of hand before opening it in front of Chen Haoyu. "See for yourself, Young Master."
[ZH] 陈浩宇当然乐意，这样蔣爷想耍手段都难：“疤子，还不赶紧过来。”
[EN] Stirring the coins with a finger—ten ordinary coins, heads and tails clean—Chen Haoyu breathed easier. "Start."
[ZH] 疤子笑嘻嘻走到赌桌旁：“陈公子，我要是摇不出九纯十纯的，您是不是就赢了。”
[EN] Master Jiang swept his eyes across the crowd and pointed straight at Scarface. "So Young Master Chen loses without a single gripe, let one of his own boys shake the box! You—step up and decide your master's fate!"
[ZH] “那是当然。”
[EN] Thrilled—no way Master Jiang could rig his own lackey!—Chen Haoyu waved him over. "Get up here, Scarface!"
[ZH] “那您保准赢，我连五纯六纯都没摇出过，何况九纯十纯？您瞧好了。”
[EN] Grinning up to the table, Scarface asked, "If I fail to roll nine or ten matching, Young Master wins, right?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇更是开心：“你若摇不出，出了赌场，本公子奖励你十两银子。”
[EN] "Damn right!"
[ZH] 疤子很自信，十两银子稳赚不赔，他伸出手：“把木盒给我。”
[EN] "Then it is in the bag! I never even rolled five or six matching in my life—let alone nine or ten! Watch this!"
[ZH] 伙计早已偷偷将盒子更换过，放在疤子手中。
[EN] "Miss nine or ten, and I hand you ten taels of silver outside!" Chen Haoyu promised.
[ZH] 疤子双手握紧木盒，上上下下，左左右右，晃好几回，才打开盒子的盖子，将里面的铜钱一个不落倒在桌面。
[EN] Easy silver! Scarface held out his hands. "Pass the box!"
[ZH] 众人向桌面瞧去，都惊叹道：“十浑纯！”
[EN] Slipping the rigged box back in with seamless sleight of hand, the attendant placed it in Scarface's palms.
[ZH] 陈浩宇只觉脑袋嗡嗡作响，差点儿晕倒。
[EN] Gripping tight, Scarface rattled the box up, down, left, and right before popping the lid and dumping all ten coins across the table.
[ZH] 有位小弟大声说：“疤子，你这是帮陈公子还是害陈公子？”
[EN] The entire hall gasped in one voice: "All ten matching!"
[ZH] 疤子很委屈：“我可是按照以前输的方法摇，谁知手气这么好。陈公子，您可不能怪我。”
[EN] A roar echoed inside Chen Haoyu's skull; his knees nearly buckled.
[ZH] 陈浩宇哪有心情理会疤子，他自语道：“我不信！我不信！我要再验验铜钱！”
[EN] "Scarface, are you helping Young Master or burying him?!" a lackey yelled.
[ZH] 他正要过去抓铜钱时，赌坊的伙计手更快，立刻将十枚铜钱收起来，装入木盒中，递给蒋爷。
[EN] "I shook it the exact loser's way I always do—who knew my luck would turn golden tonight?! Do not blame me, Young Master!" Scarface whined.
[ZH] 蒋爷接过木盒，目的到达，一脸取胜的姿态：“大家亲眼目睹，十浑纯，陈公子还有什么好说的？愿赌服输吧！”
[EN] Dazed, Chen Haoyu muttered, "No way! Impossible! Let me check those coins again!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇依然不愿认输，他此刻清醒许多，赌注可是陈家所有的财产，他岂不是坐实败家子的名声？
[EN] Before his fingers touched the wood, the attendant swept all ten coins back into the box and handed it to Master Jiang.
[ZH] “不行！再摇一回！”
[EN] Cradling the box in triumph, Master Jiang grinned. "Every eye here saw all ten matching! What excuse is left, Young Master Chen? Pay up like a man!"
[ZH] 蒋爷根本不同意：“刚说过一把定输赢，陈公子这么快就忘记？”
[EN] "No! Shake again!" Panic sobered Chen Haoyu—he had just gambled away the entire Chen empire!
[ZH] 伙计拿出一张字据，走到陈浩宇跟前，塞进他的怀里：“陈公子拿好。”
[EN] "One throw takes all—short memory again?" Master Jiang scoffed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过字据想要撕掉。
[EN] The attendant shoved one contract into Chen Haoyu's chest. "Keep your copy safe, Young Master."
[ZH] 蒋爷阻止道：“我这里也有一份，你即使撕毁也没用。如若不想我亲自带人去陈府讨要财产，最好老老实实拿着，回去告诉陈家长辈，以后陈家的一切就是蒋爷我的。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu gripped it ready to shred it to bits.
[ZH] 陈浩宇撕也不是不撕也不是，直接揉成一团塞进怀里：“哼！想要陈家的财产没那么容易。”
[EN] "I hold the twin copy—tearing yours changes nothing," Master Jiang warned. "Unless you want me marching my whole gang up to Chen Manor to collect, take it home and tell your elders everything the Chens own now belongs to Master Jiang!"
[ZH] 他准备离开赌坊，惹下这么大的祸事，得回去禀告爹娘，他们一定有办法处理。
[EN] Stuck neither tearing nor folding, Chen Haoyu crushed it into a ball and jammed it inside his robe. "Hmph! Think grabbing Chen property is that easy?!" Turning toward the door, he raced to get home and confess to his parents—they would find a way out!
[ZH] 刚走到门口，被蒋爷叫住：“陈公子慢走，还有一事需说明。”
[EN] At the threshold, Master Jiang called out, "Hold on, Young Master Chen—one last word."
[ZH] 陈浩宇站住，并未回头：“蒋爷还有什么事？”
[EN] Halting without turning around, Chen Haoyu snapped, "What now?"
[ZH] 蒋爷走到他身边：“爷爷我并非贪图陈家财产，就是想捉弄你，灭一灭你的气焰。回去告诉陈家人，只要你娶了朱家姑娘，陈家的财产我一分一毫不取。”
[EN] Stepping beside him, Master Jiang lowered his voice. "Truth be told, I have no appetite for Chen property—just wanted to knock that cocky grin off your face. Go tell your family: marry Miss Zhu, and I will not touch a single coin of Chen wealth."
[ZH] 听到这个要求，陈浩宇沉重的心情略有轻松，毕竟有了保住陈家财产的方法。他转头看向蒋爷：“你如此大费周章，难道只是让我娶朱家姑娘？”
[EN] A glimmer of air hit Chen Haoyu's lungs. Spinning around, he stared. "All this setup—just to force me to marry Miss Zhu?!"
[ZH] “陈公子果然聪明，朱家姑娘的厉害我可是领教过，你若娶她，以后恐怕没有机会再去春香楼，春金沫不就是爷爷我的？”蒋爷说完，继续叮嘱，“限你三日内娶朱家姑娘，若不娶，爷爷我可就拿着字据找陈家大老爷。”
[EN] "Sharp lad! I got a firsthand taste of Miss Zhu's fists. Once you marry her, you will never sneak near Chunxiang Brothel again—leaving Chun Jinmo free and clear for me!" Master Jiang tapped his chest. "You have three days to wed Miss Zhu. Miss the deadline, and I take this contract straight to Eldest Master Chen."
[ZH] 搞来搞去，不过是为春金沫，陈浩宇心有不甘，又无可奈何。他顾不上理会跟着他的小弟，在蒋爷手下的人打开大门后，迅速走出赌坊。
[EN] All this terror—just over Chun Jinmo! Burning with rage yet cornered tight, Chen Haoyu ignored his cowering lackeys and bolted out the moment the guards unlatched the doors.

#CH 186
[ZH] 陈浩宇从赌坊回到陈府已很晚，爹娘早已睡去，今晚无法将赌坊发生的事向爹娘言明。
[ZH] 他直接回到自己居住的院子，青瓜与其他下人也都休息，陈浩宇只好一个人进屋。
[ZH] 屋里没有点灯，黑漆漆的，应该是下人以为他不会回来，将灯全部熄灭。
[ZH] 陈浩宇摸黑来到床边，懒得脱衣裳，倒头便睡。
[ZH] 约莫睡过两刻钟， 他的头脑变得清晰许多，睁开眼睛坐起来，自语道：“对啊，我难过个什么劲儿？只要我娶朱淑真，字据不就作废？根本没必要让家人知道在赌坊立下字据之事。再说爹娘本来就同意两家的亲事，只是奶奶不同意，可奶奶听我的。我明日告诉大家，想三日内完婚，不就皆大欢喜？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇轻松许多，又躺下，盖上被子，继续自语：“蔣爷啊蔣爷，我让你既谋不到陈家家产，也别想得到春金沫，明日我便去找朱淑真商议成亲之事。嘿嘿……！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇觉得自己实在聪明，一下子将事情想明白；也觉得蔣爷实在笨，搞了一整，没什么用。
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 天亮后，青瓜打开公子的房门，进屋这里摸摸那里摸摸，没有发现灰尘，不必叫丫鬟前来打扫，正要掩门而出，听到公子的声音。
[ZH] “朱淑真！娶你可以，各过各……！”
[ZH] 青瓜纳闷：“怎么是公子的声音？昨晚明明没回来。”
[ZH] 纳闷归纳闷，还是进里屋瞧瞧。青瓜轻手轻脚走进里屋，发现床上的确躺着公子，和衣而睡，被子被蹬到一边，嘴里嘟嘟囔囔说着梦话
[ZH] 青瓜担心公子着凉，走过去将被子从公子身下往出拽。
[ZH] 拽被子的过程将陈浩宇从睡梦中拽醒，他睁开睡眼，一眼瞧见青瓜，伸腿踢去：“谁让你吵醒我！”
[ZH] 青瓜快速躲闪开来：“公子冤枉我，我怕您着凉，给您盖被子。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇坐起来：“被你打扰，好梦都没了，还睡个什么劲儿？给我拿身干净的衣裳来。”
[ZH] 青瓜一边取衣裳，一边说：“公子直唤朱家姑娘的名字，恐怕做的是噩梦。”
[ZH] 竟然在梦中唤朱淑真，陈浩宇认为肯定是昨晚睡前念叨过她的缘故。
[ZH] “少废话，赶紧给本公子换衣裳。”
[ZH] 青瓜先给公子把身上的衣裳脱下，一个纸团掉落在地上，正要去捡，陈浩宇立刻阻止：“别动！”
[ZH] 青瓜只好住手。
[ZH] 陈浩宇弯腰捡起，将纸团展开，用心读一遍上面的内容：“去，把火折子拿来。”
[ZH] 青瓜放下手中的衣裳，找来火折子，打开，吹出火苗，递给公子。
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过，用火点燃纸张，扔在地上，只见字据很快化为灰烬。陈浩宇决定等成亲后，再从蔣爷手中要回另外一份。
[ZH] 陈浩宇把火折子熄灭，递给青瓜：“还愣着干什么？赶紧给本公子穿衣裳。”
[ZH] 青瓜将火折子放在一边，再把干净衣裳给公子换上，又出去吩咐丫鬟把洗脸水端进来，他手拿面巾，伺候公子洗脸。
[ZH] 陈浩宇洗完脸，青瓜递上面巾。
[ZH] 陈浩宇接过，擦完脸将面巾往青瓜怀里一扔：“老爷和夫人可起来？”
[EN] By the time Chen Haoyu stumbled back into Chen Manor, night had grown deep and his parents were long asleep, leaving no chance to confess tonight. Heading straight to his own courtyard, where Qinggua and the other servants had turned in too, he pushed into his pitch-dark room—his servants must have assumed he was staying out again—felt his way to the bed, and collapsed fully clothed.
[ZH] 青瓜接住面巾，整理好放在桌上：“这个时辰老爷和夫人早起了。”
[EN] Half an hour later, his racing head cleared. Sitting bolt upright in the dark, he muttered aloud, "Wait a minute! What am I panicking over?! Marry Zhu Shuzhen and that contract turns to scrap paper! Nobody in the family even needs to find out I signed it! Parents already want the wedding, only Grandmother stalled—and Grandmother listens to me! Tell everyone tomorrow I want to wed within three days, and problem solved!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇二话不说往外走。
[EN] Relief flooding his chest, he flopped back down and pulled up the quilt with a smug chuckle. "Master Jiang, oh Master Jiang—you get neither Chen wealth nor Chun Jinmo! Tomorrow I go straight to Zhu Shuzhen and fix the wedding! Heh!" Feeling downright brilliant—and convinced Master Jiang was an idiot—he drifted right off.
[ZH] 青瓜在后面喊：“公子要不要用早饭？”
[EN] At dawn, Qinggua cracked open his master's door, checked the outer room for dust—clean enough to skip maids—and started to pull it shut when a voice drifted from inside: "Zhu Shuzhen! Fine, I will marry you—each lives their own life...!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇根本没搭理，径直走出去，直奔爹娘的院子。
[EN] Blinking in surprise—when had Young Master come home?—Qinggua tiptoed into the bedchamber and found him sprawled in rumpled clothes, quilt kicked aside, muttering in his sleep. Worried he might catch cold, Qinggua reached down to tug the quilt free from underneath him.
[ZH] 到达时，爹娘正在屋中用早饭，他们边吃，边聊，边笑。从小到大，陈浩宇几乎没见爹娘红过脸，他既满足又羡慕，如若他成亲，也能与对方像爹娘一般相亲相爱那该多好。
[EN] The tug jerked Chen Haoyu awake. Blinking sleep away and spotting Qinggua, he lashed out a kick. "Who told you to wake me?!"
[ZH] 想到三日之内要娶朱淑真，陈浩宇兴致全无：“爹、娘，我有事跟你们讲。”
[EN] Dodging clear, Qinggua protested, "I was only covering you up so you would not catch a chill!"
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰才发现儿子进来。
[EN] Sitting up, Chen Haoyu grumbled, "Ruined a good dream—no point sleeping now. Fetch fresh clothes!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰问：“用过饭吗？”
[EN] "Calling Miss Zhu's name in your sleep sounds more like a nightmare," Qinggua remarked while pulling out clean robes.
[ZH] “还没。”
[EN] "Quit yapping and dress me!"
[ZH] 姚轻琰吩咐丫鬟给儿子添副碗筷。
[EN] As Qinggua helped peel off his outer robe, a crumpled paper ball hit the floor. Qinggua reached down—only for Chen Haoyu to bark, "Hands off!"
[ZH] 丫鬟把碗筷放好，陈浩宇乖乖坐过去。丫鬟又开始给他的碗中添豆蔻汤，添完后，站在一旁伺候。
[EN] Scooping it up himself, Chen Haoyu flattened it out, scanned the ink lines once more, and ordered, "Bring the fire-starter."
[ZH] 陈江林懒得抬眼瞧儿子，还是叮嘱道：“今日笋肉馒头不错，尝一个。”
[EN] Blowing a flame alive, Qinggua handed it over. Chen Haoyu touched the tip to the contract and dropped it to the tiles, watching it curl into ash—once the wedding wrapped up, he would go rip the second copy out of Master Jiang's hands too.
[ZH] 陈浩宇哪有心情吃早饭，直言道：“爹、娘，我想三日内娶朱淑真过门。”
[EN] Snuffing the flame, he tossed it back. "Standing around for what? Finish dressing me!"
[ZH] 儿子破天荒答应婚事，还这么着急，倒把陈江林和姚轻琰搞得既疑惑又高兴。
[EN] After getting dressed and washing his face, Chen Haoyu tossed the damp cloth straight into Qinggua's arms. "Are Father and Mother up?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰笑着批评道：“你这孩子，不愿意时，想方设法破坏，现在同意，又着急忙慌地要成亲，总得让我和你爹缓缓。”
[EN] "Up ages ago at this hour."
[ZH] 儿子终于说出符合心意之事，陈江林放下筷子，才抬头瞧儿子：“婚事早已差人筹备好，三日内举行，也不算匆忙。只是爹不明白，你为何这么快改变想法？”
[EN] Without another word, Chen Haoyu strode straight out, ignoring Qinggua's call about breakfast, and marched to his parents' courtyard.
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞎编道：“儿子在外面浪荡许久，也该成家立业，再说见爹娘如此恩爱，儿子羡慕不已，也想娶个娘子回来恩恩爱爱过日子。”
[EN] Inside, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan sat sharing breakfast—eating, chatting, laughing warm and easy. Growing up, Chen Haoyu had almost never seen them cross a harsh word, filling him with both comfort and quiet envy; how sweet it would be if he could share a bond like that with a wife someday. Then the thought of marrying Zhu Shuzhen inside three days wiped away the warmth. "Father, Mother, I have something to say."
[ZH] 夫妻两个头一次听儿子说些懂事的话，甚是高兴。
[EN] Noticing him walk in, Yao Qingyan asked, "Eaten yet?"
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“三日内就三日内，我与你爹这就差人到朱家打声招呼。你呢，负责说服老太太，再去你两位叔叔处，向他们报个喜，就说今日准备一番，明日迎娶朱家姑娘过门儿。”
[EN] "Not yet."
[ZH] 陈浩宇无不答应，端起豆蔻汤，一口气喝下：“我这就去找奶奶。”
[EN] She signaled a maid to lay another bowl and chopsticks. Taking his seat as the maid ladled hot cardamom broth for him, Chen Haoyu heard his father remark without looking up, "The bamboo-shoot and pork buns are good today—try one."
[ZH] 夏老太太刚用过早饭，孙子就来，她甚是欢喜，叫孙子坐在榻上与她聊天儿。
[EN] With zero appetite, Chen Haoyu blurted straight out, "Father, Mother—I want to marry Zhu Shuzhen within three days."
[ZH] 陈浩宇拉住老太太的手，先是一通撒娇：“奶奶，孙儿在外面逛时，时时刻刻把奶奶挂在心上。”
[EN] Both parents froze in stunned delight at their son's sudden flip.
[ZH] 夏老太太笑着批评：“又哄我，你要是挂念奶奶，还会去青楼赌坊？”
[EN] Yao Qingyan laughed and chided him, "Look at you—first fighting tooth and nail to wreck the match, now rushing headlong into a wedding! Give your father and me a second to catch our breath!"
[ZH] “没想到奶奶什么都清楚。”陈浩宇猜想肯定是青瓜告诉老太太的。
[EN] Setting down his chopsticks to look squarely at his son for the first time, Chen Jianglin asked, "Everything is already prepared, so three days is no rush at all. Still, explain to your father why the sudden change of heart."
[ZH] “奶奶虽然老，但不糊涂，以后可不能再去那些地方，听到没？”
[EN] Thinking fast, Chen Haoyu spun a tidy tale. "I have fooled around outside long enough—time to settle down. Watching Father and Mother so devoted made me envious; I want a wife of my own to live happily with too."
[ZH] “听到听到，奶奶的教诲孙儿肯定听。”还要找朱淑真，陈浩宇不想太耽误工夫，“奶奶，我打算娶朱家姑娘过门，跟爹娘商议好，明日成亲，希望奶奶不要再想耗着的事儿。”
[EN] Hearing sensible words from their son for the first time ever filled the couple with joy.
[ZH] 夏老太太立刻没了笑脸：“她来陈家都闹成那样，你还愿意娶？”
[EN] "Three days it is!" Yao Qingyan declared. "Your father and I will send word to Zhu Manor right away. Your job is convincing Grandmother and sharing the good news with your two uncles—tell them we set up today and welcome Miss Zhu home tomorrow!"
[ZH] “愿意愿意，孙儿突然发现，就喜欢像朱家姑娘那样的。”
[EN] Agreeing to everything, Chen Haoyu downed his cardamom broth in one gulp. "Going to Grandmother right now!"
[ZH] 夏老太太怀疑道：“该不会有人逼你娶她？告诉奶奶是谁，奶奶帮你出面。”
[EN] Finishing breakfast herself, Old Madam Xia beamed at her grandson's early visit and patted the couch beside her. Taking her hand, Chen Haoyu laid on the charm thick. "Whenever I am out, Grandmother never leaves my thoughts for a second!"
[ZH] 老太太一语说中，陈浩宇赶紧掩饰：“哪会有人逼我娶她？奶奶别胡乱想，是我自己想通。朱家姑娘才貌双全，又厉害，以后有她在我身边，既能教我读书作诗，还能保护我，好处多多。”
[EN] "Sweet-talking me again!" Old Madam Xia chuckled. "If you missed Grandmother so much, would you still run off to brothels and gambling dens?"
[ZH] 老太太忧虑道：“可她名声不好，你要是娶她，陈家的长辈绝不会将陈家产业交到你手中。”
[EN] "Nothing gets past Grandmother!"—must have been Qinggua snitching!
[ZH] 原来奶奶担心此事，陈浩宇趁机表露心迹：“奶奶太高估我，就我这样儿，哪能继承陈家产业？有东哥在，他可是做生意的好手，奶奶别对我有任何期望。至于朱家姑娘名声不好，都是孙儿的错，不能怪她。”
[EN] "Old, not blind! Stay clear of those filthy places from now on, hear me?"
[ZH] 孙子一番通情达理的话，老太太心意逐渐改变：“你既没有继承家业之心，奶奶也不强求。之所以想耗着两家的婚事，也是因为你不乐意，既然你愿意娶，奶奶还有什么好阻止的？娶就是。只是明日成亲，会不会太匆忙了些？”
[EN] "Loud and clear—I obey every word Grandmother teaches!" Mindful he still had to track down Zhu Shuzhen, he cut straight to the chase. "Grandmother, I have decided to marry Miss Zhu. Father and Mother already agreed on tomorrow, so let us drop the stalling plan."
[ZH] “不匆忙，我爹该准备的已准备好，今日再把府中布置一番，明日举办婚礼刚刚好。”
[EN] The smile vanished off Old Madam Xia's face. "After the riot she raised at our gate, you STILL want to marry her?!"
[ZH] 老太太虽担忧孙子，怎奈他已同意，老人家又不愿拗着孙子，笑容中带着忧虑点头答应。
[EN] "I do! Suddenly hit me—a fiery girl like Miss Zhu is my exact type!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇又去二叔和三叔房中报喜。
[EN] Suspicion narrowed Old Madam Xia's eyes. "Did someone force you into this? Tell Grandmother who, and I will deal with them!"
[ZH] 二叔陈江树知道是儿子陈浩东谋划的结果，只觉一切顺心如意，装作替侄子高兴的样子。
[EN] Bull's-eye! Covering fast, Chen Haoyu waved it off. "Who could force me? Stop worrying, Grandmother—I came around all on my own! Miss Zhu has beauty, brains, and fierce fists: with her by my side, she can coach my reading and poetry AND protect me! Pure win!"
[ZH] 三叔陈江木搞不明白，朱家姑娘闹成那样，大哥为何还要给自家儿子把她娶回来？他不好多说什么，挤出笑容点头道喜。
[EN] "Yet her reputation is bruised," Old Madam Xia fretted. "Marry her, and the Chen elders will never hand the family business to you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇叫上青瓜准备去望月轩时，整个陈府的下人已忙碌起来，到处布置。
[EN] So that was her hang-up! Seizing the chance to speak his true mind, Chen Haoyu laughed. "Grandmother gives me way too much credit! Look at me—could I ever run the Chen empire? Brother Haodong is a natural merchant; pin no hopes on me! As for Miss Zhu's reputation, that was all my blunder—cannot blame her."
[ZH] 陈浩宇心中提不起多少兴致，为了婚后能继续自由，还得去找朱淑真在婚前将一切商议妥当。
[EN] Softened by his candor, Old Madam Xia relented. "If you have no wish to inherit the business, Grandmother will not force you. I only stalled because you hated the match; if you want to wed her, why would I stand in your way? Marry her, then! Only—isn't tomorrow terribly rushed?"
[ZH] 青瓜跟公子上了马车坐好后，见公子双眉紧锁，关切地问：“公子，明日成亲是您提出来的，为何不高兴？”
[EN] "Not rushed at all! Father already had everything lined up; decorate the manor today and we are set for tomorrow!"
[ZH] “你懂个屁！把嘴闭上！不准跟我说话！”陈浩宇骂完，将身体斜靠着，吩咐道，“告诉车夫去望月轩。”
[EN] Still uneasy yet unable to cross her beloved grandson, Old Madam Xia nodded with a worried smile.
[ZH] 青瓜不敢再多问，对车夫说：“去城东的望月轩。”
[EN] Next, Chen Haoyu stopped by his second and third uncles to spread the news. Knowing his own son Haodong had engineered the whole turn, Second Uncle Chen Jiangshu glowed inside while putting on a warm congratulatory smile. Third Uncle Chen Jiangmu was utterly baffled why Eldest Brother still welcomed such a fierce bride, yet offered polite blessings anyway.
[ZH] 马车启动后，青瓜保持沉默，一直观察公子，他的眉头始终未舒展，还不时叹气。在青瓜的印象中，从未见过公子这样，能看得出他有多么不想娶朱家姑娘。
[EN] By the time Chen Haoyu grabbed Qinggua to head for Wangyue Pavilion, servants were already scrambling across Chen Manor hanging lanterns and red silk. Still heavy-hearted despite the bustle, he needed to lock down ground rules with Zhu Shuzhen before tomorrow so he kept his freedom afterward.
[ZH] 沉默一路，来到望月轩，陈浩宇竟破天荒要求进去等。门口的小厮将他带进会客厅后，跑去通知姑娘。
[EN] Once inside the carriage, seeing his master's tight frown, Qinggua ventured, "Young Master, you were the one who asked to wed tomorrow—why the gloomy face?"
[ZH] 刚将椅子坐热，千里寻带着苦荞前来。
[EN] "What do you know?! Shut your trap and do not say another word!" Slumping back against the cushions, Chen Haoyu barked, "Tell the driver Wangyue Pavilion!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇竟然恭敬地站起来，拱手行礼。
[EN] "East quarter—Wangyue Pavilion!" Qinggua relayed, keeping silent the rest of the way as he watched his master sigh over and over. Never had he seen Young Master look this miserable—plain as day how little he wanted this wedding.
[ZH] 别说千里寻惊讶，连青瓜都惊得不行，公子什么时候这么礼貌过！
[EN] Reaching Wangyue Pavilion, Chen Haoyu amazed everyone by walking straight inside to wait in the reception hall. Hardly had he warmed a chair when Qianli Xun walked in with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“陈浩宇，你这是做什么？怎么还给我行礼？少见，少见。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shot to his feet and gave a deep, crisp cupped-hand bow.
[ZH] 陈浩宇行完礼，站直后说：“明日你就要嫁到陈家，我行礼是希望你嫁过去后对我高抬贵手，不要找我麻烦。”
[EN] Both Qianli Xun and Qinggua gaped—when had this pampered rake ever bowed with manners?!
[ZH] 明日出嫁之事千里寻并不知，既惊讶又气愤：“什么？明日成亲？昨日嫂嫂还告诉我要推迟一段时日，怎么又是明日？你们陈家人是不是想一出是一出？把我和朱家当猴耍！”
[EN] "What act is this, Chen Haoyu?" Qianli Xun asked. "Bowing to me? Now I have seen everything."
[ZH] 陈浩宇劝道：“反正两家退婚已不可能，你迟早得嫁给我，晚嫁不如早嫁，我来呢，是想和你商议……”
[EN] Straightening up, Chen Haoyu spoke earnestly. "Tomorrow you marry into the Chen family. I bow to beg you to go easy on me once you move in and stay off my back."
[ZH] 刚要说关键内容，见青瓜杵在一旁，怕他听去告诉爹娘，伸出一脚踢过去：“滚！别杵在这儿，影响我说话。”
[EN] Blindsided, Qianli Xun flared up at once. "What?! Tomorrow?! My sister-in-law JUST told me yesterday it was postponed! Now it is tomorrow?! Does your whole Chen clan just make things up on a whim, playing me and the Zhus for fools?!"
[ZH] 青瓜躲闪开：“这就滚。”
[EN] "Breaking off the match is dead anyway," Chen Haoyu urged. "Sooner or later you have to marry me—might as well get it over with. I came today to work out—" Catching sight of Qinggua standing nearby, afraid he might run straight to his parents, he swung a foot at him. "Scram! Quit standing around eavesdropping!"
[ZH] 等青瓜出去后，陈浩宇对苦荞说：“你也出去吧。”
[EN] "Scramming right now!" Qinggua hopped clear and darted outside.
[ZH] 千里寻正在气头上，拦道：“怎么？还想撵我的人？”
[EN] Once Qinggua exited, Chen Haoyu looked at Kuqiao. "Step outside too."
[ZH] 陈浩宇不敢再说什么，继续刚才的话题：“既然我俩成亲不可避免，不如商议一下婚后该如何相处。我的建议是你过你的，我过我的，两不相干，如何？”
[EN] Still fuming, Qianli Xun snapped, "Now you think you can boss my people around?!"
[ZH] 千里寻还未从明日要成亲的气愤中走出，根本顾不上陈浩宇的建议：“你说明日要成亲，我就得信你？你算老几！朱家还没给我传来消息，我凭什么跟你商议？”
[EN] Backing down fast, Chen Haoyu got to the point. "Since marrying is unavoidable, let us agree how to live afterward. My offer: you live your life, I live mine, zero interference. Deal?"
[ZH] 事出突然，朱淑真不接受也能理解，可总不能把昨晚在赌坊发生的事告诉她，只怕会被她嘲笑一番。
[EN] Too livid over the ambush wedding date to care about terms, Qianli Xun snapped, "Who put you in charge of wedding dates?! Zhu Manor has not sent a single word—why would I bargain with you?!"
[ZH] 正为难，外面传来下人的声音：“姑娘，夫人、少夫人和大公子来了。”
[EN] Unwilling to humiliate himself by confessing last night's gambling fiasco, Chen Haoyu hung stuck—when a servant's call rang from outside: "Miss, Madam, Young Madam, and Eldest Young Master are here!"

#CH 187
[ZH] 千里寻正准备出去迎接，韦嬷嬷已将家人领入。
[ZH] 陈文心见浩宇也在，故意打趣道：“在外面看到青瓜，就猜你跑来见淑真，难不成等不到明日成亲？今日就来找新娘子说心里话？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇应承道：“姐姐别瞎说，我来与她商量别的事。”
[ZH] 听了嫂嫂的话，千里寻不得不相信陈浩宇所说。心中虽有不满，并未表露出太多情绪。迎上去拉住向夫人的手，想要进一步确认：“娘，您带哥哥嫂嫂来，所为何事？”
[ZH] 向夫人笑道：“还不是为你的婚事？你爹说明日让你从朱府出发，我带你哥嫂一起过来接你回去。”
[ZH] “我不回去。”
[ZH] 朱景元劝道：“淑真，别耍小孩子脾气，你是朱家姑娘，成亲不从朱家被迎娶走，会被人笑话。”
[ZH] “我不想嫁到陈家。”
[ZH] 陈文心劝道：“不想嫁也得嫁，两家已广发喜帖，再过一个时辰全临安城有头有脸的人都会收到，你想让大家来参加婚礼，却见不到新娘吗？公爹也邀请了仪王，可不能让公爹在仪王面前丢脸。乖乖的，跟我们回去。”
[ZH] 提到仪王，千里寻不再言语，她已想过，嫁到陈家可以还梦容一个好哥哥。
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could step out to greet them, Matron Wei ushered her family inside. Spotting Chen Haoyu right there, Chen Wenxin teased at once, "Seeing Qinggua outside, I guessed you sneaked over to see Shuzhen! Could not wait till tomorrow—had to whisper sweet nothings to your bride today?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见姑娘沉默，猜她心中犹豫，既犹豫证明不嫁之心已没有以前坚决。陈公子又在，看上去想单独与姑娘说话，不如给二人留出时间。
[EN] "Quit teasing, Sis—I came to discuss other business with her," Chen Haoyu muttered.
[ZH] 于是韦嬷嬷对向夫人说：“夫人，姑娘应该与陈公子有事要说，我先陪您在望月轩走走，过会儿姑娘想通，自然会与您回朱府。”
[EN] Her sister-in-law's words confirmed the shocking news. Keeping her temper reined in, Qianli Xun stepped forward and took Madam Xiang's hands. "Mother, what brings you, Brother, and Sister-in-Law here?"
[ZH] 向夫人对韦嬷嬷的话深信不疑：“好吧，我跟你走走，来过好几回，都没在望月轩内好好转转，趁此大好春光，我们也疏散疏散筋骨。”
[EN] "Your wedding, of course!" Madam Xiang smiled. "Your father wants you riding out from Zhu Manor tomorrow, so we came to fetch you home."
[ZH] 陈文心见状，拉起朱景元：“我们也出去走走。”
[EN] "I am not going back."
[ZH] 众人带丫鬟走出会客厅，又剩下千里寻、陈浩宇和苦荞。
[EN] "Quit acting stubborn, Shuzhen," Zhu Jingyuan urged. "You are a Zhu daughter—not riding out from Zhu Manor will make us a laughingstock."
[ZH] 苦荞甚是为小姐担忧，与陈家的婚事一波三折，波折也就罢了，关键是没波折到小姐的心上。
[EN] "I do not want to marry into the Chen family."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见朱淑真眉头紧锁，在椅子上坐下，沉思不语，笑道：“现在相信我的话了吧？婚事已避免不了，还是好好合计合计婚后我们两个该怎么过。”
[EN] "Want to or not, you have to," Chen Wenxin said firmly. "Both families have already dispatched invitations—within two hours every notable household in Lin'an will have one in hand. Father-in-law even invited Prince Yi! Would you let guests gather with no bride and shame Father-in-law in front of Prince Yi? Be good and come home with us."
[ZH] 千里寻没有理会陈浩宇，继续沉思。
[EN] At the mention of Prince Yi, Qianli Xun fell mute. She had already thought of this: marrying into the Chen family would give Mengrong her brother back.
[ZH] 面对人生难题，她该如何做？尽管已想清楚婚后的生活该怎么过，可当婚姻真正来临时，她不敢轻易接受，只怕重蹈覆辙，那么之前为和离做出的努力又有何意义？
[EN] Noting her mistress's silence—meaning her refusal was no longer rock solid—and seeing Young Master Chen clearly eager for a private word, Matron Wei stepped in smoothly. "Madam, Miss and Young Master Chen seem to have matters to discuss. Let me walk you around Wangyue Pavilion for a bit; once Miss thinks it through, she will come home to Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 净光法师说过的正思正念再次被她想起。是满足所有人委屈自己？还是逃走？逃到没有人能找到的地方，让所有人恐慌？到底前者符合正思正念？还是后者？千里寻发现判断起来十分困难。
[EN] Trusting Matron Wei as always, Madam Xiang nodded. "Very well. I have visited several times without ever strolling the grounds—good weather to stretch our legs."
[ZH] 不像面对与文渊的感情，她再痛苦，心中都会有个声音告诉她，放弃是最好的选择。也不像面对仪王，她清楚地知道为了梦容必须拒绝申简辰的真情。
[EN] Taking Zhu Jingyuan's arm, Chen Wenxin pulled him along too. "Let us go stroll as well."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见朱淑真沉默不语，心中担忧，凭朱淑真的能力逃婚轻而易举。若她想方设法逃走，成亲失败，蔣爷再将字据拿到陈家，到时他便成为家族的罪人。即使家中长辈能奋力解决此事，恐怕他这辈子别想在陈家抬起头。
[EN] As the group trailed out with their maids, only Qianli Xun, Chen Haoyu, and Kuqiao remained in the hall—Kuqiao silently fretting over every twist landing against her mistress's wishes.
[ZH] 陈浩宇对苦荞说：“去，把门关上。”
[EN] Watching Zhu Shuzhen sit brooding with knitted brows, Chen Haoyu chuckled. "Believe me now? No dodging this wedding, so let us figure out how we are going to coexist."
[ZH] 苦荞很不情愿。
[EN] Ignoring him, Qianli Xun sank deep into thought. Facing this crossroads, what should she choose? Even having mapped out how to survive at Chen Manor, facing the actual wedding tomorrow rattled her—what if history repeated itself, turning all her struggle to divorce Lin Jue into a joke? Master Jingguang's "Right Thought and Right Mindfulness" echoed in her mind. Yield to please everyone else at her own expense, or bolt to the ends of the earth and throw both houses into chaos? Unlike letting go of Wenyuan or refusing Shen Jianchen for Mengrong's sake, where her gut knew the answer straight away, this choice felt foggy and impossible.
[ZH] “去吧，难道你怕我把她怎样？我哪有那本事？放心，我有重要的事情跟朱淑真说，怕别人听到。”
[EN] Watching her stay dead silent, panic crept up Chen Haoyu's spine. With her martial skills, skipping town tonight would be child's play—and if she vanished and broke the wedding, Master Jiang would march straight into Chen Manor with the contract, branding him the clan's eternal villain! Even if the elders somehow salvaged the estate, he would never lift his head in Chen Manor again.
[ZH] 见陈公子解释得情真意切，苦荞过去将门关好。
[EN] "Go shut the door," Chen Haoyu told Kuqiao.
[ZH] 当她转过身的一刻，竟然发现陈公子双膝跪在地上，苦荞惊得下巴快掉在地上。
[EN] Kuqiao balked.
[ZH] 陈浩宇也是豁出去，为从蔣爷手中拿回字据，给朱淑真下跪不算什么。
[EN] "Just shut it—scared I will hurt her? Like I even could! I have something dead serious to tell Zhu Shuzhen and cannot let anyone outside hear."
[ZH] 千里寻也深感意外，无心再寻思正思正念。意外之余，露出不屑的表情：“陈浩宇，你脑子是不是有问题？跪老娘干吗？”
[EN] Struck by his sudden earnestness, Kuqiao walked over and pulled the door closed. When she turned back around, her jaw nearly hit the floor: Young Master Chen had dropped straight to both knees on the flagstones!
[ZH] “你不答应明日跟我成亲，我就不起来。”
[EN] Desperate to get his contract back from Master Jiang, Chen Haoyu had thrown every ounce of pride out the window.
[ZH] 千里寻十分纳闷：“你不是不愿娶我吗？为何求我嫁给你？”
[EN] Startled clean out of her philosophical dilemma, Qianli Xun shot him a scornful glare. "Have you lost your mind, Chen Haoyu? Kneeling to me for what?!"
[ZH] 情势所迫，陈浩宇只好老老实实将昨晚赌坊之事一一说明……
[EN] "Until you agree to wed me tomorrow, I am not getting up!"
[ZH] 千里寻气愤道：“凭什么你做错事，让老娘替你擦屁股？”
[EN] "Hold on—weren't you fighting tooth and nail NOT to marry me? Why beg me now?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇央求道：“你就行行好，嫁给我吧，大不了我向你保证，婚后跟婚前的生活没有什么区别，在陈府你想做什么我都不拦着。如果你有喜欢的人，我随时放你离开，如何？”
[EN] Cornered, Chen Haoyu spilled the entire story of last night's gambling den ambush from start to finish.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，绕着陈浩宇走一圈，嘲笑道：“你人虽不怎么样，但看着也不笨，怎么会答应跟蔣爷赌博？还拿陈家的财产做赌注？我看你呀，就是看着聪明，其实啊不知道有多蠢！”
[EN] Qianli Xun's temper exploded. "YOU screw up and expect ME to wipe your backside?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇虽不满，可谁让他做错事，继续央求：“我一时脑子缺根弦，才中了蔣爷的圈套。随便你怎么嘲笑，我现在是死猪不怕开水烫，只求你答应嫁给我，条件任你提，我无不同意。”
[EN] "Have a heart and marry me!" Chen Haoyu pleaded. "I swear your life after marriage will be identical to right now! Do whatever you please inside Chen Manor—I will not stop you! Even if you fall for someone else, I will set you free whenever you want—deal?!"
[ZH] 千里寻又坐下：“我实在不明白，我嫁给你竟然可抵陈家所有家财？蔣爷的脑子该不会也进了水？”
[EN] Rising to circle around him, Qianli Xun scoffed, "Worthless as you are, you never looked half-wit stupid—how on earth did you fall into gambling with Master Jiang and stake your whole family's wealth?! Sharp on the surface, hollow inside!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇解释道：“蔣爷说只要我娶你，你那么厉害，肯定能管住我不去春香楼，那么春金沫就是他的人。”
[EN] Swallowing the insult, Chen Haoyu begged on. "Had a short circuit in my brain and fell straight into Master Jiang's trap! Mock me all you want—I am past caring about face! Just agree to marry me! Name any terms you like and I sign every single one!"
[ZH] 千里寻更是不解：“没想到在蔣爷心中一个青楼女子竟比陈家的家产还重要？我看蔣爷不止脑子进水，估计进了什么乌七八糟的东西。”
[EN] Dropping back into her chair, Qianli Xun shook her head. "One thing baffles me: how does marrying ME equal the entire Chen family fortune? Is Master Jiang's brain flooded with water too?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续央求：“就同意吧？嫁给我你少不了一根毫毛，大不了我答应你，我名下现有的财产和以后得到的财产全部过到你的名下。”
[EN] "Master Jiang figures you are so fierce you will lock me down and keep me clear of Chunxiang Brothel, handing Chun Jinmo straight to him!"
[ZH] 千里寻不情愿的情绪中生起些许兴趣：“你名下有多少财产？”
[EN] "A brothel girl outweighs the entire Chen family empire in Master Jiang's eyes?! His skull is not flooded with water—it is stuffed with junk!"
[ZH] “没算过，反正比你的财产多，你只赚不赔，这么好的买卖不接，只怕以后会后悔。”
[EN] "Just say yes! Marrying me costs you not a hair! Tell you what—every scrap of property in my name now, plus anything I inherit later, I transfer straight into YOUR name!"
[ZH] 千里寻还需问个明白：“你整日吊儿郎当，名下的财产都是谁帮你打理？”
[EN] A spark of interest cut straight through Qianli Xun's reluctance. "How much property is in your name?"
[ZH] “我爹手底下的人，用不着我操心。”
[EN] "Never tallied it all—way more than yours! Pure profit, zero loss—turn down a deal this sweet and you will kick yourself later!"
[ZH] “你都给了我，就不怕没银子花。”
[EN] "You fool around all day—who runs your properties?"
[ZH] “所以得跟你商量商量，财产归你，每月得给我银子花。”
[EN] "My father's stewards. Zero hassle for you."
[ZH] 千里寻发现能得到大笔财产，她的情绪比刚才好了许多；也才发现她从心底对金钱还是有渴望的，金钱总比人可靠。她总算找到一个对自己有利，又能满足他人的理由。
[EN] "Hand it all to me and what do you live on?"
