[ZH] “陈浩宇，早知有今日，何必抢走我的竹林？还不得求着还给我。”
[EN] "Which is why we negotiate: you take all the assets, and pay me a monthly allowance."
[ZH] 陈浩宇听出些意思来：“难道你答应明日跟我成亲？”
[EN] Odd how the prospect of serious wealth cheered her right up—she admitted to herself money beat relying on fickle men any day. At last she had found a deal that served her own interest while satisfying everyone else!
[ZH] “起来吧，商议一下你我婚后该怎么过。”
[EN] "If you knew today was coming, Chen Haoyu, why snatch my bamboo grove in the first place? Now you are begging to hand it right back!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇高兴地爬起来：“还是刚才的话，条件随便提。”
[EN] Catching the shift in her tone, Chen Haoyu lit up. "Does that mean you agree to marry me tomorrow?!"
[ZH] “可是你说的，我提了你可别后悔。”
[EN] "Get off your knees—let us negotiate how we live after the wedding."
[ZH] “绝不后悔。”
[EN] Scrambling up with a grin, he repeated, "Same promise: name any terms you want!"
[ZH] “头一件，我嫁到陈家不跟你住在一起，要有单独的院子……”
[EN] "Your words—no whining once I lay them down!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇为难道：“恐怕有些难……”
[EN] "Not a peep!"
[ZH] 千里寻皱起眉头：“你不是说条件随便提吗？老娘刚提第一个要求你就反悔，还有没有诚意？”
[EN] "First: once I enter Chen Manor, I do not share quarters with you. I get my own separate courtyard..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇耐心说：“你嫁到陈家肯定得住我的院子，不过你放心，院子归你，主屋也归你，把旁边的偏房收拾收拾，我住里面，再说我大多时间都住外面，还不跟你的地盘似的。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu winced. "That part is tricky..."
[ZH] “说的也是，那就按你说的办。其二，不许干涉我出去参加任何宴会和诗会，也不许阻止我与文人才子往来，外面有什么风言风语，最好别当真。”
[EN] Qianli Xun's brow snapped together. "Said any terms, and you backpedal on item one?! Sincere my foot!"
[ZH] “我连你喜欢上别人都不在乎，还在乎这些？同意同意。”陈浩宇答应得很干脆。
[EN] "Calm down! As my wife you have to move into my courtyard—yet hear me out: the whole courtyard is yours, the main hall is yours, and I will fix up a side room for myself! Besides, I stay out most nights anyway, so the whole place is practically your private kingdom!"
[ZH] “其三，婚后第二天就把你名下所有财产过继到我名下，以后再得什么财产都必须乖乖给我。”
[EN] "Fair enough—done. Second: zero interference with my attending banquets or poetry gatherings, zero blocking my friendships with scholars and poets, and pay no heed to outside gossip."
[ZH] 陈浩宇犹豫着，还是答应，话已说出，覆水难收：“同意！”
[EN] "I do not even care if you fall for another guy—why would I care about poetry salons? Agreed, agreed!"
[ZH] 本来还想提第四个条件，千里寻想了想，自由和钱财均有，还提个什么劲儿：“就三条，没了。”
[EN] "Third: the very day after the wedding, every asset under your name transfers into mine, and any future assets go straight to me too."
[ZH] “你的条件已提完，该我了。”
[EN] Wincing for half a beat before bowing to the inevitable, Chen Haoyu gritted out, "Agreed!"
[ZH] “说吧。”千里寻也想听听对方的想法。
[EN] She considered adding a fourth clause, then shrugged—freedom and wealth both locked down, what more did she need? "Three terms. Done."
[ZH] “每个月必须让我从铺子里支出一百两银子……”
[EN] "Your list is done—my turn!"
[ZH] 千里寻急了：“一百两？你干什么一个月需要花一百两？”
[EN] "Go ahead."
[ZH] “我干的事情可多了，反正少于一百两根本不够我花。”
[EN] "Every month I get to draw one hundred taels of silver from the shops..."
[ZH] “不行，太多，再少些。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shot upright. "One hundred taels?! What on earth are you doing spending a hundred taels a month?!"
[ZH] “一点儿都不多，我有时一个月还会花二百两。”
[EN] "Plenty of things! Less than a hundred barely covers pocket change!"
[ZH] “二百两！我看你就是个妥妥的败家子！”
[EN] "No way—far too much! Cut it down!"
[ZH] “那好吧，九十两。”
[EN] "Not much at all! Some months I spend two hundred!"
[ZH] “不行！”
[EN] "Two hundred taels?! Textbook prodigal son!"
[ZH] “八十两。”
[EN] "Fine, ninety!"
[ZH] “不行！”
[EN] "No!"
[ZH] 还没成亲，朱淑真如此限制于他，陈浩宇已感受到未来生活的不幸，为拿回字据，只好忍了：“我说的你都不同意，那你说每个月给我多少银子？”
[EN] "Eighty!"
[ZH] “二十两！”
[EN] "No!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇急了，瞪大眼睛：“二十两！你打发叫花子！”
[EN] Already feeling the squeeze before the wedding even started, Chen Haoyu forced himself to swallow it for the contract's sake. "You shoot down every number—how much will you give me?"
[ZH] 千里寻毫不示弱：“就二十两，你要是不乐意，明日我有的是办法不嫁到陈家。”
[EN] "Twenty taels!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇很无奈，心想，为拿回字据，先答应她，等日后再想办法要更多的银子。他很不情愿地说：“好！我答应你。不过，以后我去哪里做什么你都不许过问，以后我纳妾你也不准管。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's eyes bugged out. "Twenty taels?! What am I, a beggar?!"
[ZH] “我才懒得管你的破事。苦荞，把纸笔和印朱拿来。”
[EN] "Twenty taels, take it or leave it! Unhappy? I know ten ways to vanish before tomorrow's wedding!"
[ZH] 苦荞取来纸笔，在一旁的桌子上放好。千里寻提笔写下各自的要求，然后按了指印。
[EN] Cornered, Chen Haoyu figured he could sign now to snag Master Jiang's contract and wheedle more silver later. "Fine! Deal! Only you never pry into where I go or what I do, and you never meddle when I take concubines!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇过去按指印时，又想起一事：“我能不能再提个条件？”
[EN] "Could not care less about your sordid hobbies. Kuqiao, bring brush, paper, and red ink paste!"
[ZH] “提你个头！白纸黑字都写完，凭什么再给你加一条？”千里寻瞪了陈浩宇一眼。
[EN] Setting writing supplies on the side table, Kuqiao watched her mistress draft every clause cleanly and press her thumbprint.
[ZH] 陈浩宇装出可怜的样子：“我名下财产全都给你，提个小要求难道还不行？”
[EN] Stepping up to press his own, Chen Haoyu paused. "Wait—can I add one tiny clause?"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“有话快说，有屁快放。”
[EN] "Add my foot! Everything is already inked—why slip in extras?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“以后如果有人欺负我，你必须替我出面，你得保护我。”
[EN] Putting on puppy eyes, he pleaded, "I just handed you every copper to my name—cannot I ask one tiny favor?"
[ZH] 千里寻将陈浩宇怼得十分干脆：“保护你个头！最好别提这个要求，省得老娘本来是去保护你，却没忍住揍你一顿！”
[EN] "Spit it out quick."
[ZH] 陈浩宇只得放弃要求，乖乖按下指印。
[EN] "If anyone ever bullies me, you have to step in and protect me."
[ZH] 千里寻向苦荞吩咐：“收好了，以后陈浩宇哪一条做不到，就把它拿出来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun shut him down flat. "Protect you, my ass! Drop that request before I march out to 'protect' you and end up pounding you myself!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇回怼道：“你要是做不到，我也会要求把它拿出来。”
[EN] Giving up, Chen Haoyu stamped his thumbprint.
[ZH] 大事已解决，陈浩宇只等明日成亲后找蒋爷要回字据，再将其烧毁，说了声：“明儿见！”迅速出了会客厅，带上青瓜回陈府准备当新郎。
[EN] "Lock this up tight, Kuqiao," Qianli Xun instructed. "The second Chen Haoyu breaks a single line, pull it out."
[ZH] 明日就要成亲，千里寻想到神柳，不如去神柳下坐坐，与它说说话，也许心情会更好些。
[EN] "Same goes if you break it!" Chen Haoyu shot back. Crisis averted—all that remained was getting through tomorrow, grabbing Master Jiang's copy, and torching it! Calling out "See you tomorrow!" he darted out, scooped up Qinggua, and raced back to Chen Manor to play groom.
[ZH] “苦荞，你去告诉夫人，就说我已同意嫁到陈家，天黑前会自己回朱府，现在有事出去一趟。”
[EN] With her wedding set for tomorrow, Qianli Xun felt a quiet tug toward the Divine Willow—sitting beneath its boughs and talking things out might clear the heaviness in her chest. "Kuqiao, go tell Madam I agreed to marry into the Chen family and will return to Zhu Manor before dark. I am stepping out for a bit."
[ZH] “要不我陪小姐一起去？”
[EN] "Want me to come with you, Miss?"
[ZH] “我想一个人静一静。”
[EN] "I want some quiet by myself."
[ZH] “好的，我这就去给夫人说。”
[EN] "Alright, I will go tell Madam."
[ZH] 苦荞离开后，千里寻独自一人出了望月轩，乘坐马车，往神柳处而去。
[EN] Once Kuqiao left, Qianli Xun stepped out of Wangyue Pavilion alone and rode a carriage toward the Divine Willow.

#CH 188
[ZH] 苦荞在望月轩的亭子中找到韦嬷嬷等人，向大家转达小姐的意思。
[ZH] 向夫人忧虑道：“淑真该不会是骗我们？她会不会躲起来？”
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“夫人多虑，姑娘从来说一不二，只要是她说过的话，定然照做，您就放一百个心，今晚我定将姑娘完完整整给您和老爷送回朱府。”
[ZH] 陈文心也说：“我们就听韦嬷嬷的，她最了解淑真。”
[ZH] 朱景元叹道：“唉，白白跑一趟。苦荞，淑真到底去了哪里？”
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“小姐应该是去看城外的神柳。”
[ZH] “神柳？”朱景元若有所思，猛然醒悟，“听人说，城外的神柳主姻缘，明日要成亲，淑真去那里走走倒也正常。看来她没事，娘，我们还是回去耐心等待。”
[ZH] 向夫人点点头：“先回去吧。”
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 正值阳春三月，大山已焕发活力，包括山下的植物，一个个斗志昂扬，充满生命气息。千里寻独自一人踩着山下的路，向神柳处走去，一路上满眼尽是春天的色彩。
[ZH] 她在远处已看到神柳，它的身上像披了一层薄薄的绿纱，在春日的斜阳下显得摇曳多姿。零零散散的红绸在飘动，不仔细看，还以为是夕阳的余晖。
[ZH] 千里寻带着一腔情愫慢慢靠近神柳，还未来得及倾诉，被一阵“哼哼”声打破。
[ZH] 原来白风正在神柳下训练小猪爬树。他用两只大手扶住小猪的身体，把它的四只蹄子放在树干上：“爬啊，赶紧爬啊，再不爬，今晚让你饿肚子。”
[ZH] 小猪趴在树干上，嘴巴拱着树干，发出哼唧的声音，仿佛正在诉说心中的不满。
[ZH] 白风把小猪顺着树干向上挪，小猪很不情愿，蹄子在树干上胡乱挪动，嘴巴里发出更大的“哼哼”声。
[EN] Finding Matron Wei and the Zhu family waiting in Wangyue Pavilion's garden kiosk, Kuqiao relayed her mistress's message.
[ZH] 白风根本不管，继续挪着，像在教小孩子走路一般：“就这样，一步一步往上爬，你要相信自己有一天肯定能爬上去，我要让你成为第一头会爬树的猪，加油！加油！”
[EN] Anxiety creased Madam Xiang's brow. "Could Shuzhen be tricking us? What if she runs away and hides?"
[ZH] 小猪哪里听得懂白风的话，只顾乱叫，叫着叫着，声音越来越小，直到没了声息，脑袋耷拉着。
[EN] "Rest easy, Madam," Matron Wei reassured her. "Miss never goes back on her word. Once she promises, she follows through. Put a hundred hearts at ease—tonight I will deliver her safe and sound to Zhu Manor."
[ZH] 白风急了：“怎么？猪也会偷懒？”
[EN] "Trust Matron Wei," Chen Wenxin agreed. "Nobody knows Shuzhen better."
[ZH] 很快觉出不对来，他把小猪抱下来，放在草地上。小猪躺着，竟然一动不动。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan sighed. "Wasted a whole trip over here, then. Where did Shuzhen even go, Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “你到底是晕过去？还是死了？”
[EN] "Most likely to see the Divine Willow outside town."
[ZH] 千里寻走上前：“它已被你逼死！猪根本不会爬树，你偏让它做不会的事，它活着还有什么意思？”
[EN] "The Divine Willow?" Thought flickered across Zhu Jingyuan's face before he nodded. "Folks say that willow governs marriage ties. With her wedding tomorrow, paying it a visit makes sense. Looks like she is fine, Mother—let us head back and wait."
[ZH] 白风这才发现幽栖居士：“幽栖居士什么时候到的？也不言语一声，肯定站在一旁看笑话。”
[EN] Madam Xiang nodded. "Let us go home, then."
[ZH] 千里寻批评道：“既知道是笑话还训练？也不知道是你脑子有问题，还是你家王爷脑子有毛病。”
[EN] Out in the bright third-month spring, the mountains burst with fresh green life, roadside grass and wildflowers standing tall. Walking alone along the foothill path, Qianli Xun spotted the Divine Willow from afar, veiled in tender green shoots swaying in the slanted afternoon sun, dotted with red ribbons glowing like sunset clouds.
[ZH] 白风朝幽栖居士身旁瞧瞧，没发现苦荞的身影。
[EN] Approaching with a chest full of unspoken feelings, her quiet moment shattered at the sound of loud grunting.
[ZH] 千里寻看出他的心思：“我是一个人来的，苦荞没跟着。”
[EN] There beneath the willow stood Bai Feng, both big hands propping a piglet's four hooves against the rough bark. "Climb! Climb already! Don't climb and you go hungry tonight!"
[ZH] “既然苦荞没在，我还是赶快把小猪给埋了，再回去向王爷禀报。”
[EN] Snout shoved against the trunk, the piglet snorted in protest. Bai Feng slid it a notch higher; its little hooves scrabbled uselessly as its squeals grew louder. Undeterred, he kept inching it up like coaching a toddler to walk. "That is it—one step at a time! Believe in yourself! I am going to make you the first tree-climbing pig in history! Come on! You got this!"
[ZH] 白风把小猪抱起来，到一旁去找土质疏松之处。找到后，从腰间拔出一柄匕首，开始挖坑。
[EN] Clueless as to his pep talk, the piglet squealed weaker and weaker until its voice cut off and its head sagged limp.
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会，只顾用双目照顾神柳，用心与它沟通。
[EN] Bai Feng panicked. "Hey! Pigs slack off too?!" Sensing something wrong, he lowered it onto the grass, where it lay motionless. "Did you pass out—or die?!"
[ZH] “神柳，明日我要嫁给不喜欢的人，难道穿越而来，注定只会嫁给不喜欢的人？净光法师说过，想要改变命运需坚持正思正念，我一路走来，甚是辛苦，发现这正思正念不过是委屈自己成全他人而已，我不想委屈自己，想和喜欢的人在一起……”
[EN] Qianli Xun walked straight up. "You hounded it to death! Pigs cannot climb trees—forcing it to do the impossible, what did it have left to live for?"
[ZH] 传来一个熟悉的声音：“施主，委屈自己便是成全自己。”
[EN] Spinning around in shock, Bai Feng spotted her. "When did Youqi Jushi get here?! Standing there silent watching me make a fool of myself!"
[ZH] 千里寻心中嘀咕：“净光法师？”
[EN] "If you know it is a joke, why keep at it? Beats me whether you or your prince is crazier."
[ZH] 她转过身，眼前出现一位面目慈祥的老尼：“法师可是在山上的尼姑庵修行？”
[EN] Bai Feng glanced past her shoulder, spotting no Kuqiao.
[ZH] “正是。”
[EN] Reading his face, Qianli Xun said, "I came alone. Kuqiao stayed back."
[ZH] 千里寻继续嘀咕：“声音这么像，肯定是净光法师，今日可算见到她的真面目。”于是问道：“您是净光法师？”
[EN] "Well, since Kuqiao is not here, better I bury the poor pig and go report to His Highness." Scooping up the limp piglet, he wandered off to find soft soil, drew a dagger from his belt, and started digging a hole.
[ZH] 净光法师不愿承认：“我并非住持本人，只是声音与她相似而已，”
[EN] Paying him no further heed, Qianli Xun raised her eyes to the Divine Willow and spoke inside her heart: *Divine Willow, tomorrow I marry a man I cannot stand. Did crossing over condemn me to marrying men I never love? Master Jingguang told me changing fate takes Right Thought and Right Mindfulness—yet every step of this road has been brutal, and it feels like Right Thought just means swallowing my own misery to make everyone else happy. I do not want to swallow misery—I want to be with the man I love...*
[ZH] 千里寻感到些许失望，如果净光法师在身边，便可向她请教人生难题。转念一想，从这位师傅刚才说的话判断，她显然已听到她心里的话语，当真不简单，不如与她聊几句。
[EN] A familiar voice spoke behind her: "Patron, enduring wrong for yourself IS fulfilling yourself."
[ZH] 千里寻上前行礼：“法师，您刚才说委屈自己便是成全自己，我不甚理解，还请解惑。”
[EN] *Master Jingguang?* Turning around, Qianli Xun saw a gentle-faced elderly nun. "Does Master cultivate at the nunnery up the mountain?"
[ZH] 净光法师双手合十：“阿弥陀佛，世人皆为情和贪欲所困，深陷其中，不能自拔。之所以有情不得之痛苦，贪欲实现不了之困苦，不过是上天有好生之德，希望人们迷途知返，回归本性。可世人却不自知，得不到想要的便认为是委屈自己，其实得到才是真正的委屈。”
[EN] "Indeed."
[ZH] 千里寻不甚理解，也无法完全认同：“照法师所说，我舍弃爱的人，嫁给讨厌之人便是成全自己，这种道理谁听后都会觉得毫无道理可言。”
[EN] *The exact same voice—it has to be Master Jingguang!* "Are you Master Jingguang?"
[ZH] 净光法师问道：“请问施主，你为何不能嫁给所爱之人？”
[EN] Unwilling to claim the title, the nun smiled. "I am not the abbess—merely someone with a similar voice."
[ZH] 为何？千里寻只觉自己的感情之路多舛，刚刚穿越而来，心中无不思念申简辰，可他却不见踪影；后来与文渊相爱，却因流言蜚语劳燕分飞；慢慢走出情伤，知道仪王就是申简辰，刚穿越而来的感情逐渐复苏，又因梦容，她无法做出大胆的选择。
[EN] A pang of disappointment flickered—yet clearly this nun had read the exact cry inside her heart, no ordinary monk at all. Stepping forward, Qianli Xun bowed. "Master just said enduring wrong for oneself is fulfilling oneself. I do not understand—please enlighten me."
[ZH] 千里寻自信并未做错什么，脱口而出：“不过是命运捉弄罢了。”
[EN] Pressing her palms together, Master Jingguang chanted, "Amitabha. Mortals are trapped in passion and craving, sinking deep without escape. Why do they suffer unfulfilled love and unmet desire? Only because Heaven cherishes life and hopes people turn back from stray roads to their true nature. Yet mortals stay blind—calling unmet desire 'wronging oneself,' when getting what you crave is often the truest wrong."
[ZH] 净光法师说：“人天生有命，命非不能改也，想要改变只在一个‘运’字。满足自己委屈他人是一种运法，满足他人委屈自己也是一种运法。两种运法，结果迥然不同，种下的因也会不同，最终变现出来的果自然不同。姑娘想选哪一种？”
[EN] Unable to grasp or swallow that, Qianli Xun shook her head. "By Master's logic, giving up the man I love to marry someone I despise fulfills me? Anyone hearing that would call it nonsense."
[ZH] 千里寻叹口气：“唉，我心里想选第一种，可理智告诉我不能选，我只好委屈自己，只因净光法师告诉我要坚持正思正念。请问法师，是不是面对任何人生难题，坚持正思正念就必须委屈自己成全别人？”
[EN] "Tell me, Patron—why can you not marry the one you love?"
[ZH] 净光法师露出慈祥的笑容：“佛理无比智慧，姑娘不能一概而论，所谓正思正念亦需视情况而定，有时需要委屈自己成全别人，有时则是委屈别人成全自己。姑娘面临的问题也只能委屈自己，我送姑娘一句话：守得云开见月明。阿弥陀佛，尼姑庵里还有事，失陪。”
[EN] Why? Her whole romantic road since arriving felt cursed: longing for Shen Jianchen with no trace of him; falling for Wenyuan only for rumors to rip them apart; healing just enough to realize Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen, only for Mengrong to block every bold step. Convinced she had done nothing wrong, she blurted, "Fate playing cruel tricks, nothing more."
[ZH] 目送老尼离去的背影，千里寻无比惆怅，又转身面向神柳。此时太阳已落山，月亮慢慢向高空爬行，千里寻还是不愿离去，琢磨着老尼刚才说过的话。
[EN] "Every person is born with a destiny," Master Jingguang said, "yet destiny is not unchangeable—change lies in the single word 'course.' Pleasing yourself while wronging others is one course; pleasing others while enduring wrong yourself is another. Two courses plant different causes and yield utterly different fruits. Which does the young lady wish to choose?"
[ZH] “守得云开见月明”，难道嫁给陈浩宇后才能慢慢等来属于自己的感情？难不成佛理允许她红杏出墙？千里寻很快否认，佛理从来道义，又怎能允许她做出违背道德之事？
[EN] Qianli Xun let out a sigh. "My heart wants the first, yet reason forbids it, so I swallow the wrong—only because Master Jingguang urged Right Thought and Right Mindfulness. Tell me, Master: does Right Thought ALWAYS mean sacrificing myself for others?"
[ZH] “幽栖居士，您还不回去吗？”白风把小猪掩埋完后，走过来打招呼。
[EN] A compassionate smile warmed the old nun's face. "Buddhist wisdom is boundless, never one-size-fits-all. Right Thought shifts with circumstance—at times enduring wrong to spare others, at times letting others bear discomfort to save yourself. For the dilemma facing you today, enduring wrong is the sole path. I leave you one verse: *Wait through the clouds, and you will see the bright moon.* Amitabha. Affairs await at the nunnery; excuse me."
[ZH] 千里寻面向白风，第一次想与他多聊两句：“白风，我心中有疑惑，你能不能帮我解答？”
[EN] Watching the old nun vanish up the mountain path, Qianli Xun turned back to the willow with a heavy heart. Sunset faded as the pale moon climbed the sky, yet she lingered, turning those seven words over and over: *Wait through the clouds, and you will see the bright moon.* Did that mean true love waited AFTER marrying Chen Haoyu? Hardly—when did Buddhist precepts ever endorse adultery?!
[ZH] 白风嘿嘿笑道：“幽栖居士是才女，我大字不识几个，怎能为您解惑？您也太抬举我。”
[EN] "Still here, Youqi Jushi?" Having buried the piglet, Bai Feng strolled back over.
[ZH] “刚才尼姑庵的法师跟我聊了一会儿，说的尽是佛理，我难以理解，也难以接受，说不定你这个俗人反而能帮我解惑。”千里寻虽已答应明日嫁给陈浩宇，可还需确认她不得不嫁的理由是否正确。
[EN] Turning to him, Qianli Xun felt an unexpected urge to talk. "Bai Feng, I have a riddle in my head—mind helping me answer it?"
[ZH] 白风很是乐意：“您尽管说，我试试。”
[EN] Bai Feng grinned sheepishly. "Youqi Jushi is a great scholar, and I barely know a handful of characters—how could I answer riddles for you? Too much credit!"
[ZH] 千里寻没有立即开口，她想找到恰当的话语，不至于让白风对她有看法。可想来想去怎样开口都不行，干脆怎么想就怎么说：“佳和郡主找过我，说仪王心中有其他人，你可知此事？”
[EN] "That nun just fed me Buddhist philosophy I can neither fathom nor stomach. Maybe an ordinary mortal like you will cut straight to the bone." Having already agreed to marry Chen Haoyu tomorrow, she still craved confirmation that stepping away was right.
[ZH] 白风怎能不知？既然幽栖居士主动提起，他也没必要回避：“我当然知道。”
[EN] "Ask away—I will give it a shot."
[ZH] “你可知仪王心中的人是谁？”
[EN] Hesitating for a smooth way to phrase it and finding none, she laid it bare. "Princess Jiahe came to see me and said Prince Yi has someone else in his heart. You know about this?"
[ZH] “还用问，不就是幽栖居士您？”
[EN] "Of course I know," Bai Feng answered plainly.
[ZH] “如果……如果我硬要跟仪王在一起，你会怎么看我？”
[EN] "And you know who is in his heart?"
[ZH] “我会觉得幽栖居士不是好人！”
[EN] "Need you ask? Who else besides Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 白风态度明确，千里寻心中有说不出的滋味：“为何？”
[EN] "If... if I insisted on being with Prince Yi, how would you look at me?"
[ZH] “那还用问？王爷跟佳和郡主有婚约，还是官家赐婚，您却来搅局，能算好人吗？再说，我经常听喜兰讲，王爷病着时，都是郡主照料，您那时在哪里？凭什么王爷病好，就要跟您在一起？这两日郡主吃不下饭，睡不好觉，以泪洗面，多可怜，都是被幽栖居士给害的……”
[EN] "I would think Youqi Jushi was a terrible person!"
[ZH] 千里寻已听不下去：“别说了！今日在神柳下见到我的事不许告诉仪王，也请你劝劝他，猪不可能上树，让他好好珍惜眼前人。还有，王爷心中之人一定要对梦容保密，你放心，明日我会嫁到陈家，不会当你眼中的坏人。”
[EN] His blunt reply sent a bitter pang straight through her. "Why?"
[ZH] 春风悄然拂来，听到柳枝沙沙作响。千里寻没有心情感受春日晚风的舒适，带着莫落的心情离开。
[EN] "Need you ask?! His Highness is betrothed to Princess Jiahe by imperial decree—jumping in between them, how could anyone call that decent? Besides, Xilan tells me all the time that when His Highness lay near death, Her Highness nursed him night and day—where were you then? What gives you the right to claim him now that he is well? These last two days Her Highness cannot eat, cannot sleep, crying her eyes red—all because of Youqi Jushi..."
[ZH] 白风一人站在神柳下，自言自语：“我的话是不是说得太重？幽栖居士看上去很不高兴。不管了，只要你别跑来破坏王爷和郡主的感情就好。”
[EN] Unable to hear another syllable, Qianli Xun cut him off. "Enough! Do not breathe a word to Prince Yi that you saw me beneath the willow today. Tell him pigs will never climb trees, and tell him to cherish the girl right beside him. And swear you will never let Mengrong know who he loves! Rest easy—tomorrow I marry into the Chen family, and I will not become the villain in your eyes."
[ZH] 白风正准备走，又纳闷起来：“为何王爷和幽栖居士都喜欢来神柳下？”
[EN] A cool evening breeze rustled the willow branches. With zero heart left to enjoy the spring night, Qianli Xun walked away with a desolate chest.
[ZH] 他抬头借着月色将神柳上上下下瞧个遍，瞧不出任何吸引人之处，然后得出结论：“王爷跟幽栖居士肯定脑子都有问题，要不怎么都喜欢往神柳跟前跑，还喜欢盯着它看，好像树上有宝贝似的。”
[EN] Left alone under the moonlit boughs, Bai Feng scratched his head. "Did I lay it on too thick? Youqi Jushi looked pretty gutted. Whatever—long as you stop wrecking His Highness and Her Highness's marriage!" Starting off, he paused to squint up at the towering willow in confusion. "Why do both His Highness and Youqi Jushi love hanging around this tree?" Scanning the trunk and swaying branches under moonlight and spotting nothing special, he concluded, "Both His Highness and Youqi Jushi must be cracked in the head, running out here to stare at a tree like it grows gold!"
[ZH] 千里寻坐在马车上，回想白风刚才说过的话，直白中带着怨气，说明在他人看来选择远离仪王才是最正确的做法，她只能在委屈自己的道路上越走越远。
[EN] Riding home inside her carriage, Qianli Xun replayed Bai Feng's raw, resentful verdict. Through every outside pair of eyes, walking far clear of Prince Yi was the sole honorable choice. Down the lonely road of swallowing her own heart she went.

#CH 189
[ZH] 话说朱晞颜将婚礼的喜帖差人送到幽林别居时，申简辰正在神柳下看白风训练小猪爬树。
[ZH] 小猪怎么都爬不上去，白风着急，申简辰倒云淡风轻：“不能太着急，得慢慢来。”
[ZH] 白风心中埋怨：“王爷只顾张嘴说话，根本体会不到我一个堂堂侍卫的苦闷，学一身拳法无用武之地，却跑来训练猪爬树，传出去让人笑话！”
[ZH] 申简辰又看了一会儿，甚觉无趣，叮嘱道：“继续训练，我回别居一趟。”
[ZH] 千里寻正是在申简辰离开后不久来到神柳之下。
[ZH] 当申简辰回到别居的屋内，乔梦容缓缓走进，她手中拿着喜帖。
[ZH] “哥哥，你刚刚不在，知府大人差人送来喜帖，明日淑真与陈公子成亲，邀请你我参加，看来……淑真退婚之事以失败告终。”
[ZH] 乔梦容说淑真退婚失败的话时，故意加重语气，无非是想让哥哥明白，想退掉与她的婚约根本不可能。
[ZH] 申简辰盯着梦容手中的喜帖，没有要接的意思，甚至连一句话都未回应，便冲出门外。
[ZH] 乔梦容以为因她刚刚故意加重语气，哥哥生气才不言不语冲出去，不由失落道：“喜兰，哥哥是不是生我的气？”
[ZH] 喜兰判断不出，捡话语安慰：“郡主只说幽栖居士退婚失败，并未映射王爷，他怎会生气？”
[ZH] 乔梦容承认道：“我说淑真退婚未成功时，心里的确想让哥哥明白，官家赐婚他想退没那么容易。”
[ZH] 喜兰继续安慰：“奴婢根本没听出郡主的意思，说不定王爷也未听出。”
[ZH] “你怎能与哥哥相提并论？”乔梦容的语气中稍有责备。
[ZH] 喜兰赶紧回道：“是奴婢失礼，望郡主原谅。”
[ZH] 乔梦容并未真正生喜兰的气：“行了，跟我回屋。”
[ZH] 喜兰请示道：“要不要派人跟着王爷？”
[ZH] 乔梦容想了一会儿，说道：“不必，上次派人跟着哥哥，他知道后显然不高兴，只怕他会更加小心。”
[EN] When Zhu Xiyan's messenger delivered the wedding invitation to Youlin Retreat, Shen Jianchen had been down beneath the Divine Willow watching Bai Feng try to hoist the piglet up the bark.
[ZH] 喜兰继续请示：“要不要等王爷回来问问车夫？”
[EN] While Bai Feng fretted over the stubborn pig, Shen Jianchen stayed cool as breeze. "No rushing—take it slow."
[ZH] “不必，下人们私底下会胡乱议论，还是少让人知道为好。”乔梦容没在哥哥屋中见到白风，感到奇怪，“这两日怎么很少见白风？”
[EN] Bai Feng grumbled inside: *Easy for His Highness to flap his lips! A dignified royal guard armed with top fist arts reduced to coaching a pig up a tree—if word leaks out, I will be a laughingstock!*
[ZH] “回郡主，白风买回一头小猪，估计这会儿在神柳下训练小猪爬树。”
[EN] Bored after a short while, Shen Jianchen told him, "Keep drilling it. I am heading back to the retreat." Qianli Xun had arrived beneath the willow shortly after he left.
[ZH] “训练猪爬树？为何？”
[EN] Back in his study, Qiao Mengrong stepped slowly inside holding a crimson wedding card. "Brother, while you were out, Prefect Zhu sent an invitation—tomorrow Shuzhen marries Young Master Chen and invites us both. Looks like... Shuzhen's push to cancel the betrothal ended in failure."
[ZH] “奴婢只知是王爷的吩咐。”
[EN] She weighted those last words on purpose, driving home the quiet warning that breaking his betrothal to her was just as impossible.
[ZH] 乔梦容敏锐地感觉到哥哥如此做，肯定与他心中之人有关，温柔的语调中多出一丝愤恨：“等白风回来，把他请到我屋中，我有事要问。”
[EN] Staring at the red card in her hand, Shen Jianchen neither reached for it nor uttered a word—simply spun on his heel and bolted straight out the door.
[ZH] 喜兰立刻明白郡主的意思：“是。”
[EN] Assuming her pointed phrasing had angered him, Qiao Mengrong sagged in dismay. "Xilan, is Brother mad at me?"
[ZH] 千里寻明明说过不会嫁给陈浩宇，明日却要成婚，到底是为何？申简辰打算当面问个清楚。马车向城里疾驰而去，穿过闹市，直奔望月轩。
[EN] "Your Highness only mentioned Youqi Jushi's failed annulment—nothing aimed at His Highness. Why would he be mad?" Xilan tried soothing her.
[ZH] 到达后，韦嬷嬷出门迎接仪王，见他神色焦急，更加确定仪王对自家姑娘的心意，心中叹道：“唉，姑娘心善，不愿伤害郡主，可却不得不伤害仪王，希望仪王不要闹出大事。”
[EN] "I did say it wanting Brother to see he cannot easily break an imperial betrothal..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷上前行礼：“仪王殿下进屋坐坐。”
[EN] "Even I did not catch that meaning, so maybe His Highness missed it too."
[ZH] 申简辰问：“幽栖居士可在？我想见她。”
[EN] "How can you compare your wit to Brother's?" Mengrong chided faintly.
[ZH] “我家姑娘已出去半个时辰。”
[EN] "Forgive my loose tongue, Your Highness."
[ZH] “去了哪里？”
[EN] Softening at once, Mengrong sighed. "Enough. Come back to my room."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷摇摇头：“姑娘什么话都没说。”
[EN] "Should I send someone to tail His Highness?" Xilan asked.
[ZH] “她什么时候回来？”
[EN] "No," Mengrong decided after a pause. "He was clearly furious catching a tail last time—sending another will only put him on guard."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续摇头：“说不准。”
[EN] "Ask the driver when he returns, then?"
[ZH] “她明日真的要嫁到陈家？”
[EN] "No. Servants love gossip; keep fewer tongues wagging." Noticing Bai Feng absent from the courtyard, she frowned. "Where has Bai Feng vanished to these last two days?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷看出仪王不愿相信明日之事，肯定道：“正是。”
[EN] "He bought a piglet, Your Highness—probably down under the Divine Willow right now training it to climb trees."
[ZH] 申简辰一阵心酸，已无心在此逗留，他心酸到无法说出辞别之语，转身上了马车。
[EN] "Training a pig to climb a tree?! Whatever for?!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷对着马车行礼：“恭送仪王。”
[EN] "All I know is His Highness ordered it."
[ZH] 申简辰在马车中难过一会儿，猛然意识到，千里寻肯定是去看神柳，便吩咐车夫去城外的山脚下。
[EN] Sharp instinct flashed across Qiao Mengrong's face, icing her soft tone with resentment. "The instant Bai Feng gets back, summon him to my room. I have questions."
[ZH] 此时天已黑，当申简辰的马车正要出城时，千里寻乘坐的马车正从城门而入。他们并未瞧向车窗外，两人完美错过，各自朝不同的方向奔赴。
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰急急匆匆来到神柳下，借着月色将神柳周围瞧了个遍，根本没有千里寻的身影。他有种不祥的预感，难道他与千里寻真的不能在一起？
[EN] Qianli Xun had sworn she would never marry Chen Haoyu—so why was the wedding suddenly tomorrow?! Determined to get the truth face-to-face, Shen Jianchen whipped his carriage straight into Lin'an, racing through crowded lanes to Wangyue Pavilion.
[ZH] 申简辰走近神柳，用手摸着粗壮的树干：“神柳，你将我与千里寻一起带到这个时代，为何不给我们在一起的机会？难道让我穿越而来是为了彻底失去她？”
[EN] Matron Wei met him at the gate; one look at his frantic face sealed her suspicions about his heart, and she sighed inwardly: *Kind-hearted Miss refuses to hurt the princess, leaving Prince Yi crushed—pray he does not tear the roof off.* Dropping a curtsy, she said, "Please step inside, Your Highness."
[ZH] 神柳沉默不语。
[EN] "Is Youqi Jushi home? I need to see her."
[ZH] 申简辰失望地放下手：“你总是不言语，冷漠至极，我也没必要与你浪费时间，既然她不在此，我再去趟望月轩，不相信见不到她。”
[EN] "My young mistress stepped out an hour ago."
[ZH] 申简辰再次出发前往望月轩。到达时，望月轩的大门紧闭。
[EN] "Where did she go?"
[ZH] 他亲自上前敲门，过了许久，门才被打开。一位下人探出脑袋，见是仪王，赶快行礼：“仪王殿下。”
[EN] Matron Wei shook her head. "She left no word."
[ZH] 申简辰急切地问：“幽栖居士可回来？”
[EN] "When will she be back?"
[ZH] 下人回道：“姑娘明日出嫁，老爷要求从朱府出发，估摸着这会儿已经到达朱府。”
[EN] Another shake. "Hard to say."
[ZH] 终于得到确切消息，申简辰急急匆匆离开，又奔赴朱府，这一晚上他来来回回只为见她一面，既要当面问清楚，也要阻止这门婚事。
[EN] "Is she truly marrying into the Chen family tomorrow?"
[ZH] 来到朱府，申简辰不管不顾冲进侧门。朱家的下人很害怕，不敢说话，将贵人引至会客厅。
[EN] Reading the desperate disbelief on his face, Matron Wei confirmed softly, "Indeed she is."
[ZH] 申简辰并未落座，厉声吩咐：“把幽栖居士给我找来！”
[EN] A hollow ache slammed Shen Jianchen so hard he could not even squeeze out a farewell; turning silently, he climbed back into his carriage as Matron Wei bowed after him.
[ZH] 下人感到奇怪，仪王既焦急又生气，应该是为公务，不叫老爷却叫朱家姑娘，实在费解，又不敢多问，乖乖退出去唤自家姑娘。
[EN] Minutes down the street, realization struck: she had to be at the Divine Willow! Ordering the driver straight out the city gate toward the foothills, his carriage thundered out into the falling dusk at the exact moment Qianli Xun's carriage rolled in through the gate—neither glancing out the curtains, passing inches apart on opposite roads.
[ZH] 早已有下人将仪王到来之事报告给朱晞颜，他正忙着清点女儿的嫁妆，听下人来报，心中并不诧异，却十分为难，只怕仪王是来阻止两家的婚事。
[EN] Reaching the Divine Willow under moonlight, Shen Jianchen searched every shadow and found emptiness. Cold dread crept down his spine—were they truly barred from ever being together? Laying a palm on the thick bark, he whispered, "Divine Willow, you dragged Qianli Xun and me into this era—why deny us our shot? Did you send me across a thousand years just to lose her for good?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜无心再清点嫁妆，吩咐下人把家中其他人都叫到会客厅，希望集结家人的力量阻止即将发生的大事。
[EN] Only wind whispered through the leaves.
[ZH] 千里寻坐在朱淑真的园子里，盯着挂在一旁的婚服，心里莫名惆怅，难道就这么嫁了？又一想，既然决定要嫁，还犹豫个什么劲儿。
[EN] Dropping his hand in bitter frustration, he muttered, "Always silent, cold as stone—waste of breath talking to you! If she is not here, back to Wangyue Pavilion—I refuse to believe I cannot see her tonight!"
[ZH] 正想着，外面传来下人的声音：“姑娘，仪王来访，点名要见姑娘。”
[EN] Back at Wangyue Pavilion, the gates stood barred tight. Pounding the wood himself until a sleepy servant cracked it open and dropped in shock before Prince Yi, Shen Jianchen demanded, "Has Youqi Jushi returned?!"
[ZH] 千里寻“蹭”地起身，走到门外：“仪王在哪里？”
[EN] "Master ordered Miss to depart from Zhu Manor tomorrow, Your Highness—she must have reached Zhu Manor by now."
[ZH] 下人回道：“在会客厅。”
[EN] Hard lead at last! Back into the carriage and racing straight for Zhu Manor—criss-crossing the city all night just to see her face, get the truth, and smash this wedding!
[ZH] 一丝兴奋过后，千里寻又充满担忧，他该不会是前来阻止她嫁到陈家？若如此，该如何是好？
[EN] Arriving at Zhu Manor, Shen Jianchen stormed straight through the side gate. Terrified servants dared not block him and led him into the reception hall, where he stood without sitting and barked, "Bring Youqi Jushi to me right now!"
[ZH] 苦荞也来到门外，担忧道：“小姐，该怎么办？”
[EN] Bewildered why a fuming prince demanded the daughter instead of the Prefect, the servant scurried off to fetch her—while another had already alerted Zhu Xiyan mid-dowry-inventory. Unsurprised yet panicked that Prince Yi meant to wreck tomorrow's ceremony, Zhu Xiyan dropped the lists and summoned the entire family to the reception hall to form a united wall.
[ZH] 千里寻叹口气，说出不想说，又不得不说的话：“兵来将挡，水来土掩，即使他是仪王，也改变不了我的心意。”
[EN] Back in her old Zhu Manor courtyard, Qianli Xun sat staring at the crimson bridal gown hung by the wall in dull gloom. Was she really walking down the aisle tomorrow? *You already said yes—quit waffling!* Just then a servant called outside: "Miss, Prince Yi is here demanding to see you!"
[ZH] 苦荞替自家小姐难过，必须嫁的人比不上林觉，喜欢之人又嫁不得，错过一个乔公子，现在又得错过一个王爷。
[EN] Qianli Xun shot to her feet and stepped outside. "Where is he?"
[ZH] 千里寻与苦荞穿越朱府的花园，一路向会客厅而来。当她到达时，竟然发现爹娘与哥哥嫂嫂正跪在会客厅的门外。如此看来家人已知仪王的心思。
[EN] "In the reception hall."
[ZH] 千里寻的内心愈加难过，想起白风在神柳下说过的话：“王爷跟佳和郡主有婚约，还是官家赐婚，您却要来搅局，能算好人吗？”家人这么一跪，无非是求仪王放过她。
[EN] A sudden jolt of warmth spiked through her before worry drowned it out—had he come to stop tomorrow's wedding?
[ZH] 千里寻稳住情绪，走到向夫人跟前，伸手去扶：“娘，您跟爹，还有哥哥嫂嫂都起来吧，我的事情我来解决。”
[EN] Hurrying out beside her, Kuqiao fretted, "What do we do, Miss?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜坚决不肯：“你也跪下，仪王不答应放你，我们绝不起身。”
[EN] Letting out a long breath, Qianli Xun forced out words she hated speaking. "Meet soldiers with generals, block floods with earth. Even if he is Prince Yi, he cannot sway my decision."
[ZH] 老爷既发话，向夫人自然不敢起来，反而拉住女儿：“快跪下，求求仪王，不要破坏两家的婚事。”
[EN] Kuqiao's heart ached for her mistress: forced to marry a rake worse than Lin Jue, barred from every man she cared for—first losing Young Master Qiao, now losing a prince.
[ZH] 千里寻根本不愿为此事下跪：“你们既然愿意跪就跪着，我去找仪王面谈。”
[EN] Crossing the Zhu garden to the reception hall, Qianli Xun froze: her father, mother, elder brother, and sister-in-law all knelt on the flagstones outside the hall doorway! So her family knew Prince Yi's heart too—and knelt here begging him to let her go. Bai Feng's harsh words beneath the willow echoed sharp in her ears.
[ZH] 千里寻正要冲进屋内，听到仪王的声音：“你终于出现，让我好找。”
[EN] Steadying her pulse, she walked straight to Madam Xiang and reached for her arms. "Rise, Mother—Father, Brother, Sister-in-Law, get up! I will handle my own mess."
[ZH] 她抬起头，借着院内的灯光，分明看到仪王的身影。不知从何时起，那个身影也成为申简辰的，她比任何时候都想过去抱住他，可却不能，只好红着眼，冷笑道：“仪王来做什么？搞得我家人都跪在地上求您。”
[EN] "Kneel down too!" Zhu Xiyan barked. "Until Prince Yi promises to let you go, none of us rises!"
[ZH] 申简辰不想浪费时间，直言不讳：“我今晚来来回回找你，最终还是找到，说明只要努力，没有解决不了的难题。可你为何轻言放弃？明日就要嫁给陈浩宇？能不能答应我，跟我一起努力，让两家取消婚约？”
[EN] Refusing to defy her husband, Madam Xiang instead tugged her daughter's sleeve. "Kneel quick and beg Prince Yi not to ruin the wedding!"
[ZH] 陈文心紧张不已，今晚她算是确认了捶丸场的猜测，也不知仪王什么时候偷偷喜欢上淑真？此事若处理不好，陈家难免会得罪贵人。
[EN] "Kneel if you want—I am not kneeling for this!" Qianli Xun pulled free. "I will talk to Prince Yi face-to-face."
[ZH] 千里寻继续装作冷漠的样子：“人心变幻莫测，之前我是不同意，现在同意也很正常。再说我凭什么要答应与仪王一起努力？”
[EN] Just as she stepped toward the doorway, Prince Yi's voice rang out: "There you are at last—made me hunt half the night."
[ZH] 申简辰毫不避讳朱家人：“凭我喜欢你，想把你放在我身边。”
[EN] Lifting her eyes in the courtyard lantern glow, she saw his silhouette—no longer Zhao Pu, simply Shen Jianchen. Never had she wanted more to run straight into his arms, yet she choked it back, eyes stinging red behind a cold scoff. "What stunt is Your Highness pulling, making my whole family kneel in the dirt begging you?"
[ZH] 千里寻开心害羞之余，又急又怒：“仪王说话轻浮，先吃老娘一拳！”
[EN] Wasting zero breath, Shen Jianchen cut straight to the bone. "I chased all over town tonight and still tracked you down—proof that if you fight hard enough, no wall holds! Why give up so easy and wed Chen Haoyu tomorrow? Say the word and fight alongside me to break this betrothal!"
[ZH] 千里寻一拳打来，申简辰很自然地接住。
[EN] Kneeling nearby, Chen Wenxin held her breath—tonight proved her chuiwan hunch dead right! When on earth had Prince Yi fallen for Shuzhen?! One wrong slip and the Chens would cross imperial wrath!
[ZH] 她的拳头没有往日猛烈，却也不失力度，希望通过拳法让申简辰懂得她的心思，不要再做傻事。
[EN] Keeping her frosty mask up, Qianli Xun retorted, "People change their minds! I refused before, now I accept—what is so weird? And why on earth should I team up with Your Highness?"
[ZH] 两人对打的过程中，申简辰也想通过拳法告诉千里寻他坚决不会放弃。
[EN] Zero heed for the kneeling Zhu family, Shen Jianchen declared, "Because I love you and want you by my side!"
[ZH] 其他人甚是担心，一方面怕淑真得罪仪王，一方面又怕淑真受伤。眼前激烈对打的场面使朱家人不得不承认眼前的女儿的确不再是以前的淑真。
[EN] Thrilled, flushed, panicked, and furious all at once, Qianli Xun snapped, "Watch your loose tongue, Prince Yi—eat my fist!"
[ZH] 他们跪着不敢起身，担心焦虑中希望仪王能尽快放弃不理智的想法。
[EN] Her punch flew straight out; Shen Jianchen caught it smoothly. Her blows held less sting than usual yet kept sharp force—pouring her silent plea through every strike for him to stop throwing himself against a stone wall. Meeting strike for strike, Shen Jianchen answered right back through his palms: *Never giving you up.*
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷前来，见主人家都跪着，自家姑娘又与仪王打得激烈，即刻明白所为何事。也上前跪下：“仪王殿下与我家姑娘打来打去，又有何用？”
[EN] Watching from their knees, the Zhu family trembled—terrified Shuzhen might offend the prince or get hurt, while forced at last to admit this fierce fighter was nothing like the frail daughter they used to know.
[ZH] 两人本就不是真打，很快各自收起拳脚。
[EN] Hurrying into the courtyard and taking in the kneeling family and sparring pair at a glance, Matron Wei dropped straight to her knees too. "What good does trading fists with my young mistress do, Your Highness?!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷跪着行礼道：“仪王殿下的心意老爷和夫人还有我家姑娘尽知，事已至此，您还是退一步，大家才能各自安好。”
[EN] Never fighting in earnest, both dropped their hands at once.
[ZH] “退一步？你们是安好了，可我不安好。”申简辰拉起千里寻的手，“你跟我走吧，今晚就走，我带你离开临安，我也不当什么王爷，凭我们两个的本事，在这个时代生活下去不成问题。”
[EN] Bowing low from her knees, Matron Wei spoke steadily. "Master, Madam, and my young mistress all know Your Highness's heart. With things at this pass, take one step back so everyone can find peace."
[ZH] 当初文渊要带她离开时，她未答应，狠心将他舍弃；今日申简辰又提出，千里寻犹豫要不要答应。若答应，他们也可海阔天空，任意遨游，再说眼前的一切本就不属于她……
[EN] "Step back? YOU all find peace—what about me?!" Snatching Qianli Xun's hand, Shen Jianchen locked eyes with her. "Come with me right now! Tonight! We leave Lin'an, I ditch this princely title, and with our skills we will thrive anywhere in this dynasty!"
[ZH] “仪王殿下有没有想过您带我家姑娘离开，佳和郡主该怎么办？”
[EN] When Wenyuan had offered to elope, she had hardened her heart and walked away; hearing Shen Jianchen ask tonight made her heart stutter. Say yes, and the wide sky opened before them—none of these cages truly belonged to her anyway...
[ZH] 若不是韦嬷嬷的话，千里寻很可能会点头答应，与申简辰冲出朱府。可韦嬷嬷的话正好击中她内心最脆弱之处，她抽回手：“仪王还是回去吧，淑真嫁到陈家之心坚不可摧。”
[EN] "Has Your Highness spared one thought for what happens to Princess Jiahe if you carry my mistress off?!"
[ZH] 申简辰再次抓住她的手：“我不会放你走，我娶你之心也坚不可摧。”
[EN] Without Matron Wei's cutting question, Qianli Xun might well have nodded and bolted straight out of Zhu Manor with him. Hit dead center in her softest spot, she pulled her hand free. "Go back, Your Highness. My mind to marry into the Chen family is cast in iron."
[ZH] 手被申简辰紧紧抓住，与其说是千里寻无法挣脱，还不如说是她根本不愿挣脱。
[EN] Shen Jianchen seized her hand back tight—and truth be told, she barely even tried pulling free. "Do not fret over Mengrong, Matron Wei—I will see her well cared for..."
[ZH] 只听申简辰说：“韦嬷嬷不必担心，我会将梦容安置妥当……”
[EN] Before he finished, a trembling, familiar voice shattered the night:
[ZH] 话还未说完，传来熟悉的声音：“哥哥……原来……你心中之人……是……是……是淑真……”
[EN] "Brother... so the one in your heart... really is... Shuzhen..."
[ZH] 乔梦容满脸泪痕出现在面前，千里寻已无法再享受被申简辰抓住手的幸福感。她用力甩开，满心愧疚，埋怨自己不该产生想要跟申简辰远走高飞的想法。
[EN] Standing at the archway, cheeks glistening with tears, was Qiao Mengrong. Every drop of warmth drained from Qianli Xun's veins; ripping her hand free of Shen Jianchen's grip, she burned with shame for daring to dream of running away with him.
[ZH] 乔梦容因痛苦过度，站不稳，向下倒去……
[EN] Swaying under the blow, Qiao Mengrong's knees gave out—
[ZH] 申简辰见状一个箭步冲上前，将梦容扶住……
[EN] In one blur of motion, Shen Jianchen lunged forward and caught her before she hit the stones.

#CH 190
[ZH] 话说白风从神柳处回到幽林别居，被喜兰叫到郡主房中。一路上他已猜到郡主叫他的原因，一再叮嘱自己千万不能说漏嘴。
[ZH] 果不其然，郡主开口说：“白风，你经常跟哥哥在一起，应该知道他常去哪里，老实告诉我，我定不会亏待你。”
[ZH] 白风装作轻松的样子：“王爷除待在幽林别居外，会去乔家、府衙，偶尔为公事会去朱府，对了，还去过望月轩，郡主都知道。”
[ZH] 乔梦容以为自己没将话说明白，更加直白地问：“哥哥说他有喜欢的人，你可知道是谁？”
[ZH] “喜兰曾问过我，郡主今日又问，可我还是不知道。郡主，您别瞎想，我跟王爷这么久，从来没见过他对哪个女孩子像对您这么好。”白风甚觉违心，可又不得不如此说。
[ZH] “哪是我瞎想？那天你也亲耳听到，是哥哥亲口说的，我看你是不愿告诉我，既如此，你走吧，我迟早会知道哥哥心中的人到底是谁。”
[ZH] 问不出所以然，乔梦容不知该如何是好，此刻除了喜兰，她连个帮手都没有。
[ZH] 白风离开后，乔梦容悲戚一阵儿，无意中瞧到桌上的喜帖，想到淑真，明日她就要成亲，不如去看看她，再与她聊聊心事。
[ZH] 乔梦容乘坐马车先去趟望月轩，下人说淑真已回到朱府，她便来到朱府。
[ZH] 竟然发现哥哥的马车停在外面，担忧的情绪立刻减少许多，原来哥哥是来朱府见知府大人，不如见完淑真再与哥哥一同回去。
[ZH] 朱府的下人见佳和郡主前来，既不能照实说，也不敢阻拦，只说仪王与朱家人都在会客厅。
[ZH] 乔梦容要求下人带路，与喜兰往会客厅方向而去。她万万没想到到达后竟然目睹哥哥拉着淑真的手，还说要带她离开。
[ZH] 乔梦容只觉心口被堵住，全身跟着变得无力，眼泪控制不住地流下来，用颤抖的声音说出那些话，然后向地上倒去……
[ZH] 申简辰动作迅速，梦容并未倒在地上，被他稳稳扶住。他抱起梦容，回头对千里寻说：“不要嫁！等着我！”
[ZH] 然后抱着梦容向朱府外匆忙走去……
[ZH] 千里寻忍住泪水，她不想再为感情而流泪，因文渊已流得够多。她只顾一味埋怨自己，如果她不对申简辰产生感情，一切就不会发生。
[ZH] 朱晞颜松口气，佳和郡主来得实在及时，晕得也恰到好处，见仪王不得不离开，带头行礼，大声说：“恭送仪王！”
[ZH] 其他人也跟着大声说：“恭送仪王！”
[ZH] 等仪王走远，朱家人连同韦嬷嬷都从地上爬起来。大家跪的时间有些长，起来不免适应一番。
[ZH] 陈文心见淑真愣在那里，一言不发，走过去：“淑真，就当刚才发生的是一场梦，晚上好好休息，明日还要成亲。”
[ZH] “成亲？是，明日还要成亲。”千里寻像是提醒自己，继而担忧道，“嫂嫂，恐怕以后梦容不会再当我是朋友。”
[ZH] 陈文心安慰道：“不要想那么多……”
[ZH] 陈文心还未说完，被朱晞颜的声音打断：“不必对她废话！都回去吧！”
[ZH] 朱晞颜走到女儿跟前，指着她：“你给我记着，嫁到陈家后本本分分过日子！少去招惹仪王！！
[ZH] 朱景元担心爹收拾妹妹，上前扶好朱晞颜：“爹，您也跪累了，我扶您回屋休息。”
[ZH] 朱晞颜向女儿“哼”了一声，带着不满的情绪，被儿子扶着离开。
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷扶着向夫人，她只顾抹眼泪。见老爷已走，才来到女儿跟前：“淑真，娘不图你嫁得有多好，只希望你日子过得平平安安，不要再有什么波折，可为什么总是不顺？呜呜……！”
[ZH] 陈文心又安慰婆婆：“快顺了，快顺了，只要嫁给浩宇就顺了，婆婆快别再哭，多不吉利。”
[EN] Returning to Youlin Retreat from the Divine Willow earlier that evening, Bai Feng had been summoned straight to the princess's chambers by Xilan. Guessing the reason on the walk over, he swore to keep his mouth clamped shut.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷劝道：“少夫人说得对，朱家有喜事，您该乐，而不是哭。”
[EN] Sure enough, Qiao Mengrong opened straight away: "Bai Feng, you stay at Brother's side day in and day out and know everywhere he goes. Tell me the truth, and I will reward you handsomely."
[ZH] 向夫人这才停止哭泣，用巾帕擦擦脸上的泪水：“苦荞，快扶小姐回去。”
[EN] Putting on an easy air, Bai Feng answered, "Aside from Youlin Retreat, His Highness visits Qiao Manor, the prefecture yamen, Zhu Manor on official errands—oh, and Wangyue Pavilion, all places Your Highness already knows."
[ZH] 苦荞走到小姐身边：“小姐，我陪您回园子。”
[EN] Thinking she had been too subtle, Mengrong pressed straight out: "Brother told me he loves someone. Do you know who?"
[ZH] 千里寻没有说任何话，向母亲行过礼，默然离开。
[EN] "Xilan asked me before, and now Your Highness asks again, yet I truly do not know," Bai Feng lied against his conscience. "Stop tormenting yourself, Your Highness—in all my time serving His Highness, I have never seen him treat another girl half as warmly as he treats you."
[ZH] 向夫人担忧道：“希望明日顺顺利利，不要发生任何事。”
[EN] "Tormenting myself? You heard him say it with his own mouth that night! Fine—if you refuse to talk, get out. I will uncover who holds Brother's heart sooner or later."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“不会，夫人放宽心，姑娘肯定会老老实实嫁过去。”
[EN] Left with no answers and no ally outside Xilan, Qiao Mengrong sank into gloom until her eyes landed on the red wedding card on the table. Shuzhen married tomorrow—why not go see her and talk her heart out?
[ZH] “有嬷嬷这话，我就放心了。”向夫人在儿媳和韦嬷嬷陪伴下回屋休息。
[EN] Riding first to Wangyue Pavilion, where servants told her Shuzhen had returned to Zhu Manor, she steered straight there—and breathed a sudden sigh of relief spotting her brother's carriage parked outside! So he had only come to see Prefect Zhu; she could visit Shuzhen and ride home together with Brother!
[ZH] 朱府终于回归平静，千里寻的内心却平静不下来。今日发生太多事，她的人生之路再次被定格，她已无路可退。既如此，明日便老老实实嫁了，省得再惹出更多事端来。
[EN] Caught in a vise, Zhu Manor's servants dared neither blurt the truth nor block Princess Jiahe, saying only that Prince Yi and the Zhu family were at the reception hall. Following a guide there with Xilan, she walked straight into the sight of her brother clutching Shuzhen's hand and promising to elope with her!
[ZH] 她躺在闺床上，翻来覆去，回想发生的一切，充满对梦容的担忧，不知她可好些？不知她以后再见到自己会如何？
[EN] Chest squeezed breathless and limbs turning to water, tears poured down Qiao Mengrong's cheeks as those broken words left her throat, and she crumpled toward the stones—only for Shen Jianchen's swift arms to catch her. Scooping her up, he glanced back at Qianli Xun with blazing eyes. "Do not marry him! Wait for me!" Then he strode rapidly out of Zhu Manor with Mengrong in his arms.
[ZH] 她也回想起申简辰走时说的话：“不要嫁！等着我！”
[EN] Qianli Xun fought back her tears; she had shed enough over Wenyuan and refused to weep over love again. She blamed only herself—if she had never let feelings for Shen Jianchen creep back in, none of this wreckage would exist.
[ZH] 千里寻自言自语道：“都怪你，为何要跑来朱府？老娘才不会等你……。申简辰，早知如此，我应该在地震前接受你，可谁会想到世事难料，你忘了我吧，好好对待梦容。”
[EN] Exhaling hard—Princess Jiahe's arrival and swoon could not have been timed better!—Zhu Xiyan led the kneeling line in a loud chorus: "Farewell, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 申简辰把乔梦容抱上马车，将她搂在怀里，向幽林别居赶去。他并不后悔今晚做下令梦容伤心之事，梦容一时接受不了十分正常，相信慢慢她会从痛苦中走出来。
[EN] Once Prince Yi vanished beyond the gate, the Zhu family and Matron Wei stiffly clambered to their feet after kneeling so long.
[ZH] 乔梦容之所以晕倒，一半是因为难以接受哥哥心中之人竟然是她的闺中密友；一半儿不过是她装的而已。她实在不愿面对眼前的一切，只有晕倒才可避免尴尬和羞辱，也只有晕倒才能立刻将哥哥抢回来。
[EN] Seeing Shuzhen standing frozen and mute, Chen Wenxin stepped beside her. "Treat what just happened like a bad dream, Shuzhen. Get some sleep tonight—you have a wedding tomorrow."
[ZH] 喜兰真是配合，她装作晕倒时，她离得最近，并未迅速去扶，应是了解她的心思。
[EN] "A wedding... yes, tomorrow is the wedding." Coming back to herself, Qianli Xun murmured in quiet dread, "Sister-in-Law, I doubt Mengrong will ever count me a friend again."
[ZH] 靠在哥哥怀里，乔梦容甚是享受，也倍感悲哀，哥哥心中之人是她的好友，她该如何是好？
[EN] "Stop dwelling on that—"
[ZH] 她回忆哥哥与淑真相处的场景，实在找不出任何破绽。只怪自己太单纯善良，没有发现他们两个不对劲儿，也许他们在她面前偷偷眉来眼去不知多少回，岂不是把她当成傻子？
[EN] "Save your breath on her! Everyone back to your rooms!" Zhu Xiyan cut across, marching up to point a finger in his daughter's face. "Hear me well: once you enter Chen Manor, keep your head down and live clean! Stay clear of Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 到底他们从何时开始？乔梦容闭着眼，用心搜索时间。淑真明明喜欢的是乔公子，什么时候对哥哥动了心思？
[EN] Afraid his father might strike her, Zhu Jingyuan stepped in to take his arm. "Father, your knees must ache—let me help you back to rest."
[ZH] 乔梦容仔细回想，认为应该是哥哥在幽林别居举办完诗会后。记得就是从那以后，淑真才不再对哥哥有看法。
[EN] With a dark snort at his daughter, Zhu Xiyan let his son lead him off.
[ZH] 本以为她是为帮她才会说哥哥的好，现在才知原来她是喜欢上哥哥，才会说哥哥的好。
[EN] Dabbing away tears on Matron Wei's arm, Madam Xiang approached once her husband left. "Shuzhen, Mother never asked for some lofty match—only a calm, safe life without storms. Why does nothing ever go smooth for you? Wuuu!"
[ZH] 淑真定是得不到乔公子，才移情别恋勾引哥哥，看来曾经被百姓传的谣言说得一点儿没错，她就是个行为不检点的女子。
[EN] "Smooth days are right around the corner, Mother-in-Law!" Chen Wenxin soothed her. "Once she marries Haoyu, it all settles down! Dry those tears—crying on wedding eve brings bad luck!"
[ZH] 乔梦容甚是后悔与淑真做朋友，后悔不该在知道哥哥心中有了他人后，跑去淑真面前诉苦，还求她帮忙，傻傻地给她提供了一次见哥哥的机会。
[EN] "Young Madam is right," Matron Wei agreed. "A wedding calls for smiles, not tears."
[ZH] 她也立刻想通，哥哥为何从永泰茶楼回来会改变想法，主动提出要回汴梁亲自要求官家取消婚约……
[EN] Wiping her cheeks with a handkerchief, Madam Xiang nodded. "Kuqiao, walk Miss back to her courtyard."
[ZH] 乔梦容越想越恨，不由自主将哥哥的衣裳紧紧拽在手中。
[EN] Without a word, Qianli Xun curtsied to her mother and withdrew alongside Kuqiao.
[ZH] 申简辰发觉，轻声唤道：“梦容，你醒了？”
[EN] "Pray tomorrow passes without another hitch," Madam Xiang fretted.
[ZH] 乔梦容不能再伪装下去，她睁开泪眼，紧紧抱住哥哥。
[EN] "Not a hitch, Madam—rest easy, Miss will marry cleanly," Matron Wei reassured her as they escorted Madam Xiang inside.
[ZH] “哥哥，你别离开我，要是没有你，我死的心都有。呜呜……”
[EN] Silence settled back over Zhu Manor, yet Qianli Xun's mind churned nonstop. Every bridge behind her had burned to ash; going through with tomorrow's wedding cleanly was the sole way to prevent fresh disasters. Tossing on her bed, she worried over Mengrong—was she alright, and how would she look at her next time?—while Shen Jianchen's parting vow echoed loud: *Do not marry him! Wait for me!*
[ZH] 申简辰没有做出任何回应，梦容正处于悲伤之际，说什么都会听不进去，等她平复情绪后，再与她好好聊聊心里话。
[EN] "All your fault for storming Zhu Manor," she whispered to the dark ceiling. "I am NOT waiting for you... Shen Jianchen, if I had known things would turn out like this, I should have said yes before the earthquake. Who knew life twists like this? Forget me—treat Mengrong well."
[ZH] 抱着梦容离开朱府时，申简辰叮嘱千里寻：“不要嫁！等着我！”那一刻他已从千里寻的眼神中看出，她明日必然会嫁。
[EN] Inside the carriage racing back to Youlin Retreat, Shen Jianchen cradled Qiao Mengrong in his arms. He felt no regret for breaking the truth tonight—shock was inevitable, yet in time she would heal and move on.
[ZH] 他该怎么办？申简辰想到抢亲的办法，不如明日借仪王的身份，调动临安兵力，去陈家抢亲，不相信搅不乱两家的婚礼，到时他便带着千里寻离开。
[EN] Truth be told, Qiao Mengrong's faint had been half genuine shock at finding her best friend was her rival, and half sheer calculation—the quickest way to dodge humiliation and yank her brother straight back into her arms! Xilan had played along sharp as a tack, holding back instead of catching her so Brother would step in.
[ZH] 眼下的情形他只能顾一头，他已想好，将属于赵朴的家财大部分留给梦容，也算是替他照顾妹妹。
[EN] Nestling against his chest brought both comfort and stabbing agony. Replaying every scene between Brother and Shuzhen, she found zero slips—only cursed her own blind trust! How many secret glances had they traded right under her nose, laughing at her like a fool?! When had it started? Shuzhen had loved Young Master Qiao—when had she set her sights on Brother? Right after Brother's poetry salon at Youlin Retreat! That was the exact turning point when Shuzhen stopped badmouthing him and started singing his praises! Unable to win Young Master Qiao, Shuzhen had pivoted to seducing Brother—maybe those old street rumors calling her shameless had been dead right all along! How Mengrong loathed herself for befriending her, pouring out her heartbreak, begging her help—handing Shuzhen a golden excuse to meet Brother at Yongtai Teahouse and twist him into demanding an annulment from His Majesty!
[ZH] 申简辰在心中自语：“赵朴啊赵朴，我不能为了替你照顾妹妹放弃我爱的人，本想把梦容放在身边，当妹妹一样看待，计划赶不上变化，我想带着我爱的人远走高飞，不想再当什么王爷，也不想再理会你留给我的婚约。”
[EN] Hatred surged so hot her fingers clenched tight into Shen Jianchen's robe.
[ZH] 乔梦容哭了一会儿，见哥哥不言语，心中更是担忧，把哥哥抱得更紧：“呜呜！哥哥，你为什么不说话？你是不是想要抛弃我？呜呜……！”
[EN] Feeling the tug, he murmured, "Mengrong, you are awake?"
[ZH] 申简辰本以为乔梦容醒来会开口质问他为何会喜欢淑真，可乔梦容并未质问，只是一味地哭，说些可怜巴巴的话。
[EN] Dropping the swoon, she opened swimming eyes and clung to his neck. "Do not leave me, Brother! Without you, I would rather die! Wuuu!"
[ZH] 听着马车碾压道路的声音混着梦容可怜巴巴的哭声，申简辰觉得自己实在可恶。即便如此，他的心意也不会改变。
[EN] Staying quiet while her grief raged—reasoning now would bounce off deaf ears—Shen Jianchen made his own ruthless choice inside. Seeing the steel in Qianli Xun's eyes as he carried Mengrong out, he knew she meant to board that bridal sedan tomorrow. Only one card remained: use Prince Yi's rank to mobilize Lin'an troops tomorrow, raid the Chen bridal procession, wreck the wedding, and take Qianli Xun far away! He could only save one side; he would leave the bulk of Zhao Pu's wealth to Mengrong as his duty to the late prince. *Sorry, Zhao Pu,* he thought silently. *I cannot throw away the woman I love to play your sister's husband. I meant to keep her close as family, yet plans cannot outrun fate—I am taking my love far away, ditching this royal title and your betrothal for good.*
[ZH] “梦容，你现在什么都清楚，知道我心里喜欢的人是谁，不如成全我……”
[EN] Feeling his silence, panic shook Qiao Mengrong harder as she clung tighter. "Why won't you answer, Brother?! Are you going to abandon me?! Wuuu!"
[ZH] 乔梦容的哭声停止，抱紧他的手不由自主松开，坐端后，擦干泪水，用冷冷的声音说：“明日她就要成亲，我成全哥哥又有何用？”
[EN] Hearing her pitiful sobs blend with the rumble of carriage wheels made Shen Jianchen feel like the lowest heel alive—yet his resolve stayed iron. "Now you know everything, Mengrong—know who holds my heart. Let me go and grant us our chance..."
[ZH] “只要你愿意成全，其他事我自有办法。”
[EN] Her sobs cut off dead. Releasing him, she sat bolt upright, wiped her eyes dry, and spoke in an ice-cold voice. "She marries tomorrow—what good does me letting you go do?"
[ZH] 哥哥语气坚定，就像一根针刺进乔梦容的内心，她痛了一会儿，继续冷冷地说：“哥哥还是清醒些，现在不是我成全不成全的事，官家赐婚，想退根本不可能。”
[EN] "Just grant your blessing—leave the rest to me."
[ZH] 申简辰不再说什么，反正他准备豁出去，为了个人幸福，不想再顾及其他。
[EN] His unyielding tone pierced like a needle. Swallowing the stab, she kept her voice wintry. "Wake up, Brother. This is not up to me—His Majesty decreed our marriage, and canceling it is impossible."
[ZH] 回到幽林别居后，将乔梦容送回闺房，吩咐喜兰伺候郡主睡下，申简辰忧心忡忡地离开。
[EN] Saying no more, Shen Jianchen steeled himself to gamble everything for his own happiness.
[ZH] 哥哥刚离开，乔梦容迅速起身，冲到桌前，拿起喜帖，一边撕，一边用柔弱的声音狠狠地说：“朱淑真！枉我真心将当你朋友看待，你却如此对我，从今往后，我不再是你的朋友！”
[EN] Back at Youlin Retreat, he carried her to her chambers, told Xilan to tuck her in, and walked out with a furrowed brow.
[ZH] 与淑真的友情就像眼前撕碎的喜帖，纷纷落在地上。乔梦容还不解恨，用脚狠劲踩着：“朱淑真！你妄想得到哥哥，别说你即将成亲，即使你未嫁，我也绝不会让你轻易得到哥哥！”
[EN] The second the door shut behind him, Qiao Mengrong sprang off the bed, snatched the red wedding invitation off the table, and ripped it to shreds with soft, venomous fury. "Zhu Shuzhen! I treated you like a true sister, and this is how you stab me?! From this hour on, we are enemies!"
[ZH] 乔梦容发泄完，踉跄着走到闺床跟前，趴在床上哭起来：“呜呜……！”用哭声送别她失去的友情。
[EN] Like their dead friendship, red paper confetti snowed across the floor. Stomping the scraps under her slippers, she hissed, "Dream on about getting Brother, Zhu Shuzhen! Even if you were NOT marrying tomorrow, I would never let you have him!"
[ZH] 喜兰没有劝郡主，她太可怜，几日之内不仅知道王爷心中有他人，还失去与幽栖居士的友情，发泄发泄也是好的。
[EN] Spent, she stumbled back to bed and buried her face in the pillows weeping aloud—sending off her dead friendship in tears. Xilan let her cry without interruption: learning her betrothed loved another AND losing her closest friend in one blow—letting it out was mercy.

#CH 191
[ZH] 昨晚，王爷回来得晚，白风还没来得及将小猪累死的事禀报。
[ZH] 一早起来，他在王爷的房门外向里问：“王爷，您可醒？”
[ZH] 没听到王爷的声音，门却被打开，王爷衣着整齐，脸色稍暗，应该是晚上没睡好。
[ZH] 白风禀报道：“王爷，小猪根本不愿爬树，被活活累死。”
[ZH] 申简辰暗淡的面色中略显悲痛，难道他真的与千里寻没办法走到一起？他偏不信这个邪！
[ZH] 他拿出节度使的令牌，递给白风：“你拿着我的令牌亲自去趟练兵署，带一千人等，到城门口与我会合。”
[ZH] 白风接过，兴奋道：“王爷，是要打仗吗？”
[ZH] 申简辰坚定地说：“不，我要抢亲。”
[ZH] 白风脸上的笑容立刻消失，今日正是幽栖居士与陈家公子成亲，难道王爷想……
[ZH] 白风跪在地上，阻止道：“请王爷三思！”
[ZH] 申简辰根本不愿放弃：“你是我的侍卫，只有听命的份儿，没资格阻拦我，还不快去！”
[ZH] 白风只好起身，拿着令牌匆忙走出主院，犹豫再三后，奔跑着来到佳和郡主的小院外，敲击院门：“喜兰！喜兰！开开门！快开开门！”
[ZH] 喜兰很快从里面打开门：“一大早的，瞎吵吵什么？”
[ZH] “郡主呢？我要见郡主。”白风将喜兰挤到一边，进入院内。
[ZH] 乔梦容从闺房出来，站在门口，双眼红肿：“你找我何事？”
[ZH] 白风顾不上行礼，急切地说：“王爷他疯了，吩咐我到练兵署调遣一千士兵，他要抢亲，我是侍卫，只能听命于王爷，现在只有郡主能够阻止。”
[ZH] 白风匆忙说完，转身跑出去，他还需按照王爷吩咐前去调遣士兵。
[ZH] 乔梦容被白风的话差点击晕，哥哥的确是疯了！他竟然为朱淑真动用临安兵力！乔梦容既羡慕又嫉妒，因嫉妒恨意萌生。
[ZH] 她缓了一会儿，用充满恨意的语气说：“喜兰，找把匕首来。”
[ZH] 喜兰担心郡主想不开：“郡主，您千万别想不开！”
[ZH] 乔梦容没有心情解释：“去！让你去就去！”
[ZH] 喜兰进到屋内，找来一把匕首，双手捧给郡主。
[ZH] 乔梦容接过，拔出刀刃，明晃晃的，闪得她睁不开眼，她把匕首插回鞘中，藏入袖内：“随我去找哥哥。”
[ZH] 乔梦容来到仪王的院中，摸着袖中的匕首，待会儿若哥哥执意要去，她便拔出匕首用自己的性命阻止他。
[ZH] 喜兰向屋内说：“王爷，郡主来看您。”
[ZH] 屋内无人应答。
[ZH] 喜兰提高声音，继续说：“王爷，郡主来看您。”
[ZH] 依然无人应答。
[ZH] 喜兰进屋里里外外瞧了一遍，出来回道：“郡主，王爷不在。”
[ZH] 乔梦容心想哥哥八成已出去，她更加焦急，带喜兰匆匆来到幽林别居大门口，正好碰到李管家。
[ZH] 李管家上前行礼：“郡主是要去参加朱陈两家的婚礼吗？为何不跟王爷一起？他刚刚走，今日倒奇怪，没坐马车，骑马出去的。”
[ZH] 还没顾上问，李管家已将情况说明，乔梦容尽量用平稳的语气说：“是我让哥哥先行，辛苦李管家吩咐车夫套辆马车，我也要前去参加婚宴。”
[ZH] “早给郡主预备好，正在外面候着。”李管家一脸笑嘻嘻的样子，根本不知道两位主人之间发生的大事。
[ZH] 乔梦容谢过李管家，出门坐上马车，让喜兰吩咐车夫直接去迎亲队伍的必经之路。哥哥要抢亲，必然会在迎亲途中。
[ZH] ……
[ZH] 一大早，千里寻被苦荞唤醒，简单用过早饭，开始收拾妆容。
[ZH] 昨晚什么时候睡着，到底睡了多久，她并不知，反正醒来后觉得困意未消。
[ZH] 苦荞一边为小姐装扮，一边聊天儿。不是她喜欢说话，是希望小姐不要再想不开心的事。
[EN] Returning late the night before, Bai Feng had missed reporting the piglet's death. At dawn he stepped outside his prince's door to call, "Your Highness, awake?"
[ZH] “小姐，陈家也算懂事，送的是红色嫁衣。”
[EN] Before he finished, the door swung open—His Highness already dressed, dark rings shadowing sleepless eyes.
[ZH] 千里寻懒懒地说：“嫁衣本来就是红色，难道还有其他颜色不成？”
[EN] "Your Highness, the piglet refused to climb and dropped dead from exhaustion."
[ZH] 苦荞用螺子黛轻轻为小姐描眉：“难道小姐没听过红男绿女？”
[EN] A flicker of bleakness crossed Shen Jianchen's face. Was he truly barred from ever being with Qianli Xun? *Screw fate!* Drawing his Military Commissioner's command token, he slapped it into Bai Feng's palm. "Take my token to the Military Training Office, muster one thousand troops, and meet me at the city gate."
[ZH] “红男绿女？什么意思？”
[EN] Bai Feng's eyes lit up. "Are we riding to war, Your Highness?!"
[ZH] “意思就是女子成亲时，可以穿绿色的婚服。”
[EN] "No," Shen Jianchen said flatly. "I am snatching a bride."
[ZH] “还可以穿绿色，这么有趣。可为什么我的嫁衣是红色的？”
[EN] The grin died off Bai Feng's face—today was Youqi Jushi's wedding to Young Master Chen! Dropping straight to both knees, he pleaded, "Think thrice, Your Highness!"
[ZH] “小姐是知府千金，陈家虽有钱，却无人在朝中为官，小姐嫁过去可是低嫁，当然需穿红色，所以我说陈家人还算懂事，知道我家小姐嫁到陈家是为陈家添彩。我记得少奶奶嫁到朱家时，穿的可是绿色婚服，她可是高嫁。”
[EN] "You are my guard—obey orders, do not lecture me! Move!"
[ZH] 描完眉，苦荞仔细瞧小姐的脸：“我描眉的手法越来越好，小姐今日的眉毛比往日都好看。”
[EN] Forced to rise, Bai Feng clutched the token and dashed out of the main courtyard—paused three strides out in fierce conflict, then sprinted straight to Princess Jiahe's courtyard and pounded the door. "Xilan! Xilan! Open up! Quick!"
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会眉毛之事：“婚礼又不符合我心意，随便装扮装扮即可，别太当回事。”
[EN] Yanking the door wide, Xilan snapped, "What are you yelling for at crack of dawn?!"
[ZH] 苦荞又开始为小姐施粉：“虽不符合心意，也是成亲。打今日起，我要跟小姐去陈家生活，第一日一定要开开心心，以后才会永远开心。”
[EN] "Where is Her Highness?! I need to see her!" Brushing past Xilan into the yard, Bai Feng spotted Qiao Mengrong stepping to her doorway with swollen red eyes.
[ZH] “没想到，你这么迷信。”
[EN] "What do you want?"
[ZH] “哪是迷信？是一种美好的希望。”
[EN] Skipping ceremony, Bai Feng blurted out, "His Highness has lost his mind! He sent me to muster a thousand soldiers to raid the wedding and snatch the bride! As his guard I have to obey—only Your Highness can stop him!" Spinning around, he raced off to fulfill his orders.
[ZH] 苦荞的话给千里寻带来启示，既然决定要嫁给陈浩宇，又何必不开心？还是放松心情，忘记昨晚之事，轻轻松松进入陈家。
[EN] The shock nearly knocked Qiao Mengrong off her feet. Brother really had lost his mind—mobilizing Lin'an troops for Zhu Shuzhen?! Envy and jealousy erupted into scorching hatred. Catching her breath, she hissed, "Xilan, bring me a dagger."
[ZH] 装扮完后，换好婚服，戴好凤冠，千里寻来到爹娘房中，哥嫂与韦嬷嬷也都坐着等她。她进屋拜过爹娘，还未起身，传来一阵奏乐之声。
[EN] Terrified, Xilan gasped, "Do not do anything rash, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“起来吧，新郎已到门外，随爹娘走出朱府。”
[EN] "Fetch it—NOW!"
[ZH] 千里寻起身后，银杏端着木盘过来，里面放着一顶红盖头。苦荞将红盖头取了，为小姐盖在头上。
[EN] Dashing inside, Xilan brought out a sheathed dagger with both hands. Drawing the blade just enough to flash bright steel in her eyes, Qiao Mengrong slid it back into its sheath and tucked it up her sleeve. "Come with me to find Brother."
[ZH] 千里寻只觉眼前一片红色，被苦荞扶着，跟在爹娘后面往外走。她的身后是哥哥和嫂嫂，还有韦嬷嬷。
[EN] Arriving at Prince Yi's courtyard clutching the hidden hilt—ready to put her own life on the blade if he refused to yield—she waited as Xilan called twice into the empty room, checked inside, and hurried back out. "Your Highness, His Highness is gone!"
[ZH] 既是嫁女儿的大事，朱家自然敞开正门，千里寻也是第一次从正门走出。她悄悄将红盖头撩起一些，一眼瞅到坐在高头大马上身着红色婚服的陈浩宇，他倒一脸喜庆，像个没事人似的。
[EN] Heart racing, Mengrong hurried straight to the front gate, bumping right into Butler Li.
[ZH] 陈浩宇跳下大马，先向岳父岳母行礼。朱晞颜点点头，与向夫人朝两边闪开。陈浩宇伸出手，显然是要牵新娘的手。
[EN] "Heading to the Zhu-Chen wedding, Your Highness?" Butler Li bowed cheerfully, clueless to the storm. "Why did you not ride with His Highness? He just left—oddly enough on horseback instead of carriage."
[ZH] 千里寻没有理会，刚才撩起盖头的一刻，已瞅准婚轿，被苦荞扶着径直走去，早已有人掀开轿帘，她迅速坐进去，待轿帘放下，将盖头取下扔到一边：“别扭死了。”
[EN] Keeping her tone rock steady, Mengrong replied, "I told Brother to go ahead. Please tell the driver to bring my carriage—I am heading to the feast too."
[ZH] 接着又将头上的凤冠卸下：“沉死了，真想不通，结个婚为何要戴这么个玩意？古人就是喜欢没事找罪受。”
[EN] "Already hitched and waiting outside!"
[ZH] 新娘不给面子，陈浩宇也没当回事，无奈地笑笑，跳上大马。
[EN] Thanking him, Mengrong boarded and had Xilan order the driver straight onto the bridal procession's main route—wherever Brother meant to strike, it had to be on that road.
[ZH] 迎亲的队伍没有起步抬轿的意思。
[EN] Over at Zhu Manor, Kuqiao woke Qianli Xun early for a quick breakfast and bridal dressing. Sleepless half the night, Qianli Xun sat fighting off yawns as Kuqiao chattered nonstop to keep gloom at bay.
[ZH] 朱晞颜见状，给儿子朱景元示意。
[EN] "At least the Chens have sense, Miss—sending crimson bridal robes!"
[ZH] 朱景元抬手招呼绿桃，绿桃端来放有红包的盘子，朱景元全都拿了，塞给抬轿子的人，众人这才抬起轿子，在乐声中向陈府而去。
[EN] "Aren't wedding robes always red?" Qianli Xun murmured sleepily. "What else would they be?"
[ZH] 迎亲队伍所走的线路是陈家找风水先生认真看过后才确定下来，全是宽阔的街市，取的就是个热热闹闹，红红火火之意。
[EN] Tracing her brows gently with indigo eyebrow pigment, Kuqiao explained, "Never heard 'red for the groom, green for the bride'? Brides can wear green robes too!"
[ZH] 千里寻第一次坐轿子，很不习惯，晃来晃去，差点儿将早饭给晃出来。可晃着晃着，轿子突然停下，乐曲声戛然而止，周围一阵寂静。
[EN] "Green wedding robes? Neat. Then why is mine red?"
[ZH] 千里寻纳闷，掀开轿帘，向外一瞧，竟然发现被很多士兵团团围住：“苦荞，发生什么事？”
[EN] "Miss is the Prefect's daughter, while the Chens—rich as they are—hold zero official rank! You are marrying down, so red honors you bringing luster to their house! Remember when Young Madam married into Zhu Manor, she wore green because she was marrying up." Stepping back, Kuqiao beamed. "My brow-painting keeps getting better—Miss looks lovelier than ever today!"
[ZH] 苦荞凑近轿子，小声说：“小姐，仪王肯定是疯了，他带人把迎亲队伍给拦住。”
[EN] "This wedding was not my idea—just slap something basic on and skip the fuss."
[ZH] 千里寻不再说话，放下帘子，屏住呼吸，认真体会内心的声音，是趁此机会跟申简辰远走高飞？还是坐在轿子里继续往前走？
[EN] Dusting powder across her cheeks, Kuqiao insisted, "Wanted or not, a wedding is a wedding! Starting today I live at Chen Manor with Miss—start day one cheerful so every day after stays bright!"
[ZH] 轿子外，申简辰骑着一匹骏马，堵住迎亲队伍。
[EN] "Didn't know you were this superstitious."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见状，不明所以，跳下马，来到仪王面前，先是行礼：“仪王殿下，今日我成亲，您这是？……”
[EN] "Not superstition—good wishes!"
[ZH] 申简辰没做任何解释，用平静而又不失力度的声音说：“你成你的亲，把轿子里的人给我留下。”
[EN] Kuqiao's bright spirit nudged Qianli Xun loose from her funk. Since she had agreed to walk through Chen Manor's door, why drag a sour face along? Set last night aside and step in light.
[ZH] 陈浩宇再笨，此刻也明白过来，原来仪王瞧上朱淑真，难怪上次知道谣言之事将他狠狠揍一顿。当时以为是为乔文仙，现在才明白真正的原因。他长十个脑袋也想不到，娶朱淑真还会惹来如此大的麻烦。
[EN] Dressed in crimson robes and crowned with a heavy phoenix coronet, she walked to her parents' hall where her brother, sister-in-law, and Matron Wei waited. Hardly had she knelt to bow to her parents when festive pipes and drums struck up outside.
[ZH] 陈浩宇靠近仪王骑的马，小声说：“仪王殿下有所不知，我根本不想娶朱淑真，都是被蒋爷给逼的，我要不娶朱淑真，他就得谋了陈家的家产，您说我该怎么办？若仪王能帮我从蔣爷那里要回字据，这婚我当场取消，您随便带朱淑真离开，我绝不会说一个‘不’字。”
[EN] "Rise," Zhu Xiyan said. "The groom is at the gate. Walk out with your parents."
[ZH] 陈浩宇为自己的聪明才智感到满意，有仪王出面不怕蒋爷不给字据，还解决掉朱淑真，真是一举两得。
[EN] As Qianli Xun stood, Yinxing approached with a lacquer tray holding a square red veil, which Kuqiao draped over her head. Guided through a red haze by Kuqiao behind her parents, with her brother, sister-in-law, and Matron Wei following, she passed through Zhu Manor's wide-open main gate for the very first time.
[ZH] 谁知仪王却说：“你与蒋爷之间的事与我何干？我只要轿子里的人。”
[EN] Peeking up under the red hem, she spotted Chen Haoyu astride a tall steed in crimson groom's robes, grinning brightly as though yesterday's panic had never happened. Dismounting to bow to his in-laws, he reached out a hand to lead his bride—only for Qianli Xun, having already sighted the bridal sedan, to stride straight past him with Kuqiao and hop right inside!
[ZH] 申简辰跳下马，根本未将陈浩宇放在眼里，径直朝婚轿走去，当他即将掀开轿帘时，被韦嬷嬷的声音阻止。
[EN] The second the curtain dropped, she yanked the veil off and tossed it aside. "Suffocating thing!" Next came the phoenix coronet. "Weighs a ton! Why on earth do brides wear torture gear on their heads? Ancients love making themselves miserable!"
[ZH] “仪王此举是要再次毁掉我家姑娘的名声吗？”
[EN] Unbothered by the snub, Chen Haoyu chuckled wryly and swung back onto his horse. When the sedan bearers lingered without lifting, Zhu Xiyan nodded to Zhu Jingyuan, who grabbed a tray of red gift packets from Lutao and stuffed them into the bearers' hands. Pipes blared back up, the sedan rose, and the procession marched off down Lin'an's widest avenues—picked by a geomancer for maximum fanfare.
[ZH] “嬷嬷不必拦我，谁的话我都不会听。”
[EN] Riding a palanquin for the first time made Qianli Xun's stomach pitch until her breakfast threatened to come back up—then without warning, the sedan lurched to a dead stop, the music snapped off mid-note, and hush fell over the street.
[ZH] 申简辰继续伸手去揭轿帘，手刚刚触碰到红色的帘子，传来梦容的声音：“哥哥要敢掀开帘子，我便死给你看！”
[EN] Cracking the curtain, Qianli Xun blinked at a wall of armed soldiers ringing the procession tight. "What is going on, Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 听到“死”字，申简辰不由收回手，向传来声音的方向看去。
[EN] Leaning close, Kuqiao whispered in panic, "Miss, Prince Yi really lost his marbles—he brought an army to block the wedding!"
[ZH] 梦容手执匕首，对准自己的要害。
[EN] Dropping the curtain, Qianli Xun held her breath and listened inward: bolt with Shen Jianchen right now, or stay seated and ride on?
[ZH] 申简辰才意识到梦容根本不会放下感情。他的心在婚轿中，又不得不担忧梦容的安危。
[EN] Outside, astride a tall warhorse barring the street, Shen Jianchen stared down. Dismounting to bow in confusion, Chen Haoyu called, "Your Highness Prince Yi—today is my wedding! What is the meaning of this?"
[ZH] 他看向白风，希望他趁郡主不注意，夺走她手中的匕首。
[EN] Shen Jianchen's voice rang calm and iron-hard: "Hold your wedding if you like—leave the woman inside that sedan to me."
[ZH] 白风接收到王爷的眼神，时刻注意观察郡主。
[EN] Dim as he could be, the truth finally clicked for Chen Haoyu—Prince Yi wanted Zhu Shuzhen! No wonder His Highness had beaten him half to death over those rumors! Wait—marrying Zhu Shuzhen brought trouble THIS big?! Edging beside Prince Yi's stirrup, he whispered fast, "Your Highness does not know—I never even wanted to marry Zhu Shuzhen! Master Jiang forced me into it, holding a contract for the whole Chen fortune! If Your Highness gets that paper back from Master Jiang for me, I cancel this wedding on the spot and you can carry Zhu Shuzhen wherever you please—not a peep out of me!"
[ZH] 申简辰希望利用说话引开乔梦容的注意力：“梦容，你为何要逼我？”
[EN] *Pure genius!* Chen Haoyu congratulated himself—get Prince Yi to crush Master Jiang AND offload Zhu Shuzhen in one shot!
[ZH] “是哥哥逼我才对！”
[EN] "Your business with Master Jiang means nothing to me," Shen Jianchen answered coldly. "I want only the woman in that sedan."
[ZH] “乖，放下匕首，有什么话好好说。”
[EN] Swinging off his horse without sparing Chen Haoyu a glance, he strode straight toward the bridal chair—only for Matron Wei to step into his path. "Does Your Highness mean to shred my mistress's reputation to bits all over again?!"
[ZH] 千里寻早已在轿中听到外面的一切，梦容竟然用性命相逼，令她痛心不已，更为梦容担忧。她无法在轿子里躲下去，用轻功冲出婚轿……
[EN] "Stand aside, Matron. I am listening to no one today."
[ZH] 众人只见一袭红衣飞过，接着看到乔梦容手中的匕首掉落在地，发出清脆的“咣当”声。
[EN] Reaching past her, his fingers grazed the crimson curtain—when Qiao Mengrong's sharp cry pierced the street: "Pull back that curtain, Brother, and I die right before your eyes!"
[ZH] 幽栖居士像仙子一般落在众人面前，一身嫁衣，美艳无比。
[EN] Hand freezing midair, Shen Jianchen whipped around. There stood Mengrong, bared dagger pressed straight against her chest!
[ZH] 乔梦容顾不上欣赏，走上前，狠狠在淑真的脸上扇了一巴掌：“朱淑真！我恨你！”
[EN] With a jolt, he realized Mengrong would never yield. Heart torn between the sedan behind him and the blade at her breast, he flicked a sharp look at Bai Feng—*Wait for an opening and disarm her!*—and tried distracting her with words. "Why push me like this, Mengrong?"
[ZH] 她趁众人不注意时，瞅准一旁士兵的利刃，飞奔而去……
[EN] "YOU pushed me, Brother!"
[ZH] 千里寻正沉浸在被梦容抽打后的伤痛中分了神；白风一方面被幽栖居士精妙的轻功给镇住，另一方面也被郡主的一巴掌给吓傻，尽管不是打在他的脸上，也一时失了神。
[EN] "Be good—put the dagger down and let us talk."
[ZH] 申简辰因担忧千里寻，迟钝片刻，反应过来后，大喊着：“梦容！”他用轻功加快步伐向梦容奔去……
[EN] Having heard every word inside the chair, Qianli Xun felt her heart wrench at Mengrong putting her life on the line. Unable to hide behind red silk another second, she kicked off with light-foot skill and burst straight out of the sedan!
[ZH] 还是晚了一步，乔梦容已撞上剑刃，伤口处流出鲜血，倒在地上……
[EN] Spectators gasped as a streak of scarlet flew through the air—*clang!*—and Qiao Mengrong's dagger spun clattering across the cobblestones! Floating down like a celestial maiden in crimson bridal silk, Youqi Jushi stood dazzling before the crowd.
[ZH] 拿剑的士兵被吓坏，扔掉手中的剑，跪在地上：“不怪我！不怪我！”
[EN] Blind to everything save rage, Qiao Mengrong stepped straight up and cracked a ferocious slap across Shuzhen's cheek! "I hate you, Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 申简辰上前抱起梦容，朝街市的医馆跑去，喜兰紧随其后……
[EN] Before anyone moved, Mengrong's eyes locked onto a soldier's drawn sword beside her—and she threw herself straight toward the blade!
[ZH] 白风需帮王爷处理后续事务，对士兵头领说：“把大家带回去吧。”
[EN] Stunned by the stinging slap, Qianli Xun hesitated half a heartbeat; Bai Feng stood frozen between awe at her flying leap and shock at the slap; Shen Jianchen flinched in worry over Qianli Xun—then snapped back with a roar: "Mengrong!"
[ZH] 士兵们撤退后，白风向陈浩宇拱手道：“陈公子，打扰了。”
[EN] Launching forward with light-foot speed, he arrived half a breath too late. Qiao Mengrong slammed into the blade's point; scarlet bloomed across her robe as she crumpled to the ground!
[ZH] 他向苦荞看上一眼，迅速跳上马去追王爷。
[EN] Dropping his sword in sheer terror, the soldier crashed to his knees. "Not my fault! Not my fault!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇震惊之余，见朱淑真像傻子一样站在那里，走过去问道：“喂，你惹的祸，跟我可没关系，还嫁不嫁？”
[EN] Scooping Mengrong into his arms, Shen Jianchen sprinted down the street toward a clinic, Xilan racing weeping at his heels.
[ZH] 被梦容打过的脸还有些疼，没想到她看着柔弱，打她的劲儿却不小，足以说明已将她恨到极点。
[EN] Left cleaning up the chaos, Bai Feng barked at the unit captain, "March the men back to barracks!" Once the troops pulled out, he cupped his hands to Chen Haoyu. "Apologies for the disturbance, Young Master Chen." Shooting one quick look at Kuqiao, he leaped onto a horse and galloped after his prince.
[ZH] 面对陈浩宇的问题，想想刚才发生的一切，千里寻又怎能不嫁？
[EN] Still gaping at the whirlwind, Chen Haoyu walked over to where Zhu Shuzhen stood motionless. "Hey—that circus was all your mess, nothing to do with me. Still marrying or what?"
[ZH] “你敢娶，老娘就敢嫁。”
[EN] Her cheek still burned from Mengrong's slap—for someone so fragile, the blow packed pure venom, proving hatred had replaced every drop of friendship. Looking at what had just unfolded, how could she NOT marry?
[ZH] 为了拿回字据，陈浩宇豁出去：“你敢嫁，我就敢娶。”
[EN] "If you have the guts to marry me, I have the guts to marry you!"
[ZH] “那还犹豫个什么劲儿？”
[EN] Thinking of Master Jiang's contract, Chen Haoyu squared his shoulders. "If you have the guts to marry, I have the guts to take you!"
[ZH] 千里寻走上婚轿，继续坐好。
[EN] "Then what are we standing around for?!"
[ZH] 外面的乐声再次响起，她没有一丝高兴，一心担忧梦容的安危。尽管她刚才注意过，梦容的伤并非在害处，不会有生命危险，可她还是忍不住会担心。
[EN] Turning sharply, Qianli Xun climbed straight back into the bridal sedan. Festive drums and pipes picked back up, yet not a shred of cheer lived in her chest—only heavy dread over Mengrong's wound. Even knowing she had seen the point miss vital organs, worry refused to let her go.

#CH 192
[ZH] 迎亲队伍终于到达陈家大门外，乐官、茶酒司等人堵住门口不让新娘进入，他们都以陈浩东为首，个个满面喜色。尤其是陈浩东，计谋得逞，更是春风得意。
[ZH] 他代表乐官念起《拦门诗》：“拦门礼物多为贵，岂比寻常市道交。十万缠腰应满足，三千五索莫轻抛。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇向迎亲队伍中摆摆手，他的堂弟陈浩飞跑过来，看上去大约十四五岁的模样：“二哥，叫我何事？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“东哥拦门，你知道该怎么做。”
[ZH] 陈浩飞双手叉腰，大声念出《回拦门诗》：“从来君子不怀金，此意追寻意转深。欲望诸亲聊阔略，毋烦介绍久劳心。”
[ZH] 众人听后，都笑笑，让开路。占卜的阴阳人手拿斗，里面装着谷类、豆类、铜钱及彩果等，一边念咒文一边往门上撒。
[ZH] 差不多时，苦荞向轿子里提醒：“小姐，该下轿。”
[ZH] 千里寻还沉浸在梦容冲向利刃的一刻，听到苦荞的声音，平复心情，将凤冠戴好，红盖头顶上。感觉到轿帘被掀开，起身走出轿子，扶住苦荞伸过来的手。脚下是提前铺好的软青锦，踩上去软绵绵的。
[ZH] 被苦荞引到陈浩宇身边，两人并肩而行走进陈府，一路上脚下尽是软青锦。
[ZH] 顶着盖头，只能看到脚下，她发现只给新娘脚下放有软青锦，新郎脚下什么都没有，心中甚是疑惑。没疑惑多久，已被引入陈家的礼堂内。
[ZH] 千里寻跨过礼堂门槛的一刻，依靠第六感，觉出里面很大。她的脚下已换成红毯，借着红毯，她胡乱猜想，会不会里面有前来参加婚礼之人？到底会有多少人？千里寻靠感觉难以猜测。
[ZH] 礼堂的正前方挂着一张画像，画像前放着供桌，上有供品香炉，香炉内燃着三炷香。供桌左边坐有夏老太太，右边坐着陈江林和姚轻琰。其他陈家人则围坐在一边，除陈江树和陈江木外，不过是陈浩宇的两位婶婶，还有堂嫂，也就是陈浩东的发妻。
[ZH] 主持婚礼之人是陈家专门请的临安官员，当然也是看在朱晞颜的面子上才答应为陈家主持婚礼。那官员身着便服，站立一旁，见新人走得差不多时，笑道：“新人请止步。”
[ZH] 千里寻停住脚步，心想：“应是拜天地。唉，也不知梦容如何？”
[ZH] 只听官员说：“一拜先灵！”
[ZH] 千里寻鞠躬行礼，心中疑惑：“不都是先拜天地吗？怎么宋代是先拜祖先？”
[ZH] 接着拜公婆和陈家老太太，又是夫妻对拜。尤其听到“夫妻对拜”，千里寻内心无比难过，搞来搞去，与她夫妻对拜之人竟是她最瞧不上的人。
[ZH] 拜完后，跟陈浩宇进入洞房，坐在大大的婚床边，走进几个丫鬟和婆子，向床上撒同心果和钱币。
[ZH] 撒完后，有婆子说：“陈公子，该揭新娘的盖头。”
[ZH] 千里寻不愿陈浩宇来揭，自行将盖头取下。
[ZH] 婆子说：“新娘怎么能自行娶盖头？得新郎取，否则不吉利。”婆子想给新娘盖上，结果手被千里寻挡住，怎么都盖不上。
[EN] At last the bridal procession reached Chen Manor's main gate, where musicians and banquet staff barred the doorway under Chen Haodong's lead, everyone beaming—none brighter than Chen Haodong, basking in his plot's success.
[ZH] 陈浩宇劝道：“算了，她揭开跟我揭开没什么区别。”
[EN] Speaking for the musicians, he chanted the *Gate-Blocking Poem*:
[ZH] 婆子立刻换副嘴脸，将盖头放到一边：“新郎懂得尊重新娘，预示你们以后的日子红红火火。”
[EN] "Rich gifts to clear the gate count most of all, Far above ordinary marketplace trade; A hundred thousand cash strapped to the waist should satisfy— Three thousand and five hundred strings—do not toss them lightly!" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 有丫鬟过来，分别在两人头上取出一撮头发，用剪刀剪下，再将两撮头发用红色发带绑在一起，放入锦盒；又有丫鬟端来两瓣系着红绸的瓢瓜。
[EN] Chen Haoyu waved into the procession, and his fourteen- or fifteen-year-old cousin Chen Haofei trotted up. "Need me, Second Brother?"
[ZH] 千里寻见瓢瓜中有酒，心想该是该喝交杯酒的环节。用余光瞅瞅身旁的陈浩宇，他竟然面带笑容，伸手去拿瓢瓜。
[EN] "Haodong is blocking the gate—you know the drill."
[ZH] 婆子见新娘坐着不动，提醒道：“新娘子快行合卺礼，没见新郎都等不及。”
[EN] Planting fists on his hips, Chen Haofei belted out the *Reply to the Gate-Blocking Poem*:
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“没事没事，我一个人饮也可。”
[EN] "True gentlemen never keep gold in mind; True feeling sought grows deeper still with time. We beg our worthy kin to ease the way, And spare the go-betweens prolonged delay!" [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] “不可。”婆子将瓢瓜拿起来，递到千里寻手中，“新娘子快些。”
[EN] Laughing, the crowd parted. A geomancer bearing a wooden measure filled with grains, beans, copper coins, and festive fruits chanted blessings while scattering handfuls over the doorway.
[ZH] 千里寻端起瓢瓜一口喝下，将瓢瓜放进盘中。
[EN] When the moment came, Kuqiao called softly beside the curtain, "Time to step out, Miss."
[ZH] 婆子急了：“怎能这么饮？应该先喝一半，再与新郎交换着喝。”
[EN] Still haunted by Mengrong plunging onto the sword, Qianli Xun forced herself steady, settled the phoenix coronet back on her head, draped the red veil, and stepped out into Kuqiao's waiting hands. Soft blue-green brocade cushioned her slippers all the way forward. Guided beside Chen Haoyu, she walked into Chen Manor shoulder to shoulder with him, peering down under her veil to wonder why brocade lined only her path and not the groom's—until they crossed the threshold into a vast ceremony hall where red carpet replaced brocade.
[ZH] 陈浩宇劝婆子：“我不在意这些，都是形式。”也将瓢瓜中的酒喝完。
[EN] At the front of the hall hung an ancestral portrait above an offering table bearing fruit and three burning incense sticks. To its left sat Old Madam Xia; to the right sat Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan, flanked by Second Uncle Chen Jiangshu, Third Uncle Chen Jiangmu, both aunts, and Chen Haodong's primary wife. A Lin'an official invited out of courtesy to Prefect Zhu stood in plain robes as master of ceremonies.
[ZH] 婆子与丫鬟们无奈，只能收了，离开洞房。
[EN] "Halt, bride and groom!" he announced with a warm smile.
[ZH] 待众人走后，陈浩宇站起来：“委屈你在此坐着，我得出去应酬。”
[EN] Bracing to bow to heaven and earth—while still fretting over Mengrong—Qianli Xun heard instead: "First bow: to the ancestral spirits!"
[ZH] 千里寻并不理会，早都不希望陈浩宇坐在身边，任由其离开。她取下凤冠，递给苦荞：“放起来。”
[EN] *Wait—don't weddings bow to heaven and earth first? The Song bows to ancestors first?* Dutifully she bowed, then bowed to her in-laws and Old Madam Xia, then faced Chen Haoyu for the mutual spousal bow—a heavy lump rising in her throat at bowing as wife to the very man she despised most.
[ZH] 苦荞接过，放到桌上，回头见小姐坐在床边一脸忧愁，猜想一定是因佳和郡主受伤之事。
[EN] Escorted next to the bridal chamber, she sat on the wide wedding bed while maids and matrons scattered paired wedding fruits and coins across the quilt.
[ZH] 她不愿小姐继续难过下去，想到一个话题：“小姐，没想到陈家吃酒席的地方好大，我从来没见过这么大的地方，您说官家吃酒席的地方会不会比陈家的还要大？”
[EN] "Young Master Chen, time to lift the bride's veil!" an elderly woman announced.
[ZH] 千里寻毫无兴致，一言不发。
[EN] Unwilling to let Chen Haoyu touch it, Qianli Xun reached up and pulled the red veil off herself.
[ZH] 苦荞又想到一个话题：“小姐，今日来了好多人，大部分我都不认识。对了，我看到夏果，她真是，嫁了人也不知道回望月轩看看您。”
[EN] "Oh, a bride cannot remove her own veil—unlucky! The groom must lift it!" The matron tried draping it back on, only for Qianli Xun's hand to block her solidly.
[ZH] 提到夏果，千里寻自然想到文渊。仔细算算日子，她与文渊从相恋到分手也就七个来月，可对她来讲恍若隔世。
[EN] "Leave it," Chen Haoyu shrugged. "Her taking it off or me taking it off—same difference."
[ZH] 记得捶丸场时，情还在，后来不知不觉慢慢消失，申简辰再次住进她的心里。尤其是近日，她从未想起过文渊。
[EN] Flipping smooth as oil, the matron set the veil aside. "Look how the groom respects his bride—a sure sign of glowing days ahead!"
[ZH] 苦荞一直说话，她不好不理，随口问道：“文渊可来？”问完又觉得这个问题实在多余，夏果都来了，文渊岂能不来？
[EN] Next a maid clipped a tiny lock of hair from each of their heads, bound the two locks together with a red cord, and laid them in a brocade box; another brought forward two split gourd halves linked by red silk and filled with wine. Guessing this was the nuptial gourd-cup rite, Qianli Xun glanced sideways to see Chen Haoyu already smiling and reaching for his half.
[ZH] 苦荞还是回道：“我朝男宾区瞧过一眼，人太多，没看到乔公子，可我看到南双，他站着，容易被发现，他既在，乔公子肯定在。”
[EN] "Hurry with the nuptial gourd rite, bride—even the groom cannot wait!" the matron nudged.
[ZH] 关于文渊，千里寻没有什么可说的，继续担心起梦容。
[EN] "No fuss—I can drink mine solo," Chen Haoyu offered.
[ZH] 苦荞再找不出任何话题，站在小姐身旁陪她一起沉默。
[EN] "Impossible!" Pressing the gourd into Qianli Xun's hand, the matron urged, "Quick now, bride!"
[ZH] 千里寻再次想起白风的话，开口问：“苦荞，你会不会觉得我是坏人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun tossed the whole gourd down in one swallow and set it back on the tray.
[ZH] “小姐为何这么问？”
[EN] "Not like that!" the matron yelped. "Drink half and swap gourds with the groom!"
[ZH] “今日之事你也看到。”
[EN] "Quit fussing over formalities—I do not mind," Chen Haoyu said, draining his own half clean.
[ZH] 苦荞才品出小姐的意思：“怎么能怪您？您可是一直拒绝仪王。……，其实……其实我一直想不通，小姐为何不答应做仪王的妾室？您跟佳和郡主是朋友，再共同服侍仪王，岂不完美？”
[EN] Defeated, the matrons and maids cleared the tray and withdrew.
[ZH] “哪有你想得那么简单。梦容为了仪王连命都不要，若有人与她一起分享仪王，她定然不会好过。正因为我把梦容当好友看待，也清楚她对仪王的付出，才更不会答应待在仪王身边。”
[EN] Once the room emptied, Chen Haoyu stood up. "Sorry to leave you sitting here, yet I have guests to greet outside."
[ZH] 小姐心善，总是替他人考虑，如此善良之人，为何还会碰到许多不顺心之事？苦荞在心中慨叹老天实在不公。
[EN] Glad to see the back of him, Qianli Xun said nothing as he left, lifting off the heavy coronet and handing it to Kuqiao. "Put this away."
[ZH] 千里寻继续说：“再说，我也不愿与人共事一夫。”
[EN] Setting it on the table, Kuqiao saw her mistress's glum face and guessed she was brooding over Princess Jiahe's injury. Casting about for a distraction, she chatted, "Miss, I never saw a banquet hall as massive as the Chens'! Wonder if His Majesty's own hall is even bigger?"
[ZH] 若有人与苦荞分享白风，她也会不乐意，小姐无法答应仪王的难处她完全理解。
[EN] No response.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来，伸伸懒腰：“不想了，想这么多有什么用？新生活开始，我要好好安排一番。”
[EN] Kuqiao tried again. "So many guests today—half of them strangers! Oh, I spotted Xiaguo too! Can you believe she never once dropped by Wangyue Pavilion to visit you after getting married?"
[ZH] 她放下伸完懒腰的手臂，将婚房四周仔细瞧一遍，皱起眉头：“陈家将婚房布置得奢华，可惜没有书香之气，去，把韦嬷嬷叫来。”
[EN] Mention of Xiaguo brought Wenyuan briefly to mind. Counting back, her whole romance and breakup with Wenyuan had spanned barely seven months, yet it felt lifetimes ago. Back at the chuiwan grounds the ache had still lingered; then bit by bit it vanished as Shen Jianchen moved back into her heart—these past days she had hardly spared Wenyuan a single thought.
[ZH] 苦荞答应后，来到宴请宾客之处寻找韦嬷嬷。
[EN] Not wanting to leave Kuqiao hanging, she asked idly, "Did Wenyuan come?"—then realized how pointless the question was if Xiaguo was here.
[ZH] 要说这陈家的确为婚事费尽心思，竟将对陈家来讲十分重要的兴隆阁腾出来使用。
[EN] "I peeked into the men's section," Kuqiao reported. "Too crowded to spot Young Master Qiao, yet I saw Nan Shuang standing tall—if he is here, Young Master Qiao definitely is."
[ZH] 兴隆阁内设议事厅，每隔几日陈家重要人物，以及临安的掌柜会来此商议生意中的大事，许多重要决策都是在此处定夺下来。每年年底也要在兴隆阁内宴请各地掌柜，安排来年的生意。
[EN] With nothing more to ask about Wenyuan, Qianli Xun drifted back to worrying about Mengrong, while Kuqiao ran out of topics and stood quietly beside her.
[ZH] 刚刚陪小姐进入兴隆阁时，苦荞并未细看。此时一跨进大门，先看到两边放置着两个形状像大鼓一样的玩意儿，上面刻镂着大大的喜字。
[EN] Bai Feng's sharp condemnation surfaced again. "Kuqiao, do you think I am a bad person?"
[ZH] 中间红毯的两边放置各色花卉，正值春季，一盆一盆的花开得甚艳。墙壁和柱子上也都贴有喜字。屋顶上挂着各色灯笼，轻轻垂下，抬头望去，像漫天繁星。
[EN] "Why on earth ask that, Miss?"
[ZH] 苦荞觉得兴隆阁仿佛一个用红色涂成的世界，周围尽是一片红光。她顾不上欣赏，向右边的女宾区拐去。
[EN] "You saw what happened today."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷本与朱家女眷一起坐在桌旁享用珍馐美味，同时替向夫人应酬各家女眷。
[EN] Catching on, Kuqiao protested at once, "How could anyone blame YOU?! You turned Prince Yi down nonstop! Actually... one thing still puzzles me, Miss: why not just agree to be Prince Yi's concubine? You and Her Highness are best friends—serving Prince Yi side by side would be lovely!"
[ZH] 她刚刚离开席位与秦夫人饮过一杯酒，见苦荞站在女宾区的入口处向她瞧来，猜到姑娘找她有事，与秦夫人笑着说了句话，回到向夫人跟前，耳语几句，得到允许，离开宴席朝苦荞走去。
[EN] "If only life were that tidy. Mengrong was ready to die for Prince Yi—sharing him would tear her apart. Precisely because I count Mengrong a real friend and saw how deeply she loves him, I could never stay by his side."
[ZH] “可是姑娘吩咐你来找我？”
[EN] Kuqiao sighed silently at Heaven's injustice toward someone so kind.
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Besides, I refuse to share a husband with anyone," Qianli Xun added. Thinking how furious she would be sharing Bai Feng, Kuqiao got it completely.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷迅速走出女宾区，再走出兴隆阁，在苦荞的引领下，来到婚房。推开门的一刻，姑娘已将婚服脱去，换上平常的衣裳。
[EN] Standing up with a big stretch, Qianli Xun shook off the gloom. "Enough moping—what does brooding fix? New chapter starts today, time to organize my turf!" Scanning the ornate bridal suite, she wrinkled her nose. "Flashy luxury without a shred of literary taste! Go call Matron Wei."
[ZH] “婚礼还未结束，姑娘就把婚服换了，陈家人看到会批评姑娘不懂事。”
[EN] Kuqiao hurried out to the banquet hall to find her.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的责备，千里寻并未当回事，上前扶住嬷嬷：“婚服穿着实在不舒服，没有日常的衣裳受用。嬷嬷快坐下，我有事跟您商议。”
[EN] The Chens had spared zero expense, clearing out their prized Xinglong Pavilion for the feast—normally the nerve center where Chen elders and Lin'an shop managers met every few days to decide major trade moves and gather each year-end to map out the next year's commerce. Too busy guiding Miss earlier to look close, Kuqiao stepped through the doors now onto a dazzling scarlet world: twin drum-shaped lanterns carved with giant double-happiness characters flanking the doorway, blooming spring potted flowers lining the crimson carpet, joy stickers on every pillar, and colored lanterns dangling from the rafters like a sky full of stars. Turning right into the women's wing, she spotted Matron Wei returning from a toast with Madam Qin, caught her eye, and waited while Matron Wei whispered an excuse to Madam Xiang before hurrying over.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷在桌旁坐下：“姑娘说吧，我洗耳恭听。”
[EN] "Miss sent you for me?"
[ZH] 千里寻也坐下，暂时忘记梦容受伤之事：“第一件，烦请嬷嬷明日将陈浩宇名下的财产过继到我名下。”
[EN] "Yes!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷并不惊讶：“此事我已听苦荞说过，明日定帮姑娘办妥。”
[EN] Slipping back to the bridal chamber, Matron Wei pushed open the door to find her mistress already stripped out of wedding crimson into everyday clothes. "The feast is not even over and you changed out of your bridal gown! The Chens will call you ill-mannered!"
[ZH] “这第二件……”千里寻朝屋子周围瞧了瞧，“屋子的布置实在不合我心意，陈浩宇说了，他的院子以后就是我的，这屋子也归我，请嬷嬷安排人帮我重新布置一番。”
[EN] Taking her arm lightly, Qianli Xun brushed it off. "That gown felt like armor—give me everyday clothes any day. Sit down, Matron; I have errands to go over."
[ZH] “姑娘放心，不出两日，定布置得跟望月轩的闺房一模一样。”韦嬷嬷爽快答应。
[EN] Sitting at the table, Matron Wei nodded. "I am all ears."
[ZH] “还有我读过的书……”
[EN] Pushing Mengrong's injury aside for now, Qianli Xun ticked off her list. "First, tomorrow please transfer all of Chen Haoyu's assets into my name."
[ZH] “姑娘更不用担心，天黑前红豆会带人用马车将姑娘的书统统搬来。”
[EN] "Kuqiao already told me," Matron Wei nodded without blinking. "I will handle it first thing tomorrow."
[ZH] 千里寻很满意，无论什么事，韦嬷嬷总能替她安排妥当。
[EN] "Second..." Qianli Xun gestured around the gaudy room. "This decor drives me crazy. Chen Haoyu promised me this whole courtyard and main hall—please have someone redecorate to my taste."
[ZH] 提起红豆，韦嬷嬷想起件事，向姑娘请示：“我刚经过院子，仔细观察，姑爷身边的下人不少，不缺伺候的人。望月轩的下人姑娘不必尽数带来，只留训练过拳法的十人，其他人给些银子都遣散回去，再将望月轩转手卖了，姑娘可同意？”
[EN] "Two days tops, and it will match your chamber at Wangyue Pavilion down to the brush-rest."
[ZH] 既已开启另一段人生，以前的也没必要留着，千里寻点点头：“嬷嬷说的我都同意。”
[EN] "And my books..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷还得去婚宴应酬：“姑娘若没其他事，我先去应酬，只怕夫人应付不过来。”
[EN] "Before nightfall, Hongdou will bring every volume over by carriage."
[ZH] “有劳嬷嬷。”
[EN] Everything ran like clockwork with Matron Wei. Mention of Hongdou reminded the matron of one more item. "Walking through this courtyard, I noticed Young Master already has plenty of attendants. No sense hauling all of Wangyue Pavilion's staff here—keep just the ten trained in fist arts, send the rest off with generous severance silver, and sell Wangyue Pavilion. Agree?"
[ZH] 苦荞将门打开，韦嬷嬷的脚还未走出去，夏果出现在门口。
