[EN] With a new chapter open, no need to cling to the old shell. "Agreed on every count."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷笑道：“原来是乔家的夏姨娘，今日终于想起姑娘。”
[EN] "If nothing else, I must head back to the feast before Madam gets overwhelmed."
[ZH] “嬷嬷别拿话噎我，是迟了些，可我到乔家也没几日。”夏果侧身从韦嬷嬷身边经过，进入屋内。
[EN] "Much obliged, Matron."
[ZH] 她身边的丫鬟艾墨出自望月轩，对韦嬷嬷十分恭敬，退到一边行礼，等韦嬷嬷离开后，才跟在夏果后面进入。
[EN] Just as Kuqiao opened the door for Matron Wei to exit, Xiaguo stepped into view.
[ZH] 夏果见到小姐自然高兴，行礼道：“小姐，我没及时回门看您，您可别怪我。”
[EN] "Well now—Concubine Xia of the Qiao house finally remembers her old mistress!" Matron Wei teased.
[ZH] 千里寻没有心情露出笑脸，淡淡地地说：“望月轩又不是你的娘家，谈不上回门。再说你我都在临安，见面的机会多的是。”
[EN] "Save the barbs, Matron—only been at Qiao Manor a few days!" Sliding past Matron Wei into the room—while her maid Aimo stepped aside to bow respectfully before following—Xiaguo curtsied warmly. "Please do not blame me for not coming back to visit sooner, Miss."
[ZH] 夏果毫不客气坐在小姐的桌旁，以前她只有站在小姐身旁的份儿，现在哪怕是给人当妾室，也可与小姐平起平坐。她瞧一眼苦荞，还是老样子，真是个当丫鬟的命。
[EN] With zero mood for fake warmth, Qianli Xun replied evenly, "Wangyue Pavilion is hardly your maiden home, so skip talk of post-wedding visits. Both of us live in Lin'an—plenty of chances to cross paths."
[ZH] “今日陈家的婚礼果真气派，完全将乔家比下去。小姐，您可是没看到，苗知瑜和秦夫人那脸色，呵呵……！”
[EN] Without waiting for an invite, Xiaguo perched straight down beside her former mistress—no longer a standing maid, even as a concubine she sat level now! Throwing a smug glance at Kuqiao still standing in servant attire, she chuckled, "The Chen wedding really is grand—blew the Qiaos' clean out of the water! You should have seen the sour faces on Miao Zhiyu and Madam Qin! Heh!"
[ZH] 苦荞一脸不满：“夏姨娘何必笑乔家少夫人，她成亲那日，你也被接到乔家，她的婚礼也是你的婚礼。”
[EN] Kuqiao frowned sharply. "Why mock Young Madam Qiao, Concubine Xia? You entered Qiao Manor the exact same day—her wedding was your wedding too."
[ZH] 夏果忧伤道：“我只是个妾室，哪有资格谈婚礼。”
[EN] Xiaguo's face fell. "I am only a concubine—what right do I have to call it a wedding?"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“算你还有自知之明。”
[EN] "At least you know your place," Kuqiao muttered.
[ZH] 见此情景，千里寻打算岔开话题：“你可将家人接到城里住？”
[EN] Stepping in to shift topics, Qianli Xun asked, "Did you bring your family into town?"
[ZH] 提起家人，夏果想起小姐的恩情：“我是有此想法，凭小姐送给我的嫁妆，把家人接到城里，日子也能过得不错，可家人不同意，他们已习惯乡下的生活，我打算每隔一段时日给家里送些银子，也可让家人日子过得好些，省得我爷爷到处卖唱。”
[EN] Gratitude softened Xiaguo's eyes. "I wanted to—with the dowry Miss gave me, we could live well in Lin'an—yet my folks refused, too used to country life. I plan to send silver home regularly so Grandfather never has to wander singing for coin."
[ZH] “老人家可好？”千里寻想起负鼓盲翁，“有机会还想再听他唱蔡伯喈的故事。”
[EN] "How is the old gentleman?" Remembering the blind drummer, Qianli Xun smiled. "I would love to hear him sing Cai Bojie's ballad again sometime."
[ZH] “爷爷身体尚可，等过段时日，我差人把他接到城里，专门唱给小姐听。”
[EN] "Healthy enough! In a while I will bring him into town to sing privately for Miss."
[ZH] “那可真是太好了！”
[EN] "That would be lovely!"
[ZH] 夏果脸上的笑容很快消失：“小姐，您不知道，我到乔家这些日子，还未与文渊圆房，苗知瑜也是。”
[EN] Then the smile slid right off Xiaguo's face. "Miss, you have no idea—all these days at Qiao Manor, Wenyuan has not consummated our marriage yet. Nor with Miao Zhiyu either."

#CH 193
[ZH] 夏果猛然提起此话，千里寻不知该如何回应。她无心关注他人之事，何况还是这等闺中私密。
[ZH] 苦荞小心提醒：“夏姨娘怎能将您闺中之事说与小姐？也不怕羞得慌。”
[ZH] 夏果批评道：“我与小姐说话，你少插嘴。”
[ZH] 苦荞撇撇嘴，不再说什么。
[ZH] 夏果继续说：“小姐别介意，我之所以这么说，无非是将您当家人看待，也想让您知道，文渊还未放下小姐……”
[ZH] 千里寻感觉出试探的意味，她向来讨厌别被人试探：“你能将我当家人，我很欣慰，至于我跟文渊之前的那段情，已过去。你今日也看到，我连婚都结了，还有什么好担心的？”
[ZH] 夏果放心许多：“说句心里话，我很羡慕小姐，能得到文渊的真情。我不敢奢求真情，只要能在文渊身边伺候，能与他说说话，便心满意足。”
[ZH] 夏果又露出笑容：“有件事，说起来我倒开心，文渊好几次到我等房中聊天儿，却从来没理过苗知瑜。”
[ZH] 千里寻庆幸自己没有答应做他人的妾室，不管是文渊，还是仪王。她才不愿像夏果一样，为争夺一个男人与其他女人互相攀比，到最后只会严重到勾心斗角。
[ZH] 夏果见小姐什么话都不说，还露出不想谈论此话题的表情，也觉多说无益，站起来告辞：“此处是婚房，说不定新郎一会儿回来，我不便在此久留，还得回到婚宴，以后有时间再来看望小姐。”
[ZH] 千里寻只说出两个字：“慢走。”
[ZH] 夏果行完礼，带着艾墨离开。
[ZH] 苦荞见人走远，把门关起好：“小姐，我不喜欢现在的夏果。”
[ZH] “为何？”
[ZH] “说不清楚，反正感觉不舒服。”
[ZH] “管她呢，跟你我没什么关系。我累了，想睡一会儿，待会儿陈浩宇回来，让他到后院找个地方睡。”
[ZH] “小姐不都跟姑爷说好，让他睡偏房？”
[ZH] “我改主意了，偏房离我太近，我可不想一早起来就看到陈浩宇那张脸。”
[ZH] 她和衣躺在床上，毕竟第一次睡在别人家，说不定陈浩宇会喝醉酒而归，要是跑错地方，自己岂不被他看光？
[ZH] 苦荞猜出小姐的心思，伺候她躺好，放下床幔，出去将门轻轻关好，打算去后院安排一番，省得姑爷回来埋怨没给他准备好睡觉的地方。
[ZH] 在婚宴的男宾区内，乔文仙一直喝闷酒。
[ZH] 乔家接到喜帖时，他感到难过。本不想目睹淑真与别人的婚礼，可乔守仁一再要求他必须代表乔家参加。乔文仙拗不过父亲，遵从父命带着一妻一妾一起前来。
[ZH] 刚一到被东方尘泽等人拉到男宾区，他的妻妾则去了女宾区，没有她们在一旁，清静许多。
[ZH] 与东方尘泽等人聊一会儿，终于看到淑真身着嫁衣，与陈浩宇一起走来。他内心充满嫉妒，为何陈浩宇有这等福分将他喜欢的人娶走？他狠劲捏着手中的酒盅。
[ZH] 酒盅没有任何反应，他的手指却捏得疼。他不再说话，只顾喝闷酒。
[ZH] 仪式举行完，淑真离开婚宴，他的心也跟着离开。东方尘泽等人劝他少喝些，他并不听劝，依然一杯接着一杯喝。
[ZH] 南双担心自家公子喝坏身体，走过去劝道：“公子，少喝些。”
[ZH] 乔文仙根本不管，推开南双：“有你什么事？一边待着！”
[ZH] 青瓜端着酒壶陪陈浩宇前来敬酒。
[ZH] 陈浩宇见乔文仙喝闷酒，并未理会，端起酒盅，对其他人说：“诸位，感谢前来参加婚礼，我敬诸位一杯。”
[ZH] 东方尘泽、曹班和安明轩，以及其他人都起身端起酒盅回敬。大家再瞧不上这位纨绔子弟，再为幽栖居士感到惋惜，毕竟是婚礼，面子上还需过得去。
[ZH] 唯独乔文仙坐着，不予理会。
[ZH] 酒过一巡，陈浩宇打算去其他酒桌敬酒。
[ZH] 乔文仙才借着酒劲儿说：“你还没向我敬酒。”
[EN] Blindsided by the bedroom confession, Qianli Xun had zero idea how to answer—wanting no part of anyone's private laundry.
[ZH] 陈浩宇不爽：“我刚敬了，是你不接。”
[EN] Kuqiao spoke up sharply. "How can Concubine Xia spill bedchamber secrets in front of Miss?! Have you no shame?!"
[ZH] 乔文仙倒满一盅，站起来：“一桌人一起敬酒有什么意思，为何不一个一个敬？”
[EN] "Mind your tongue when I am speaking with Miss!" Xiaguo snapped back.
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“没办法，我成亲，大家都给面子，来得人多，一个一个敬，只怕到明日都敬不完。”
[EN] Kuqiao pursed her lips and fell quiet.
[ZH] 乔文仙将手中的酒泼在陈浩宇的脸上：“你根本不配娶淑真！”
[EN] "Do not take offense, Miss," Xiaguo said softly. "I only speak freely because I think of you as family—and wanted you to know Wenyuan still has not let go of you..."
[ZH] 一旁的青瓜把酒壶放在桌上，赶紧用袖子替陈浩宇擦脸：“乔公子喝多了，公子不跟他计较。”
[EN] Catching the subtle probe—and hating being tested—Qianli Xun answered smoothly, "I am glad you think of me as family. As for Wenyuan and me, that chapter is closed. As you saw today, I just got married myself—what is left to fret over?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇推开青瓜，过去抓住乔文仙的衣领：“怎么？想打架？”
[EN] Relief washed over Xiaguo's face. "Truth be told, I envy you winning Wenyuan's real love. I dare not ask for love—just waiting on him and chatting with him makes me content." A small triumphant smile curved her lips. "One thing does cheer me up, though: Wenyuan has dropped by my room to chat several times, yet he never spares Miao Zhiyu a glance."
[ZH] 东方尘泽见情势不妙，起来劝道：“大喜的日子，陈公子别跟乔兄计较。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thanked her lucky stars she had never accepted concubinage—not for Wenyuan, not for Prince Yi! Measuring petty victories against rival wives until hearts rotted with schemes sounded like pure hell.
[ZH] 曹班和安明轩一起将陈浩宇拉走，此事才算结束。
[EN] Seeing her former mistress stay silent with obvious boredom, Xiaguo rose to excuse herself. "This is the bridal chamber, and the groom might return any minute—I should head back to the feast and visit again when you have time."
[ZH] 这一幕被另一桌的林觉瞧见，心中不屑：“一个我不要的人，你们却来抢，真可笑！
[EN] "Take care," Qianli Xun offered two cool words as Xiaguo curtsied out with Aimo.
[ZH] 很快林觉倍感失落，毕竟朱淑真是他一见钟情之人，当时的感觉永生难忘，他也端起一盅酒猛然饮下。
[EN] Latching the door once they cleared the yard, Kuqiao wrinkled her nose. "I do not like the new Xiaguo, Miss."
[ZH] 乔文仙继续坐下喝闷酒，喝着喝着想起仪王，心中纳闷，怎么不见仪王的身影？
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] 乔文仙纳闷之际，主桌上，陈江林也纳闷，为何仪王到现在也未出现？小声向一旁的亲家朱晞颜打听：“亲家可知仪王为何没来？”
[EN] "Hard to put my finger on—just rubs me the wrong way."
[ZH] 朱晞颜想起昨晚发生的事，解释道：“估计有事给绊住，贵人未到，大不了明日你我一起上门亲自拜见。”
[EN] "Leave her be—nothing to do with us. I am exhausted and need a nap. When Chen Haoyu gets back, send him to find a bed in the back courtyard."
[ZH] “说的也是。”陈江林继续露出笑脸与他人敬酒。
[EN] "Wait—didn't Miss tell Young Master he gets the side room?"
[ZH] 陈江木已知仪王半路抢亲之事，他的儿子陈浩飞跟着迎亲队伍，什么都看到，悄悄告诉过他，他准备等婚宴结束后再告诉大哥。
[EN] "Changed my mind. Side room is too close—last thing I want waking up is staring at Chen Haoyu's mug." Lying down fully clothed—first night in a strange house, what if Chen Haoyu staggered back drunk into the wrong room?—she let Kuqiao draw the bed curtains and slip out to ready a back-courtyard room before the groom griped.
[ZH] 陈江树还不知仪王抢亲一事，沉浸在儿子计谋得逞的喜悦中。他实在搞不明白，大哥高兴个什么劲儿！娶个离过婚，名声又不好的儿媳妇，有什么好开心的？还把婚礼举行得这么盛大。
[EN] Out in the men's banquet wing, Qiao Wenxian drank cup after bitter cup. Heartbroken at receiving the invitation, he had wanted to stay home, yet Qiao Shouren insisted he represent the Qiaos, so he arrived dutifully with wife and concubine—glad at least to split off with Dongfang Chenze's table while the women headed to their own wing. Watching Shuzhen walk past in bridal crimson beside Chen Haoyu stoked blazing jealousy; he gripped his wine cup so tight his knuckles ached. Once the bride withdrew, his soul walked out with her, and he ignored every friend's plea to slow down.
[ZH] 女宾区内，临安有头有脸的家眷实在不少。
[EN] Worried for his health, Nan Shuang stepped up. "Go easy, Young Master."
[ZH] 新媳妇不能出面敬酒，姚轻琰带着几名能喝酒的丫鬟挨桌敬酒，实在逃不过时，则以清水代酒，谁让她的酒量根本不怎么样。
[EN] "Stay out of it! Back off!" Qiao Wenxian shoved him away.
[ZH] 转完一圈才发现，佳和郡主未到，心中也十分纳闷，向身旁的向夫人问起。
[EN] Soon Chen Haoyu strolled over to make toasts with Qinggua carrying the wine jug. Ignoring Qiao Wenxian's black cloud, Chen Haoyu raised his cup to the table. "Thank you all for gracing my wedding—a toast to you, gentlemen!"
[ZH] 向夫人与朱晞颜一样想起昨晚之事，只因韦嬷嬷并未将迎亲路上发生的事告知她。昨晚之事不便与姚轻琰提起，向夫人也仅是轻描淡写：“贵人事多，恐怕有什么事耽搁住。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze, Cao Ban, An Mingxuan, and the others rose politely to drink back—much as they scorned this rake and pitied Youqi Jushi, wedding courtesy came first. Only Qiao Wenxian stayed seated without lifting a finger.
[ZH] 再问女儿，陈文心不好将昨晚的事在这等场合说了，仅说不知。
[EN] As Chen Haoyu turned to move to the next table, wine-bold Qiao Wenxian called out, "You have not toasted me yet."
[ZH] 姚轻琰不再问下去，继续招呼众人。
[EN] "I just toasted the table—you refused to drink," Chen Haoyu shot back.
[ZH] 许多贵妇与大家闺秀也会借喜宴之际互相走动。
[EN] Filling a cup to the brim and rising, Qiao Wenxian sneered, "What kind of toast lumps a whole table together? Why not toast man to man?"
[ZH] 白梅若本与别人坐一桌，瞧到苗知瑜的母亲秦夫人去了别处，端起酒盅走到苗知瑜身边坐下。
[EN] "Cannot help being popular—half Lin'an showed up. Toast one by one and we would still be drinking tomorrow!"
[ZH] “乔家少夫人怎么看上去不高兴？”
[EN] With one flick of his wrist, Qiao Wenxian flung his wine straight into Chen Haoyu's face! "You are not fit to marry Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜向白梅若看一眼：“还是唤我知瑜吧。”
[EN] Dropping the jug, Qinggua scrambled to dab his master's wet face with his sleeve. "Young Master Qiao had too much wine—do not mind him, Young Master!"
[ZH] 白梅若故意问：“怎么？不喜欢乔家少夫人的称呼？”
[EN] Shoving Qinggua clear, Chen Haoyu snatched Qiao Wenxian by the collar. "Looking for a brawl?!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜淡淡地说：“你我也算认识，虽未走动过，但见到你，我并不觉得讨厌，故而你没必要如此客气，还是像朋友一样唤我知瑜吧。”
[EN] Springing up, Dongfang Chenze stepped between them. "On your wedding day, Young Master Chen—do not trade blows with a drunk!" With Cao Ban and An Mingxuan hauling Chen Haoyu back, the flare-up cooled.
[ZH] 白梅若刚嫁到临安不久，认识的人不多，也希望能结交到好友。上次捶丸场大家第一次见面，不免客气一番，事后她也没敢去打扰苗知瑜，只是在苗知瑜的婚礼上又见过一次而已。
[EN] Watching from a nearby table, Lin Jue scoffed silently: *Look at them fighting over a woman I already ditched—hilarious!* Yet an instant later, a dull hollowness hit him; Zhu Shuzhen had been his love at first sight, a rush he could never erase, and he knocked back a full cup himself.
[ZH] 至于幽栖居士，白梅若更不好随意打扰，毕竟她以前也是林家人，与她做朋友总觉怪怪的。
[EN] Slumping back into drinking, Qiao Wenxian suddenly blinked—where was Prince Yi?
[ZH] 倒是在乔家的婚礼上与佳和郡主聊过几句，后来她想过要去幽林别居拜见郡主，若能与郡主成为朋友，不管对自己还是夫君都是好的。
[EN] At the head table, Chen Jianglin wondered the same thing, leaning to whisper to Zhu Xiyan, "Any idea why Prince Yi has not arrived?"
[ZH] 可惜她去过一次，扑了空，下人说郡主去了望月轩。一打听，才知望月轩是幽栖居士居住之所。如此看来，郡主与幽栖居士关系十分要好。
[EN] Recalling last night's storm, Zhu Xiyan answered smoothly, "Likely tied up with urgent affairs. If His Highness missed today, you and I will call on him together tomorrow to pay respects."
[ZH] 白梅若便放弃继续拜访郡主的想法，只怕郡主因幽栖居士不会真正将她当成朋友看待。不过今日之后她们之间的朋友关系肯定会走到尽头，白梅若的机会终于到来。
[EN] "Fair enough," Chen Jianglin nodded, smiling back into his toasts.
[ZH] 发生那么大的事，郡主没来，没办法利用婚宴的机会与郡主结交，她便将苗知瑜视为最佳人选。
[EN] At another table, Chen Jiangmu already knew about Prince Yi's raid on the bridal procession—his son Haofei had walked with the escort and whispered every detail—and planned to tell Eldest Brother after guests cleared out. Second Brother Chen Jiangshu knew nothing of the raid yet, still basking in his son's triumph while marveling at why Eldest Brother looked so thrilled throwing a fortune at a bride with a divorced past and messy name.
[ZH] 看上去，苗知瑜与幽栖居士走得并不近。况且有一次参加贵妇人的聚会，偶然听到有人谈论幽栖居士与乔公子的一段情，白梅若心领神会，更觉得她与苗知瑜可以成为朋友。
[EN] Over in the women's wing, packed with Lin'an's elite ladies, Yao Qingyan circulated table to table with stout-drinking maids covering for her weak tolerance—swapping in plain water whenever trapped. Completing her rounds, she noticed Princess Jiahe absent too and queried Madam Xiang. Uninformed by Matron Wei about the street ambush yet haunted by last night's scene at Zhu Manor, Madam Xiang deflected mildly: "Royals stay busy—probably delayed." Asking Chen Wenxin brought only a discreet "No idea," so Yao Qingyan dropped it and kept hosting.
[ZH] 从苗知瑜的话语判断，显然是愿意与她做朋友，白梅若亲切称呼道：“知瑜，你我同饮一杯。”
[EN] Meanwhile, guests mingled between tables. Spotting Madam Qin step away, Bai Meiruo carried her cup over and slid into the empty seat beside Miao Zhiyu. "Why does Young Madam Qiao look so downcast?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜端起酒盅，两人碰过后，慢慢饮下。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu glanced at her. "Just call me Zhiyu."
[ZH] 白梅若放下酒盅，无意中瞧见夏果走进女宾区，朝妾室的席位走去。
[EN] "Oh? Not fond of 'Young Madam Qiao'?" Bai Meiruo probed lightly.
[ZH] “不知多少男子羡慕乔公子，既娶了知瑜你这等才女，还纳了一位貌美的丫鬟做妾室。可不知为什么，我瞧着夏姨娘身着绫罗，还是一脸丫鬟相。”
[EN] "We know each other well enough, and I actually find your company pleasant—no need for stiff titles. Call me Zhiyu like a friend."
[ZH] 有人奚落夏果，苗知瑜自是欢心：“来，你我再饮一杯。”
[EN] New to Lin'an with few acquaintances, Bai Meiruo had hoped to build her own circle. Approaching Youqi Jushi felt awkward given her past as Lin Jue's wife; after chatting briefly with Princess Jiahe at the Qiao wedding, she had even tried calling at Youlin Retreat once—only to learn the princess was out at Wangyue Pavilion, Youqi Jushi's home! Figuring the two were thick as thieves, Bai Meiruo had backed off—until today's bombshell tore that friendship to ribbons! With the princess absent today, Miao Zhiyu made the ideal ally—especially after Bai Meiruo overheard noblewomen whispering about Youqi Jushi's old romance with Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 她为二人满上，两人继续对饮。
[EN] Warming at once to Miao Zhiyu's invitation, she raised her cup. "A toast between us, Zhiyu."
[ZH] 饮完，苗知瑜说：“我还有几位闺中密友，今日她们没来，估计是家人不愿他们抛头露面，改日大家相约去永泰茶楼畅聊，如何？”
[EN] Clinking cups, they sipped—just as Xiaguo strolled back into the hall toward the concubines' table.
[ZH] “能得知瑜邀请，不胜荣幸。”白梅若悄悄靠近苗知瑜：“你可知佳和郡主今日为何没来？”
[EN] "How men envy Young Master Qiao," Bai Meiruo murmured slyly, "marrying a talent like you AND taking a pretty maid as concubine! Yet funny—deck Concubine Xia in fine silks, and she still reeks of servant ways."
[ZH] 苗知瑜摇摇头：“不知。”
[EN] Hearing Xiaguo jabbed delighted Miao Zhiyu. "Pour another—let us drink again!"
[ZH] “听说郡主被剑刺伤……”
[EN] Finishing the second cup, Miao Zhiyu suggested, "A few of my close friends skipped today because their families keep them out of crowds. Another day, let us all meet for tea and talk at Yongtai Teahouse!"
[ZH] “啊，何人如此大胆？敢刺伤贵人？”
[EN] "I would be honored!" Leaning closer, Bai Meiruo dropped her voice. "Do you know why Princess Jiahe missed today?"
[ZH] “我身边的丫鬟喜欢看热闹，非要跟着迎亲队伍跑，我没拦着。等她回来时，告诉我仪王今日跑去抢亲，佳和郡主前来阻拦，以性命威胁……”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu shook her head.
[ZH] 苗知瑜惊得声音不由变大：“什么？”
[EN] "Word is Her Highness took a sword wound..."
[ZH] 同桌的人都不解地看向苗知瑜。
[EN] "What?! Who dared stab royalty?!"
[ZH] “没什么事，你们继续。”苗知瑜意识到自己失态，尴尬地笑笑。她拉起白梅若，来到喜宴一旁无人之处，急切地问，“听你刚才所说，仪王抢的是陈家的亲？”
[EN] "My nosy maid chased after the bridal procession for fun. When she ran back, she told me Prince Yi tried snatching the bride mid-street, and Princess Jiahe stepped in threatening her own life..."
[ZH] 白梅若点点头：“没想到幽栖居士连仪王都勾引。”
[EN] "WHAT?!" Miao Zhiyu's gasp drew puzzled stares across the table.
[ZH] 苗知瑜仔细回想：“怎么可能？她喜欢的人明明是文渊？”
[EN] Flushing, she forced a polite smile. "Nothing—carry on!" Pulling Bai Meiruo aside to a quiet corner, she whispered urgently, "You mean Prince Yi tried snatching the CHEN bride?!"
[ZH] 白梅若虽跟幽栖居士没有往来，但她作为林觉的前任，让她想起来甚觉不爽，不免胡乱猜测一番。
[EN] Bai Meiruo nodded. "Who knew Youqi Jushi even hooked Prince Yi?!"
[ZH] “你嫁给乔公子，她没什么机会，为恶心你，把她身边的丫鬟给乔公子当妾室，自己则移情别恋，跑去勾引仪王。”
[EN] "Impossible! She loved Wenyuan!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜听着别人污蔑幽栖居士，心中多少受用，可依然需保持大家闺秀的风范。
[EN] Bearing quiet resentment toward her husband's ex-wife, Bai Meiruo spun freely. "Once you married Young Master Qiao, she had no shot left—so to spite you she shoved her maid into his house, pivoted straight around, and set her hooks in Prince Yi!"
[ZH] “幽栖居士应该不是你说的那种人。”
[EN] Pleasant as it felt hearing Youqi Jushi torn down, Miao Zhiyu kept her refined mask intact. "Youqi Jushi hardly seems that sort..."
[ZH] “是不是，很难说，能确定的是她将佳和郡主得罪。郡主今日受伤，若出什么意外倒也罢了；若无碍，幽栖居士以后的日子可不好过。”
[EN] "Hard to say—what IS certain is she crossed Princess Jiahe! If the princess recovers, Youqi Jushi is in for stormy weather."
[ZH] 苗知瑜无法想象即将发生的事：“她已嫁到陈家，郡主又能将她怎样？”
[EN] "She is already married into the Chen family—what can the princess do to her?"
[ZH] “我们就等着看热闹吧。现下情形，你我及时关注郡主的消息，好找机会拜见她。”
[EN] "Wait and watch the fireworks! Right now, let us both keep an ear out for Her Highness's condition so we can call on her when the moment is right."
[ZH] 苗知瑜点点头，心中不解：“你为何对幽栖居士如此不满？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu nodded, then asked curiously, "Why do you dislike Youqi Jushi so strongly?"
[ZH] 白梅若面色平静，吐露心声：“她倒没做过得罪我的事，可他是我夫君的前妻，夫君嘴上不说，心里依然对她念念不忘，没事总抱着本《王右丞诗集》看。本以为他喜欢诗，好几次见他连书都拿反，脸上却挂着笑容，便知他心不在诗。后来向他身边的下人打听，才知他是因幽栖居士才会捧着诗集看。”
[EN] A flat calm settled over Bai Meiruo's face. "She never wronged me directly—yet she is my husband's former wife. He says nothing aloud, yet his heart still clings to her! Always sitting around holding *Collected Poems of Wang Youcheng*. At first I thought he loved poetry—until I caught him grinning at it while holding the book UPSIDE DOWN! Asking his servants gave me the answer: he only holds it because of Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 苗知瑜叹口气：“唉，没想到你我同病相怜。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu let out a quiet sigh. "Looks like we are two sisters sharing the same ache."
[ZH] 二人怕引起他人注意，不便再说下去，回到喜宴，各自归位，继续与其他人说些客套话。
[EN] Wary of drawing stares, both returned to their seats and resumed polite small talk.

#CH 194
[ZH] 喜宴结束，陈家将亲朋好友以及临安权贵送走后，陈江木拦住二哥陈江树，请他到一旁叙话。
[ZH] 二人来到花园的亭子里，亭子周围春意盎然，谈不上百花齐放，也是花团锦簇。
[ZH] 陈江树问：“三弟要跟我说什么？为何避开其他人？”
[ZH] 陈江木说：“二哥，发生了大事。”
[ZH] “什么大事？”
[ZH] “恐怕陈家会得罪贵人。”
[ZH] “仪王？”
[ZH] “是。”
[ZH] “为何如此讲？”
[ZH] 陈江木说：“浩飞跟随迎亲队伍，亲眼目睹仪王带兵在半道上抢亲，要不是佳和郡主以性命威胁，恐怕朱家姑娘嫁不到陈家。”
[ZH] “什么？仪王抢亲？有这等事？”陈江树惊讶不已，也担忧不已，陈家可不能得罪贵人，瞬间也理解了为何仪王没来参加陈家的婚宴。
[ZH] 陈江木是三兄弟中的老实人，遇事总是拿不定主意，喜欢向两位哥哥请教：“我听说时也是又惊又怕，不知该不该告诉大哥？所以问问二哥的意思。”
[ZH] 陈江树想想说：“此事瞒不住，大哥迟早会知道，说不定浩宇已告诉他，你与我一同去找大哥，听听他的意思。”
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰刚回屋坐下，品过几口茶，陈家二爷和三爷来访，应该有要事商谈，姚轻琰不便在此待着，去里屋休息。
[ZH] 陈江林招呼两个弟弟：“来，一起坐坐，尝尝我泡的茶。”
[ZH] 陈江树坐在大哥对面。因木榻上仅容两人，陈江木则挑椅子坐好。
[ZH] 陈江林倒好茶，吩咐丫鬟给三弟端到跟前。
[ZH] 陈江树还未品茶，急忙说：“大哥，发生一件大事，不知您有没有听浩宇说起？”
[ZH] “什么大事？”陈江林显然还不知。
[ZH] “大哥难道没有怀疑过仪王为何今日没来？”
[ZH] 老二的问题令陈江林担忧：“难道你们听说了什么？”
[ZH] 陈江木大胆说：“浩飞悄悄对我讲，仪王今日半道上抢亲，佳和郡主以死相逼，受了伤，此事才算作罢。”
[ZH] 陈江林本要拿茶盏品茶，听到这话，被吓着，手不由哆嗦一下，茶盏竟被碰翻，茶水倒在桌上。
[ZH] 一旁伺候的丫鬟赶紧上前收拾。
[ZH] 陈江林急忙大声说：“去！给我把浩宇找来！”
[EN] Once the banquet ended and the last guests departed, Chen Jiangmu drew Second Brother Chen Jiangshu aside into a spring-blooming garden pavilion.
[ZH] 有下人听到，跑着去找公子。
[EN] "What is the secret, Third Brother?" Chen Jiangshu asked.
[ZH] 喜宴结束后，陈浩宇想到朱淑真还未用饭，吩咐青瓜备些饭菜，用食盒提上。
[EN] "Huge trouble, Second Brother—the Chens might have crossed royalty!"
[ZH] 他这么做并非关心朱淑真，毕竟两人以后要住在一个屋檐下，需把关系处理好，往后的日子才好过。
[EN] "Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇抬头望一眼天空，太阳已偏西，这一日过得实在太快。偏西的太阳很容易让他想到洞房花烛，可新娘是只母老虎，他不敢轻易上手，打算送完饭去找春金沫。
[EN] "Yes! Haofei marched with the bridal escort and watched with his own eyes as Prince Yi brought soldiers to snatch the bride mid-road! Had Princess Jiahe not put her own life on the line, Miss Zhu would never have reached Chen Manor!"
[ZH] 当陈浩宇一身酒气走进院子时，发现所有的丫鬟正排着队，接受朱淑真训话。而朱淑真已换去婚服，那样子根本不像刚嫁过来的新妇。
[EN] Alarm shot through Chen Jiangshu—offending royalty was no joke, and now Prince Yi's absence made total sense!
[ZH] 原来千里寻睡过大概半个时辰后醒来，感到腹中饥饿，用过韦嬷嬷派人送来的饭菜，无事可做，想起韦嬷嬷提到院子里下人比较多，便想了解一番，也好寻些事做，尽快忘记仪王抢亲之事。
[EN] Honest and cautious by nature, Chen Jiangmu always leaned on his elder brothers. "I froze when Haofei told me—was not sure whether to tell Eldest Brother, so I came to you first."
[ZH] 苦荞陪她在院中观察一会儿，陈浩宇的院中尽是些丫鬟，人数还不少，一个个长得水灵，连洒扫丫鬟都有几分姿色。
[EN] "No hiding something this huge—Eldest Brother will find out sooner or later, maybe Haoyu already told him. Come with me to see Eldest Brother right now."
[ZH] 千里寻心中不爽，陈家的纨绔子弟真是色迷心窍，整日不着家还放这么多人，实在过分。既然她已是院子的主人，一切得按照她的喜好来。
[EN] In the main courtyard, Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan had just sat down for quiet tea when the two brothers arrived; sensing serious talk, Yao Qingyan excused herself to the inner room.
[ZH] 她将所有丫鬟聚集到前院，大概二三十人。也把陈府的王管家差人找来，商议丫鬟的去处。
[EN] Pouring tea and inviting his brothers to sit—Chen Jiangshu opposite on the couch, Chen Jiangmu on a chair—Chen Jianglin had barely handed cups out when Chen Jiangshu cut straight in. "Big trouble, Eldest Brother—did Haoyu tell you?"
[ZH] 刚商量好，陈浩宇走进来，脸色微红，便知他在喜宴中喝过不少酒。
[EN] "Tell me what?"
[ZH] “回来得正好，顺便知会你一声，这些丫鬟我一个都不需要，准备将她们打发走，刚才详细了解过，一半人愿意拿了卖身契离开陈府，一半人想继续留下。我让王管家把想留的人分派到陈府其他院子。”
[EN] "Did you not wonder why Prince Yi skipped the feast?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得酒醒一半儿，指着朱淑真：“你！你实在过分！”
[EN] "Heard something?" Chen Jianglin tensed.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来：“不要用手指老娘，我现是院子的主人，一切得按照我的喜好来。”
[EN] Chen Jiangmu spoke up straight. "Haofei whispered to me that Prince Yi raided the bridal procession today! Princess Jiahe forced him back at risk of her own life and got wounded before he withdrew."
[ZH] 陈浩宇无法反驳，继续强辩：“总得给我留几个样貌好些的？”
[EN] Reaching for his tea bowl, Chen Jianglin jerked in shock, tipping the cup clean across the tabletop. As a maid scrambled to wipe the spill, he barked, "Go fetch Haoyu this instant!"
[ZH] “你经常不着家，留着有什么用？”千里寻看向青瓜，“叫什么名字？”
[EN] A servant sprinted off.
[ZH] 青瓜老实回道：“小的叫青瓜。”
[EN] Meanwhile, after the feast wrapped up, Chen Haoyu figured Zhu Shuzhen had not eaten all day and had Qinggua pack a food carrier—not from romance, purely keeping peace under one roof. Glancing at the sinking sun reminded him of his wedding night; no way he dared lay a hand on that tigress, so he planned to drop off the meal and head straight to Chun Jinmo.
[ZH] 千里寻果断说：“我看青瓜不错，他一个人伺候你足矣。”
[EN] Reeking of wine as he stepped into his courtyard, he stopped dead: twenty or thirty maids stood lined up in formation while Zhu Shuzhen—already out of her bridal gown—addressed them like a general!
[ZH] 陈浩宇忍住怒气：“好男不跟女斗，你想怎样就怎样。只是将她们都打发走，这院子没人收拾，难道你想什么事都让苦荞一个丫鬟做？不得把她累死！”
[EN] Waking from a one-hour nap hungry, Qianli Xun had eaten the meal Matron Wei sent over, then decided to inspect the household staff to shake off thoughts of Prince Yi's ambush. Strolling the courtyard with Kuqiao revealed an absurd crowd of pretty young maids—even the sweepers looked handpicked for looks! Disgusted by this rake keeping a private harem while never even sleeping home, she summoned all twenty or thirty maids alongside Chen Manor's Butler Wang to clear house.
[ZH] 正说着从门外进来十人，陈浩宇认得，正是在望月轩外与蔣爷的人对打的十人。
[EN] Spotting Chen Haoyu walk in flushed from wine, she greeted him briskly. "Good timing! Just letting you know I need zero of these maids and am clearing them out. Already asked each one: half want their deeds of sale back to leave, half want to stay on at Chen Manor, so Butler Wang will reassign those to other courtyards."
[ZH] 红豆上前报告：“小姐，韦嬷嬷吩咐我先把人带进来，您的书全部在陈府大门外的马车里，是现在搬进来？还是等明日？”
[EN] Half his wine buzz evaporated in sheer outrage as Chen Haoyu stabbed a finger at her. "You—you have gone way too far!"
[ZH] “瞧见了吧，老娘有人伺候。”千里寻得意完，对红豆说，“先把马车在陈府安置好，等韦嬷嬷找人把屋子里重新布置完，再将书搬进来。”
[EN] Rising to her feet, Qianli Xun snapped, "Point that finger somewhere else! This courtyard belongs to me now, and it runs my way!"
[ZH] “是！”红豆领命后，对带来的九人说，“都散了吧，各自去忙活。”她则继续出门安排马车之事。
[EN] Unable to contradict her, he tried bargaining. "At least leave me a couple pretty ones!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得不行：“朱淑真！屋子里的家具可都是名贵木材打造，你要重新布置，难道也要把家具搬走不成？”
[EN] "You barely sleep here—what for?" Flicking a look at Qinggua, she asked, "Your name?"
[ZH] “既然要重新布置，必然会把不需要的搬走。当然，我若觉得有用自会留下。对了，还有一件事需要说明，偏房离我近，我准备安排苦荞和红豆住在里面。院子后面还有两间屋子，左边的给韦嬷嬷住，右边的你住着。苦荞已替你安排好，床铺得很舒服，只是简陋些。”
[EN] "Qinggua, Young Madam."
[ZH] 陈浩宇气得嘴唇发抖：“朱……朱淑真，你太……太过分！枉我关心你，让青瓜给你带饭，我把饭喂猪都不给你吃！”
[EN] "Qinggua looks handy enough. One attendant is plenty for you."
[ZH] 千里寻早已注意到青瓜提的食盒：“多谢关心，可惜我已用过饭，等你送饭，老娘不得被活活饿死。”
[EN] Choking down fury, Chen Haoyu sneered, "A real man does not wrestle women—have it your way! Still, boot everyone out and who sweeps the courtyard? Working Kuqiao to death?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇正不知该如何是好，父亲身边的下人进来：“公子，老爷找您。”
[EN] Right on cue, ten familiar figures marched through the courtyard gate—the exact crew who had flattened Master Jiang's gang outside Wangyue Pavilion!
[ZH] 陈浩宇无处发泄，伸出一脚，将青瓜手中的食盒踢翻，只见饭菜全部倒在地上。
[EN] Hongdou stepped forward to report: "Miss, Matron Wei sent us ahead. Carts loaded with all your books are waiting outside the gate—move them in now or wait till tomorrow?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇的脚也被踢疼，他抬起脚“哎吆”两声后，准备去见父亲，却被千里寻堵在面前。
[EN] "See? Plenty of help!" Qianli Xun smirked, then told Hongdou, "Park the carts inside the manor and wait until Matron Wei finishes redoing the hall before unloading the books."
[ZH] “收拾完再走！”
[EN] "Yes, Miss!" Dismissing the nine others to settle in, Hongdou turned back out to direct the carts.
[ZH] 青瓜见状，担心二人打起来，上前帮公子开脱：“少夫人，我收拾，我收拾。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu turned purple. "Zhu Shuzhen! Every stick of furniture in that hall is rare hardwood! Redoing the room—are you hauling MY furniture out too?!"
[ZH] 千里寻这才让开，陈浩宇攥紧拳头气呼呼地离开。
[EN] "Whatever I do not want goes; whatever fits stays. Oh, one more thing: the side room sits close to me, so Kuqiao and Hongdou move in there. Out back sit two rooms—Matron Wei takes the left, you take the right. Kuqiao already made your bed nice and cozy, just a bit plain."
[ZH] 青瓜蹲下边收拾食盒与倒在地上的饭菜边想：“公子总算遇到克星，也不知是好是坏。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's lips trembled with rage. "Zhu... Zhu Shuzhen! Too far! Here I felt sorry for you and brought you dinner—I would rather feed it to pigs than give you a bite!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇暂时先将怒气放到一边，来到父亲的屋子，见二叔与三叔都在，猜到他们肯定是知道仪王抢亲之事，才唤他前来。
[EN] Glancing at the food carrier in Qinggua's hand, she smiled coolly. "Thanks for the thought, yet I already ate. Wait for your leftovers and I would starve to death."
[ZH] 陈浩宇先行礼：“爹，二叔，三叔。”
[EN] Just then a servant hurried in from the master's wing: "Young Master, Master summons you!"
[ZH] 陈江林急切地问：“你老实告诉我，仪王抢亲之事可否属实？”
[EN] Desperate to vent, Chen Haoyu swung a kick straight into Qinggua's food carrier, sending bowls and dishes smashing across the flagstones—and stubbing his own toes so hard he hopped yelling "Ow! Ow!" Starting out the gate, he slammed straight into Qianli Xun blocking his path.
[ZH] 陈浩宇老实承认：“属实。”
[EN] "Clean it up before you leave!"
[ZH] “佳和郡主伤得如何？”
[EN] Terrified a brawl was about to erupt, Qinggua scrambled onto his knees. "I will clean it, Young Madam! Leave it to me!"
[ZH] “没伤在要害，应该无性命之忧。”
[EN] Only then did Qianli Xun step aside; fists clenched tight, Chen Haoyu stormed out. Sweeping shattered bowls into the tray, Qinggua mused to himself: *Young Master finally met his nemesis—wonder if that is blessing or curse!*
[ZH] “那便好。你觉得会不会因为此事对陈家不利？”
[EN] Swallowing his anger, Chen Haoyu reached his father's hall, saw both uncles seated inside, and knew at once word of the ambush had leaked. Bowing to all three, he braced himself.
[ZH] 刚刚受过朱淑真的气，不如趁此机会将她编排一番：“此事不能怪陈家，要怪只怪朱淑真，她跟仪王有私情，却跑来祸害陈家，祸害我！”
[EN] "Tell me straight," Chen Jianglin demanded. "Did Prince Yi try to snatch your bride?"
[ZH] 陈江树听到这话更觉儿子的计谋实在高明，浩宇娶不对人，只会惠及自己的儿子：“大哥，浩宇说得没错，这事不能怪陈家。我听说仪王是最明事理的人，应该不会因为个人之事为难陈家。”
[EN] "Yes, Father."
[ZH] 陈江林避开谈论仪王与朱家姑娘的私情，毕竟她已嫁到陈家，是他陈江林的儿媳妇，便点点头：“说得有道理，耐心等等，再找机会登门向仪王谢罪。”
[EN] "How bad is Princess Jiahe's wound?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想不通：“爹，我们何罪之有？为何要登门谢罪？”
[EN] "Missed vital organs—she will pull through."
[ZH] 陈江林说：“不管有罪无罪，毕竟事情发生在陈家迎亲之时，总需有个态度，才不会让贵人面子上挂不住，再说仪王明理，见陈家态度诚恳，应该不会怪罪。”
[EN] "Thank Heaven. Do you think this spells trouble for the Chen family?"
[ZH] 两位叔叔也都同意，陈浩宇还想继续编排朱淑真，话还未出口，听到陈江林的叮嘱：“今日是你大喜的日子，别让新娘等得焦急，回去吧。”
[EN] Fresh off being steamrolled by Zhu Shuzhen, Chen Haoyu leaped at the chance to paint her black. "None of this is the Chens' fault! Blame Zhu Shuzhen—carrying on with Prince Yi behind closed doors, then dragging that disaster into Chen Manor onto my head!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇行礼退出。
[EN] Privately thrilled—the messier Haoyu's marriage, the cleaner Haodong's path to the empire!—Chen Jiangshu chimed in smoothly, "Haoyu speaks true, Eldest Brother. None of this falls on us. Besides, Prince Yi is known for fairness and will hardly punish the Chens over private heartache."
[ZH] 天色已暗下，想到自己的院子已被霸占，他产生不了任何回去的欲望，打算直接出府去春香楼。
[EN] Steering clean clear of gossip about his brand-new daughter-in-law, Chen Jianglin nodded. "Fair point. Wait a beat, then we call on Prince Yi in person to apologize."
[ZH] 走到半道，被红豆和两个丫鬟给拦截住。
[EN] "Apologize for what?!" Chen Haoyu balked. "What crime did we commit?!"
[ZH] “你们三个是不是想找死？在陈府敢随便拦本公子？”
[EN] "Crime or not, it unfolded during our bridal escort," Chen Jianglin explained. "Showing humility saves royal face, and a fair prince will not hold a grudge once he sees our respect."
[ZH] 红豆说：“姑爷误会，我们不是拦您。只因韦嬷嬷说明日一早，我家小姐还需与您一同拜见陈家长辈，给公婆献茶，没有姑爷在场礼不周全，小姐才派我们三人带姑爷回去。”
[EN] Both uncles agreed. Chen Haoyu opened his mouth to trash Zhu Shuzhen further when his father waved him off. "Your wedding night—do not leave your bride waiting. Head back."
[ZH] “我要偏不回去呢？”
[EN] Bowing out into the dark, Chen Haoyu had zero desire to crawl back to his hijacked courtyard and steered straight for the front gate toward Chunxiang Brothel—only to get boxed in halfway across the grounds by Hongdou and two sturdy maids!
[ZH] “小姐说，扛也要把姑爷扛回去。”
[EN] "Got a death wish, blocking this young master inside his own manor?!"
[ZH] 两名练习过拳法的丫鬟上去一人一个胳膊，架着陈浩宇就走，任其怎么反抗都没用。
[EN] "No offense, Young Master," Hongdou answered politely. "Matron Wei reminded us you must accompany Miss at dawn to serve morning tea to the Chen elders. Skipping out breaks rite, so Miss sent us to escort you back."
[ZH] 回到院子，红豆把院门关好，两个丫鬟才罢手。
[EN] "And if I refuse?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇大喊：“朱淑真！你不守信用！白纸黑字写着不干涉我自由！现在怎么变卦？”
[EN] "Miss said carry you back over our shoulders if we have to."
[ZH] 听到喊声，千里寻从屋内出来：“吼什么吼？老娘才懒得干涉你的自由。你也不瞧瞧自己身上穿着什么？新婚头一晚新郎跑出去，让人笑话……”
[EN] Before he blinked, the two martial-trained maids hooked his arms from either side and frog-marched him straight back despite his thrashing! Dumping him inside the yard, Hongdou latched the gate before letting him go.
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机调侃：“怎么？难道想跟本公子洞房花烛不成？”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen! Oath-breaker!" Chen Haoyu roared. "Signed in black and white zero interference with my freedom—going back on your word night one?!"
[ZH] “洞房你个头！明日一大早你还得陪老娘拜见长辈，你跑出去，难道想让老娘一个人见长辈不成？放心，就一晚上限制你，以后你随便去哪里浪荡，老娘绝不会干涉。”
[EN] Stepping onto the porch, Qianli Xun shot back, "Quit howling! Like I care about your freedom! Look at what you are wearing—a groom bolting on his wedding night makes both of us a joke!"
[ZH] 千里寻转身回屋，留下陈浩宇一个人待在院子倍感落寞。
[EN] "Oh?" Chen Haoyu leered slyly. "Hoping to consummate the marriage after all?"
[ZH] ﻿“多亏本公子有先见之明，婚前跟你立下契约，否则以后过的都不是人该过的日子。”
[EN] "Consummate my foot! Tomorrow morning you escort me to greet your elders—leave me going solo? Relax, just tonight! Starting tomorrow afternoon, go rot in any gutter you want and I will not lift a finger!"
[ZH] 自语完，乖乖朝后院拐去。
[EN] Turning back inside, she shut the door, leaving Chen Haoyu standing lonely under the stars. "Thank heaven I had the brains to sign a contract first, or living under this roof would be hell!" Muttering, he shuffled off toward the back courtyard.

#CH 195
[ZH] 来到后院，瞧见韦嬷嬷住的屋子亮着灯，陈浩宇心中不满：“让我跟个老女人住对面，哼！亏你想得出！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇虽埋怨，又拿朱淑真没办法，只好朝他住的屋子走去。推开门，青瓜正在收拾床铺。
[ZH] 青瓜见公子进来，迎上去：“公子，少夫人差人已把屋子打扫干净，床铺我也给您收拾好。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇没好气地说：“瞧瞧，你家公子我可怜成什么样？被逼到后院来住，屋子还这么简陋。”
[ZH] 青瓜也心疼自家公子：“新婚之夜，公子连洞房都进不了，实在可怜，不如明日在老爷和夫人跟前告少夫人一状，让老爷与夫人收拾她。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇伸出一脚，向青瓜踢去：“你懂个屁！本公子才不愿跟她入洞房！”
[ZH] 青瓜朝一旁闪开：“真想不通公子不愿入洞房，还成哪门子亲？”
[ZH] 陈浩宇懒得回答青瓜的问题，无意中瞧见屋里多出一张床：“为什么我睡觉的地方多出一张床来？”
[ZH] 青瓜怕再被踢，躲远些，笑嘻嘻地说：“嘿嘿嘿，少夫人说，让我跟公子住一个屋子，方便伺候。”
[ZH] “朱淑真！你欺人太甚！等我办完重要的事情，定要找个欺压夫君的恶名休了你！”陈浩宇一拳砸在身边的桌上，桌子一点儿事没有，手却生疼，直喊，“哎吆！疼死我了！”
[ZH] 青瓜上前替公子揉手：“您这一天尽伤自己，还是悠着点儿，别再生少夫人的气。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇甩开手：“伺候本公子睡觉！”
[ZH] 青瓜伺候自家公子宽去新郎装，待他睡下后，也倒在自己的床上。
[ZH] 第一次跟公子睡一个屋，青瓜颇感好玩儿，也佩服这位少夫人，刚嫁过来，就把公子收拾得无法还击，照这个节奏下去，公子以后应该会大变样。青瓜忽然之间似乎理解了老爷和夫人为何要坚持娶朱家有过和离经历的姑娘。
[ZH] 话说申简辰抱着乔梦容来到一家医馆，大夫帮乔梦容止血包扎后说：“伤口并无大碍。”
[ZH] 申简辰松口气，抱起梦容坐上马车回幽林别居。
[ZH] 今日抢亲以失败告终，申简辰一路埋怨仪王的身份一点儿用处都没有。他穿越前看过的影视剧里，很多王爷想怎样就怎样，纳个把妾室不在话下。而自己，一个乔梦容就把他搞得手足无措，错失心爱之人。
[ZH] 回到幽林别居，他把梦容放在床上，见她依旧泪眼婆娑，宽慰道：“以后别再做傻事。”
[ZH] “是哥哥别再做傻事才对。”
[ZH] 申简辰想要回屋，被梦容拉住手：“哥哥，陪我一会儿。”
[ZH] 因愧疚，他在床边坐下：“好，哥哥陪会儿你。”
[ZH] 乔梦容闭上眼睛，泪水从眼角滚落下来。
[ZH] 申简辰从喜兰手中接过丝帕，轻轻为梦容拭去眼泪。
[ZH] 手还未来得及挪开，被梦容握住：“哥哥，告诉我，什么时候的事？”
[ZH] 梦容终于问出，申简辰怕说出她会更伤心，回避道：“等你的伤好后，我再回答你。”
[ZH] 乔梦容睁开泪眼：“不，我现在就想知道。”
[ZH] 申简辰对一旁的喜兰说：“你跟白风出去吧。”
[ZH] 王爷与郡主现下的景况，两人也不好再待下去，他们一起走出，将闺房的门从外面关好。
[ZH] 放下梦容的手，申简辰继续用丝帕帮她擦去泪水。
[ZH] “我不是赵朴，她也不是幽栖居士，我们在另一个时代就认识，我一直爱着她，来到这个时代，好不容易遇见，自然不会轻易放弃……”
[ZH] 乔梦容想起淑真说她失忆过，而哥哥也什么都不记得，难道一切都是真的？哥哥说好不容易遇见，看来他占卜寻人寻的并不是千里寻，而是淑真。难怪知道千里寻是写话本的才子后，再无后续。
[EN] Reaching the back courtyard and seeing lamplight glowing across from him in Matron Wei's room, Chen Haoyu grumbled, "Shoving me right across from an old matron—she really has nerve!" Still powerless against Zhu Shuzhen, he pushed into his assigned room, where Qinggua was patting down the bedding.
[ZH] 乔梦容想不通，也不愿相信：“哥哥移情别恋，何必找个冠冕堂皇又无法让人信服的理由。淑真美貌多才，男子见了个个喜欢，只是没想到我的哥哥也被她魅惑，她就是个行为不检点善于勾 引男子的人……”
[EN] "Young Madam had this room scrubbed spotless and I made your bed fresh, Young Master," Qinggua greeted him.
[ZH] 乔梦容越说越气，不由咳嗽起来：“咳咳咳……”因为咳嗽，伤口一阵疼痛，再痛也比不上她内心的疼痛。
[EN] "Look how pathetic your master has been brought—banished to a plain shack in the back yard!"
[ZH] 申简辰束手无策，继续安慰：“梦容，别再想这些事情，我最后的努力被你破坏，我与她已不可能……”
[EN] "On your wedding night too—barred from your own bridal chamber!" Qinggua sympathized. "Why not tattle to Master and Madam tomorrow and let them put Young Madam in her place?"
[ZH] 听到“已不可能”，乔梦容的情绪稳定许多，不再咳嗽：“哥哥是怨我吗？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu kicked out at him. "What do you know?! Like I even WANT to share a bridal chamber with her!"
[ZH] 申简辰觉出许多人生的无奈，无法将一切归咎到梦容身上：“不，我是怨自己，既不能让你开心，也不能让自己开心。”
[EN] Dodging aside, Qinggua scratched his head. "Beats me why you even got married if you refuse the bridal chamber."
[ZH] 乔梦容累了，不想再说下去，再次闭上眼睛：“哥哥，我想睡会儿，你别走，陪着我，好吗？”
[EN] Ignoring him, Chen Haoyu spotted an extra cot against the wall. "Why is there a second bed in MY room?!"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，心里已答应。他坐在床边，又为她拭去眼角的泪水。
[EN] Backing out of kicking range with a cheeky grin, Qinggua chuckled, "Heh, Young Madam ordered me to bunk in here so I can wait on you handy!"
[ZH] 大约过去两刻钟，梦容带着痛苦进入梦乡，申简辰将手中的丝帕放在她的枕边，起身走出闺房，小心将门带上。
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen! You pushed me too far! Wait until I wrap up my business—I will slap you with a charge of abusing your husband and divorce your hide!" Slamming his fist onto the tabletop, Chen Haoyu winced—not a scratch on the wood, while his knuckles throbbed red. "Ow! Damn, that hurts!"
[ZH] 他百感惆怅，梦容的做法令他无路可退。可他还是想朝前迈出一步，哪怕千里寻已嫁人。
[EN] Rubbing his master's hand, Qinggua murmured, "You keep injuring yourself today—take it easy and stop feuding with Young Madam."
[ZH] 望望天空，太阳还挂在高空，此时她应该已和陈浩宇拜堂成亲。
[EN] "Hands off—help me undress for bed!"
[ZH] 申简辰竟然羡慕起陈浩宇，这个纨绔子弟，不学无术，跟千里寻还是死对头，却有幸把她娶回家，天道实在不公！
[EN] Peeling off his master's crimson groom robes and climbing into his own cot once Chen Haoyu lay down, Qinggua grinned into the dark—sharing a room with his master felt downright novel, and watching Young Madam tame him flat on day one finally explained why Master and Madam had fought so hard to bring a divorced Miss Zhu home!
[ZH] 申简辰不由自主走出幽林别居去往神柳处。
[EN] Meanwhile, back when Shen Jianchen had carried Qiao Mengrong to a clinic, the physician bandaged her wound and reassured him it was shallow and posed no danger. Exhaling hard, Shen Jianchen carried her back to Youlin Retreat, cursing how hollow his princely title had proved—in every costume drama back home, princes took wives and concubines at will, yet one Qiao Mengrong had tied his hands and cost him the woman he loved!
[ZH] 密林中的树木早已发芽，树叶长出一些，满目新绿。一路嗅着春日的芬芳，申简辰孤身一人穿越密林。
[EN] Laying her onto her bed at Youlin Retreat and watching tears gleam on her lashes, he murmured softly, "Never pull a stunt like that again."
[ZH] 到达池塘边，望向对岸，神柳神采依旧，在阳光下如同挂了一树的碧玉。申简辰想起唐代诗人贺知章的一首诗《咏柳》，上小学时已会背诵，不由脱口而出：“碧玉妆成一树高，万条垂下绿丝绦。不知细叶谁裁出，二月春风似剪刀。”
[EN] "Only if Brother stops pulling stunts first."
[ZH] 他一边吟诵一边沿着池塘岸边靠近神柳。
[EN] When he moved to rise, she caught his hand. "Stay with me a little while, Brother."
[ZH] 连他自己都搞不懂，明明心里难过，为何会想到这么美的诗来？
[EN] Weighed down with guilt, he sat on the bedside. "Alright. Brother will sit with you."
[ZH] 越来越靠近神柳时，才找出能说服自己的理由，应该是神柳想用这么美的诗句洗去他内心难过的情绪，他才会开心一些。
[EN] Closing her eyes, Qiao Mengrong let silent tears roll down her temples. Taking a silk handkerchief from Xilan, Shen Jianchen gently blotted them away. Before he drew back, her fingers closed around his wrist. "Tell me, Brother—when did it start?"
[ZH] 神柳的枝条在春风的吹拂下轻轻摆动，系在上面的红绸如同鲜艳的衣裙。那红色让他想到身着红色嫁衣的千里寻。
[EN] Dreading fresh grief, he hedged. "Wait until your wound heals, then I will answer."
[ZH] 她从婚轿中飞身而出，落在众人面前时，申简辰看得呆住。她那么美好，再次激发他想要拥有的欲望，梦容的举动如同一盆凉水，将他的欲望之火狠狠浇灭。
[EN] Opening swimming eyes, she shook her head. "No. I want to know right now."
[ZH] 欲望还在，他不敢再让欲望燃烧出火苗，只怕那火没将自己烧毁，却要了梦容的命。
[EN] Glancing over, Shen Jianchen told Xilan, "Step outside with Bai Feng." Both attendants exited and closed the door behind them.
[ZH] 申简辰向神柳发出灵魂的拷问：“神柳，你告诉我，我该怎么办？是替赵朴迎娶他的妹妹，还是利用仪王的身份逼迫陈家把她还给我？”
[EN] Freeing his wrist to wipe another tear, Shen Jianchen spoke the raw truth. "I am not Zhao Pu, and she is not Youqi Jushi. We knew each other in another age altogether. I loved her long ago, and after finding her again in this world against all odds, I could not just walk away..."
[ZH] 许久，神柳沉默不语，柳条继续在风中摆动，将一条红绸吹落下来，吹到一旁的荆棘上，被尖刺挂住。
[EN] Flashes returned to Qiao Mengrong—Shuzhen claiming amnesia, Brother remembering nothing of his past—could it really be true? Was THAT why Brother had cast lots on the street and stopped hunting the writer Qianli Xun? Still, she refused to believe it. "If Brother's heart wandered, why invent some tall tale nobody could swallow?! Shuzhen is gorgeous and brilliant, turning every man's head—I just never thought even my own brother would fall for her spells! Shameless seductress..."
[ZH] 那红绸与他毫无关系，申简辰懒得理会，继续沉浸在等待中。
[EN] Fury triggered a fit of coughing that pulled sharp pain at her bandaged side—still nothing next to the agony tearing her heart.
[ZH] 有一只手把红绸从尖刺上取下，走近神柳，挑选好柳枝，将红绸系上。
[EN] Helpless, Shen Jianchen patted her shoulder. "Stop dwelling on it, Mengrong. You wrecked my last push—there is no road left between her and me..."
[ZH] 申简辰仔细瞧那人，身上背着包袱，面目慈祥，正是上次在神柳下遇见的女尼，也正是净光法师。
[EN] Hearing "no road left" calmed her ragged breathing. "Does Brother blame me?"
[ZH] 净光法师系完红绸，跟申简辰打招呼：“阿弥陀佛。”
[EN] Sighing under life's crushing ironies, Shen Jianchen shook his head. "No—I blame myself, unable to bring joy to you or to myself."
[ZH] 申简辰回礼道：“法师安好，又遇见您，真是有缘。”
[EN] Spent, Qiao Mengrong closed her eyes. "I want to sleep a bit, Brother. Stay beside me—do not leave, alright?"
[ZH] 净光法师说：“我听说临安首富陈家今日娶亲，便下山化缘。陈家倒慷慨，给了好些名贵的茶叶和药材。来到后山又遇见施主。既是为化缘下山，施主需给我些什么才对。”
[EN] Saying nothing aloud, he stayed put, dabbing the last tears from her lashes until, half an hour later, exhaustion pulled her into sleep. Setting the handkerchief by her pillow, he slipped quietly out and shut the door.
[ZH] 申简辰出来得匆忙，身上并未带钱财，上下摸摸，摸到挂在腰间的一块玉佩，取下来，递给法师：“我身上只有这玩意儿值几个钱，法师若不嫌弃，尽管拿去。”
[EN] Gloom pressed heavy on his chest. Mengrong's desperate gamble had walled off every escape—yet his heart still tugged toward Qianli Xun. Glancing at the afternoon sun still high in the sky, he knew she must be bowing beside Chen Haoyu right now. Actually envying that worthless playboy Chen Haoyu! How unfair Heaven could be.
[ZH] 净光法师接过：“施主不怕我是骗您？”
[EN] Feet carrying him on autopilot, Shen Jianchen wandered through the blooming woods out toward the Divine Willow. Reaching the pond's rim, he saw the willow draped in fresh green shoots glittering like jade in the sunlight. Lines from He Zhizhang's *The Willow*—memorized back in grade school—slipped off his tongue unbidden:
[ZH] 申简辰说：“钱财乃身外之物，生不带来，死了也带不走。玉佩放在我身边只是个装饰，给法师，如若能卖几个钱，既可修缮尼姑庵，亦可填补用度，也算我功德一件，岂有法师骗我的道理？”
[EN] "The slender beauty's dressed in emerald all about; A thousand branches droop like fringes made of jade. But do you know by whom these slim leaves are cut out? The wind of early spring is sharp as scissor blade." [TN: Translated by Xu Yuanchong] Strange how such bright poetry surfaced while his chest felt hollowed out—maybe the Divine Willow was nudging brighter lines into his head to soften the blow. As red prayer ribbons fluttered on the breeze like scarlet bridal silk, Qianli Xun's dazzling leap from the wedding sedan flashed back before his eyes, fanning longing he dared not feed lest it cost Mengrong's life. "Tell me, Divine Willow," he whispered upward, "what am I supposed to do? Marry Zhao Pu's sister in his place, or use Prince Yi's power to pry her back from the Chens?"
[ZH] 净光法师赞道：“施主活得通透，令老尼佩服，看在施主送我玉佩的份儿上，我送施主一句话：守得云开见月明。”
[EN] Only wind answered, blowing one red ribbon loose onto a nearby briar bush where thorns snagged it fast. Too drained to care, Shen Jianchen stood still—until a weathered hand plucked the ribbon clear of the thorns and tied it neatly back onto a willow bough.
[ZH] 申简辰不能体会其中真意：“法师能否说得明白些？”
[EN] Turning, he recognized the kind-faced elderly nun with a cloth bundle slung over her shoulder—Master Jingguang!
[ZH] 净光法师笑道：“那我再送施主一句话：随缘而不攀缘。”
[EN] "Amitabha," she greeted him.
[ZH] 申简辰更觉难以理解：“还请法师解惑。”
[EN] "Peace to you, Master. What fate meeting you again."
[ZH] 净光法师说：“随缘如同顺风顺水，人生才会过得顺遂。过分攀缘如同逆流而上，逆风而行，自然阻碍重重。施主心中之事还是随缘得好，只有随缘才能守得云开见月明。”
[EN] "Hearing Lin'an's wealthiest Chen family held a wedding today, I came down the mountain for alms—and generous they were, gifting fine teas and herbs! Coming round the back hill, I bump into Patron too. Since I came down for alms, surely Patron owes me a donation?"
[ZH] 申简辰有所领会：“感谢法师解惑。”
[EN] Having rushed out empty-handed, Shen Jianchen patted his sash, untied a fine jade pendant from his belt, and held it out. "This trinket is the only thing of value on me—if Master does not mind it, take it."
[ZH] “阿弥陀佛。”净光法师合掌后向后山走去。
[EN] Accepting it, Master Jingguang smiled. "Not worried I am a swindler?"
[ZH] 申简辰目送法师的身影消失在眼前，才回头继续用目光照顾神柳。
[EN] "Wealth is external—you bring none at birth and carry none in death. On my waist it is just ornament; sold for coin to mend your nunnery or cover daily needs, it earns merit. Why fear being swindled?"
[ZH] “随缘而不攀缘”，难道他想和千里寻在一起便是攀缘？从目前来看的确困难重重，有种攀援险峰的困苦。从随缘来讲，难道他必须跟梦容在一起？
[EN] "Patron sees clean through worldly vanity," Master Jingguang praised. "In return for your jade pendant, I gift you seven words: *Wait through the clouds, and you will see the bright moon.*"
[ZH] 申简辰从心底还是不愿接受这样的结果。
[EN] Puzzled, Shen Jianchen asked, "Could Master put it plainer?"
[ZH] “王爷，官家身边的梁内侍从汴梁赶来，带来官家的圣旨。”
[EN] Smiling gently, she offered another phrase: "Follow fate; never force it."
[ZH] 传来白风的声音，申简辰并未理会圣旨之事，转过身厉声问：“该不会是你告诉郡主我前去抢亲？”
[EN] "Please enlighten me further, Master."
[ZH] 白风跪下，老实承认：“是我，我是为王爷和郡主好，不希望王爷辜负郡主。再说幽栖居士都要嫁人，王爷何必揪着她不放？”
[EN] "Following fate moves with wind and current, carrying life smooth; forcing fate rows upstream against headwinds, hitting snag after snag. Better to follow fate with the longing in your heart—only then will the clouds part to reveal the bright moon."
[ZH] 他从白风身旁绕过，用不满的语气警告道：“以后我的事，你少插手，既然做错事，罚你在神柳下一直跪到明日一早！”
[EN] A quiet click settled in his mind. "Thank you for your guidance, Master."
[ZH] 白风很委屈，等王爷走远，埋怨道：“明明是王爷自己做错事，还怪我罚我？实在不公平！”
[EN] "Amitabha." Joining her palms, Master Jingguang turned back up the mountain trail.
[ZH] 白风跪了一会儿，觉得自己像个傻子，王爷又没派人看着，老实跪着，着实可笑。不如起来在神柳下睡会儿，或者到山上的尼姑庵转转，或者到城里逛，也可去找苦荞，挨到明日再简单不过。
[EN] Watching her vanish among the trees, Shen Jianchen mulled it over: chasing Qianli Xun felt like clawing up a sheer cliff against every wind—was following fate telling him to accept his tie to Mengrong? Deep down, he still hated bowing to it.
[ZH] 白风决定还是去城里逛，打定主意后，起来拍拍身上的泥土，绕着山脚下往城里的方向走去。
[EN] "Your Highness! Imperial Attendant Liang arrived from Bianliang bearing an imperial decree!"
[ZH] 幽林别居的会客厅内，申简辰以仪王的身份接见了梁师成。
[EN] Spinning around at Bai Feng's voice, Shen Jianchen ignored the decree and snapped, "Was it you who tipped Her Highness off about the raid?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜陪同而来，说明陈家的喜宴已结束。现在已经是申时，喜宴又怎能不结束？
[EN] Dropping to both knees, Bai Feng owned up straight. "It was me! For Your Highness's and Her Highness's own good—I could not watch you betray the princess! Besides, Youqi Jushi was marrying today—why keep clinging?"
[ZH] 申简辰问：“梁内侍何日到达临安？”
[EN] Stepping past him, Shen Jianchen snapped, "Stay out of my private affairs! For meddling, kneel right here under the Divine Willow until dawn tomorrow!"
[ZH] 梁师成回道：“今日刚到，可惜没赶上知府大人家千金的婚事，一到府衙没敢歇着，急急忙忙让知府大人带我前来传旨。”
[EN] Indignant once his prince walked out of earshot, Bai Feng muttered, "HE messes up and punishes ME?! Totally unfair!" Kneeling five minutes without a soul watching felt idiotic—why not take a nap, hike to the nunnery, or stroll into town to see Kuqiao until morning? Choosing town, he hopped up, dusted off his knees, and headed around the foothills toward Lin'an.
[ZH] 申简辰客气道：“有劳梁内侍。”
[EN] Inside Youlin Retreat's reception hall, Shen Jianchen received Liang Shicheng, escorted by Zhu Xiyan now that the Chen banquet had wrapped up by mid-afternoon.
[ZH] 梁师成拿出圣旨：“仪王殿下，跪下听旨。”
[EN] "When did Imperial Attendant Liang reach Lin'an?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] 申简辰跪下，朱晞颜也陪着跪下。
[EN] "Just today, Your Highness! Shame I missed Prefect Zhu's daughter's wedding—came straight from the yamen without resting to deliver the decree."
[ZH] 梁师成展开圣旨，大声念道：“朕乃天命所归，承袭圣祖之德，忧国忧民，虑及国家安危，拟定一桩大事。朕家儿赵朴，才华出众，品性端正，乃我大宋未来之栋梁，当早日成婚，定下门庭。其婚期虽定，然朕欲早日见证佳儿佳媳之美满姻缘，故令婚期提前，必于接到圣旨后半月内归京，完婚后再回临安就职。钦此！”
[EN] "Much obliged, Imperial Attendant."
[ZH] 一面是梦容以性命相威胁，一面又是圣旨，申简辰已骑虎难下，看来必须随缘替赵朴娶他的妹妹。
[EN] Unrolling the yellow silk scroll, Liang Shicheng announced, "Prince Yi, kneel to receive the imperial decree!"
[ZH] 申简辰说出最不想说，又不得不说的话：“儿臣接旨。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen and Zhu Xiyan knelt together.
[ZH] 梁师成将圣旨放入仪王手中：“仪王殿下、知府大人快快请起。”
[EN] Liang Shicheng read aloud in sonorous tones: "By the Mandate of Heaven, inheriting ancestral virtue, We ponder the realm's welfare and decree a joyous matter. Our son Zhao Pu, gifted in talent and upright in character, stands as a future pillar of Our Song and ought to wed early and establish his household. Though his wedding date was set, We yearn to witness the blissful union of fine son and fair bride sooner, and hereby command the date advanced: return to the capital within half a month of receiving this decree, complete the wedding, and thereafter resume your post in Lin'an. Respect this!"
[ZH] 接过圣旨，申简辰更是感到无比沉重，由无法说出拒绝的话。
[EN] Pinned between Mengrong's threat on her own life and His Majesty's decree, every exit slammed shut—he had no choice left but to follow fate and marry Zhao Pu's sister.
[ZH] “梁内侍好不容来一趟，不如多住几日，欣赏欣赏临安美景，过几日与我一同回汴梁。”
[EN] Forcing out the heaviest words on his tongue, he bowed. "Your son receives the decree."
[ZH] 梁师成客气道：“感谢仪王盛情，本不该拒绝，怎奈朝中事多，近期又与金人商议燕京等地归还事宜，下官不能在此久留，今日就得赶路，还望仪王见谅。”
[EN] Placing the scroll into his hands, Liang Shicheng bade both men rise. The silk weighed like iron in Shen Jianchen's palms. "Since Imperial Attendant Liang traveled all this way, stay a few days to enjoy Lin'an and travel back to Bianliang with me."
[ZH] 本想对燕京等地归还之事谈谈看法，想到历史不可逆转，奸臣和昏君听不得忠言，便把想说的话咽回肚内。
[EN] "Grateful as I am for Your Highness's hospitality, court business presses—especially ongoing talks with the Jurchens over returning Yanjing. I must set out on the return road today; please excuse me."
[ZH] “既如此，我就不留梁内侍，您请便。”
[EN] Words criticizing the court's appeasement rose to Shen Jianchen's lips, only to die unspoken—history would not bend, and neither emperor nor sycophant wanted hard truth. "In that case, safe journey, Imperial Attendant."
[ZH] 朱晞颜亲自将梁师成送至幽林别居外，目送官家身边的红人坐上马车离开后，朱晞颜没有离开的意思。他撩起官服，在幽林别居外跪下。
[EN] Escorting the emperor's favorite eunuch out to his carriage and watching it roll away, Zhu Xiyan did not leave; lifting the hem of his official robe, he dropped straight to his knees outside Youlin Retreat's front gate.

#CH 196
[ZH] 幽林别居守门的下人见知府大人跪在大门外，甚是不解，又不敢上前劝说，将此事报告李管家。
[ZH] 李管家急急匆匆来到主院的屋外，向里禀报：“仪王，不知为何知府大人跪在别居大门外，下人们不敢贸然上前询问。”
[ZH] 申简辰手握圣旨，沉浸在婚期提前带来的不快中，对外说：“你去将知府大人请进来。”
[ZH] 李管家答应一声，出去叫人。
[ZH] 朱晞颜早已料到他这么一跪，仪王自然会问个究竟。果然没多时，李管家前来：“知府大人，仪王有请。”
[ZH] 朱晞颜爬起来，跟着向里走。再次来到仪王居住的院子，走进屋中，见仪王手里握着诏书，面无表情。他赶快恭敬地跪下：“下官朱晞颜请仪王殿下恕罪！”
[ZH] 申简辰淡淡地说：“知府大人何罪之有？”
[ZH] 朱晞颜低下头：“下官的女儿惹下大祸，都是下官教女无方，还请仪王恕罪。”
[ZH] 申简辰才明白原来朱晞颜是因抢亲之事：“今日之事是本王所为，与他人无关，知府大人不必自责。”
[ZH] 朱晞颜悬着心终于落地，庆幸遇到一位明事理的王爷：“仪王此言，更令下官自责，下官除今日谢罪，改日还会同陈家一起登门谢罪。”
[ZH] 申简辰听出些意味来，显然陈家想用钱财摆平他。他对千里寻的感情岂能用钱财衡量？他脸上露出不满：“我已说过与他人无关，若陈家真想谢罪，就请把幽栖居士还给我。如若还不了，最好别出现在我面前，省得让我看着心烦。”
[ZH] 一句话引得仪王不开心，朱晞颜紧张不已：“仪王殿下恕罪！”
[ZH] “下去吧，我想静一静，不要再来打扰。”申简辰只想将朱晞颜打发走，好安安静静适应突如其来的圣旨。
[ZH] 朱晞颜不敢再跪下去，起身低头行礼，慢慢退出。到门外后才站直，擦擦额头的汗，脸上露出轻松的笑容。
[ZH] 今日算是虚惊一场，一来仪王明理，他自己做错事不会随便牵连别人；二来京城的圣旨实在及时，既帮了郡主，也帮了朱陈两家。
[ZH] 朱晞颜偶尔也惋惜，错失一次与皇家结亲的机会。不过打今日起，他不再惋惜。
[ZH] 当他从陈家的喜宴回到朱府时，跟随迎亲队伍的几个下人回来向他说起仪王抢亲一事，还说郡主受伤，朱晞颜被吓得差点儿从椅子上掉下来。
[ZH] 佳和郡主看似柔弱，做起事来真是绝。若让淑真跟了仪王，面对那样的王府主母，估计没什么好日子过。相比较而言，选择陈家才是明智之举。
[ZH] 喜兰听李管家说官家身边的梁内侍从汴梁赶来向王爷传旨，猜到是郡主写的书信起到作用，兴奋地跑回闺房小院，推开门：“郡主！郡主！好消息！梁内侍来了！”
[ZH] 听到好消息，乔梦容精神许多，也不觉得伤口有多疼，从床上爬起来：“快给我收拾一番，我要去见梁内侍。”
[ZH] 喜兰到床前扶好郡主：“听李管家说，梁内侍来是传旨，传完又匆匆赶回汴梁。”
[ZH] “走了。”乔梦容倍感失望，立刻又问，“圣旨的内容是什么？”
[ZH] 喜兰肯定道：“那还用说？肯定是婚期提前的好消息，郡主很快能如愿以偿。”
[ZH] 喜兰的话如同一剂良药，乔梦容不仅觉得伤口好些，也顿觉腹中饥饿：“我饿了，弄些吃的来。”
[ZH] “好，郡主再躺会儿，奴婢很快把饭食给您端来。”
[ZH] 喜兰想先扶郡主躺下。
[ZH] 乔梦容执意不肯：“我想坐会儿。”
[ZH] 喜兰不再强求，去后厨为郡主安排饭食。
[ZH] 白风在城里逛得差不多，也去了趟陈府，想找苦荞。来到陈府大门外，敲开大门，说要找幽栖居士身边的丫鬟苦荞。
[ZH] 那下人根本不认识苦荞，还说今日陈家办喜事，下人一律不准出去。白风强辩道：“这个点儿婚礼早已结束，为何不让下人出来？”
[ZH] 陈家的下人说：“婚礼是举办完，可后续还有很多事情要做。”然后把大门“砰”地关上。白风只好悻悻地离开。
[ZH] 他想找家客栈，准备对付一晚，可每家客栈一晚上都得五十文钱，白风觉得太贵，他现在每日疯狂攒钱，只为以后娶了苦荞能过上安稳日子。
[ZH] 为攒钱，还是回幽林别居。
[EN] Startled to see the Prefect of Lin'an kneeling outside the front gate, the gatekeepers dared not budge him and sprinted for Butler Li, who hurried straight to the main courtyard. "Your Highness, Prefect Zhu is kneeling outside our gate for some reason, and the guards dare not question him!"
[ZH] 到达别居的大门口时，天已变黑。王爷让他在神柳下跪一晚，到底是该进别居还是去神柳下跪着？白风犹豫起来。
[EN] Still sitting grimly with the yellow decree in hand, Shen Jianchen called out, "Invite Prefect Zhu inside."
[ZH] 没犹豫多久，白风想进去碰碰运气，说不定王爷早已忘记惩罚他的事。
[EN] Right as Zhu Xiyan had calculated, Butler Li emerged moments later to summon him. Rising and following him to the prince's quarters, Zhu Xiyan saw Prince Yi holding the imperial scroll with an unreadable face and dropped straight back to his knees. "This humble official Zhu Xiyan begs Your Highness's forgiveness!"
[ZH] 白风来到王爷居住的院外，听到里面传出练习拳法的声音。他轻轻推开院门，小心走进去，正走着被王爷叫住。
[EN] "What offense have you committed, Prefect Zhu?" Shen Jianchen asked evenly.
[ZH] “白风，你跑去哪里？怎么一直没见你人影？”
[EN] Bowing his head low, Zhu Xiyan answered, "My daughter caused a grave incident—all due to my poor parenting. I beg Your Highness's pardon!"
[ZH] 白风庆幸，王爷果然忘记惩罚他的事：“王爷还问我？明明是你让我在神柳下跪着。”
[EN] Realizing he meant today's raid, Shen Jianchen replied, "Today's commotion was my own doing and involves no one else. Do not blame yourself, Prefect."
[ZH] 申简辰这才想起，一边打拳一边说：“那你跑回来干什么？继续到神柳下跪着。”
[EN] A huge breath of relief eased Zhu Xiyan's chest—thank heaven for a fair-minded prince! "Your Highness's graciousness shames me even more. Aside from today's apology, I will return another day with the Chen family to offer formal amends."
[ZH] 白风后悔自己无意中提醒王爷，还是赶快求饶：“王爷就饶了我吧，让我在院子里跪一晚上都比在神柳下强。”
[EN] Catching the hint that the Chens might try smoothing things over with lavish gifts, Shen Jianchen's face darkened. Could silver measure his heart for Qianli Xun?! "I already said no one else is to blame. If the Chens truly wish to make amends, hand Youqi Jushi back to me! If they cannot, keep out of my sight—looking at them only sets my teeth on edge."
[ZH] 申简辰无心与白风计较，今日抢亲未成功，又被一道圣旨限制，他心中多少窝着火。既不能埋怨朱陈两家，也不能责备梦容，他无处发泄，想着在院中打打拳也许会好些。
[EN] Startled by the sudden chill, Zhu Xiyan pressed his brow down. "Forgive my presumption, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 可打了半天心中依然窝火，他收起拳法：“白风，你想不想找人打一架？”
[EN] "Go. I want peace and quiet—do not disturb me."
[ZH] 王爷不再与他计较，白风变得开心起来：“当然想，王爷要打谁，尽管吩咐。”
[EN] Bowing out backward, Zhu Xiyan straightened outside the gate, swiped sweat off his brow, and smiled in sheer relief. False alarm! Not only did Prince Yi take full blame without dragging others down, His Majesty's decree had arrived right on cue to calm the storm for both the princess and the Zhu and Chen families. If Zhu Xiyan had felt a brief pang over missing royal in-laws before, that regret died dead today: hearing from escort servants how fragile-looking Princess Jiahe had thrown herself onto a sword proved what a ruthless mistress she would make—marrying Shuzhen into the Chen household had been far wiser.
[ZH] 申简辰想到林觉向他提起有伙金人打入邵家镖局，一直想到邵家镖局探探，因心系个人感情，将此事暂时搁置。今晚产生找人打一架的想法，不如到邵家走一趟。
[EN] Hearing from Butler Li that Imperial Attendant Liang had arrived from Bianliang with a decree, Xilan guessed Her Highness's letter had worked and dashed straight into her courtyard shouting, "Good news, Your Highness! Imperial Attendant Liang came!"
[ZH] “去邵家镖局。”
[EN] Energy surging back through her, Qiao Mengrong sat straight up ignoring her bandage. "Quick, dress me so I can see Imperial Attendant Liang!"
[ZH] “邵家镖局我知道，好几次逛街从门口经过，听人说镖局里个个武艺超群，曾想过去会会，今日终于得偿所愿。王爷，我们要不要乔装打扮一下，穿上夜行衣，蒙上面，偷偷潜入邵家？”
[EN] Steadying her, Xilan shook her head. "Butler Li says he delivered the decree and rode right back to Bianliang."
[ZH] “想什么呢！你我又不是见不得人？穿什么夜行衣？蒙什么面？光明正大去！”申简辰说着就往外走。
[EN] "Gone already?" Disappointment flashed before eagerness took over. "What did the decree say?"
[ZH] 白风赶紧跟上，他们刚走出院门，喜兰扶着梦容出现在面前。
[EN] "Need you ask? Advancing the wedding date, of course—Your Highness gets your wish at last!"
[ZH] 申简辰关切地问：“伤口还没好，怎么出来？”
[EN] Xilan's words worked better than any tonic; pain faded and appetite roared back. "I am starving—bring food!"
[ZH] 乔梦容轻声说：“我的伤无碍，心中挂念哥哥，过来看看，哥哥要去哪里？”
[EN] "Right away! Lie back a minute while I fetch dinner."
[ZH] “出去办些事。”申简辰不想让梦容知道去邵家镖局之事。
[EN] "No, I want to sit up." Yielding, Xilan hurried to the kitchen.
[ZH] 乔梦容寻思，朱淑真已嫁入陈家，哥哥肯定不是去找她。只要哥哥不是去找她，乔梦容便放心：“哥哥，听说今日梁内侍从汴梁赶来，不知所为何事？”
[EN] Meanwhile, after wandering Lin'an, Bai Feng had stopped by Chen Manor hoping to see Kuqiao—only for the gatekeeper to neither know Kuqiao nor let any servant out on wedding day, slamming the gate in his face. Looking into inns, fifty copper coins a night felt outrageous when he was pinching every copper to marry Kuqiao, so by dusk he crept back to Youlin Retreat torn between kneeling at the willow or gambling His Highness had forgotten the punishment.
[ZH] “前来传诏。”申简辰并不想隐瞒，“官家要求你我半月内回汴梁成亲。”
[EN] Approaching the prince's yard and hearing fist drills inside, he eased open the gate—only for Prince Yi to spot him at once. "Where did you vanish to all day, Bai Feng?"
[ZH] 果然是此事，乔梦容与喜兰感到高兴。
[EN] *Jackpot—he forgot!* "Your Highness asks me?! You ordered me to kneel beneath the Divine Willow!"
[ZH] “没想到官家如此着急，也说明官家心中时刻挂念哥哥。”
[EN] Remembering now, Shen Jianchen threw another crisp punch. "Then why crawl back here? March right back to the willow!"
[ZH] 申简辰不想再耽搁下去：“喜兰，扶郡主回屋歇着，不要再到处走动。”
[EN] Kicking himself for opening his big mouth, Bai Feng pleaded, "Spare me, Your Highness! Let me kneel right here in the yard all night—beats the willow!"
[ZH] 乔梦容很听话，忍着伤口的疼痛，慢慢转过身，被喜兰扶着回房休息。
[EN] With bigger storms roaring in his chest, Shen Jianchen had zero appetite for punishing his guard. Bottled-up rage over the failed raid and the forced decree had nowhere to go; hitting air wasn't cutting it. Dropping his stance, he asked, "In the mood for a real brawl, Bai Feng?"
[ZH] 申简辰则带上白风赶马车趁夜拜访邵家镖局。
[EN] Grinning wide at being let off the hook, Bai Feng cheered, "Always! Point the target, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 邵家镖局是临安最大的镖局，上上下下约二三百人，掌舵人是邵正邺，练得一身功夫，善用的兵器是大刀。他有一儿子，名唤邵志泽，十七八岁，继承了邵家刀法，人长得相貌堂堂。
[EN] Remembering Lin Jue's report about Jurchen raiders hitting the Shao Escort Agency—something he had sidelined over personal drama—Shen Jianchen decided tonight was perfect for paying them a visit. "Shao Escort Agency."
[ZH] 邵家镖局从不与临安富贵人家往来，今日陈家给儿子成亲，邵家镖局也没参加。不是怕送礼破费，而是邵正邺认为邵家镖局是靠本事和能力立足于世，无需对他人溜须拍马，搞得邵家如同临安城富户中的一股清流。渐渐，临安的达官贵人及富贵人家也将其遗忘。
[EN] "I pass their gate all the time!" Bai Feng lit up. "Folks say everyone inside is a blade master—always wanted to test them! Do we slip into black stealth suits and masks and sneak over the wall?"
[ZH] 在邵正邺看来，被人遗忘再好不过，他们邵家便可闷着头将家业发扬光大。
[EN] "What nonsense! Are we sneaking thieves? No masks, no black suits—we walk straight through the front door!"
[ZH] 来到邵家镖局大门外，白风敲开大门，命令对方：“去，告诉你们主人，我家公子前来邵家镖局挑衅。”
[EN] Heading out, they ran right into Qiao Mengrong leaning on Xilan's arm outside the gate.
[ZH] 对方并不害怕，向里喊道：“兄弟们，有人前来挑衅，出去会会！”
[EN] "Your wound is fresh—why are you out walking?" Shen Jianchen asked gently.
[ZH] 很快聚集十来人，手拿大刀，将门口堵住。
[EN] "It barely hurts—just worried about Brother," Qiao Mengrong murmured. "Where are you heading?"
[ZH] 领头的说：“挑衅是吧？先打败我们几个再说。”
[EN] "Errands," he deflected, keeping the escort agency to himself.
[ZH] 白风完全能对付，申简辰先站在一旁看热闹。
[EN] Knowing Zhu Shuzhen had married into the Chen family today, Mengrong breathed easier—at least he wasn't chasing her! "I heard Imperial Attendant Liang came from Bianliang today—what was the decree?"
[ZH] 有机会跟这么多人对打，白风斗志满满，二话不说，出拳打去。对方还没反应过来，白风已打在一人的脸上。只见那人向后倒去，手中的刀也不由自主落地。
[EN] "His Majesty commanded us back to Bianliang within half a month to complete our wedding," Shen Jianchen answered plainly.
[ZH] 其他人见状，手举大刀将白风团团围住。
[EN] Quiet triumph glowed across both princess and maid. "How eager His Majesty is—shows how deeply he holds Brother in his heart."
[ZH] 白风笑道：“别以为我手无寸铁就能被你们打败，有本事一起上！”
[EN] Unwilling to linger, Shen Jianchen instructed Xilan, "Help Her Highness back inside to rest and stop her from wandering."
[ZH] 明晃晃的大刀向白风杀将而来，他并不紧张，用轻功飞身而上，几把大刀扑了空，还被白风踩着，抽不出去，几人还没反应过来，被白风的连环脚，踢得一个个人倒刀落。
[EN] Docile as a lamb now, Qiao Mengrong turned back inside while Shen Jianchen and Bai Feng drove their carriage out into the night toward Shao Escort Agency.
[ZH] “就你们几个，还想跟小爷打？还是省省吧！”
[EN] The biggest escort outfit in Lin'an boasted two to three hundred armed guards under Master Shao Zhengye, renowned for his broadsword skill, alongside his seventeen- or eighteen-year-old handsome son Shao Zhize, heir to the family blade. Proud of standing on pure martial grit without sucking up to officials or tycoons—even skipping today's Chen wedding—the Shaos stood aloof from Lin'an's elite circles, happy to be left alone to build their trade.
[ZH] 白风见王爷径直走进邵家镖局，很快跟上去。
[EN] Pulling up outside their gate, Bai Feng pounded the wood and barked at the guard who opened up, "Go tell your boss my young master is here to challenge Shao Escort Agency!"
[ZH] 他们走进后，被更多拿大刀的人围起来。白风一个人对付起来有些困难，申简辰便与白风一起与拿大刀的人对战起来。
[EN] Unfazed, the guard hollered inward, "Brothers! Challengers at the door—come greet them!"
[ZH] 别看两人是用拳头对付明晃晃的大刀，他们每次都能巧妙躲开大刀的袭击，用拳头击打在对方的要害处。
[EN] Within moments, a dozen broadsword-wielding escorts blocked the entrance. "Looking for a fight?" their leader sneered. "Get past us first!"
[ZH] 越打申简辰觉得越爽，他心中的怒气随着拳头击倒每一个人得到疏散，直到所有人被他与白风打倒，才觉得心中不再压抑，也才心甘情愿接受“随缘而不攀缘”的说法。
[EN] More than enough for Bai Feng—Shen Jianchen folded his arms to watch. Eager for action, Bai Feng flashed forward and planted a punch dead on one guard's jaw before anyone blinked, dropping man and blade to the stones. As the rest ringed him with gleaming steel, Bai Feng laughed, "Think bare fists lose to blades? Come all at once!"
[ZH] 千里寻都嫁了，这会儿说不定已跟陈家的浪荡公子进入洞房，他再心痛，也奈何不了，不如安心接受命运安排，不求跟千里寻日日相守，只求知道她一切都好，在远处默默关心她，也算是全了自己的真情。
[EN] As blades slashed inward, Bai Feng vaulted with light-foot skill straight onto the flat of their broadswords, pinning their arms down before unleashing a blur of sweeping kicks that sent men and steel clattering everywhere. "Just you bunch? Save your breath!"
[ZH] 感情之事已想通，接下来该解决来此的主要目的。
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi stroll casually inside the main yard, Bai Feng followed straight into a wider ring of dozens more armed escorts. Outnumbered now, Bai Feng welcomed his prince stepping beside him. Bare fists against flashing steel, both dodged razor edges by a hair and struck crisp non-lethal knockout blows. With every guard dropped, the knot of fury in Shen Jianchen's chest loosened notch by notch until the last man fell and real clarity washed over him: Master Jingguang had been right—follow fate, never force it. Qianli Xun was married now, maybe already in Chen Haoyu's chamber; railing against destiny changed nothing. Accept the hand dealt him, wish her well from afar, and hold his true love quietly in his heart.
[ZH] 申简辰正打得投入时，邵正邺带儿子出现，两人一直观察，发现前来挑衅的二人虽厉害，并无伤人之意。令他们惊讶的是，这二人的拳法与颜池十分相似，该不会是他派来的？
[EN] Heart settled, time for business.
[ZH] 等到二人将手下打败后，邵正邺拱手道：“这位公子，你我素不相识，更谈不上得罪，为何要来我邵家寻事？”
[EN] Watching from the porch throughout the melee, Shao Zhengye and Shao Zhize had noticed two vital clues: first, neither intruder aimed to maim; second, their fist style looked uncannily like Yan Chi's! Were they his allies?
[ZH] 申简辰说：“并非来寻事，只想探探邵家的武功底细，今儿一试，还真不怎么样，也不知你们邵家是怎么在江湖上混出名头来？”
[EN] Stepping forward with cupped hands as the last guard groaned on the stones, Shao Zhengye spoke evenly. "We have never met nor crossed paths, Young Master. Why raid our Shao compound?"
[ZH] 邵志泽气得就要上去对打，被父亲阻拦。
[EN] "Not raiding—just testing Shao martial skill," Shen Jianchen answered coolly. "Turns out it is nothing special—beats me how you built such a big name in the martial world."
[ZH] “江湖上高手如云，我邵家今日被公子打败，他日也会有打败公子之人。邵家练武一来为押镖送镖混口饭吃，二来为强身健体。至于江湖上的名头，邵家从不看重。”
[EN] Shao Zhize bristled ready to charge, yet his father raised a hand to stay him. "The rivers and lakes teem with masters; beating us today means someone stronger awaits you tomorrow. We practice blades to guard caravans and keep fit, not chase hollow fame."
[ZH] 邵正邺的话说得在理，倒搞得申简辰不好再奚落下去：“邵家家主话说得冠冕堂皇，无懈可击，佩服。闲话少说，我来邵家有更重要的事情向你打听。”
[EN] A poised answer that took the edge off further taunts. "Smoothly spoken, Master Shao—respect. Enough banter; I came to ask about something serious."
[ZH] “公子请说。”邵正邺倒客气。
[EN] "Ask freely, Young Master."
[ZH] 申简辰直入主题：“听说有金人来邵家闹事，不知那伙金人现在何处？又为何来邵家寻事？”
[EN] "I hear a band of Jurchens raided Shao Escort Agency. Where are they now, and why did they come looking for trouble here?"
[ZH] 听到这个问题，邵正邺心中嘀咕，如此看来眼前这位公子跟颜池并不认识，可为何他们的拳法如此相似？
[EN] Shao Zhengye blinked inwardly—so this man wasn't allied with Yan Chi after all! Then how on earth did they share the exact same fist art?!

#CH 197
[ZH] 邵正邺将眼前的公子仔细瞧一遍，从对方的衣着和容貌来看，应该不是出自普通人家，说不定是哪位临安权贵。
[ZH] 邵正邺仔细回想，那日金人带人来寻事，只有林统制知道。他定然会禀报上司，难道眼前这位官职比林统制还高？没想到临安也有这等年轻有为的官员。
[ZH] 邵正邺拱手行礼道：“不知公子怎么称呼？”
[ZH] 申简辰本就不想隐瞒，向白风示意。
[ZH] 白风骄傲地说：“你眼前这位是官家的十三子仪王。”
[ZH] 邵正邺与儿子邵志泽怎么都没想到节度使仪王会来光临邵家镖局，武功还如此高强，二人既欣喜又敬重，上前跪下。
[ZH] “不知公子正是仪王，刚才多有得罪，还请恕罪。”
[ZH] “刚才跟你的人对打，算是帮本王疏散筋骨，你何罪之有？起来吧，给我说说那伙金人之事。”
[ZH] 邵正邺起身，先将仪王请入屋内，好茶伺候。他与儿子不敢入座，恭敬站立着。
[ZH] 待仪王品过茶后，与儿子互相交换眼色，才说道：“那伙金人，邵家也不知是何来头，看那样子，并不是普通客商。他们之所以来邵家镖局闹事，恐怕是听说我邵家有抗金之心，而且杀过几个金人，所以前来寻仇。”
[ZH] 听到“抗金”二字，申简辰兴致勃勃：“邵家爱国，甚合本王心意，你这个朋友本王交定了！”
[ZH] 一位王爷要与自己交朋友，邵正邺当然开心，同时也觉得自己对待朋友不够真诚，刚才的说辞真假参半，有些事邵家不能随意张扬。他与儿子行礼道：“仪王能瞧得上小人，是邵家之幸，还有一事需向仪王禀明。”
[ZH] “什么禀明不禀明的，既是朋友，坐下说话。”申简辰早已想结交些好汉，没有遇到梁山好汉，认识像邵家这等抗金之人也是好的。
[ZH] 邵正邺不再客气，挑椅子坐下；邵志泽是晚辈，不敢落座，老实站在父亲身旁。
[ZH] “今日见仪王拳法精妙，甚是佩服。令我感到奇怪的是仪王的拳法竟然与来邵家寻事的金人如出一辙。”
[ZH] “竟有此等事？”申简辰是申拳第三百六十代传人，仔细推算，北宋到他穿越前的时代大约一千年，怎么会在这个时代也有申拳？他继续问，“不知那伙金人现在何处？”
[EN] Studying the tall young man before him—fine robes, noble bearing—Shao Zhengye guessed he was no common citizen, perhaps a high Lin'an official sent after Division Commander Lin reported the Jurchen raid. Cupping his hands, he asked politely, "May I ask Young Master's name?"
[ZH] “那金人带七八个人先后两次前来闹事，第二次来时，对打过程中七八人全死在我儿手中，至于那领头之人被我一刀刺伤，但其轻功了得，逃了出去，我儿带人前去追赶，追到一条巷子不见踪影。”
[EN] Seeing no need to hide, Shen Jianchen nodded to Bai Feng, who announced with pride: "Standing before you is His Majesty's thirteenth son, Prince Yi!"
[ZH] “巷子在何处？”申简辰想亲自前去查探一番，金人受伤，应该不会跑太远，估计正躲起来疗伤。
[EN] Neither father nor son had ever dreamed Military Commissioner Prince Yi himself would step through their gate—let alone pack martial skill like that! Thrilled and awed, both dropped to their knees. "Ignorant of Your Highness's identity, we gave grave offense—please pardon us!"
[ZH] 邵正邺说：“在离蔣爷的赌坊不远处的迷 魂巷。听名字，仪王便知此巷进得去不容易出来，尤其是天黑时，躲个把人还真不好找，那金人误打误撞逃进巷内，又是晚上，我儿寻不得，只能放弃。”
[EN] "Sparring with your men stretched my stiff limbs—no offense taken. Rise and tell me about those Jurchens."
[ZH] “迷 魂巷可住人？”
[EN] Rising at once, Shao Zhengye ushered Prince Yi into the main hall, served fine tea, and stood respectfully alongside his son.
[ZH] “三教九流，什么人都有。只有长期居住在迷 魂巷的人才能摸得清楚方向。”
[EN] Once Prince Yi sipped, father and son traded a quick look before Shao Zhengye answered, "We do not know those Jurchens' true backing either—only that they were no ordinary traders. Likely they got wind of our Shao family's anti-Jurchen sentiment and a few Jurchens we killed in the past, and came for revenge."
[ZH] 邵志泽插嘴道：“那天晚上，我带人追赶金人，进入迷 魂巷，寻找一会儿没有结果，想原路返回，结果也迷了路，还是敲开一户人家的门，塞些银子，才被带出来。”
[EN] At the words "anti-Jurchen," Shen Jianchen's eyes lit up. "Patriots after my own heart—consider yourself my friend!"
[ZH] 申简辰对迷魂巷产生兴趣，打算亲自走一趟。刚才邵正邺提到蔣爷，他想起白天抢亲时，陈浩宇说他娶朱淑真是被蔣爷所逼。申简辰想不通陈家的婚礼与蔣爷有何关系？
[EN] A royal prince calling him friend thrilled Shao Zhengye—even as guilt pricked him over telling only half the truth about a family secret best kept quiet. Bowing alongside his son, he said, "Your Highness's favor honors our entire house. One more detail I must mention..."
[ZH] 一日之内两次听到此人的名字，又是他逼得两家婚礼突然举行，搞得申简辰措手不及，失去所爱，必须让此人尝尝他的拳头。
[EN] "Skip the formal reporting—friends sit and talk!" Having longed to befriend bold heroes—failing to meet Mount Liang's outlaws, anti-Jurchen fighters were next best—Shen Jianchen waved him to a chair while young Shao Zhize remained standing respectfully behind his father.
[ZH] “你刚提到的蔣爷是何许人？”
[EN] "Seeing Your Highness's extraordinary fist technique tonight amazed me," Shao Zhengye said carefully. "What struck me strangest is that your fist style matches the Jurchen leader who raided us almost stroke for stroke."
[ZH] 邵正邺说：“蔣爷是黑道中人，是钱塘帮的帮主，住在城东最偏僻之处，此人亦正亦邪。说他好吧，临安城的赌场一大半都是他开的，谁需要烧杀抢夺，只要给银子，蔣爷都敢接。说他不好吧，有时还会救济百姓。”
[EN] "Wait—really?!" Shen Jianchen froze. He was the 360th-generation heir of Shen Fist—nearly a thousand years separated the Northern Song from his own era, so how could a Jurchen here know Shen Fist?! "Where are those Jurchens now?"
[ZH] 申简辰才不管蔣爷是正是邪，既然影响到他的事，必然得挨打。
[EN] "He led seven or eight men raiding us twice," Shao Zhengye explained. "The second time, my son slew all seven or eight in the fight. I sliced the leader myself, yet his light-foot skill was astonishing—he escaped, and my son chased him into a maze of lanes where he vanished."
[ZH] 该了解的已了解，天色不早，不好继续打扰，申简辰起身：“感谢邵当家详细告知，深夜造访，甚是叨扰，改日到幽林别居与我痛饮几杯。”
[EN] "Which alley?" Shen Jianchen meant to track him down himself—wounded, the man could not have run far.
[ZH] 邵正邺起身相送：“能得仪王邀请，不胜荣幸，改日定当带上重礼登门造访。以后若有用得上邵家，仪王尽管言语。”
[EN] "Mihun Alley—literally 'Bewildering Alley'—not far from Master Jiang's gambling house. By the name alone, Your Highness can tell it is easy to enter and brutal to escape, especially after dark. That Jurchen stumbled inside at night and my son had to call off the hunt."
[ZH] 申简辰对这位新认识的朋友甚觉满意，按说与对方并不熟识，如此冒冒失失认领朋友似乎不妥，可谁让申简辰一穿越而来要么接触的是像文渊那样的才子，要么是昏君和奸臣，好不容易碰到像邵正邺这类人物，自然不管不顾认作朋友。
[EN] "People live in Mihun Alley?"
[ZH] 邵正邺父子将仪王送出门外，送上马车。
[EN] "Every walk of life—only longtime locals know their way through its twists."
[ZH] 目送对方离开后，邵志泽说：“爹，刚才没有向仪王说明实情，他以后知道肯定会怪罪我们。”
[EN] Shao Zhize chimed in, "That night I chased him inside, came up empty, and even got lost trying to back out—had to knock on a random door and bribe a resident with silver to lead us out."
[ZH] 邵正邺叹口气：“唉，能瞒就先瞒着，完颜池那小子暂时应该不会再来，看在你姑姑的面子上放他一马，下次若再来，我就用手中的大刀结果了他的小命！”
[EN] Making a mental note to check Mihun Alley—and catching the mention of Master Jiang! Twice in one day that underworld boss's name had crossed his path, the exact bastard who forced Chen Haoyu into rushing today's wedding and ripping Qianli Xun away! That man was definitely catching his fists. "Who is this Master Jiang you mentioned?"
[ZH] 话说陈家的婚宴结束后，乔文仙与一妻一妾同乘一辆马车回乔府。在外人看来，他甚是幸福，同一时间身边多出两个女人。
[EN] "Boss of the Qiantang Gang over on the far east side," Shao Zhengye explained. "Half villain, half rogue—owns over half Lin'an's gambling dens and takes dirty jobs for silver, yet sometimes hands out relief to the poor."
[ZH] 只有熟悉他的人清楚，不能跟喜欢的人在一起，他有多难过。新婚那晚，他难过得谁的房中都没去，搞得眼前的两个女人也难过一晚。
[EN] Rogue or saint, anyone who messed up Shen Jianchen's life was getting tuned up. With night deepening, he rose to take his leave. "Much obliged for the briefing, Master Shao. Forgive the late intrusion—drop by Youlin Retreat another day for a real drinking session!"
[ZH] 乔文仙打了一个酒嗝：“呃。”他喝得太多，脸色微红，双眼迷离。
[EN] "Honored beyond words, Your Highness! I will call with proper gifts soon—and if Your Highness ever needs the Shao Escort Agency, just send word."
[ZH] 一股浊酒之气立刻充满马车，苗知瑜掩住鼻孔；夏果则撩起马车窗帘，换换空气。
[EN] Walking out to his carriage, Shen Jianchen felt rare warmth over his blunt new friend—after months surrounded by dreamy poets, spineless emperors, and corrupt ministers, a straight-shooting martial man felt like fresh mountain air.
[ZH] 乔文仙借着酒劲儿打趣：“你们两个现在知道了吧，我……我没那么好，后悔还……还来得及……呃……”
[EN] Watching the carriage roll away, Shao Zhize murmured uneasily, "Father, hiding the full truth from Prince Yi—won't he be furious if he finds out later?"
[ZH] 又一阵浊酒之气，苗知瑜不好再掩着鼻孔，放下玉手：“文渊说的哪里话？我已嫁给你，无论你是什么样，我都不嫌弃。”
[EN] Shao Zhengye let out a heavy sigh. "Hide it while we can. That brat Wanyan Chi should stay clear for a spell—out of regard for your aunt, I let him off light. Dare show his face here again, and my blade ends his life!"
[ZH] 夏果撇撇嘴，脸上闪过不满之色，很快露出笑容：“不要瞎想，我跟苗姐姐怎么会嫌弃？”
[EN] Meanwhile, after the Chen wedding banquet ended, Qiao Wenxian rode back to Qiao Manor sharing a carriage with both wife and concubine—looking enviable from outside, hollow to the bone inside. On his own wedding night, grief had kept him out of both women's rooms, leaving them both sleepless till dawn.
[ZH] 夏果靠近乔文仙，故意将鼻子凑近他的嘴巴：“你看，我一点儿不嫌弃，要不文渊再打一个嗝儿，让我闻闻你的酒气。”
[EN] Flushed red with cloudy eyes, Qiao Wenxian let out a loud hiccup, filling the cab with stale wine fumes. Miao Zhiyu pinched her nose while Xiaguo lifted the window curtain for fresh air.
[ZH] 乔文仙半迷糊半清醒，虽知是夏果靠近他，面前的这张脸朦朦胧胧中像极了淑真。他的脸上露出笑容，准备伸手去触摸，结果那张脸消失在面前。
[EN] Slurring bitterly, Qiao Wenxian chuckled, "Now you two see... I am no prize... still time to regret... *hic*..."
[ZH] 原来苗知瑜嫌弃夏果装出娇媚的样子，没羞没臊当着她面勾引文渊，顾不上大家闺秀的风范，伸手将夏果推开。
[EN] Dropping her hand politely at his second hiccup, Miao Zhiyu replied gently, "What talk is that, Wenyuan? I am already your wife—no matter how you are, I would never look down on you."
[ZH] 夏果的头碰到一边，马车也因此晃了两下。碰得虽不严重，可也有些疼痛，夏果用手捂住右边的额头：“苗姐姐这是怎么了？想害我吗？”
[EN] Hiding a brief smirk, Xiaguo beamed right back. "Stop talking nonsense—Sister Miao and I could never turn up our noses at you!" Sliding straight against him, she tilted her nose right near his mouth. "See? Not disgusted one bit—hiccup again so I can smell it!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜坐端后，批评道：“夏果，你作为妾室，在外一举一动要懂得分寸。”
[EN] Through wine-blurred vision, Xiaguo's face pressing close shifted into Shuzhen's features! A soft smile broke over Qiao Wenxian's face as he raised a hand to touch her cheek—only for her face to vanish with a jolt!
[ZH] 夏果揉了揉额头，疼痛感已减弱，她放下手，不甘示弱：“我现在在乔家的马车里，不是在外面，也没外人，就我们三个，难道我还不能跟自己的夫君亲密些？”
[EN] Disgusted by Xiaguo flaunting herself shamelessly right before her eyes, Miao Zhiyu had forgotten lady-like restraint and shoved Xiaguo sideways! Xiaguo's forehead bumped the cab wall hard enough to rock the carriage. Rubbing her bruised temple, she snapped, "What is wrong with you, Sister Miao—trying to cripple me?!"
[ZH] “夫君也是你叫的？”苗知瑜更是不满。
[EN] Sitting stiffly upright, Miao Zhiyu rebuked her. "As a concubine, know proper conduct outside the house!"
[ZH] 乔文仙醉眼紧闭，听着二人吵闹，甚觉聒噪：“别吵了！”
[EN] Rubbing away the sting, Xiaguo shot right back, "We are inside a closed Qiao carriage with zero outsiders—just the three of us! Cannot I show a little affection to my own husband?!"
[ZH] 夏果立刻换副腔调：“我听文渊的，不跟苗姐姐吵，苗姐姐也不要主动找我吵。”
[EN] "Since when do you have the right to call him 'husband'?!" Miao Zhiyu snapped.
[ZH] 苗知瑜不再言语，心中更加讨厌夏果，她那股子骚情的劲儿自己实在学不来。回想夏果在幽栖居士身边当丫鬟时，怎么都看不出她还有这等本事，只能说明人是最善变的动物。
[EN] Eyes squeezed shut against the noise, Qiao Wenxian barked, "Shut up, both of you!"
[ZH] 想到幽栖居士，苗知瑜想起仪王抢亲之事，不如告诉文渊，既能让他彻底对幽栖居士死心，也可恶心夏果，毕竟幽栖居士曾是她的主子。
[EN] Switching tones on a dime, Xiaguo cooed, "I listen to Wenyuan—I will not argue with Sister Miao, long as she stops picking fights with me."
[ZH] 苗知瑜顿了顿，用平稳的语气说：“文渊，我今日听到一件与幽栖居士有关的事，甚是惊讶，到现在都不敢相信是真的。”
[EN] Clamping her lips shut, Miao Zhiyu seethed inwardly—she could never stoop to that cheap flirtiness! Back when Xiaguo served Youqi Jushi she had looked quiet enough—how fast people showed their claws! Wait: thinking of Youqi Jushi brought back Prince Yi's raid on the wedding today! Telling Wenyuan would kill his lingering devotion to Youqi Jushi AND rub salt in Xiaguo's pride over her former mistress!
[ZH] 听到“幽栖居士”的称谓，乔文仙自然睁开双眼，酒意少去一些：“什么事？”
[EN] Steadying her voice, Miao Zhiyu spoke smoothly. "Wenyuan, I heard astonishing news today about Youqi Jushi—still can hardly believe it."
[ZH] 夏果没当回事，近期也没听说小姐身边发生什么大事，苗知瑜又能编排出什么事来。
[EN] At the name "Youqi Jushi," Qiao Wenxian's eyes snapped open, sobering half a shade. "What news?"
[ZH] 只听苗知瑜说：“今日陈家迎亲途中，有人前去抢亲……”
[EN] Curious yet unbothered, Xiaguo listened too.
[ZH] 乔文仙酒醒了一半儿：“谁？”
[EN] "During the Chen bridal procession today, someone raided the street to snatch the bride..."
[ZH] 夏果心中第一个想到仪王，又不敢相信，也问道：“谁？”
[EN] Sobering fast, Qiao Wenxian demanded, "Who?!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜从文渊的表情中看出，他根本不愿相信还有人像他一样喜欢幽栖居士。于是大胆吐出两个字：“仪王。”
[EN] A guess flashed in Xiaguo's mind before she voiced the same word: "Who?!"
[ZH] 乔文仙果然不信：“你胡说，仪王怎么可能去抢亲？他与佳和郡主有婚约。”
[EN] Reading his utter disbelief that any other man could love Shuzhen like he did, Miao Zhiyu dropped two crisp words: "Prince Yi."
[ZH] 心中的答案得到确证，夏果不再言语，仔细观察文渊。她也希望文渊知道仪王偷偷喜欢小姐后，彻底将小姐从心中清除，她才有机会得到文渊的心。
[EN] "Rubbish!" Qiao Wenxian barked at once. "How could Prince Yi snatch a bride when he is betrothed to Princess Jiahe?!"
[ZH] 苗知瑜继续说：“此事闹得甚大，很多人都看到，听说佳和郡主以性命相逼，受了重伤，仪王才不得不罢手，否则今日恐怕陈家的婚礼只有新郎，而没有新娘，文渊若不信，可以去幽林别居亲自问仪王，或者看看佳和郡主有没有受伤便知。”
[EN] Suspicions confirmed, Xiaguo held her tongue and watched Wenyuan close—hoping this bomb would blast her old mistress clean out of his heart so she stood a real shot!
[ZH] 此事如同一碗效果绝佳的醒酒汤，乔文仙完全从醉意中清醒，他半晌没有言语。苗知瑜说得有鼻子有眼，不得不令人相信。他搞不明白何时仪王对淑真动情？到底是在他与淑真分手之前还是之后，他难以确定。
[EN] "Half the street saw it!" Miao Zhiyu pressed smoothly. "Word is Princess Jiahe put her own life on the line and took a serious blade wound before Prince Yi backed down—otherwise the Chens would have had a groom with no bride! If you doubt me, ride to Youlin Retreat and ask Prince Yi yourself, or check Princess Jiahe's wound."
[ZH] 用余光瞅到夏果，她以前是淑真的贴身丫鬟，应该知道些什么：“夏果，你一直跟着淑真，应该最清楚不过，告诉我，什么时候仪王对淑真动了心思？”
[EN] The shock hit like a bucket of ice water, wiping every drop of wine from Qiao Wenxian's brain. Speechless, he remembered how detailed Miao Zhiyu's tale sounded—far too specific to invent! When had Prince Yi fallen for Shuzhen?! Before he and Shuzhen broke up, or after?!
[ZH] 事已至此，夏果没必要再瞒：“具体什么时候我不大清楚，只知那时满城都是关于小姐的流言蜚语，乔家因此不再上门提亲，小姐十分痛苦，后来与文渊你分手，前前后后仪王来过几次望月轩，有时陪佳和郡主，有时一个人来，我才发现他对小姐动了心思……”
[EN] Snapping his eyes onto Xiaguo—her former personal maid—he demanded, "You stayed by Shuzhen's side all day! Tell me straight: when did Prince Yi start chasing Shuzhen?!"
[ZH] 没有恶心到夏果，苗知瑜失望的同时，第一次对夏果十分满意。这人啊，只要有共同的目标，不愁说不到一起。夏果如此配合，说明她也希望文渊快些忘记幽栖居士。
[EN] Seeing no point hiding now, Xiaguo laid it out. "I do not know the exact day—only back when rumors flooded the city and the Qiaos stopped calling, leaving Miss devastated right before breaking up with you, Prince Yi visited Wangyue Pavilion several times—sometimes with Princess Jiahe, sometimes alone—and I caught on he had fallen for her..."
[ZH] 乔文仙本希望夏果果断否认，她却一句否认的话都没有。还说仪王是在他与家人抗争，闹着绝食的日子里喜欢上淑真，那时他还拜托仪王帮他与淑真传递消息，没想到仪王明着是传送消息，实则暗藏私心。
[EN] Disappointed she hadn't rattled Xiaguo, Miao Zhiyu nonetheless felt unexpected warmth toward the girl—shared enemies made smooth allies, and Xiaguo clearly wanted Youqi Jushi wiped out of Wenyuan's mind just as badly!
[ZH] 乔文仙已坐不住，对外大声喊道：“去幽林别居！”
[EN] Having prayed Xiaguo would deny it, Qiao Wenxian froze as she pinned the exact timeline to his own hunger strike—the very days he had begged Prince Yi to carry messages to Shuzhen! Playing messenger on the surface while harboring private schemes underneath?!
[ZH] 苗知瑜看出文渊想要找仪王问个究竟，劝道：“你找仪王问话，也不急于一时。佳和郡主刚受伤，仪王这会儿正处于忙乱中，再说眼看着太阳就要偏西，不如明日再去。”
[EN] Unable to sit still another breath, Qiao Wenxian yelled out the window, "To Youlin Retreat!"
[ZH] 乔文仙心里虽急，怎奈时机不对，仍需耐心等待。他打算明日再去幽林别居，找仪王当面问些事情。
[EN] Stepping in smoothly, Miao Zhiyu urged, "No need to rush tonight—Princess Jiahe just got wounded so Youlin Retreat is in chaos, and dusk is already falling. Go tomorrow morning instead."
[ZH] 苗知瑜见文渊已默认她的建议，对外说：“回乔府。”
[EN] Burning inside yet seeing her logic, Qiao Wenxian stayed silent. Catching his mute consent, Miao Zhiyu called out, "Back to Qiao Manor."
[ZH] 乔文仙靠在马车里，听着街市嘈杂的喧闹声，回想仪王帮助他与淑真的过程，心中不断追问：“到底什么时候仪王对淑真动了心思？”他着实找不出任何端倪。
[EN] Leaning back against the swaying cab amidst street clamor, Qiao Wenxian replayed every favor Prince Yi had done for him and Shuzhen, tormenting himself with one question—*When did it start?*—and finding zero clues.

#CH 198
[ZH] 第二天一大早，乔文仙急急匆匆来到幽林别居。婚后，他还是第一次来。
[ZH] 他直奔仪王的院子，进入屋内。仪王刚刚用过早饭，丫鬟正收拾碗筷，仪王则看似悠闲地品着茶。
[ZH] 申简辰见文渊来访，甚是热情：“我还以为你有了妻妾，把我忘了，以后不会再来幽林别居。”
[ZH] 乔文仙很客气地拜过仪王，没有说话，坐到一旁，面无表情。
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐道：“白风，倒茶。”
[ZH] 白风提起茶壶，为乔公子倒上一盏：“乔公子，请用茶。”
[ZH] 南双给白风使眼色，白风放下茶壶，跟南双走出屋子，两人自乔公子婚宴后一直未见，想好好聊聊。
[ZH] 申简辰问：“你怎么不说话？”
[ZH] 乔文仙盯着仪王，反问道：“仪王为何昨日没去参加陈家的婚宴？”
[ZH] 昨日抢亲之事肯定已传开，从文渊的表情判断，应该为此事而来。他既然敢抢亲，也不怕向文渊承认自己的感情。
[ZH] “你既已知，不必我多说。”
[ZH] “什么时候的事？”
[ZH] “要说很久很久以前不大合适，应该是很久很久以后，我喜欢上她，喜欢上那个有趣的灵魂。”
[ZH] 乔文仙听不明白：“仪王难道想说你在另一个世界就已喜欢上淑真？”
[ZH] “可以这么理解。”
[ZH] 乔文仙认为仪王不过是想找个冠冕堂皇的理由，就像曾在官家面前想要讽刺官家是昏君时编的理由一样。
[ZH] 乔文仙忍不住不难过，听得出仪王是在他与淑真分手之前就喜欢上她。
[ZH] “你帮我和淑真时可否真心？”
[ZH] “我还不知她是幽栖居士时，自然乐意帮忙。后来……，我就不怎么乐意。”
[ZH] 乔文仙很迷惑，仪王口中的她与淑真有何区别？他无心思虑太久，顺着仪王的话，担忧地问：“什么时候仪王开始不乐意？”
[ZH] “你与幽栖居士第一次在西湖幽会时，我便知她正是我喜欢的人。”
[ZH] 乔文仙根本不愿接受仪王的理由：“仪王真会编故事，为自己偷偷喜欢上淑真找出这么一个无法令人信服的理由，还不如直接说你是在我与淑真分手前已偷偷喜欢上她，我还会觉得仪王是个爽快之人。”
[ZH] “是，我是在你未与她分手之前就喜欢上她。”
[ZH] 得到仪王肯定的回答，乔文仙更加难过：“那你为何还要帮忙劝说我爹答应到朱家提亲？”
[ZH] 申简辰叹口气：“唉，我虽喜欢她，可她那时满心满眼都是你，我希望她幸福，心里再不愿意，还是会劝说外公。”
[ZH] “老实告诉我，是不是第二次劝说我爹时，你根本没尽全力？”乔文仙的眼中充满担忧，他怕仪王的答复是肯定的。
[ZH] 只听仪王回道：“不完全是……”
[ZH] 乔文仙闭上眼睛体会内心之痛，他失去爱情，难道还会失去朋友？他睁开双眼，盯着仪王：“那就是……是了？”
[ZH] “你也知道，外公以死相逼，我毫无办法。加之我心中有她，自然庆幸老天替我找到一个不帮你的理由……”
[ZH] 乔文仙已听不下去，站起来，走到仪王跟前，抓住他的衣领：“我与家人抗争的那段时日，你是不是故意不帮我和淑真传送消息？”
[EN] At first light the next morning, Qiao Wenxian hurried to Youlin Retreat—his very first visit since his wedding—and strode straight into Prince Yi's courtyard. Inside, maids were clearing breakfast dishes while Prince Yi sat sipping morning tea at ease.
[ZH] 申简辰不由自主站起来，盯着文渊泛红的眼神。
[EN] Shen Jianchen greeted him warmly. "Thought wife and concubine had wiped me clean out of your mind and you would never darken my door again!"
[ZH] 文渊的劲儿不算大，申简辰毫无感觉，让文渊发泄发泄也是好的：“是，你与她已没有希望，我希望我是那个能与她在一起的人。”
[EN] Giving a stiff, formal bow without a word, Qiao Wenxian took a side seat with an expressionless face.
[ZH] “你一直都有婚约，比我好不到哪里去！有什么资格跟她在一起？”乔文仙将仪王胸前的衣裳抓得更紧。
[EN] "Pour tea, Bai Feng," Shen Jianchen instructed. Serving a bowl to Young Master Qiao, Bai Feng caught Nan Shuang's discreet signal and stepped outside with him to catch up after days apart.
[ZH] “有没有资格，对你我来讲已不重要，她已嫁给陈浩宇，你我只能接受命运安排。”申简辰既是安慰文渊，也是安慰自己。
[EN] "Why the silent treatment?" Shen Jianchen asked.
[ZH] “命运安排”几个字让乔文仙变得清醒，淑真已不属于他，他连一点儿希望都没有，又何必如此可笑地埋怨仪王？
[EN] Fixing him with a steady stare, Qiao Wenxian countered, "Why did Your Highness skip the Chen wedding feast yesterday?"
[ZH] 乔文仙松开仪王胸前的衣裳，抱着仪王哭起来：“呜呜……！你若是把她抢走，也比她跟着陈浩宇强！呜呜……！”
