[EN] Word of the raid was clearly all over town—and by Wenyuan's face, that was why he came. Bold enough to snatch a bride, Shen Jianchen saw no reason to dodge now. "Since you already know, why make me repeat it?"
[ZH] 申简辰用双手轻拍文渊的脊背：“男儿有泪不轻弹，别动不动就哭，让人看见笑话。情势逼得她与你分手，而后又无法选择我，都是命运捉弄。我该努力的已努力，差点将梦容的命给搭进去，也于事无补。不早不晚，官家诏书正好昨日送达，让我半月内回汴梁完婚，一切仿佛有人操纵一般。尼姑庵的法师对我讲‘随缘而不攀缘’，以前我过于攀缘，不如试试随缘，也许有一日可美梦成真，毕竟法师送我一句话：守得云开见月明。”
[EN] "When did it start?"
[ZH] 仪王一长串话，乔文仙没听进几句，倒是止住他的哭声，其中有一句让他琢磨不已：“你刚说淑真无法选择你，是什么意思？”
[EN] "Saying a long, long time ago is not quite right—more like a long, long time in the future. I fell for her—for that bright soul inside."
[ZH] 申简辰猜到文渊不过是担心淑真已完全放下他们之间的感情，倒不如让他清醒些，安心接受现实。
[EN] Baffled, Qiao Wenxian frowned. "Are you telling me you loved Shuzhen in another world?"
[ZH] “她与梦容是闺中密友，不愿伤害梦容，才多次拒绝我的感情，也才心甘情愿嫁到陈家……”
[EN] "You could put it that way."
[ZH] 乔文仙放开仪王，泪眼中充满痛苦：“你的意思是她已爱上你？”
[EN] Dismissing it as another slick fairy tale—just like the yarn Prince Yi once spun mocking His Majesty—Qiao Wenxian felt bitter grief rise: plain as day, Prince Yi had fallen for her BEFORE they even broke up! "When you offered to help Shuzhen and me, were you ever sincere?"
[ZH] 千里寻只说过刚穿越而来时心里有过他，至于现在，申简辰不是很确定，但他希望如此，更希望文渊不要再念着他爱的人。
[EN] "Before I realized she was Youqi Jushi, yes. Afterward... not so much."
[ZH] “是的，她已爱上我。”
[EN] Confused by his distinction between "her" and Shuzhen, Qiao Wenxian pressed past it. "When did you stop being willing?"
[ZH] 乔文仙一把推开仪王：“你胡说！”
[EN] "The very first time you and Youqi Jushi met secretly by West Lake, I realized she was the woman I loved."
[ZH] 然后痛苦地往出走，口中不断重复着“你胡说”三个字。他没有心情寻找南双，失魂落魄地坐上马车离开幽林别居。
[EN] Refusing to buy the tall tale, Qiao Wenxian scoffed painfully. "Always spinning fancy stories to dress up stealing a friend's girl! Better just admit straight out you fancied her before we broke up—at least I would respect your candor!"
[ZH] 申简辰没有追出去，文渊迟早要面对幽栖居士爱上别人的事实，不如现在让他彻底清醒过来。
[EN] "Fine. Yes—I loved her before you two broke up."
[ZH] 白风与南双离开后，爬上木质高台，边聊天儿，边欣赏远处的风景。
[EN] Hearing the flat admission twisted the blade deeper. "Then why did you still try talking my father into proposing to the Zhus?!"
[ZH] “你跟苦荞好着没？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen let out a low sigh. "Even though I loved her, her whole heart and eyes belonged to you back then. I wanted her happy—gritted my teeth and leaned on Grandfather anyway."
[ZH] “那还用问？”白风对自己的感情十分自信，转而问，“你呢？跟那个叫幼凡的怎么样？”
[EN] "Tell me the truth," Qiao Wenxian pressed, dread burning in his eyes. "The second time you spoke to my father—did you hold back?"
[ZH] “幼凡倒挺乖，也喜欢跟我聊天儿，可我……，算了，不说了，都是过去的事。”
[EN] "Not entirely..."
[ZH] 白风猜到南双的意思：“我警告你，夏果已是乔公子身边的人，你可不能再想着人家。”
[EN] Squeezing his eyes shut against the stab—losing his love AND his friend both?!—he snapped them open. "So... you DID hold back?!"
[ZH] “我知道，你放心，我再笨也不会抢公子的人。”南双从高处瞧见自家公子已走到大门口，“我家公子怎么不叫我就离开？改日跟你聊，我要去追我家公子。”
[EN] "You saw Grandfather threatening to kill himself—what could I do? And yes, loving her as I did, part of me thanked Heaven for handing me an excuse not to help you..."
[ZH] 南双快步走下木质高台，向公子追去。追到门口，公子的马车已离开，他在后面喊了好几声，公子都没有回应。还好幽林别居的车夫与他相熟，把马车赶过来，送他追赶自家公子。
[EN] Snapping at last, Qiao Wenxian leaped up, strode straight across, and seized Prince Yi by the collar! "While I was starving myself fighting my family—did you deliberately refuse to carry messages between Shuzhen and me?!"
[ZH] 白风则走下木质高台，去找王爷。刚来到正院外，王爷正好走出来。
[EN] Rising calmly to meet his bloodshot gaze—feeling barely a tug from the scholar's grip, letting him vent—Shen Jianchen owned it flat. "Yes. Your road with her was dead, and I hoped I would be the man who ended up with her."
[ZH] 申简辰并未理会白风，拐向佳和郡主的闺房小院。白风很自然地跟上。
[EN] "You have an imperial betrothal—you are no better off than me! What right did you have to be with her?!" Qiao Wenxian twisted the silk tighter.
[ZH] 诏书就像一剂良药，乔梦容只觉伤口比昨日好许多，她一早起来精神焕发，早饭也吃下不少。
[EN] "Right or no right means nothing to either of us now," Shen Jianchen replied quietly. "She married Chen Haoyu. All either of us can do is bow to fate." Comforting Wenyuan and himself in the same breath.
[ZH] 本想一早去见哥哥，听喜兰说乔公子来访，便安心坐在书桌前随手拿本诗集看，诗集里收录了不少本朝人的诗作。
[EN] "Bow to fate" hit like cold water. Shuzhen was gone—zero hope left—so what joke was he playing brawling with Prince Yi? Loosening his fists from the collar, Qiao Wenxian collapsed against Prince Yi's chest and wept aloud: "Wuuu! Even YOU carrying her off would have been better than handing her to Chen Haoyu! Wuuu!"
[ZH] 她随意翻看，以此消磨时光，安心等待乔公子离开。翻着翻着，看到一首绝句《对景漫成》，被题目吸引，再看诗句，充满情趣之美，不由念出声来：“半窗残照一帘风，小小池亭竹径通。枫叶醉红秋色里，两三行雁夕阳中。”
[EN] Patting his shaking back gently, Shen Jianchen murmured, "Real men do not shed tears easy—stop bawling before someone laughs. Circumstances forced her to leave you, then barred her from choosing me—fate toys with us all. I threw everything I had at it, nearly cost Mengrong her life, and still came up empty. Right on cue yesterday arrived His Majesty's decree ordering me back to Bianliang within half a month to wed—like invisible strings pulling every move. The nun up the hill told me: *Follow fate, never force it.* I forced things too hard before; maybe letting fate steer will turn a dream true someday—she also told me *Wait through the clouds, and you will see the bright moon.*"
[ZH] 念完后，乔梦容仔细品着：“真是一幅残阳夕照，秋高气爽的美景图，‘帘’字用得甚好，一下子将秋风与闺阁之情联系起来；‘通’字也用得好，将池塘、亭阁台榭、竹中的小径串在一起，还能写出竹中小径笔直幽深的特点；‘醉’字极其生动，将枫叶写活，仿佛是位娇羞的闺阁女子沉醉在爱情中红了脸；‘两三行’用得最妙，轻快松弛中渲染出高空的景象。”
[EN] Half the speech drifted past Qiao Wenxian, yet it dried his tears—and one phrase snagged sharp in his ears: "Wait—what did you mean saying Shuzhen was 'barred from choosing you'?!"
[ZH] 喜兰赞道：“只听诗句，觉不出有多美，被郡主这么一品，倒让奴婢感觉出诗的好来。”
[EN] Guessing Wenyuan dreaded hearing Shuzhen had truly moved on, Shen Jianchen figured a clean cut would finally set him free. "Because she and Mengrong are closest friends. Refusing to hurt Mengrong, she turned me down over and over and willingly married into the Chen family..."
[ZH] 乔梦容谦虚道：“诗不好，我也品不出好来，也不知是谁写的？”乔梦容向诗句后面瞧去，“幽栖居士”四个小字映入眼帘。
[EN] Pulling back with pain flashing through wet eyes, Qiao Wenxian whispered, "You mean... she fell in love with you?"
[ZH] 她顿时失去兴趣，将书扔到一边：“我也有眼拙的时候，刚才一番赞叹，她的诗根本配不上！”
[EN] Qianli Xun had only admitted feelings right after crossing over; how deep they ran today, Shen Jianchen could not be 100% certain—yet he hoped so, and wanted Wenyuan to stop chasing ghosts. "Yes. She fell in love with me."
[ZH] 因为生气，伤口不由疼起来，她将手放在左腹处，眉头紧皱：“喜兰，好好在里面翻翻，凡是有她的诗，全部撕掉。”
[EN] Shoving Prince Yi back hard, Qiao Wenxian choked out, "Liar!" Spinning away, he stumbled blindly out the doorway muttering "Liar! Liar!" under his breath—forgetting even Nan Shuang as he boarded his carriage in a daze and rattled out of Youlin Retreat. Shen Jianchen did not chase him; sooner or later Wenyuan had to face reality and move forward.
[ZH] 又一想，撕掉会留下痕迹，瞧着也会想起：“不，把书拿出去烧掉！”
[EN] Up on the wooden watch-platform outside, Bai Feng and Nan Shuang had been trading updates on Kuqiao and Youfan ("Quit eyeing Xiaguo—she is your master's woman now!" / "Don't worry, I am not that stupid!") when Nan Shuang spotted his master bolting out the gate solo. Sprinting down the stairs too late to catch the departing carriage, he hitched a ride from a friendly Youlin Retreat driver to chase him down.
[ZH] 喜兰已猜到刚才的诗是谁人所写，见郡主难受，想上前安慰，又被吩咐烧书，只能顾一头，还是先把书给烧掉，省得郡主看到难受。
[EN] Coming down to find his master, Bai Feng saw Prince Yi stepping out toward Princess Jiahe's courtyard and fell into step behind him.
[ZH] 她拿起书，到院子里打开火折子，将书点着，目睹整本书在火中化为灰烬。
[EN] Inside her quarters, the imperial decree had acted like miracle medicine on Qiao Mengrong: her wound felt worlds lighter, breakfast went down clean, and while waiting for Qiao Wenxian to leave before seeing Brother, she browsed a volume of contemporary Song verse at her desk.
[ZH] 当喜兰再次进入屋内时，郡主的脸色好了很多。她回道：“郡主，书已烧毁。”
[EN] Flipping pages, her eye caught a quatrain titled *"Impromptu Upon a View"* (*Duijing Mancheng*). Drawn by its charm, she read it aloud:
[ZH] 乔梦容挪开捂住伤口的手：“我以前收集过不少关于她的诗词，你今日在我房中认真搜寻一番，全部找出来，统统烧掉，我不想在自己的屋里看到任何与她有关的字迹。”
[EN] "Half a window of dying light, a single curtain of wind; A modest pond pavilion where a bamboo path leads through. Maple leaves flush drunk with red amid the autumn hues; Two or three lines of wild geese cross the evening sun." [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] “是，郡主。”喜兰忙着去搜寻。
[EN] Savoring every character, Qiao Mengrong praised it brightly: "What a crisp autumn sunset picture! 'Curtain' links the autumn breeze right to a lady's boudoir longing; 'leads through' threads pond, pavilion, and bamboo grove together while sketching a straight, deep trail; 'drunk' brings the maple leaves alive like a shy maiden blushing in love; and 'two or three lines' sweeps wide, airy height across the sky!"
[ZH] 喜兰正忙时，申简辰走进来。
[EN] "Hearing the lines alone I missed half of it," Xilan marveled, "yet once Your Highness unpacks them, it glows!"
[ZH] ﻿见王爷到来，喜兰停下手头之事，上前拜见：“王爷。”她不好再去忙，怕王爷问起原因，便站立一旁伺候。
[EN] "Only fine poetry yields fine reading—wonder who wrote it?" Glancing at the signature line, four tiny characters jumped out: *Youqi Jushi*.
[ZH] 哥哥到来，乔梦容开心许多，她身上有伤，并未起身，主要是担心哥哥看到她站起来时艰难的样子，会推迟回汴梁的时日。
[EN] Every spark of delight died on the spot as she flung the book aside! "Even my eyes go blind sometimes—wasting praise on verse she does not deserve!" Rage pulled sharp pain across her bandaged side; clutching her left flank with knotted brows, she barked, "Xilan, flip through that book and tear out every single poem by her—no! Take the whole book outside and burn it!"
[ZH] “我正要去看望哥哥，没想到哥哥就来。”
[EN] Guessing the author at once, Xilan scooped the volume out into the yard, struck a fire-starter, and watched it curl to black ash before reporting back to find her mistress's breathing calmer.
[ZH] 申简辰关切地问：“今日感觉如何？”
[EN] Dropping her hand from her side, Qiao Mengrong ordered coldly, "I collected stacks of her poems and lyrics before. Search my entire room today, pull out every single one, and burn the lot! I refuse to look at a single brushstroke tied to her under my roof!"
[ZH] 乔梦容掩饰道：“好多了，只盼早些跟哥哥回到汴梁。”
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰已放平心态，决定随了这段缘分：“等你伤好，就回汴梁完婚。”
[EN] Just as Xilan began rummaging shelves, Shen Jianchen walked in. Stopping her search to curtsy politely, Xilan stepped back in silence lest His Highness ask what she was clearing out.
[ZH] 乔梦容面露喜色：“我的伤无碍，随时可以出发。”
[EN] Brightening at her brother's arrival—staying seated lest rising awkwardly tempt him to delay their trip to Bianliang—Qiao Mengrong smiled. "I was just about to visit Brother, and here you are!"
[ZH] 申简辰犹豫片刻：“官家在诏书中要求半月内回汴梁，时间来得及，你再养两日，两日后出发。”
[EN] "How does the wound feel today?"
[ZH] 得到哥哥确定的答复，乔梦容更加开心：“哥哥放心，两日后我的伤会彻底痊愈。”
[EN] "Much better! Cannot wait to ride back to Bianliang with Brother."
[ZH] 申简辰勉强挤出微笑，向喜兰吩咐：“好生照顾郡主。”
[EN] Heart settled on following fate, Shen Jianchen nodded. "Once you mend, we head back to Bianliang for the wedding."
[ZH] 正要离开时，被梦容叫住：“哥哥要去哪里？”
[EN] "It barely hurts—we can leave anytime!"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回头：“临安城内有位叫蒋爷的人，听说是黑道，他得罪过我，打算前去会会。”
[EN] After a brief pause, he said, "His Majesty gave us half a month; plenty of time. Rest two more days—we depart the day after tomorrow."
[ZH] 只要哥哥不是去见朱淑真，乔梦容便放心：“哥哥小心，早去早回。”
[EN] "Two days from now I will be good as new!" Mengrong beamed.
[ZH] 哥哥带着白风离开后，乔梦容对喜兰说：“你继续忙吧，统统找出来，哪怕只有‘幽栖居士’四个字，也要烧毁，我不想再让她出现在我的生活中。”
[EN] Forcing a thin smile, Shen Jianchen told Xilan to tend her well and turned to go—only for Mengrong to call out, "Where is Brother heading?"
[ZH] “是。”喜兰答应完，继续忙活。
[EN] Without turning back, he answered, "There is an underworld boss in Lin'an called Master Jiang who crossed me. Going to pay him a visit."
[ZH] 乔梦容又暗自伤神，回忆曾与淑真躺在一张床上互诉衷肠；回忆与她围坐在火炉旁说些闺中私密之事。
[EN] Long as he wasn't sneaking off to Zhu Shuzhen, she felt at peace. "Be careful and come back early, Brother."
[ZH] 以后恐怕再也找不到一位能与她互诉衷肠之人。
[EN] Once he left with Bai Feng, Mengrong turned ice-cold to Xilan. "Keep searching—even if a sheet only bears the four characters 'Youqi Jushi,' burn it! Wipe her clean out of my life!"
[ZH] 她恨朱淑真，不仅仅因为她抢走哥哥的心，还因为她亲手葬送了她重视的友情。
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness."
[ZH] 喜兰已收拾出一堆书籍，从外面叫来几个下人将书搬出屋外，一把火点着，火势比烧一本书时大了许多。
[EN] Left brooding, memories of sharing a bed whispering secrets and warming hands by a brazier with Shuzhen flooded back—never again would she trust a friend like that. She hated Zhu Shuzhen not merely for stealing Brother's heart, yet for murdering their cherished sisterhood.
[ZH] 乔梦容在屋内也能看到火苗，在他人眼中是书籍被烧毁，在乔梦容看来烧毁的则是她的友情。
[EN] Soon Xilan hauled out armfuls of volumes with several servants and set a tall bonfire in the yard. Watching flames dance through her window, others saw burning paper; Qiao Mengrong watched her friendship turn to ash.

#CH 199
[ZH] 新婚头一日，千里寻早早起来，收拾完毕，用过早饭，准备去拜见长辈，左等右等不见陈浩宇前来。
[ZH] “苦荞，你去后院看陈浩宇起来没，让他快些。”
[ZH] 苦荞出门拐到后院，直接朝右边的屋子走去。屋门紧闭，她向里说：“姑爷，您起来没？我家小姐叫您一起去拜见长辈。”
[ZH] 门被青瓜打开：“公子还没起，我怎么叫都叫不醒。”
[ZH] “好吧，我去回小姐。”苦荞很无奈。
[ZH] 没过一会儿，千里寻来到后院，一把推开门：“陈浩宇！赶紧起床！再不起，小心老娘一拳把你打醒！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇吓得一骨碌爬起来，抱住被子：“你别过来，本公子没穿衣裳，不想被你这只母老虎看光。”
[ZH] 青瓜在一旁直想笑，他千呼万唤公子就是不起床，还用脚踢他，少夫人几句话吓得公子完全清醒，实在厉害。
[ZH] 千里寻转过身：“你那熊样儿，老娘才懒得看你，我数十下，你必须穿好衣裳站在老娘面前，一……二……三……”
[ZH] 青瓜伺候公子迅速将衣裳穿好，在千里寻数到“九”时，陈浩宇已收拾整齐，站在她面前。
[ZH] 千里寻满意道：“很好，陈府我不熟悉，你负责带路前去拜见长辈。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇牢骚满腹：“早知如此麻烦，连懒觉都睡不成，我才不结这破婚。”
[ZH] 千里寻奚落道：“你以为老娘愿意嫁给你，是你跪下求我，老娘看你可怜才勉强答应。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞅到青瓜在一旁笑，提醒道：“不要在下人面前揭我的短，行不行？”
[ZH] 千里寻强调道：“你啊，不用我揭，全身都是短，陈家的下人谁看不到？只是不敢说而已。少废话，赶紧带路。”
[ZH] 青瓜拦道：“少夫人，公子还没用早饭。”
[ZH] 千里寻怼道：“一顿不吃死不了人。”
[ZH] 青瓜不再说什么，老实跟着公子和少夫人。
[ZH] 三人走出屋外，陈浩宇抬头瞧见对面的屋子，门紧闭着，嘲笑道：“瞧见没，你身边那位嬷嬷还在睡懒觉，要不要本公子前去也把她唤醒？”
[ZH] 千里寻怼道：“你以为天下所有人都像你一样是头懒猪！这院子里韦嬷嬷起得最早，她一早去忙过继你名下财产之事。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇嘲笑不成，开始后悔将名下财产给朱淑真，后悔已来不及，只能走一步看一步。
[ZH] 苦荞在正屋门口等小姐，见他们走来，跟在后面。
[ZH] 刚要出院门，碰上姚轻琰身边的丫鬟映雪。
[ZH] 映雪行礼道：“公子，少夫人，夫人吩咐我前来带路。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇高兴道：“还是我娘最好，连带路这种事情都怕你麻烦我，专程派人来做。”
[ZH] 千里寻懒得再理会陈浩宇，对映雪说：“有劳。”
[ZH] 昨日进入陈府时，一直顶着盖头，注意不到府内的景致。等揭去盖头，又不能到处乱走动，故而陈府对住了一晚上的千里寻来讲完全是陌生的。她一边在心里准备着见到长辈时的说辞，一边用好奇的目光欣赏陌生环境。
[ZH] 陈府并不比朱府差，还比朱府多出明丽和鲜艳之色，最令千里寻满意的是供人行走的正路方方正正，只有花园中才会出现弯曲的小径。对于她这种路痴来讲，辨别起来简单许多。
[ZH] 想来想去，也想不出见长辈的说辞，还是见机行事。刚放空心情，昨日之事出现在脑海：梦容打她一巴掌，然后奔向士兵手中的利刃……
[ZH] 梦容用一巴掌结结实实地向她传递出她们的友情已走到尽头。
[ZH] 千里寻并不埋怨梦容，她暗暗告诫自己，无论梦容怎么对待她，以后绝不会再做伤害梦容之事。
[ZH] 只要她在陈家认真过下去，不去招惹梦容的哥哥，也许有一日，她们的关系还会恢复到从前。
[ZH] 千里寻已想好，跟陈浩宇做好名誉上的夫妻，过上一年半载，给他纳一房妾室，让妾室为陈家生几个大胖小子，陈家人也就不会只关注她，她也可乐得逍遥自在。
[ZH] 正想着，已来到公婆的院子。
[ZH] 开门的小厮高兴地进屋报告，又跑出来说：“公子，少夫人，老爷和夫人让你们进去。”
[ZH] 映雪依然前面带路：“公子，少夫人，这边请。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇不满道：“我爹娘的屋子本公子不知来过多少回，还用你请？不用对我说，对她说就行。”
[ZH] “是，公子。”映雪回道，转而说，“少夫人，请。”
[ZH] 快要见公婆，千里寻不好与陈浩宇计较，抬起脚踏进屋内，眼前比她想象的人要多。正位坐着陈浩宇的爹娘，来陈府闹时已见过，自然认识。
[ZH] 两边的侧位还坐着人，左边坐着两个男的，右边坐着三个女的。千里寻并不认识，却能判断出他们应该也是陈家的长辈。
[ZH] 千里寻上前拜道：“儿媳拜见公爹和婆婆，还有各位长辈。”
[ZH] 陈江林与姚轻琰很满意，今日的淑真通身一派大家闺秀的气派，与前来闹事那日判若两人。
[ZH] 见自家儿子站着一点儿礼数都不懂，陈江林立刻板下脸。
[ZH] 姚轻琰赶快给儿子使眼色。
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才行礼：“爹，娘，儿子给你们行礼了。”
[ZH] 儿子已行礼，陈江林不好一直板着脸，否则新媳妇还以为是对她不满。于是露出笑容：“你既嫁过来，也该熟悉熟悉陈家的长辈，这两位是你的二叔和三叔。”
[EN] On her first morning as a Chen bride, Qianli Xun rose at dawn, dressed cleanly, finished breakfast, and waited to go pay respects to the family elders—waiting and waiting with zero sign of Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 千里寻转向左边，大概根据座位已猜到哪位是二叔哪位是三叔：“两位叔叔好。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, check the back courtyard and tell Chen Haoyu to shake a leg."
[ZH] 两位叔叔都以笑容回应。
[EN] Rounding into the back yard to knock on the right-hand door, Kuqiao called out, "Young Master, awake yet? Miss wants you to greet the elders together."
[ZH] 姚轻琰说：“这边是你的两位婶婶和大嫂。”
[EN] Qinggua cracked the door wide with a grimace. "Dead to the world—cannot drag him up!"
[ZH] 千里寻转向右边：“淑真向两位婶婶和大嫂问好。”
[EN] "Alright, I will tell Miss."
[ZH] 两位婶婶点头回应。
[EN] Moments later, Qianli Xun marched straight into the back yard and slammed the door wide open! "Chen Haoyu! Out of bed NOW—stay down and I punch you awake!"
[ZH] 大嫂叶安雅上前扶住淑真：“早听说朱家有位女词人，一直想找机会见见，现在倒好，跟我成一家人。以后要是写了什么诗和词，定要让我拜读拜读。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu shot bolt upright clutching the quilt to his chest. "Back off! I am naked under here—do not want a tigress eyeballing me!"
[ZH] 面对此种状况，自然需谦虚，千里寻还是懂得：“淑真在外不过有些虚名罢了，大嫂若不嫌弃我写得字丑，改日写上一首，让丫鬟送到你房中，谈不上拜读，能帮你解闷儿也算我功德一件。”
[EN] Beside the bed, Qinggua fought back laughter—he had yelled and even kicked the bed to no avail, yet one bark from Young Madam jolted him wide awake!
[ZH] 陈浩宇在一旁观察，母老虎在陈家长辈面前不仅知礼，还装出一副文弱的样子，说出的话恰到好处，也不自称老娘，实在虚伪。他露出不屑的表情，心中骂道：“哼！装腔作势！”
[EN] Spinning around to face the wall, Qianli Xun scoffed, "Like I care to look at your scrawny hide! I count to ten—be dressed and standing before me. One... two... three..."
[ZH] 叶安雅笑道：“妹妹人长得美，又有才华，还会说话，浩宇真是有福气。”
[EN] With Qinggua shoving robes onto him at lightning speed, Chen Haoyu stood fully dressed at "nine."
[ZH] 新妇还要献茶，叶安雅不便多说，放开朱淑真坐回原位。
[EN] "Good," Qianli Xun nodded. "I do not know Chen Manor's layout—lead the way to the elders."
[ZH] 过来一个丫鬟，用盘子端着两盏茶，来到新妇面前。
[EN] "If I knew marriage meant losing my beauty sleep, I would never have bothered!" Chen Haoyu whined.
[ZH] 昨晚韦嬷嬷叮嘱过，今日要给长辈献茶。看到递过来的盘子，千里寻自然明白，她接过盘子，走上前，继续行礼：“公爹、婆婆请用茶。”
[EN] "Think I wanted to marry you?! You knelt begging me—only pitied you enough to agree!"
[ZH] 一旁的两名丫鬟从盘中各端起茶盏，送至老爷和夫人面前。陈江林与姚轻琰接过，象征性地品一口。
[EN] Catching Qinggua snickering on the side, Chen Haoyu snapped, "Quit airing my dirty laundry in front of servants!"
[ZH] 有丫鬟接过千里寻手中的空盘，又有人送上两盏茶。千里寻猜到应该是给两位叔叔献茶，她仍旧依礼而行。
[EN] "No need to air what hangs out in plain sight—every servant here sees it, just too polite to say! Move it!"
[ZH] 再送来两盏茶时，千里寻猜出最后的两盏是给两位婶婶。至于大嫂，与她算是同辈人，自然不用献茶。
[EN] "Wait, Young Madam—Young Master has not had breakfast!" Qinggua piped up.
[ZH] 礼毕后，姚轻琰说：“浩宇，带淑真前去拜见老太太。”
[EN] "Skipping one meal will not kill him."
[ZH] 来陈府闹时，见过陈浩宇的奶奶，千里寻有深刻的印象。
[EN] Shutting his mouth, Qinggua trailed behind them. Stepping past Matron Wei's shut door across the yard, Chen Haoyu jeered, "Look—your precious matron is still sleeping in! Want me to go bang her door down too?"
[ZH] 上次没给老太太留下什么好印象，只怕老人家没有公婆好说话。以后的日子还要在陈府过，千里寻打定主意，作为新妇的第一日，尽量在老人面前表现得听话些。
[EN] "Think everyone is a lazy hog like you? Matron Wei was up before dawn heading off to transfer all your assets into my name!"
[ZH] 拜别众人后，千里寻跟着陈浩宇前去见夏老太太。
[EN] Regret pricking him all over again over signing away his fortune, Chen Haoyu swallowed his grumble and kept walking.
[ZH] 刚走出爹娘的院子，陈浩宇已忍不住奚落道：“朱淑真，装什么装？有本事在陈家长辈面前拿出母老虎的架势来，让他们看到你的真面目。”
[EN] Picking up Kuqiao at the main hall, they reached the courtyard gate just as Yao Qingyan's personal maid Yingxue curtsied up. "Young Master, Young Madam—Madam sent me to guide you."
[ZH] 千里寻停住脚步，回过头，歪着脑袋，斜眼盯向陈浩宇：“你不说话，没人当你是哑巴！告诉你，什么样都是老娘的真实面目。你的家人对我和颜悦色，我当然知礼懂礼。只有对待你这种人，老娘才会变成母老虎。再说那日来闹事，你爹娘和奶奶早已看到，老娘何须装？哼！”
[EN] "Mother really loves me—sparing me even guide duty!" Chen Haoyu cheered.
[ZH] 千里寻不再理陈浩宇，继续朝前走。
[EN] Ignoring him, Qianli Xun nodded politely. "Much obliged, Yingxue."
[ZH] 陈浩宇寻思：“我这种人？什么意思？”然后喊道：“我这种人，是什么人？”
[EN] Under her red veil yesterday she had seen zero of Chen Manor; walking through now, she admired its bright, open elegance—best of all, main paths ran dead straight in neat squares, curving only inside gardens, pure mercy for someone with terrible sense of direction. Yet the quiet stroll pulled yesterday's nightmare back: Mengrong's stinging slap, lunging toward steel... One slap had declared their friendship dead. Bearing no grudge, Qianli Xun swore silently never to harm Mengrong again; live calmly inside Chen Manor, stay clear of Prince Yi, and maybe someday their sisterhood would heal. In six months or a year, let Chen Haoyu take a concubine to bear Chen heirs while she enjoyed peace and total freedom!
[ZH] 这个问题直接将千里寻气到，她转过身，逼近陈浩宇：“你竟然不知道自己是什么样的人？今日就让老娘给你说个明白……”
[EN] Arriving at her in-laws' courtyard, where a smiling door boy announced them inside, Yingxue gestured ahead—prompting Chen Haoyu to snap, "Walked into my parents' house a thousand times—say 'after you' to HER, not me!" Smiling smoothly, Yingxue pivoted to usher Young Madam across the threshold.
[ZH] 千里寻手叉腰，俊目圆睁：“你就是……纨绔子弟！浪荡公子！逼良为娼！眠花醉柳！浪子班头！欺男霸女！好赌成性！道貌岸然！作恶多端！自以为是！不务正业！好吃懒做！卑鄙无耻！鼠目寸光！油嘴滑舌！趾高气扬！得意忘形！不学无术！……”
[EN] Inside sat more elders than expected: Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan in the seats of honor, two men to the left, three women to the right. Stepping forward, Qianli Xun sank into a crisp, graceful curtsy. "Your daughter-in-law pays respects to Father-in-Law, Mother-in-Law, and all elders."
[ZH] 千里寻将自己知道的所有含义不好的四字词语一股脑倒给陈浩宇，直到实在想不出来时，才停下，喘口气：“说完了！”
[EN] Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan beamed in quiet delight—every inch a polished noble daughter today, worlds away from the rock-smashing terror at their gate! Catching his son standing slack beside her without bowing, Chen Jianglin's face went stone cold; Yao Qingyan shot her son a frantic glance until Chen Haoyu muttered a hasty bow: "Father, Mother, your son greets you."
[ZH] 从朱淑真口中蹦出许多词，陈浩宇气愤的同时，也慨叹她不愧是才女，竟能想出这么多词骂他，心想：“我有这么差吗？”
[EN] Softening at once lest his bride feel unwelcome, Chen Jianglin smiled and introduced the room. "Meet the Chen elders: on your left sit your Second Uncle and Third Uncle."
[ZH] 青瓜和苦荞看到这一幕，只顾在一旁偷乐。
[EN] Turning left, Qianli Xun greeted them smoothly. "Greetings, Second Uncle and Third Uncle." Both smiled back.
[ZH] 两个下人都笑他，陈浩宇更来气：“朱淑真，你把我说得如此差，还不是嫁给我？也没见你嫁给乔文仙，或者仪王。”
[EN] "And on the right," Yao Qingyan added, "your two aunts and Eldest Sister-in-Law."
[ZH] 此话正好戳在千里寻的痛处，她不再言语，放下插在腰间的双手，将陈浩宇甩在后面，一味朝前走。
[EN] "Shuzhen greets both aunts and Eldest Sister-in-Law," Qianli Xun curtsied cleanly.
[ZH] 苦荞快走几步跟上自家小姐。
[EN] Both aunts nodded while Eldest Sister-in-Law Ye Anya rose warmly to take her hands. "Long have I heard of Zhu Manor's celebrated poetess and hoped to meet you—now we are sisters under one roof! Whenever you pen fresh poems or lyrics, you must let me admire them!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇终于胜出一局，不由吹起口哨，表达兴奋的心情，也跟在后面朝前走。
[EN] Giving the textbook modest answer, Qianli Xun smiled. "Merely empty fame outside, Sister-in-Law. If you do not mind my clumsy brush, I will send a poem over soon—hardly worthy of admiration, yet worth merit if it eases a dull afternoon."
[ZH] 来到一个路口，见朱淑真打算向左拐，陈浩宇才停止吹口哨：“喂！左边是通往二叔的院子，往右边拐才对！”
[EN] Watching from the side, Chen Haoyu rolled his eyes inside: *What a phony! Acting sweet, refined, not one swaggering boast out of her mouth—total hypocrite!*
[ZH] 千里收住脚步向右拐，走着走着，一座院子出现在眼前，应该是老太太住的地方。她停住脚步等会儿陈浩宇，打算与他一同走入。
[EN] "Gorgeous, gifted, and silver-tongued—Haoyu struck gold!" Ye Anya laughed before returning to her seat for the tea rite.
[ZH] 陈浩宇走近后，根本没理她，径直进去。千里寻不想计较，跟在陈浩宇身后。
[EN] Briefed by Matron Wei the night before, Qianli Xun accepted a lacquer tray holding two bowls of tea from a maid, knelt gracefully, and raised it. "Please take tea, Father-in-Law and Mother-in-Law." Maids conveyed the bowls to Chen Jianglin and Yao Qingyan, who took ceremonial sips. Following suit, she presented fresh bowls to both uncles, then both aunts—skipping peer-generation Ye Anya.
[ZH] 陈浩宇像变了个人似的，疾步向屋内走去，边走边压着腔调：“奶奶！孙儿好想您！”
[EN] Rites finished, Yao Qingyan said, "Haoyu, take Shuzhen to pay respects to Old Madam."
[ZH] 千里寻只觉全身的鸡皮疙瘩都已起来，强忍着走进屋内。
[EN] Remembering Old Madam Xia's fury on her earlier raid—unlikely to go as smooth as her in-laws—Qianli Xun resolved to keep polite and low-key on day one.
[ZH] 夏老太太正坐在榻上，陈浩宇扑进老太太怀里，那个骚劲儿，实在令千里寻看着恶心。
[EN] Hardly had they cleared the courtyard gate before Chen Haoyu sneered, "Quit the fake act, Zhu Shuzhen! Got guts? Pull your tigress act in front of the elders so they see the real monster!"
[ZH] 千里寻行礼道：“奶奶，淑真前来拜见。”
[EN] Stopping dead, Qianli Xun tilted her head and glared sideways. "Nobody thinks you are mute if you keep your jaw shut! Hear me straight: BOTH faces are real! Treat me warm like your family does, and I return every ounce of grace; act like slime like YOU do, and out comes the tigress! Besides, your parents and grandmother already saw me wreck the gate—who am I fooling?!" She marched right ahead.
[ZH] 老太太见到孙子满面欢喜，顾不上理会一旁的孙媳妇，也想冷落冷落她，省得嫁过来欺负自家孙子。
[EN] "Slime like me?!" Chen Haoyu yelled after her. "What kind of man do you think I am?!"
[ZH] “我孙子就是好，知道一大早来看奶奶，赶紧坐下，陪奶奶喝茶。”
[EN] Spinning on her heel and stepping right into his nose, fists planted on hips and bright eyes blazing wide, Qianli Xun unloaded every single scathing four-character epithet in her vocabulary in one breathless volley: "A spoiled dandy! Libertine rake! Bully of decent women! Brothel hound! Chief of scoundrels! Tyrant of the streets! Compulsive gambler! Sanctimonious fraud! Habitual crook! Arrogant fool! Idle loafer! Gluttonous sluggard! Shameless creep! Shortsighted dimwit! Slick-tongued liar! Pompous peacock! Smug idiot! Ignorant dunce!..." Stopping only when out of breath, she gasped, "Done!"
[ZH] 老太太没理会朱淑真，陈浩宇很开心，坐在奶奶对面聊起天儿来。
[EN] Hit by a lexical avalanche, Chen Haoyu blinked half-furious, half-awed by a poetess's insult range—*Am I REALLY that awful?!*—while Qinggua and Kuqiao burst into muffled giggles behind them.
[ZH] “奶奶，前几日我逛街，听人说有一位大夫很会治疗腿脚疾病，改日我把他请到家里来，给奶奶瞧瞧。”
[EN] Stung by the servants laughing too, Chen Haoyu lashed back where it hurt: "Call me all that trash—and yet YOU STILL MARRIED ME! Didn't see Qiao Wenxian or Prince Yi marrying you, did I?!"
[ZH] “还是我孙子会体贴人。”老太太满眼都是爱溺。
[EN] The cheap shot landed dead on the raw bruise. Dropping her hands without another syllable, Qianli Xun turned sharply and strode off ahead, Kuqiao hurrying right behind. Scoring one win at last, Chen Haoyu whistled triumphantly down the path—pausing only at a fork when she steered left: "Hey! Left goes to Second Uncle's—right for Grandmother!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续压着腔调：“主要是奶奶疼我，对我好。”
[EN] Turning right without a word, Qianli Xun paused outside Old Madam Xia's gate to enter together, only for Chen Haoyu to brush straight past her and dash inside putting on a syrupy baby voice: "Grandmother! Your grandson missed you SO much!"
[ZH] 婆孙两个聊得火热，将千里寻晾在一边。
[EN] Goosebumps prickling up her arms, Qianli Xun gritted her teeth and walked inside to see Chen Haoyu throwing himself into Old Madam Xia's lap on the couch—gross enough to turn her stomach! Dropping into a curtsy, she said evenly, "Shuzhen pays respects to Grandmother."
[ZH] 面对老太太的冷漠，千里寻路上想的那些哄老太太的话一句也说不出口。冷静片刻后，她决定忍住一时之气。
[EN] Beaming over her grandson while intentionally freezing out the bride to knock her down a peg, Old Madam Xia ignored her completely. "What a sweet boy, coming bright and early! Sit right down and share tea with Grandmother."
[ZH] 苦荞在一旁为自家小姐担心，本以为公婆好一切都好，没承想陈家还有一个厉害的老太太。
[EN] Delighted by the snub, Chen Haoyu perched opposite and laid it on thick. "Heard on the street about a miracle doctor for sore legs—I will bring him over for you soon, Grandmother!"
[ZH] 老太太关心道：“来，靠近些，让奶奶瞧瞧是不是瘦了。”
[EN] "My boy is always so thoughtful!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇把脸伸过去，故意鼓起腮帮子。
[EN] "Only because Grandmother spoils me best!"
[ZH] 老太太用手摸上去，批评道：“收回去，奶奶的眼神好着呢。”
[EN] Leaving Qianli Xun standing like a lamp post in the middle of the floor, the two cooed back and forth—killing every flattering phrase Qianli Xun had rehearsed on the walk over. Steadying her breath, she chose patience while Kuqiao fretted silently beside her: lovely in-laws, terrifying grandmother!
[ZH] 陈浩宇恢复正常：“我没瘦，还胖了一些。”
[EN] "Lean closer—let Grandmother see if you lost weight," Old Madam Xia cooed.
[ZH] 老太太摸过后，又批评道：“又骗奶奶，明明瘦了。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu leaned in puffing his cheeks out; she tapped them chuckling. "Quit puffing—Grandmother's eyes are sharp!" Dropping his cheeks, he said, "Not thinner—plumper!" Touching his face again, she fretted, "Lying to Grandmother—definitely thinner!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想趁机编排朱淑真，好让奶奶收拾她：“奶奶不知，一大早，我连早饭都没吃就来拜见您，您当然会觉得我变瘦。”
[EN] Seeing a golden opening to sick Grandmother on Zhu Shuzhen, Chen Haoyu sighed dramatically. "No wonder I look gaunt, Grandmother—marched straight over here on an empty stomach without a bite of breakfast!"
[ZH] 老太太急了：“什么？没用早饭？青瓜，你是怎么伺候公子的？为何连早饭都不让公子吃？”
[EN] Old Madam Xia snapped straight up. "No breakfast?! Qinggua! How are you looking after Young Master, letting him starve?!"
[ZH] 青瓜支吾半天不好言语。
[EN] Qinggua stammered helplessly.
[ZH] 陈浩宇趁机说：“不能怪青瓜，怪只怪我娶了一个厉害的娘子，是她不让我吃早饭。”
[EN] "Do not blame Qinggua," Chen Haoyu sighed like a martyr. "Blame me for marrying a terrifying wife—SHE refused to let me eat!"
[ZH] 老太太终于瞧向朱家姑娘，厉声道：“跪下！”
[EN] At last, Old Madam Xia whipped a blistering glare onto Miss Zhu and barked: "KNEEL!"

#CH 200
[ZH] 一直被老太太冷落，好不容易说话，还如此严厉，竟让自己跪下，千里寻哪愿受这等气？
[ZH] 她没有按照老太太的要求跪在地上，再次行礼，用平静的语气说：“奶奶在上，不能只听陈浩宇一面之词。不是孙媳不让他用早饭，皆因他睡懒觉耽搁，已到拜见长辈的时间，还赖在床上不起来，等他穿戴完毕，已过用早饭的时间。”
[ZH] 老太太只想帮孙子杀一杀孙媳的气焰，对方说得再有理，她都无动于衷。
[ZH] “长辈让你跪下，你不立刻下跪，还强词夺理。告诉你，在陈家，我孙子想睡懒觉便睡懒觉，想干什么就干什么，有我在，谁敢约束？你刚嫁过来头一日，如此欺压我孙子，以后还了得？今日我偏罚你在这院子里跪上一日。”
[ZH] 陈浩宇十分得意，有奶奶替他撑腰，朱淑真吃不了兜着走。
[ZH] 千里寻觉得老太太甚是无理，一味溺爱陈浩宇，难怪他会成为现在这样。既然对方无理，她又何必知礼。
[ZH] 千里寻轻松下来，见旁边的桌上放着茶壶和茶盏，亲自走过去，提起茶壶倒上一盏，双手捧着，来到老太太面前：“我既嫁到陈家，又唤您一声奶奶，今日这茶还是要敬的。”
[ZH] 夏老太太并不接，头转向一边。
[ZH] 千里寻将茶盏放在老太太面前的桌上：“茶我也敬了，奶奶既然看孙媳不顺眼，我还是尽快离开，省得惹奶奶不高兴。”
[ZH] 夏老太太用手将茶盏打翻在地，茶水泼了一地，茶盏也摔成两半。
[ZH] “我让你在我院子跪上一日，难道你没听见？”
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“孙媳当然听到，可我没错，为何要跪？难道只因您是长辈就可以随意欺负打压晚辈不成？”
[ZH] 夏老太太被气得无话可说，大声说：“来人！”
[ZH] 四五个丫鬟出现在千里寻身旁。
[ZH] 夏老太太命令道：“按住她！让她老老实实跪在地上！”
[ZH] 几个丫鬟那日见识过少夫人的厉害，都站在原地不敢动手。
[ZH] 千里寻看着几个手无缚鸡之力的丫鬟，冷笑道：“老太太，您还是省省吧，您又不是没见识过我的厉害，她们几个，我一拳就打倒，还想把老娘按在地上，简直是笑话！”
[ZH] 她懒的再继续待下去，大喊道：“苦荞！我们走！”
[ZH] 刚走到门口，千里寻又回过头：“老太太，我送您三句话：事败皆因懒，人废皆因闲，家败皆因奢。您要是真爱您孙子，就不要惯着他，否则有一日他把陈家的家业都霍霍光了，您想找个哭的地儿都没有。”
[ZH] 说完，千里寻扬长而去。
[EN] Frozen out for half an hour and barked to kneel on her first line? No way Qianli Xun was eating that! Staying upright, she offered a calm second bow. "Respectfully, Grandmother, do not take only Chen Haoyu's side of the story. I did not deny him breakfast—he slept past the hour for greeting the elders, refused to leave bed, and by the time he dressed, breakfast hour had long passed."
[ZH] 陈浩宇指着门口：“奶奶，您瞧瞧，她多嚣张，连您的话都不听。”
[EN] Dead set on breaking the new bride's pride on day one, Old Madam Xia refused to hear reason. "An elder orders you to kneel, and instead of dropping down you talk back?! Hear me plain: inside Chen Manor, my grandson sleeps late when he wants and does as he pleases—who dares police him while I breathe?! Bullying my grandson day one—what next?! I sentence you to kneel in this courtyard for the entire day!"
[ZH] 谁知老太太脸上竟露出笑容：“不是你自己主动要娶的吗？怎么这会儿又嫌弃？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu beamed—with Grandmother backing him, Zhu Shuzhen was cooked!
[ZH] 陈浩宇被奶奶搞糊涂：“奶奶，您怎么不生气？还笑得出来？”
[EN] Disgusted by the blind coddling that had rotted Chen Haoyu into a useless rake, Qianli Xun dropped every ounce of meekness. Spotting teapot and bowls on a side table, she strolled calmly over, poured one cup, and carried it in both hands before Old Madam Xia. "Having married into the Chen family and called you Grandmother, I still owe you morning tea."
[ZH] 夏老太太继续笑道：“刚才生气都是装的，不过是想杀杀她的威风，让她对你好些。听她刚才那些话倒是个明事理的人，肯定不会随便欺负你。更重要的是她面对我的无理打压毫不畏惧，有几分我年轻时的样子，奶奶甚是喜欢。你既然主动要求娶她，好好跟她过日子，别再整日往出跑，去那些不三不四的地方。”
[EN] Old Madam Xia jerked her face away without reaching out.
[ZH] 陈浩宇万万没想到，连奶奶都被朱淑真收买，还是以这种方式收买：“奶奶，您是不是有了孙媳妇，就不再爱孙子？”
[EN] Setting the bowl calmly on the low table beside her, Qianli Xun said, "Tea is served. Since Grandmother cannot stand the sight of me, I will excuse myself rather than spoil your mood."
[ZH] “胡说，奶奶当然爱你。”
[EN] With one sharp sweep of her hand, Old Madam Xia knocked the tea bowl crashing off the table, shattering it across the tiles in a splash of hot tea! "Did you go deaf?! I ordered you to kneel in my courtyard all day!"
[ZH] “她要是打我，奶奶是向着她还是向着我？”
[EN] "Heard you loud and clear, Grandmother—yet I committed zero wrong, so why kneel? Does seniority hand out a blank check to bully juniors at will?!"
[ZH] “当然向着你。”
[EN] Purple with fury, Old Madam Xia bellowed, "Servants!" Four or five maids rushed in. "Hold her down! Make her kneel!"
[ZH] 婆孙两个又说会儿话，陈浩宇想起朱淑真刚才说他有一日会将陈家的家业霍霍光，想到找蒋爷要字据的事儿，便离开夏老太太的住处，准备去钱塘帮走一趟。
[EN] Remembering the splintered chair at the front gate, every maid froze in her tracks, not one daring to step forward.
[ZH] 到陈府门口时，见青瓜还跟着，转身就是一脚：“谁让你还跟着我？赶紧滚！本公子要去办正事，你跟着算哪门子事儿？”
[EN] Sweeping a cool look over the trembling girls, Qianli Xun scoffed, "Save the show, Old Madam! You already saw what my fists do—one punch drops all five of them, and you expect them to pin ME down? Laughable!" Spinning around, she snapped, "Come on, Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 这一脚青瓜没有预料到，被踢在肚子处，他捂住肚子：“哎吆！少夫人让我跟着您，我得听她的话。”
[EN] Pausing at the threshold, she tossed one last volley over her shoulder: "Take three truths from me, Old Madam: ventures fail through laziness, men rot through idleness, and houses ruin through indulgence. If you truly love your grandson, quit spoiling him—lest the day he burns the entire Chen fortune to ash, you have nowhere left to cry!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇逼迫道：“再说一遍！你听谁的话？”
[EN] Striding out with head high, she left silence behind her.
[ZH] 青瓜继续捂住肚子：“当然，也听公子的话。”
[EN] Pointing at the empty doorway, Chen Haoyu cried, "Look how cocky she is, Grandmother—ignoring even YOU!"
[ZH] “既然听我的，赶紧滚！”陈浩宇丢下青瓜，大摇大摆走出陈府。
[EN] To his utter shock, a warm chuckle broke across Old Madam Xia's face. "Weren't you the one begging to marry her yesterday? Turning sour already?"
[ZH] 当陈浩宇来到陈府外时，疤子与几个小弟早躲在外面等他。见陈公子出来，一个个围上来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu blinked in total confusion. "Grandmother—you aren't mad?! You are LAUGHING?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想起与蒋爷赌博那晚，他们像缩头乌龟的样子就来气：“还来找我干吗？那天晚上都不敢帮我，以后少跟爷爷混。”
[EN] "The whole tantrum was an act—just wanted to test her nerve and knock her down a peg so she goes easy on you! Listening to those sharp words, she has a clear head and will not bully you without cause. Better still, standing dead steady under my bullying—spitting image of me in my youth! I actually like her! Since you wanted her, settle down and live right with her, and stay out of filthy haunts!"
[ZH] 疤子笑嘻嘻地说：“陈公子，您大人有大量，原谅我们，那晚您也看到，蒋爷人多势众，我们想帮都不敢帮。事后哥几个也后悔，下定决心以后不管陈公子有任何吩咐，赴汤蹈火，在所不辞。”
[EN] Floored that Zhu Shuzhen had won Grandmother over by wrecking her tea ritual, Chen Haoyu whined, "Got a granddaughter-in-law and stopped loving your grandson already?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇要去见蒋爷，只身一人显得太单薄，说不定还会打起来，总得带些人前去。
[EN] "Nonsense—Grandmother loves you most."
[ZH] “给你们一个将功补过的机会，现在！立刻！马上！给本公子把陆远峰找来，除了他再多找几名打手，本公子要去会会蒋爷，一会儿钱塘帮门口见。”
[EN] "If she hits me, whose side are you on?"
[ZH] 疤子与众小弟说：“收到！我们这就去给陈公子寻人。”
[EN] "Yours, of course."
[ZH] 众位小弟散去后，陈浩宇上了马车，往城东最偏僻的钱塘帮而去。
[EN] Chatting a few minutes more, Qianli Xun's warning about burning the Chen fortune jolted him back to Master Jiang's contract! Bidding Grandmother goodbye, he marched straight out to settle with the Qiantang Gang.
[ZH] 到达钱塘帮大门外，陈浩宇从马车的窗户瞅到外面还放着一辆陈府的马车，心中纳闷：“为何还有辆陈府的马车？”
[EN] At Chen Manor's gate, spotting Qinggua trailing him, he whirled with a sudden kick straight into his belly! "Who asked you to tail me?! Beat it—important business!"
[ZH] 正疑惑时，瞧见陈浩东从钱塘帮的大门走出。
[EN] Doubled over clutching his gut, Qinggua wheezed, "Ow! Young Madam ordered me to stick with you—got to obey her!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇很担心，该不会他与蒋爷赌博立字据的事暴露？他爹才一早派东哥前来解决此事？
[EN] "Say that again! WHOSE orders do you take?!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇顾不上仔细思索，走下马车，等待东哥靠近。
[EN] "Young Master's too, of course!"
[ZH] 陈浩东走着走着瞧见陈浩宇，心中先是一惊，他与蒋爷的勾当可不能被堂弟识破，还好他提前早有准备，立刻面露笑容走上前：“浩宇怎么也来钱塘帮？”
[EN] "Then SCRAM!" Ditching Qinggua behind, Chen Haoyu swaggered out the gate straight into Scarface and his lackeys lurking outside.
[ZH] 东哥脸上笑容可掬，应该还不知道，陈浩宇放松心情：“我还想问东哥怎么跑到这里来？”
[EN] Remembering them turning tail like cowards in the gambling house, Chen Haoyu snapped, "Got the nerve to show your faces after ditching me?! Find another boss!"
[ZH] 陈浩东装作埋怨的样子，解释道：“你啊，从不理会陈家生意，当然不知做生意的难处，白道得供着，黑道也不能落下，若照顾不周，必然给陈家惹来麻烦。今儿我来，不过是为跟蒋爷走动走动，免得他以后给陈家的生意制造麻烦。”
[EN] "Mercy, Young Master Chen!" Scarface grinned sheepishly. "Master Jiang had a whole army that night—we wanted to jump in yet dared not! Afterward we kicked ourselves and swore: from now on, say jump and we dive straight into fire!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇更加轻松，庆幸惹下的丑事家人还不知：“有劳东哥，陈家的生意有东哥操心，我爹也能轻松许多。”
[EN] Unwilling to walk into the Qiantang Gang solo in case blows flew, Chen Haoyu barked, "Fine—earn back your spots! Right this second, drag Lu Yuanfeng PLUS a few extra musclemen to meet me at the Qiantang Gang gate!"
[ZH] “不能如此说，这么大的生意哪能只靠我一人？大家各司其职。什么时候你也能加入进来，帮我分些担子，我也好轻松轻松，相信大伯和奶奶会更开心。”陈浩东虚伪地试探着。
[EN] "On it!" The lackeys bolted in all directions while Chen Haoyu boarded his carriage for the eastern edge of town.
[ZH] 陈浩宇对他的东哥毫无防备之心，认为对方说的是真心话：“我什么样东哥还不知道？我对做生意不感兴趣，只要有钱花，有得玩儿便心满意足。”
[EN] Pulling up outside the Qiantang Gang, Chen Haoyu squinted out the window in surprise—a second Chen Manor carriage already sat parked outside! Moments later, Chen Haodong strolled straight out of the gang's gates!
[ZH] 堂弟的话令陈浩东满意：“你还没告诉我为何来这里？”
[EN] Panic shot through Chen Haoyu—had his gambling disaster leaked, sending Father's right-hand man Haodong here to mop up?! Hopping down, he braced himself as his cousin approached.
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞎编道：“前几日在赌坊遇见蒋爷，他邀请我没事来坐坐，今日正好有空，过来走动走动。”
[EN] Spotting Haoyu gave Chen Haodong an instant start—must not let his cousin sniff out his secret deal with Master Jiang!—yet prepared with a ready cover, he flashed a smooth smile. "Haoyu? What brings you to the Qiantang Gang?"
[ZH] 陈浩东心中不屑，表面装作相信的样子：“你与蒋爷搞好关系，对陈家也有好处，既如此，去忙吧，我还得去茶庄转转，查查账本。”
[EN] *Smiling warmly—phew, he has no clue!* Relaxing at once, Chen Haoyu countered, "I was about to ask what brings Brother Haodong out here!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇目送东哥上马车离开，认为东哥是他拍马都赶不上的人。
[EN] Putting on a weary elder-brother sigh, Chen Haodong explained, "You stay clear of Chen commerce, so you miss the headaches of trade—got to stay friendly with officials on the bright side AND underworld bosses in the shadows, or trouble hits our shops. Dropped by to keep Master Jiang sweet so our business runs smooth."
[ZH] 高手还未来，陈浩宇不能贸然进去，需在外面等会儿。他瞧着钱塘帮大门两边站着的壮汉，身材壮硕，面目谈不上狰狞，但让人看了也有种不敢靠近之感。
[EN] Relieved beyond words, Chen Haoyu praised him. "Hard work on your shoulders, Brother Haodong—spares Father tons of grief."
[ZH] 陈浩宇等来等去，没等来高手，却等来仪王的马车。心中一乐，仪王能前来肯定是昨日成亲路上他对仪王说的话起到作用。
[EN] "Do not flatter me—an empire this vast is too heavy for one pair of hands. Join in someday to share the load; Eldest Uncle and Grandmother would be thrilled!" Chen Haodong probed smoothly.
[ZH] 仪王手下侍卫的厉害陈浩宇已领教过，能想象到仪王肯定更厉害，如果跟着这么厉害的人去见蒋爷，不愁要不回字据。
[EN] Totally blind to his cousin's guile, Chen Haoyu waved it off. "You know me—zero taste for trade! Long as silver keeps rolling and fun stays hot, I am set."
[ZH] 陈浩宇赶紧迎上去：“没想到在此也能遇见仪王殿下。”
[EN] Satisfied completely, Chen Haodong smiled. "Still didn't say why you came out here."
[ZH] 申简辰瞧向车窗外，看到是陈浩宇，一个最没资格娶，事实上又娶了他喜欢之人的人，他心中极其不爽，并未理会，走下马车，带着白风径直朝钱塘帮大门走去。
[EN] "Ran into Master Jiang at a gambling den a few days back and he invited me to drop by—figured I had a free morning," Chen Haoyu fibbed.
[ZH] 陈浩宇猜出仪王肯定是不满他娶了朱淑真，不如让他高兴高兴，好帮自己要回字据。
[EN] Sneering inside yet nodding smoothly, Chen Haodong replied, "Good for the Chens if you stay chummy with Master Jiang. Carry on—I have books to check at the tea shop." Watching his cousin drive away, Chen Haoyu shook his head in awe—he could never match Brother Haodong in a million years.
[ZH] 他快走几步，赶上仪王：“仪王殿下，能否借一步说话，是关于朱淑真的。”
[EN] With no hired muscle arriving yet, he lingered outside eyeing the burly gate guards—when another familiar carriage rolled up: Prince Yi! Elation surged in Chen Haoyu's chest! His tip during yesterday's raid had paid off! With Prince Yi's lethal guard—and surely an even deadlier prince—backing him, Master Jiang would cough up that contract in seconds!
[ZH] “朱淑真”三个字还真管用，仪王果然停下脚步，将陈浩宇瞧了一眼，朝一边走去。
[EN] Hurrying forward, he greeted him eagerly: "What luck running into Your Highness here!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇赶紧跟上。
[EN] Glancing out at the worthless rake who had somehow married the woman he loved, Shen Jianchen felt pure bile rise and ignored him flat, stepping down with Bai Feng straight toward the gang's gate.
[ZH] 感觉白侍卫听不到时，陈浩宇才说：“昨日我已告诉仪王，我娶朱淑真是被蒋爷给逼的，所以我跟朱淑真成亲是假……”
[EN] Guessing the prince held a grudge over the wedding, Chen Haoyu hustled alongside him. "Your Highness, one private word—about Zhu Shuzhen!"
[ZH] 申简辰虽希望是假，可还是怀疑陈浩宇欺骗他：“陈家广发喜帖，婚礼又十分盛大，你说是假，本王就得信？”
[EN] Magic words—Shen Jianchen halted, eyed him once, and stepped aside out of Bai Feng's earshot.
[ZH] 陈浩宇解释道：“婚礼当然不假，可我跟朱淑真是假的，我们在婚前已说清楚，互不干涉，昨晚我连洞房都没入，还被朱淑真赶到后院住一晚，不信仪王可以让白侍卫问问苦荞。”
[EN] Lowering his voice, Chen Haoyu spilled fast: "Like I told you yesterday, Master Jiang forced me into marrying Zhu Shuzhen—so our marriage is completely fake!"
[ZH] 听到还未入洞房，申简辰既高兴，又心疼千里寻。朱淑真有段不幸的婚姻，她成为她，帮朱淑真摆脱掉婚姻限制，好不容易遇到一段属于千里寻的婚姻，却不是因为喜欢对方才坐上花轿。
[EN] "Invitations across Lin'an and a massive feast—and you expect me to call it fake?" Shen Jianchen scoffed.
[ZH] “她那么有主见的人，不喜欢你，又怎能嫁给你？”
[EN] "The feast was real, yet the marriage is pure paper! We signed terms beforehand—total non-interference! Last night I never even entered the bridal chamber; Zhu Shuzhen booted me straight to a back shack! Doubt me? Have Guard Bai ask Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇将赌博那晚的情形详细叙述一遍，继续说：“蒋爷设局欺我，逼我娶朱淑真，我没办法，去求她，她不同意，我只好跪在她面前，也许是她见我可怜才答应成亲，当然我也给了她不少好处，我们还立下契约。”
[EN] Relief spiked through Shen Jianchen alongside a sharp pang of pity for Qianli Xun: freed from one loveless cage only to climb into a sham marriage out of necessity. "Stubborn as she is, why would she marry you without caring for you?"
[ZH] “契约？”申简辰不解。
[EN] Recounting the entire gambling ambush, Chen Haoyu added, "Master Jiang rigged a bet to force me to wed her. Desperate, I dropped to my knees begging her—maybe she pitied me, plus I promised her sweet perks and we signed a written contract."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续解释：“白纸黑字写下婚后大家怎么过日子，还有关于财产的安排。”
[EN] "A written contract?"
[ZH] 申简辰心疼千里寻的同时，甚是怀疑陈浩宇：“你为何对我说这些？”
[EN] "Spelled out in black and white how we live apart—and transferring all my assets to her."
[ZH] 陈浩宇见仪王的态度比刚才好很多，更加大胆：“我娶了仪王喜欢的人，心中甚是不安。不希望仪王对我有看法，才对您说这些。当然也希望仪王帮我从蒋爷手中要回字据，我向仪王保证，以后绝不动朱淑真一根毫毛，若机缘成熟，定将她还给仪王。”
[EN] Pitying Qianli Xun yet suspicious of Chen Haoyu's sudden candor, Shen Jianchen narrowed his eyes. "Why tell me all this?"
[ZH] 申简辰总觉得不对劲，蒋爷面对陈家的财产不动心，搁谁谁信？里面定有阴谋，还需搞搞清楚。他露出笑容：“看在你这么懂事的份上，字据本王帮你要定了。”
[EN] Emboldened by the warmer reception, Chen Haoyu pitched straight: "Marrying the woman Your Highness loves made me sweat bullets! Wanted to clear the air—and beg Your Highness to help pry my contract out of Master Jiang's claws! I swear on my life I will never touch a hair on Zhu Shuzhen's head, and the day the moment ripens, I hand her straight back to Your Highness!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇认为自己实在聪明，把仪王哄得高兴，既能解决蒋爷，也为未来解决朱淑真打好基础。
[EN] Something smelled fishy as hell—an underworld boss turning down the whole Chen fortune? Definitely a scheme underneath worth digging out! A thin smile touched Shen Jianchen's lips. "For being so sensible, I will get your contract back."
[ZH] 申简辰甩下陈浩宇，继续向大门走去。刚到门口，被两名壮汉拦住：“你是何人？为何来钱塘帮？”
[EN] *Pure genius!* Chen Haoyu beamed—flattered Prince Yi, crushed Master Jiang, AND laid a clean escape hatch from Zhu Shuzhen!
[ZH] 话音刚落，白风一人一拳，迅速将二人打倒在地：“怎么说话呢？这位是申公子，前来会会你们帮主。”
[EN] Leaving him behind, Shen Jianchen strode up to the gate. "Who are you? What business at Qiantang Gang?" the two guards barked.
[ZH] 对方如此厉害，两名壮汉不敢再拦着。
[EN] Before the echoes faded, Bai Feng dropped both with two crisp punches. "Watch your mouths! Young Master Shen is here to see your boss!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟着向里走，不觉威风起来，对两名壮汉吼道：“听清没？是申公子！申公子！厉害着呢！打得你们落花流水！”
[EN] Strutting past the groaning guards behind them, Chen Haoyu crowed, "Heard that?! Young Master Shen! Going to wipe the floor with you lot!"
[ZH] 刚走进院中，围上来一伙人，手拿利刃，堵在三人面前。
[EN] Stepping into the main yard, a ring of armed gangsters closed around all three with gleaming steel.
